### [1] [Evolving to Match Capabilities: Improving Harness Self-Evolution via Skill Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33024)
*Zhihao Yang,Haojin Wang,Juncheng Wu,Kuan Zhang,Yukun Chen,Run Luo,Yaojie Lu,Yuyin Zhou,Lu Wang,Xianpei Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现harness（智能体外壳/技能框架）带来的收益随模型能力而异：提升强模型的技能可能拖累弱模型；为此提出"技能适配"——用固定meta-skill指导技能改写以匹配执行者能力，在自进化循环中稳定带来2.0–5.1分提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: harness自进化让LLM智能体无需更新参数即可从执行经验中提升，但其有效性取决于模型能否真正执行harness给出的指导。作者发现harness收益并不均匀：对强模型有效的技能对弱模型收益很小甚至有害。

Method: 在7个模型、3个基准上做任务-技能配对实验，识别出103对"强模型增益、弱模型受损"的技能；分析失败运行发现86%的失败其实违背了技能中已写明的要求，从而提出技能适配。具体做法：诊断工具执行、上下文管理、指令遵循、验证四类失败，蒸馏为固定的meta-skill；先由外部强改写器Kimi-K3在无执行证据条件下改写技能，再将适配纳入五轮自进化循环，由各模型依据自身执行轨迹与验证器反馈修订技能。

Result: 103个任务-技能对中，技能使GPT-5.6-luna提升31.1分，却使Gemma-4-E4B下降8.8分；Kimi-K3在meta-skill指导下改写技能，使三个弱执行器较原始技能提升5.1–14.1分；将适配嵌入五轮自进化循环后，7个执行器（Gemma-4-E4B至Kimi-K2.5）最终得分比无meta-skill的同款循环高2.0–5.1分。

Conclusion: harness自进化需以执行者能力为约束：技能需被"适配"而非直接迁移；用失败诊断蒸馏的固定meta-skill可作为通用适配指导，既可用于外部改写器也可用于自身循环。这为设计更有效的harness自进化方法提供了实践性设计准则。

Abstract: Harness self-evolution offers a practical route for LLM agents to improve from execution experience without updating model parameters. Its effectiveness depends on whether models can successfully act on the guidance provided by their harnesses. However, we find that harness benefits are not uniform across model capabilities: skills that improve stronger models can provide little benefit or even degrade performance for weaker ones. Across seven models and three benchmarks, we identify 103 task-skill pairs where skills improve GPT-5.6-luna by 31.1 points but degrade Gemma-4-E4B by 8.8 points. In 86% of failed weak-model runs, the violated requirement is already stated in the skill. This motivates skill adaptation: reformulating existing skills to match the executor's capabilities and make their guidance easier to execute. To achieve this, we diagnose failures in tool execution, context management, instruction following, and verification, and distill these diagnoses into a fixed meta-skill that guides adaptation during harness self-evolution. Guided by the meta-skill, a capable external rewriter, Kimi-K3, adapts skills without execution evidence, improving the three weak executors by 5.1-14.1 points over the original skills. We then incorporate this adaptation into a five-round self-evolution loop, where each model revises its own skill from execution trajectories and verifier feedback. Across seven executors, from Gemma-4-E4B to Kimi-K2.5, the meta-skill consistently improves final scores by 2.0-5.1 points over the same loop without the meta-skill. Our findings provide practical design guidance for developing more effective harness self-evolution methods.

</details>


### [2] [Can Language Models Learn While They Think? Testing Gradient Adaptation Across Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33025)
*Surya Saka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统测量了「动态评估」（在推理时对所读文本做梯度更新）在混合线性注意力、扩散语言模型及长上下文基准上的行为，发现其增益主要来自风格与词汇适应而非检索，并提出以蒸馏"结论条件判断"为低秩适配器的方式实现行为级结论写入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态评估此前只在标准 softmax 注意力模型上有证据，其在混合线性注意力架构、扩散语言模型和标准长上下文基准上的表现尚属空白；同时，"权重写入事实"与"上下文检索"之间的关系、以及写入代价（对推理能力的影响）也不清楚，需要系统性测量以明确该方法的边界与机制。

Method: 在最后四分之一 block 的 MLP 输出投影上，每 128 token 做一次 SGD 更新，窗口 2k token，不进行训练，在 Qwen3-1.7B/4B、Gemma-4-E4B、混合 Gated-DeltaNet 模型（Qwen3.5-4B、Qwen3.8-27B）及 masked-diffusion 模型（Nemotron-Diffusion-8B、DiffusionGemma-26B）上测量。随后在 RULER、BABILong、LongBench-v2、SCOTUS、HotpotQA、MuSiQue、ContractNLI 与引文补全等基准（≥100 条目、两个模型规模）上对比权重更新与上下文截断的精度。最后以蒸馏模型自身"结论条件判断"到低秩适配器的方式写入结论，并让结论由信息增益提问的探究循环产生。

Result: softmax 注意力模型在下个 token 损失上降低 5–8%，优于同一冻结模型使用完整 32k 上下文的水平；混合 Gated-DeltaNet 在各学习率下增益均低于 1%（线性注意力层已自带适应能力）；扩散模型在降低学习率并裁剪更新后效应更大（Nemotron-Diffusion-8B 约 8%，DiffusionGemma-26B 17–20%）。但在八类长上下文基准上，基于全文的权重更新未能恢复任何因上下文截断而损失的准确率。以下个 token 损失写入事实只能记住提示格式（聊天提示下回忆率 30%，上下文内为 80%），并在各剂量下损失 36–38 个 GSM8K 点；改用低秩适配器蒸馏结论条件判断可达到上下文内上限（100 局中 0.83/0.88 对 0.84/0.88），且 GSM8K 不变。

Conclusion: 动态评估的收益本质是风格与词汇层面的适应，而非长上下文检索能力的补偿；直接以损失为目标写入事实会损害推理性能。将结论以"行为"方式（蒸馏为低秩适配器）写入，可在不损失通用能力的前提下达到上下文内的表现上限，且结论可由自动探究循环获取。

Abstract: Letting a language model take gradient steps on the text it is reading, known as dynamic evaluation, lowers its loss on the rest of the document. Its behaviour on hybrid linear-attention architectures, on diffusion language models, and on standard long-context benchmarks has not been measured. We measure it. With one SGD step per 128-token chunk on the MLP output projections of the last quarter of blocks, no training, and a 2k-token window, softmax-attention models (Qwen3-1.7B, Qwen3-4B, Gemma-4-E4B) lose 5-8% of their next-token loss over a document and outperform the same frozen model given the full 32k-token context. Hybrid Gated-DeltaNet models (Qwen3.5-4B, Qwen3.8-27B) gain under 1% at every learning rate: the linear-attention blocks already perform the adaptation. The mechanism transfers to masked-diffusion models with a larger effect, 8% on Nemotron-Diffusion-8B and 17-20% on DiffusionGemma-26B, once the rate is lowered and updates are clipped. Across RULER, BABILong, LongBench-v2, SCOTUS, HotpotQA, MuSiQue, ContractNLI and citation completion, at 100 or more items on two model sizes, weights updated on the full document recover none of the accuracy lost to context truncation: the adaptation is style and vocabulary, not retrieval. Writing facts with the next-token loss stores the prompt format rather than the fact (30% recall under chat prompting against 80% in context) and costs 36-38 GSM8K points at every dose. Writing a conclusion as behaviour instead, by distilling the model's own conclusion-conditioned judgments into a low-rank adapter, reaches the in-context ceiling (0.83 and 0.88 against 0.84 and 0.88 on 100 games) with GSM8K unchanged, and the conclusion can come from an inquiry loop that found it by asking information-gain questions.

</details>


### [3] [Lessons from Luck: Do LLM-Written Lessons Carry Chance into an Agent's Next Decision?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33029)
*Kazuki Nakayashiki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在256个合成世界、11个LLM端点的注册研究中，模型从含随机性结果的运行中写出的经验教训会把"运气"带入后续决策：更优工具偶然失败后，读者在11.6%的下一次运行中改选较差工具，而对照仅0.4%；明确说明失败独立可使效应从+0.181降至+0.042。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自改进智能体依据运行结果撰写"经验教训"，当结果含噪声时，教训可能把偶然性固化为后续决策依据。作者希望以可重复的注册研究精确测量这一链条中的单个环节：噪声结果→教训文本→下一次工具选择。

Method: 预注册研究，协议在任何确证性调用前冻结、存档并加时间戳。256个合成世界，每个为含两个可互换工具（记录有可靠性）的重复任务；脚本化前次运行选用较可靠（好决策）或较不可靠（坏决策）工具，并指定结果为成功或瞬时失败。模型按ReasoningBank的记忆写入指令写出教训；同一模型的新会话持有权威日志与上次结果，在单一记忆条目条件下（该教训／另一结果的教训／无关条目／无推理的指令）选择下次首个工具。11个LLM端点共90,084次回答。

Result: 较优工具偶然失败后，面板教训携带了偶然性（注册读数luck-carrying；交互项+0.112，95%区间[+0.097,+0.126]）：由该失败写出的教训使读者在11.6%的下次运行中选择较差工具，无关条目为0.4%，由成功写出的教训为0.4%。日志声明失败相互独立时效应为+0.042，未声明时为+0.181（描述性）。较差工具侥幸成功后，交互项被界定于0.10以下（+0.002），属边界而非"无害"证据。探索性读数中6个端点携带偶然性（最高+0.352），5个被界定于0.10以下。次要比较：写入指令中加入三句决策质量提示使偶然失败后的较差工具率降至7.5%；对两种结果给出统一指令并含"不改变"选项降至3.1%；但二者均未在每个相关端点满足注册的减害规则。

Conclusion: 模型撰写的教训确实可作为一个可测量的因果通道，将结果随机性传递到后续决策，幅度受日志是否声明失败独立性调节；仅靠指令层面的提示干预可降低但未能稳定消除该危害，减害规则在全部端点未达标。

Abstract: Self-improving agents write lessons from the outcomes of their runs; when outcomes are noisy, a lesson can carry chance into later decisions. We measure one link of this chain in a registered study. In 256 synthetic worlds, each a recurring task with two interchangeable tools of recorded reliability, a scripted previous run used the more reliable tool (a good decision) or the less reliable one (a bad decision), with an assigned outcome: success or a transient failure. A model wrote a lesson from the run with ReasoningBank's memory-writing instruction; a fresh session of the same model, holding the authoritative log and the previous result, chose the next run's first tool with one memory item: that lesson, the lesson from the other outcome, an irrelevant item, or an unreasoned directive. The protocol was frozen, deposited and timestamped before any confirmatory call; 11 LLM endpoints gave 90,084 answers. After a chance failure of the better tool, the panel's lessons carried the chance (registered reading luck-carrying; interaction +0.112, 95% interval [+0.097, +0.126]): readers named the worse tool in 11.6% of next runs with the lesson written from that failure, against 0.4% with an irrelevant item and 0.4% with the lesson written from a success. The effect was +0.042 where the log stated that failures are independent and +0.181 where it did not (descriptive). After a lucky success of the worse tool, the interaction was bounded below 0.10 (+0.002), a bound, not evidence of no harm. In exploratory readings, 6 endpoints carried the chance (up to +0.352); 5 were bounded below 0.10. In secondary comparisons, a three-sentence decision-quality note in the writing instruction lowered the worse-tool rate after a chance failure to 7.5%, and one instruction for both outcomes with a no-change option to 3.1%; neither met the registered harm-reduction rule at every such endpoint.

</details>


### [4] [Feature Scaling Beats Architecture Choice in Offline Respiratory Screening for Low-Resource Clinics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33030)
*Abubakar Chilala,Cleophas Kadima,Innocent Anyaele,Victor Olufemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在五类呼吸音数据集上（1,211段、201个患者组、3.86:1不平衡），经典SVM与深度CRNN精度相当；最大效应来自特征标准化（+27点macro recall），而非架构；录音采样率与类别强相关（Cramer's V=0.65）是主要混杂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床环境常缺少GPU与网络连接，深度模型的部署成本高昂；论文希望检验在呼吸音分类任务中，经典机器学习分类器能否作为低成本替代方案，并在严格控制评估协议下公平比较两类模型。

Method: 数据为五类呼吸音语料（哮喘、COPD、肺炎、支气管音、健康），1,211段片段，来自201个患者组，类别不平衡比3.86:1。采用预注册的患者分组交叉验证（每配置25次运行）。基线为基于30维汇总特征（MFCC、ZCR、RMS统计量）的SVM；对照为基于log-Mel谱图的1.13M参数卷积循环网络。同时考察特征标准化与数据增强（SMOTE、波形增强、SpecAugment）的影响，测量CPU推理延迟与运行时体积，并做片段级划分与患者级划分的对比及录音源混杂分析。

Result: SVM达到macro recall 0.78，最稀类召回0.71；CRNN为0.75–0.77，两者差异未被解析。特征标准化使SVM的macro recall由0.51升至0.78（27点），幅度大于任何模型族间差距；SMOTE、波形增强与SpecAugment均无可测增益。CPU笔记本上单片段延迟51–74 ms，模型间无差异，但经典模型无需449 MB的PyTorch运行时。片段级划分会使macro recall虚高7.5–9.8点。原生采样率与类别的Cramer's V=0.65；探索性分析显示以某类少数采样率录制的片段多被分到该类典型速率对应的类别。

Conclusion: 在资源受限的临床场景中，经恰当预处理的经典分类器可与深度模型比肩，且部署成本更低；评估协议（患者级划分、特征标准化）对结果的影响大于架构选择。录音源采样率与标签的强关联构成主要混杂，结论需在此局限下解读。

Abstract: Deep models are the usual choice for respiratory sound classification, but in clinics without GPUs or connectivity that choice carries a cost. We compare classical and deep classifiers on a five-class respiratory-sound corpus of 1,211 clips (asthma, COPD, pneumonia, Bronchial, healthy) from 201 patient groups, with a 3.86:1 class imbalance, using pre-registered patient-grouped cross-validation (25 runs per configuration). An SVM over 30 summary features (MFCC, ZCR and RMS statistics) reaches macro recall 0.78 and 0.71 recall on the rarest class. A 1.13M-parameter convolutional recurrent network over log-Mel spectrograms reaches 0.75 to 0.77, and no difference between model families is resolved. The largest single effect we measure is a preprocessing effect rather than an architectural one. Standardizing the SVM's features, a routine step for RBF kernels, moves its macro recall from 0.51 to 0.78, a 27-point swing wider than any gap between model families here. Augmentation did not help: SMOTE, waveform augmentation and SpecAugment have no measurable effect. Latency does not separate the models (51 to 74 ms per clip on a CPU-only laptop), but the classical models avoid a 449 MB PyTorch runtime. Clip-level splits, which let a patient's recordings fall on both sides, inflate macro recall by 7.5 to 9.8 points. Recording source is strongly tied to label (Cramer's V = 0.65 between native sampling rate and class), and in an exploratory analysis clips recorded at a class's minority rate are mostly assigned to classes typical of that rate; we state this confound as the paper's main limitation.

</details>


### [5] [Inference-Time Prior Adaptation for Imbalanced Data Streams under Concept Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33126)
*Tobias Callies,Eirini Ntoutsi,Arthur Zimek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PAI-KNN 是一种基于 k 近邻的数据流分类器，通过在推理阶段动态调整类别先验（prior adjustment）来应对概念漂移与类别不平衡，而非依赖实例删除、重采样或合成数据等主动记忆管理手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据流分类在概念漂移与类别不平衡下需持续适应并保持少数类性能。现有方法多依赖主动记忆管理（实例删除、记忆重构、重采样、合成数据生成），开销大且复杂；作者希望验证是否可仅通过推理阶段的决策规则调整实现有效适应。

Method: PAI-KNN 采用简单的滑动窗口缓冲区存储近期样本，不做记忆修改；在推理时利用由近期观测估计的类别先验，动态调整 kNN 决策规则（先验调整推断，Prior-Adjusted Inference）。

Result: 在覆盖多种不平衡与漂移场景的大量合成数据流上评估，PAI-KNN 与当前最优方法性能相当（competitive），在动态类别不平衡与概念漂移场景中表现尤为突出。

Conclusion: 结果凸显推理阶段自适应在挑战性非平稳环境中的潜力，并表明基于记忆的数据流分类器的适应不必仅依赖记忆修改，为更轻量、简洁的流式分类设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Data stream classification under concept drift and class imbalance requires models to adapt continuously while maintaining strong minority-class performance. Many existing approaches rely on active memory management, in- cluding instance removal, memory restructuring, resampling, and synthetic data generation. We present PAI-KNN, a k-nearest neighbor classifier that adapts via inference-time prior adjustment rather than memory modification. PAI-KNN main- tains a simple sliding-window buffer and dynamically adjusts its decision rule using class prior estimates derived from recent observations. Evaluated on a mul- titude of synthetic data streams covering various imbalance and drift scenarios, PAI-KNN achieves competitive performance with state-of-the-art methods, per- forming particularly well under scenarios involving dynamic class imbalance and concept drift. These findings highlight the potential of inference-time adaptation in challenging non-stationary environments and suggest that adaptation in memory- based stream classifiers need not rely exclusively on memory modification.

</details>


### [6] [Physics Informed Neural Networks for Solving the Drift-Diffusion Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33133)
*Luke Ragan,Matteo G. C. Alasio,Enrico Bellotti,Luca Dal Negro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作使用物理信息神经网络（PINN）求解半导体漂移-扩散方程，并在一维/二维突变PN结上验证其精度，进而求解由电场或电势反演掺杂分布的非平衡逆问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 漂移-扩散方程是半导体器件建模、设计与优化的基础，但其解析解几乎不存在，且方程强非线性、解跨越多个数量级。现有商业TCAD数值求解器存在仿真时间长、对初值敏感、依赖网格生成等问题，且不适合由器件期望功能特性反推设计参数的逆问题。

Method: 采用物理信息神经网络（PINN）框架求解漂移-扩散方程，并引入先进训练技术以突破良态的平衡态区域限制；研究对象为一维与二维突变PN结，其特征为Ohmic接触处载流子浓度的极端梯度以及掺杂分布的突变不连续；同时将PINN求解器用于逆问题，由期望的电场分布或静电势在非零偏压下直接反演掺杂分布。

Result: 所提出的PINN架构在所有偏置条件下均能给出所有可观测量（观测量）的精确结果，误差与传统仿真软件相当；并能从期望电场分布或静电势在非零外加偏压下准确反演出掺杂剖面。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一种强有力的基于PINN的半导体器件建模方法，具备在非平衡条件下求解逆问题的能力，可服务于微电子研究与器件发现。

Abstract: The drift-diffusion equations are the fundamental building block for the modeling, design, and optimization of modern semiconductor devices. Solutions of the drift-diffusion equations rarely give way to analytical forms, and exhibit strong nonlinearity, with the solutions ranging over several orders of magnitude. These attributes have led to research into numerical solvers, and while many commercial Technology Computer Aided Design (TCAD) packages now exist, they may require long simulation times, exhibit sensitivity to initial guesses, and rely on mesh generation. Further, these solvers are not well suited for inverse problems where the design parameters of a device are retrieved from its desired functional characteristics. To address all these challenges, this work demonstrates the usage of Physics Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) for the solution of the drift-diffusion equations with errors comparable to traditional simulation software using advanced training techniques to solve problems beyond the well-behaved equilibrium regime. For this study, we specifically choose an abrupt PN-junction in both one and two dimensions that features extreme gradients of the carrier concentrations at the Ohmic contacts and an abrupt discontinuity in the doping profile. Using the proposed PINN architecture, we are able to retrieve accurate results for all observables under all bias conditions. Additionally, we apply the PINN solver towards the inverse problem of retrieving doping profiles directly from desired electric field distributions or electrostatic potentials under non-zero applied biases. Our work establishes a powerful PINNs-based approach for semiconductor device modeling with the ability to solve inverse problems under non-equilibrium conditions for microelectronics research and discovery.

</details>


### [7] [Neural-DISCO: Source-Conditioned Counterfactual Editing of Neural Population Activity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33145)
*Nico Policzer,James Ho,Joseph Soo,Xin Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Neural-DISCO，一个解耦表示学习框架，将神经活动潜空间划分为与各行为/刺激标签对应的标签特化分量及捕捉标签外变异的残差潜变量，从而支持在保留原始活动其余内容的前提下对单一测得的标签进行反事实编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统神经科学需要超越"由标签预测活动"的模型，转而支持源条件反事实编辑：给定一条真实记录的神经响应，编辑某个测得的行为或刺激标签并预测神经活动将如何变化，同时保持其余观测标签以及标签无法解释的源特异变异性不变。

Method: Neural-DISCO 将潜空间解耦为若干标签特化分量（各关联一个观测到的行为或刺激标签）与一个残差潜变量（捕捉未由观测标签提供的变异）；并在该解耦框架上结合特征归因方法，识别对每个标签最重要的神经元，以在单神经元分辨率上探究编码方式。

Result: 在仿真与真实神经记录数据集上，该结构支持对测得标签的选择性编辑以生成反事实神经响应，同时保留原始活动的剩余内容；解耦还能提升对已知功能细胞角色的恢复效果（合成数据与真实数据均成立）。

Conclusion: Neural-DISCO 可作为神经活动的反事实建模框架，并为探究神经群体如何编码行为与感觉信息提供实用接口；代码已开源。

Abstract: Understanding how neural activity encodes behavioral and sensory variables is a key challenge in systems neuroscience. This requires models that go beyond predicting activity from labels and instead support source-conditioned counterfactual editing: given a recorded source response, we should be able to edit one measured behavioral or stimulus label and predict how neural activity would change while preserving other observed labels and source-specific variability not explained by the labels. Here, we introduce \textbf{Neural-DISCO}, a disentangled representation learning framework for counterfactual modeling of neural activity. Neural-DISCO partitions the latent space into label-specific components, each associated with an observed behavioral or stimulus label, together with a residual latent that captures variability not supplied through observed labels. Across simulated and real neural recording datasets, we show that this structure enables selective editing of measured labels to generate counterfactual neural responses while preserving the remaining content of the original activity. Further, to gain insight into how behavioral and stimulus labels are encoded at single-neuron resolution, we pair this disentangled framework with feature attribution methods that identify which neurons are most important for each label. We find that disentanglement enhances the recovery of known functional cell roles in both synthetic and real data. Together, these results support Neural-DISCO as a framework for counterfactual modeling of neural activity and provide a practical interface for probing how neural populations encode behavior and sensory information. Code is available at https://github.com/TangLab-UBC/Neural-DISCO-neurips.

</details>


### [8] [How Much Fresh Data Should CTR Recalibration Use? A Temporal Evaluation of Post-hoc Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33168)
*Rui Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在Avazu数据上以滚动时间起点与随机划分训练三种CTR模型，发现随机划分严重低估校准误差，且Platt/等渗重校准的效果高度依赖于拟合时间窗宽度：12小时切片比最新6小时更稳健，但在模拟竞价中收益极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CTR模型的离线评估仍默认使用随机划分，然而实际部署中模型需对未来流量打分且下游系统依赖校准后的概率。因此需要系统考察随机划分与时间划分在校准度量上的差异，并检验部署中常用的重校准（Platt、等渗回归）在不同时间窗下的收益与风险。

Method: 在Avazu（4040万次曝光）上训练LR、DeepFM、DCN-v2，采用两个滚动时间起点加一个随机划分，每种设置3个随机种子。对每个测试日，用前若干小时数据拟合Platt缩放与等渗回归进行重校准；比较6、12、24小时拟合切片（含将12小时下采样至1小时规模）对ECE的削减效果，并设置基于切片的ECE门控，最后在模拟竞价中评估利润变化。

Result: 在起点1最近测试日，随机划分报告的LR期望校准误差（ECE）仅为时间划分值的55-62%（各随机种子一致），深度模型该比值不稳定；没有模型的ECE随时间距离单调变化。6小时拟合切片在严重失准单元中最多削减61%的ECE，但在约三分之一的应用中反而使ECE上升（48个中14-17个）。固定评估集上，12小时切片把受损应用数从36个中的13-17个降至3-5个，24小时则回升。12小时切片下采样至1小时规模（5%曝光、拟合快20-25倍）仍可保留88%的收益，说明样本量本身不是原因。切片ECE门控在跨起点场景无收益，模拟竞价中重校准带来的利润变动仅为-0.21%至+0.26%。

Conclusion: 离线评估应跨多个时间起点与随机种子、以时间维度衡量校准；在本数据中，较宽时间跨度拟合的重校准优于最新数小时拟合。该结论需在其他日志上进一步验证，且重校准对下游竞价的实际经济影响很小。

Abstract: Offline evaluation of click-through rate (CTR) models still defaults to random splits, although deployed models score future traffic and downstream systems consume calibrated probabilities. On Avazu (40.4M impressions) we train logistic regression (LR), DeepFM, and DCN-v2 under two rolling temporal origins and a random split, three seeds per arm, and recalibrate each test day with Platt scaling and isotonic regression fitted on the preceding hours. At origin 1's nearest test day, random splits report LR's expected calibration error (ECE) at 55-62% of the temporal value in every seed (the deep models' ratio is seed-unstable), and no model's ECE is monotone in temporal distance. With a 6-hour fitting slice, recalibration removes up to 61% of ECE in badly miscalibrated cells but increases it in about a third of applications (14-17 of 48 across seeds). On fixed evaluation sets, a 12-hour slice cuts harm from 13-17 to 3-5 of 36 applications, and 24 hours raises it again. The 12-hour slice subsampled to the 1-hour slice's size (5% of the impressions; 20-25x faster to fit) recovers 88% of the full slice's gain, so impression count alone does not explain it. A slice-ECE gate shows no benefit out-of-origin, and in a simulated auction recalibration moves profit by only about -0.21% to +0.26%. Offline protocols should measure calibration temporally, over multiple origins and seeds; in this data, recalibration fitted on a wide time span beat the freshest few hours, which other logs must confirm.

</details>


### [9] [Lower expected Fisher rank implies better PAC generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33208)
*Marco Miani,Albert Kjøller Jacobsen,Albert Lopez i Serrano,Søren Hauberg,Georgios Arvanitidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种与Fisher信息度量对齐的参数分布，通过在函数不变子空间内探索以保证低损失，并利用Fisher矩阵零特征值数量获得大的对数体积，从而同时提升PAC-Bayes泛化界的两项要素，得到随Fisher矩阵期望秩缩放的高概率泛化误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络泛化良好但缺乏解释。i.i.d.假设下PAC-Bayes界紧致性依赖于能覆盖大量低损失解的参数分布，而如何在几何上构造这样的分布尚不明确，Fisher信息度量能刻画函数变化与函数不变性。

Method: 定义并研究一种Fisher对齐的参数分布：在Fisher度量诱导的几何下，将参数探索约束在函数不变（零特征值）方向上，从而在保持低损失的同时使参数对数体积与Fisher矩阵零特征值数目成正比，再代入PAC-Bayes框架推导泛化误差界。

Result: 该分布可同时保证(1)低损失（探索受限在函数不变方向）与(2)与零特征值数量成正比的大对数体积，进而给出高概率泛化误差界，其界随Fisher信息矩阵的期望秩缩放。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了Fisher信息矩阵期望秩对泛化的重要性，说明函数不变方向上的高体积与低损失可以并存，为理解深度网络泛化提供了几何视角。

Abstract: Deep learning architectures tend to generalize well to unseen data, but explaining this capability is still an open area of research. Under the assumption of i.i.d. data, PAC-Bayes provides a bound on generalization error, and its tightness relies on a parameter distribution that covers a large volume of low-loss solutions. The Fisher information metric endows the parameter space with a geometry for which distances reflect functional changes, and whose zero-component captures functional invariances. We define and study a Fisher-aligned parameter distribution for which we can simultaneously guarantee (1) a low-loss, thanks to the exploration constrained on the functional invariances and (2) a large log-volume of parameters, proportional to the number of zero eigenvalues. This distribution results in a high-probability generalization error bound, which scales with the expected rank of the Fisher information matrix, shedding light on the relevance of this quantity.

</details>


### [10] [Model-agnostic predictability quantification of multivariable time series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33209)
*Yuxuan Shu,Vasileios Lampos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MAPS，一个模型无关的多变量时间序列任务内在可预测性度量，利用协变量信息在不同回看窗口与预测步长下量化可用预测信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测精度同时反映模型能力与任务可预测性，难以独立刻画任务难度；已有可预测性度量无法捕捉非线性依赖，或仅限单变量序列。

Method: MAPS基于协变量信息评估可预测性，覆盖不同lookback窗口与forecasting horizon，给出可用预测信息的定量评估；在合成与真实数据集上与6种基线可预测性度量对比。

Result: 合成数据上MAPS与期望参考可预测性高度吻合（r=0.963）；实证结果显示在多数实验配置下MAPS给出更可靠的预测性估计。

Conclusion: MAPS可作为模型无关的任务可预测性度量，弥补非线性与多变量场景下现有度量的不足，为基准数据集的难度刻画提供更可靠依据。

Abstract: Multivariable time series forecasting plays an important role in various domains, where predictions depend on complex interactions among covariates. Forecasting models are commonly evaluated across numerous benchmark datasets. However, forecasting accuracy reflects both model capability and task predictability, making it difficult to characterise the difficulty of a task independently of the model used. Existing predictability measures often fail to capture nonlinear dependencies or are limited to univariate series. In this work, we propose Multivariable Predictability Score (MAPS), a model-agnostic metric for measuring the intrinsic predictability of multivariable time series forecasting tasks. MAPS uses covariate information to assess predictability across different lookback windows and forecasting horizons, and provides a quantitative assessment of the predictive information available for forecasting. We evaluate MAPS across synthetic and real-world forecasting datasets against 6 baseline predictability measures. The former confirms that our method closely aligns with the expected reference predictability (r=0.963). Empirical results further corroborate that MAPS yields more reliable projections in the majority of experimental configurations.

</details>


### [11] [Vision Token Pruning via Query-Vision Interaction Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33215)
*Harshithanjani Athi,Sravan Kumar Ankireddy,Jianzhong Charlie Zhang,Hyeji Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出免训练、查询感知的视觉token剪枝方法QViD，利用查询-视觉交互矩阵的低秩结构实现高效剪枝。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型因视觉token数量庞大而推理成本高，视觉token剪枝是自然的加速手段。已有工作用查询信息指导token选择，但多依赖注意力模式或token动态等启发式规则，未显式利用查询-视觉交互的内在结构。

Method: 观察到由注意力中已有的query/key投影自然导出的查询-视觉交互矩阵本质上是低秩的，少数主导潜在交互模式即可刻画查询相关语义；据此提出QViD（Query-Vision Decomposition），一种免训练的查询感知视觉token剪枝方法，基于该低秩分解进行token选择。

Result: 在图像与视频理解基准上，相同token预算下QViD均稳定提升性能，且在激进压缩条件下增益最大。

Conclusion: 显式建模查询-视觉交互的低秩结构是有效的查询感知token剪枝思路，无需训练即可在严格压缩预算下取得更优的精度-效率权衡。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (VLMs), which process both visual inputs and text queries, incur high inference costs due to the large number of visual tokens, making visual token pruning a natural approach for improving efficiency. Recent work has leveraged query information to guide token selection, often through heuristics derived from attention patterns or token dynamics. However, these approaches do not explicitly exploit the underlying structure of query-vision interactions. We address this gap by observing that the query-vision interaction matrix, naturally induced by the query and key projections already present in transformer attention, is effectively low-rank, with a small number of dominant latent interaction modes capturing query-relevant semantics. Motivated by this observation, we propose Query-Vision Decomposition (QViD), a novel training-free query-aware visual token pruning method that exploits this structure. Extensive experiments on both image and video understanding benchmarks show that QViD consistently improves performance under matched token budgets, with the largest gains appearing under aggressive compression regimes.

</details>


### [12] [Neyman Orthogonal Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33217)
*David Arbour,Erfan Jahanparast,Serina Chang,Avi Feller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将激活引导中的"估计目标（steering direction）"与"估计量"分离，把现有方法统一为结果回归平均梯度的plug-in估计，并借助去偏机器学习与Stein score构建正交化、双稳健的梯度直接估计量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活引导常用对比式（有/无目标概念的prompt激活差）来估计引导方向，但文献普遍混淆了"想要的估计目标"与"实际使用的估计量"，且二值对比标签限制了对连续结果（如对数概率、oracle评分）的利用，拟合良好的ML模型也未必能准确估计梯度。

Method: 将几乎所有现有方法统一表述为"以二值对比标签为结果"的结果回归平均梯度的plug-in估计量；据此可引入灵活建模并扩展到连续结果。进一步借鉴去偏机器学习，基于Stein score（梯度泛函的Riesz表示子）构造直接针对梯度的估计量，并将两条路线组合为正交化估计量。

Result: 所提出的正交化估计量具有经典的双稳健性质，并在多个基准数据集上的激活引导任务中验证了该框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 分离估计目标与估计量为激活引导提供了统一的统计视角，使现有方法可自然扩展至连续结果，并通过Stein score正交化获得更稳健的方向估计。

Abstract: Activation steering is a popular approach for shifting LLM output toward a desired behavior, and increasingly for generating realistic synthetic data. The standard recipe estimates a steering direction by contrasting LLM activations on prompts with and without a target concept, such as truthfulness. In this paper, we separate the estimand (the steering direction these methods target) from the estimator used to recover it, two notions the literature often conflates. We frame nearly all existing methods as plug-in estimators of the average gradient of an outcome regression, with the binary contrastive label fixed as the outcome. Using this framing, we can immediately incorporate flexible modeling approaches and extend existing methods to allow for continuous outcomes such as log-probabilities or oracle ratings. Flexible ML models that fit the outcome well, however, might not estimate the gradient well, which motivates estimators that target the gradient directly. Drawing on the debiased machine learning literature, we develop a complementary estimator built on the Stein score, the Riesz representer of the gradient functional. Combining the two routes gives an orthogonalized estimator with classical double-robustness properties. We illustrate the framework on activation steering applied to a number of benchmark datasets.

</details>


### [13] [Reinforcement Learning for Controller Parameters in the Auto-Bidding System at Pinterest](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33248)
*Yichu Zhou,Qinyi Chen,Fei Feng,Jacob Loh,Mehdi Ben Ayed,Lin Yang,Andrei Curelea,Jiajing Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RLBP 是部署于 Pinterest 的混合出价架构，用强化学习作为元控制器每日为各广告计划选择 PID 参数，同时保留 PID 做高频出价调整，在线 A/B 测试实现 eCPC 显著下降且收入不降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线广告自动出价需在非平稳市场下平衡预算投放与成本（CPA/eCPC）两个竞争目标，且该权衡随计划生命周期变化：早期预算可结转时侧重成本优化，临近截止时投放紧迫性上升。生产广泛使用的 PID 控制器虽稳定可解释，但固定参数无法适应多样化计划与流量波动。

Method: 提出 RLBP：RL 作为元控制器，按天为单个计划选择 PID 参数，PID 负责高频出价微调。训练多头 Q 网络，采用相对于生产基线的归一化"相对提升"奖励形式；推理阶段进行融合，在不重训的情况下依据计划状态自适应调整成本-投放权衡。

Result: Pinterest 在线 A/B 测试显示 eCPC 有统计显著下降且收入无损；上线后两个月对照 holdout 的测量同样显示成本持续下降。

Conclusion: 元控制器式强化学习可在生产规模上有效增强既有控制系统：保留 PID 的稳定性与可解释性，同时获得 RL 对异构计划与非平稳环境的自适应能力，是一条低风险、可落地的工业路径。

Abstract: Auto-bidding systems in online advertising must balance competing objectives-maximizing budget delivery while minimizing cost per action-across heterogeneous campaigns under non-stationary market conditions. This trade-off evolves over campaign lifespans: early on, cost optimization dominates when budgets roll over; near deadlines, budget delivery becomes urgent. While PID controllers are widely used in production for their stability and interpretability, fixed tunings cannot adapt to diverse campaigns and shifting traffic patterns. We present RLBP (Reinforcement Learning for Bidding Parameters), a hybrid architecture deployed at Pinterest that uses RL as a meta-controller to select campaign-specific PID parameters daily while retaining PID for high-frequency bid adjustments. RLBP trains a multi-head Q-network using a relative-lift reward formulation normalized against the production baseline, and performs inference-time fusion that adapts the cost-delivery trade-off to campaign states without retraining. Through online A/B testing at Pinterest, RLBP achieves a statistically significant reduction in effective cost-per-click (eCPC) with no revenue degradation, and two months of post-rollout measurements against a holdout also show consistent cost reductions. These results demonstrate that meta-controller RL can successfully augment production control systems at scale.

</details>


### [14] [RadonBench: A Benchmark of Classical and Learning-Guided Search Techniques for Problems in Combinatorial Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33259)
*Xiaoan Xu,Ethan X. Fang,Jesus A. De Loera,Junwei Lu,Luze Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 RadonBench 基准，用统一的成功判据（目标规模凸配置的整点 Radon 点数是否为零）比较穷举扩展、贪心局部搜索、PatternBoost、AlphaZero 与 AlphaEvolve 在构造最大基数无 Radon 格点配置上的表现；所有审计运行均未达到零，仅给出可复现的"近失"基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典优化与学习引导搜索在离散数学开放问题上各有优势，但在搜索空间、有效性检验与停止规则不一致时，输出难以横向比较；因此需要一个统一口径的基准来评估各类方法在"无 Radon 配置"这一开放问题上的真实进展。

Method: 构建 RadonBench：任务为构造 Z^d 中不含整数 Radon 划分的最大基数有限子集；聚焦两个开放目标（Z^3 中 11 点、Z^4 中 21 点，若存在可改进整数 Radon 数下界）。统一成功谓词：目标规模凸配置成功当且仅当其整数 Radon 点数为 0。在此框架下对比穷举扩展（固定种子与声明有限搜索盒）、贪心局部搜索、PatternBoost、AlphaZero 与 AlphaEvolve。

Result: PatternBoost 与 AlphaZero 构造出经核验的无 Radon 配置，规模分别为 10 与 20，各差目标一点；AlphaZero 在两维度上最佳凸目标终点均含 2 个整数 Radon 点；固定种子与有限搜索盒下的穷举扩展同样最小为 2 个整数 Radon 点；贪心局部搜索与 AlphaEvolve 产出目标规模、仅含 1 个整数 Radon 点的近失解；无任何审计运行达到 0。

Conclusion: 在明确界定的搜索域上建立了可复现的近失基线，表明现有方法距两个开放目标均仅一步之遥但尚未突破；RadonBench 提供了统一、可审计的比较口径，为后续改进下界的工作设定参照。

Abstract: Classical optimization and learning-guided search offer complementary approaches to open problems in discrete mathematics, but their outputs are difficult to compare when search spaces, validity checks, and stopping rules differ. We present \emph{RadonBench}, a benchmark for constructing maximum-cardinality Radon-free lattice configurations: finite subsets of $\mathbb{Z}^d$ admitting no integral Radon partition. It remains open whether Radon-free configurations with $11$ points in $\mathbb{Z}^3$ or $21$ points in $\mathbb{Z}^4$ are possible. If they exist, they would improve the corresponding lower bounds on the integer Radon numbers. We compare exhaustive extension, greedy local search, PatternBoost, AlphaZero, and AlphaEvolve. All outputs use one success predicate: a convex target-sized configuration succeeds exactly when its number of integral Radon points is zero. PatternBoost and AlphaZero construct verified Radon-free configurations of sizes $10$ and $20$, one point short of the respective targets; AlphaZero's best convex target terminals have two integral Radon points in both dimensions. Exhaustive extension over fixed seeds and declared finite search boxes likewise reaches a minimum of two integral Radon points. Greedy local search and AlphaEvolve each produce target-sized near misses with one integral Radon point. No audited run reaches zero. These results establish reproducible near-miss baselines over explicitly scoped search domains.

</details>


### [15] [Individual Fairness via Local Predictive Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33285)
*Zhouting Zhao,Tin Lok James Ng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"局部预测一致性"作为个体公平的节点级松弛：将每个样本的预测与其局部邻域内相似度加权平均预测比较，得到可解释的节点级公平分数，并据此构造局部残差正则化学习框架；同时显式化邻域图构建与阈值选择，适用于二分类、回归和生存时间预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个体公平定义多为成对（pairwise）形式，要求全局或局部邻域内满足Lipschitz约束。此类成对约束在实践中过于严格，且不能直接指出哪些个体获得了局部不一致的预测，缺乏可解释性与可操作性。

Method: 以节点级松弛替代成对约束：对每个样本，将其预测值与邻域内相似度加权平均预测进行对比，形成局部残差与节点级公平分数；在该残差上引入正则项进行学习。由于方法依赖相似度结构，作者显式构造邻域图，并利用图覆盖率诊断（graph-coverage diagnostics）讨论阈值选择；同时与成对约束方法作对比分析。

Result: 方法可统一应用于二分类、回归与时间到事件（time-to-event）预测；能够定位局部预测不一致的具体个体并给出可解释的公平分数；实验/分析显示其与成对约束方法在公平—性能权衡（fairness–performance trade-off）上存在差异，阈值选择对结果影响显著。

Conclusion: 局部预测一致性提供了比成对Lipschitz约束更宽松、更可解释的个体公平刻画，可作为节点级诊断与正则化工具；邻域图结构与阈值是效果的关键，需借助覆盖率诊断显式选择；该方法在多类任务上通用，并为公平—性能权衡提供分析视角。

Abstract: Individual fairness in machine learning requires that similar individuals receive similar predictions. Most existing definitions are pairwise in nature, imposing a Lipschitz constraint that must hold either globally across all pairs of data instances or locally within specified neighborhoods. In practice, however, such pairwise constraints may be overly restrictive and may not directly identify which individuals receive locally inconsistent predictions. We study local predictive consistency as a node-level relaxation of pairwise individual fairness, where each prediction is compared with a similarity-weighted average of predictions in its local neighborhood. This yields an interpretable node-level fairness score and a corresponding regularized learning approach based on local residuals. Since graph-based individual fairness depends critically on the underlying similarity structure, we make the construction of the neighborhood graph explicit and discuss threshold selection through graph-coverage diagnostics. Our approach applies to binary classification, regression, and time-to-event prediction. We further compare this formulation with pairwise constraint-based approaches and analyze the resulting fairness--performance trade-offs.

</details>


### [16] [All-in-One: A Composable Attack on LLM Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33298)
*Xiaohua Zhan,Kazuki Egashira,Robin Staab,Mark Vero,Martin Vechev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个可组合的投毒攻击框架，通过在训练目标中联合优化原始模型与多种"模拟变换后模型"（将变换近似为权重扰动），使模型在量化、剪枝、模型合并等任一部署时变换被应用后即触发恶意行为，在多种场景与模型族上取得高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对LLM变换（量化、剪枝、模型合并）的安全威胁研究表明，攻击者可训练出"变换前安全、变换后恶意"的模型；但已有攻击通常只针对单一变换且依赖变换特定技巧，难以组合，从而限制其实用性与威胁面。作者希望构建一个统一、可同时针对多种变换的攻击。

Method: 核心洞察：每种变换的效果可近似为对模型权重的扰动。据此设计联合训练目标：在原始模型上施加良性损失，在每个"模拟变换"后的模型上施加恶意损失；由于这些扰动模拟足够简单，可在每次训练迭代中对多种变换同时进行，从而把多变换攻击统一到单一训练目标中。

Result: 在多种攻击场景、多个模型族以及广泛的部署时变换（量化、剪枝、模型合并等）上进行大量实验，攻击成功率很高；表明模型变换可使模型行为相较原始模型发生显著变化。

Conclusion: 单一攻击即可联合针对多种部署时变换，且成功率很高，强化了"变换可作为后门触发器"的安全担忧，提示部署前安全检查不足以覆盖变换后的行为，需要针对变换链路的安全评估与防御。

Abstract: Quantization, pruning, and model merging are popular transformations applied to deployment-ready LLMs to improve efficiency and downstream performance. Recent work has shown that these transformations can introduce a security threat: an adversary can train a model that passes safety checks in its pre-transformed state, only turning malicious once it is transformed for deployment. However, prior attacks target one specific transformation (e.g., quantization) and generally rely on transformation-specific techniques, making them difficult to combine in practice. In this work, we introduce a simple, composable attack framework that targets multiple transformations simultaneously and triggers when any one of them is applied by a user. Our key insight is that the effect of each transformation can be approximated as a perturbation to the model weights, allowing us to jointly train the model with a benign loss on the original model and a malicious loss on each simulated transformed model. These perturbations are simple enough to simulate at every training iteration across diverse transformations, making them practically combinable within a single training objective, enabling a unified attack. Through extensive experiments across attack scenarios, model families, and a wide range of deployment-time model transformations, we show that our attack achieves high success rates. This demonstrates that model transformations may be utilized to trigger significantly different behavior from the original model and reinforces the associated security concerns by demonstrating that a single attack can jointly target diverse transformations.

</details>


### [17] [Tree Depth and Sample-Quality-Associated Features: Two Hidden Factors in Boruta Selection for RNA-Seq Data Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33307)
*Tareque Mohmud Chowdhury,Abu Raihan Mostofa Kamal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究用大规模泛癌与乳腺癌数据证明，Boruta常用的树深3–7限制会显著改变基因选择结果并降低面板精度，建议取消深度上限或依实际树深设限，并加入类内质量过滤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Boruta是RNA测序基因面板筛选的常用方法，但其最常用实现推荐树深3–7，而该默认设置从未被检验；作者要确认这一未经验证的限制是否会改变被选中的基因及其下游性能。

Method: 使用TCGA泛癌数据（10,469例肿瘤、33种癌型）与乳腺癌PAM50分型数据（981例、5个亚型）；采用无泄漏流程，将所有依赖标签的步骤置于训练折内，以种子间变异为基准衡量效应，并为每个任务保留锁定测试集；以top-500重叠度、确认基因数、50基因面板平衡准确率及置换重要性进行对比，另设类内样本质量过滤实验。

Result: 泛癌中浅树带来的选择变化远超换种子：深度5时与不限深度的top-500重叠仅0.37，而深度5或不限深度下种子间重叠为0.84–0.93；浅树确认的基因少3.5倍，漏掉大类标志物，50基因面板平衡准确率0.809 vs 0.889。当深度上限达到不受限树实际深度的约60%–75%（主要由类别数而非样本量决定）时效应消失，且不限深度在任何任务中都不损失精度；结果在第二次交叉验证重复与置换重要性下可复现。约三分之一预筛候选基因在类内与样本质量相关，类内过滤可去除之且不损失测试精度（1,000基因时平衡准确率0.943 vs 0.942）。

Conclusion: Boruta默认的浅树深度限制是有害的未检验设定，应改用不限深度的树，或依据实际生成的树深度来设定上限；并建议在Boruta之前施加类内样本质量过滤，以剔除与质量混杂的候选基因。

Abstract: Boruta is a widely used method for selecting gene panels from RNA sequencing data, and its most used implementation recommends Random Forest trees of depth 3 to 7, an untested setting. We tested whether this setting changes which genes are selected, using pan-cancer data from The Cancer Genome Atlas (10,469 tumors, 33 cancer types) and breast cancer PAM50 subtyping (981 tumors, 5 subtypes). A leakage-free protocol fitted every label-dependent step inside training folds, measured every effect against seed-to-seed variation, and held out a locked test set per task. In pan-cancer data, shallow trees changed the selection far more than a new seed did: the top-500 overlap with unlimited-depth selections was 0.37 at depth 5, against 0.84 to 0.93 between seeds at depth 5 or unlimited depth. Shallow trees confirmed 3.5 times fewer genes, missed markers of large classes and produced less accurate 50-gene panels (balanced accuracy 0.809 vs 0.889). The effect vanished once the cap reached about 60 to 75% of the depth that unrestricted trees grow to, set mainly by the number of classes, not sample size, and leaving depth uncapped cost no accuracy in either task. The effect replicated in a second cross-validation repeat and under permutation importance. About a third of pre-filtered candidates also tracked sample quality within class; a within-class filter removed them without loss of test accuracy (balanced accuracy 0.943 vs 0.942 with 1,000 genes). We recommend uncapped trees, or a cap chosen from the realized tree depth, and a within-class quality filter before Boruta.

</details>


### [18] [When Does Pre-Trained Capacity Help Causal Learning? From Scalar Prediction to Counterfactual Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33311)
*Qi Xu,Zijie Lei,Zhifei Deng,Zhigang Hua,Luke Simon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文比较Qwen 2.5（0.5B与1.5B）上两种因果任务系统——CausalLLM（分治疗头估计标量潜在结果）与CausalGen（软前缀+事实SFT+反事实SFT+偏好对齐生成治疗条件化文本/代码）——发现容量增大对IHDP标量估计效果参差，但显著提升CodeGen的pass@1（0.267→0.575）与治疗效果分数（0.231→0.515），并据此提出"可用容量 vs 可转换容量"的区分假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是：预训练模型容量与因果任务适配后性能之间是何关系。现有工作缺少对"容量是否被有效转换"的讨论，作者希望区分模型拥有的容量（available）与经后训练可被激活的容量（convertible）。

Method: 构建两个基于Qwen 2.5（0.5B/1.5B）的系统：CausalLLM用治疗特异的标量输出头估计潜在结果；CausalGen通过软前缀条件化，结合事实SFT、反事实SFT与偏好对齐来生成治疗条件化文本或代码。比较不同骨干规模、输入表征（叙事 vs 表格）与无LLM基线，各设置3次重复。

Result: IHDP上标量估计随骨干增大结果混合、不一致；完整生成流水线后CodeGen pass@1由0.267升至0.575，治疗效果分数由0.231升至0.515；0.5B CausalLLM中叙事输入使平均sqrt(PEHE)由1.842降至1.780（相对表格输入提升3.4%），相对无LLM基线提升5.8%，但该提升混杂了表征与输入格式两方面变化。

Conclusion: 结果仅为描述性比较，无法分离后训练贡献（两系统在数据集、架构、输出模态与监督上均不同，且缺中间阶段评估）。样本仅两个小骨干、三次重复，故支持的是关于"容量—适配对齐"的假设，而非通用缩放律或已证明的因果机制。

Abstract: How does pre-trained model capacity relate to performance after adaptation for causal tasks? We study two systems built on the Qwen 2.5 family: CausalLLM, which estimates scalar potential outcomes with treatment-specific heads, and CausalGen, which generates treatment-conditioned text or code using soft prefixes and factual supervised fine-tuning (SFT), counterfactual SFT, and preference alignment. Increasing backbone size from 0.5B to 1.5B yields mixed scalar-estimation results on IHDP, but improves CodeGen pass@1 from 0.267 to 0.575 and treatment-effect score from 0.231 to 0.515 after the complete generative pipeline. Within 0.5B CausalLLM, narrative inputs reduce mean sqrt(PEHE) from 1.842 to 1.780, a 3.4% improvement over tabular inputs; the 5.8% improvement over a no-LLM baseline combines changes in representation and input format. These descriptive comparisons motivate a distinction between available and convertible capacity. They do not isolate the contribution of post-training: the two systems differ in dataset, architecture, output modality, and supervision, and intermediate-stage evaluations are unavailable. With only two small backbones and three replications, the results support a hypothesis about capacity--adaptation alignment rather than a general scaling law or a demonstrated causal mechanism.

</details>


### [19] [MLIPDiff: Guiding Denoising Diffusion Models for Structure-Based Drug Design via Machine-Learned Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33315)
*Yiyan Liao,Amin Tavakoli,Yu Zhang,Anima Anandkumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MLIPDiff 是一种推理时引导方法，将 UMA 机器学势（MLIP）的物理能量约束注入预训练扩散模型，在无需重训练的情况下生成无碰撞、几何合理且结合亲和度更优的配体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于结构的药物设计扩散模型主要拟合参考结构，常生成与蛋白发生原子碰撞的物理不合理配体构象；已有方法（如 NucleusDiff）虽可减少碰撞，却以牺牲局部几何为代价。

Method: 在预训练扩散模型的推理阶段加入物理引导：以 UMA（Universal Models for Atoms）机器学势为引导来源，组合蛋白-配体相互作用、配体应变、空间碰撞和口袋锚定四项加权能量项作为引导信号；直接使用原始模型检查点，不进行额外训练。

Result: 在 CrossDocked2020 上完全消除蛋白-配体碰撞，同时保持配体局部几何，改善键长分布与物理合理性；相较基模型与 NucleusDiff，保持 PoseBusters 通过率，并取得最佳中位 Vina Score、Vina Min 和 Vina Dock，每个口袋中中位 59.6% 的配体结合强于参考结构。

Conclusion: MLIPDiff 以单一冻结模型、无需额外训练即可生成兼具物理合理性与良好预测结合亲和度的结构，弥补了现有方法在碰撞消除与局部几何保持之间的权衡缺陷。

Abstract: Diffusion models have become a dominant approach to structure-based drug design. However, because they are trained primarily to fit reference structures, they often place ligands in physically implausible poses that clash with the protein. We introduce MLIPDiff, an inference-time guidance method that can be applied to pretrained diffusion models for ligand generation. The method uses physics-informed guidance from UMA (Universal Models for Atoms), a machine-learned interatomic potential (MLIP). The guidance combines four weighted energy terms (protein-ligand interaction, ligand strain, steric clash, and pocket anchoring) and uses the original model checkpoint without retraining. On CrossDocked2020, MLIPDiff completely eliminates protein-ligand collisions while preserving the ligand's local geometry, improving bond-length distributions and physical plausibility relative to the base model. Compared to models such as NucleusDiff that are specifically designed to reduce atomic clashes, MLIPDiff achieves clash-free structures without compromising local geometry. It maintains the base model's PoseBusters pass rate and attains the best median Vina Score, Vina Min, and Vina Dock, surpassing both the base model and NucleusDiff, with a median of 59.6\% of ligands per pocket binding more strongly than the reference. MLIPDiff thus addresses a limitation of prior methods by generating physically plausible structures with favorable predicted binding affinity using a single frozen model and no additional training.

</details>


### [20] [Augmentation-Guided Adversarial Training via Local Stability-Based Sample Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33331)
*Daniel Agyei Asante,Mark Tetteh,Deborah Asamoah,Bernard Asante,Regina Esi Turkson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 AGAT，一种基于增强诱导局部稳定性的选择性对抗训练框架，仅对 20-30% 样本施加对抗监督即可达到可比或更高的鲁棒精度，且训练时间最少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 PGD 对抗训练需对每个样本生成对抗样本，计算代价高；现有选择性对抗训练依赖逐点难度信号（loss、熵、margin、梯度），无法刻画样本邻域内的局部不稳定性。

Method: 以增强诱导的局部变化为依据分配对抗训练样本：定义 Augmentation-based Local Stability (ALS) 分数，度量输入与其保标签增强变体之间 logits 的归一化变化，据此对样本排序并选出邻域不稳定的样本施加对抗训练。

Result: 在 MNIST、CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、Tiny-ImageNet-200 上跨多种架构实验，AGAT 在仅对每个 minibatch 中 20-30% 样本施加对抗监督的情况下取得可比或更高的鲁棒精度；计算成本评估中训练时间少于所有其他对抗训练基线。

Conclusion: 样本邻域局部不稳定性是比逐点难度更有效的选择性对抗训练信号；ALS 作为轻量排序准则可显著降低对抗训练成本而不损鲁棒性。

Abstract: Adversarial training remains one of the most effective defenses against adversarial attacks, but its standard PGD-based training is computationally expensive because adversarial examples are generated for every training sample. Selective adversarial training reduces this cost by perturbing only a subset of samples. However, existing selective techniques typically rely on point-wise difficulty signals such as loss, entropy, margin, or gradients. We show that these point-wise criteria do not capture local instability in a sample's neighborhood. Motivated by this, we propose Augmentation-Guided Adversarial Training (AGAT), a selective adversarial training framework that uses augmentation-induced local variation to allocate samples with unstable neighborhoods for adversarial training. AGAT ranks samples using Augmentation-based Local Stability (ALS), a lightweight score that measures the normalized change in model logits between an input and its label-preserving augmented variant. Experiments across different architectures on MNIST, CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and Tiny-ImageNet-200 demonstrate that AGAT achieves comparable or higher robust accuracy while applying adversarial supervision to only 20-30% of the training samples in each minibatch. AGAT also requires less training time than all other adversarial training baselines in the computational cost evaluation.

</details>


### [21] [Gaussian Approximation for Composition and Subsampling of Differentially Private Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33333)
*Vitaly Feldman,Moshe Shenfeld*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了子采样机制组合（DP-SGD 隐私会计）的隐私损失近似服从高斯分布，在截断罕见的大隐私损失后，其隐私损失分布与同均值方差的高斯分布双向接近，偏差仅为随 ln(1/β) 增长的 ε 平移，从而给出紧致的类高斯差分隐私（GDP）界及基于 χ² 散度的方差型界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DP-SGD 的隐私分析依赖于子采样机制组合的界。现有闭式界要么损失一个对数因子，要么对噪声尺度施加严格条件并需精细的子采样分析；已有的基于中心极限定理（CLT）的技术在 δ 上给出较大误差。作者希望获得既紧致又条件宽松、以隐私损失近似高斯性为基础的组合分析框架。

Method: 将组合隐私界定为乘积分布对的隐私损失（独立项之和）。证明当每一步除小概率外隐私损失都很小时，截断后的隐私损失分布与同均值方差的高斯分布双向接近，误差为增长量级仅 ln(1/β) 的 ε 平移。并给出基于单步机制 χ² 散度的简单方法来分析所得方差型界；进一步推广到随机分配（balls-and-bins 采样）。

Result: 组合在低阶 ε 平移与加性误差 β 下满足高斯差分隐私。对采样率 1/t 且 t>ln³(1/δ) 的高斯机制，在 σ≳max{1,√ln(1/δ)/ln t} 时即可达到预测速率，而标准 Rényi 散度分析需要 σ≳√(t ln(1/δ))/ln t。结果可推广到随机分配，并解释了 connect-the-dots 方法的高精度。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了更紧致、条件更弱的 DP-SGD 组合隐私界：隐私损失近似高斯的理论刻画消除了对数因子损失与严格噪声条件，并为子采样机制与随机分配提供统一、易用的方差型分析工具，解释了 connect-the-dots 方法的实际高精度。

Abstract: The privacy analysis of differentially private stochastic gradient descent (DP-SGD) relies on bounds for the composition of subsampled mechanisms. The privacy of such a composition is bounded by that of a pair of product distributions, whose privacy loss is a sum of independent terms and has been analyzed via a variety of notions and concentration results. Yet, existing closed-form bounds either lose a logarithmic factor or require restrictive conditions on the noise scale and delicate analysis of subsampling. Further, while it is well known that sums of independent random variables are approximately Gaussian, existing CLT-based techniques give an approximation with a large error in $δ$.
  We show that the privacy loss of this pair is approximately Gaussian as long as each step has a small privacy loss except with small probability. Specifically, its privacy loss distribution is close to a Gaussian with the same mean and variance, after truncating the rare large privacy losses. The closeness in both directions is up to a shift of $\varepsilon$ that grows only as $\ln(1/β)$ for an additive error $β$ in $δ$ and is a lower-order term in typical applications. In particular, the composition satisfies Gaussian differential privacy up to a lower-order shift of $\varepsilon$ and the additive error $β$. We also give a simple way to analyze the resulting variance-based bounds for subsampled mechanisms which relies on the $χ^2$ divergence of a single step. For the Gaussian mechanism at sampling rate $1/t$ and $t>\ln^{3}(1/δ)$, this gives the predicted rate for $σ\gtrsim\max\{1,\sqrt{\ln(1/δ)}/\ln(t)\}$, whereas the standard Rényi-divergence analyses need $σ\gtrsim\sqrt{t\ln(1/δ)}/\ln(t)$. We extend these results to random allocation (balls-and-bins sampling), and show that it explains the high accuracy of the connect-the-dots method.

</details>


### [22] [Information-Theoretic Scaling Laws for Generative Diffusion Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33337)
*Druv Pai,Dejan Stancevic,Yi Ma,Luca Ambrogioni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文以信息论的"模型信息"（模型输出依赖具体训练集的程度的可计算度量）为视角，研究扩散模型在模型规模与训练集规模变化下的标度行为，发现两个定性不同的标度区间（记忆主导的非线性区间与泛化主导的线性区间），并提出可解释二者的信息论标度律，进而导出记忆与样本质量的诱导标度律，且外推表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理论上，在无限大数据上训练的扩散模型应收敛到同一分布；但实际中有限数据训练的模型会依赖具体训练集，表现为记忆（memorization）与样本质量等输出性质。作者希望在信息论框架下定量刻画这种"数据集依赖性"，理解其随模型规模与数据集规模的标度规律，并解释记忆与样本质量的标度行为。

Method: 引入"模型信息"（model information）作为可计算的信息论度量，量化模型样本对具体训练集的可依赖程度；在不同模型规模与训练集规模下进行实验，测量该度量的标度行为，识别不同标度区间；在此基础上提出一个信息论上可解释的标度律来刻画两个区间，并推导记忆与样本质量的诱导标度律，检验其外推能力。代码已公开。

Result: 发现两个定性不同的标度区间：(1) 非线性模型信息区间，标志从纯记忆向不完美泛化的过渡；(2) 泛化区间，模型信息呈线性标度，且泛化性与样本质量逐步提升。所提出的信息论标度律能刻画这两种区间，并成功导出记忆与样本质量的诱导标度律；这些诱导标度律在拟合域之外外推效果良好。

Conclusion: 模型信息为理解扩散模型的记忆与样本质量提供了信息论原理性解释，可统一描述从记忆到泛化的标度转变，并给出可外推的记忆与样本质量标度律，为数据规模与模型规模选择提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Diffusion models trained on increasingly large datasets sampled from the same data distribution will converge to the same learned distribution. However, in practice, a model trained on finite data depends on the particular training dataset; this dependence is reflected in properties of the model's outputs, such as memorization and sample quality. To understand this dependence through an information-theoretic lens, we study \textit{model information}, a computationally tractable measure of how much samples from the trained model depend on the particular training dataset. We explore its scaling behavior as model size and training dataset size vary. Specifically, we uncover two qualitatively distinct scaling regimes: (1) a nonlinear model information regime marking the transition from pure memorization to imperfect generalization, and (2) a generalization regime characterized by linear scaling of the model information and progressively improved generalization and sample quality. We then posit an information-theoretically principled scaling law to quantify these two regimes, and show that this law yields induced scaling laws for memorization and sample quality. We finally demonstrate that these induced scaling laws extrapolate well outside the fit's domain. Our results demonstrate information-theoretic principles for memorization and sample quality scaling laws in diffusion models. Our code is publicly available on \href{https://github.com/DruvPai/diffusion_info_theory_scaling_law}{GitHub}.

</details>


### [23] [The Clustering Occupation Kernel (CLOCK) Method for Time Series Arising from Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33354)
*Michael L. Wells,Kamel Lahouel,Victor Rielly,Bruno Jedynak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CLOCK：基于时间平均占据核将动力系统时间序列嵌入RKHS，用核范数（近似SRB不变测度的MMD）作为轨迹距离，支持不等长与不规则采样，配合Takens嵌入重构相空间，在合成数据与颅内EEG数据上优于基准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力系统产生的时间序列聚类缺乏一种既有理论依据、又能处理不等长轨迹与不规则观测的表示型距离度量；传统方法难以刻画吸引子的不变测度层面的相似性。

Method: 提出Clustering Occupation Kernel（CLOCK）：用时间平均占据核把观测序列嵌入再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS），以RKHS范数比较轨迹；对由微分方程支配的系统，理论上该嵌入收敛到不变SRB测度的核均值嵌入，故相异性度量近似吸引子间的MMD；对一般时间序列，引入时间滞后，借助Takens嵌入定理重构相空间；方法原生支持不等长轨迹和不规则采样。验证采用谱聚类。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实颅内大规模脑电（EEG）数据集上，谱聚类性能优于基准方法；Lorenz-96系统上的消融实验与占据核的理论收敛速率一致，并显示方法对零均值高斯噪声具有强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CLOCK是一种有理论保证的表示型时间序列聚类框架：其核范数距离近似SRB测度间的MMD，结合Takens嵌入可推广至一般序列，且对轨迹长度不等、采样不规则及噪声稳健，实证表现优于现有基准。

Abstract: We introduce the Clustering Occupation Kernel (CLOCK) method, a novel representation-based framework for clustering time series arising from dynamical systems. CLOCK embeds observed sequences into a reproducing kernel Hilbert space (RKHS) via a time-averaged occupation kernel, enabling trajectory comparison through their RKHS norm. For systems governed by differential equations, this formulation rigorously approximates the kernel mean embedding of the invariant Sinai-Ruelle-Bowen (SRB) measure, meaning our dissimilarity metric approximates the maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) between attractors. To accommodate general time series, we introduce time lags via Takens' Embedding Theorem to reconstruct the phase space. Crucially, CLOCK natively handles trajectories of unequal lengths and irregularly-spaced observations. We validate the proposed metric using spectral clustering, demonstrating superior performance against benchmark methods on both synthetic datasets and a real-world intracranial large-scale electroencephalogram (EEG) dataset. Finally, ablation studies on the Lorenz-96 system are consistent with the theoretical convergence rates of the occupation kernels and highlight the method's strong robustness to zero-mean Gaussian noise.

</details>


### [24] [LSTM-Style Looped Transformers with Shared KV Caches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33360)
*Andreas Kirsch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种LSTM风格的循环Transformer，在循环核心中共享单一KV缓存；用一致性损失对齐并行训练与递归续写两阶段的缓存条目，在减少参数与缓存的同时基本消除续写性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环Transformer通过复用权重以更少参数实现深层计算，但并行训练（标准注意力产生的缓存）与续写推理（共享注意力递归产生的缓存）之间存在缓存条目分布不匹配，导致续写损失惩罚；同时希望减少持久化KV缓存集合的存储开销。

Method: 构建LSTM风格循环Transformer，在循环核心中跨层共享一个KV缓存（宽度1024时持久缓存集合由15降至4）。并行训练阶段的缓存条目来自标准注意力前向，续写阶段则用共享注意力递归生成；在固定训练token预算的最后10%引入一致性损失，对齐两阶段缓存条目。

Result: 宽度1024下比未绑定权重的对照模型参数少40.4%，前向FLOPs接近匹配。FineWeb上三个模型宽度均消除81–89%的续写损失惩罚（相对全缓存评估）；两个训练种子在不相交文档上复现。四个宽度、1.05–4.19B训练token范围内，权重绑定的损失代价在较低token预算时最小。

Conclusion: 一致性损失可有效弥合并行训练与递归续写间的缓存不匹配，使共享KV缓存的循环Transformer在低参数、低缓存开销下保持性能；权重绑定代价随数据预算降低而减小，说明在数据与模型存储受限时循环计算具有应用价值。

Abstract: Recurrent transformers reuse weights to perform deep computation with fewer parameters. We study an LSTM-style transformer that also shares one key--value cache across its recurrent core. At width 1024, it uses 40.4% fewer parameters than an untied control at nearly matched forward FLOPs, and reduces persistent cache sets from fifteen to four. Parallel training supplies cache entries from a standard-attention pass, whereas continuation reads entries produced recursively using shared attention. We address this mismatch by aligning the two training passes' cache entries with a consistency loss during the final 10% of a fixed training-token budget. Across three model widths on FineWeb, this removes 81--89% of the continuation-loss penalty relative to full-cache evaluation. The improvement replicates on disjoint documents for two training seeds. Across four widths and 1.05--4.19B training tokens, the loss cost of weight tying is smallest at lower token budgets, suggesting an opportunity to use recurrent computation when data and model storage are limited.

</details>


### [25] [Multiple Importance Sampling for Guided Speculative Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33368)
*Joseph Rowan,Ashish J. Khisti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将引导式推测推理(GSI)扩展到多个小型LLM组成的草稿提案集成，用多重重要性采样(MIS)框架设计加权聚合方案，证明balance heuristic最优，并给出KL散度与期望奖励退化的新界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有GSI依赖单个调优良好的草稿模型，当该草稿模型的支撑集无法覆盖目标分布支撑集时可能失效；因此需要探索由多个多样化小型LLM共同提案的设定，以在同等采样预算下提升稳健性。

Method: 在测试时扩展框架下，令一组较小的LLM产生草稿推理步骤，由更大的基座模型与过程奖励模型进行验证；采用多重重要性采样(MIS)框架，为来自多个不同提案分布的样本引入加权聚合方案，并从理论上推导相对最优奖励倾斜目标分布的KL散度界与期望奖励退化界，据此分析各加权方案的优劣。

Result: 理论上给出了新的KL散度界和期望奖励退化界，并证明经典的balance heuristic在该设定下是最优的；实证上，与使用单个调优草稿的GSI相比，在总采样数相同的情况下，组合多个多样化LLM可保持有竞争力的pass@N下游性能，同时在单一草稿模型支撑集不覆盖目标分布的情形下（GSI可能失败）具有数学上的稳健性。

Conclusion: 多草稿集成的MIS加权聚合是GSI的一种有理论保障的推广：balance heuristic为最优加权，且在相同采样预算下不损失性能的同时增强了对草稿支撑集不足的鲁棒性。

Abstract: We study an extension of the Guided Speculative Inference (GSI) algorithm for test-time-scaling of large language models (LLMs) to a novel setting where an ensemble of smaller LLMs proposes draft reasoning steps which are later verified by a larger base model and a process reward model. Using the established framework of multiple importance sampling (MIS), we introduce weighting schemes to aggregate samples from a diverse collection of proposal distributions. We provide new bounds on the KL divergence from the optimal reward-tilted target distribution and the expected reward degradation, then demonstrate that the well-known balance heuristic is optimal. Compared to GSI with a single well-tuned drafter, combining several diverse LLMs while retaining the same total number of samples maintains competitive pass-at-$N$ downstream performance while being mathematically robust to situations where the support of a single draft model does not cover that of the target, where GSI may fail.

</details>


### [26] [One Call Ahead: Packed Self-Speculative Decoding with Diffusion Drafting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33379)
*Yuxiao Chen,Changxiao Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Packed-SS：一种"打包自推测解码"调度，在一次模型调用中同时完成验证与下一轮起草，且输入规模随推测宽度线性增长，并保持目标模型分布不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归（AR）模型逐token生成，长输出代价高；推测解码用轻量drafter并行提议、目标模型验证以加速。在同时具备AR与扩散模式的模型中，同一网络既起草又验证，但起草仍需等待验证，标准调度每轮需两次串行模型调用；已有的一次调用调度虽免去等待，却导致输入规模随推测宽度二次增长。效率瓶颈由此产生。

Method: 核心思想是让扩散drafter"提前一轮"运行：在验证当前草稿的同时，模型基于较旧的已验证前缀构造下一轮草稿，并保留其未解析后缀供下一次迭代使用；由此实现单次调用内验证+起草，输入规模对推测宽度线性。作者还在对drafter的温和单调性假设下，从理论上分析了所需模型调用次数。

Result: 理论：在上述单调性假设下，Packed-SS 在贪心设置中所需的模型调用次数不多于标准两次调用解码。实验：在 Nemotron 3B/8B 与 SetDLM 1.7B 上，贪心解码下吞吐量高于两个基线，平均实际耗时（mean wall time）更低。

Conclusion: Packed-SS 通过"起草领先一轮"的调度设计，在保持目标模型分布的前提下以线性输入代价消除起草对验证的等待，实现单次调用完成验证与起草，并在实验中获得优于标准两调用调度与既有单调用调度的吞吐与延迟表现。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) language models generate tokens sequentially, so generating long outputs is costly. Speculative decoding mitigates this by using a lightweight drafter to propose multiple tokens for the target AR model to verify in parallel, while a diffusion-based drafter can produce an entire draft block in one forward pass. In models with AR and diffusion modes, one network both drafts and verifies, but drafting still waits for verification, so the standard schedule needs two sequential model calls per iteration. Existing one-call schedules avoid the wait but incur input size quadratic in the speculative width. Motivated by this efficiency bottleneck, we introduce Packed-SS, a self-speculative decoding schedule that verifies and drafts in a single model call with input size linear in the speculative width, while preserving the target model's distribution. The key idea is to let the diffusion drafter run one call ahead: while verifying the current draft, the model simultaneously constructs the next draft from an older verified prefix and keeps its unresolved suffix for the next iteration. Under a mild monotonicity assumption on the drafter, we further show that Packed-SS requires no more model calls than the standard two-call decoding in the greedy setting. Experiments on Nemotron 3B/8B and SetDLM 1.7B show higher throughput and lower mean wall time than both baselines under greedy decoding.

</details>


### [27] [The Cost of an Unknown Horizon for the Last Iterate of Clipped SGD under Heavy-Tailed Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33381)
*Yuxing Peng,Zhiqing Tang,Weijia Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文确定了重尾噪声、光滑凸目标下clipped SGD末次迭代的最优时间一致（time-uniform）收敛速率β_p=2(p-1)/(3p-1)，并证明任何沿当前迭代随机梯度步进的规则（即便自适应步长与阈值）都无法突破该速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练常在有数据依赖的停时点终止，因此末次迭代的保证必须在所有时刻同时成立。已有工作给出的时间一致速率为Õ(t^{-2(p-1)/(3p)})，且原作者猜测存在更优调度可达已知时域下的最优速率O(T^{-α_p})，需澄清这一猜想是否成立。

Method: 在中心p阶矩有界（1<p≤2）的重尾噪声、光滑凸目标下分析clipped SGD；构造无需时域或置信度输入的调度，达到Õ(t^{-β_p})且几乎必然成立；并给出下界，证明沿当前迭代的随机梯度方向步进的任何规则（含自适应步长与阈值）无法超过Õ(t^{-r}), r>β_p。同时考察在滑动平均处查询梯度、以及几何遗忘的heavy-ball动量与多项式步长的表现。

Result: 最优时间一致指数β_p=2(p-1)/(3p-1)，介于已知时域最优指数α_p=(p-1)/p与先前时间一致指数2(p-1)/(3p)之间；有限方差情形对应1/2、2/5、1/3。在滑动平均处查询梯度可达Õ(t^{-α_p})；几何遗忘的heavy-ball动量配多项式步长无法优于β_p。

Conclusion: Chezhegov等人的猜想在有已知时域时成立（Liu; Jiang等），但在无时域情形下不成立：时间一致的最优速率严格慢于已知时域最优速率，且在所述算法类内β_p为不可突破的下界。

Abstract: Training is often stopped at a data-dependent time, so guarantees for the last iterate must hold at all times simultaneously. We determine the optimal time-uniform rate for the last iterate of clipped SGD on smooth convex objectives under heavy-tailed noise with a bounded central $p$th moment, $1<p\le2$. A schedule without horizon or confidence inputs attains $\tilde O(t^{-β_p})$ at all times, with $β_p=2(p-1)/(3p-1)$, also almost surely, and no rule that steps along the stochastic gradient at its current iterate attains $\tilde O(t^{-r})$ for any $r>β_p$, even with adaptive step sizes and thresholds. This rate lies between the optimal rate $O(T^{-α_p})$ for a known horizon $T$, $α_p=(p-1)/p$, and the previous time-uniform rate $\tilde O(t^{-2(p-1)/(3p)})$ (Chezhegov et al., ICLR 2026); under finite variance, the exponents are $1/2$, $2/5$, and $1/3$. Their conjecture that a better schedule reaches the optimal rate thus holds with a known horizon (Liu; Jiang et al.) but fails without one. Querying gradients at the running average attains $\tilde O(t^{-α_p})$ at all times, whereas heavy-ball momentum with geometric forgetting and polynomially bounded steps cannot improve on $β_p$.

</details>


### [28] [Faithful Federated Tabular Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33403)
*Zilong Cao,Hai Zhang,Xuan Bi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FITS框架，联邦场景下基于局部线性回归估计条件CDF的两步合成算法，并引入连续化方法无损处理混合型表格数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据是AI时代重要数据源，但联邦环境下生成混合型表格合成数据面临两大挑战：保证分布保真度，以及有效处理混合型（离散+连续）数据。

Method: 核心为两步联邦算法，用局部线性回归估计条件累积分布函数（CDF）；基于概率链式法则构建联邦合成流程以保证分布保真；引入连续化（continualization）方法将离散变量提升到连续空间，并在密度函数与CDF层面无损还原其离散分布，从而统一表示混合型变量。

Result: 理论分析刻画了连续化后混合型变量的条件密度与CDF，并证明联邦条件CDF估计量的一致收敛性；实验表明FITS生成的合成数据提升所有客户端下游任务表现，收益大小取决于合成数据的使用方式。

Conclusion: FITS为联邦混合型表格数据合成提供了兼具分布保真与统一处理离散/连续变量的可行方案，且对下游任务有普适增益。

Abstract: Synthetic data is an important data source in the AI era. This paper studies the generation of mixed-type tabular synthetic data in federated settings, where two key challenges arise: ensuring distributional fidelity and effectively handling mixed-type data. We propose Federated Integrative Two-Step Synthesis (FITS), a framework for mixed-type tabular data synthesis. At its core is a two-step federated algorithm for estimating conditional cumulative distribution functions (CDFs) using local linear regression. Building on this algorithm, we develop a federated synthesis procedure based on the probability chain rule to ensure distributional fidelity. To handle mixed-type data, FITS introduces a continualization method that lifts discrete variables into a continuous space and recovers their discrete distributions without loss at both the density-function and CDF levels. This transformation provides a unified continuous representation of mixed-type variables, facilitating the application of local linear regression and synthetic data generation. Our theoretical analysis characterizes the conditional density and cumulative distribution functions of the continualized mixed-type variables and establishes the uniform convergence of the resulting federated conditional CDF estimators. Experiments demonstrate that FITS-generated synthetic data improves downstream task performance for all clients, with the benefits depending on how the synthetic data is used.

</details>


### [29] [COPE: Composable Performance Emulation for Mixture-of-Experts Offloading Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33404)
*Hanming Zhang,Erhu Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: COPE 是一个可在运行时集成前为目标"模型-平台"配置选出最优 MoE 卸载策略的仿真式策略选择系统，通过 Policy Matrix 与 Virtual Heterogeneous Machine 两组件实现，最优策略选择准确率提升 24%–100%，性能预测误差降低 1.7×–6.8×。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE 稀疏激活使"专家权重驻留 CPU、按需传输/执行到 GPU"的卸载部署成为可能，但现有 MoE 卸载系统通常只针对特定模型-平台配置；模型稀疏度、CPU/GPU 吞吐、显存容量与主机-设备带宽的差异会改变策略优劣排序。逐一实现并穷举评估所有候选策略的工程成本过高，因此需要一种在集成前高效选出最优策略的方法。

Method: 提出 COPE（可组合卸载策略仿真器），含两个组件：(1) Policy Matrix——将设计空间分解为"专家驻留（expert residency）"与"MoE 执行策略"两个独立维度，实现候选策略间的机制复用，并由此派生出四种新候选策略；(2) Virtual Heterogeneous Machine——在目标平台上刻画组合算子调度（combined-operator schedules），并将测量结果与离线路由轨迹结合，估计各候选策略的性能。

Result: 与所评估基线相比，COPE 将"最快策略"的选择准确率提升 24%–100%，将性能预测误差降低 1.7×–6.8×；通过在集成前识别偏好策略，最多可减少 74% 的策略专属实现工作量。

Conclusion: COPE 表明：将 MoE 卸载设计空间结构化分解，并结合目标平台上的细粒度算子测量与离线路由轨迹进行性能仿真，可在无需完整实现所有候选策略的前提下，准确、低成本地为任意模型-平台配置挑选最优卸载策略，显著降低工程投入。

Abstract: As LLMs scale, mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures increase model capacity while keeping per-token computation manageable through sparse expert activation. This sparsity also enables memory-efficient deployment by retaining most expert weights in CPU memory and transferring or executing only the routed experts on demand. Existing MoE offloading systems typically target specific model-platform configurations, yet differences in model sparsity, CPU and GPU throughput, VRAM capacity, and host-device bandwidth can change the policy ranking. Because implementing and exhaustively evaluating all candidate policies incurs substantial engineering cost, efficiently selecting the best-performing policy for each model-platform configuration remains a key challenge for practical MoE offloading.
  We present COPE (Composable Offloading Policy Emulator), which selects the best-performing MoE offloading policy for a target model-platform configuration before runtime integration. COPE comprises two components. Its Policy Matrix factors the design space into two independent axes, expert residency and MoE execution strategy, enabling mechanism reuse across candidate policies and exposing four new candidates. Its Virtual Heterogeneous Machine profiles combined-operator schedules on the target platform and combines these measurements with offline routing traces to estimate candidate performance. Compared with the evaluated baselines, COPE improves fastest-policy selection accuracy by 24%-100% and reduces performance-prediction error by $1.7\times$ to $6.8\times$. By identifying the preferred policy before integration, COPE further reduces policy-specific implementation effort by up to 74%.

</details>


### [30] [Performance Gaps Between Open-Loop, Certainty-Equivalent, and Optimal Control Under Action-Dependent Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33412)
*Leo Maynard-Zhang,Sunmook Choi,Yahya Sattar,Yassir Jedra,Sarah Dean,Maryam Fazel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在线性二次、双线性观测系统中，引入信息强度参数 q，按 q 划分性能区间，证明在中等 q 区间内存在"偶尔探测"策略相对开环控制取得 Ω(T) 优势，而确定性等价控制仅取得 O(1) 优势，从而建立 CE 与最优控制之间 Ω(T) 的性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分观测下，若动作同时影响状态与观测（双重效应），确定性等价（CE）控制——把估计状态当作真实状态施加状态反馈——可能失效。作者希望定量刻画这种次优性何时会随决策时域 T 增长而放大为显著性能差距。

Method: 在带双线性观测的线性二次（LQ）系统中，比较三类策略：最优策略、确定性等价控制、最优开环（OL）策略。定义依赖于具体实例的信息强度参数 q，并基于刻画"信息获取即时代价与未来收益权衡"的 regret 分解进行分析；在中等 q 区间构造偶尔主动探测信息的策略。

Result: 随 q 变化呈现三个性能区间：(1) q 充分小时，任何策略相对 OL 控制的累计代价改进不超过 O(1)；(2) q 较大时，CE 相对 OL 取得 Ω(T) 优势；(3) q 中等时，所构造的探测型策略相对 OL 取得 Ω(T) 优势，而 CE 仅取得 O(1) 优势，故 CE 与最优控制间存在 Ω(T) 差距。

Conclusion: 双重效应下 CE 的次优性并非总能被界定为常数级，在中等信息强度区间内其与最优策略的差距可随时域线性增长，说明主动信息探测在大时域下不可或缺；regret 分解为量化信息获取的价值提供了统一框架。

Abstract: In dynamical systems, feedback can improve performance by allowing decisions to depend on past observations, rather than being designed a priori. Under full observation, the current state is sufficient for decision-making; under partial observation, the controller must instead estimate the state from measurements. When actions also affect observations, control can have a dual role: actions influence both the state and the information available for future decisions. This interaction can invalidate certainty-equivalent (CE) control, a widely used strategy that applies state feedback policies as if the estimated state were the true state. We ask when this suboptimality leads to a quantitative performance gap that grows with the horizon. We study this question in a linear-quadratic system with bilinear observations by comparing the optimal policy, certainty-equivalent control, and the optimal open-loop (OL) policy. We identify an instance-dependent information-strength parameter $q$ and establish distinct performance regimes as $q$ varies. When $q$ is sufficiently small, we show that no policy can improve over OL control by more than $O(1)$ cumulative cost. For large values of $q$, we find that CE achieves an $Ω(T)$ advantage over OL control. Our main finding is that for intermediate values of $q$, we can construct a policy that occasionally probes for information and achieves an $Ω(T)$ advantage over OL control while CE only achieves an $O(1)$ advantage, establishing an $Ω(T)$ gap between CE and optimal control. Our analysis relies on a regret decomposition that captures the tradeoff between the immediate cost of gathering information and its future benefit.

</details>


### [31] [An Empirical Study of Reinforcement Learning Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33420)
*Zeyu Zheng,Clare Lyle,Khimya Khetarpal,Will Dabney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文对actor-critic强化学习算法在Starpilot与Craftax两个程序生成环境中开展实证缩放律研究，发现可在大至两个数量级训练算力范围内准确预测性能、最优资源分配与最佳超参配置的缩放律；并提出以Elo分数作为更可预测的替代指标，刷新Craftax的SOTA，同时揭示Craftax中算力最优模型规模的非单调模式并与Ray interference现象相关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督/自监督学习的缩放律已使大模型训练可预测，而强化学习虽应用广泛，仍缺乏可预测的缩放指导原则。作者希望填补这一空白，探索RL是否存在类似的可预测缩放规律。

Method: 在Starpilot与Craftax两个程序化生成环境中，对actor-critic算法进行实证缩放律研究；预测目标为性能、最优资源分配策略和最佳超参数配置三项量；用单智能体RL范式的Elo分数变体替代常用的平均回合回报作为性能度量并比较两者的可预测性；将所得缩放律用于提升训练算力以冲击SOTA，并分析算力最优模型规模的趋势。

Result: 在两个环境中均得到预测性能强的经验缩放律，可准确预测大至两个数量级总训练算力下的上述三项量；Elo分数比平均回合回报显著更可预测，更适合拟合缩放律；应用缩放律增大算力后在Craftax上取得新的SOTA；在整体缩放趋势之下，观察到Craftax中算力最优模型规模的非单调缩放模式，并将其归因于Ray interference这一病态现象。

Conclusion: 强化学习中同样存在具有强预测能力的经验缩放律，可用于算力分配与超参选择；选择恰当的性能度量（如Elo分数）对缩放律拟合至关重要；全局缩放趋势下可能存在非单调的局部结构，需以Ray interference等机制加以解释。

Abstract: The discovery of scaling laws enables prediction of the performance, the optimal resource allocation strategy, and the best hyperparameter configuration of a large-scale training job based on inexpensive experiments at smaller scales. Guided by these empirical scaling laws, the deep learning community has achieved great success in training large (self-)supervised models on Internet-scale datasets. In contrast, despite its potential and wide application, reinforcement learning (RL) has yet to find a guiding principle for predictable scaling. In this work, we conduct an empirical study of RL scaling laws of an actor-critic algorithm in two procedurally generated environments, Starpilot and Craftax. We discover empirical scaling laws with strong predictive performance in both environments. The discovered scaling laws accurately predict the aforementioned three quantities for larger-scale training jobs up to two orders of magnitude in total training compute. In particular, we employ a variant of Elo scores for the single-agent RL paradigm as an alternative performance metric than the commonly used mean episode return. Our results show that the Elo score is a much more predictable metric than the mean episode return, making it a better choice for fitting scaling laws. By applying our discovered scaling laws to increase training compute, we establish a new state of the art in the challenging Craftax environment. Beneath the surface of the global scaling trend, we also observe a non-monotonic scaling pattern of the compute-optimal model size in Craftax. We connect this observation to a pathology known as Ray interference.

</details>


### [32] [Exact Unlearning After Data Curation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33426)
*Priyank Jayraj,Devanshu sahoo,Uday Mittal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究去重式训练管线中的"删除请求"问题：删除一条原始记录会改变相似度筛选结果，使先前被排除的记录重新进入训练集。作者提出"保留契约"：服务承诺支持的未来删除数恰好刻画了哪些被排除记录仍可能被需要，并据此构建可精确修复的训练统计摘要与有限精度岭回归特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练管线常在建模前按相似度去重（先出现者屏蔽后出现者）。用户提出删除请求后，原始记录消失会改变筛选链条，导致原本被排除的样本进入训练集，从而破坏已计算好的统计量和模型。因此需要回答：为在删除后精确复现训练结果，服务究竟必须保留哪些信息，尤其是那些当前被排除的数据。

Method: 固定"先到者屏蔽后到者"的相似度筛选规则；引入未来删除总数的上界，刻画仍可能进入训练的候选记录边界；基于该边界构造训练统计摘要，使重复删除后可精确修复（等价于在剩余数据与剩余预算上重建）；并针对带系数惩罚的线性岭回归构造有限精度特征，用精确有理数输出分析删除数增加一位时私有信息需求的变化，给出一族满足该条件私有信息最小值的编码。

Result: 未来删除预算恰好界定可再进入训练的排除记录集合；修复后的摘要与在留存数据加剩余预算上重建结果一致。岭回归精确输出下，多允许一次删除会使所需的私有信息从零跃升为全部被排除候选的私有特征符号；所构造编码在该族上于整段请求序列中达到此条件最小值。在1,001条AskUbuntu开发记录上，删除一条初始被排除记录，在51个固定相似度阈值中有13个会引入另一条记录；精确成员修复通过13,056次删除步检查；两项基于模型的语义校准未达既定标准，故下游任务研究未开展。

Conclusion: 核心结论是"保留契约"：服务承诺支持的未来删除数量决定哪些被排除数据仍可能被需要。删除预算既是修复正确性的边界，也是私有信息需求的开关；实践上应据预算决定保留范围，而非整库保留或直接丢弃排除样本。

Abstract: Training pipelines often remove similar examples before fitting a model. Deleting a raw record can change that selection and admit an example that was excluded before. What must a service retain to reproduce training after such requests? We answer this question for a fixed rule in which earlier similar records block later ones. A limit on the total number of future deletions identifies exactly which records can still enter training. We use this boundary to build a summary of training statistics that can be repaired exactly after repeated deletions. The repaired summary is the same as rebuilding it on the surviving data with the remaining budget. We also construct finite-precision features for ridge regression, a linear learner with a penalty on coefficient size. With exact rational outputs, allowing one more deletion changes the required private information. It rises from zero to every private feature sign in the excluded candidates. An encoding attains this conditional private-information minimum on the constructed family throughout a sequence of requests. On 1,001 AskUbuntu development records, deleting an initially excluded record admits another record at 13 of 51 fixed similarity cutoffs. Exact membership repair passes 13,056 deletion-step checks. Two model-based semantic calibrations fail their fixed criteria, so the downstream task study is not run. The central result is a retention contract: the future deletions a service promises to support determine which excluded data can still be needed.

</details>


### [33] [From Intelligence to Autonomy: Foundations and Construction of Autonomous Agents for Future Intelligent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33445)
*Hongda Zhang,Ziqing Zhou,Yuning Chen,Linqiang Hu,Yi Liu,Ding Zhou,Zhao Huang,Haining Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文从稳态调节与反馈控制出发，经自创生与生物自主性理论，梳理六条可操作化研究路线，提出"一边界、四回路"自主智能体框架（运行回路、存续回路、反身回路、学习回路），并用五组配对仿真实验初步验证其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI正从一次性感知与生成任务转向长期规划、工具使用与真实环境交互，但任务能力提升并不等于自主性：仍需外部主体设定目标、维持运行条件、检测故障并批准更新。因此需要明确界定"自主智能体"，并为其提供可操作、可实现的机制框架。

Method: 首先给出自主智能体的定义：在明确的身份与权限边界内，同时维持任务目标与存续条件，基于世界模型与自我模型持续行动，并通过反馈调整控制机制与长期能力。随后梳理从稳态调节、反馈控制到自创生与生物自主性的概念脉络，区分六条操作化路线：计算自创生、基于行为的机器人学、智能体形式化、自适应软件、具身适应、机器人自我建模。在此基础上提出"一边界、四回路"框架，由运行、存续、反身（元控制）与学习四个回路分别组织任务执行、自我维持、元控制与受治理的学习。

Result: 五组配对仿真实验分别检验权限约束、道路封闭响应、健康维持、能力重估计与跨任务学习，结果为上述机制的可行性提供了初步证据。

Conclusion: "一边界、四回路"框架在仿真层面对自主性的关键机制具备可行性，但更广泛的环境与物理系统仍有待验证，当前结论仅为初步证据。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is moving from one-off perception and generation tasks toward long-term planning, tool use, and interaction with real environments. Greater task competence, however, does not automatically yield autonomy: an external actor may still have to set goals, sustain operating conditions, detect failures, and approve updates. We define an autonomous agent as a system that, within explicit identity and authority boundaries, maintains both task goals and viability conditions, acts continuously using models of the world and itself, and adjusts its control mechanisms and long-term capabilities through feedback. We trace the conceptual path from homeostasis and feedback control to autopoiesis and biological autonomy. We then distinguish six lines of operationalization: computational autopoiesis, behavior-based robotics, agent formalization, adaptive software, embodied adaptation, and robotic self-modeling. On this basis, we propose a one-boundary, four-loop framework in which Operational, Viability, Reflexive, and Learning loops organize task execution, self-maintenance, metacontrol, and governed learning. Five paired simulation experiments test permission constraints, road-closure response, health maintenance, capability re-estimation, and cross-task learning. Their results provide preliminary evidence for the feasibility of these mechanisms, while broader environments and physical systems remain to be tested.

</details>


### [34] [FG-ConvNO: A Geometry-Aware Neural Operator for Propeller CFD Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33456)
*Yichen Di,Valentin Duruisseaux,Di Zhou,Xinyi Li,Daniel Leibovici,Jean Kossaifi,Anima Anandkumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出因子化几何卷积神经算子 FG-ConvNO，用于小样本、复杂螺旋桨几何下的表面压力与整体推力预测，在自建CFD数据集上取得最高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CFD 高保真仿真成本极高（数千 CPU/GPU 小时），而现有科学机器学习代理模型多依赖大规模同质数据集或简单几何，在复杂且数据稀缺的配置上泛化能力差。

Method: 构建 FG-ConvNO：(1) 几何感知编码；(2) 高效插值式解码；(3) 全局物理量辅助监督；(4) 引入基于离散-连续卷积的 DISCO 模块，使模型对离散化不敏感；并采用随机顶点子采样增强鲁棒性。

Result: 在程序化生成的螺旋桨 CFD 数据集上，FG-ConvNO 在表面压力与整体推力预测两项任务上精度最高；几何感知编码与结构简化提升稳定性，随机顶点子采样增强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 在基于网格的卷积算子中注入几何先验，可缓解复杂螺旋桨几何上的过拟合，并在低数据条件下改善泛化性能。

Abstract: Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) is essential in contemporary engineering design, yet it remains expensive to compute and can take thousands of CPU/GPU hours or more for a single high-fidelity simulation.
