Recent developments in machine learning for scientific modeling have led to powerful surrogate frameworks that can approximate flow fields and physical quantities by using a fraction of the computational cost. However, most of these methods require largescale homogeneous datasets or simple geometries, exhibiting severe generalization gaps when applied to complex and data-scarce configurations. To address this, we propose the Factorized Geometry Convolutional Neural Operator (FG-ConvNO) for robust geometry-aware propeller pressure prediction under limited training data. We introduce geometryaware encoding, efficient interpolation-based decoding, and auxiliary global quantity supervision. In addition, we incorporate DISCO blocks that use discrete continuous convolutions to make the model discretization agnostic. In our experiments, FG-ConvNO achieves the highest accuracy for both surface pressure and global thrust prediction on a procedurally generated propeller CFD dataset. Geometry-aware encoding and architectural simplification improve stability, while random vertex subsampling enhances robustness. Overall, adding geometric priors to grid-based convolutional operators reduces overfitting on complex propeller geometries and improves generalization in low-data regimes.

</details>


### [35] [A Unified Analysis of Importance-Corrected Momentum Actor-Critic over a Markovian Trajectory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33477)
*Yanjie Dong,Zhou Su,Wei Wang,Gang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: We present a generic actor-critic method that combines a ridge-regularized pathwise least-squares temporal-difference (LSTD) critic with importance-corrected momentum (ICM) actors. The critic solves a ridge-regularized system per frame by Newton-Schulz inversion and needs no stepsize, no knowledge of the mixing time, and no rank condition. Averaging stochastic policy gradients over frames of $T$ consecutive transitions, the ICM actors incur a frame-dependent gradient bias of order $\frac{1}{T}$. To our knowledge, we establish the first finite-time bounds for ICM actor-critic methods with normalized and unnormalized steps along a single Markovian trajectory. Up to a stationary-to-discounted distribution mismatch, the averaged gradient norm over $K$ updates is bounded by ${\cal O}(K^{-1/3}+\frac{1}{T})$ for normalized steps and by ${\cal O}(K^{-1/4}+\frac{1}{T})$ for unnormalized steps. We specialize these bounds to Muon, normalized stochastic gradient descent (SGD) with momentum, SGD with momentum, and AdaNorm, and compare them in the nuclear norm. With a dimension-free stepsize, Muon confines the factor $\min\{m,n\}^{1/2}$ associated with an $m\times n$ actor parameter matrix to the noise and correction terms. The comparison also characterizes when orthogonalization guarantees a larger noiseless ascent than normalization: exactly when the gradient's nuclear rank exceeds the spectral-to-Frobenius curvature ratio.

</details>


### [36] [Climate downscaling with likelihood functional flow matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33482)
*Zhi-Song Liu,Helmi Toropainen,Michael Boy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CD-FFM——一种函数式流匹配降尺度模型,将高分辨率场视为连续随机场,任意位置与分辨率均可查询,速度场逐点作用,噪声先验拟合观测谱,观测具备精确似然;在芬诺斯坎迪亚 1 km 日温度降尺度中 CRPS 显著优于回归、大 40 倍神经算子与 CorrDiff 类扩散模型,采样快约 50 倍,并可迁移至降水。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热浪、霜冻与洪水的决策发生在局地尺度,但再分析资料与气候模式仅能解析数十公里尺度。生成式降尺度方法虽能补充公里级细节并给出不确定性,却绑定于固定网格、采样代价高且缺乏可计算的似然,限制了灵活查询与概率评估。

Method: 构建 CD-FFM 函数式流匹配模型:以连续随机场为建模对象,单一模型可在任意位置、任意分辨率查询;速度场采用逐点(pointwise)形式,空间变异性由拟合观测功率谱的噪声先验提供;由此保证每个观测具有精确似然。实验中将 ERA5 降尺度至芬诺斯坎迪亚 1 km 日温度,并与回归基线、大 40 倍的神经算子及 CorrDiff 风格扩散模型对比。

Result: 在三个测试年份上,CD-FFM 使 CRPS 较回归降低 27–31%,较大 40 倍神经算子降低 12–18%;性能超过 CorrDiff 风格扩散模型,采样速度约快 50 倍。同一模型可迁移至降水,并能以可承受成本生成公里尺度大集合。

Conclusion: 函数式、逐点速度的流匹配范式可在无网格约束、低采样成本与精确似然之间取得统一,为公里级局地气候决策提供高效、可概率评估的降尺度工具,并具备跨变量泛化能力。

Abstract: Decisions about heat, frost and flooding are made locally, yet reanalyses and climate models resolve tens of kilometres. Generative downscalers add kilometre-scale detail with uncertainty, but are tied to a grid, costly to sample and lack a tractable likelihood. Here we introduce CD-FFM, a functional flow-matching model that treats the high-resolution field as a continuous random field that one model can query at any location and resolution. Its velocity acts pointwise, so spatial variability comes from a noise prior fitted to the observed spectrum and every observation has an exact likelihood. Downscaling ERA5 to 1 km daily temperature over Fennoscandia, CD-FFM lowers the continuous ranked probability score by 27-31% against regression and 12-18% against a forty-times larger neural operator in each of three test years, and outperforms a CorrDiff-style diffusion model while sampling about fifty times faster. The same model transfers to precipitation and makes large kilometre-scale ensembles affordable.

</details>


### [37] [Error-Subspace Calibration for Multi-Task Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33483)
*Yeseul Cho,Hanseul Cho,Chulhee Yun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出后合并校准框架ESCal，利用专家模型与合并模型的权重差构造低维误差子空间，通过轻量桥接模块在该子空间内做线性特征校正，从而缩小合并模型与单个专家之间的性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并无需联合训练即可把多个任务专家整合为单一模型，但合并模型相对各专家存在明显性能下降。已有的后合并校准虽能通过任务特定表征对齐缓解该问题，但对齐目标本身并未指明应修正哪些特征方向，缺乏明确的方向选择依据。

Method: ESCal从专家模型与合并模型的权重差中提取低维误差子空间。在每个中间块边界，桥接模块将合并表征投影到下一块输入投影的误差子空间中，再把这些坐标映射为当前块输出投影误差子空间中的加性修正；子空间基底保持固定，仅学习这两个线性映射，因此参数开销很小。

Result: 表明ESCal使合并模型在表征与预测两方面都更接近各专家，说明其更忠实地恢复了任务特定行为；在视觉与自然语言理解基准上，ESCal在使用每任务少量校准数据的条件下，对多种架构与多种合并方法均带来性能提升。

Conclusion: 误差子空间为后合并校准提供了明确且有效的修正方向，基于固定子空间基底的线性桥接是一种参数高效、架构与合并方法无关的通用校准方案。

Abstract: Model merging combines task-specific expert models into a single model without joint training on their original datasets, but often incurs a performance gap relative to the individual experts. Post-merging calibration can reduce this gap through task-specific representation alignment, but the alignment objective alone does not specify which feature directions to correct. We propose Error-Subspace Calibration (ESCal), a post-merging calibration framework that identifies low-dimensional error subspaces from weight differences between expert and merged models. At each intermediate block boundary, a bridge module projects the merged representation onto the next block's input-projection error subspace and maps these coordinates to an additive correction in the current block's output-projection error subspace. The subspace bases remain fixed, so calibration learns only these linear maps, yielding a parameter-efficient mechanism for feature correction. We find that ESCal brings the merged model closer to the experts in both representations and predictions, indicating more faithful recovery of task-specific behavior. Experiments on vision and natural language understanding benchmarks show that ESCal improves merged-model performance across diverse architectures and merging methods using a small calibration set for each task.

</details>


### [38] [RBGA: Residual-Budget Guide Arbitration for Offline-to-Online Safe Reinforcement Learning in Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33494)
*Zongyou Yu,Ao Zhou,Xiongbo Shi,Jiayi Guan,Shihang Du,Xudong Zhang,Shangding Gu,Junqiao Zhao,Hanli Wang,Guang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Residual-Budget Guide Arbitration (RBGA) 算法，显式跟踪回合内剩余安全预算，并据此在离线引导策略与在线演化策略之间逐步仲裁动作选择，实现离线到在线安全强化学习中的稳定策略提升与安全保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作要求机器人在利用已有数据集快速习得技能的同时，保证真实部署中的安全性。离线到在线安全强化学习（O2O safe RL）可借助先验经验在安全约束下进行在线改进，缓解从零学习安全策略的样本低效与探索风险。但现有 O2O safe RL 方法在决策时未显式考虑回合内已消耗的安全预算，早期过高的代价会使后续探索与策略改进空间受限。

Method: RBGA 显式跟踪当前回合的剩余安全预算，并将其用于自适应地在离线引导（guide）与不断演化的在线策略之间进行选择。每一步中，算法将引导动作与在线动作的 cost-to-go 估计与剩余预算进行比较，从而在整个回合内实现逐步的动作仲裁；同时给出理论分析，刻画基于剩余预算的选择对安全性的影响及其对引导辅助探索的作用。

Result: 大量实验表明，RBGA 在保持较强安全性能的同时能够实现有效的在线策略改进。

Conclusion: 通过在回合内显式利用剩余安全预算进行逐步仲裁，RBGA 克服了现有 O2O safe RL 忽视预算消耗的缺陷，兼顾了策略提升与部署安全性，为灵巧操作等安全敏感场景提供了可行方案。代码已开源。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation requires robots to leverage existing datasets for rapid skill acquisition while maintaining safety during real-world deployment to prevent damage to the robot or its surroundings. Offline-to-online safe reinforcement learning (O2O safe RL) provides a promising solution for this problem by leveraging prior experience for online improvement under safety constraints, thereby mitigating the sample inefficiency and exploration risk of learning safe policies from scratch. However, existing O2O safe RL approaches do not explicitly account for the safety budget already consumed when making decisions within an episode. Excessive cost incurred early can therefore leave little room for subsequent exploration and policy improvement. To address this limitation, we propose \textbf{R}esidual-\textbf{B}udget \textbf{G}uide \textbf{A}rbitration (RBGA), an algorithm that explicitly tracks the remaining episode safety budget and uses it to adaptively select between offline guidance and the evolving online policy. At each step, RBGA compares the cost-to-go estimates of the guide and online actions against the remaining budget, enabling step-wise action arbitration throughout the episode. We provide theoretical analysis characterizing the safety implications of residual-budget-based selection and its effect on guide-assisted exploration. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RBGA enables effective online policy improvement while maintaining strong safety performance. Our code is available at this \href{https://github.com/sii-pilab}{link}.

</details>


### [39] [FedSEAM: Federated Continual Learning of LLMs via Subspace Expert Agreement Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33496)
*Fatema Siddika,Abhijit Chunduru,Zilinghan Li,Ali Jannesari,Ravi Madduri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedSEAM 将联邦持续学习的任务知识组织为稀疏专家，通过子空间兼容性检验与方向性合并实现跨客户端的选择性知识共享，缓解遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦持续学习中，稀疏 MoE 虽可冻结主干并只适配部分参数，但各客户端随任务演化学到不同的专家内容；仅凭 tile 坐标匹配无法判断所学内容是否兼容，因此难以在不破坏任务/客户端特化的前提下共享知识。

Method: 客户端侧：任务感知的 tile 选择为新任务分配容量，基于子空间的兼容性检验决定复用哪些既有专家 tile、何处引入新容量。服务器侧：提出 Subspace Expert Agreement Merging（SEAM），将客户端更新视为子空间进行比较，仅沿一致方向将各客户端推向共识，其余更新保持不变。此外识别出路由漂移与合并擦除两类残余遗忘来源，并以三项保护措施应对，其中一项在每次合并时精确保持选中先验重叠子空间中的 tile 分量。

Result: 在 TRACE 基准上，使用 Qwen3-4B 与 Llama-3.2-3B 进行评估，并给出组件级消融与数据规模研究，用以考察新任务学习与先验知识保留之间的平衡。

Conclusion: 选择性、子空间受限的专家共享可在多客户端持续学习场景中兼顾特化与知识迁移；精确子空间保护对抑制合并引起的遗忘具有关键作用。

Abstract: Federated continual learning trains a model on a sequence of tasks across several clients without sharing their data while retaining knowledge of earlier tasks. Sparse mixture-of-experts models support this setting by adapting selected parameters on a frozen backbone. However, clients learn different expert content as tasks evolve, making it difficult to share knowledge across clients without disrupting task-specific and client-specific specialization. Matching tile coordinates alone does not establish whether their learned content is compatible. We present FedSEAM, a federated continual learning framework that organizes task knowledge into sparse experts and selectively shares their content across tasks and clients. On each client, task-aware tile selection allocates capacity to a new task, while a subspace-based compatibility test determines which existing expert tiles to reuse and where to introduce fresh capacity. On the server, Subspace Expert Agreement Merging (SEAM) compares client updates as subspaces and moves each client towards consensus only along agreed-upon directions, leaving the rest of its update unchanged. We identify two remaining sources of forgetting, routing drift and merge erasure, and address them with three protections, one of which exactly preserves tile components in a selected prior-overlap subspace at every merge. We evaluate FedSEAM on TRACE using Qwen3-4B and Llama-3.2-3B, with component-wise ablations and studies of data size to assess its balance between learning new tasks and retaining prior knowledge.

</details>


### [40] [Overlap-Aware Sub-hypergraph Pattern Frequencies for Hypergraph-Level Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33514)
*Nafiul Hasan Anik,Nazira Jesmin Lina,Md Tanvir Alam,Md. Fahim Arefin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出用"带重叠标签的频繁子超图模式"构建定长表示，并以模式的相对频率描述超图，用于超图分类，在DHG-Bench六个数据集中的五个上取得更高Macro-F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 整图超图分类需要定长输入描述，而顶点与超边数量可变。频繁子超图模式可提供此类描述，但以往仅记录模式是否出现，丢失了两类信息：两条超边间重叠的大小，以及每个模式在超图中占比多少。

Method: 扩展频繁子超图挖掘，引入重叠标签（模式两条超边交集大小按分箱离散化，继承自目标超图）；用每个挖掘模式的相对频率表示超图。词表仅在训练超图上挖掘后冻结，验证/测试超图与之匹配；采用依赖模式大小的支持度规则以控制词表规模。

Result: 在DHG-Bench六个超图分类数据集上，用验证分数选定的单一配置，五个数据集平均Macro-F1高于已发表最佳均值，Twitter-Friend提升18.67点，Steam-Player提升10.02点；RHG-3低0.21点。消融显示：以出现性替代相对频率使Steam-Player与Twitter-Friend的Macro-F1下降约18点；移除重叠标签下降4–9点。

Conclusion: 频率式表示与重叠标签均对性能有实质贡献，二者互补；该方法在多数数据集上优于现有最好结果，仅在RHG-3上略有差距。

Abstract: Classifying whole hypergraphs requires a fixed-length description of inputs whose numbers of vertices and hyperedges vary. Frequent sub-hypergraph patterns provide such a description, but previous work records only whether each pattern is present, which loses two kinds of information: how large the overlap between two hyperedges is, and how much of a hypergraph each pattern makes up. We extend frequent sub-hypergraph mining with overlap labels, the binned intersection size of two pattern hyperedges inherited from the target hypergraph, and represent each hypergraph by the relative frequency of each mined pattern. The vocabulary is mined from training hypergraphs only and then frozen, and validation and test hypergraphs are matched against it. A support rule that depends on pattern size keeps the vocabulary small. On the six hypergraph-classification datasets of DHG-Bench, with a single configuration chosen on validation scores, the method has a higher mean Macro-F1 than the best published mean on five datasets, by 18.67 points on Twitter-Friend and 10.02 points on Steam-Player; on RHG-3 it is 0.21 points lower. Ablations show that on Steam-Player and Twitter-Friend, replacing relative frequencies with presence lowers Macro-F1 by about 18 points, and that removing overlap labels lowers it by 4 to 9 points.

</details>


### [41] [Collective Collaboration of Recursive Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33515)
*Ziqing Zhou,Linqiang Hu,Yuning Chen,Hongda Zhang,Zhao Huang,Ding Zhou,Haining Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出递归自治代理（RAA）集体协作框架，用统一的任务-能力接口实现生产目标的递归分解与能力自底向上聚合，并以组织内核在设备服务、车间、工厂三个尺度实例化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能工厂需把订单级目标转化为多尺度生产任务，并把个体单元能力合成为整体交付能力；但现有组织机制在设备、车间、工厂间需反复适配，且在动态生产条件下缺乏跨层级协调。

Method: 构建统一任务与能力接口，支持自上而下递归分解目标、自下而上经同伴协作聚合条件能力；复合单元作为同类代理参与更高层组织；形式化叶单元与复合单元的组织模型，刻画组合下的接口闭包及有限任务展开条件；建立连接任务分解、资源承诺、结果聚合与变更影响域内协调的运行机制。

Result: 以跨多车间、集成加工-检测-物流的生产网络为例，验证同一组织内核可在设备服务、车间、工厂三个尺度上实例化。

Conclusion: 该框架将递归组织结构与明确的任务责任、能力条件和执行接口相联系，为制造系统提供可跨尺度复用的协作组织基础。

Abstract: Smart factories must translate order-level objectives into production tasks at multiple scales and combine the capabilities of individual units into an overall delivery capability. To address repeated adaptation of organizational mechanisms across equipment, workshops, and factories, as well as coordination across levels under changing production conditions, this paper develops a framework for the collective collaboration of recursive autonomous agents (RAAs). A common task and capability interface supports the recursive decomposition of production objectives from the top down and the aggregation of conditional capabilities through peer collaboration from the bottom up. Composite units can therefore participate in higher-level organizations as agents of the same kind. We formulate organizational models for leaf and composite units, specify interface closure under composition and conditions for finite task expansion, and develop operational mechanisms linking task decomposition, resource commitments, result aggregation, and coordination within the scope affected by change. A production network integrating machining, inspection, and logistics across multiple workshops illustrates how a common organizational kernel is instantiated at the equipment-service, workshop, and factory scales. The framework connects recursive organization with explicit task responsibilities, capability conditions, and execution interfaces for manufacturing systems.

</details>


### [42] [Implicit Beliefs as Scalable Process Rewards for Strategic Persuasion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33527)
*Minqian Liu,Yuexi Shen,Heajun An,Qi Zhang,Pamela J. Wisniewski,Sang Won Lee,Jin-Hee Cho,Lifu Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出PersuArena说服模拟环境与Probe-RPO在线强化学习框架，通过从冻结的被说服者logits中读取内省探针，将说服者内部状态转化为稠密过程奖励，在多领域任务上实现52.5%的完全接受率，显著优于GRPO（35.8%）和现成LLM（25.8%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮策略性说服的LLM训练存在两个耦合难题：一是缺乏心理机制上可信的被说服者模拟，二是策略对话中信用分配缺乏稠密且忠实的监督信号。仅用结果奖励过于稀疏，无法识别哪些对话动作导致成功；而LLM评判者提供的过程奖励成本高，且与底层交互动力学脱耦。

Method: 构建PersuArena说服模拟环境，产生真实且非单调的对话轨迹；在此基础上提出Probe-RPO（Process Rewards via Online Belief Elicitation for Relative Policy Optimization）在线RL框架：每一轮从冻结被说服者的logits中读取三个内省探针，生成多维稠密过程奖励，并与相对策略优化结合进行在线训练。

Result: 在覆盖医疗、财务咨询、职场管理、教育和人际关系五大领域的PersuArena实验中，Probe-RPO达到52.5%的完全接受率，而最强GRPO消融为35.8%，最佳现成LLM为25.8%；并进一步分析了跨被说服者模型的泛化性与对话中的说服动力学演变。

Conclusion: 内省探针驱动的在线信念引出可将被说服者内部状态转化为忠实、低成本的稠密过程监督，为策略性对话的过程奖励设计提供了有前景的新范式。

Abstract: Training large language models (LLMs) for multi-turn strategic persuasion faces two coupled obstacles: (i) realistic simulation of psychologically grounded persuadees, and (ii) dense, faithful supervision for credit assignment in strategic dialogues. Outcome-only rewards are sparse and uninformative about which conversational moves drove success, while LLM-judge process rewards are expensive and decoupled from the underlying interaction dynamics. We address both challenges with a unified framework. First, we introduce PersuArena, a persuasion simulation environment that produces realistic, non-monotonic trajectories. Second, building directly on this environment, we propose Process Rewards via Online Belief Elicitation for Relative Policy Optimization (Probe-RPO), an online RL framework that converts the persuadee's internal state into dense process rewards. At each turn, three introspective probes are read from the frozen persuadee's logits, yielding a multi-dimensional reward signal that is faithfully aligned with persuasion dynamics. With extensive experiments on PersuArena spanning healthcare, financial advising, workplace management, education, and personal relationships, we demonstrate that our Probe-RPO achieves the full acceptance rate of 52.5%, compared with the 35.8% for the strongest GRPO ablation and 25.8% for the best off-the-shelf LLM. We also conduct comprehensive analyses on the generalization across persuadee models and the persuasion dynamics across conversations. Our work offers a promising paradigm that can provide effective process supervision for strategic dialogue.

</details>


### [43] [Clustering of High-Dimensional Human Gene Score Data for Target Cell Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33531)
*S. Das,V. Madala,P. Majumder,N. S. Sinha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文考察全人类基因评分数据集与筛选的激酶基因数据集在相同769个特征下是否呈现相同聚类数，结果表明所有情形下的最优聚类数均为2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为检验数据集规模（全基因组 vs. 激酶基因子集）是否影响基于相同特征空间的聚类结构，即两数据集是否具有一致的聚类数目。

Method: 构建两个数据集（完整基因评分集与激酶基因子集），采用两种缺失值处理策略：Case I 删除含缺失值的行；Case II 剔除缺失比例超过20%的行并用列均值填补其余缺失。每种情形下先标准化并做PCA降维，再比较四种聚类方法：K-Means、层次聚类、模糊C均值与图聚类。

Result: 在两种数据集与两种缺失值处理的全部组合下，最优聚类数均为2；在缺乏明确最优聚类数时以轮廓系数评估聚类质量，各情形轮廓系数均相对较高。

Conclusion: 全基因评分集与激酶基因子集在769个特征空间中呈现一致的二聚类结构，且聚类质量较好，说明筛选后的激酶子集保留了整体数据的聚类结构。

Abstract: The goal is to investigate whether the total human gene-score dataset and the selected human kinase-gene dataset exhibit the same number of clusters when analysed using the same set of 769 features. Two datasets were considered: the complete gene score dataset and a subset containing selected kinase genes. Two missing-data strategies were investigated. In Case I, rows containing missing gene-score values were removed. In Case II, rows with more than 20% missing values were excluded and the other missing values were replaced by the corresponding column mean. For each case, the data were scaled and principal component analysis (PCA) was performed before clustering. Four clustering approaches were considered: K-Means, Hierarchical Clustering, Fuzzy C-Means, and Graph-based Clustering. Across both datasets and both missing-data treatments, the optimal number of clusters was found to be two. In the absence of an optimal number of clusters, the quality of clustering was assessed using the Silhouette score. Relatively high Silhouette scores were obtained in all cases.

</details>


### [44] [Nowcasting aerosol particle size distributions from PM2.5 with physics-regularized flow matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33557)
*Zhi-Song Liu,Jenni Köykkä,Wenqing Peng,Ammar Kheder,Petri Clusius,Michael Boy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文以芬兰两个站点（北方森林Hyytiälä与城市Kumpula）为可控测试平台，探索用生成式深度学习模型由PM2.5记录和八项气象参数在24h窗口内"临近预报"颗粒物数谱分布（PSD），并对六种生成模型进行基准评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PM2.5质量浓度测量廉价且监测广泛，而颗粒物数谱分布（PSD）所需仪器昂贵、仅少数研究站可测。然而气溶胶的许多效应（如蒸汽摄取、云滴形成、人体呼吸道沉积）取决于数谱而非质量。因此亟需从常规空气质量监测数据中反演尺寸分辨的气溶胶信息。

Method: 从PM2.5记录及8项气象参数出发，在24h窗口内生成一组合理的逐小时数谱集合（体现同一质量可由多种数谱构成）。采用两个对比站点（北方森林Hyytiälä与城市Kumpula）构建受控测试平台，将六种生成模型与确定性及气候态基线对比，分别在本地及跨站点迁移条件下评测，并检验用于神经网络训练的物理约束（基于简化的气溶胶通用动力学方程GDE）。

Result: Flow matching整体表现最佳，其引导变体在两站点均取得最低连续分级概率评分（CRPS）。基于简化GDE的物理约束使训练站点CRPS降低5.5%–11.5%，并减少跨站点迁移后的误差。跨站点迁移仍难于本地估计。

Conclusion: 该基准表明，物理信息生成模型是从常规空气质量监测迈向尺寸分辨气溶胶信息的有前景途径；下一步应在大规模多站点数据集上训练，以实现对未监测地点的PSD估计。

Abstract: The mass concentration of particles smaller than 2.5um (PM2.5) is cheap to measure and widely monitored, whereas the particle number size distribution (PSD) requires costly instruments and is measured at only a few research stations. Yet many aerosol effects, such as vapor uptake, cloud droplet formation and deposition in the human respiratory tract, depend on the PSD rather than on mass. We explore whether recent generative deep learning models can nowcast the PSD: from the PM2.5 record and eight meteorological parameters over a 24h window, a model generates an ensemble of plausible hourly size distributions for the same window, reflecting that one mass can arise from many size distributions. Rather than proposing a single best model, we use two contrasting Finnish stations, the boreal forest site Hyytiälä and the urban site Kumpula, as a controlled testbed. On it, we benchmark six generative models against deterministic and climatological baselines, locally and after transfer between sites, and test physical constraints for neural network training. Flow matching performs best overall; its guided variant has the lowest continuous ranked probability score (CRPS) at both sites. A constraint based on a reduced aerosol general dynamic equation (GDE) lowers the CRPS by 5.5-11.5% at the training site and reduces errors after transfer between sites. Transfer remains harder than local estimation, identifying training on large multi-station data sets as the next step towards PSD estimates at unmonitored locations. This benchmark establishes physics-informed generative models as a promising route from routine air-quality monitoring to size-resolved aerosol information.

</details>


### [45] [Variational Atlas Autoencoders and Self-Supervised Chart Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33577)
*Arno Coomans,Merlin Nimier-David,Eugene d'Eon,Boris Bonev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出变分图册自编码器（VAAE），将图册学习形式化为潜变量后验推断，用"潜证据路由"实现自监督的图表分配，并可从训练好的图表重建图册以支持跨图表连续插值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂高维数据位于流形上，单一全局潜坐标系统难以无失真地表示；"混合图表/图册"思路的核心难题是无标签情况下如何决定哪个图表负责哪个数据点，以往做法多为启发式路由，缺乏原理性依据。

Method: 将图表索引视为潜变量，由证据下界（ELBO）自然导出训练目标，并把图表分配转化为后验推断，该结论对任意路由分布均成立。在此框架下提出潜证据路由：按某图表潜先验下编码证据的大小成比例地分配数据点——点越靠近某图表潜空间中心、放置越自信，就越可能属于该图表。分配仅依赖编码器，完全自监督。此外，通过识别图表间的重叠区域与转移映射，从训练好的图表中重建图册。

Result: 图表能自主发现数据中的类别与子类结构（如不同类型的鞋或人脸），无需标签；重建的图册支持图表内与跨图表的连续插值，插值路径贴近数据流形而非穿越无支撑的潜空间区域；在 CelebA 等图像数据集上，与容量匹配的基线相比，潜空间结构更规整、样本质量更高，且采样时只需评估单个图表的解码器。

Conclusion: 图表分配可被严格表述为后验推断而非启发式规则，从而实现自监督的图册学习；该框架统一了混合图表建模、路由与流形插值，并在表示质量与生成质量上优于容量匹配基线。

Abstract: Complex, high-dimensional data lie on manifolds that a single global latent coordinate system cannot represent without distortion. We introduce Variational Atlas Autoencoders, a mixture-of-charts framework that learns an atlas of local latent representations, each responsible for one region of the data manifold. The central difficulty in learning an atlas is deciding which chart should be responsible for which data point without labels. We show that this decision need not be a heuristic. By treating the chart index as a latent variable, the evidence lower bound yields the training objective and casts chart assignment as posterior inference, valid for any routing distribution. Within this framework, we propose latent-evidence routing, which assigns a point to a chart in proportion to the evidence of its encoding under that chart's latent prior. This has a simple interpretation: the closer a point lands to the center of a chart's latent space, and the more confidently it is placed there, the more likely it is to belong to that chart. Chart assignment thus emerges as posterior inference, depends on the encoder alone, and is fully self-supervised, so the charts discover structure in the data on their own, separating classes and subclasses such as distinct types of shoes or faces without access to labels. We further recover an atlas from the trained charts by identifying their overlaps and transition maps, enabling continuous interpolation within and across charts that stays close to the data manifold rather than traversing unsupported regions of latent space. Compared to capacity-matched baselines, Variational Atlas Autoencoders yields better-structured latent spaces and higher sample quality on image datasets including CelebA, while sampling requires evaluating only a single chart's decoder.

</details>


### [46] [H-GLow: Accelerating Graph Generation via Hazard-Guided Discrete Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33599)
*Sirine Ayadi,Stephan Günnemann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: H-Glow：将图DFM的连续时间马尔可夫链生成器分解为hazard与条件跳变分布，据此规划图编辑并动态调整采样时间，实现少步高质量图生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图DFM虽将训练与采样解耦，但现有采样器依赖任务特定启发式、超参数空间大；且采样预算仍与扩散模型相当，加速潜力未被充分释放。图生成中节点与边强依赖，使少步高质量生成困难。

Method: 提出hazard引导的H-Glow框架：把CTMC生成器分解为hazard与条件跳变分布，用这两个量规划图编辑并按图活动度动态调整采样时刻；可即插即用于预训练图DFM。另提出基于conditional generator matching的新图DFM模型，直接预测hazard与条件跳变，其训练目标精确分解为hazard匹配与hazard加权跳变预测。

Result: 在分子与合成图基准上，以显著更少的采样步数取得更优生成质量。

Conclusion: 以hazard为核心的分解视角能统一并替代启发式采样策略，减少超参数调节，同时释放图DFM的少步加速潜力。

Abstract: Discrete flow matching (DFM) offers a promising approach to graph generation; however, generating high-quality graphs with few steps remains challenging due to the strong dependencies between nodes and edges. Although DFM decouples training from sampling, enabling flexible sampling strategies without retraining, existing graph DFM samplers often rely on task-specific heuristics, substantially expanding the hyperparameter space. Moreover, graph DFM models still require sampling budgets comparable to those of diffusion-based approaches, leaving their potential for accelerating graph generation only partially realized. In this work, we introduce H-Glow, a hazard-guided framework for accelerating graph generation built on the decomposition of Continuous-Time Markov chain (CTMC) generators into hazards and conditional jump distributions. H-Glow leverages these quantities to plan graph edits and dynamically adjust the sampling time to graph activity. H-Glow can be adapted to pretrained graph DFM models, substantially improving the generation quality with fewer steps. We further introduce a novel graph DFM model based on the conditional generator matching framework, which directly predicts hazards and conditional jumps, and show that its training objective decomposes exactly into hazard matching and hazard-weighted jump prediction. Experiments on molecular and synthetic graph benchmarks demonstrate improved generation quality with substantially fewer sampling steps.

</details>


### [47] [Satellite-derived bathymetry of Arabian Gulf ports under leakage-free validation: a multi-site benchmark of empirical, machine-learning and deep-learning models against 285,000 multibeam soundings, with an outlook for UAE waters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33609)
*Muhammad Saeed,Fatema Al Hajri,Mohamed H. Alhosani,Dmitry Mikhaylov,Yasser F. Al Wahedi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在阿布扎比浑浊浅水潮汐海域用285,619个多波束测深点检验卫星测深(SDB)，发现验证设计对结果的影响大于模型本身：随机像素划分0.57 m、空间分块1.30 m、未见站点1.66 m；加入本地控制后混合模型在0–11 m达1.11 m，可作约10 m以浅的勘测/变化监测工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SDB常被提议作为港口多波束测量低成本补充，但其精度很少在模型从未见过的区域上进行评估，且行业报告常采用随机划分导致精度虚高，缺乏可操作的精度基准。

Method: 以12次测量、6个港区、归算至最低天文潮位的285,619个多波束点为真值，用72景Sentinel-2训练Stumpf比值、Lyzenga变换、LightGBM梯度提升树与U-Net集成；测试包括未见站点迁移与本地控制（约20%测深点、225 m缓冲外空间分块）；并用ICESat-2 ATL24校准生成17,200 km²、60 m分辨率的侦察镶嵌图。

Result: 同一LightGBM在随机像素划分RMSE 0.57 m、空间分块1.30 m、未见站点1.66 m；迁移条件下Stumpf比值(1.45 m)未被LightGBM、U-Net或Lyzenga超越。本地控制下树模型+波段比值混合在0–11 m达1.11 m，67%像元满足IHO S-44 Order 2，保形95%区间实测覆盖率95%；清水区0.29–0.53 m。11 m以深不可测，夏季影像浑浊度为冬季5–6倍。ICESat-2 ATL24经深度相关偏差校正后与测量一致至0.25 m；纯ICESat-2逐格校准的镶嵌图在半数产品区留出分块上RMSE 1.42 m。

Conclusion: 在该水域，每景约百个控制深度的Sentinel-2 SDB可作为约10 m以浅的勘测与变化监测工具；无本地控制时达不到S-44 Order 1，且随机划分所得精度不可视为业务精度，验证设计应作为SDB精度报告的核心要素。

Abstract: Satellite-derived bathymetry (SDB) is often proposed as a low-cost complement to multibeam surveys of ports, but its accuracy is seldom measured on an area the model has never seen. We test SDB in the turbid, shallow and tidal waters of the Abu Dhabi coast against 285,619 multibeam soundings from twelve surveys at six port areas, reduced to Lowest Astronomical Tide. The Stumpf ratio, the Lyzenga transform, a monotonic gradient-boosted tree model (LightGBM) and a U-Net ensemble were trained on 72 Sentinel-2 images and tested in transfer to an unseen site and with local control (about 20% of a site's soundings, spatial blocks beyond a 225 m buffer). The validation design changed the result more than the model: the same LightGBM model gave 0.57 m RMSE under a random pixel split, 1.30 m under spatial blocks and 1.66 m at an unseen site. In transfer the Stumpf ratio (1.45 m) was not beaten by LightGBM, the U-Net or Lyzenga. With local control a hybrid of the tree output and the band ratio reached 1.11 m over 0-11 m, 67% of pixels within the IHO S-44 Order 2 tolerance and a conformal 95% interval with 95% observed coverage; in clear water it reached 0.29-0.53 m. The seabed could not be read beyond 11 m, and summer images were five to six times more turbid than winter ones. ICESat-2 ATL24 agreed with the surveys to 0.25 m after a depth-dependent offset was removed. A 60 m reconnaissance mosaic of 17,200 km2 of the UAE coast, calibrated cell by cell on ICESat-2 without any vessel survey, reached 1.42 m RMSE on held-out blocks over half of the product. Sentinel-2 SDB with about a hundred control depths per image is a usable reconnaissance and change-monitoring tool in these waters to about 10 m; it does not meet S-44 Order 1 without local control, and accuracies from random splits should not be taken as operational accuracy.

</details>


### [48] [Geodesic-SVDD Latent Mixup for One-Shot Distributed Tabular Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33619)
*Andrea Moleri,Christian Internò,Markus Olhofer,Barbara Hammer,Ali Raza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Geodesic-SVDD 框架，将每个客户端的分布式学习任务重构为超球面上的单类密度估计（DSVDD），借助残差球面编码器与测地隐空间 Mixup 保证严格球面约束，并设计了单轮"专家乘积"隐私聚合协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度提升树效率高但不可微，无法接入标准基于梯度的联邦协议；而可做联邦优化的深度分类器在标签分布偏斜下易发生灾难性遗忘。现有方法难以同时兼顾结构数据的效率与联邦场景下的隐私和鲁棒性。

Method: 1）将每个客户端任务表述为超球面上的单类密度估计，使本地模型可作为概率流形在一次通信轮内聚合；2）每个客户端使用残差球面编码器与测地隐空间 Mixup，强制满足 ‖φ(x)‖≡1 的球面约束，避免线性近似的流形坍塌；3）基于该性质提出一次性（One-Shot）"专家乘积"协议，无需迭代同步即可隐私聚合；4）从机理上分析插值策略的权衡。

Result: 在五个表格基准与四种标签偏斜水平下，Geodesic-SVDD 在七种联邦方法中取得最高平均 AUROC；在 Covertype 上比联邦树集成高最多 21 个 AUROC 点；One-Shot 变体在 IID 与轻度偏斜下仅用单轮通信即达到集中式 Deep SVDD 水平。机理分析表明线性 mixup 虽计算便捷，但会使优化梯度不稳定，幅度相差约 10^9 倍。

Conclusion: 将联邦学习重构为球面单类密度估计可为结构化数据提供高效、隐私友好且对标签偏斜更鲁棒的替代方案；严格的测地插值与单轮专家乘积聚合是实现通信高效与性能兼顾的关键。

Abstract: Distributed Learning on structured data is a key and widely adopted approach in privacy-sensitive sectors, ranging from financial fraud prevention to federated healthcare diagnostics. While Gradient Boosted Decision Trees are widely used in centralized settings due to their efficiency, they are incompatible with standard gradient-based federated learning protocols because of their non-differentiability. Conversely, standard deep classifiers that are amenable to federated optimization often suffer from catastrophic forgetting under label distribution skew. In this work, to address these gaps we propose Geodesic-SVDD, a framework for Distributed Spherical Prototype Learning that recasts each client's task as one-class density estimation on the hypersphere, so that local models can be aggregated as probabilistic manifolds in a single round. Specifically, for each client, we employ a Residual Spherical Encoder and Geodesic Latent Mixup that enforces strict hyperspherical constraints ($\|φ(x)\| \equiv 1$), preventing the manifold collapse observed in linear approximations. Furthermore, leveraging this insight, we propose a novel One-Shot "Product of Experts" protocol that enables privacy-preserving aggregation without iterative synchronization. Finally, we mechanistically analyze the trade-off between interpolation strategies, demonstrating that while linear mixup offers an enticing computational shortcut, it destabilizes optimization gradients by orders of magnitude ($10^9\times$). Across five tabular benchmarks and four levels of label skew, Geodesic-SVDD attains the highest mean AUROC among seven federated methods, outperforms federated tree ensembles by up to 21 AUROC points on Covertype, and its One-Shot variant matches centralized Deep SVDD under IID and mild skew with a single communication round.

</details>


### [49] [Beyond Affine Reconstruction: Multi-Resolution Transformer DeepONets for the Radiative Transfer Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33668)
*Tanguy Azéma,Marc Bocquet,Fatiha Nmira,Xiaoyi Lu,Jean-Louis Consalvi,Sibo Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于Transformer的DeepONet（Perceiver编码器+交叉注意力解码器），实现多分辨率辐射传热方程(RTE)代理建模，可在不重采样的情况下处理不同分辨率输入，并以非仿射重构突破仿射重构的理论下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RTE求解是火灾仿真的计算瓶颈；神经算子可加速，但传统DeepONet依赖固定网格输入与仿射重构，跨分辨率灵活性差、固定潜维下精度受限。

Method: 构造Transformer-based DeepONet：用Perceiver编码器将不同空间分辨率的温度场与吸收系数场映射为固定尺寸潜表示，用交叉注意力解码器实现辐射强度的非仿射重构；并给出先前理论下界的可计算经验版本，用于在同一数据集上直接比较模型误差。

Result: 在二维池火数据集上，预测辐射强度与入射辐射均优于基于自编码器的DeepONet，且可训练参数更少；功率谱密度分析显示小尺度结构保持更好；两个仿射基线在所有评估秩上均高于下界，而所提模型在一个匹配潜维下训练误差低于该下界；在三分辨率混合数据集上各输入分辨率均具精度优势。

Conclusion: 非仿射重构可突破仿射重构的表示能力限制，Transformer-DeepONet为多分辨率RTE代理建模提供了更准确、更参数高效的方案。

Abstract: Radiative heat transfer plays a dominant role in fire simulations, where solving the radiative transfer equation (RTE) is a computational bottleneck. Neural operators can accelerate these computations by learning solution operators from offline simulation data. However, conventional DeepONet surrogates rely on fixed-grid inputs and affine reconstruction, limiting their flexibility across resolutions and their approximation accuracy at a fixed latent dimension. In this paper, we develop a transformer-based DeepONet for multi-resolution RTE surrogate modelling. The proposed approach combines a Perceiver encoder, which maps temperature and absorption coefficient fields at different spatial resolutions into a fixed-size latent representation, with a cross-attention decoder that enables non-affine reconstruction of the radiative intensity. This architecture allows a single model to process inputs at varying resolutions without resampling them onto a common grid. We formulate a computable empirical version of a previously derived theoretical lower bound for affine reconstruction, enabling direct comparison with model errors on the same dataset. Experiments on a two-dimensional pool-fire dataset demonstrate that the proposed approach outperforms autoencoder-based DeepONets in predicting both radiative intensity and incident radiation, with fewer trainable parameters. Power spectral density analysis shows improved preservation of small-scale structures. Both affine baselines remain above the corresponding lower bound at all evaluated ranks, whereas the proposed model achieves a training error below it at one matched latent dimension, demonstrating the potential of non-affine reconstruction to overcome this representational limitation. Experiments on a dataset combining three spatial resolutions confirm the accuracy advantage of the approach at each input resolution.

</details>


### [50] [Extraction meets Distillation - cryptanalytic techniques to recover parameters from a Deep Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33671)
*Biprarshi Biswas,Arya Anshuman Ojha,Yubaraj Das,Devansh Khosla,Kamran Rais,Saankhya Samanta,Subhamoy Maitra,Pantelimon Stanica*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究全连接神经网络的"重建"攻击，将密码学式的权重方向提取与基于预测标签的学习相结合，把 ReLU 神经元提取方法推广到 Leaky ReLU，并给出方向恢复、局部距离投票保持、参数误差对齐下的预测变化与独立评分数据符号选择的条件性理论界；实验显示在最大合成架构上 Leaky ReLU 可恢复深层 51–57 个神经元而 ReLU 为零，且在 CIFAR-10 上重建模型与受害模型预测一致率约 56%–58%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型提取/重建工作多依赖查询接口，缺少对权重方向（参数本身）的直接恢复与理论刻画；同时缺乏对激活函数差异（ReLU 与 Leaky ReLU）在提取可行性上的系统性比较。作者希望在特权访问（梯度、隐藏激活、受害权重）条件下厘清"方向提取＋标签学习"两阶段的可行性与局限性，并区分预测一致率与参数还原这两个不同目标。

Method: 方法包含两部分：(1) 将已有的 ReLU 权重方向提取实现适配到 Leaky ReLU，比较多种符号搜索（sign-search）策略，并估计偏置与输出层；(2) 基于预测标签做学习式重建。理论方面给出四类条件性结果：带近似前缀的方向恢复界、局部距离投票保持性、对齐参数误差引起的预测变化界、以及用独立评分数据做符号选择；并给出精确行恢复的规格说明，并将其保证与梯度掩蔽的保证区分开。实验分三组：合成的辅助式提取实验（特权访问梯度、隐藏激活、受害参数）＋标签重建；去掉受害权重访问的符号游走消融；CIFAR-10 受害模型的重建与蒸馏基线对比。

Result: 在最大合成架构、8 个搜索种子上，Leaky ReLU 在最深隐藏层可恢复 51–57 个神经元，ReLU 一个也恢复不到；但该优势在不同架构与层上并不普遍成立。从符号游走中移除受害权重访问后符号精度下降，尽管最终预测一致率仍很高。两个最小合成受害模型上 40 次种子运行的留出 5,000 输入一致率为 0.9890–0.9992。两个 CIFAR-10 受害模型的重建模型分别达到 56.40% 与 58.29% 的一致率，并在三项功能指标上超过蒸馏基线。文中单独报告标签查询次数与特权访问，并解释为何预测一致率并不能标识网络参数。

Conclusion: 该研究表明：在特权访问条件下，方向提取＋标签学习可实现较高的功能级重建（预测一致率），而参数级精确恢复仅在特定架构/层与特定激活（如 Leaky ReLU 在深层）下可行；Leaky ReLU 的可提取性优势并非普适。符号精度对受害权重访问敏感，但高预测一致率与参数还原之间存在本质差距。作者强调应把标签请求与特权访问分开计量，并以功能一致率而非参数还原作为该类攻击的评价口径。

Abstract: We study fully connected neural-network reconstruction by combining cryptanalytic weight-direction extraction with learning from predicted labels. We adapt a ReLU extraction implementation to Leaky ReLU, compare sign-search methods, and estimate biases and output layers. We prove conditional bounds for direction recovery with approximate prefixes, preservation of local distance votes, prediction changes from aligned parameter errors, and sign selection with independent scoring data. We also specify exact row restoration and distinguish its guarantee from that of gradient masking. The experiments evaluate assisted extraction, with privileged access to gradients, hidden activations, and victim parameters, followed by label-based reconstruction. Across eight search seeds on the largest synthetic architecture, Leaky ReLU recovers 51--57 neurons in the deepest hidden layer, while ReLU recovers none. This advantage does not hold uniformly across architectures and layers. Removing victim-weight access from the sign walk gives less accurate signs, despite high final prediction agreement. Across 40 seeded runs on the two smallest synthetic victims, agreement on 5,000 inputs excluded from fitting and selection ranges from $0.9890$ to $0.9992$. On two CIFAR-10 victims, the reconstructed models achieve $56.40\%$ and $58.29\%$ agreement and exceed the distillation baseline on three functional metrics. We report label requests separately from privileged access and explain why prediction agreement does not identify network parameters.

</details>


### [51] [Latent dynamics for reconstructing fine-scale PDE trajectories from coarse observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33722)
*Chenhao Si,Zishan Qian,Shing-Tung Yau,Rongling Wu,Ming Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SLD框架通过时间插值、学习门控的隐状态更新（融合中心差分与五点拉普拉斯算子）及Haar小波合成，从低分辨率PDE观测重建高分辨率轨迹，误差优于ConvLSTM等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粗粒度PDE观测缺失空间细节和演化场的中间状态，需要从时空下采样的仿真数据重建高分辨率轨迹。

Method: 时间插值输入并加入学习的时间校正，用其序列引导粗网格上的隐状态；每次隐状态更新结合学习门控响应、水平/垂直中心差分与五点拉普拉斯（三者具学习到的逐通道系数）；再用两个Haar小波合成分支将隐状态与精化粗输入映射到高分辨率场。

Result: 在二维Gray-Scott、复Ginzburg-Landau和黏性Burgers系统上（25帧输入重建100帧输出，每空间方向8倍细化），SLD的平均相对L2误差与MAE低于参数量相当的非结构化隐动力学和ConvLSTM基线，在留出轨迹与训练轨迹后段窗口均成立；在大梯度区域及多数或全部后段时间区间降低误差；Gray-Scott示例中多数中高波数谱误差更低；移除固定空间响应后误差上升。

Conclusion: 结构化空间算子（中心差分与拉普拉斯）与门控隐状态更新及小波合成的组合有效提升粗观测下的PDE轨迹重建精度，消融验证了固定空间响应的贡献。

Abstract: Coarse PDE observations omit spatial detail and intermediate states of evolving fields. We present Structured Latent Dynamics (SLD), a framework that reconstructs high-resolution trajectories from spatially and temporally downsampled simulations. SLD interpolates the input in time, adds a learned temporal correction and uses the resulting sequence to guide a hidden state on the coarse grid. Each update of the latent state combines a learned gated response with horizontal and vertical central differences and a five-point Laplacian of the state. The three spatial terms have learned channel-wise coefficients. Two Haar wavelet synthesis branches map the hidden state and refined coarse input to the high-resolution field. We evaluate SLD on two-dimensional Gray--Scott, complex Ginzburg--Landau and viscous Burgers systems, reconstructing 100 output frames from 25 input frames with eightfold refinement in each spatial direction. SLD achieves lower mean relative $L_2$ error and mean absolute error than unstructured latent dynamics and ConvLSTM baselines with comparable parameter counts, both on held-out trajectories and on later windows of trajectories used during training. SLD also reduces errors in regions with large reference gradients and across most or all of the evaluated later time intervals. A representative Gray--Scott comparison shows lower spectral error over most intermediate and high wavenumbers. Removing the fixed spatial response increased reconstruction error, supporting its contribution to the SLD architecture.

</details>


### [52] [How Geometry Shapes Generative Model Evaluation on Riemannian Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33731)
*Misha Oberski,Valentijn Oldenburg,Xanthos Kekkos,Soham Chaudhuri,Emma Oosterhuis,Floris de Kam,Sagi Schwartz,Alison Pouplin,Federica Valeau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在黎曼流形上，生成模型评估依赖几何结构：曲率与维度共同决定距离和体积增长。本文系统比较了测地、环境空间与切空间三种版本 Wasserstein 与 MMD 在不同几何下的表现，发现不存在普适可靠的距离度量，并据此给出评估距离报告、度量类型比较与几何标定的实用建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流形上的生成模型（如 Riemannian Flow Matching）日益普及，但评估指标多在欧氏空间设计。在弯曲流形上，曲率与维度共同影响距离与体积增长，使得评估结果对所选距离定义敏感；现有工作缺乏对测地、环境、切空间三类距离的系统比较，导致评估结论的可比性与可靠性存疑。

Method: 构建统一框架，在多种几何（不同曲率与维度）上系统对比 Wasserstein 与 MMD 的三种实现：测地距离版本、环境空间（ambient）版本与切空间（tangent-space）版本。实验采用受控噪声扰动样本与 Riemannian Flow Matching 生成样本，考察度量间一致性（metric agreement）、噪声敏感性，以及二者与几何诱导体积缩放之间的关系。

Result: 没有任何一种度量在所有几何下都可靠：(1) 评估距离的选择会显著改变所测得的分布差异数值；(2) 噪声敏感性随曲率与维度变化，不同度量表现不一致；(3) 度量间的一致性与体积缩放效应相关，说明差异部分源于几何而非样本质量本身。

Conclusion: 流形生成模型的评估必须显式考虑几何，距离类型不是无关的实现细节。作者给出实践建议：报告时明确说明评估距离的类型与设定；比较时同时汇报多种度量类型而非单一指标；依据样本所处几何（曲率、维度、体积增长）对度量进行标定与解读。

Abstract: On Riemannian manifolds, generative-model evaluation is geometry-dependent since curvature and dimension jointly determine distances and volume growth. Our framework systematically compares geodesic, ambient, and tangent-space versions of Wasserstein and MMD across different geometries. Using controlled-noise perturbations and Riemannian Flow Matching samples, we study metric agreement, noise sensitivity, and their relation to geometry-induced volume scaling. We find that no metric is uniformly reliable: evaluation distance can affect both measured discrepancies and noise sensitivity, varying across curvature and dimension. Our analysis leads to practical recommendations for reporting evaluation distances, comparing metric types, and calibrating metrics to the geometry of the samples.

</details>


### [53] [Polynomial Residual Networks are Generically Identifiable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33738)
*Isabel Dahlgren,Giovanni Luca Marchetti,Kathlén Kohn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了多项式激活、带偏置的恒定宽度残差网络的参数可辨识性：度数≥5且系数一般的激活，泛型参数原像为单点；度数≥3且可平移消去线性项的激活，泛型原像由偏置平移+实单位根缩放给出；同法还给出ResNet参数化临界点的必要条件（奇异学习理论核心对象）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 残差网络（ResNet）被广泛使用，但其参数化与函数之间的映射是否可辨识（同一函数是否对应唯一或有限个参数）在理论上不清楚。可辨识性是理解模型复杂度、奇异学习理论（SLT）中Fisher信息与实对数典则阈值（RLCT）等分析的前提，而已有结果多针对浅层或无非线性偏置的网络。本文试图对恒定宽度、多项式激活、含偏置的ResNet给出严格的纤维（fiber）结构刻画。

Method: 采用代数/解析方法研究参数到函数的映射的纤维。对多项式激活（含偏置项），通过在输入端做适当平移以规范形式，利用多项式恒等比较与系数匹配来约束参数；借助"平移偏置后以实单位根缩放权重与偏置"这一对称结构分类纤维；并将同一证明技巧用于ResNet参数化的临界点方程，推导其必要条件。最后说明结果可退化到无偏置（bias-free）网络的情形。

Result: （1）激活度数≥5且系数一般：泛型参数对应的纤维是单点，即局部可辨识到参数本身。（2）激活度数≥3且存在输入平移使线性项消失：泛型纤维恰为"偏置平移 + 以实单位根缩放权重和（平移后的）偏置"所生成的有限轨道。（3）结果对无偏置网络给出专门化形式。（4）用同一技巧得到ResNet参数化临界点的必要条件，可用于奇异学习理论。

Conclusion: 多项式激活的恒定宽度ResNet在泛型参数处具有强的可辨识性：高次激活近乎完全可辨识，较低次激活的不可辨识性完全由平移-实单位根缩放对称性解释，无额外隐藏对称。该纤维分类为奇异学习理论提供了结构性工具，并给出临界点的必要条件。

Abstract: We prove identifiability results for constant-width residual networks with polynomial activations and bias terms. For activations of degree at least five with generic coefficients, the fiber over a generic parameter is a singleton. For activations of degree at least three with no linear term after a suitable translation of the input, the generic fiber is given by translating biases and then scaling weights and translated biases by real roots of unity. These results specialize to networks without bias terms. With the same proof technique, we further derive necessary conditions for critical points of the ResNet parametrization, that are central in singular learning theory.

</details>


### [54] [A quantum k-nearest neighbors implementation using magnitude-aware comparisons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33739)
*Christopher Rector,Jiangwen Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种实用的量子编码技术，通过在向量末尾追加单个分量，使不同L2范数的向量之间可以直接计算欧氏距离，并将其用于量子KNN（QKNN）。该方法更易理解与实现、使用更少量子比特，并在含噪声模拟中优于同类欧氏距离QKNN方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子KNN（QKNN）近年来被视为量子算法的典型案例研究，已有多种基于相似度度量的QKNN方案。但现有方法在编码上较为复杂、所需量子比特较多，且难以在真实噪声条件下保持电路相干性，因此需要一种更简洁、更省资源且实用的欧氏距离编码方案。

Method: 提出一种量子编码技巧：为每个向量追加一个额外分量，从而在向量L2范数不同的情况下仍可直接计算欧氏距离；将该编码应用于QKNN问题，并给出算法实现，在标准带标签数据集上与近期一种欧氏距离QKNN方法进行基准对比。

Result: 与同类方法相比，该编码更简单、更易实现且占用更少量子比特；在标准标注数据集上的基准测试表明其正确构造了问题形式，并且在贴近真实噪声的模拟中，得益于更少的量子比特和更高的电路相干性，性能较同类实现有显著提升。

Conclusion: 通过追加单分量的量子编码可简洁地实现不同范数向量间的欧氏距离计算，降低量子比特开销并提升噪声下的电路相干性，是一种实用且优于同类实现的QKNN方案。

Abstract: Much attention has recently been devoted to the k-nearest neighbors (KNN) algorithm as a case study for quantum computing algorithms. Several algorithms have devised methods to compute a similarity metric to solve Quantum KNN (QKNN). This paper presents a practical quantum encoding technique that enables Euclidean distance calculation between vectors with different L2 norms by appending a single component to each vector, then uses it in the QKNN problem. It is arguably simpler to understand and implement than comparable methods and uses fewer qubits. Further, an implementation of this algorithm is provided and is used to benchmark it against a recent Euclidean-distance QKNN method on standard labeled datasets. In addition to demonstrating proper formulation, the results show marked improvement over similar implementations during simulation with realistic noise due to the use of fewer qubits and thus more coherent circuits.

</details>


### [55] [A Distribution-Matching Perspective on LLM Training Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33740)
*Yanxing Huang,Bingzhang Yan,Zihan Tang,Wenzhe Li,Peng Li,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出一个基于"分布匹配"的统一框架，将大模型各阶段训练算法统一解释为最小化当前模型与目标分布之间的某种散度，并据此分析OPD/KD的优劣条件、目标分布退化机制，进而提出CKL正则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代大模型训练流程中算法繁多（预训练、蒸馏、强化学习等），缺乏统一的理论视角；同时现有RL算法长期行为（如目标分布退化、模式塌缩）缺少解释，需要一种能刻画目标分布性质的分析框架来指导算法设计与改进。

Method: 以分布匹配视角构建统一框架：将一大类训练算法视为最小化当前模型与目标分布间的某一散度，通过刻画目标分布的性质推断对应算法行为；分析目标分布的具体形式以比较OPD与经典KD；在目标分布依赖被优化模型时分析估计方差累积导致的目标分布确定性退化；据此设计Conditional KL（CKL）正则项。

Result: 理论分析表明：当当前模型分布接近目标分布时，On-Policy Distillation（OPD）优于经典Knowledge Distillation（KD），而当两者相距较远时则相反；当目标分布依赖于被优化模型时，估计方差的累积会引起目标分布的确定性退化，这在一定程度上解释了现有RL算法的长期行为。CKL正则可缓解目标分布退化，在放宽经典KL正则对奖励提升的约束的同时有效保持模式多样性。

Conclusion: 分布匹配视角可为大模型训练算法提供统一的理论理解与实用指导，CKL正则是一种兼顾模式多样性与奖励优化的有效手段，该视角有望为后续研究提供方向。

Abstract: In this work, we propose a unified framework for training algorithms across different stages of modern large language model development, based on the perspective of distribution matching. We show that a broad class of language model training algorithms can be interpreted as minimizing a certain divergence between the current model and a target distribution, and that characterizing the properties of this target distribution provides substantial insight into the behavior of the corresponding training algorithms. By analyzing specific forms of the target distribution, we establish that On-Policy Distillation (OPD) enjoys an advantage over classical Knowledge Distillation (KD) when the current model distribution is close to the target distribution, while exhibiting a disadvantage when the two distributions are far apart. Furthermore, when the target distribution itself depends on the model being optimized, we show that the accumulation of estimation variance can induce a deterministic degradation of the target distribution. These results provide a theoretical explanation, at least in part, for the long-term behavior of existing Reinforcement Learning (RL) algorithms. With this intuition we further designed Conditional KL (CKL) regularization for mitigating the degradation of the target distribution. CKL can effectively preserve mode diversity while relaxing the constraint imposed by classical KL regularization on reward improvements. We believe this theoretical perspective can bring some new understanding of language model training algorithms and provide useful guidance for future research.

</details>


### [56] [CFL-aware edge design for graph neural solvers of hyperbolic transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33769)
*Jinyoung Jeong,Joseph B. Choi,Xinlun Cheng,H. S. Udaykumar,Sanghun Choi,Stephen S. Baek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在固定GNN架构下，仅改变边的信息形式，发现将CFL数作为边局部输运信号注入时，只有当边表示已分离"交互方向"与"空间尺度"时才有效，可将末步误差降低10.4–14.4%，且步长越大收益越明显，其机制是抑制激波附近的振荡而非抹平解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双曲PDE以有限速度沿特征线输运信息，易产生激波与间断，数值上难以捕捉。GNN的邻域聚合本身无法告知信息需传播多远、沿哪条边传播，因此当推理步长大于训练步长时，激波前沿被抹平、振荡加剧。作者希望探究：把输运尺度显式化为边局部CFL信号，能否提升GNN对双曲PDE的建模能力。

Method: 采用受控消融设计，保持网络架构不变，仅改变每条边所获信息的表示形式：从原始边形式，逐步引入方向、长度、波速，显式暴露边局部输运交互，再考察附加CFL数的效果。由于各形式由相同要素计算，变化的是信息形式而非信息量，从而可分离"特征数量"与"表示结构"的贡献。

Result: 特征数量并非限制因素；将CFL直接拼接到原始边形式上几乎无收益；当边信息已显式暴露输运交互时，CFL条件化最有效，末步误差降低10.4–14.4%，且步长增大时改善幅度扩大。误差下降来自激波附近振荡的抑制，而非解的抹平。

Conclusion: 提出明确的设计原则：CFL条件化在边表示已将交互方向与空间尺度分离时最有效，此时CFL值才有良定义的被调制量，为面向双曲PDE的GNN边特征设计提供了可操作指导。

Abstract: Hyperbolic partial differential equations (PDEs) transport information at finite speed along characteristics, often producing shocks and discontinuities that are challenging to capture numerically. Graph neural networks (GNNs) offer learned update rules, but neighbor aggregation alone does not tell a GNN how far information must travel and along which edge. As a result, when a GNN model takes a time step larger than what was used in training, shock fronts smear and oscillations grow. In this work, we ask whether making the scale of transport explicit as an edge-local Courant-Friedrichs-Lewy (CFL) signal can enhance GNNs' ability to model hyperbolic PDEs. Holding the architecture fixed, we vary only the information given to each edge, from a raw edge form to forms that progressively expose the edge-local transport interaction through direction, length, and wave speed. Because all of these are computed from the same ingredients, what changes is not the amount of information but its form. The results show a consistent pattern, in which feature count is not the limiting factor, and appending CFL directly to a raw edge form produces almost no benefit. The CFL conditioning proves most effective when the edge information already exposes the transport interaction, reducing final-step error by 10.4-14.4%, and the improvement widens as the step size grows larger. Notably, this improvement arises not from smearing the solution but from suppressing oscillations near shocks. These results point to a specific design principle that CFL conditioning is most effective when the edge representation already separates the direction of interaction from spatial scale, so that the CFL value has a well-defined quantity to modulate.

</details>


### [57] [Greedy Orthogonal Polynomial Manifolds with Lipschitz Control for Dimensionality Reduction and Reduced Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33784)
*Tomoki Koike,Benjamin Peherstorfer,Elizabeth Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出用训练数据上正交的特征表示多项式解码项，实现贪心算法Greedy-OPM，在解码器Lipschitz常数约束下联合选择编码器模态与多项式特征，兼顾重构精度与离数据泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多项式流形通过多项式解码器提供非线性低维表示，但解码器的多项项缺乏良好条件数控制，导致在训练数据之外的泛化能力差，需要一种既保持重构精度又能约束模型平滑性的构造方式。

Method: 在训练数据上构造经验正交的多项式特征来表示解码器的多项式项；基于该正交性设计贪心算法Greedy-OPM，在解码器Lipschitz常数满足给定上界的条件下，联合选择编码器模态与多项式特征以最小化重构误差。

Result: 数值实验表明，Lipschitz约束在保持较高重构精度的同时，改善了模型在训练数据之外的泛化表现。

Conclusion: 经验正交特征使贪心选择可行且高效，Lipschitz约束是控制多项式流形离数据泛化的有效手段。

Abstract: Polynomial manifolds provide nonlinear low-dimensional representations through polynomial decoders. We propose to represent the polynomial terms of the decoder using features that are orthonormal on the training data. This empirical orthogonality enables a greedy algorithm, Greedy-OPM, which jointly selects encoder modes and polynomial features while minimizing the reconstruction error under a prescribed bound on the decoder's Lipschitz constant. Numerical experiments show that this Lipschitz constraint improves generalization away from the training data while retaining high reconstruction accuracy.

</details>


### [58] [Memory or a Clock? Auditing a Memory Advantage in XLand-MiniGrid with Seeds, Maps and Evaluation Noise Apart](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33816)
*Rodrigo Reveron-Fonseca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在XLand-MiniGrid部分可观测任务上，LSTM相对无记忆MLP的25个百分点差距，主要源于"知道时间步"而非记忆：噪声分析显示二元"学会"判据76%为评估噪声，而给予时间步后无记忆MLP差距缩至3.3点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文针对两个方法论痛点：(1) 强化学习对比实验中测得的性能差距有多少是训练种子、地图与评估随机性造成的噪声，尤其是常用的二元"任务是否学会"判据的可靠性；(2) 在部分可观测任务中，LSTM相对无记忆基线的优势是否真的归因于记忆能力。

Method: 在部分可观测的XLand-MiniGrid任务上进行分解式随机性分析：60次运行、每次重复评估3次，分离训练种子、地图与评估噪声；在新地图的封存测试面板（仅开启一次）上做对照实验——与LSTM同规模的无记忆MLP（分别有无时间步输入）、四分之一规模MLP、以及每步清空状态的LSTM；并对训练后LSTM的隐状态做线性探针以检验时间步信息。

Result: 约10个配对种子即可检出10个百分点差距；二元"任务习得"判据中76%为评估噪声，同样差距需约100个种子。封存面板上，同规模无记忆MLP在给定时间步时与LSTM差距仅3.3点，且无时间步时增加宽度无可测收益；给定时间步后，四分之一规模MLP和每步清空状态的LSTM差距也仅约5点；线性探针可从LSTM状态中读出时间步。

Conclusion: 该任务上的表观记忆优势大部分实为"知晓时间"的优势，原因在于回合按固定上限截断且奖励随时间衰减。建议为无记忆基线提供时间步输入，并公开实验协议、事件驱动工具与全部检查点。

Abstract: On a partially observable XLand-MiniGrid task, an LSTM agent beats a memory-free MLP by 25 percentage points. The gap looks like a clear case of memory. We ask two questions about it. First, how much of a measured gap is noise? Separating training seed, map and evaluation randomness in 60 runs, each re-evaluated three times, we find that about ten paired seeds detect a 10-point gap in final success. The binary "task acquired" verdict, a common summary of learning curves, is 76% evaluation noise and would need about a hundred seeds for the same gap. Second, is the gap memory? On a sealed panel of new maps, opened once, a memory-free MLP of the LSTM's size comes within 3.3 points of the LSTM when it is given the time step, and gains no measurable benefit from its width without it. With the time step, an MLP a quarter of the LSTM's size, and the LSTM with its state erased at every step, also come within about 5 points. A linear probe reads the time step from the trained LSTM's state. On this task, most of the apparent memory advantage is an advantage of knowing the time, which matters because episodes are cut at a fixed limit and the reward shrinks with time. We recommend giving memory-free baselines the time step, and we release the protocol, the event-driven tooling and every checkpoint.

</details>


### [59] [Learning What to Share: Expert Specialization for Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33822)
*Bashar Khoury,Shadi Omari,Assaf Schuster*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文通过受控实验证明，多变量预测中专家分配（而非单纯增加容量）带来增益，并提出可插拔的 Persistent Closed-Form Routing (PCR) 方法，在冻结骨干上显著降低 MSE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量预测的核心挑战在于决定跨通道共享什么、在何处专门化。混合专家模型虽提供灵活共享，但不清楚其收益来自增加的容量还是专家分配本身。

Method: 1) 在 11 个数据集、3 个骨干网络上进行控制对比，比较信息性通道分配与匹配共享容量、随机分配；2) 冻结 Transformer 骨干上对比持久通道专属混合与输入相关路由；3) 构建 GalaxyTS 合成数据集（含精确共享与通道专属参考）以分析专门化的时机与幅度；4) 提出 PCR：在冻结预训练预测器周围，交替进行通道专属混合权重的精确条件估计与低秩残差专家的梯度训练。

Result: 信息性通道分配优于匹配共享容量与随机分配；冻结骨干上持久通道混合平均优于输入相关路由；GalaxyTS 揭示持久通道混合能利用短输入窗口所掩盖的预测差异；PCR-K4 相对三个冻结骨干在四个预测长度上取得 3.86–14.25% 的数据集均衡 MSE 降低，且在匹配对比中平均优于竞争专门化方法，可训练参数显著更少。

Conclusion: 专门化收益主要源于有信息的通道分配而非容量；持久通道专属混合是有竞争力的设计；PCR 作为即插即用方法在参数量更少的前提下实现更优的多变量预测性能。

Abstract: A central challenge in multivariate forecasting is deciding what to share across channels and where to specialize. Mixture-of-experts models offer flexible sharing, yet it remains unclear how much of their forecasting gains comes from added capacity or expert allocation. Through controlled comparisons across eleven datasets and three backbones, we show that informative channel assignments improve forecasting beyond matched shared capacity and random allocation. On frozen transformer backbones, persistent channel-specific mixtures outperform input-dependent routing on average. To examine when and how much specialization helps, we introduce GalaxyTS, a controlled synthetic dataset with exact shared and channel-specific forecasting references. It reveals how persistent channel mixtures exploit predictive differences concealed by short input windows and measures gains over matched shared corrections. These insights motivate Persistent Closed-Form Routing (PCR), a plug-and-play method that alternates exact conditional estimation of channel-specific mixture weights with gradient-based training of low-rank residual experts around a frozen pretrained forecaster. Averaged over four forecasting horizons, PCR-K4 achieves dataset-balanced mean squared error reductions of 3.86-14.25% relative to the three frozen backbones. In matched comparisons, PCR outperforms competing specialization methods on average while requiring substantially fewer trainable parameters.

</details>


### [60] [Reactive Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33826)
*Xingjian Bai,Jia-Hao Liang,Jayden Teoh,Vincent Sitzmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 OPAL：一种"反应式优化器"，让预训练语言模型智能体在单次训练过程中检查训练动态并改写可执行的优化器代码，从而按参数组调整更新规则与调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优化器与训练配方通常需针对目标设定人工调参，且常规元学习/迁移方案依赖优化器专用元训练或跨任务记忆；作者希望在"配方未针对目标场景调优"的情况下，实现无需额外元训练、训练中自适应的优化器。

Method: 使用预训练语言模型智能体读取训练动态（如损失/梯度等信号），直接重写可执行的优化器代码；可针对单个参数组修改更新规则与调度；不依赖优化器专属元训练，也不使用其他训练轮次迁移的记忆。在语言建模与图像生成任务上评估，并辅以受控实验（重复数据损坏、过拟合场景）及智能体计算开销与实现需求分析。

Result: 在所评估的各设定中，OPAL 达到或优于选定的固定配方；其对优化器决策的分析显示，改动集中在学习率调度、优化器结构、数值精度与动量方面；受控实验还显示其能适应重复数据损坏并降低过拟合；同时报告了智能体的计算成本与实现要求。

Conclusion: 以 LM 智能体在线改写优化器代码是一种可行的自适应优化范式，可部分替代人工配方调优；但需权衡智能体带来的额外计算开销与工程实现成本。

Abstract: We study a reactive optimizer that uses a pretrained language-model agent to inspect training dynamics and rewrite executable optimizer code during a single training run. The agent can change update rules and schedules for individual parameter groups, without optimizer-specific meta-training or memory transferred from other runs. We evaluate this approach, OPAL, on language modeling and image generation when a recipe has not been tuned for the target setting. Across the evaluated settings, it matches or improves on selected fixed recipes. Analysis of its decisions shows changes to learning-rate schedules, optimizer structure, numerical precision, and momentum. Controlled experiments also show adaptation to repeated data corruption and reduced overfitting. We report these behaviors together with the agent's computational cost and implementation requirements.

</details>


### [61] [EEGBiFormer: Real-Time EEG Attention Classification via Hybrid CNN-Transformer with EEG-Informed Auditory Protocol Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33832)
*Isabella Guan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EEGBiFormer混合CNN-Transformer模型，基于16通道EEG实现注意状态（专注/非专注）的实时二分类，并结合自研采集板、频谱与连通性分析、以及EEG引导的音频协议完成端到端闭环验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续注意力对学业表现至关重要，但现有听觉辅助工具是静态的、缺乏实时神经证据的支撑，无法根据个体当前注意状态进行自适应调节。

Method: 自研模拟前端采集板构建任务态EEG数据集；先用功率谱密度（PSD）与相位锁值（PLV）做频谱与连通性分析以确认两类状态的神经特征差异；再训练CNN-Transformer混合模型EEGBiFormer进行窗口级二分类；最后用模型输出的专注置信度对听觉刺激排序，生成EEG-informed音频协议，并在37名被试的配对组内行为实验中与静音对照比较。

Result: 窗口级验证准确率91.3%、F1为94.5%；17个独立session的记录级准确率88.2%；端到端系统延迟低于100 ms；音频协议使综合注意力得分较静音对照显著提升20%，效应量为中到大。

Conclusion: 工作构成从自研EEG硬件、神经信号分析、深度学习分类到循证听觉协议设计的完整pipeline，验证了实时神经反馈驱动听觉干预的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Sustained attention is critical for academic performance, yet existing auditory support tools are static and not grounded in real-time neural evidence. This paper presents EEGBiFormer, a hybrid CNN-Transformer model for realtime binary classification of attention states, focus versus nonfocus, from 16-channel EEG signals. A custom analog front-end acquisition board was designed and built to collect a task-based EEG dataset. Before model training, spectral and connectivity analysis, using Power Spectral Density and Phase Locking Value, confirmed distinct neural signatures between attention states, providing physiological grounding for the classification approach. EEGBiFormer achieved 91.3 percent window-level validation accuracy with an F1 score of 94.5 percent, and 88.2 percent recording-level accuracy across 17 independent sessions. End-to-end system latency was measured below 100 ms, supporting real-time responsiveness. The trained model was further used to objectively rank auditory stimuli by focus-state confidence, yielding EEG-informed audio protocols. In a paired within-subject behavioral study with 37 participants, these protocols produced a statistically significant 20 percent improvement in composite attention scores over silent controls, with a medium-to-large effect size. This work demonstrates a complete pipeline from custom EEG hardware through neural signal analysis, deep learning classification, and evidence-based auditory protocol design.

</details>


### [62] [Hessian-Similar Optimization with Capped Participation: Lower Bounds and Virtual Masters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33835)
*Rui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究每轮最多接触 m 个客户端中 s 个时的 Hessian-similar 优化轮复杂度，给出零尊重局部算法的下界，并提出虚拟主节点 SVRS 方法在二次问题上达到对 δ_a 无关的期望轮复杂度，上下界在 s≤√m 与 s=Ω(m) 区间多项式阶匹配，最坏差距 O(m^{1/8})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有复杂度结果假设主节点免费或全客户端参与，无法刻画每轮至多 s 个客户端的严格上限；同时希望去除对较差锚点 Hessian 质量（δ_a）的依赖。

Method: 1) 引入隐藏客户端链构造，为具有自适应队列与随机停时的零尊重局部算法建立下界；2) 提出虚拟主节点 SVRS：使用相互独立的估计子队列与近端子队列，并对近端回复做平均，再结合 Catalyst 加速。

Result: 强凸二次、高精度高异质区间下，s≤(m−2)/2 时需 Ω((m/s)√(min{δ_a, δ̄/√m}/μ)·log(1/ε)) 轮；虚拟主节点 SVRS 在强凸客户端二次问题上以 Õ(m/s+(√(m/s)+m^{3/4}/s)√(δ̄/μ)) 期望轮达到相对间隙 ε，且与 δ_a 无关；与既有算法计费分析结合，在 s≤√m 与 s=Ω(m) 时匹配下界多项式阶，最坏差距 O(m^{1/8})。12 种子合成实验显示，在 10 个 dip 族不完整队列单元中 9 个轮数少于调优 SVRP，其他 30 个单元中仅 2 个更优。

Conclusion: 严格 s-上限下的轮复杂度由 δ̄/μ、m/s 共同决定；虚拟主节点设计可消除锚点依赖并接近下界。下界仅适用于零尊重算法，不涵盖不受限消息模型，留作开放问题。

Abstract: What is the round complexity of Hessian-similar optimization when each round can contact at most $s$ of $m$ clients? Prior rates permit a free master or full participation and do not characterize strict caps. We count every one-vector query and reply. A hidden-client chain gives a lower bound for zero-respecting local algorithms with adaptive cohorts and random stopping: on strongly convex quadratics, the required rounds are $Ω((m/s)\sqrt{\min\{δ_a,\barδ/\sqrt m\}/μ}\,\log(1/ε))$ for $s\leq(m-2)/2$ in the high-accuracy, high-dissimilarity regime. Here $μ$ is strong convexity, while $δ_a$ and $\barδ$ bound anchor and average Hessian variation. To remove dependence on a poor anchor, virtual-master SVRS uses independent estimator and proximal cohorts and averages the proximal replies. With Catalyst, it reaches relative gap $ε$ in $\tilde O(m/s+(\sqrt{m/s}+m^{3/4}/s)\sqrt{\barδ/μ})$ expected rounds on quadratics with strongly convex clients, independent of $δ_a$. Combining this method with charged analyses of existing algorithms matches the lower bound's polynomial dependence for $s\leq\sqrt m$ and $s=Ω(m)$; the worst gap is $O(m^{1/8})$. A held-out 12-seed synthetic study finds fewer rounds than tuned SVRP in nine of ten dip-family incomplete-cohort cells, versus two of 30 other cells. The lower bounds concern zero-respecting algorithms and do not cover unrestricted messages.

</details>


### [63] [Sliding Networks: A Family of Residual Structures Preserving Feature Learning and Feature Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33840)
*Datong Zhou,Blake Bordelon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种"联合缩放配方"（joint scaling recipe），在同时放大残差宽度 N_♭、隐层宽度 N_♯ 与深度 L 时，既保持局部最大特征学习（feature learning），又保留特征多样性（feature diversity)。关键量为极限比 N_♭/(N_♯L)→α，相关初始化（Half Copy）可部分放宽由此产生的形状约束；理论与残差MLP实验支持在固定正 α 下收敛到具有局部最大特征学习的 SDE 极限，并可在保持该极限的前提下于宽度与深度间重新分配隐藏计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在大宽度、大深度下表现最佳，缩放定律研究推动了宽度与深度的放大流程，需保持训练动力学的稳定性与计算效率。现有深度缩放方法被迫在"保留局部最大特征学习"与"保留由通道随机差异产生的残差流特征多样性"之间二选一，本文希望同时满足二者。

Method: 引入联合缩放配方：以残差宽度 N_♭、隐层宽度 N_♯、深度 L 为参数，核心控制量为极限比 N_♭/(N_♯L)→α；采用相关初始化（Half Copy）以部分放松形状约束；通过理论分析与残差 MLP 实验验证极限行为，并在语言模型上做固定参数量下的重新分配（regrouping）实验。

Result: 理论推导与残差 MLP 实验表明：在固定正 α 下收敛到具有局部最大特征学习的 SDE 极限；隐藏计算量可在宽度与深度之间重新分配（"旋律变和声再变回"）而保持该极限；但在语言模型实验中，固定参数总量下的重新分配仍出现性能差异。

Conclusion: 研究区分了"逼近某一极限（ODE 或 SDE）"与"为更优性能而缩放"两件事：保持极限不变并不保证性能不变或提升。联合缩放配方在理论极限层面统一了特征学习与特征多样性，但实际性能仍受宽度/深度分配方式影响。

Abstract: Deep learning models tend to perform best at large widths and depths. The growing interest in scaling laws, especially in the language modeling domain, has motivated procedures for scaling up network widths and depths in order to preserve stability and computational efficiency of training dynamics. Existing depth-scaling approaches force a choice between retaining locally maximal \emph{feature learning} and preserving a form of \emph{feature diversity} in the residual stream arising from random differences between channels. We examine a \textit{joint scaling recipe} that preserves both. With residual width $N_\flat$, hidden width $N_\sharp$, and depth $L$, the key quantity is, by default, the limiting ratio $N_\flat/(N_\sharp L)\toα$, while correlated initialization (Half Copy) can partially relax the resulting shape constraints. Theory and residual-MLP experiments support convergence at fixed positive $α$ to an SDE limit with locally maximal feature learning. Hidden computations can then be redistributed between width and depth while preserving this limit, like turning melody into harmony and back. Language-model experiments nevertheless show performance differences under regrouping at fixed parameter count. These results distinguish approximating a limit (ODE or SDE) from scaling for better performance.

</details>


### [64] [Approximate Universal Prediction under Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33842)
*Richard Cornelius Suwandi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究"规划器反馈"下贝叶斯混合（含Solomonoff归纳）近似保证的存续问题：转移系数可分解为策略似然比与任务证据之积，稀有任务与稀有动作分支会复合放大超额预测误差（阶为κη2^{K(μ)}，且为sharp），有界回报下价值误差由"低覆盖分支进入概率+η的平方根项"控制，并给出deferral决策保持的margin与条件KL预算（含逆历史概率惩罚）及反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯混合与Solomonoff归纳是AIXI等通用智能体的理想化预测模型，其保证基于最小化总体对数损失。但实际智能体用近似预测器做规划，而规划本身决定了预测器在何处被评估，导致规划器被引向其模型偏乐观的分支——即预测器与规划器之间的反馈回路。作者要回答：哪些近似保证能在这种反馈下依然成立。

Method: 1) 对归一化有限时域因果混合，推导转移系数的因子分解：策略似然比（衡量动作分支稀有性，由覆盖度κ刻画）× 每次预测前可得的任务证据（任务稀有性），说明二者可复合。2) 在单一固定通用混合下构造无穷多可计算环境，给出超额预测误差阶κη2^{K(μ)}（η为训练KL预算，K(μ)为环境复杂度），证明该阶sharp，且对每个目标存在可计算预测器在合适有限时域达到该阶，从而说明可计算性本身不能消除惩罚。3) 对有界回报，给出价值误差上界：进入低覆盖分支的概率 + O(√η)项，并论证两项均必要，且不需要有限的最大覆盖比。4) 受Golden Handcuffs的mentor override启发，推导保持deferral决策的margin与条件KL预算，含不可避免的逆历史概率惩罚；构造反例区分deferral决策一致性与逐任务事件规避。5) 保证适用于任何满足显式总体近似条件与覆盖条件的预测器架构。

Result: 得到转移系数的显式因子分解与复合放大机制；超额预测误差阶κη2^{K(μ)}的sharp性与可计算预测器可达性；有界回报下价值误差的"低覆盖分支概率+√η"双项刻画及两项必要性；deferral决策保持的margin与条件KL预算（含逆历史概率惩罚）；以及说明deferral一致性与任务级事件规避不等价的反例。

Conclusion: 规划-预测反馈会破坏部分近似保证：稀有任务与稀有动作分支的复合可使超额误差随环境复杂度指数增长，可计算性无法免疫。但有界回报下价值误差仍可由覆盖缺口与√η项控制，且无需有限最大覆盖比。所给保证对满足明确总体近似与覆盖条件的任意预测器架构成立，为理想化通用智能体的实现提供了具体设计目标。

Abstract: Bayesian mixtures, and Solomonoff induction in particular, provide an idealized model of prediction learned by minimizing population log loss, and they underlie universal agents such as AIXI. An agent that plans with an approximation of such a predictor decides where that predictor is evaluated, and a planner can be drawn toward branches on which its model is optimistic. We study which approximation guarantees survive this feedback. For normalized finite-horizon causal mixtures, the transfer coefficient factors into a policy likelihood ratio and task evidence available before each prediction, so task rarity and action-branch rarity can compound. Within one fixed universal mixture, infinitely many computable environments admit excess prediction error of order $κη\,2^{K(μ)}$, where $η$ is the training KL budget and $κ$ measures policy coverage. This order is sharp, and for each target a computable predictor attains it at a suitable finite horizon, so computability alone does not remove the penalty. For bounded returns, value error is controlled by the probability of entering a poorly covered branch plus a square-root term in $η$. Both terms are necessary, and finite maximum coverage ratios are not required. Motivated by the mentor override of Golden Handcuffs, we derive margins for preserving deferral decisions and conditional KL budgets with an unavoidable inverse-history-probability penalty, and a counterexample separates agreement of deferral decisions from task-wise event avoidance. The guarantees apply to any predictor architecture that meets explicit population approximation and coverage conditions, which gives concrete targets for implementing idealized universal agents.

</details>


### [65] [When is early stopping beneficial under label noise? A phase diagram for kernel gradient flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33846)
*Fabrizio Boncoraglio,Lenka Zdeborová*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文质疑"存在不可约标签噪声时必须在插值前早停"的传统观点：深层大网络在完全拟合随机标签后测试误差仍不劣化，并在可解设定下给出解释与相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早停常被用来防止拟合标签噪声。若训练集中有恒定比例的内在不可预测标签，似乎最优测试误差必然出现在插值之前。作者希望通过实验与理论检验这一隐含假设是否普遍成立。

Method: 在CIFAR10上施加随机标签，比较固定特征模型、浅层模型与更大更深卷积网络的训练/测试曲线；在合成的含噪分类任务上分析核梯度流，推导高维精确学习曲线，并按线性核成分（学习输入-标签共享关系）与高阶成分（拟合标签噪声）的平衡刻画相图。

Result: 浅层与固定特征模型出现后期测试误差恶化（需早停）；大型深层卷积网络即使已正确分类所有随机标签，早停需求基本消失。理论上得到三种情形：一种需在有限时刻早停；一种泛化持续改善至插值但端点次优；在二者边界上，插值端点在该核族与所有训练时刻中取得最低泛化误差。

Conclusion: 即使存在大量不可约标签噪声，插值也可优于早停。神经网络实验中"学习到的表示如何获得类似有利几何"仍是开放问题。

Abstract: Early stopping is commonly used to prevent fitting label noise from degrading generalization. Does this mean that, when a positive fraction of the training labels is intrinsically unpredictable, the best test error must occur before interpolation? Experiments on a CIFAR10 classification task with randomly labeled examples motivate the opposite possibility: although fixed-feature and shallow models exhibit late-time deterioration of the test error, the need for early stopping essentially disappears in larger, deeper convolutional networks even after the random labels have all been classified correctly. We determine when late-time deterioration disappears entirely in a solvable setting. For kernel gradient flow on a synthetic noisy classification task, we derive sharp high-dimensional learning curves and obtain a phase diagram controlled by the balance between the linear kernel component that learns the shared relation between inputs and labels and the higher-order components that enable fitting the label noise. In one regime, generalization is optimized at finite time and early stopping is necessary. In another, generalization improves until interpolation, but the endpoint is suboptimal across kernels. At the boundary, the interpolating endpoint attains the lowest generalization error across the considered kernel family and all training times. Thus, even with extensive irreducible label noise, interpolation can outperform early stopping. The neural-network experiments leave open how learned representations acquire an analogous favorable geometry.

</details>


### [66] [Stable Dimensionality Reduction via Isometric Centroid-Encoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33872)
*Tomojit Ghosh,Siva Rama Krishna Prasad Changala,Prathyusha Kanakamalla,Harigovind Harikumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出等距质心编码器（ICE），在潜空间中学习类别质心并保持预设的类别质心成对几何结构，同时提出质心邻域稳定性（CNS）指标度量嵌入可复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有监督降维方法只强调类内紧凑与类间分离，未显式约束类别几何结构，导致独立运行间类别的相对排布存在差异，降低低维嵌入的可复现性与可解释性。

Method: ICE 框架：在潜空间直接学习类质心，并显式正则化潜类质心的相对排布以保持给定的成对质心几何；与样本级度量学习不同，约束对象为类质心，避免大量样本对/三元组约束。理论分析证明 M 个类的质心距离构型所需内在维数至多为 M−1。提出 CNS 指标衡量独立运行间类级邻域拓扑一致性。

Result: 在图像与生物数据集上，ICE 生成稳定嵌入，类质心几何保持程度提升，计算效率良好；M−1 上界解释了距离保持度量随潜维数增加而饱和的现象；对优化动态、敏感性与预处理的附加分析给出潜维数与显式几何建模的实践启示。

Conclusion: 显式建模类质心几何可提升监督降维嵌入的可复现性、可解释性与稳定性，且计算开销低；CNS 可作为现有嵌入质量度量的补充。

Abstract: Many supervised dimensionality reduction methods promote intra-class compactness and inter-class separation but do not explicitly constrain class geometry, leading to variability in the relative arrangement of classes across independent runs. Such variability limits the reproducibility and interpretability of low-dimensional embeddings. We propose the Isometric Centroid-Encoder (ICE), a supervised dimensionality reduction framework in which class centroids are learned directly in the latent space while preserving a prescribed pairwise class-centroid geometry. Unlike existing centroid- or prototype-based methods that primarily encourage intra-class compactness, ICE explicitly regularizes the relative arrangement of latent class centroids. Unlike multidimensional scaling and many metric-learning approaches that preserve sample-level relationships, ICE operates on class centroids, preserving inter-class geometry without requiring large numbers of sample-pair or triplet constraints. We further show that the intrinsic dimensionality required to represent the centroid-distance configuration of $M$ classes is at most $M-1$, explaining the observed saturation of distance-preservation measures as the latent dimensionality increases. To quantify embedding reproducibility, we introduce the Centroid Neighborhood Stability (CNS) metric, which measures the consistency of class-level neighborhood topology across independent runs and complements existing embedding quality measures. Experiments on image and biological datasets demonstrate that ICE produces stable embeddings with improved preservation of class-centroid geometry while remaining computationally efficient. Additional analyses of optimization dynamics, sensitivity, and preprocessing further provide practical insights into latent dimensionality and explicit geometry modeling for supervised dimensionality reduction.

</details>


### [67] [DIGEST: Discovery-Guided LLM Tuning for Symbolic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33880)
*Kaifeng Jin,Ruizhong Qiu,Zihao Li,Xiao Lin,Dongqi Fu,Yinglong Xia,Hong Li,Hanghang Tong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DIGEST 将轻量 LLM 的预训练解码器块改造为符号策略，用于遗传规划（GP）种群播种，并利用 GP 发现的高质量表达式在线更新该策略，从而提升符号回归的生成灵活性与搜索发现利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有符号回归方法存在两大局限：其一是"生成灵活性受限"，传统搜索类方法依赖固定搜索规则或专用学习生成器，难以跨多样表达式结构自适应生成候选；其二是"搜索发现利用不足"，多数 LLM 方法无法有效利用 LLM 消化历史表达式或自适应引导后续搜索，因而难以稳定恢复真实表达式。

Method: DIGEST 采用双机制：1）生成侧——将轻量级 LLM 中的一个预训练解码器块复用为符号策略，为遗传规划（GP）提供种群播种，借用 LLM 更强的建模能力提升生成灵活性；2）利用侧——将 GP 搜索到的高质量表达式反馈给该策略进行在线更新，使 LLM 逐步内化历史搜索发现，并转化为更优的后续种群生成。

Result: 在两个真值基准上，DIGEST 的恢复率最高超过最强基线 2.59%；在 LLM 类基线中，恢复率至少提升 54.91%，同时平均搜索时间仅为最强对手的至多 17.4%。在 122 个 BlackBox 数据集上取得最高精度，并保持适中的模型复杂度。

Conclusion: 将 LLM 解码器块作为 GP 的符号策略生成器并配合在线策略更新，可同时缓解生成灵活性不足与搜索发现利用率低的问题，在精度与效率上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Symbolic regression (SR) seeks to discover mathematical expressions directly from data, without requiring task-specific scientific descriptions. Despite tremendous progress in both conventional search-based methods and recent LLM-based methods, two critical limitations largely remain. First (Limited Generation Flexibility), conventional search-based methods rely on either fixed search rules or specialized learned generators, severely limiting adaptive candidate generation across diverse expression structures. Second (Underutilized Search Discoveries), most LLM-based methods cannot reliably recover ground-truth expressions because they fall short in effectively leveraging the LLM itself to thoroughly digest past expressions or adaptively guide future search. To address these limitations, we propose DIGEST. To render greater flexibility in generation, DIGEST repurposes a pretrained decoder block from a lightweight LLM into a symbolic policy for Genetic Programming (GP) population seeding, thereby leveraging the broader modeling capacity of the LLM. To more thoroughly utilize search discoveries, high-quality expressions discovered by GP are fed back to update the LLM-derived policy online, thereby enabling the LLM to progressively internalize past search discoveries and translate them into improved subsequent population generation. Experiments show that on two ground-truth benchmarks, DIGEST outperforms the strongest competitor by up to 2.59% in recovery. Among LLM-based baselines, it improves recovery by at least 54.91% while using at most 17.4% of the strongest competitor's mean search time. On 122 BlackBox datasets, DIGEST achieves the highest accuracy while maintaining a moderate model complexity.

</details>


### [68] [Intervention-Certified Amplification: A Validity Gate for $σ$-Weighted Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33882)
*Leiyue Chen,Meng Wei,Xuefeng Hu,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文揭示混合式后训练中基于训练侧信号的样本权重会放大目标无关的预训练行为，并提出干预认证放大（ICA）方法以目标证据门控权重，从而抑制TIR泄漏并提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SFT与RL混合后训练常用训练侧信号决定每样本的平衡权重，但这类信号只刻画锚数据与策略，无法判断被强调的行为是否真正有益于目标任务与评测场景。这种错配会重新激活并放大预训练行为，即使其字面形式已从监督中移除。

Method: 以DIAA（双隐式对抗放大）作为反例，即逐样本σ加权的SFT/RL混合训练；采用配对干预实验（抑制TIR、分离SFT模仿与σ放大贡献、以及0%-TIR教师锚的学生训练）进行归因分析；提出ICA，用配对rollout的目标证据对σ进行门控，并给出冻结版与在线版两种实现。

Result: 锚数据中已移除全部字面Python代码围栏后，DIAA仍在29%的MATH-500提示上输出未执行的工具集成推理（TIR），为普通GRPO的三倍；配对干预显示抑制TIR可恢复10.6个百分点准确率，SFT模仿与σ放大贡献相当；用0%-TIR教师锚训练的学生TIR发生率仍达45.8%。冻结与在线ICA将TIR降至13.8%与11.2%，残留拖累减半，准确率由47.5%提升至48.7%与49.3%，并在无需重新调参的情况下迁移到留出GSM8K。

Conclusion: 逐样本混合权重会因训练侧与评测目标的错配而隐性放大无关预训练行为；以配对rollout的目标证据门控权重（ICA）可有效抑制此类行为、降低残留拖累并提升且可迁移的准确率。

Abstract: Hybrid post-training often mixes supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL), using training-side signals to set each sample's balance. Such signals characterize the anchor data and policy, but not whether the emphasized behavior helps in the target task and evaluation setting. This mismatch can reactivate and amplify pretrained behavior even after its literal form is removed from supervision.
