We demonstrate this with DIAA (Dual-Implicit Adversarial Amplification), a per-sample $σ$-weighted SFT/RL hybrid. With all literal Python fences removed from its anchor, DIAA emits unexecuted Tool-Integrated Reasoning (TIR) on $29%$ of MATH-500 prompts, three times vanilla GRPO. Paired interventions show $10.6$ percentage points of accuracy recovery when TIR is suppressed and comparable contributions from SFT imitation and $σ$-amplification; a student trained on a $0%$-TIR teacher anchor emits it at $45.8%$. Intervention-Certified Amplification (ICA) gates $σ$ with paired-rollout target evidence. Frozen and online ICA reduce TIR to $13.8%$ and $11.2%$, halve residual drag, improve accuracy from $47.5%$ to $48.7%$ and $49.3%$, and transfer to held-out GSM8K without retuning.

</details>


### [69] [Min-Marginal Power Indices for Multi-Objective Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33885)
*Wenjie Shi,Chi Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出最小边际功效指数（MinPI），在带效用阈值的二元合作博弈中按最小边际贡献聚合特征，将特征标记为有害、中性或决定性，并据此进行保守的特征删除与多目标搜索预筛选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统博弈论特征选择用特征在所有联盟上的平均边际贡献打分，只能反映特征整体是否有利，无法判断在某联盟中该特征是否会把可接受模型变为不可接受模型，即无法捕捉特征的"有害"风险。

Method: 在二元效用-阈值博弈上，基于最小边际聚合定义MinPI：给定固定配额，MinPI取值为-1、0或+1，分别标注特征为有害、中性、决定性。有害标签自带证据（存在被评估的联盟，加入该特征使效用跌破配额），无需设置分数阈值；有限预算估计器保留此性质但可能漏检有害特征。MinPI用于提出特征删除，删除须通过验证门控方可接受，并用于多目标搜索的预筛选。

Result: 在6个基准数据集、5个随机种子上，MinPI删除了12.5%的特征且几乎不损失留出集效用；在删除最多的Madelon与Abalone上，其删除的留出效用代价远低于同规模随机删除；预筛选在全部5个种子上提升了数据集平均超体积。

Conclusion: MinPI是一种保守的特征删除步骤，其收益在数据中含大量可删除特征时最大；有害标签的证据性避免了阈值调参，验证门控保障了删除的安全性。

Abstract: Game-theoretic feature selection scores each feature by its average marginal contribution across coalitions. An average shows whether a feature helps overall, but not whether, in some coalition, adding it turns an acceptable model into an unacceptable one. Building on minimum marginal aggregation, we define the Min-Marginal Power Index (MinPI) on a binary utility-threshold game. For a fixed quota, MinPI takes the value -1, 0, or +1 and labels each feature harmful, neutral, or decisive. A harmful label carries its own evidence, an evaluated coalition in which adding the feature drops utility below the quota, so no score cutoff is needed; finite-budget estimators keep this property but may miss harmful features. We use MinPI to propose feature removals, which are accepted only if they pass a validation gate, and to prefilter multi-objective search. On six benchmark datasets over five seeds, MinPI removed 12.5% of analyzed features with almost no loss of held-out utility. On Madelon and Abalone, where it removed the most, its removals cost far less held-out utility than random removals of the same size. Prefiltering increased the dataset-averaged hypervolume of multi-objective search in all five seeds. MinPI is therefore a conservative feature-deletion step whose gains are largest where the data contain many removable features.

</details>


### [70] [Counterfactual Test-Time Augmentation for Shortcut Removal in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33904)
*Katie Matton,Rosalind W. Picard,John Guttag,Maggie Makar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究测试阶段的捷径去除问题，提出反事实测试时增强（CTTA）方法族：用辅助模型生成改变捷径值的反事实编辑，并对大模型在这些编辑上的预测做加权平均；证明最优捷径不变预测器属于该族并刻画其权重，在特定假设下可由 token 概率得到权重，进而提出 Select-CTTA 选择方法，实验中优于测试时基线并接近捷径平衡微调模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型会利用训练数据中的捷径（虚假相关），当测试时相关性发生偏移时预测性能下降。现有去捷径方法多在训练时进行，需要标注数据、模型权重访问与大量算力，成本高且不可行，因此本文关注无需训练、仅靠无标注测试数据的测试时捷径去除。

Method: 提出 CTTA（counterfactual test-time augmentation）方法族：对给定样本，用辅助模型生成将捷径值替换的反事实编辑，再对 LLM 在各编辑上的预测取加权平均。理论上证明最优捷径不变预测器是 CTTA 族的一个成员并刻画其权重；指出权重依赖训练分布、一般无法从训练好的模型与无标签测试数据中辨识，但给出使权重可由 LLM token 概率获得的假设，并提出检测假设违背的检验。基于这些检验设计 Select-CTTA 聚合方法，用于在多个 CTTA 方法间选择。

Result: 实验表明 Select-CTTA 优于现有测试时基线方法，且性能接近在捷径平衡数据上微调得到的模型。

Conclusion: 测试时捷径去除可通过反事实增强加加权聚合实现，理论上最优捷径不变预测器涵盖于 CTTA 族；在可验证的假设下，权重可由模型 token 概率估计，Select-CTTA 提供了实用的方法选择策略，无需标注数据、权重访问或再训练即可有效缓解捷径问题。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can exploit shortcuts, i.e., correlations present in training data that may not hold at test time. This can lead to poor predictions when correlations shift. Most existing solutions for removing shortcuts operate at training time, requiring labeled training data, model weight access, and significant computational resources. Here, we study the problem of test-time shortcut removal. We propose a family of counterfactual test-time augmentation (CTTA) methods: given an example, each method uses an auxiliary model to generate counterfactual edits in which the shortcut value is changed, and then takes a weighted average of the LLM's predictions across these edits. We prove that the optimal shortcut-invariant predictor is a member of this CTTA family, and we characterize its weights. The weights depend on the training distribution, and therefore are, in general, not identifiable from the trained model and unlabeled test data. However, we present assumptions under which the weights can be obtained using the token probabilities of the trained LLM, and propose tests to detect violations of these assumptions. We use these tests to develop Select-CTTA, an aggregate method that selects among CTTA methods. Empirically, we find that Select-CTTA outperforms test-time baselines and approaches the performance of models fine-tuned on shortcut-balanced data.

</details>


### [71] [Learning Population Dynamics with Variational Doob-Wasserstein Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33922)
*Weizhu Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出无模拟框架VDWF，在黎曼WFR流形上学习非平衡群体动力学，基于非平衡平均场Schrödinger桥理论，结合几何模型、Doob h-变换与增长模型实现轨迹推断与质量演化建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以刻画群体动力学的内在几何结构，且在非平衡传输（质量可变）情形下进行基于流的轨迹推断需要昂贵仿真；需要一种兼具几何建模能力与无模拟轨迹推断能力的理论框架。

Method: 1) 独立学习配备数据依赖度量的几何模型，捕捉群体动力学的内蕴几何结构；2) 利用Doob h-变换引入边界信息以改进基于流的轨迹推断；3) 采用增长模型学习非平衡动力学，并在WFR空间中无仿真地构造中间质量样本；4) 在一致Log-Sobolev不等式（LSI）假设下进行收敛速率分析。

Result: 在多个真实数据集上验证，于平衡与非平衡群体动力学学习任务中均取得与SOTA方法相当（具竞争力）的性能；并借助一致LSI给出框架的收敛速率理论保证。

Conclusion: VDWF将黎曼WFR几何、Doob h-变换与增长建模统一于无仿真的MFSB框架，兼具几何刻画、边界条件利用与理论收敛保证，能有效处理平衡与非平衡群体动力学建模问题。

Abstract: This paper introduces a novel simulation-free framework for learning unbalanced population dynamics on the Riemannian Wasserstein-Fisher-Rao (WFR) manifold, termed Variational Doob-Wasserstein Flow (VDWF), built upon the theory of the unbalanced mean-field Schrödinger bridge (MFSB). First, by independently learning a geometry model equipped with a data-dependent metric, our model captures the intrinsic geometric structure of population dynamics. Furthermore, leveraging Doob's h-transform, the proposed method incorporates boundary information to improve flow-based trajectory inference. For unbalanced transport, a growth model is employed to learn the dynamics while constructing intermediate mass samples in the WFR space without simulation. Additionally, we analyze the convergence rate of the proposed framework using a uniform Log-Sobolev inequality (LSI). Finally, we validate the proposed approach on multiple real-world datasets, demonstrating competitive performance in learning both balanced and unbalanced population dynamics compared with state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [72] [Multitask Interactions in Recurrent Dynamics across 100 Cognitive Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33926)
*Sílvia García-Hernández,Leema Hamid,Manuel Brenner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文在100个认知任务构成的多任务电池上联合训练分层近似线性RNN（AL-RNN），系统刻画任务间迁移与干扰的条件，并检验其与任务复杂度、非线性容量及模型结构的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务电池规模与异质性不断扩大后，任务之间何时相互迁移、何时相互干扰尚不清楚，且这些效应与任务复杂度、模型结构的关系缺乏系统研究。

Method: 同时训练分层AL-RNN求解覆盖广泛认知需求的100个任务：AL-RNN可调节递归中的非线性程度，分层任务特征提供低维接口，使任务特定适应嵌入共享动力学之中；并以分层任务特征、任务特定线性区域使用情况和任务几何构造多种任务相似性度量，与单任务模型、无结构联合模型对比。

Result: 性能随非线性容量与任务特征维度提升，分层联合模型优于单独训练模型与单层无结构联合模型；许多任务单独可由线性动力学求解，却无法被共享线性模型共同求解；多种相似性度量分别关联任务复杂度、联合可解性、成对迁移学习与留一法适应。

Conclusion: 实验刻画了多任务递归学习中的任务交互规律，既展示了可复用动力学骨干用于认知计算的潜力，也揭示了其挑战。

Abstract: The brain continually learns tasks with very different computational demands. Recurrent neural networks (RNNs) have been used as dynamical surrogates for studying task solutions, with recent work extending this approach to joint training on multitask batteries. However, it remains unclear how tasks interact as these batteries grow in scale and heterogeneity: under what conditions do they transfer or interfere with one another, and how do these effects relate to task complexity and model structure? We study this question by simultaneously training hierarchical Almost-Linear RNNs (AL-RNNs) on 100 tasks spanning a wide range of cognitive demands. AL-RNNs allow us to vary the amount of nonlinearity in the recurrence, while hierarchical task features provide a low-dimensional interface for task-specific adaptation within shared dynamics. On this task battery, performance improves with both nonlinear capacity and task-feature dimensionality, with the joint hierarchical model outperforming both individually trained models and a single unstructured joint model. Many tasks are individually solvable with linear dynamics, yet cannot be solved together by a shared linear model. Through hierarchical task features, task-specific linear region usage, and task geometry, the trained model provides several related but distinct measures of task similarity that capture how computations are separated and combined within the shared backbone. Across a series of experiments, we relate these representations to individual task complexity, joint solvability, pairwise transfer learning, and leave-one-out adaptation. Together, our experiments characterize task interactions in multitask recurrent learning while demonstrating both the potential and the challenges of reusable dynamical backbones for cognitive computation.

</details>


### [73] [EnerGNN: Learning Optimization-Compatible Energy Functions for Exact Constrained Combinatorial Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33930)
*Niki Triantafyllou,Maria M. Papathanasiou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EnerGNN 学习完整二值配置上的能量函数，并用混合整数规划在显式硬约束下精确最小化该能量，从而在保证可行性的同时获得近优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的方法通常直接预测解或用预测限制搜索，而表达力强的能量模型多依赖近似推断，难以保证硬约束下的可行性；需要一种既能精确约束推断、又能兼顾解质量的方法。

Method: EnerGNN 由 GNN 编码器、与优化兼容的读出层及非线性能量头组成，将能量构造为可嵌入 MIP 的形式并精确最小化；采用最优性感知的对比损失训练能量，使其反映解质量与约束违反程度。

Result: 在二次背包、组合拍卖与个性化细胞疗法供应链问题上均达到近优解且可行性 100%；供应链问题上平均原始间隙 0.05%，相较整体式 MIP 加速 17.4 倍；学习到的能量可泛化到更大规模实例，并支持零样本约束注入，无需重训练即可适应约束变化且保持可行性。

Conclusion: 将 GNN 能量建模与 MIP 精确推断相结合，可在硬约束下实现高可行性、近优且高效的组合优化求解，并具备规模泛化与约束灵活适应的能力。

Abstract: Learning-based approaches to combinatorial optimization often predict solutions directly or use predictions to restrict search, while expressive energy-based models typically rely on approximate inference. We introduce EnerGNN, a framework that learns energy functions over complete binary configurations and minimizes them under explicit hard constraints through mixed integer programming (MIP). EnerGNN combines a graph neural network encoder with an optimization-compatible readout and a nonlinear energy head, enabling exact constrained inference over the learned energy. An optimality-aware contrastive loss trains the energy to reflect solution quality and constraint violations. Across Quadratic Knapsack, Combinatorial Auctions, and a personalized cell therapy supply chain problem, EnerGNN achieves near-optimal solutions with 100% feasibility. On the supply chain problem, it achieves a 0.05% mean primal gap and a 17.4$\times$ speedup over the monolithic MIP. The learned energy also generalizes to larger instances and supports zero-shot constraint injection, adapting to changing constraints without retraining while preserving feasibility.

</details>


### [74] [Stable and Calibrated Latent Force ODE Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33939)
*Paul Fischer,Jonathan Schmidt,Philipp Hennig*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析近似高斯滤波中"潜变量力ODE滤波"框架的标定与稳定性问题，证明联合后验等价于重加权后的传统潜变量力后验，揭示稳定性依赖于系统状态先验方差，并提出改进的即插即用推理算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学机器学习中的核心任务是估计机制系统中的未观测力。现有融合机制信息与经验数据的近似高斯滤波方法能在单次前向-后向传播中联合辨识潜在过程与系统状态，但其标定性与稳定性存在未解问题。

Method: 在潜变量力ODE滤波框架下进行理论分析，证明状态与力的联合后验可表示为传统潜变量力后验的重新加权形式；据此识别出系统状态先验方差对稳定性的关键控制作用（该先验方差调节观测信息经机制系统流向潜在力的通路），并基于此洞见设计改进算法。

Result: 理论上给出联合后验的重加权刻画，揭示先验方差—信息流—稳定性之间的依赖关系；改进算法在若干仿真实验设定中实现稳定且标定良好的即插即用推理。

Conclusion: 联合后验的重加权视角解释了原方法的标定与稳定性缺陷，指出状态先验方差是关键调节量；改进算法可稳定、标定地完成潜变量力估计，并在仿真实验中验证有效。

Abstract: Estimating unobserved forces in mechanistic systems is a central task of scientific machine learning. Approximate Gaussian filtering methods that synthesize mechanistic and empirical information have shown remarkable capacity in jointly identifying latent processes together with the system state in a single forward-backward pass. The resulting latent force ODE filter framework channels information from observational data specifically into the unknown parts of the system. This work addresses open questions about calibration and stability of the method. We show that the joint posterior over state and force is the reweighted conventional latent force posterior. This uncovers a crucial dependence of the stability of the method on the prior variance of the system state, which effectively controls the flow of information from observations to the latent force through the mechanistic system. From this insight, we construct an improved algorithm that provides stable and calibrated plug-and-play inference. We demonstrate the improved framework on several simulated experimental settings.

</details>


### [75] [Continual Learning Exam: Benchmarking Long-Horizon RSI and Experiential Learning in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33956)
*Haoyu Zhao,Sitian Tu,Yuxuan Huang,Huichi Zhou,Zhangyue Yin,Jiahao Huang,Jingyuan Xu,Tianmiao Shi,Jin Shi,Nianxi Zhang,Yangmiao Zhang,Zhuolang Chan,Huiyan Hu,Chenchao Sheng,Yaqing Luo,Yue Chen,Huaxiu Yao,Meng Fang,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Continual Learning Exam (CLE)，一个由50+领域专家构建的大规模基准，用120个人工撰写任务、每个任务五级阶梯（共600级）横跨12个职业域的89个职业角色，衡量LLM智能体能否通过积累经验持续改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习（从经验中获取、精炼并应用知识，同时保留有用先验知识）对长时程智能体至关重要，但现有评测多衡量单次任务表现，而非跨经验的累积改进能力。

Method: 构建CLE基准：120个人工任务，每个组织为五级阶梯，共600级，覆盖12个职业域、89个职业角色；必须逐级通过才能解锁下一级，后续级别依赖前面积累的经验。由评估智能体依据人工撰写的评分标准（rubric）评判提交。设置两个对比条件：单次提交 vs. 失败后提供诊断反馈并允许一次修订。在17种 harness-模型配置上使用前沿LLM评测。

Result: 反馈加修订带来中位5.3个百分点的完成率提升；但最优系统仅通过47.7%的级别，仅在23.3%的任务中完成全部五级，说明现有前沿LLM进行持续学习仍很困难。

Conclusion: CLE揭示了当前前沿模型在经验驱动持续学习上的显著不足；该数据集还可用作后训练语料，以强化LLM的持续学习能力。

Abstract: Continual learning is the ability of LLM agents to improve over time by acquiring, refining, and applying knowledge from experience while retaining useful prior knowledge. It is essential for agents learning over long time horizons, yet current evaluations largely assess individual task performance rather than improvement through accumulated experience. We introduce Continual Learning Exam (CLE), a large-scale benchmark developed by 50+ domain experts to measure experience-driven improvement in realistic professional workflows. CLE comprises 120 human-authored tasks, each organised as a five-level ladder, yielding 600 levels spanning 89 professional roles across 12 occupational domains. Agents must pass each level to unlock the next, with later levels building on experience accumulated during earlier assignments. An evaluation agent assesses each submission against human-authored rubrics. We compare a single-submission setting with one that provides diagnostic feedback after failure and allows one revision. Across 17 harness-model configurations using frontier LLMs, feedback and revision yield a median completion gain of 5.3 percentage points. Yet the best-performing system clears only 47.7% of levels and completes all five levels in just 23.3% of tasks, highlighting the difficulty of sustained learning from experience for existing frontier LLMs. As such, the dataset can also be used to post-train LLMs to strengthen their continual learning capabilities.

</details>


### [76] [QECGBERT: A Scalable ECG Foundation Model Based on Self-Supervised Language Learning via VQ-VAE Waveform Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33964)
*Aruna Mohan,Burcu Ozek,Or Zilbershot,Fatemeh Afghah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QECGBERT：将12导联ECG按P波、QRS波、T波及等电位背景波形进行向量量化（VQ-VAE）分词并融合连续波形特征，构建短序列BERT掩码语言模型，在MIMIC-IV（>80万条无标注ECG）上预训练、在PTB-XL/CPSC2018/Chapman-Shaoxing-Ningbo上微调，性能可比或优于现有基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心脏病诊断（尤其罕见病）面临标注数据稀缺，自监督学习是重要方向；但现有ECG自监督方法把心电图当作通用序列或图像处理，未利用其生理结构，且长序列建模计算成本高。

Method: 提出生理语义感知的自监督ECG语言模型QECGBERT：按P、QRS、T及等电位背景波段对12导联ECG切分，对每个波段用VQ-VAE进行离散分词，并以连续波形特征增强token以保留量化中丢失的形态信息且不增加序列长度；随后在短序列上执行BERT式掩码语言建模预训练。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV逾80万条无标注ECG上完成预训练，并在PTB-XL、CPSC2018、Chapman–Shaoxing–Ningbo三个有标注数据集上微调评估，性能与已发表基准相当或更优。

Conclusion: 显式编码心电生理结构的分词+短序列MLM路线可兼顾可扩展性与计算效率，为心脏诊断提供一种生理感知的ECG基础模型范式。

Abstract: AI foundation models play a pivotal role in advancing heart disease diagnosis from electrocardiogram (ECG) data, particularly for rare diseases where the lack of sufficient labeled data hinders supervised learning. However, most self-supervised learning methods treat ECGs as generic sequence or image data without exploiting underlying physiological structure. To address this limitation, we propose QECGBERT, a self-supervised ECG language model that explicitly encodes physiological semantics by tokenizing the P, QRS, T, and isoelectric background waveforms. Vector-quantized variational autoencoder (VQ-VAE) tokenization is applied to each 12-lead ECG, with tokens enriched by continuous waveform features to preserve morphology lost during quantization without increasing sequence length. This design enables compact BERT masked language modeling with short sequences, improving scalability and reducing computational cost relative to long-sequence approaches. QECGBERT applies BERT pre-training to the MIMIC-IV dataset containing over 800,000 unlabeled ECG recordings, and fine-tuning on labeled PTB-XL, CPSC2018, and Chapman--Shaoxing--Ningbo datasets for evaluation. The results demonstrate that QECGBERT achieves comparable or superior performance to published benchmarks, offering a scalable, physiologically aware approach to ECG foundation modeling for cardiac diagnosis.

</details>


### [77] [Performance of Neural Operator Baselines on 'The Well' Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33967)
*Yash Pincha,Andy Dimnaku,Andrew Chiang,Adarsh Ganeshram,Valentin Duruisseaux,Anima Anandkumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出The Well基准中发布的神经算子基线仅是低成本的示例性结果，而非充分调优的性能上限；在更严格的设置（仅单步输入、同样12小时时限）下对TFNO做少量超参搜索，即可在17个任务中的15个上以VRMSE超过已发表的FNO和TFNO基线，有时领先数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The Well基准随附的基线方法被作者声明为在极小计算代价、几乎无超参调优、12小时单次运行时限下得到的示例性结果，但后续研究却将其当作"已充分调优的基准"甚至"无约束性能基线"来解读，这会导致对神经算子架构性能的错误比较。作者希望通过验证超参敏感性来纠正这一误用。

Method: 在保持与原始基线相同的12小时/单次运行wall-clock限制下，对Tucker分解的傅里叶神经算子（TFNO）进行简短、非穷尽的超参数搜索；同时采用更严格的输入设置，仅使用单个输入时间步，而非已发表基线所使用的至多4步历史输入。

Result: 尽管设置更受限，仅靠调参就在17个任务中的15个上使VRMSE低于已发表的FNO与TFNO基线，部分任务上提升达数个数量级。

Conclusion: 神经算子基线对调参的敏感度很高，The Well发布的基线不应被当作架构层面的基准来比较；后续工作在使用这些基线时需明确其未经充分调优的性质。

Abstract: The Well, a 15-terabyte dataset spanning 16 physical simulation domains, provides an extensive benchmark for developing and evaluating machine learning surrogate models for complex physical systems. The baselines released with the benchmark were presented by their authors as illustrative examples obtained at small computational cost, trained with zero to very limited hyperparameter tuning and under a highly constraining 12-hour per-run wall-clock limit. These results have subsequently been treated as fully tuned benchmarks, and in some cases effectively interpreted as unconstrained performance baselines. We perform a short non-exhaustive hyperparameter search for the Tucker-factorized Fourier Neural Operator (TFNO) under the same 12-hour per-run wall-clock limit, while using only a single input timestep rather than the history of up to four used by the published baselines. Despite this more restrictive setup, tuning alone reduces VRMSE below both published FNO and TFNO baselines on 15 of 17 tasks, sometimes by several orders of magnitude. This sensitivity to a modest tuning effort underscores that the released neural operator baselines should not be treated as architectural benchmarks.

</details>


### [78] [Hierarchical Cooperative GFlowNets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33968)
*Pedro Dall'Antonia,Juan Belieni,Ana Carolina Erthal,Saullo H. G. de Oliveira,Eliezer de Souza da Silva,Diego Mesquita*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出分层协作GFlowNets（HCG），通过动态划分搜索空间协调多个采样器，避免重复坍缩于同一高奖励区域，从而提升模式覆盖与目标分布逼近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GFlowNets按奖励比例采样组合空间对象，但探索不足会使采样器集中于少数高奖励区域；简单并行训练多个GFlowNet并合并样本无法防止模型坍缩到相同高奖励区，导致大范围状态空间未被探索。

Method: HCG对模型排序：最高排名模型可探索全空间，低排名模型被逐步推向高排名模型未覆盖的区域，无需人工设定边界；理论上刻画特定学习目标如何将概率质量从指定区域移开，并在架构中实例化该机制；由共享分类器对搜索空间进行划分以协调层级，各GFlowNet保持独立参数化并支持异步训练；同时给出归一化加权聚合规则用于集成。

Result: 理论上证明在给定假设下，归一化加权聚合可恢复目标奖励比例分布；实验（多峰分布、序列生成、组合优化、生物序列设计）显示HCG改善模式覆盖与目标逼近，且增加协作采样器数量可转化为更广泛、互补的探索。

Conclusion: HCG通过共享分类器协调的分层去重叠机制，实现无需手工边界的协同探索，并以归一化加权聚合保证分布正确性，是多GFlowNet集成采样的一种理论可证且经验有效的方案。

Abstract: Generative Flow Networks (GFlowNets) are trained to sample objects from compositional spaces with probability proportional to a reward function, but ineffective exploration can cause learned samplers to concentrate on a few high-reward regions. An intuitive remedy is to train multiple GFlowNets independently in parallel and combine their samples. However, this does not prevent multiple models from collapsing onto the same high-reward areas, leaving large regions of the state space unexplored. With this in mind, we propose Hierarchical Cooperative GFlowNets (HCG), an approach that dynamically partitions the search space to coordinate discovery without relying on hand-crafted boundaries. HCG imposes an ordering in which the highest-ranked model may explore the entire space, while lower-ranked models are progressively pushed toward regions not covered by higher-ranked ones. We provide a theoretical characterization of how specific learning objectives can shift probability mass away from designated areas, instantiating this mechanism within our architecture. A shared classifier coordinates the hierarchy by partitioning the search space, keeping the GFlowNets independently parameterized and enabling asynchronous training. Furthermore, we derive a normalizer-weighted aggregation rule for the ensemble and prove that it recovers the target reward-proportional distribution under the stated assumptions. Finally, experiments across multimodal distributions, sequence generation, combinatorial optimization, and biological sequence design show that HCG improves mode coverage and target approximation, translating increases in the number of coordinated samplers into broader, complementary exploration.

</details>


### [79] [Constrained Gradient Equilibrium: Approachability under Locally Constrained Adversaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33985)
*Kaicheng Zhang,Lujing Zhang,Yifei Pang,Yuxiao Wen,Zhun Deng,Yanjun Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究约束条件下的梯度均衡（CGEQ），先证明即使约束简单且缓慢变化，CGEQ 也可能不可达，因此需要对对手施加局部时间结构；随后在局部约束对手下建立可逼近性框架，给出类似 Blackwell 可逼近性的充要刻画，并提出将窗口级无遗憾学习器转化为 CGEQ 算法的构造原则，实验验证其在达成 GEQ 与满足约束两方面均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度均衡（GEQ）要求学习者轨迹上的时间平均梯度趋于零，但现有研究未考虑学习者受约束的情形。作者希望回答：在对抗环境下，当学习者受到约束时，这种均衡何时仍可实现。由于无约束下的结论不能直接推广，需要刻画约束带来的根本性障碍并寻找可实现的充分必要条件。

Method: 首先构造反例证明约束 GEQ 在一般对抗环境下不可实现（即便约束简单且缓慢变化），据此引入对对手的局部时间结构假设；然后在局部约束对手下发展 CGEQ 的可逼近性框架，借鉴经典 Blackwell 可逼近性理论给出必要与充分刻画；最后提出算法原则：在带余量的局部可行性条件下，把窗口级无遗憾学习器转化为 CGEQ 算法。

Result: 理论上给出了 CGEQ 在局部约束对手下的充要刻画，明确了可实现与不可实现的边界；算法上证明了窗口级无遗憾学习器在局部带余量可行性条件下可转化为 CGEQ 算法；合成数据与真实数据实验表明所得算法在实现梯度均衡和遵守约束两方面均表现强劲。

Conclusion: 约束会显著削弱梯度均衡的可达性，一般对抗环境下 CGEQ 不可行，必须依赖对对手的局部时间结构假设；在该假设下，CGEQ 具备类似 Blackwell 理论的完整刻画，并可通过窗口级无遗憾学习器配合局部可行性余量条件高效实现，为约束在线学习中的均衡问题提供了理论边界与实用算法。

Abstract: Gradient equilibrium (GEQ) is a recent framework in online learning that requires the time-averaged gradients along the learner's trajectory to vanish. We study GEQ when the learner is constrained and ask when such an equilibrium remains achievable in an adversarial environment. We first show that constrained GEQ (CGEQ) can be impossible even under simple and slowly varying constraints, motivating imposing local temporal structure on the adversary. We then develop an approachability framework for CGEQ under locally constrained adversaries, and obtain necessary and sufficient characterizations in the spirit of classical Blackwell approachability. Beyond these theoretical characterizations, we provide an algorithmic principle that converts window-wise no-regret learners into CGEQ algorithms under a local feasibility condition with margin. Experiments on synthetic and real data demonstrate that the resulting algorithms perform strongly in both achieving GEQ and respecting the constraints.

</details>


### [80] [Resolving the Mobility-Equity-Emissions Trilemma: A Causal Machine Learning Framework for Dynamic Tolling Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33994)
*Christian Mulomba Mukendi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出因果机器学习框架，用于整合动态拥堵收费以同时实现出行效率、公平性与气候目标，破解长期存在的政策三难困境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态拥堵收费在出行效率、公平性与气候目标之间存在长期的政策三难困境，缺乏能同时兼顾三者的量化方法。

Method: 采用Double Machine Learning估计分区类型的异质性处理效应；构建自适应配额机制以扩展居民区覆盖；并进行拥挤敏感定价下的排放测算与需求情景敏感性分析。

Result: 商业区延迟削减效果约为居民区的两倍；自适应配额使居民区覆盖扩大34倍，同时保留93%的总延迟节省并维持较高投资回报；在中等交通条件下减少743.5公吨CO2（价值141美元），敏感性区间为557.6–929.3吨；发现一个在不同需求情景下性能稳健的“政策不变收费区间”。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了政策不变收费区间等此前未被识别的重要洞见，可显著简化实际部署；方法在模拟真实路网的合成数据上验证，为未来公共部署奠定方法基础。

Abstract: We propose a causal machine learning framework to integrate dynamic tolling for mobility, equity, and climate objectives, resolving a long-standing policy trilemma. Using Double Machine Learning, we estimate zone-type-specific treatment effects, showing that commercial zones yield nearly twice the delay reduction of residential zones. Our adaptive quota mechanism expands residential coverage 34-fold while preserving 93% of total delay savings and sustaining high return on investment. The framework delivers climate co-benefits, reducing emissions by 743.5 metric tons of CO2 (valued at 141 USD) via congestion-sensitive pricing under moderate traffic conditions with sensitivity bounds from 557.6 to 929.3 tons. Crucially, we uncover a policy-invariant toll range where performance remains robust across demand scenarios, simplifying implementation. This study reveals critical, previously unidentified insights, such as a policy-invariant toll range, that can significantly simplify real-world implementation. Validated on synthetic data mirroring real-world networks, our methodological advances lay the foundation for future public deployment.

</details>


### [81] [Steering the Teacher: Improving On-Policy Self-Distillation for Thinking Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34005)
*Tiejin Chen,Xiaoou Liu,Hanyang Chen,Hua Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Steered-Teacher OPSD (ST-OPSD)，通过在教师模型隐状态上施加激活引导来同时抑制"参考幻觉"与"反思压制"两种失败模式，学生在未被引导的教师分布上学习，在AIME与HMMT上优于vanilla OPSD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-policy self-distillation（OPSD）让学生在同源教师（以参考为条件）的token级监督下学习自身推理轨迹，但"可访问参考"会诱发两类问题：一是参考幻觉（学生引用不可得的参考来为推理步骤辩护），二是反思压制（教师抑制不确定步骤处的核验与修订），从而损害模型的独立推理能力。

Method: ST-OPSD从模型生成的推理轨迹中提取两个方向向量，在计算蒸馏目标时将其施加于教师的隐状态：一个方向用于抑制引用参考的行为，另一个方向结合marker-family校准以鼓励重新思考。学生仅在最终教师分布上学习，自身不做激活引导。

Result: 在AIME与HMMT上，ST-OPSD相比vanilla OPSD及其他干预方法提升了准确率，同时将重新思考率与参考幻觉率恢复到基座模型水平。

Conclusion: 将激活引导作用于教师端而非学生端，可作为一种轻量、有效的干预手段，在保留OPSD收益的同时修复由参考条件化引发的两类失效模式。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) trains a student on its own reasoning trajectories using token-level supervision from a reference-conditioned teacher initialized from the same model. However, access to the reference can induce teacher behaviors that undermine independent reasoning in thinking models, leading to reference hallucinations, where the student cites an unavailable reference to justify reasoning steps, and reconsideration suppression, where the teacher discourages verification and revision at uncertain steps. We introduce Steered-Teacher OPSD (ST-OPSD) to jointly address these failure modes through activation steering. ST-OPSD extracts two directions from model-generated reasoning trajectories and applies them to the teacher's hidden states during distillation target computation, suppressing reference-invoking behavior and encouraging reconsideration with marker-family calibration. The student learns from the resulting teacher distributions without being steered. Experiments on AIME and HMMT show that ST-OPSD improves accuracy over vanilla OPSD and other interventions while restoring reconsideration and reference-hallucination rates to base-model levels.

</details>


### [82] [Cross-User Adaptation for sEMG Gesture Recognition via Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34011)
*Aran Ramakrishnan,Shubhi Mishra,Tyler Ham,Assel Kembay,Jason Eshraghian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文评估了三种持续学习策略在sEMG手势解码RNN上的表现，发现经验回放最优，并指出训练必须采用打乱、独立分窗的无状态方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 假肢与人机辅助接口依赖sEMG解码手势，而RNN需在新用户与新手势类别出现后持续适应；但循环训练本身易失稳，且何种持续学习策略在真实跨用户漂移下有效尚无定论。

Method: 在NinaPro DB1数据集上，于域增量与类增量漂移条件下对比三种代表性持续学习策略（经验回放、弹性权重巩固EWC、朴素微调），并考察训练分窗方式（时间顺序流式 vs 打乱独立分窗）及手工时域特征的影响。

Result: 经验回放优于EWC与朴素微调：类增量漂移下最终准确率83.3%，遗忘仅1.8–5.8%，而EWC与朴素训练遗忘达16.2–28.5%。按时间顺序流式分窗会使网络退化为预测单一主导手势，打乱的无状态训练则稳定。手工时域特征可使单任务准确率较原始信号提升约40%，但仅回放在类增量漂移下保留该增益。

Conclusion: 经验回放是跨用户漂移下RNN基sEMG持续学习最稳健的策略；训练流程应采用打乱、独立分窗的无状态方案，以避免循环训练崩溃，并配合手工特征以提升精度。

Abstract: Prosthetic and assistive interfaces increasingly depend on decoding hand gestures from surface electromyography (sEMG). Because the signal is shaped by person-specific factors, the recurrent neural networks (RNNs) commonly used to model these temporal streams must adapt to new wearers and new gesture classes after deployment. Continual learning (CL) enables this adaptation without retraining from scratch, yet applying it to recurrent sEMG models is not straightforward. Standard recurrent training practices can destabilize learning, and which CL strategy holds up under realistic cross-user drift is unestablished. We evaluate three representative CL strategies under domain- and class-incremental drift on NinaPro DB1, and find experience replay to outperform elastic weight consolidation (EWC) and naive fine-tuning. It reaches 83.3% final accuracy for class-incremental drift while limiting forgetting to 1.8-5.8% accuracy drop, as against 16.2-28.5% for EWC and naive training. We further show these models must be trained on shuffled, independently-windowed segments. Streaming windows in temporal order collapses the network into predicting a single dominant gesture, while a shuffled, stateless regime trains stably. Finally, hand-crafted time-domain features raise per-task accuracy by up to about 40% over raw signals, but only replay preserves that gain under class-incremental drift.

</details>


### [83] [The Information Geometry of Representation Formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34015)
*Sanjukta Krishnagopal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将表征学习视为表征空间中的几何轨迹，用Bures几何测运动、量子Fisher信息测局部敏感度，发现轨迹几何揭示所构建计算的性质、预测表征稳定性并可用于优化干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经表征分析多关注训练终点，表征内部结构如何形成的过程基本未被测量；作者想知道训练路径是否携带终点所不含的信息。

Method: 把表征学习形式化为几何轨迹：用Bures几何度量表征空间中的移动，用量子Fisher信息刻画对输入因素的局部敏感度；在匹配性能与训练时长控制下分析轨迹，并从合成向量输入迁移到残差CNN处理渲染与自然图像；进一步用表征感知的优化主动塑造轨迹。

Result: 学习将局部敏感度集中于任务相关因素，仅改变预测目标即可使几何围绕对应视觉因素重组；计算所需输入因素间高阶交互越强，轨迹越不直接（更曲折）；路径曲折度在控制交互阶数后可预测后续训练中的漂移及对参数扰动的敏感度，而终点位移不能。

Conclusion: 表征形成几何是学习的新信息来源：既能反映所构建计算的性质、预测表征稳定性，又可作为优化过程中的干预目标。

Abstract: Most analyses of neural representations focus on trained endpoints, leaving the process by which internal structure forms comparatively unmeasured. We instead treat representation formation itself as an object of study, asking whether the path a representation takes through training reveals information that its endpoint does not. We formalize this idea by viewing representation learning as a geometric trajectory, using Bures geometry to measure movement through representation space and quantum Fisher information to characterize local sensitivity to input factors. We find that learning concentrates local sensitivity on task-relevant factors, and that changing only the prediction target reorganizes this geometry around the corresponding visual factor. More strikingly, representation trajectories become systematically less direct as the learned computation requires higher-order interactions among input factors. This effect persists under matched-performance and training-duration controls and transfers from synthetic vector inputs to residual CNNs on rendered and natural images. Importantly, formation history contains predictive information about the stability of the learned representation: controlling for interaction order, path tortuosity predicts its subsequent drift under continued training and sensitivity to parameter perturbations, whereas endpoint displacement does not. Finally, representation-aware optimization lets us actively shape these trajectories, showing that formation geometry is not only measurable but actionable. Together, these results establish representation formation as a new source of information about learning: its geometry reveals properties of the computation being constructed, predicts subsequent representational stability, and provides a target for intervention during optimization.

</details>


### [84] [BriLLM-MetaPred: A Representation-Prediction Unity Language Model with Adaptive Self-Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34018)
*Junyuan Liu,Hongyue Lin,Jiale Hong,Yi Zhao,Hai Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作给出 MetaPred 原语在语言建模上的首个工程实现 BriLLM-MetaPred：以单一持续演化的动力状态同时充当上下文表示与预测读出状态，实现训练与推理的内在耦合，并在同规模下取得与 GPT 基线相当的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由 AGI 数学理论提出的 MetaPred 被论证为通用认知的必要且充分的机器学习原语，但此前仅有理论构造与 BriLLM 的 SiFu 学习范式，缺乏可运行的工程实现，因此需要验证该原语在真实软硬件上的可行性。

Method: 以 MetaPred 为设计前提，单一持续演化的动力状态同时承载上下文表示与预测读出；通过对自身状态的连续测量降低有限深度带来的不一致性、维持内部一致性，并以不动点形式提供内生动力自监督信号；借助 BriLLM 的节点信号流（node-signal-flow）设计使其可在现有软件与硬件加速器上运行。

Result: 实现的语言模型在预测性能上与同规模 GPT 基线相当；训练与推理因共享单一动力状态而内在耦合，并在机器学习各关键要素上呈现对称性。

Conclusion: 结果为首个完全由数学理论导出（而非人工增量设计拼装）的机器学习范式的工程可行性提供了初步证据；它既非循环语言模型也非 BriLLM 的扩展，而是连接 AGI 理论的 MetaPred 构造与 BriLLM SiFu 学习范式的桥梁。

Abstract: We present BriLLM-MetaPred, the first engineering implementation of the MetaPred machine-learning framework established by a mathematical theory of AGI, instantiated for language modeling. The theory argues that MetaPred is both necessary and sufficient as a machine-learning primitive for general cognition. Taking this theoretical conclusion as a design premise, we report an implementation of the primitive for language modeling. A single continuously evolving dynamical state serves as both the context representation and the state from which predictions are read out. Continuous measurement of its own state reduces finite-depth inconsistency, maintains internal consistency, and provides an endogenous dynamical self-supervision signal in fixed-point form. The single dynamical state of MetaPred intrinsically couples training and inference. MetaPred therefore exhibits symmetry across almost all key elements of machine learning, an intuitive and observable property of the mathematical optimum over the space of all machine-learning paradigms. BriLLM-MetaPred is neither another recurrent language model nor an extension of BriLLM. It provides a bridge between two prior contributions: the construction and motivation of the MetaPred primitive provided by AGI theory, and the SiFu learning paradigm provided by BriLLM (Zhao et al., 2025). The latter's node-signal-flow design enables the primitive to run on available software and hardware accelerators. Our implementation of BriLLM-MetaPred achieves predictive performance comparable to a GPT baseline of the same size. This provides initial evidence for the engineering feasibility of the MetaPred framework established by the mathematical theory of AGI. MetaPred is the first machine-learning paradigm derived entirely from mathematical theory rather than assembled and incrementally improved through human design---and perhaps the last such paradigm.

</details>


### [85] [The Symmetry of Differential Neural Tangent Kernel under the Orthogonal Group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34023)
*Bangti Jin,Longjun Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究无限宽度DNTK在正交群作用下的对称性，涵盖两层与深层网络、含/不含偏置的一般激活函数，并据此推导浅层网络积分算子谱的同伦性质与特征值衰减率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNTK是分析过参数化PINN训练动力学的工具，但其无限宽度极限下的结构性质（尤其对称性）尚不清楚；理解这些性质有助于刻画核的结构与谱行为。

Method: 基于DNTK的显式表达式（两层网络）与递归公式（深层网络），分析无限DNTK在正交群作用下的对称性，适用于任意激活及偏置设置；进而证明同一群轨道上算子诱导的单位球面积分算子具有相同谱、其RKHS仅相差一个正交变换，且对称算子对应的固定DNTK积分算子具有球带对称性（zonal symmetry）。

Result: 证明：同一正交群轨道算子的积分算子谱相同；对应RKHS仅相差正交变换；对称算子的固定DNTK积分算子具有zonal对称性；由此对RePU激活、零偏置初始化的浅层网络给出关联积分算子的特征值衰减率。

Conclusion: 无限宽度DNTK的结构由正交群对称性主导，该结果统一了两层与深层情形，并为浅层RePU网络导出可量化的谱衰减率。

Abstract: The differential neural tangent kernel (DNTK) is a tool for analyzing the training dynamics of overparameterized physics informed neural networks. In this work, we investigate the symmetry of the infinite DNTK under an orthogonal group, for two layer and deep neural networks separately, based on the explicit expression of the DNTK and recursive formula, respectively. The results are for all activations with or without a bias term, and shed insight into the structure of the infinite width DNTK. Then we prove that the integral operators over the unit sphere induced by operators from the same group orbit share the spectrum, and their reproducing kernel Hilbert spaces differ only by an orthogonal transformation, and that the operator fixed DNTK exhibits zonal symmetry on the unit sphere for symmetric operators. Thus the eigenvalue decay rate of the associated integral operator can be derived for shallow networks with RePU activation and zero bias initialization.

</details>


### [86] [Holding a Steady Course: Knowledge Evolution from Expertise and Lessons for Continual Agent Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34043)
*Wenzhe Fan,Benjamin Steenhoek,Gaurav Mittal,Yu Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出KEEL框架：用验证器证据把智能体的成功行为转化为可复用技能、失败转化为纠错教训，并随新证据对技能主张进行支持、反驳、替换或退役，在SkillFlow基准上显著提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体需在连续相关任务中积累知识，但现有方法把交互轨迹直接转成记忆或技能，而自动收集的技能与记忆并不可靠，会传播错误、误导并污染已有有用知识，因此"经验≠可信知识"。

Method: KEEL为agent-agnostic框架：以验证器（verifier）证据作为信号，将成功行为提炼为可复用技能（skills）、将失败提炼为纠错教训（lessons）；随证据累积，对单条技能主张执行支持、反驳、替换、退役等生命周期操作。

Result: 在SkillFlow基准上，KEEL相较无知识基线提升任务成功率4.2–21.7个百分点，而SkillFlow默认方法仅提升2.4–9.6个百分点；消融实验表明技能与教训具有互补性。

Conclusion: 可靠的持续智能体学习不仅依赖经验积累，更依赖对所派生知识的持续验证与演化；代码已开源。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are increasingly deployed across sequences of related tasks, yet they often fail to retain and improve their knowledge over time. Existing approaches convert interaction trajectories into memories or reusable skills, but experience is not automatically reliable knowledge. Automatically collected skills and memories can propagate errors, mislead, and corrupt useful knowledge. To address this gap, we introduce Knowledge Evolution from Expertise and Lessons (KEEL), an agent-agnostic framework that uses verifier evidence to transform successful behavior into reusable skills and failures into corrective lessons. As new evidence accumulates, individual skill claims can be supported, contradicted, replaced, or retired. On SkillFlow benchmark, KEEL improves task success by 4.2 to 21.7 percentage points over the no-knowledge baseline, compared with gains of 2.4 to 9.6 points from SkillFlow's default method. Ablations show that skills and lessons are complementary. These results show that reliable continual agent learning depends not simply on accumulating experience, but on continually validating and evolving the knowledge derived from it. Our code is available at: https://github.com/microsoft/KEEL-TTCL26.

</details>


### [87] [A Sign Law for Entropy Drift under Sampler Mismatch in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34044)
*Zhiyuan Wang,Jacob Steeves,Chang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出RLVR中采样器（top-k/top-p/温度）与学习器不匹配导致的熵漂移机制，并证明常数优势偏移会以固定符号改变softmax策略熵，且该漂移可由token加权净优势与锐度间隙协方差刻画；实验表明零化净优势或重新归一化学习器可稳定熵并提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR中熵对"本应中立"的选择（如超长响应是掩码还是计为失败）异常敏感，现有研究缺乏对此的机理解释。作者意图将这一现象归因于采样器与学习器的失配（sampler mismatch），并给出可证明的熵漂移理论。

Method: 理论层面：证明在采样器截断（top-k、top-p、min-p、温度<1）下，常数优势偏移一阶、期望意义下会按采样器锐度方向改变softmax策略熵；在组基RLVR+token级损失下，将漂移分解为(a)由token加权净优势决定的主项与(b)优势与采样器锐度间隙的协方差项。实证层面：2B模型上测量熵斜率与净优势偏移的线性关系、token级重要性比的影响；100步训练对比净零基线、固定正偏移、锐度间隙加权基线；9B推理模型10步内比较未校正规则、零化净优势、学习器重归一化。

Result: 2B模型上：截断采样时熵斜率随净优势偏移线性变化，精确采样下近乎平坦；token级重要性比仅占漂移的2%（即98%漂移未被解释）。100步训练中净零基线仅延迟熵爆炸，固定正偏移进一步延迟，锐度间隙加权基线可保持熵稳定且不改学习器。9B推理模型10步内，未校正规则损失held-out准确率，零化净优势与重归一化学习器分别领先8.2和8.8个点，精确采样下无此效应。

Conclusion: RLVR的熵不稳定源于采样器失配而非本质性问题；漂移符号由采样器锐度决定，可由token加权净优势主导。通过对齐采样器与学习器、零化净优势或用锐度间隙加权基线，可在不改变学习目标的情况下稳定熵并保持/提升性能，为RLVR中超长响应处理等实现细节提供了原理性指导。

Abstract: Entropy in reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) moves up or down depending on choices that should be neutral, such as whether over-length responses are masked or counted as failures. We trace this to sampler mismatch, where rollouts are drawn with top-k/top-p truncation or at a temperature the learner does not apply, and prove that a constant advantage shift then changes the entropy of a softmax policy, at first order and in expectation, with a sign fixed by the sampler: positive shifts sharpen the policy under any sampler whose likelihood ratio to the policy increases with token probability, including top-k, top-p, min-p, and temperatures below one, and flatten it under flatter samplers. In group-based RLVR with token-level losses, the baseline-controlled part of this drift is set by the token-weighted net advantage, which masking and failing over-length responses can push to opposite signs; the remainder, a covariance between advantages and the sampler's sharpness gap, can feed back over long horizons. On a 2B model, the entropy slope is linear in shifts of the net advantage under truncated sampling and indistinguishable from flat under exact sampling, and token-level importance ratios leave 98% of the drift. In 100-step runs, a net-zero baseline only delays an entropy explosion that exact sampling does not show, a fixed positive shift delays it further, and a baseline weighted by the sharpness gap keeps entropy stable without changing the learner. On a 9B reasoning model over ten steps, the uncorrected rule loses held-out accuracy, whereas zeroing the net advantage or renormalizing the learner gains 8.2 and 8.8 points over it, with no held-out effect under exact sampling.

</details>


### [88] [Minimax Regret for Sobolev Kernel-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34045)
*Haichen Hu,David Simchi-Levi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究Sobolev再生核Hilbert空间下连续状态-动作、对抗初始状态的episodic核强化学习的极小极大遗憾，证明其阶为T^{max{e_A,e_B}}，并提出SOVI算法达到该最优速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核强化学习中遗憾的最优依赖关系（对回合数T）此前未完全确定，Vakili(2024)留下开放问题；作者希望在Sobolev核设定下刻画动作选择与跨状态学习两种难度的不同指数。

Method: 在期望奖励与转移密度属于Sobolev RKHS、连续状态与动作空间的设定下，建立极小极大下界；提出Sobolev Optimal Value Iteration (SOVI)，将初始动作选择与延续价值估计分离，结合局部多项式逼近与逼近误差共享的RKHS结构进行估计。

Result: 证明极小极大期望遗憾为Õ(T^{max{e_A,e_B}})，其中e_A=(ν+d_A)/(2ν+d_A)，e_B=(ν+d_A+3d_S/2)/(2ν+d_A+2d_S)；SOVI达到该速率，其遗憾界前因子在固定光滑度与参考核长度尺度下关于维数d、horizon H、RKHS范数界u、B_r及核比较因子Λ呈多项式，仅差对数因子。

Conclusion: 上下界相匹配，确定了Sobolev核强化学习中回合数的最优依赖，两个指数分别反映动作选择与跨状态学习的难度，部分解决了Vakili(2024)的开放问题。

Abstract: We study the minimax regret rate for episodic kernel-based reinforcement learning, where the expected reward and transition density belong to a Sobolev reproducing kernel Hilbert space, with continuous state and action spaces, and adversarial initial states. For Sobolev smoothness parameter $ν>0$, state dimension $d_S$, and action dimension $d_A$, we prove that the minimax expected regret over $T$ episodes is $\widetildeΘ(T^{\max\{e_A,e_B\}})$, where $e_A=\frac{ν+d_A}{2ν+d_A}$, $e_B=\frac{ν+d_A+3d_S/2}{2ν+d_A+2d_S}$ with the other model parameters fixed. The two exponents reflect the distinct difficulties of action selection and learning across states. We introduce Sobolev Optimal Value Iteration (SOVI), which attains this rate from observed rewards and transitions. For fixed smoothness and reference-kernel lengthscale, its regret bound has a prefactor polynomial in the state-action dimension $d=d_S+d_A$, horizon $H$, transition and reward RKHS norm bounds $u,B_r$, and kernel norm-comparison factor $Λ$, up to logarithmic factors. The algorithm separates initial action selection from continuation-value estimation and combines local polynomial approximation with the shared RKHS structure of the approximation errors. Our matching upper and lower bounds determine the optimal dependence on the number of episodes for Sobolev kernel-based reinforcement learning. Thus, we partially address the open problem in Vakili(2024) under the condition of the Sobolev kernel setup.

</details>


### [89] [Leveraging Dale's Principle as an Inductive Bias in Recurrent Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34052)
*Jiyi Wang,Chenxiao Yang,Yujia Zhao,Jingzhao Zhang,Lu Mi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EISep RNN，通过可学习的兴奋-抑制分离实现Dale原则，在多任务、持续与强化学习中获得更稳定训练、更稀疏模块化的连接与稳定潜在动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物神经元遵循Dale原则（单个神经元只投射兴奋性或抑制性输出），这是生物环路的核心约束；但以往在人工神经网络中施加该约束常导致训练不稳定或性能下降，因此需要一个能稳定实现该约束的架构。

Method: 设计EISep RNN循环网络架构，以可学习的兴奋-抑制分离机制显式满足Dale原则，并在多任务学习、持续学习和强化学习等多种学习设定下与其他模型对比评估。

Result: EISep相比普通RNN及既往Dale约束模型，训练动力学更稳定，性能持平或更优，连接更稀疏、更具模块化，且潜在动力学稳定且信息丰富。

Conclusion: Dale原则可作为循环神经网络中具有生物依据且计算有效的归纳偏置。

Abstract: One of the fundamental differences between biological neural networks and artificial neural networks (ANNs) is that biological neurons obey Dale's principle: each neuron projects either exclusively excitatory or exclusively inhibitory outputs. While this constraint is central to biological circuits, previous attempts to impose Dale's principle in ANNs have often led to training instability or degraded performance. In this work, we introduce EISep RNN, a recurrent neural network architecture that implements Dale's principle through a learnable excitatory-inhibitory separation. Across a series of learning settings, including multi-task learning, continual learning, and reinforcement learning, EISep achieves more stable training dynamics, competitive or improved performance, and sparser, more modular connectivity than vanilla RNNs and previous Dale-constrained models. EISep also exhibits stable and informative latent dynamics. These results establish Dale's principle as a biologically grounded and computationally effective inductive bias for recurrent neural networks.

</details>


### [90] [INTRA: Interleaved Non-contiguous Token spaRse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34056)
*Yuxuan Xia,Wenbo Lu,Shaoyi Zheng,Shengjie Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: INTRA 是一种硬件感知的静态稀疏注意力设计，通过"中继通信"视角和互补的 Scatter/Gather 稀疏分组，在 CQS 约束下用跨层的短中介路径替代层内全 token 交互，并配套 CQS 感知的 FlashAttention 风格 kernel，在生成、长上下文预填充和纯注意力基准上均取得显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer 的注意力计算代价随序列长度二次增长，成为跨模态应用的瓶颈。稀疏注意力可降低开销，但有效稀疏需同时满足两点：(1) 保留有用的长程通信能力；(2) 稀疏模式能高效映射到 GPU 硬件。现有方法往往难以兼顾。

Method: 提出 INTRA（Interleaved Non-contiguous Token spaRse Attention），基于 Computational Query Set (CQS) 约束下的"中继通信"视角构建静态稀疏模式：不要求单层内覆盖所有 token 对交互，而是用互补稀疏组跨层提供短中介路径——Scatter 在局部邻域广泛采样形成中继组，Gather 在后续层将这些组重新连接；并在分区模型下保证全局可达性。实现上采用 CQS 感知的 FlashAttention 风格 kernel，运行时计算结构化索引，加载选中的非连续 token 并密集打包后计算。

Result: A100 上：2K 分辨率 FLUX.1-dev 生成加速 1.53×，LoRA 自蒸馏后图像质量优于所评估的 CLEAR 配置；LLaMA-3.1-8B 上提升长上下文预填充效率，在匹配 LoRA 适配下教师强制检索表现强；纯注意力基准上相对 MoBA 和密度受限 NSA 分别最高加速 8.4× 与 10.4×。

Conclusion: 跨层中继式的静态稀疏注意力可在保持长程通信与全局可达性的同时获得实际硬件加速，说明稀疏设计应与 kernel 实现协同优化；代码已开源。

Abstract: Transformers achieve strong performance across modalities but are bottlenecked by the quadratic cost of attention. Sparse attention can reduce this cost, but effective sparsity must preserve useful long-range communication and map well to GPU hardware. We introduce INTRA--Interleaved Non-contiguous Token spaRse Attention--a hardware-aware static sparse attention design based on a relay-communication view under Computational Query Set (CQS) constraints. Rather than directly covering all token-to-token interactions within each sparse layer, INTRA uses complementary sparse groups to provide short mediated communication paths across layers. Scatter forms relay groups that sample broadly across local neighborhoods, while Gather reconnects these groups in subsequent layers. Under a partition model, this structure guides CQS-compatible sparse patterns with global reachability. Our CQS-aware FlashAttention-style kernel computes structured indices at runtime, loads selected non-contiguous tokens, and packs them densely for computation. On an A100, INTRA achieves a $1.53\times$ speedup in $2K$ FLUX.1-dev generation, with better measured image quality than evaluated CLEAR configurations after LoRA self-distillation. On LLaMA-3.1-8B, it improves long-context prefill efficiency with strong teacher-forced retrieval performance under matched LoRA adaptation. Attention-only benchmarks further show up to $8.4\times$ and $10.4\times$ speedups over MoBA and density-constrained NSA, respectively. Code is available at https://github.com/NovTi/INTRA.

</details>


### [91] [Enhancing Type 2 Diabetes Diagnosis with Evolutionary Algorithms and Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34123)
*Parisa Rezaei,Mohsen Saffar,Hamid Reza Naji,Mohammad Mehdi Faghih,Rasoul Nouriazar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将随机森林分类器与蝙蝠优化算法(Bat Optimization Algorithm)结合，同时进行特征选择与超参数调优，并先用SMOTE-ENN处理Pima Indians糖尿病数据集的类别不平衡与噪声问题，最终模型达到89%准确率、88%精确率、90%召回率和89%的F1值，优于基线随机森林及其他已有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2型糖尿病是广泛流行的慢性疾病，需要早期准确诊断以避免严重并发症；传统诊断方法准确性不足，因此需要更可靠的计算方案。同时，现有方法常受数据类别不平衡与噪声样本影响，且特征选择与超参数调优往往分离处理，限制模型性能。

Method: 1) 数据预处理：采用SMOTE-ENN对Pima Indians Diabetes Database进行混合重采样，先过采样少数类再以编辑最近邻清除噪声与模糊样本，得到更平衡、更干净的数据集；2) 模型构建：以随机森林为基分类器，利用蝙蝠优化算法(BOA)进行特征选择与超参数联合优化，在统一搜索框架内提升分类性能；3) 与基线随机森林及已有方法进行对比评估。

Result: 优化模型在精炼数据集上取得89%准确率、88%精确率、90%召回率、89% F1值，明显优于基线随机森林及其他现有方法，且召回率较高有利于减少漏诊。

Conclusion: 将进化/群智能优化算法与集成学习相结合，可为2型糖尿病早期检测提供实用且具成本效益的临床辅助工具；SMOTE-ENN与联合优化的组合能有效提升不平衡医疗数据的分类表现。

Abstract: Type 2 diabetes is a widespread chronic disease that requires early and accurate diagnosis to prevent serious complications. Traditional diagnostic methods often lack sufficient accuracy, which highlights the need for more reliable computational solutions. In this study, we propose a model that integrates the Random Forest classifier with the Bat Optimization Algorithm for simultaneous feature selection and hyperparameter tuning. The SMOTE-ENN method was first applied to the Pima Indians Diabetes Database to correct class imbalance and remove noisy or ambiguous samples, producing a more balanced and cleaner dataset. The optimized model trained on this refined dataset achieved 89% accuracy, 88% precision, 90% recall, and an F1-score of 89%, clearly outperforming the baseline Random Forest and other existing approaches. These results demonstrate the potential of combining evolutionary algorithms with ensemble learning to provide a practical and cost-effective tool for early detection of type 2 diabetes in clinical practice.

</details>


### [92] [Self-Evolution Reasoner: Continuous Optimization via Policy-Intrinsic Exploration and Exploitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34137)
*Wenhang Shi,Yiren Chen,Zhe Zhao,Jinhao Dong,Wei Lu,Xiaoyong Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SEPO框架，利用标签引导策略自身产生分布内推理，再以自教师方式进行在策略蒸馏，实现持续自我进化，突破RLVR与自监督蒸馏的探索瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR的标量奖励稀疏、自监督在策略蒸馏虽有更密监督信号，但二者都存在探索瓶颈：当策略采样不到有效解时易陷入局部最优。引入外部示范虽可绕过，却属离策略，分布不匹配导致无法真正自我改进，推理能力也难以被充分利用。

Method: SEPO（Self-Evolution Policy Optimization）：用标签作为提示让当前策略诱导出正确的分布内推理（policy-intrinsic exploration），解锁探索潜力；再将该“以标签条件化的当前策略”作为自教师，把新发现的解蒸馏回策略（in-distribution exploitation）。二者迭代循环，形成持续的能力进化。

Result: 在科学、工具使用、复杂逻辑推理领域，相同训练时间或步数下SEPO均取得最优性能，效率与收敛上限更佳；在基线严重欠佳甚至完全失败的复杂任务和小规模模型场景下仍保持稳定提升。

Conclusion: 以“标签引导探索+分布内自蒸馏利用”的迭代闭环替代标量奖励与离策略示范，可在同分布内实现稳健、持续的推理能力自我进化。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) enables large language models (LLMs) to continuously self-improve their reasoning capabilities. Compared to RLVR's scalar rewards, recent self-supervised on-policy distillation methods leverage denser supervision signals. However, both paradigms suffer from exploration bottlenecks, struggling to escape local optima when the policy fails to sample valid solutions. Relying on external demonstrations can bypass this issue; however, the off-policy nature hinders genuine self-improvement and the mismatched-distribution reasoning can not be fully exploited. In this work, we propose Self-Evolution Policy Optimization (SEPO), a framework that drives continuous reasoning capability evolution via guided exploration and in-distribution exploitation. Specifically, SEPO utilizes labels to elicit correct reasoning from the policy, unlocking the exploration potential. By treating current policy conditioned on this in-distribution reasoning as a self-teacher, SEPO distills the newly discovered solution back into the policy. Through this iterative cycle of policy-intrinsic exploration and exploitation, SEPO achieves robust and continuous capability evolution. Across scientific, tool-use, and complex logical reasoning domains, SEPO consistently achieves the best performance under identical training time or steps, demonstrating superior efficiency and convergence ceilings. Notably, SEPO maintains stable improvements even in scenarios where baselines severely underperform or fail entirely, such as on complex tasks or with small-scale models. Our code is available at https://github.com/Eric8932/SEPO.

</details>


### [93] [Decoupled Prune-and-Reconstruct: Training on a Compact Core with Double-Layer Sparsification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34140)
*Siqi Gu,Gaozhen Ma,Liheng Huang,Weiqi He,Jiawen Luo,Jiaming Wu,Lixin Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DPR提出"双层稀疏化"框架：先用紧凑节点集裁剪训练图以降低显存，再在推理阶段以该节点集为锚点，用结构感知稀疏解码器重建全部被裁剪节点，从而把剪枝从"精度税"转为"可偿还"的加速手段。30%预算模型单卡6GB、峰值训练显存1,450 MiB，恢复稠密参考精度的96.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 百万节点图上显存墙先于算力墙到来：GNN无法完整驻留VRAM，就无法兑现其精度所承诺的全节点推理。传统剪枝被视作一次性付出、永不退还的精度税，作者试图打破这一框架。

Method: 核心洞察是同一紧凑节点集可同时承担两种图学习计算：作为编码器的训练图，并在推理时作为锚点，由结构感知的稀疏解码器重建所有被剪枝节点（双层稀疏化）。顺序被反转——先为效率剪枝，再为精度重建。

Result: 30%预算部署模型峰值训练显存1,450 MiB（单张6GB GPU），恢复稠密重建参考的96.9%；同GPU下较边稀疏基线DSpar精度+1.36 pp、峰值训练显存低50.1%；相较同协议全批不剪枝编码器，精度税仅2.64 pp而训练占用小2.8倍。仅剪枝不重建在40轮内精度仅0.378（图4）；全节点服务比采样式GraphSAGE快46倍，牺牲约5.4 pp测试精度。框架可扩展至2.45M节点生产图，峰值训练显存6,285 MiB；长程上下文、外部文本特征、CAN总线应用作为扩展变体报告，不与主方法竞争；41.8%预算的M4点在粗化超节点图及其自有划分上单独报告，不与原始节点基线比较。

Conclusion: DPR将激进的节点剪枝从精度税转变为面向VRAM受限全节点推理的安全训练加速器，实现了以较小精度代价换取显著的显存与速度收益。

Abstract: On million-node graphs, the memory wall arrives before the compute wall: a GNN that never fits in VRAM cannot deliver the all-node inference its accuracy promises. The standard remedy, pruning, has always been an accuracy tax - paid once and never refunded. DPR refuses this framing. Its central insight is that the mask that shrinks training can also scaffold recovery: one compact node set bounds both computations of graph learning, serving as the encoder's training graph and, at inference, as the anchor from which a sparse structure-aware decoder rebuilds every pruned node (double-layer sparsification). The order is inverted - prune for efficiency, then reconstruct for accuracy - and the tax is repaid: the deployed 30%-budget model trains in 1,450 MiB of peak memory on one 6 GB GPU and recovers 96.9% of its dense-reconstruction reference, beating a same-GPU rerun of the edge-sparsification baseline DSpar by +1.36 pp at 50.1% lower peak training memory; against the same-protocol full-batch no-prune encoder the deployed point pays a measured 2.64 pp accuracy tax for a 2.8x smaller training footprint. Pruning without reconstruction leaves the encoder at 0.378 within the same 40-epoch window (Fig. 4), and all-node serving runs 46x faster than sampling-based GraphSAGE, giving up approx. 5.4 pp of test accuracy. The framework scales to a 2.45M-node production graph at 6,285 MiB of peak training memory; extended variants (long-range context, external text features, a CAN-bus application) are reported as extensions and do not compete with the deployed method, and the 41.8%-budget M4 point, scored on a coarsened super-node graph with its own split, is reported separately rather than compared with the original-node baselines. DPR thus turns aggressive node pruning from an accuracy tax into a safe training accelerator for VRAM-limited all-node inference.

</details>


### [94] [Execution Logs Sway VLM Judges of Robot Progress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34151)
*Weiyan Zhao,Sunny Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 视觉语言模型（VLM）在给机器人视频打分时，会受到帧旁附加文本的影响：一句"机器人在取得进展"的日志行可使错误判决率提升10–35个百分点，而一句"日志可能有误"的警示即可消除大部分影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人视频评估日益依赖VLM自动评分，但此类评审系统对输入的日志/文本上下文是否敏感缺乏系统性验证；若上下文能系统性扭曲判决，则自动评估结果的可靠性存在问题。

Method: 在机器人进展受阻的视频片段上，向帧旁注入单行误导性日志（如声称"正在取得进展"），以中性日志行作对照，比较Gemini、Qwen、GPT三类评判模型的判决翻转率；并在另一组实验中注入伪造的"任务完成"声明及无关日志行，另加"该日志可能有误"的警示行做干预检验。

Result: 在进展受阻片段上，误导性日志使正确判决被翻转为"取得进展"的比例较中性日志高出10–35个百分点（三模型均有此现象）；加入一句"日志可能有误"的警示可消除其中大部分效应。选定的伪造"任务完成"声明可翻转Qwen约一半的正确判决，而无关日志行仅约十分之一；对Gemini则无可检测效应。

Conclusion: VLM机器人视频评审器对帧旁文本高度敏感，存在被上下文"注入"误导的系统性脆弱性，且不同模型敏感性差异明显；简单的警示提示是有效的低成本缓解手段。研究提示自动机器人评估需引入上下文鲁棒性检验。

Abstract: Vision-language models now grade robot videos, and text placed beside the frames can change their verdicts. On clips where the robot is losing progress, we add one log line saying the robot is making progress. Across Gemini, Qwen and GPT judges, this flips correct verdicts to progress 10 to 35 percentage points more often than a neutral line does. A one-line warning that the log may be wrong removes most of this effect. Selected fake "task complete" claims flip half of Qwen's correct verdicts, against a tenth for unrelated log lines. They have no detectable effect on Gemini.

</details>


### [95] [Edge-Centric Adaptive Neural Activation via Parametric Tanh Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34154)
*Andreas Falkenberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"参数化tanh连接函数"，作为KAN样条边缘函数的轻量、平滑、有界替代，以4个可学习参数实现边级可学习映射，在MNIST/CIFAR-10上优于仿射基线并显著降低训练开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KAN将可学习单变量函数放在连接边上，表达能力强，但依赖非局部B样条，导致优化困难、内存开销大、硬件扩展性差。作者希望在保留边级函数表达性的同时，克服KAN的计算与部署瓶颈。

Method: 提出参数化tanh连接函数 a_tanh(x;δ,α,β,γ)=δ+α·tanh(βx+γ)，将偏移、幅度、局部斜率与水平位移统一为4参数的可微映射，通过反向传播逐边优化；在稠密架构上以MNIST与CIFAR-10评测，并与传统仿射基线及7参数指数连接基线对比。

Result: 相对仿射基线取得显著绝对精度提升：MNIST达95.63%±0.08%，CIFAR-10达44.74%±0.18%；表达力与无约束7参数指数连接基线持平或更优，同时训练运行时间最多减少85%。

Conclusion: 以有界、解析光滑的双曲正切核替代复杂样条或高灵活度指数，为异构连接级深度学习提供了一种高度可扩展、硬件友好的折中方案；但结论主要基于小规模数据集与稠密架构，需在更大规模任务上进一步验证。

Abstract: Within the broader landscape of artificial intelligence architectures, this paper introduces a compact, edge-centric neural framework that resolves the computational bottlenecks of modern edge-activated paradigms like Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) while retaining their expressiveness over traditional Multi-Layer Perceptrons (MLPs). While KANs demonstrate remarkable representational capacity by placing learnable univariate functions on connection edges rather than hidden nodes, their reliance on non-local B-splines introduces severe optimization hurdles, high memory overhead, and poor hardware scaling. To bridge this gap, we propose the Parametric Tanh Connection Function, a smooth, bounded architectural primitive defined as $a_{\tanh}(x;δ,α,β,γ) = δ+ α\tanh(βx + γ)$. This formulation unifies offset, amplitude, local slope, and horizontal displacement into a lightweight, 4-parameter differentiable mapping optimized edge-by-edge via backpropagation. Evaluated on dense architectures using MNIST and CIFAR-10, our parametric tanh connection model achieves significant absolute accuracy gains over traditional affine baselines-reaching 95.63% $\pm$ 0.08% on MNIST and 44.74% $\pm$ 0.18% on CIFAR-10. Crucially, it matches or exceeds the expressiveness of unconstrained 7-parameter exponential connection baselines while requiring up to 85% less training runtime. By replacing complex splines or hyper-flexible exponents with a bounded, analytically smooth hyperbolic tangent kernel, this work delivers a highly scalable, hardware-friendly compromise for heterogeneous connection-level deep learning.

</details>


### [96] [Last-Iterate Stationarity of Muon with Finitely Many Newton-Schulz Iterations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34158)
*Ping Li,Vincent Y. F. Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究实用版有限步 Muon（quintic Newton–Schulz 迭代）在光滑、下有界非凸目标上的末迭代（last-iterate）梯度收敛性，分别针对确定性梯度与随机梯度给出收敛与不收敛的完整刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon 在大规模语言模型训练中表现优异，但理论分析多基于理想化的极分解更新，实际实现使用固定步数的 Newton–Schulz 迭代；已有分析仅给出平均迭代或最优迭代的稳定性保证，缺乏对末迭代收敛的理解，因此需要更贴近实际的有限步实现的理论刻画。

Method: 针对确定性梯度：分析无输入稳定化（input stabilization）时 Muon 的行为，并证明在光滑凸二次函数上会出现循环；在正稳定化下，对任意小于 1 的动量系数和足够小的常数学习率证明末迭代梯度收敛及梯度平方可和性。针对随机梯度：先构造持续有界无偏噪声在固定学习率与动量下（即便有正稳定化）导致不收敛的反例；再在梯度有界方差下结合递减学习率与递增动量，证明几乎必然与均方意义下的末迭代梯度收敛；最后构造光滑反例表明上述收敛定理不蕴含逐点多项式速率，并进一步给出 density-one 收敛速率，即除消失比例的迭代外均保证多项式梯度衰减。

Result: 确定性情形：无稳定化时 Muon 在光滑凸二次函数上可循环；正稳定化 + 动量系数<1 + 充分小常数学习率下，末迭代梯度收敛且梯度平方可和。随机情形：持续有界无偏噪声 + 固定学习率与动量（含正稳定化）可导致不收敛；在递减学习率 + 递增动量 + 有界方差下获得几乎必然与均方末迭代梯度收敛。此外存在光滑反例说明收敛定理不保证逐点多项式速率，但可证 density-one 多项式梯度衰减速率。

Conclusion: 实用有限步 Muon 的收敛性强烈依赖于输入稳定化、学习率与动量调度：常数步长下随机噪声可破坏收敛，需采用递减学习率配合递增动量；且收敛性本身不等价于逐点速率，只能保证除消失比例迭代外的多项式衰减。

Abstract: Muon has achieved strong empirical performance in large-scale language-model training, motivating a better theoretical understanding of its practical implementation. Unlike the idealized polar-factor update, practical Muon uses a fixed number of quintic Newton--Schulz iterations. Existing analyses mainly establish average- or best-iterate stationarity guarantees, leaving last-iterate convergence less understood. We study last-iterate gradient convergence of practical finite-step Muon on smooth, lower-bounded nonconvex objectives.
  For deterministic gradients, we show that without input stabilization, Muon can cycle even on a smooth convex quadratic. With positive stabilization, we establish last-iterate gradient convergence for any fixed momentum coefficient below one and a sufficiently small constant learning rate, together with square-summability of gradients. For stochastic gradients, we first show that persistent bounded, unbiased noise can prevent convergence with fixed learning rates and momentum, even with positive stabilization. We then establish almost-sure and mean-square last-iterate gradient convergence by combining decreasing learning rates with increasing momentum under bounded-variance stochastic gradients. Finally, we construct a smooth counterexample showing that neither convergence theorem guarantees a pointwise polynomial rate. We further establish density-one convergence rates, guaranteeing polynomial gradient decay for all but a vanishing fraction of iterations.

</details>


### [97] [Single-Cycle Battery Remaining Useful Life Prediction with Compressed Continuous Wavelet Features and Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34159)
*Sungeun Kim,Mingyu Jung,Yuri Kim,Jin-Sung Park,Jongbin Ryu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于连续小波变换的电池早期剩余寿命预测框架：将逐周期电压、电流、容量信号转为时频图，并进行尺度压缩以去除冗余，在8个基准数据集上实现优于已有方法的单周期寿命预测且具备可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锂电池性能随使用逐渐退化，准确寿命预测对安全与可靠性至关重要；但可靠评估依赖大量退化测试，成本高、耗时长。现有数据驱动方法希望仅用最初几个周期预测终止寿命，然而早期老化动态微弱且非线性，难以从有限早期数据中提取可靠退化模式。

Method: 采用连续小波变换（CWT）将逐周期电压、电流、容量信号转换为时频表示（scalogram），实现退化行为的多分辨率刻画；在此基础上施加尺度压缩，在保留结构信息的同时降低冗余；以端到端方式完成早期周期到剩余使用寿命（RUL）的映射，并与梯度可视化、冗余—保真度分析结合。

Result: 在由7个公开电池数据集构建的8个基准数据集（覆盖不同电极化学体系、电芯形态与循环协议）上，所提框架在单周期RUL预测中一致优于先前方法，并在输入周期数从1增至100时保持稳健；冗余—保真度分析刻画了尺度压缩带来的权衡；梯度可视化显示模型关注与充放电过程中结构化信号转变相关的局部时频区域。

Conclusion: 时频表征结合尺度压缩能够在极早期数据条件下有效、稳健且可解释地预测电池剩余使用寿命，为降低退化测试负担提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Lithium-ion batteries are widely used in industrial applications, but their performance gradually degrades over time, making accurate lifetime prediction important for safety and reliability. However, reliable lifetime assessment generally requires extensive degradation testing, which is both costly and time-consuming. To reduce this burden, recent data-driven approaches have sought to predict the end-of-life cycle of batteries from only the initial few cycles. Nevertheless, identifying reliable degradation patterns from such limited early-cycle data remains challenging because early aging dynamics are often subtle and nonlinear. To address this issue, we adopt the continuous wavelet transform to convert cycle-wise voltage, current, and capacity signals into time-frequency representations, enabling multi-resolution characterization of degradation behavior. Building on this representation, we apply scale compression to reduce redundancy while preserving structural information in the scalograms. We evaluate the proposed framework on eight benchmark datasets derived from seven public battery datasets covering diverse electrode chemistries, cell form factors, and cycling protocols. Experimental results show that the proposed framework consistently outperforms prior approaches in single-cycle remaining useful life prediction and remains robust as the number of input cycles increases from 1 to 100. In addition, redundancy-fidelity analysis characterizes the trade-off introduced by scale compression, and gradient-based visual explanations indicate that the model focuses on localized time-frequency regions associated with structured signal transitions during charge-discharge operation. These results demonstrate the effectiveness and interpretability of the proposed framework for early-cycle battery remaining useful life prediction.

</details>


### [98] [Reasoning or Just Prompting? Probing the Mechanism of Latent Chemical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34173)
*Kaiyuan Lu,Jiatong Li,Yatao Bian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过因果扰动实验质疑"潜空间推理"是否构成隐含推理链，发现潜状态内容分子特异性影响很小，其作用更接近soft prompt（软提示）而非逐步推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学领域的显式推理一旦出错会传播到最终答案，因此潜空间推理（连续空间中的隐式计算）颇具吸引力。但若潜状态真是隐含推理链，就应具备因果重要性——作者据此检验这一核心假设。

Method: 对LatentChem的中间潜状态进行因果干预（交换、适度扰动、完全移除），并做对照实验：用跨分子共享的单一固定前缀替代逐输入潜生成；在受控模型中比较可学习静态soft prompt与逐输入潜生成的表现。

Result: 交换或适度扰动中间潜状态对性能影响甚微，而整体移除有时导致显著分数下降；单一固定前缀即可复现LatentChem性能；学习到的静态soft prompt与逐输入潜生成表现相当；潜序列对替换与适度扰动的容忍性进一步支持soft prompt式解释。

Conclusion: 潜状态确实重要，但其分子特异性内容影响甚小；潜化学推理更应被理解为soft prompting，而非隐藏在连续空间中的推理链。

Abstract: Latent reasoning performs intermediate computation in continuous space rather than through explicit tokens, which is particularly appealing in chemistry, where errors in explicit reasoning can propagate to the final answer. Yet if latent states truly constitute an implicit reasoning chain, they should be causally important. We find otherwise: swapping or moderately perturbing intermediate latent states has little effect on performance, while removing them entirely sometimes causes a substantial score drop. Thus, the latent states matter, but their molecule-specific content appears to matter far less. This points to a simpler interpretation. Rather than encoding a step-by-step reasoning chain, latent states may primarily steer subsequent model computation, much like soft prompts. Indeed, a single fixed prefix shared across molecules reproduces LatentChem's performance, and a learned static soft prompt performs comparably to per-input latent generation in controlled models. The tolerance of latent sequences to substitution and moderate perturbation further supports this connection. We therefore suggest that latent chemical reasoning may be better viewed as soft prompting than as a reasoning chain hidden in continuous space.

</details>


### [99] [Entropic Control for Homeostatic Optimization: Pre-empting Catastrophic Forgetting via Dynamic Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34182)
*Shangxi Wu,Xiaorong Yan,Xiaojun Meng,Yuxiang Zhang,Jiansheng Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ECHO闭环控制框架，用输出熵作为在线替代信号监测灾难性遗忘风险，并通过弹性控制器动态调节回放数据的梯度权重，在0.6B–9B模型上扩展了可塑性-稳定性前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的监督微调（SFT）存在灾难性遗忘，现有缓解策略难以兼顾可塑性-稳定性两难（plasticity-stability dilemma），且基于参考模型推断的KL监测计算开销大。

Method: 对KL散度进行分解，发现输出熵是KL变化的主导可观测分量，故以熵作为计算高效的在线代理实时监测遗忘风险，免去参考模型推理；进而设计ECHO弹性控制器，以偏离预训练熵平衡点的偏差为误差信号，按比例控制律动态调制回放数据的梯度权重。

Result: 在0.6B至9B参数规模的架构上评估，ECHO位于并可多次扩展所观测到的可塑性-稳定性前沿，且在不同数据混合比例下保持较高的运行鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 以熵作为KL变化的主导可观测代理，配合比例控制的回放权重调节，是一种低开销、鲁棒且有效的SFT遗忘缓解范式。

Abstract: Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) of LLMs suffers from catastrophic forgetting, and prevailing mitigation strategies struggle to reconcile the plasticity-stability dilemma. To address this, we propose Entropic Control for Homeostatic Optimization (ECHO), a closed-loop control framework. By decomposing the KL divergence, we identify output entropy as a dominant observable component of KL variation. This enables us to leverage entropy as a computationally efficient online proxy to monitor forgetting risks in real-time, bypassing expensive reference-model inference. Consequently, ECHO employs an elastic controller that dynamically modulates the gradient weight of replay data via a proportional control law, using deviations from the pre-trained entropic equilibrium as an error signal. Empirical evaluations across 0.6B to 9B parameter architectures demonstrate that ECHO lies on, and in several comparisons extends, the observed plasticity-stability frontier and maintains high operational robustness against varying data mixing ratios.

</details>


### [100] [SRA: Selective Residual Adaptation for LLM Reasoning Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34199)
*Zhenxing Dou,Kun He,Mingyang Yi,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出即插即用的选择性残差适配框架SRA，通过定位师生分布差异并对top-K logit做局部校正，提升蒸馏小模型的多步推理能力，理论上给出严格序列级增益的误差界，Qwen3 0.6B–4B上Pass@16平均最高提升4.37个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识蒸馏虽可将大模型推理能力迁移至轻量学生模型，但蒸馏后的小模型在多步推理上仍表现不佳；作者观察到师生沿推理路径的分布差异高度稀疏，且目标token几乎总落在学生top-K候选集内，因此无需更新学生主干或依赖运行时教师计算。

Method: 提出选择性残差适配（SRA）：由差异门控（Discrepancy Gate）识别分布发散位置，再由候选残差适配器（Candidate Residual Adapter）对top-K logit进行校准以纠正不匹配；作为即插即用框架内部化局部修正。理论上建立误差界以保证严格的序列级性能提升。

Result: 在Qwen3 0.6B至4B的数学基准上，SRA稳定提升蒸馏学生模型，平均Pass@16最高提升4.37个百分点，同时保持通用能力。

Conclusion: 基于分布差异稀疏性与top-K候选覆盖的观察，局部化残差校正即可有效缓解师生推理失配，方法轻量、可插拔且有理论保证，兼具性能增益与通用能力的保持。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation transfers reasoning capabilities from large language models to lightweight students. However, distilled small models still struggle with multi-step reasoning. We observe that the distribution divergences between teacher and student models along reasoning paths are highly sparse, and target tokens almost always fall within the student top-$K$ candidate set. Based on this finding, unlike approaches updating student backbones or requiring runtime teacher computation, we propose Selective Residual Adaptation (SRA), a plug-and-play framework that internalizes localized corrections. Concretely, SRA employs a Discrepancy Gate to identify divergence positions alongside a Candidate Residual Adapter for top-$K$ logit calibration to correct the mismatch. Theoretically, we establish error bounds ensuring strict sequence level performance gains. Across mathematical benchmarks on Qwen3 models from 0.6B to 4B, SRA consistently enhances distilled student models, raising average Pass@16 by up to 4.37 percentage points while preserving general capabilities.

</details>


### [101] [CORMA: Constraint-Routed Decision Mamba for Generalizable Multi-Constraint Offline Safe Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34208)
*Changxu Ni,Feiran You,Yongan Guo,Hongyang Du,Kang Wei,Le Liang,Yiyang Ni,Jun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CORMA，一种约束路由的决策 Mamba 框架，用于可泛化的多约束离线安全强化学习，兼顾奖励-安全权衡、低推理延迟与零样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 Decision Transformer 的离线安全 RL 方法仅面向单约束任务；在多约束场景下，异质约束相互冲突，难以泛化到未见的安全预算组合，且 Transformer 的二次复杂度因需更多 CTG token 而显著增加计算开销。

Method: 将策略推理分解为约束感知的 RTG 估计与自适应动作生成：用约束专属专家建模可行 RTG 分布，通过路由引导的专家乘积（product-of-experts）机制融合分布，为基于 Mamba 的动作解码器提供安全感知引导；并设计三阶段优化策略提升训练稳定性。

Result: 在 OSRL 基准上取得更优的奖励-安全权衡，相较 DT 类方法推理延迟降低 37%，对未见安全预算组合的零样本泛化优于离线安全 RL 基线。

Conclusion: 以线性复杂度的 Mamba 替代 Transformer 并结合约束路由的专家融合，可有效支撑多约束离线安全 RL 的可泛化决策。

Abstract: Offline safe reinforcement learning (RL) learns safety-aware policies from static datasets while satisfying safety constraints during deployment. Motivated by the strong trajectorymodeling capabilities of Transformers, recent Decision Transformer (DT)-based methods formulate offline safe RL as conditional sequence modeling by conditioning action generation on return-to-go (RTG) and cost-to-go (CTG) tokens, thereby enabling adaptation to varying safety budgets. However, existing DT-based methods are developed for single-constraint tasks. For tasks with multiple constraints, generalizing learned policies to unseen combinations of safety budgets remains challenging due to the conflicting requirements imposed by heterogeneous constraints. Moreover, the quadratic complexity of Transformer architectures significantly increases the computational overhead in multi-constraint tasks, as representing additional constraints requires more CTG tokens. Recently, Mamba has emerged as an efficient sequence-modeling backbone, offering linear computational complexity and strong generalization capabilities. In this paper, we propose CORMA, a constraint-routed decision Mamba framework for generalizable multi-constraint offline safe RL. CORMA decomposes policy inference into constraint-aware RTG estimation and adaptive action generation, where constraint-specific experts model feasible RTG distributions and a router-guided product-of-experts mechanism fuses these distributions to provide safety-aware guidance for a Mamba-based action decoder. A three-stage optimization strategy is designed to improve training stability. Extensive experiments on OSRL benchmarks demonstrate that CORMA achieves superior reward-safety trade-offs, reduces inference latency by 37% compared with DT-based methods, and exhibits stronger zero-shot generalization to unseen safety-budget combinations than offline safe RL baselines.

</details>


### [102] [Interpretable and Steerable Molecular Graph Generation using Concept Bottlenecks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34227)
*Sarabeshwar Balaji,Ritesh Kumar,Lav R. Varshney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将概念瓶颈（concept bottleneck）引入深度图生成器（DGG），以提升分子生成的可解释性与可控性，并验证其不损害生成质量，同时探索基于优化的 Concept Controller 引导生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度图生成器缺乏可解释性，这在药物发现这一关键应用中构成显著限制：可解释、透明的生成有助于更好地控制分子的期望性质，减少候选筛选中的试错。常见做法（如解耦表示）无法保证所学表示对人类可解释。

Method: 把概念瓶颈结构嵌入深度图生成器，使用户能够解释、调试并引导生成过程；进一步引入 Concept Controller——一种在可引导图像生成中被证明有效的基于优化的引导方法；在 one-shot 分子图生成框架上评估，并指出该思路可迁移至序列式图生成。

Result: 引入概念瓶颈后分布式保真度稳定提升（FCD 更低），对有效性与唯一性仅有适度且非系统性的影响；Concept Controller 作为优化式引导方法被进一步探索。

Conclusion: 概念瓶颈可作为提升 DGG 可解释性与可引导性的有效途径，且不以牺牲生成保真度为代价；所提方案在 one-shot 分子图生成中验证，原理上可推广到序列式图生成。

Abstract: Deep graph generators (DGGs) largely lack interpretability, which is a significant limitation for one of their most crucial applications, drug discovery, where interpretable and transparent generation could provide greater control over the desirability of generated molecules, thereby reducing trial-and-error in candidate selection. Common approaches to interpretability, such as disentanglement, cannot guarantee that the learned representations are human-interpretable. To address this, we propose incorporating concept bottlenecks into DGGs, allowing users to interpret, debug, and steer the generation process. We show that this consistently improves distributional fidelity (lower FCD), with only modest, non-systematic effects on validity and uniqueness. We further explore Concept Controller, an optimization-based steering approach that has proven to be effective for steerable image generation. We evaluate our approach on a one-shot molecular graph generation framework, though the underlying ideas can be transferred to sequential graph generation as well.

</details>


### [103] [Alignment via Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34242)
*Shai Shalev-Shwartz,Amnon Shashua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Aligned-PAC 学习框架，把"对齐"问题归约为"泛化"问题：训练中所使用的需求上的成功，能否推广到未遇到的需求上？


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 系统可能在完成给定目标上越来越强，却未必更符合我们真正想要的行为；要严格研究这一对齐问题，需要一个把可度量的性能与意图行为联系起来的框架。

Method: 不把全部意图压缩成单一训练目标，而是用一组具体需求的集合来表达意图的不同侧面；在此设定下用学习理论工具进行分析：证明独立评估可对已表达需求上的对齐提供严格证据，并给出所需评估量的界；同时提出一种优化方法，在显式假设下组合已学模型以满足训练需求。

Result: 给出了关于评估证据强度与所需评估规模的理论结论，并在显式假设下得到可满足训练需求的模型组合优化方法。

Conclusion: 该框架并未完全解决对齐问题：如何选择忠实反映意图的需求、以及学习何时能泛化到未见需求，仍是开放挑战；但它为使这些问题精确化并用学习理论工具加以研究提供了基础。

Abstract: AI systems can become better at achieving the goals we give them without becoming better at doing what we actually want. Studying this alignment problem rigorously requires a framework connecting measurable performance to intended behavior. We propose Aligned-PAC learning, whose central idea is to reduce alignment to generalization: does success on the requirements used for training extend to requirements the system has not encountered? Rather than express our entire intention through a single training objective, we represent its different aspects through a collection of specific requirements. This perspective already yields strong theoretical results. We show how independent evaluation can provide rigorous evidence of alignment across the represented requirements, and establish how much evaluation is needed. We also develop an optimization method that, under explicit assumptions, combines learned models to satisfy the training requirements. The framework does not solve alignment in full: choosing requirements that faithfully capture our intentions, and understanding when learning will generalize to unseen requirements, remain open challenges. It provides a foundation for making these questions precise and studying them with the tools of learning theory.

</details>


### [104] [Recovering Business Rules as Cascade Lookups for Enterprise Field Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34258)
*Changxu Duan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出知识图谱引导的LLM工作流，从历史交易中恢复企业销售订单缺失或过期的配置规则，并编译为确定性级联查找；LLM仅用于离线规则发现，推理时无需模型服务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业销售订单系统依赖人工维护的配置规则来确定付款条件、发货点、销售归属等字段。规则不完整或过期时需操作员手工录入，错误会扩散到开票、物流与报表环节。论文希望验证：缺失或过期规则能否从历史交易中恢复，而非重新学习为不透明的统计模式。

Method: 知识图谱引导的工作流：LLM智能体提出候选业务键（如客户与单据类型的列组合），通过SQL剖析进行验证，并将证据编译为确定性级联查找。LLM仅在离线规则发现阶段使用，推理阶段为透明的键查找。

Result: 在企业销售订单预测与公开关系基准上的实验表明：当字段遵循稳定业务规则时级联方法最有效；当预测依赖时间或邻域模式时，学习型图模型更优。漂移分析可将残差错误转化为配置维护的诊断信息。

Conclusion: 配置规则可从历史交易中以确定性、可解释的方式恢复，适用于遵循稳定业务规则的字段；对依赖时序/邻域模式的字段仍宜采用学习型模型。方法兼具零推理开销与可运维性，残差可驱动规则维护。

Abstract: Enterprise sales-order systems rely on manually maintained configuration rules to determine fields such as payment terms, shipping points, and sales ownership. When these rules are incomplete or stale, operators must enter values by hand, and downstream errors spread into billing, logistics, and reporting. This paper studies whether missing or outdated rules can be recovered from historical transactions instead of being relearned as opaque statistical patterns. We introduce a knowledge-graph-guided workflow in which an LLM agent proposes candidate business keys, meaning column combinations such as customer and document type, validates them through SQL profiling, and compiles the resulting evidence into deterministic cascade lookups.
