The LLM is used only during offline rule discovery. At inference time, prediction is a transparent key lookup with no model-serving requirement. Experiments on enterprise sales-order prediction and public relational benchmarks show that cascades are most effective when fields follow stable business rules, while learned graph models remain preferable when prediction depends on temporal or neighborhood patterns. Drift analysis further turns residual errors into diagnostics for configuration maintenance. Code and data: https://github.com/Fireblossom/CascadER.

</details>


### [105] [DoubleFree:Exploiting Exploration History for Rollout-Free Multi-Task LLMs Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34272)
*Yingping Wang,Min Cai,Chenyu Yuan,Xi Ye,Zhijiang Guo,Jiayi Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DoubleFree 复用领域 RL 训练中产生的历史轨迹，实现无需在线 rollout 与在线教师的蒸馏式多任务整合，在保持多域性能的同时获得约 3–6 倍加速、GPU 时数降低约 81%–90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务后训练的标准流程是多教师在线策略蒸馏（MOPD）：需为每个学生响应实时生成 on-policy rollout，并全程保持多个教师模型在线，系统开销大。而领域 RL 阶段除产出专家模型外还留下大量训练轨迹，既有方法只用前者、浪费后者，本文旨在把这一被忽视的资源转化为降低整合成本的手段。

Method: 将整条流程重新解释为"领域内探索—跨域利用"：直接复用领域 RL 历史轨迹代替学生在线 rollout（rollout-free consolidation），并预计算教师监督信号以替代在线教师（online-teacher-free training）。理论上刻画历史轨迹更新在何种条件下可近似 on-policy 蒸馏更新，并分析跨探索阶段混合轨迹如何匹配不断演化的学生分布。方法对现有后训练框架改动极小。

Result: 在 Qwen3-4B、Qwen3-8B、SmolLM3-3B 上，覆盖数学、代码、指令遵循三类任务：整合阶段加速 2.99–5.75 倍，GPU 时数下降 80.9%–90.1%，多域性能与标准 MOPD 相当。

Conclusion: 领域 RL 轨迹是可再利用的整合资源；通过 off-policy 轨迹近似 on-policy 蒸馏并预计算教师监督，可在几乎不损性能的前提下大幅削减多任务后训练的算力与系统成本，且易嵌入现有框架。

Abstract: Multi-task post-training can develop domain specialists through independent reinforcement learning (RL) and consolidate their capabilities through multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD). However, standard MOPD generates on-policy student responses and keeps multiple teachers online throughout consolidation, introducing substantial system costs. We view this pipeline as domain-wise exploration followed by cross-domain exploitation. Domain RL produces not only the final specialists, but also the trajectories collected throughout their training. Existing methods primarily use the former while leaving the latter underutilized. We introduce DoubleFree, which reuses these historical trajectories for rollout-free consolidation and precomputes teacher supervision for online-teacher-free student training. To understand why this works, we analyze when updates from historical trajectories can approximate on-policy distillation updates and how combining trajectories across exploration stages can better support an evolving student. DoubleFree requires only minimal changes to existing post-training frameworks. Across math, code, and instruction following on Qwen3-4B, Qwen3-8B, and SmolLM3-3B, DoubleFree achieves $2.99$-$5.75\times$ consolidation speedups and reduces GPU-hours by $80.9$-$90.1\%$, while maintaining comparable multi-domain performance.

</details>


### [106] [Birth, Death and Diff: Insertion-Based Sequence Generation with Corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34310)
*Durga Prasad Maram,Dhruvesh Patel,Tahira Naseem,Benjamin Rozonoyer,Gaurav Pandey,Ramón Fernandez Astudillo,Andrew McCallum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 δMDM，在插入式掩码扩散模型中加入替换与删除操作，使中间生成结果可修正，从而支持激进并行解码，在速度—质量权衡上帕累托优于 FlexMDM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 插入式掩码扩散模型虽能实现变长并行生成，但生成决策不可逆，激进并行解码引入的错误无法被后续步骤纠正，只能保守解码、增加前向次数（NFEs）；同时训练中对多种对齐的边际化导致其熵高于标准 MDM，缺少纠错机制时需更保守解码。

Method: 为插入式模型配备替换（substitution）与删除（deletion）操作，使中间生成可修订。训练时主动向模型暴露由激进 unmasking 与插入造成的错误，教会其自我纠错；推理时将插入、unmasking 与纠错步骤交错进行，纠错步骤可修订已 unmask 的 token 并移除多余掩码。

Result: 在速度—质量权衡上帕累托支配 FlexMDM；在 OpenWebText 高多样性无条件文本生成中，最高推理预算下生成困惑度减半，且在全部推理预算下均优于以变长填充序列从零训练的 MDM；在 TinyGSM 低多样性条件生成中以一半 NFEs 达到与 FlexMDM 相当的准确率。

Conclusion: 引入可修正的替换与删除操作与交错纠错解码，可消除不可逆决策带来的保守性约束，使激进而高效的并行解码成为可行，同时提升生成速度与质量，为插入式掩码扩散模型提供了更优的生成范式。

Abstract: Insertion-based masked diffusion models enable flexible-length, parallel generation by interleaving mask insertion with token unmasking. However, because their generation decisions are irreversible, aggressive parallel decoding can introduce errors that subsequent steps cannot correct, necessitating conservative decoding with more forward passes (NFEs). Moreover, they exhibit higher entropy than standard MDMs because they marginalize over multiple alignments during training and therefore require even more conservative decoding in the absence of a correction mechanism. To address this limitation, we introduce $δ$MDM, which makes intermediate generations revisable by equipping an insertion-based model with substitution and deletion operations. During training, we expose the model to errors caused by aggressive unmasking and insertion, teaching it to correct its own mistakes. At inference time, we interleave insertion and unmasking with correction steps that can revise previously unmasked tokens and remove unnecessary masks. This mechanism makes aggressive decoding practical, improving both generation speed and quality. $δ$MDM Pareto-dominates FlexMDM, a vanilla insertion-based MDM, on the speed--quality trade-off. On high-diversity unconditional text generation on OpenWebText, it halves the generative perplexity relative to FlexMDM at the highest inference budget. It also achieves lower generative perplexity at all inference budgets compared to MDM trained from scratch with variable-length padded sequences. On low-diversity conditional text generation on TinyGSM, it matches FlexMDM accuracy with half as many NFEs.

</details>


### [107] [SHIFT-JEV: Certified Abstention and Gain-Gated Continual Adaptation for LLM-Based Decision Models under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34329)
*Muhammad Atif Saeed,Azka Atiq,Adarsh Kumar,Akhtar Jamil,Uzair Iqbal,Roland Aydin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SHIFT-Jev框架，用于LLM用作决策模型时的监控、弃答与持续自适应，在分布漂移下改进校准、风险控制与类别先验漂移检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为在固定选项集上输出概率分布的决策模型时，会面临域漂移、标签先验漂移、新出现类别和候选集漂移，现有方法缺乏对这种失效的系统刻画与应对手段。

Method: SHIFT-Jev：从不确定性、格式与选项一致性信号为每个决策打分；用Learn-then-Test认证弃答阈值；用漂移与标签漂移检测器监控决策流（对预测标签做卡方检验检测类别先验漂移）；仅当期望增益门在留出集上发现显著提升且不损害早期环境时接受模型更新。训练侧使用KL锚正则微调与温度缩放。基准为10个漂移测试环境。

Result: KL锚微调+温度缩放将漂移下校准误差从0.289降至0.087。目标风险10%时，学习型不确定性分数+认证阈值仅在27个环境-种子测试的3–5个中超标，而最大概率分数为5–15个、保形预测集为9–14个；基于漂移的路由影响很小。卡方检验检出95–99%的类别先验漂移，优于置信度与嵌入检测器。持续自适应下Qwen3-0.6B达70.5%准确率，每个种子均优于replay、LwF、EWC和微调基线；Qwen3-4B上保守门控使其落后基线0.4–1.4个百分点。

Conclusion: SHIFT-Jev在漂移下有效降低校准误差并提供可靠的弃答风险控制与类别先验漂移检测，小模型上持续自适应优势明显；但在较大模型上过保守的更新门控会带来性能损失，需在安全性与增益间权衡。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can act as decision models that return a probability distribution over a fixed set of options, but deployed decision models face domain, label-prior, emerging-class and candidate-set shift. We study how such models fail under shift and propose SHIFT-Jev, a monitoring, abstention and continual-adaptation procedure for them. It scores the risk of each decision from uncertainty, format and option-consistency signals, certifies abstention thresholds with Learn-then-Test, monitors the decision stream with drift and label-shift detectors, and accepts a model update only when an expected-gain gate finds a significant held-out improvement without loss on earlier regimes. On a benchmark of ten shifted test environments with Qwen3-0.6B and Qwen3-4B (three seeds each) and Phi-4-mini, fine-tuning with KL anchors and temperature scaling lowers calibration error under shift from 0.289 to 0.087. At a target risk of 10%, a learned uncertainty score with a certified threshold exceeds the target in 3 to 5 of 27 environment-seed tests, against 5 to 15 for a max-probability score with the same certification and 9 to 14 for conformal prediction sets; drift-based routing changes little. A chi-squared test on predicted labels detects 95 to 99% of class-prior shifts that confidence- and embedding-based detectors miss. In continual adaptation, SHIFT-Jev reaches 70.5% accuracy on Qwen3-0.6B, above replay, LwF, EWC and fine-tuning baselines in every seed, whereas on Qwen3-4B its conservative gate leaves it 0.4 to 1.4 points behind them.

</details>


### [108] [SEEK: Skill Extraction with Evolving Knowledge against Frontier Science Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34365)
*Cheng Gui,Xiang Zheng,Yutao Wu,Hanxun Huang,Yige Li,Kaidi Xu,Cong Wang,Wei-Ying Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建40项受保护技能、8个科学智能体平台的评测基准，发现技能泄露普遍存在（100%平台至少泄露一项，4个平台单次直接请求即100%成功），并提出自适应红队智能体SEEK，在SpatialAgent与Biomni 12项技能上达100%攻击成功率，首次成功轮次较bandit引导探测减少31.8%和38.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学智能体将领域知识封装为可复用技能，但交付技能能力的交互本身可能暴露技能内容，形成保密性风险；现有工作缺乏对跨平台、跨攻击策略的系统性技能泄露评测，也缺少针对具备简单探测防护平台的强对抗攻击方法。

Method: 构建可扩展基准：40项受保护技能 × 8个开源/闭源科学智能体平台，对比直接请求、固定策略与bandit引导迭代探测，并采用基于参考与无参考两类评测；进一步提出SEEK——黑盒、自演化的红队智能体，将响应中提取的证据固化于持久记忆，并演化可复用攻击技能，仅依赖用户可见回复。

Result: 泄露普遍：8个平台（100%）至少泄露一项受保护技能，其中4个在单次直接请求下攻击成功率达100%。SEEK在SpatialAgent与Biomni的12项技能上攻击成功率100%，平均首次成功轮次分别降低31.8%和38.2%（相对bandit引导探测）。面对阻断全部三种基准协议的保密策略，SEEK仍能提取6项受保护技能中的1项。

Conclusion: 当前科学智能体无法可靠保护其技能资产，静态/单轮防御不足；防御评估必须面向自适应、多轮攻击者，技能保密性应作为智能体平台的一类独立安全指标。

Abstract: Science agents increasingly package domain expertise as reusable skills that plan analyses, select tools, and execute workflows. This modularity creates a confidentiality risk: the interactions that deliver a skill's capability can also reveal the skill itself. We build an extensible benchmark of 40 protected skills across eight open- and closed-source science-agent platforms, comparing direct, fixed-strategy, and bandit-guided iterative probing under reference-based and reference-free evaluation. Leakage is pervasive: all eight platforms (100%) leak at least one protected skill, and four reach 100% attack success under a single direct request. To stress-test platforms that resist simple probing, we propose SEEK (Skill Extraction with Evolving Knowledge), a black-box, self-evolving red-teaming agent that consolidates response-derived evidence in persistent memory and evolves reusable attack skills, using only user-visible responses. On 12 skills from SpatialAgent and Biomni, SEEK achieves 100% attack success while reducing the mean first-success round by 31.8% and 38.2% relative to bandit-guided probing. Against a confidentiality policy that blocks all three benchmark protocols, SEEK still extracts one of six protected skills. These findings show that current science agents do not reliably protect their skills and that defenses must be evaluated against adaptive, multi-turn attackers.

</details>


### [109] [Whether or Where? Tail Calibration Signals Finite-Budget Reward Hacking in Best-of-N Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34375)
*Cheng Gui,Yihua Shao,Lei Li,Jingcai Guo,Maolin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将 Best-of-N (BoN) 推理的价值精确刻画为条件真实奖励在提示内分数分位上的 Beta 加权积分，证明 BoN 只依赖分数的秩，并给出 BoN 随 N 下降的充要条件与基于误差尾部分布的失效分类体系，进而提出一种对奖励分数单调重标度不变的尾部校准统计量作为审计工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BoN 通过取代理奖励最高的候选来提升性能，但 N 增大时实际上检验的是代理奖励的极端尾部而非平均排序质量。现有工作多关注平均排序指标（如排序准确率），缺乏对"更大 N 是否可能反而变差"这一尾部现象的理论刻画，也缺少可操作的诊断手段来判断哪些预算区间存在风险。

Method: 1) 理论推导：将精确 BoN 值表示为条件真实奖励关于提示内分数分位（rank 分位）的 Beta 加权积分，从而得出 BoN 仅通过秩依赖分数；2) 下降条件分析：证明 BoN 只能在校准曲线尾部向下弯折时下降，下降出现的预算位置取决于该弯折点的位置；3) 有界加性误差模型下的分类：高斯误差尾部持续改进、拉普拉斯误差达到平台、重尾误差回归先验均值；当误差方差在低真实奖励处最大时，即使高斯噪声也可使 BoN 低于随机选择；4) 合成相图验证该分类；5) 在多个可验证领域、基座策略与学习奖励模型上开展真实候选池实验；6) 构造留出集尾部校准统计量用于预测交叉拟合预算上限（budget cap）在何处有效，并与 Soft-BoN 基线比较。

Result: 合成相图与理论分类一致。在跨多个可验证领域、基座策略和学习奖励模型的真实候选池上，尾部失效很罕见，且全部出现在代码生成任务中。留出集尾部校准统计量能够可靠预测交叉拟合预算上限在何处有帮助，其表现与秩准确率大致相当，且该上限与 Soft-BoN 基线具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 尾部校准不能替代排序指标，而应作为一种审计手段：它对奖励分数的任意单调重标度保持不变，可指出哪些预算区间存在风险。BoN 的有效性由尾部校准而非平均排序质量决定，实践者应同时报告排序指标与尾部校准诊断，并对高风险预算施加交叉拟合的预算上限。

Abstract: Best-of-N (BoN) inference returns the candidate with the highest proxy reward, so a larger $N$ tests the proxy's extreme tail more than its average ranking quality. We show that the exact BoN value is a Beta-weighted integral of conditional true reward over within-prompt score quantiles. It follows that BoN depends on scores only through their ranks. It also follows that BoN can decline only if the calibration curve in the tail turns downward, and the budget where the decline appears depends on where that turn lies. Under a bounded additive model, the error tail sets the limit: Gaussian errors keep improving, Laplace errors reach a plateau, and heavy-tailed errors return to the prior mean. When error variance is largest at low true reward, even Gaussian noise can push BoN below random selection. Synthetic phase maps agree with this taxonomy. On real candidate pools spanning several verifiable domains, base policies, and learned reward models, tail failures are rare and all occur in code generation. A held-out tail calibration statistic reliably predicts where a cross-fitted budget cap helps. It does about as well as rank accuracy, and the cap is competitive with a Soft-BoN baseline. Tail calibration therefore does not replace ranking metrics. It is an audit that is unchanged by any monotone rescaling of the reward scores and shows which budgets are at risk.

</details>


### [110] [AndroidFlux: Evaluating and Reward Modeling Failure Recovery Capabilities in Mobile Use Multimodal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34384)
*Gyubeum Lim,Yemo Koo,Ghunwoo Jang,Zsolt Kira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 AndroidFlux——一个基于 AndroidWorld 构建的失败恢复基准，通过将已记录轨迹恢复到首个可恢复错误之前、当时和之后的受控检查点，隔离评估移动多模态智能体的错误恢复能力，并发布配套的奖励建模基准与离线强化学习数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动多模态智能体通常以"干净初始状态下的任务成功率"进行评估，无法分离智能体从中间错误中恢复的能力。由于移动环境是动态变化的，通用智能体必须在意外状态和未见环境中仍能稳健完成任务；这要求明确智能体何时、何处出错，以及能否从错误及后续状态中恢复。因此需要一个专门针对失败恢复能力的评估基准。

Method: 1）构建 AndroidFlux 基准，基于 AndroidWorld；2）将已记录轨迹回放至首个可恢复错误"之前/当时/之后"的受控检查点，使不同智能体从相同的设备状态与历史继续执行，从而保证可比性；3）在这些检查点上设计互补的奖励建模基准，利用下游 rollout 结果评估对恢复动作的偏好；4）发布一个离线强化学习数据集，用于同时提升错误恢复能力与整体任务成功率。

Result: 实验表明：智能体的恢复表现与其在干净初始状态下的成功率仅部分相关；显式推理（explicit reasoning）仅对经过 GUI 领域专用强化学习训练的模型有帮助。

Conclusion: 任务成功率不足以刻画移动智能体的稳健性，恢复能力应作为独立维度单独评估。AndroidFlux 提供了受控、可复现的恢复评估范式，配合奖励建模与离线 RL 数据，可用于诊断错误位置、改进恢复策略，并揭示了恢复能力与干净起点成功率之间的解耦关系。

Abstract: Mobile-use multimodal agents are typically evaluated by task success from a clean initial state, which does not isolate their ability to recover from intermediate errors. Because mobile environments change dynamically, a general mobile-use agent must complete tasks robustly even from unexpected states and in unseen environments. This requires knowing when and where an agent makes errors, and whether it can recover from errors and subsequent states. To evaluate this capability, we introduce AndroidFlux, a failure-recovery benchmark built on AndroidWorld. AndroidFlux restores recorded trajectories to controlled checkpoints before, at, and after the first recoverable error, so that different agents continue from the same device state and history. On these checkpoints, a complementary reward-modeling benchmark evaluates recovery-action preferences using downstream rollout outcomes. We further release an offline reinforcement learning (RL) dataset that improves both error recovery and overall task success. Our evaluations show that recovery performance only partially correlates with clean-start success, and that explicit reasoning helps only models trained with GUI-domain specific RL.

</details>


### [111] [Outcome-Aware Agents: When Does Causal Structure Help a Forward Model Foresee the Consequences of Actions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34385)
*Mallesh Madapathi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究因果结构何时有助于学习式前向模型进行安全规划：在已知真实因果结构的合成SCM中，将AC-LSCM（基于学习到的稀疏图逐因子建模，动作作为do干预）与三种无结构前向模型对比，发现因子等于观测变量时AC-LSCM能精确恢复真实图并显著降低多步预测误差与安全违规；但使用学习潜因子时反而劣于基线，且所有规划器仍远未达到oracle水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作用于真实系统的智能体在行动前应预测后果。作者希望厘清因果结构究竟在何种条件下能提升学习式前向模型的预测与安全规划能力，而非笼统宣称"因果模型更好"。

Method: 构建AC-LSCM：在学习到的稀疏图上做逐因子机制建模，动作以do干预表示。实验采用可获知真实后果的合成结构因果模型，任务由固定规则配置；规划器拒绝多步模拟结果不安全的候选动作。对比三种无结构前向模型，覆盖7个随机图（10或20个变量）；设置两个控制：do-wrapper（给所有模型相同动作语义）与反事实数据（给全部或不给任何模型），并做396组配对比较。

Result: 当因子即观测变量时，AC-LSCM在所有10变量图上精确恢复真实图，且在全部396组配对比较中三步预测误差均低于所有基线。相对oracle的额外安全违规：10变量图为其无模型水平的0.25（最佳基线0.31），20变量图为0.41（基线0.53）；对训练中未见过的两变量动作优势最大（0.18 vs 0.28）。使用学习潜因子时该模型劣于基线。

Conclusion: 因子选择比显式do算子或反事实数据更关键；所有规划器均显著落后于oracle。作者撤回此前版本的空图结论与"36倍安全提升"主张（该结论源自仅测动作值外推的任务），并发布代码与全部逐种子结果。

Abstract: Agents that act on real systems should predict what their actions will cause before acting. We study when causal structure helps a learned forward model do this, using a planner that rejects candidate actions whose simulated multi-step outcome is unsafe, in synthetic structural causal models where the true consequences are known. We compare the Action-Conditioned Latent Structural Causal Model (AC-LSCM: per-factor mechanisms over a learned sparse graph, actions as do-interventions) with three unstructured forward models on seven random graphs with 10 or 20 variables, a task configured by a fixed rule, and two controls: a do-wrapper that gives every model the same action semantics, and counterfactual data given to all models or to none. When its factors are the observed variables, AC-LSCM recovers the true graph exactly on every 10-variable graph and has lower three-step prediction error than every baseline in all 396 paired comparisons. Its excess safety violations over an oracle are 0.25 of the no-model level on 10-variable graphs (best baseline 0.31) and 0.41 on 20-variable graphs (0.53), and its margin is largest for two-variable actions never seen in training (0.18 versus 0.28). With learned latent factors the same model is worse than the baselines; the choice of factors matters more than the explicit do-operator or counterfactual data, and every planner stays far from the oracle. This version corrects an earlier one, whose model learned an empty graph and whose headline 36x safety improvement came from a task that tested only extrapolation of action values; we withdraw that claim. Code and all per-seed results are released.

</details>


### [112] [ParetoRSI-Gym: Can AI Agents Improve Language Models Across Multiple Domains?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34386)
*Haojin Wang,Yangzhen Wu,Zeyi Chen,Jian Xie,Jiaying Ye,Lili Meng,Shuyun Tang,Shangding Gu,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 ParetoRSI-Gym 多领域后训练评测环境，让智能体自主搜索、筛选数据并训练，在固定初始模型/领域/工具/流程下考察跨领域权衡，最强系统 Claude Code + Opus-5 使代理最优检查点的泛化分数相对提升 9.2%（+2.3 绝对点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归自我改进（RSI）需要智能体自主完成数据驱动的后训练闭环，但现有评测仅在单一基准上进行：要么从静态数据池中挑选，要么蒸馏更强模型以提升单一得分。真实后训练是多领域的，某领域的收益必须与另一领域的退化权衡，单基准评测无法反映这种取舍，也无法区分能力提升与对优化目标的过拟合。

Method: 构建 ParetoRSI-Gym 环境：智能体搜索数据、筛选整理、训练、读取分领域结果并迭代。除数据外全部固定——初始 LLM、五个领域（数学、代码、金融、医学、科学）、工具集与 SFT 流程。每个领域设置泛化基准，用以区分真实能力增益与对智能体所优化分数的过拟合。实验覆盖 3 个智能体系统、3 个基础模型、5 轮迭代及多种子。

Result: 最强系统 Claude Code 搭配 Opus-5，使其代理最优检查点的泛化分数相对初始模型提升 9.2%（+2.3 绝对点），按三个基础模型与五个领域等权平均；单领域相对增益最高达 63%（Ministral-3-3B 上的医学领域）。分析还显示：harness 的重要性与其驱动的模型相当，且各智能体形成了各自独特的数据选择策略。

Conclusion: 单一基准评测不足以刻画多领域后训练中的权衡关系；ParetoRSI-Gym 提供了可分离"能力增益 vs. 目标过拟合"的 RSI 评测框架，向真实机器学习工程场景下的 RSI 系统评测迈出一步。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) requires an agentic system to design, train, and evaluate its own successor. Data-driven post-training is a central part of that loop, yet existing evaluations measure it on a single benchmark, where an agent selects from a static pool or distills a stronger model to push one score up in one run. Real post-training is multi-domain, and a gain in one domain has to be weighed against a regression in another. We propose ParetoRSI-Gym, in which an agent searches for data, curates it, trains, reads back per-domain results, and repeats, with everything but the data held fixed: the initial LLM, the five domains (math, code, finance, medicine, science), the toolset, and the SFT pipeline. Generalization benchmarks in each domain separate capability gains from overfitting to the scores the agent optimizes against. Across three agent systems, three base models, five rounds and multiple seeds, the strongest system, Claude Code with Opus-5, raises the generalization scores of its proxy-best checkpoint by 9.2% relative to the starting model (+2.3 absolute points), averaged with equal weight over three base models and five domains, with a single-domain relative gain of up to 63% (medicine on Ministral-3-3B). Further analysis shows that the harness matters as much as the model it drives, and that each agent keeps a unique data selection strategy. We see our work as a step towards evaluation of RSI systems in real-world machine learning engineering.

</details>


### [113] [From Human Intent to In-Orbit Action: A Self-Learning Large Language Model Agent for 6G Satellite Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34387)
*Zhengqian Xu,Yasheng Zhang,Chenhua Sun,Shan Lu,Chengcheng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ALMOND 是一个"智能体化大模型编排"的认知框架，通过双向语义引擎把自然语言意图翻译为多目标奖励函数、并从失败的强化学习轨迹中反推隐性约束，配合大模型引导的图神经进化，在星上仅部署少于 5 万参数的蒸馏策略，实现 6G 低轨卫星网络的自主闭环控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G 低轨卫星网络的自主控制存在"高层人类意图—底层控制逻辑"之间的鸿沟：把"不间断北极视频"这类请求转成合法的强化学习奖励需数天人工试错；现有大模型翻译器又无法从其生成策略的失败中学习。同时星上算力受限，难以在轨运行大模型。

Method: 提出 ALMOND 框架：(1) 双向语义引擎，将自然语言需求转为多目标奖励函数，并反向通过反事实验证的"轨迹取证"从失败的强化学习轨迹中挖掘隐性运行约束；(2) 大模型引导的图神经进化，用于生成种子策略架构并对其进行评审判定；(3) 以地面为中心、算力节俭的架构，卫星只执行蒸馏后的少于 5 万参数策略，推理低于 15 ms，星上不部署大模型。在基于真实星历数据的 350 星数字孪生上验证。

Result: 极区切换成功率由 55% 提升至 95% 以上；收敛速度约为基线强化学习的 3 倍；奖励工程设计时间由 48 小时缩短至 4.5 小时。

Conclusion: ALMOND 在轨算力约束下打通了"意图—行动"闭环，兼顾大模型语义推理与星上轻量执行，为可信的在轨自主性提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Autonomous control of sixth-generation (6G) low Earth orbit satellite networks is hindered by the gap between high-level human intent and low-level control logic: translating a request such as "uninterrupted Arctic video" into a valid reinforcement learning reward still takes days of manual trial and error, and existing large language model translators cannot learn from the failures of the policies they generate. This article presents ALMOND (Agentic Large-Model-Orchestrated autonomous Network Design), a cognitive framework that closes this intent-to-action loop under on-orbit compute constraints. The contribution in artificial intelligence is a bidirectional semantic engine that translates natural-language demands into multi-objective reward functions and, conversely, mines implicit operational constraints from failed reinforcement-learning trajectories through counterfactually validated forensics, together with a large language model-guided graph neuroevolution that seeds and critiques policy architectures. The contribution in engineering is a ground-centric, compute-frugal architecture for autonomous 6G non-terrestrial network operation, in which satellites execute only distilled policies below 50,000 parameters with sub-15 ms inference and no on-board large language model. On a 350-satellite digital twin driven by real ephemeris data, ALMOND raises polar handover success from 55% to over 95%, converges about three times faster than baseline reinforcement learning, and reduces reward engineering from 48 hours to 4.5 hours, demonstrating a practical path toward trustworthy in-orbit autonomy.

</details>


### [114] [Dynamic Multi-Step Decoding for Time-Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34409)
*Birmjune Kim,Hyungju Lee,Hyun Oh Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种推理时动态策略选择器，为冻结的时间序列基础模型按实例自适应选择"一次性预测"或"自回归分块解码"的滚动调度，仅用主干隐状态与轻量选择器、无需微调，在 fev-bench 上显著提升点预测 MASE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列预测模型在推理时常用固定解码策略：一次性输出全时域可减少误差累积但丢弃序列结构，自回归分块滚动保留时序依赖但会放大误差。作者认为最优解码策略应随输入而变，应对每个预测实例自适应选择。

Method: 将预测滚动调度视为冻结预训练时序基础模型的推理时决策问题。选择器为学习网络，利用主干隐状态对候选调度排序，并针对每个主干在评测任务的历史窗口上训练。为在指数级时域划分空间中可行，采用动态规划将候选评估降至二次复杂度并保留大部分 oracle 增益。部署时由五个独立训练的选择器投票，按共识决策，不一致时回退到主干的一次性预测；全程不微调预测主干。

Result: 在 fev-bench 上用冻结的 Chronos-2 主干，一致性门控选择在 MASE 上相对一次性解码有统计显著提升，且主要体现在长时域；可恢复同一动态规划在真值选择下约 6-8% 的增益。用冻结的 Moirai-1.1-R-small 主干时，在整个基准上提升同样显著。两个主干均仅声称点精度收益。

Conclusion: 解码策略应作为推理时的自适应决策而非固定超参；结合隐状态驱动的轻量选择器、动态规划候选剪枝与多选择器投票门控，可在完全不微调冻结时序基础模型的前提下获得显著且稳健的点预测精度提升。

Abstract: Time-series forecasting models typically use a fixed decoding strategy at inference time: either one-shot prediction of the full horizon or autoregressive rollout over atomic chunks. While one-shot decoding reduces error accumulation, it can discard useful sequential structure; conversely, autoregressive decoding preserves temporal dependencies but may compound errors. We argue that the optimal decoding strategy is not fixed across inputs, but should be selected adaptively for each forecasting instance. We propose a dynamic strategy selector that treats the forecast rollout schedule as an inference-time decision problem for frozen pretrained time-series foundation models. The selector is a learned network that ranks candidate rollout schedules using hidden states of the backbone, and is trained on historical windows of the evaluated tasks for each backbone. To make the candidate set tractable over the exponentially large space of horizon partitions, we use dynamic programming that reduces candidate evaluation to quadratic complexity while preserving much of the oracle gain. At deployment, five independently trained selectors vote on each decision, and the policy follows their consensus, falling back to the backbone's one-shot forecast where they disagree. The construction requires only the backbone's hidden states and lightweight selectors, with no fine-tuning of the forecasting backbone. On fev-bench with a frozen Chronos-2 backbone, agreement-gated selection improves point-forecast skill (MASE) over one-shot decoding with statistical significance, mostly on longer horizons. It recovers about 6-8% of the gain that the same dynamic program attains with ground-truth selection. With a frozen Moirai-1.1-R-small backbone, the improvement is also significant on the full benchmark, and for both backbones we claim gains in point accuracy only.

</details>


### [115] [ScaleRLT: Exact Log-Probabilities and Fast Deferred Rollouts for Reinforcement Learning on Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34427)
*Dezhen Lu,Zixiang Ni,Runguo Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出混合学习器对数概率 ℓ = ℓ_dense + sg(ℓ_sched − ℓ_dense)，使循环（loop）语言模型的延迟调度rollout与稠密学习器之间的浮点不一致被逐位消除，在保持稠密梯度的同时将rollout吞吐提升至1.47–1.56×，并在GRPO训练中显著提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环模型（如Ouro）每个token复用同一层块多次。rollout引擎可把多个响应位置的深层循环批量化，仅在需要深层读出时执行（延迟调度），速度快；但on-policy RL的学习器用稠密前向重算采样token的log-prob，两者在浮点上不同：FP32、4096 token时约18%的token无法通过1e-5的同权重一致性门限，bf16几乎全部失败。需在保持延迟调度加速的同时消除该不一致。

Method: 采用混合对数概率 ℓ = ℓ_dense + sg(ℓ_sched − ℓ_dense)，其中sg为stop-gradient。其数值通过无梯度回放、严格按照rollout自身的调度计算得到，梯度则完全来自稠密学习器。另配合一拍KV准入与CUDA graphs，并针对多节点场景分析层到GPU的环形映射及其通信路径。

Result: 回放在三个平台的全部167个FP32与28个bf16真实权重单元中与rollout逐位一致（含停止token处批大小收缩的rollout）；在96–4096 token的102组预注册比较中梯度FP32误差与其他稠密实现相同；单步GRPO总损失与float64参考差3.1e-4。单GPU上延迟调度使rollout吞吐提升1.47–1.56×，全CUDA graphs下为1.18–1.54×且逐位一致。两节点GRPO中一次更新缩短2.71×且每次更新门限逐位通过，四节点在同每GPU负载下更新时间控制在2%以内。100次更新后held-out GSM8K准确率：混合深度读出23%→82%，原生读出34%→90%（后者以更少时间达到更高精度）。

Conclusion: stop-gradient混合对数概率可在不牺牲稠密梯度的前提下，把延迟调度rollout与学习器的浮点差异严格消除，实现逐位一致的on-policy RL训练与显著的吞吐与精度收益；对超过单节点的循环模型，环形层映射是一种可行方向。

Abstract: Looped language models such as Ouro reuse one block of layers several times per token. A rollout engine can batch the deeper loops of several response positions and run them only when a deep readout is due. This deferred schedule is fast, but the learner of an on-policy RL algorithm recomputes the sampled tokens' log-probabilities with a dense pass, and the two computations differ in floating point: in FP32 at 4096 tokens about 18% of the sampled tokens miss a $10^{-5}$ same-weight gate, and with bf16 weights nearly every token does. We use a hybrid learner log-probability, $\ell=\ell_{\mathrm{dense}}+\mathrm{sg}(\ell_{\mathrm{sched}}-\ell_{\mathrm{dense}})$, where $\mathrm{sg}$ denotes stop-gradient. Its value is a no-grad replay through the rollout's own schedule, and its gradient is the dense learner's. The replay reproduces the rollout bit for bit in all 167 FP32 and 28 bf16 real-weight cells we ran on three platforms, including rollouts whose batch shrinks at stop tokens, and the gradient has the same FP32 error as other dense implementations in all 102 pre-registered comparisons from 96 to 4096 tokens; the full GRPO loss of a training step matches a float64 reference to $3.1\times10^{-4}$. On one GPU, deferral raises rollout throughput by $1.47$ to $1.56\times$, and by $1.18$ to $1.54\times$ once every step runs from CUDA graphs, bit for bit. In a two-node GRPO loop, one-wave KV admission and CUDA graphs shorten an update by $2.71\times$ with the gate bitwise at every update, and four nodes keep the update time within 2% at the same per-GPU load. In 100-update runs held-out GSM8K accuracy rose from 23% to 82% with the mixed-depth readout that deferral relies on and from 34% to 90% with the native readout, which reached a higher accuracy in less time. For looped models beyond one node we analyse a ring mapping of layers to GPUs and measure its communication paths.

</details>


### [116] [Speculative Decoding for Mixture-of-Experts Models: Expert Bytes, Kernel Bandwidth and the Best Draft Length](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34440)
*Yuzheng Lan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文用"每步时间 = 内核请求字节数/有效带宽 + 固定开销"的解析模型分析MoE模型上投机解码的收益，推导出最优草稿长度随接受率呈单调阶梯结构，并给出带宽误差对结论的影响界（部分步骤用Lean验证）。关键判据：去重引擎更快当且仅当其字节占比ū(n)低于其带宽比r(n)。在llama.cpp Metal、MLX-LM与去重改造的llama.cpp CUDA上测得每槽内核带宽更高（r=0.32–0.75），故去重仅当每次验证5–8个token时才更快，否则永不胜出。路由轨迹预测节省时间，与实测在CUDA/MLX-LM全部点、Metal 36/48点吻合，交叉点预测12个配置中10个误差在一个pass size内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型上相邻token常选中相同专家；逐槽（per-slot）引擎每token读一次专家权重，而去重引擎每pass只读一次，理论上字节更省，但实测未必更快。作者希望给出可预测的判据：在何种接受率与草稿长度下投机解码（以及去重引擎）真正加速，并量化带宽测量误差对结论的影响。

Method: 1) 建立步时模型：一步时间=各内核请求字节/有效带宽之和+固定时间；2) 在几何接受分布下推导投机快于普通解码的条件，最优草稿长度为接受率的单调阶梯函数；3) 给出带宽误差对各结论的敏感度界，并用Lean机器验证主要推导步骤；4) 提出去重优越性判据：ū(n)<r(n)（字节占比 vs 带宽比）；5) 在llama.cpp Metal、MLX-LM、改造为去重的llama.cpp CUDA上，于单层专家算子单独运行时拟合并行带宽（每模型<1小时）；6) 用路由轨迹的字节数结合带宽预测去重节省的时间，并计算交叉点（首次使去重更快的pass size）。

Result: 三个后端上per-slot内核有效带宽更高，r=0.32–0.75，因此去重仅在每pass 5–8个token区间更快，其余情况永不更快。基于路由轨迹的时间节省预测在CUDA与MLX-LM所有测点上落在误差界内，Metal为36/48点；交叉点预测在12个（引擎、模型、深度）配置中10个误差≤1个pass size（预测为事后计算）。DeepSeek-V2-Lite上，投机运行之前测得的算子时间所给最优草稿长度在全部22个接受率取值上与真实最优相差≤1。

Conclusion: 去重是否加速由字节占比与带宽比的比较唯一决定：当去重内核带宽较低时，仅中等pass size（5–8）可行，否则无加速空间。该解析模型加一次性带宽拟合即可在小样本、低成本下预测投机解码的加速与最优草稿长度，且预测误差可被显式界定。

Abstract: Speculative decoding checks several drafted tokens in one verification pass. On a mixture-of-experts (MoE) model, consecutive tokens often select the same experts. A deduplicating engine reads each selected expert once per pass, so it requests fewer bytes than a per-slot engine, which reads it once per token. We write a step's time as the bytes each kernel requests over its effective bandwidth, plus fixed time. Under geometric acceptance, we derive from this step time when speculation is faster than plain decoding. The best draft length is a monotone staircase in the acceptance rate. We bound how a bandwidth error moves each result, and we check the main steps in Lean. Deduplication is faster exactly where its share $\bar u(n)$ of the per-slot bytes is below its ratio $r(n)$ of the per-slot bandwidth. On llama.cpp Metal, MLX-LM and llama.cpp CUDA, which we modified to deduplicate, the per-slot kernel has the higher effective bandwidth (r = 0.32 to 0.75). Thus deduplication is faster only from 5 to 8 tokens per pass, or never. We fit the bandwidths on one layer's expert operation, run alone, in under an hour per model. Bytes from routing traces over these bandwidths predict the time that deduplication saves. The measured saving lies inside its error bound at every point on CUDA and MLX-LM and at 36 of 48 points on Metal. The crossover is the first pass size at which deduplication is faster. The prediction places it within one pass size in 10 of 12 configurations of engine, model and depth. We computed these predictions after the measurements. On DeepSeek-V2-Lite, operation times measured before its speculative runs give the best draft length on CUDA within one at all 22 acceptance values.

</details>


### [117] [From Explaining to Enabling: Actionable Transparency in Algorithmic Insurance Pricing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34459)
*Agathe Fernandes Machado,Fei Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出可解释保险定价的三项要求（有效、可操作、可靠），将其形式化为公理，并结合保形预测约束构建反事实解释方法，在真实洪灾保险与车险数据上验证该约束推荐比无约束推荐更可靠且风险降幅更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习系统日益参与信贷、招聘与保险等对个人有重大影响的决策。已有研究关注保险定价机制本身的可问责性，但如何向保单持有人解释其算法决定的保费仍不清晰，且现有反事实解释在模型不确定的稀疏区域可能给出不可靠建议。

Method: 将"有效（按模型降低保费）、可操作（仅改变可控特征）、可靠（实际结果大概率达标的）"三项要求形式化为公理；用保形预测约束筛除模型过于不确定的推荐，从而界定不可靠推荐率；并将四种反事实解释技术适配于保险场景（仅修改可控特征、推荐样本似真实保单持有人），应用于57,832条佛罗里达真实洪水保险理赔与98,058条合成车险驾驶行为保单。

Result: 在高风险保单持有人样本中，带约束的推荐比无约束推荐更可靠，且伴随更大的预测风险降幅。

Conclusion: 保险公司可以为保单持有人提供既可操作又可信的解释，研究对算法问责文献有贡献，并为保险公司与监管机构提供启示。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) systems increasingly make consequential decisions about individuals, including credit, hiring, and insurance. Research on ML-driven insurance pricing has asked how the pricing mechanism itself can be made accountable. Yet it remains unclear how an individual policyholder's algorithmically determined premium should be explained to them. We argue that a useful explanation must be valid, actionable, and reliable. A valid recommendation lowers the premium according to the model. An actionable recommendation changes only characteristics the policyholder can control. A reliable recommendation has an actual outcome that is likely to meet the target, even in sparsely observed regions of the feature space where the model is uncertain. We formalize these three requirements as axioms and develop a method that turns an algorithmic premium into recommendations that meet them. The method meets the third requirement with a Conformal Prediction constraint, which screens out recommendations that the model is too uncertain about and thereby bounds the rate of unreliable recommendations. We apply four Counterfactual Explanation techniques to 57,832 real Florida flood-insurance claims and 98,058 synthetic driver-telematics policies. We adapt these techniques for insurance applications, so that their recommendations change only controllable characteristics and resemble policyholders observed in the data. In our illustrations, the constrained recommendations for high-risk policyholders are both more reliable and associated with larger predicted risk reductions than the unconstrained ones. These results suggest that insurers can give policyholders explanations that are both actionable and trustworthy. The work contributes to research on algorithmic accountability and offers implications for insurers and regulators.

</details>


### [118] [Finite-Sample Sensitivity of Kernel Metrics for Generative Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34477)
*Tomasz Radzikowski,Mateusz Modrzejewski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文将KID与KAD的默认核配置视为置换双样本检验，系统刻画出它们在给定样本量下能检测与不能检测的分布变化类型：在780样本/侧时对均值平移敏感、对保持均值与协方差迹的旋转几乎失效，并给出恢复敏感性的核带宽与核函数选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KID、KAD等核距离在嵌入空间比较生成样本与参考样本，模型优劣判断依赖其数值。但每个指标只配一个固定核，报告值本身并不说明该核在使用样本量下能检测哪种变化、不能检测哪种变化，因此需要量化其统计功效的边界。

Method: 在符号音乐、音频和图像的真实嵌入上，把各默认核作为置换双样本检验运行。对照样本施加三类变化：均值平移、各向同性收缩、以及保持均值与协方差迹且在传输距离上与平移匹配的旋转。比较固定核与替代核（更窄的高斯带宽、ℓ1 Laplace核、聚合检验）在不同样本量下的检验功效与一类错误率。

Result: 780样本/侧时，两种默认核在五个评估空间中对旋转几乎无检测力，但对均值平移在每次运行中均能检出。KAD所用的中位带宽高斯核能检出收缩，并在样本量高一个数量级时于图像嵌入上恢复对旋转的检测力。KID的三次核虽含二三阶矩信息，却在多数运行中漏检收缩，且在所测样本量范围内未找到高功效的旋转检测区间。更窄高斯带宽可恢复音乐与音频的敏感性，ℓ1 Laplace核对图像有效，聚合检验可在名义水平下同时兼顾两者。

Conclusion: 报告核距离时应同时说明样本预算以及该核配置在此预算下能够检测的变化类型。默认核配置并非普适于各类差异，应依据关注的差异结构与可用样本量选择带宽、核函数或聚合检验。

Abstract: Kernel distances such as the Kernel Inception Distance (KID) and the Kernel Audio Distance (KAD) compare generated and reference samples in an embedding space, and model comparisons rest on their values. Each ships with one fixed kernel, and a reported value does not say which changes that kernel detects at the sample size used. We run each default as a permutation two-sample test on real embeddings of symbolic music, audio and images, comparing a sample with an independent sample changed by a mean shift, an isotropic contraction, or a rotation that keeps the mean and the covariance trace and is matched to the shift in transport distance. At 780 samples per side, both defaults rarely detect the rotations in five evaluation spaces, while they detect the shifts in every run. The median-bandwidth Gaussian kernel of KAD detects the contraction and regains power against rotations on image embeddings with an order of magnitude more samples. The cubic kernel of KID contains second- and third-moment information but misses the contraction in most runs, and our experiments do not establish a high-power rotation regime for it over the tested sample sizes. Narrower Gaussian bandwidths restore sensitivity in music and audio, an $\ell_1$ Laplace kernel does so for images, and aggregated tests combine both at nominal level. We recommend that a reported kernel distance state its sample budget and the changes its configuration detects at that budget.

</details>


### [119] [Models Verify Sufficiency, Not Validity: Usable Falsehoods Evade the Checks that Gaps Trigger](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34488)
*Naveen Madiraju*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示大模型对"不可用/无依据前提"的检查与拒绝并非稳定能力，而高度受"可用材料是否存在"所驱动：保持源文支持不变、仅让无支撑前提变得可用，模型对缺失信息的标记率下降8-30个点，作答承诺上升1.5-2.3倍；且判分模型对删步解答的通过率高达78-99%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有"不可回答问题"基准几乎全部以"删去事实+评分其拒答"的方式构建，只能测出模型在信息缺失时的拒绝，却无法探测"事实未被源文支持但恰好可被利用"这一情形；因此既有评测无法区分模型是"核查了依据"还是"仅仅因无可用材料而拒答"。

Method: 核心设计为固定源文支持、只操纵"无支撑前提是否可用"：在GSM8K上跨两个模型家族、4个模型（2B–26B）做16组配对对比，并加Holm校正；另设两项操纵复现——事实以"陈述"而非"提问"形式出现、以及将中间值由"删除"改为"篡改"（后者亦在SQuAD上复现）；同时考查模型对自身缺口的言语化程度，并单独评估判分模型（judge）对删步解答的通过情况，以及一种提示词层面的缓解措施。

Result: 标记（flagging）下降8–30个点，对答案的承诺上升1.5–2.3倍，16组配对对比经Holm校正后全部显著；两项补充操纵均能复现，其中SQuAD上亦成立。模型能准确说出缺口（如"未提供任何财务信息"），却在前提变得可用后默默采用编造价格。判分场景下，必需步骤被删除的解答在各级规模上仍有78–99%被通过；提示词缓解使准确率下降22个点，却未提升检测能力。

Conclusion: 作者将上述现象归为单一行为：模型是否核查，在很大程度上取决于是否存在可用材料，而非源文是否支持。需说明的是，主设计并未指示模型拒绝题中引入的前提，故采用该前提也可解读为条件推理，论文据此界定了结论边界：所证明的是"可用性驱动的核查失败"，而非单纯的"轻信"；同时表明当前判分模型同样缺乏对依据的敏感性，且简单提示词手段无效。

Abstract: Ask a model "how many settlements did the Normans control?" and it answers that the passage does not say. Tell it "given that the Normans controlled 12 settlements of 400 inhabitants, what is the total?" and it computes 4,800. Nothing was added to the passage; the unsupported fact merely became usable. Benchmarks for unanswerable questions are built almost entirely by omission, deleting the fact a question needs and scoring refusal, so they never probe this. Holding source support fixed and varying only whether an unsupported premise is usable, flagging drops 8-30 points and commitment to an answer rises 1.5-2.3x on GSM8K across four models spanning two families and 2B-26B; all 16 paired contrasts remain significant after Holm correction. Two further manipulations reproduce it, one also on SQuAD: a fact asserted rather than asked about, and a corrupted intermediate value rather than a removed one. Models articulate the exact gap they then ignore, stating that "there is no financial information provided" for an inert premise and silently adopting a fabricated price for the same item. We read these as one behaviour: whether a model checks depends substantially on whether usable material is present, even when source support is held fixed. Because the main design never instructs models to reject premises introduced in the question, adopting such a premise can also be read as conditional reasoning, and we delimit what the design does and does not show. In the judging seat the pattern differs: asked whether the answer follows, judges pass solutions with a required step deleted 78-99% of the time at every scale tested. A natural prompt-level mitigation lowers accuracy by 22 points without improving detection.

</details>


### [120] [Approximate Unlearning in Prototype-based-classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34495)
*Sreejita Ghosh,Mohammad Mohammadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对原型分类器广义学习矢量量化（GLVQ），提出一种对遗忘集特征更鲁棒的近似机器遗忘技术，在6个表格数据基准上实现与重训练近乎无偏差（约0）的参数与性能，效率比朴素重训练高6倍至10^5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据与AI监管及治理需求要求从已训练模型中移除特定数据（删除请求，或被判定为噪声/损坏样本），而全量重训练虽理论上简单但计算代价高昂，因此需要高效的机器遗忘方法；同时作者发现原GLVQ遗忘方法会因遗忘集大小与数据特征而产生不稳定性。

Method: 先系统分析遗忘集规模及数据特性对遗忘后模型的影响，定位不稳定性来源；随后基于GLVQ的可解释性（原型）提出改进的鲁棒近似遗忘策略，使其对遗忘集特征不敏感，且无需修改GLVQ原有训练流程；并采用遗忘文献中的指标评估效能与效率。

Result: 在文献推荐的6个表格数据基准上，遗忘后模型相对重训练模型的参数与性能偏差约为0；效率方面，最小数据集约提速6倍，最大数据集达10^5倍。研究限于二分类问题。

Conclusion: 所提鲁棒遗忘策略可有效去除样本影响，兼顾高效与近似精确，并可直接应用于任何已训练的GLVQ模型而无需改动训练方案；未来可扩展至多分类问题。

Abstract: The recent data and AI regulations and governance needs have highlighted the need for removing specific data even from models trained on or with them. While retraining the entire model is a theoretically trivial solution, it is computationally expensive, thereby motivating research in Machine Unlearning. This paper introduces a robust approximate unlearning technique for the family of prototype-based classifiers called Generalized Learning Vector Quantization, aimed at removal of effects of specified samples, either due to deletion request or due to them being identified as noisy or corrupt. We first show the impact of the different sizes of the forget set, as well as the characteristics of the data in this set, on the unlearned model. To address the sources of instabilities detected in this unlearning method we propose an adapted version of this technique, such that the unlearning is robust to the characteristics of the forget set. Thereafter using the intrinsic interpretability property of GLVQ and appropriate metrics defined in unlearning literature, we show the efficacy (approximately 0 deviation of parameters and performance of unlearned model relative to retrained model) and efficiency (6X for smallest dataset to 10^(5)X in the largest dataset) of the proposed unlearning techniques compared to naive retrained, on six benchmark datasets for tabular data recommended by literature. Although in this work we investigated only binary class problems, the proposed robust unlearning strategy can be readily applied for removing sample effects from any trained GLVQ model, i.e., it does not require any modification in the training scheme of GLVQ.

</details>


### [121] [Best Policy Tracking in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34501)
*Judith Echevarrieta,Etor Arza,Aritz Pérez,Josu Ceberio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对单任务策略型强化学习中"非单调性能动态"如何影响策略选择进行实证研究，提出事后诊断指标量化性能退化与选择准则的准确性和效率，发现常用选择做法（最终策略或周期评估最优）显著偏离真实最优，并推荐受计算成本约束的基于验证集的选择策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习训练过程中策略序列的真实性能未知且可能剧烈波动（非单调），而标准实验实践通常只报告最终策略或周期评估中的最优策略，隐含假设这两种准则能可靠近似整个训练序列中的最优策略。这一假设缺乏检验，策略选择本身成为评估协议中被忽视的因素。

Method: 提出事后诊断指标（post-hoc diagnostic metrics），用于刻画性能退化程度并评估不同选择准则的准确性与效率；在标准连续控制基准上，使用成熟的同策略演员-评论家（on-policy actor-critic）方法，对比多种常用选择策略；并考察基于验证集、受计算成本约束的替代选择策略。

Result: 在该代表性实验设定下，广泛采用的选择做法与训练中遇到的最优策略存在相当大偏差，导致性能估计偏低并造成计算资源的次优使用；而受成本约束的验证式选择策略能更一致、更准确地逼近最优访问策略，同时提升选择效率。

Conclusion: 策略选择是强化学习评估协议中研究不足的关键因素；应推动显式、标准化的基于验证的策略选择流程，并对其开展进一步研究。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning commonly exhibits non-monotonic performance dynamics in single-task settings. During learning, a sequence of policies is generated whose true performance is unknown and may oscillate significantly across iterations. Despite this well-known phenomenon, standard experimental practice typically reports either the final visited policy or the best among periodically evaluated ones, implicitly assuming that these selection criteria reliably approximate the best-performing policy visited throughout the entire sequence.
  In this work, we present an empirical study on the implications of non-monotonicity for policy selection in single-task policy-based reinforcement learning. We introduce post-hoc diagnostic metrics to characterize performance degradation and assess the accuracy and efficiency of different selection criteria. These metrics allow us to evaluate commonly used selection strategies across standard continuous-control benchmarks using a well-established on-policy actor-critic method. Our results show that, in this representative experimental setting, widely adopted selection practices can deviate considerably from the best policy encountered during learning, leading to underperforming estimates and suboptimal use of computational resources. As an alternative, we recommend a validation-based policy selection strategy constrained by computational cost, which provides a more consistent and accurate approximation of the best visited policy while improving selection efficiency. Overall, our findings highlight policy selection as an underexplored factor in the evaluation protocols of reinforcement learning and motivate further study on explicit and standardized validation-based selection procedures.

</details>


### [122] [Ambiguous Fits: The Rashomon Effect and Challenges in Scaling Law Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34514)
*Bruno Mlodozeniec,Spencer Frei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出标准神经缩放定律拟合存在Rashomon效应（近优参数区域宽泛，外推结果差异显著），并提出确定性分支定界算法以保证近优拟合损失并穷尽刻画近优解对应的缩放曲线范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缩放定律拟合的最后一步常被视为常规流程，采用网格初始化加局部优化取最优解，但这类启发式搜索既无近优性保证，也掩盖了不同参数是否同样拟合良好。

Method: 证明标准拟合受Rashomon效应影响：近优极小值的下水平集对应宽泛参数区域，其大规模外推显著发散；进而提出确定性分支定界（branch-and-bound）算法，保证近优拟合损失并穷尽映射近优解所蕴含的缩放曲线范围。

Result: 标准缩放定律拟合的局部搜索难以保证近优解且掩盖参数不确定性；所提分支定界算法可实现近优损失保证并完整刻画近优参数区间及其外推范围。

Conclusion: 应将缩放定律拟合从启发式局部优化转向具有保证的确定性全局方法，并显式报告近优解的外推不确定性。

Abstract: Estimating neural scaling laws involves an end-to-end pipeline: designing a scaling recipe, training models across scales, evaluating models on a target metric, and deploying an algorithm to fit a scaling law to the resulting data. This final fitting stage is often treated as routine: standard procedures minimize a non-convex fitting loss using local optimization from a grid of initializations, and return the best solution found. Yet such heuristic searches can fail in two distinct ways: they offer no guarantee of returning a nearly optimal solution, and they obscure whether fundamentally different parameter choices fit the data nearly as well. Indeed, we show that standard scaling law fitting is subject to a Rashomon effect (Breiman, 2001): the sublevel sets of near-minimizers correspond to broad parameter regions whose extrapolations diverge substantially at scale. We resolve these issues with a deterministic branch-and-bound algorithm that guarantees nearly optimal fitting loss and exhaustively maps the near-minimizers to the implied range of scaling curves.

</details>


### [123] [MOFjson: An Explicit Topological Representation of Metal-Organic Frameworks for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34549)
*Anabel Yong,Matthew Hart,Philippe Schwaller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 MOFjson——将 MOF 结构分为含显式键平移向量的原子层与暴露 SBU 和网络拓扑的派生层——并构建四类问题的晶体真值基准；在四个模型上全面超越三个基线，消融平移标签后准确率降至 12.7%，证明模型读取的是周期图而非表层格式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MOF 语言模型表示将原子几何与结构拓扑分离，迫使模型在无结构坐标列表与非空间字符串标识之间二选一，无法同时兼顾几何与拓扑信息。

Method: 设计 MOFjson 双表示：(1) 原始原子层，带显式键平移向量以编码周期性；(2) 派生层，显式给出二级构建单元（金属节点与有机连接体）及网络拓扑。同时构建基于晶体结构真值的四类别问答基准，并与三个基线在四个模型上对比，另设平移标签消融实验。

Result: 四类别全部领先：最近周期副本定位 100% vs 18–47%，晶系识别 96% vs 71%，金属节点/有机连接体配位 60% vs 48%；消融平移标签后准确率跌至 12.7%。

Conclusion: MOFjson 让语言模型"读取"结构而非"重建"结构，为智能体工具工作流与受约束的 MOF 生成奠定基础。

Abstract: Language models for metal-organic frameworks (MOFs) rely on formats that separate atomic geometry from structural topology, forcing models to choose between unstructured coordinate lists and non-spatial string identifiers[1]. We present MOFjson, the first representation separating structural data into a raw atomic layer with explicit bond shift vectors and a derived layer exposing secondary building units (metal nodes and organic linkers) and network topology. Additionally, we introduce a benchmark with crystal-derived ground truth across four question categories. Across four models, MOFjson beats all three baselines in every category: 100% vs. 18-47% on reaching a block's nearest periodic copy, 96% vs. 71% on crystal system characterisation, and 60% vs. 48% on metal node and organic linker coordination. Ablating shift labels drops accuracy to 12.7%, proving models read the periodic graph rather than the surface format. MOFjson gives language models structure to read rather than reconstruct, establishing a foundation for agentic tool workflows and constrained MOF generation.

</details>


### [124] [Error Diffusion Replaces Latent Weights: Training Binary-Weight Language Models on a Few Bits of Dithered State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34566)
*Pierre-Olivier Bonin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出误差扩散（ED）优化器，用2–8比特随机舍入残差缓冲替代全精度潜权重，把二值网络的训练状态从97比特/权重压到3–9比特（最多19倍），在MLP、字符级Transformer及enwik8/wikitext-103规模阶梯上逼近全精度参考，并可在部署设备上以275MB内存完成继续训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二值权重模型部署时每权重仅存1比特，但训练需保留全精度潜权重副本与Adam状态（约97比特/权重），使已部署的二值模型无法在运行它的设备上被继续适配。目标是消除潜权重，使训练状态与部署形态一致，从而支持端侧在线训练。

Method: 用2–8比特、随机舍入量化的残差缓冲替代潜权重，将图像半色调中的误差扩散循环搬到权重更新路径：缓冲累积每次更新的未实现部分，累积值越过阈值即翻转权重。关键约束为"阈值对齐规则"——量化网格必须存在恰好位于该阈值的电平，否则失效。另引入每步缓冲更新上限以抑制启动阶段的不稳定。给出了融合CPU内核与250步探针以在新规模上设定翻转学习率。

Result: 4比特缓冲在二值MLP基准上与8比特持平；同一阈值对齐规则使另一潜权重无关优化器Bop从4比特下的10个百分点差距恢复到全精度精度。97%参数为二值的字符级Transformer上，ED在2000步预算内与潜权重参考相差5–11%，收敛时相差1.7%。enwik8/wikitext-103规模阶梯（至19M参数，含wikitext-103嵌入后70M）六个规模上损失相对参考位于−2.3%至+2.9%；无每步上限时最大规模落后10%。已部署二值模型在ED下以十分之一训练状态继续训练，效果匹配潜权重继续训练，融合CPU内核在275MB内存预算内完成适配。实测翻转学习率仅需250步探针即可确定。

Conclusion: 误差扩散式残差缓冲可在无潜权重、无Adam状态的前提下高效训练二值语言模型，显著压缩训练态并支持设备端继续训练；其有效性依赖阈值对齐规则与每步更新上限，翻转学习率可由短探针低成本设定。

Abstract: Binary-weight language models store one bit per weight, but training them keeps a full-precision latent copy of each weight plus Adam state, about 96 extra bits per weight, so a deployed binary model cannot be adapted on the device that runs it. We replace the latent weight with a 2-8-bit residual buffer quantized by stochastic rounding, which cuts the training state per weight from 97 bits to 3-9, up to 19x less. The resulting error-diffusion (ED) optimizer moves the residual-carrying loop of image halftoning onto the weight-update path. Each buffer accumulates the unrealized part of every update and flips the weight when the accumulated value crosses a threshold, and it works only when its quantization grid has a level exactly at that threshold. Under this threshold-alignment rule, 4-bit buffers match 8-bit buffers on a binary multilayer perceptron benchmark, and the same rule lifts a different latent-free optimizer, Bop, from a 10-point deficit at 4 bits to full-precision accuracy. On character-level transformers with 97% of parameters binary, ED trains within 5-11% of the latent-weight reference at a 2,000-step budget and within 1.7% at convergence. On scaling ladders over enwik8 and wikitext-103, up to 19M parameters on enwik8 (70M with the wikitext-103 embeddings), the latent-free loss stays within -2.3% to +2.9% of the reference at all six sizes once a per-step cap on the buffer update removes a launch instability; without the cap it trails by 10% at the largest tokenized size. A deployed binary model continues training under ED at one tenth of the latent training state, matching latent-weight continuation, and a fused CPU kernel completes the adaptation within a 275 MB training-memory budget, which latent-weight training cannot. A 250-step probe sets the flip learning rate at any new scale.

</details>


### [125] [Conditional Diffusion Generation of DVL and Inertial Signals for Underwater Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34574)
*Michal Levin,Itzik Klein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种条件扩散生成框架，合成DVL与惯性传感器信号以替代真实海试记录用于AUV导航深度学习模型训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AUV无法使用卫星定位，依赖DVL与惯性传感器进行运动估计；而处理这些传感器的深度学习方法需要覆盖AUV实际运动模式的训练数据，通常只能通过昂贵海试获得，数据稀缺成为瓶颈。

Method: 采用Elucidated Diffusion Model（EDM）作为生成器，以三个逐通道运动描述子为条件，生成DVL与惯性信号；评估三种配置：仅DVL波束、仅惯性通道、以及单一模型联合生成全部通道；在真实数据集的13条轨迹上验证条件一致性、跨轨迹泛化性和是否复制训练窗口。

Result: 生成信号与指定条件的Pearson相关系数在0.72–0.997之间，可迁移至未参与生成器训练的轨迹，且未复制任何训练窗口；仅用生成信号（不含真实记录）训练的BeamsNet速度估计器，其速度误差与使用完整真实训练集相当；将最多全部真实窗口替换为生成窗口也未带来可测的性能损失。

Conclusion: 条件扩散合成数据可有效降低AUV学习方法对海试数据的依赖，在记录稀缺时仍可训练，并有助于水下导航深度学习方法的开发与评估。

Abstract: Autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) navigate without satellite positioning and rely on Doppler velocity log (DVL) and inertial sensors for motion estimation. Deep learning methods for processing these sensors require training data covering the motion the AUV encounters, commonly obtained through costly sea trials. To overcome this limitation, we present a conditional diffusion framework that generates synthetic DVL and inertial signals to replace real recordings in training. The generator employs an Elucidated diffusion model conditioned on three per-channel motion descriptors. Three configurations are evaluated: DVL beams alone, inertial channels alone, and all channels jointly from one model. Across thirteen trajectories of the real dataset, the generated signals followed the requested conditions with Pearson correlations between 0.72 and 0.997, transferred to trajectories withheld from generator training, and did not replicate any training window. A BeamsNet velocity estimator trained on generated signals alone, with no real recording retained, matched the velocity error obtained with the full real training set. Replacing up to all real windows with generated ones caused no measurable loss. Thus, this approach reduces the dependence of learned AUV navigation on sea-trial data, enables training when recordings are scarce, and facilitates developing and evaluating deep learning methods for underwater navigation.

</details>


### [126] [Cumulative Marginal Matching for One-Step Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34575)
*Paribesh Regmi,Rui Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出累积边缘匹配（CMM）目标，通过在整个生成轨迹上直接最小化基础模型与少步模型的边缘分布Wasserstein-2累积差异，实现更优的少步生成，并证明Shortcut-CSL是其目标的一阶近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散与流匹配采样需数百步去噪，推理开销大。现有少步/一步生成方法的训练目标多只约束轨迹单点处的局部对齐（point-level alignment）；Shortcut-CSL虽引入累积目标，但仅在轨迹层面强制对齐，并未直接约束两模型在整个生成过程中induced的分布一致性，因此次优。

Method: 将流匹配视为连接先验与数据分布的一系列中间边缘分布；用Wasserstein-2距离刻画基础模型与少步模型在整条边缘分布序列上的累积差异。借助流匹配的pushforward表示，将难以求解的累积Wasserstein目标转化为可利用中间边缘分布样本（由条件插值过程易得）优化的可处理替代目标。理论上证明Shortcut-CSL目标为该目标的一阶近似。

Result: 在标准基准上，相同训练预算下，CMM在生成质量上优于Shortcut-CSL及其他先进的一步/少步基线。

Conclusion: 以分布级、全轨迹的累积边缘匹配作为训练目标，是少步生成更原则化且更有效的方案，并统一解释了既有Shortcut-CSL目标的理论地位。

Abstract: The sampling procedures of diffusion and flow-matching models involve hundreds of denoising steps incurring substantial inference time, which has motivated a growing body of work on training schemes for few-step and one-step generation. Existing approaches primarily seek to align the generation trajectory of few-step and base models, yet their training objectives only involve discrepancies at a single point in the trajectory, encouraging only local \emph{point-level} alignment. While recent methods such as \emph{Shortcut-CSL} introduce cumulative objective that enforce alignment over the entire trajectory, this is suboptimal as they do not directly enforce alignment between the distributions induced by the two models throughout the generation process. We propose \emph{Cumulative Marginal Matching} (CMM), a principled objective for few-step generation: by viewing flow-matching as a sequence of intermediate marginal distributions connecting the prior and data distributions, CMM directly optimizes the cumulative discrepancy between the base and few-step marginal distributions over the entire sequence. Specifically, we formulate this discrepancy using the Wasserstein-2 distance. By exploiting the pushforward representation of flow-matching, we transform an intractable cumulative Wasserstein objective into a tractable surrogate that can be optimized using samples from the intermediate marginal, which are readily generated through the conditional interpolation process of flow-matching. Furthermore, we show that the existing \emph{Shortcut-CSL} objective arises as a first-order approximation of that of the CMM, establishing a theoretical connection between the two objectives. Experiments on standard benchmarks demonstrate improvement in generation quality over \emph{Shortcut-CSL} and other state-of-the-art one- and few-step baselines under identical training budgets.

</details>


### [127] [Sparse Sum-of-Squares Layers for Certified Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34590)
*Ege Gursoy,Nicolas Mansard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了 Sparse SOS Layers：一类可微的 SOS（平方和）值层，其反向传播复用前向求解的稀疏局部坐标，避免构建稠密证书，从而在训练中保持 SDP 的稀疏收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习缺乏超越经验性样本评估的形式化保证；SOS 方法可通过 SDP 对连续域上的多项式不等式提供证书。已有工作把 SDP 层作为可微组件嵌入网络，但重复学习调用会丢失前向程序稀疏表示带来的计算优势。

Method: 在固定模板与可学习目标系数下，每层不构建稠密证书，而是沿用前向求解的稀疏结构与局部坐标完成反向传播，使稀疏结构在训练步骤间被复用。

Result: 总热启动调用时间最高 2.7 倍加速；较大问题规模下内存占用降低近 100 倍（1.7 GiB 对 160 GiB，针对所测试的稠密参数化）；设计研究在未见问题实例与规模上实现零证书违例，且优化求解次数比有限差分法估计梯度少约 7 倍。

Conclusion: 当反向传播尊重验证器的结构时，稀疏证书值可被有效集成到学习流程中。实验涵盖稀疏多项式认证、摆控制、组合松弛、机器人投影、振子链与位姿图同步。

Abstract: A central limitation of modern machine learning systems is the lack of formal certificates guaranteeing their behavior beyond empirical, sample-based evaluation. Sum-of-squares (SOS) methods provide such guarantees by certifying polynomial inequalities over continuous domains via semidefinite programming (SDP). While prior work has introduced SDP layers as differentiable components in neural networks, repeated learning calls can lose the computational benefit of the forward program's sparse representation. We propose Sparse SOS Layers, differentiable value layers whose backward passes reuse the local coordinates of the forward solve. Under a fixed template and learned objective coefficients, each layer avoids building dense certificates and reuses its sparse structure across training steps. Compared with existing methods, our approach achieves up to 2.7 times speedup in total warm-call time, with nearly 100 times lower memory usage (1.7 vs. 160 GiB) for relatively large problem sizes for the tested dense parameterization. Experiments cover sparse polynomial certification, pendulum control, combinatorial relaxations, robotics projection, oscillator chains, and pose graph synchronization. A design study achieves zero certified violations across unseen problem instances and sizes, while requiring roughly 7 times fewer optimization solves than estimating gradients by perturbing the objective coefficients and re-solving the problem (finite differences). These results show that sparse certificate values can be effectively integrated into learning pipelines when the backward pass respects the verifier's structure.

</details>


### [128] [Valid Early Detection of Memorization in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34617)
*Yixiao Jing,Chia-Yu Hsu,Milad Hoseinpour,Zekai Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究无条件下扩散模型中无需提示词的记忆(memorization)检测，将split conformal校准与序列监控结合，提出逐时间步Bonferroni校准与轨迹级最大值直接校准两种方案，并在CIFAR-10 EDM上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型记忆检测方法通常在预先选定的单个时间步上进行，无法随去噪轨迹自适应调整检测时刻；而序列监控虽可实现自适应检测，但跨时间步重复检验会带来轨迹级虚警控制难题。

Method: 将split conformal校准与序列监控结合用于免提示记忆检测，比较两种方式：(1)逐时间步Bonferroni校准，将虚警预算在各时间步间分摊以控制重复检验；(2)轨迹级校准，直接对记忆分数的时间维最大值（"曾经越过阈值"事件）进行校准。采用基于几何特征与表示"尖峰性"($spikiness$)两种互补的逐步统计量，在无条件CIFAR-10 EDM上评估。

Result: 序列监控相较固定时刻检测改善了检测功效与时序之间的权衡；与Bonferroni校准相比，轨迹级校准在检测功效相当的情况下更不保守地使用虚警预算，并能在更高噪声水平下实现更早检测。

Conclusion: 轨迹级最大值直接校准是无条件扩散模型序列记忆检测中更可取的虚警控制方式，兼顾功效与检测时效。

Abstract: Diffusion models can sometimes reproduce examples from their training data, a phenomenon known as memorization. Existing memorization detection methods for unconditional diffusion models typically operate at a pre-selected timestep and therefore cannot adapt the detection time to the evolving denoising trajectory. Sequential monitoring enables adaptive detection but repeated testing across timesteps complicates trajectory-level false-alarm control. We study two ways of combining split conformal calibration with sequential monitoring for prompt-free memorization detection: per-step Bonferroni calibration and direct trajectory-level calibration of the maximum memorization score. The former controls repeated testing by splitting the false-alarm budget across timesteps, whereas the latter calibrates the event of ever crossing a threshold directly through the trajectory-level maximum. We evaluate this formulation on an unconditional CIFAR-10 EDM using two complementary per-step statistics based on geometry and representation spikiness. Experiments show that sequential monitoring improves the power-timing tradeoff over fixed-time detection. Compared with Bonferroni calibration, trajectory-level calibration achieves comparable detection power while using the false-alarm budget less conservatively and enabling earlier detection at higher noise levels.

</details>


### [129] [Cross-Loop Query Handoff: How Later Loops Redirect Two-Hop Retrieval in the Ouro Looped Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34639)
*Ziyu Mu,Zehua Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在冻结的循环语言模型 Ouro-1.4B/2.6B 中，通过跨循环移植注意力查询证实：后段循环的查询向量集合可迁移地修复两跳检索，且修复机制偏向"桥接实体路由"而非"答案复制"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文探讨循环（looped）预训练语言模型为何能用后续循环纠正前段循环失败的两跳检索，即后段循环究竟通过何种表征载体、以何种机制提供修复能力，并检验其是否为可迁移的"交接"。

Method: 采用注意力查询/键值移植的因果干预：在同一层、同一注意力头、同一最终提示位置上，将 loop-3 的查询向量（按层索引的集合）注入 loop-1；设置打乱捐赠者（shuffled donors）、交叉双向交换（crossed donor-recipient swaps）、键值移植、以及将查询冻结在 loop-1 数值等对照。数据包括合成上下文问答与自然问题，模型为 Ouro-1.4B 与 Ouro-2.6B。

Result: 合成题上，注入 loop-3 查询集合可修复 90%（1.4B）与 97%（2.6B）在 loop-1 答错、loop-3 答对的样本，打乱捐赠者仅 24%/21%；2.6B 在贪心生成下同样成立，1.4B 仅限四选一排序。交叉交换显示修复更倾向于经由捐赠者的桥接实体检索，而非复制其答案，但 2.6B 存在较强的位置性成分。后期键/值移植修复率显著更低。冻结 loop-1 查询造成的两跳准确率损失大于提供桥接实体的匹配对照题。自然问题上修复幅度较小，仅 2.6B 可复现，且交叉交换未能确立桥接重定向。

Conclusion: 研究表明存在一组可迁移的后段循环查询，能在合成场景中支撑以桥接实体为条件的检索；但未定位桥接计算的具体位置，也不支持单向量"交接"假说。

Abstract: How do later loops in a pretrained looped language model repair two-hop retrieval? We study Ouro-1.4B and Ouro-2.6B by transplanting attention queries between loops at the same layer, head and final prompt position. On synthetic in-context questions, injecting the layer-indexed collection of loop-3 queries throughout loop 1 rescues 90% and 97% of questions answered incorrectly at loop 1 and correctly at loop 3, versus 24% and 21% for shuffled donors. The rescue also holds under greedy generation in Ouro-2.6B; in Ouro-1.4B it is limited to four-candidate ranking. Crossed donor-recipient swaps favor retrieval through the donor's bridge entity over copying its answer, although a substantial positional component remains in Ouro-2.6B. Late keys and values rescue far fewer targets. Freezing queries at their loop-1 values also damages two-hop accuracy more than a matched question that supplies the bridge. On natural questions, rescue is smaller and replicates only in Ouro-2.6B, and crossed swaps do not establish bridge redirection. These results identify a transferable collection of late-loop queries that supports bridge-conditioned retrieval on synthetic scenes; they do not locate where the bridge is computed or establish a single-vector handoff.

</details>


### [130] [Video World Models Need Predictable Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34663)
*Mattia Scardecchia,Sherry Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出将时序目标与过去条件化解码器引入自编码器训练，使潜空间既利于未来预测又保留像素级重建精度，从而显著提升视频世界模型的效率与泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型存在长时程 rollout 不稳定、分布外泛化差、推理计算开销大的问题。根源在于潜空间设计的两难：重建训练的自编码器潜空间未针对动力学建模优化，而为潜空间规划设计的可预测表征又牺牲了像素空间观测预测精度。

Method: 作者提出兼顾两者的方案：在自编码器表征学习中引入时序（temporal）目标，使潜几何尽可能简化未来预测；同时采用过去条件化（past-conditioned）解码器架构，保证对未来观测的准确重建。在相同训练预算下与生成式视频世界模型对比验证。

Result: 推理成本最高降低约5倍，训练速度提升超过7倍；在分布外及长时程条件下，像素误差与感知误差降低15–24%；使用视觉语言模型基于像素预测评判任务成功时，世界模型评估与真实世界策略评估的一致性更高。

Conclusion: 时序可预测性应作为视频世界模型潜空间的重要设计准则，论文给出了将这一准则融入自编码器训练的具体指导，实现了潜空间可预测性与观测重建精度"两全其美"。

Abstract: Video world models trained on large-scale robot data promise a safe and scalable approach for learning and evaluating policies that act in the physical world. However, they still struggle with long-horizon rollouts and out-of-distribution generalization, and they remain computationally demanding at inference time. Typically, video world models make predictions in the latent space of a reconstruction-trained autoencoder, which however is not directly optimized for dynamics modeling. On the other hand, world models designed for latent planning usually optimize latents to be predictable, but they forgo accurate prediction of observations in pixel space. In this paper, we show that it is possible to achieve the best of both worlds: a latent geometry optimized to make future prediction as simple as possible without sacrificing accurate prediction of future observations. More precisely, we find that temporal objectives for representation learning, paired with a past-conditioned decoder architecture, lead to consistent improvements in generative video world models at the same training budget. The gains include up to 5x cheaper inference, over 7x faster training, and 15-24% lower pixel and perceptual errors out of distribution and over long horizons. Our world models also agree more closely with real-world policy evaluation when using a vision-language model to judge task success from their pixel-space predictions. Overall, our study indicates that temporal predictability is an important design criterion for the latent space of video world models, and provides concrete guidance on how to incorporate it into the training of autoencoders.

</details>


### [131] [Exploring perceptual straightness in learned visual representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34678)
*Anne Harrington,Vasha DuTell,Ayush Tewari,Mark Hamilton,Simon Stent,Ruth Rosenholtz,William T. Freeman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探究神经网络架构、鲁棒性类型、生物启发滤波机制与时间变化输入下的表征"直化"（straightness）之间的关系，发现对抗训练能使CNN与Transformer表征更直，但该效应依赖任务；直化表征带来类别预测的时间稳定性，生物启发要素仅提升浅层直化度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类对随时间演化的自然视觉世界采用"直化"编码（Hénaff et al. 2019）：离散视频帧间变化在逐层加深的表示空间中沿更线性的路径变化。深度卷积网络常被视为人类视觉加工的模型，但许多网络并不能对自然视频实现直化，因此需要厘清哪些因素决定表征直化度，并评估"直化"作为评价网络表征手段的价值与局限。

Method: 以离散视频序列为输入，在CNN与Transformer架构上系统操纵并比较：网络架构、不同类型鲁棒性训练（如对抗训练）、生物启发滤波机制，并在分类、分割、帧预测等不同任务上度量表征直化度（帧间表示变化的线性程度），并考察直化度与类别预测时间稳定性及分布外数据表现的关系。

Result: (1) 对抗训练在CNN与Transformer中均带来更直的表征；(2) 该效应依赖任务，不推广到分割与帧预测等任务（这些任务中直表征不利于预测），也不推广到其他鲁棒性类型；(3) 更直的表征赋予类别预测时间稳定性，即使对分布外数据亦然；(4) 生物启发要素提升网络早期阶段的直化度，但不能保证CNN下游层直化度增加。

Conclusion: 直化度是一种易于计算的表征鲁棒性与稳定性度量，也是人类表征的标志性特征之一，并对计算机视觉模型具有益处；但其作为评价指标存在任务依赖等局限，需谨慎使用。

Abstract: Humans have been shown to use a "straightened" encoding to represent the natural visual world as it evolves in time (Hénaff et al. 2019). In the context of discrete video sequences, "straightened" means that changes between frames follow a more linear path in representation space at progressively deeper levels of processing. While deep convolutional networks are often proposed as models of human visual processing, many do not straighten natural videos. In this paper, we explore the relationship between network architecture, differing types of robustness, biologically-inspired filtering mechanisms, and representational straightness in response to time-varying input; we identify strengths and limitations of straightness as a useful way of evaluating neural network representations. We find that (1) adversarial training leads to straighter representations in both CNN and transformer-based architectures but (2) this effect is task-dependent, not generalizing to tasks such as segmentation and frame-prediction, where straight representations are not favorable for predictions; and nor to other types of robustness. In addition, (3) straighter representations impart temporal stability to class predictions, even for out-of-distribution data. Finally, (4) biologically-inspired elements increase straightness in the early stages of a network, but do not guarantee increased straightness in downstream layers of CNNs. We show that straightness is an easily computed measure of representational robustness and stability, as well as a hallmark of human representations with benefits for computer vision models.

</details>


### [132] [AT-AT: Automatic Tool Optimization from Agent Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34679)
*Maya Ravichandran,Pragya Tripathi,Dharamendra Kumar,Adith Swaminathan,Nathan Kallus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 AT-AT：以编码智能体读取历史执行轨迹，离线优化共享工具集的描述与（获许时）实现，在保持智能体不变的前提下提升任务完成率并降低成本与工具错误率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体改进多聚焦于修改智能体本身，但成功同样依赖其所在环境。工具是智能体与环境交互的中介：工具描述决定智能体如何调用，实现决定返回结果与错误。而 API、MCP 服务器等工具被多个智能体共享、由提供方维护，提供方无法修改这些智能体，因此需要从环境侧进行优化。

Method: 提出 AT-AT（Automatic Tool Optimization from Agent Traces），一种智能体式工具优化器。它基于历史智能体执行轨迹离线优化工具集，同时固定智能体不变；由一个轻量编码智能体读取智能体轨迹及其评估结果，有选择地编辑工具描述，并在允许时编辑工具实现。

Result: 在 τ²-bench-verified 上，AT-AT 在 Airline 与 Telecom 两个领域取得最高任务完成率，且成本低于强提示优化与工具优化基线，Telecom 上的提升在配对置换检验下统计显著。在编码智能体框架内优化工具也提升了 TerminalBench-2 与 TBLite 上的留出性能。在企业级三个生产 MCP 服务器上，AT-AT 降低了每个服务器的成本或工具错误率，并在其中一个服务器上提升了留出任务通过率。

Conclusion: 通过将累积的执行轨迹转化为对共享工具环境的更新，AT-AT 为"环境侧学习"式的智能体系统持续改进提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Efforts to improve LLM-based agents typically focus on modifying the agent itself, yet success also depends on the environment in which an agent acts. Tools mediate this interaction: a tool's description shapes how an agent calls it, and its implementation determines the results and errors the agent receives afterwards. Many tools, such as APIs and MCP servers, are shared by many agents and maintained by providers who cannot change those agents. We introduce AT-AT (Automatic Tool Optimization from Agent Traces), an agentic tool optimizer that improves a shared toolset offline from historical agent executions while holding the agents fixed. A lightweight coding agent reads agent trajectories and their evaluation results, and selectively edits tool descriptions and, when permitted, implementations. On $τ^2$-bench-verified, AT-AT achieves the highest task completion rates in the Airline and Telecom domains at lower cost than strong prompt- and tool-optimizer baselines, and its Telecom gain is statistically significant under paired permutation tests. Optimizing the tools within a coding agent harness also improves held-out performance on TerminalBench-2 and TBLite. When applied to three production MCP servers at a large enterprise, AT-AT lowered cost or tool error rates on every server and increased held-out task passes on one. By turning accumulated execution traces into updates to shared tool environments, AT-AT provides a path toward continual improvement of agent systems through environment-side learning.

</details>


### [133] [Beyond Deduplication: Sharp Retrieval Limits for Attention with Correlated Distractors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34682)
*Heehyun Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在两层广义随机能量模型（planted retrieval model，指数多高斯干扰项组织成已知、指数大的簇）中，将"冗余上下文影响注意力"的两种机制——重复项增加归一化质量、相关组内分数方差导致极端个体分数——区分开，证明了对簇大小的log扣除修正存在尖锐极限（信号下界√(2α₁)），而先平均后指数化可将下界降至√(2ρα₁)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冗余上下文对softmax注意力的影响常被笼统归因于"重复/多重性"，但实际可能来自两种不同机制：重复项带来的归一化质量增加，以及相关组内分数波动导致的极端分数。现有做法（如从pre-softmax分数中减去log簇大小）只修正了多重性，未处理组内极端值，作者希望厘清两种机制的独立作用并给出检索阈值的精确刻画。

Method: 构建植入式检索模型：指数多的独立高斯干扰项排列于已知的、指数大的簇中，整体为两层广义随机能量模型（two-level GREM），用经典自由能得到精确检索阈值；用高斯插值（Gaussian interpolation）分析固定token数下正相关对干扰项自由能的影响；比较两种去偏策略——pre-softmax减去log簇大小 vs. 先对簇内分数求平均再指数化；并扩展到加入指数多单例干扰项及有限层轻尾独立增量分数的层级结构；辅以有限样本、稳定性与参与度分析及冻结句向量的可控检索实验。

Result: 1) 固定token数下增加正相关会降低干扰项自由能，而与添加重复项的效果不同；2) 减去log簇大小的修正虽消除多重性，却保留簇内极端值：即使在观测全部分数后选温度，所需信号也无法低于√(2α₁)（α₁为簇数目的指数速率）；3) 先平均后指数化抑制波动，达到√(2ρα₁)（ρ为簇内相关）；4) 该差距在加入指数多单例干扰项后依然存在，说明仅凭组大小无法识别目标，并推广到有限层轻尾独立增量分数；5) 冻结句向量的检索实验支持该区分：当相关项为单例时平均有益，但当相关组含多个匹配项时会丢弃有用证据。

Conclusion: 冗余上下文的两种机制可被清晰分离，且log簇大小扣除类修正对组内极端分数存在不可逾越的阈值下界；聚合（先平均）策略在理论上更优但并非普适——其收益依赖相关项的结构（单例 vs. 多匹配组），实际应用需按组内证据结构自适应选择。

Abstract: Redundant context affects attention through two distinct mechanisms: repeated items add normalization mass, while score variation within correlated groups can produce extreme individual scores. We separate these effects in a planted retrieval model with exponentially many Gaussian distractors arranged in known, exponentially large clusters. The model is a two-level generalized random energy model, whose classical free energy yields exact retrieval thresholds. Gaussian interpolation further shows that increasing positive correlation at fixed token count lowers the distractor free energy, unlike adding duplicates. Our main result identifies a sharp limitation of subtracting log cluster size from pre-softmax scores: this correction removes multiplicity but retains within-cluster extremes. Even a temperature chosen after observing all scores cannot lower the required signal below $\sqrt{2α_1}$, where $α_1$ is the exponential rate of the number of clusters. Averaging scores before exponentiation instead suppresses these fluctuations and achieves $\sqrt{2ρα_1}$, where $ρ$ is the within-cluster correlation. The gap persists after adding exponentially many singleton distractors, so group size alone does not identify the target, and extends to fixed finite hierarchies of light-tailed independent-increment scores. Finite-sample, stability, and participation results complement the asymptotic analysis. A controlled retrieval experiment with frozen sentence embeddings supports this distinction while delineating its scope: averaging helps when the relevant item is a singleton, but can discard useful evidence when a relevant group contains multiple matching items.

</details>


### [134] [IsoAct: Geometry-Aware Post-hoc Debiasing via Isometric Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34690)
*Sumin Park,Taero Kim,Minhyeong Cho,Subeen Park,Jong Hwan Lee,Kyungwoo Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IsoAct：一种后处理方法，在切空间用FDA识别冗余方向，并通过流形原生作用进行编辑，以去除非欧几里得表示中的干扰因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表示对干扰因素（nuisance factors）的鲁棒性对可靠部署至关重要，但当表示位于非欧几里得流形上时仍具挑战。现有去偏方法通常假设欧氏表示或采用线性干预，会扭曲结构化潜在空间的几何。

Method: 提出IsoAct后处理框架，在干扰识别与表示编辑两个环节都考虑几何：在切坐标（tangent coordinates）中用Fisher判别分析（FDA）识别可预测干扰的方向，再通过保持流形归属的流形原生作用（manifold-native actions）施加修正。

Result: 在超球面、双曲与SE(3)表示的受控与真实实验中联合评估干扰削减、效用保持与流形有效性。IsoAct降低干扰可恢复性，同时保持有竞争力的效用；在KLIFS上取得最清晰的联合结果：激酶组可恢复性降低、结构预测强、刚性位姿有效。

Conclusion: IsoAct表明，在非欧几里得流形上处理几何一致性的去偏是可行且有效的，能在减少干扰信息的同时保持任务效用与流形合法性，为流形表示的后处理去偏提供了通用框架。

Abstract: Learning representations that are robust to nuisance factors is essential for reliable deployment, but remains challenging when the representations lie on non-Euclidean manifolds. Existing debiasing methods typically assume Euclidean representations or apply linear interventions, which can distort the geometry of structured latent spaces. We propose IsoAct, a post-hoc framework that accounts for geometry in both nuisance identification and representation editing. It identifies nuisance-predictive directions using Fisher discriminant analysis (FDA) in tangent coordinates and applies corrections through manifold-native actions that preserve manifold membership. Controlled and real-world experiments on hyperspherical, hyperbolic, and $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ representations evaluate nuisance reduction, utility preservation, and manifold validity jointly. IsoAct reduces nuisance recoverability while retaining competitive utility. Its clearest joint result is on KLIFS, combining reduced kinase-group recoverability, strong structural prediction, and valid rigid poses.

</details>


### [135] [Stable Continuous-Time Consistency Distillation: An Empirical Study with a Multistep Extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34704)
*Vinay Saji Mathew,Soundar R. Kumara,Gretta D Kellogg,William KM Lai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文对Lu & Song (2025)的TrigFlow连续时间一致性模型框架在CIFAR-10与ImageNet-64上做独立复现研究，并据此提出多步推广MS-sCD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间一致性模型可消除离散时间步网格带来的离散化误差，但训练长期不稳定；TrigFlow虽缓解了稳定性问题，却缺乏独立验证，且原文部分架构细节未公开。

Method: 从零训练TrigFlow教师模型，再以sCD（一致性蒸馏）与sCT（一致性训练）验证学生模型；分析参数量差异来源；提出MS-sCD——将sCM的两步中间时刻替换为固定分段边界调度、采用分段条件化的连续时间多步推广，M=1时退化为sCD。

Result: CIFAR-10上一、二步FID复现原文结果；ImageNet-64上存在差距，归因于教师模型与未明确的架构选择；发现论文所述模型规模与忠实重实现之间存在约5%参数量差距，源于Adaptive Double Normalization中的scale-and-shift条件投影。给出M=2、4、8的FID并与离散时间MSCD基线比较。

Conclusion: TrigFlow框架在CIFAR-10上可复现，在ImageNet-64上受教师与架构细节影响而难以复现；MS-sCD为连续时间多步一致性蒸馏提供了统一且可退化的新形式，代码与权重开源。

Abstract: Continuous-time consistency models are an attractive route to few-step generation. They remove the discretization error inherent to the timestep grid that discrete-time consistency models depend on. Until recently, however, training them was unstable. Lu & Song (2025) address this with the TrigFlow formulation and a coordinated set of architectural and training-objective changes. We conduct an independent empirical study of this framework on CIFAR-10 and ImageNet-64. No open-weight TrigFlow teachers exist at these scales, so we train them from scratch and then verify the consistency distillation (sCD) and consistency training (sCT) behavior of students initialized from these teachers. On CIFAR-10, our students reproduce the reported FID at one and two steps. On ImageNet-64, they fall short, a gap that appears related to our teacher and to architectural choices the paper leaves unspecified. We also report a parameter-count gap of about 5% between the model size stated in the paper and a faithful re-implementation, which we attribute to the scale-and-shift conditioning projections in Adaptive Double Normalization. We then propose Multistep sCD (MS-sCD), a continuous-time, segment-conditioned multistep generalization of sCD. The segment-conditioning idea is borrowed from the discrete-time multistep consistency distillation of Heek et al. (2024); we build on their preliminary continuous-time experiments within the TrigFlow/sCM framework. The rest of the algorithm follows sCD. MS-sCD replaces sCM's two-step intermediate time with a fixed segment-boundary schedule and recovers sCD at M = 1. We report FID at M = 2, 4, 8 and compare against the discrete-time MSCD baseline. Code, model weights, and training scripts are available on GitHub (Mathew et al., 2026).

</details>


### [136] [Sparse Reconstruction by Direct Measurement-to-Field Generation in Function Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34709)
*Linzheng Wang,Jason Chen,Nicolas Tricard,Zituo Chen,Xingsen Guo,Sili Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Direct Measurement-to-Field Generation（DMF），一种函数式rectified flow框架，从稀疏测量端到端生成物理场，在边界未知、缺高分辨训练场或不可直接测量量的场景下优于基线并可靠量化不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅由稀疏位置测量恢复完整空间物理场是难题：物理边界未知、缺少高分辨率训练场、关键量无法直接测量，且稀疏观测可能对应多个合理状态，导致显著重构不确定性。

Method: DMF采用函数式rectified flow，将稀疏测量到物理场的生成端到端学习；不依赖网格栅格化或运行时引导，而是联合学习sensor–global–sensor表示与生成传输，动态融合局部锚定与全局上下文，从而在任意查询坐标上评估物理场。

Result: 在涉及未观测边界、缺失高分辨率训练场和未测量物理量的基准上，DMF显著降低重构误差并保持物理属性保真度；其随机集成生成可在提升连续排序概率得分（CRPS）的同时可靠量化不确定性。

Conclusion: 该方法将稀疏重构从固定设定下的点估计推进到在不完整、变化信息条件下以测量为条件的物理场生成。

Abstract: Understanding physical systems often requires recovering their full spatial state from measurements available only at sparse locations. This reconstruction becomes challenging when physical boundaries are unknown, high-resolution training fields are unavailable, or important quantities cannot be measured directly. Sparse observations may also be consistent with multiple plausible states, leading to significant reconstruction uncertainty. Here we introduce Direct Measurement-to-Field Generation (\DMF{}), a functional rectified flow framework that learns how sparse measurements drive physical-field generation end to end. Instead of relying on grid-based rasterization or runtime guidance, \DMF{} jointly learns a sensor--global--sensor representation with the generative transport, dynamically fusing local grounding with global context to evaluate physical fields at arbitrary query coordinates.
