Across benchmarks involving unobserved boundaries, missing high-resolution training fields, and unmeasured physical quantities, \DMF{} outperforms alternative baselines by significantly reducing reconstruction errors and preserving the fidelity of physical properties. Furthermore, its stochastic ensemble generation reliably quantifies uncertainty while improving continuous ranked probability scores. These results extend sparse reconstruction from fixed-setting point estimation toward measurement-conditioned generation of physical fields under incomplete and changing information.

</details>


### [137] [Mechanistic Insights into LoRA: Layer Sparsity for Adaptive Fine-Tuning via Path Patching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34756)
*Fei Zuo,Yizhou Huang,Kezhi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文通过机制可解释性分析发现LoRA的作用在组件与层维度上高度稀疏且关键层具有强任务依赖性，据此提出基于冻结底座零消融校准的自适应层选择方法Adaptive-LoRA，仅用30%的层即超越全层LoRA，适配器参数减少3.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准全层LoRA在Transformer所有层施加适配，但各组件贡献异质、存在大量冗余；已有层选择方法多依赖穷举搜索，且未系统揭示LoRA对模型内部表征的作用机制。作者希望从机制可解释性角度理解LoRA适配的非均匀性，并据此设计高效、有原理支撑的层选择方案。

Method: 首先对LoRA适配效果进行系统的机制可解释性分析，量化组件级与层级的稀疏性，并比较不同任务间重要层的重叠程度；在此基础上提出Adaptive-LoRA：利用冻结底座模型上的零消融（zero-ablation）作为校准信号，估计各层对任务的重要性，从而自动识别任务特定层，无需穷举搜索。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-1.5B上针对11个多样化推理任务的实验表明，Adaptive-LoRA在仅使用30%层的情况下性能优于全层LoRA，适配器参数减少3.5倍；分析层面证实重要层跨任务重叠有限，即层重要性高度任务依赖。

Conclusion: LoRA的适配效应具有显著的结构稀疏性与任务特异性，全层适配存在明显冗余；基于校准的层选择可在保持甚至提升性能的同时大幅降低参数开销。该工作将机制可解释性与实际效率相结合，为自适应参数高效微调提供了原理性方法。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) has emerged as the dominant paradigm for parameter-efficient fine-tuning of large language models. Standard all-layer LoRA applies adaptation throughout the transformer, despite heterogeneous contributions from its components. In this work, we conduct a systematic mechanistic interpretability analysis to understand how LoRA affects model internals. We observe that LoRA's adaptation effect is highly non-uniform, with significant component-wise and layer-wise sparsity. Most critically, we find that important layers are highly task-dependent, with limited overlap across tasks. Motivated by these insights, we propose Adaptive-LoRA, a calibration-driven method that automatically identifies task-specific layers via frozen-base zero-ablation, enabling efficient layer selection without exhaustive search. Extensive experiments on Qwen2.5-1.5B across 11 diverse reasoning tasks demonstrate that Adaptive-LoRA outperforms full-layer LoRA while using only 30% of layers, achieving a $3.5\times$ reduction in adapter parameters. This work bridges mechanistic interpretability and practical efficiency, providing both fundamental insights and a principled approach for adaptive fine-tuning.

</details>


### [138] [TimeWorld: Towards Action-Conditioned Multimodal World Models for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34797)
*Hang Ni,Weijia Zhang,Hao Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出跨域多模态基准 TSWM-Bench 并揭示预训练时序基础模型隐含世界知识，进而提出插件式框架 TimeWorld 实现动作条件预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列世界模型依赖固定的领域特定协变量模式，难以灵活指定干预；多模态预测器仅将文本作为被动上下文。需要系统评估动作条件（干预条件）时序预测的能力缺口。

Method: 构建 TSWM-Bench：覆盖 7 个真实数据集，文本干预基于真实动作—结果数据构造。基于评估发现（简单文本前缀适配即可在多种 TSFM 骨干上提升），提出 TimeWorld 插件框架：原型引导的隐空间调制（PLM）通过组合代表性响应原型，依据动作与历史模式调制未来表示；动作对比学习（ACL）在相同历史的替代干预下进行对比，区分动作类型并保持相对强度关系。

Result: 常规多模态预测器尽管经过数据集特定训练，仍不如仅用历史数据的时间序列基础模型；而简单文本前缀适配在所有评测的 TSFM 骨干上均带来提升。

Conclusion: 预训练 TSFM 已编码时序动态的隐式世界知识，是动作条件建模的强基础，优于传统多模态融合；TimeWorld 以插件方式显式建模动作—响应，为灵活干预条件下的时序预测提供新范式。

Abstract: Time series forecasting supports decision-making in web applications such as online retail and network management, where planned actions can reshape future dynamics. World models provide a natural paradigm for predicting such action-dependent changes beyond extrapolating historical patterns. However, existing time series world models (TSWMs) typically rely on fixed, domain-specific covariate schemas that limit flexible intervention specification, while multimodal forecasters treat text primarily as passive predictive context. To investigate these gaps, we introduce TSWM-Bench, a cross-domain benchmark for multimodal TSWMs spanning seven real-world datasets, with textual interventions grounded in recorded action--outcome data. Our evaluation reveals that conventional multimodal forecasters underperform history-only time series foundation models (TSFMs) despite dataset-specific training, whereas simple text-prefix adaptations improve forecasting across all evaluated TSFM backbones. These findings suggest that pretrained TSFMs encode implicit world knowledge of temporal dynamics, offering a strong foundation for action-conditioned modeling beyond conventional multimodal fusion. Building on this insight, we propose TimeWorld, a plug-in framework that adapts pretrained TSFMs through explicit action-response modeling. Specifically, the Prototype-Guided Latent Modulation (PLM) module grounds textual actions in temporal dynamics by composing representative response prototypes to modulate future representations based on the action and historical patterns. The Action Contrastive Learning (ACL) component strengthens action-specific response modeling by contrasting these updates under alternative interventions sharing the same history, distinguishing action types while preserving relative intensity relationships.

</details>


### [139] [When Does Data Value Reduce to Class Balance? A Coverage View of Per-Point Data Valuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34801)
*Yingjie Bai,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 固定逐点估值分数取 top-k 的数据选择规则有缺陷，因为点价值依赖已选集合；应按标签可用性选择类别平衡或显式覆盖，并结合诊断判断适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Data Shapley、influence、leverage 等逐点估值被惯例性地用于选数据（保留 top-k）。但作者认为：在数据选择真正重要的场景下，固定、模式盲的逐点分数取 top-k 恰恰是错误规则，因为点价值并非固定数，而依赖已选内容（已覆盖模式中的近似重复点贡献近乎为零），标量分数无法表达这种交互。

Method: 理论：在等潜模式模型中，推导固定 top-k 相对自适应覆盖的闭式超额风险，刻画因预算反复投入已覆盖模式而产生的差距，并给出差距最大化的条件（预算紧张、表示冗余、漏模式代价高）。实践：提出规则——当选数据时可得标签能识别学习器模式，用类别平衡；不能识别时，用显式覆盖。在强预训练特征的标准分类任务上，对同一逐点分数做类别平衡；无标签时用无标签覆盖；标签比模式更粗时，在粗层内做覆盖。并将此区分转为预先指定的诊断，在有利与压力测试设定下验证。

Result: 类别平衡同一逐点分数可恢复缺失覆盖，在各数据集上匹配或超越通用集合覆盖；无标签时无标签覆盖最高提升 +0.20 准确率，而原始 top-k 可能低于随机；粗标签情形下粗层内覆盖最优。局限亦被验证：覆盖优势是表示相对的，当相关模式未体现在学习器几何中（如伪子群）或数据质量与覆盖无关时，覆盖会失效，与理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 逐点分数的 top-k 选择在预算紧、表示冗余、漏模式代价高时系统性次优；应以类别平衡或显式覆盖替代，并以标签可识别性为核心诊断依据。该优势是表示相对的，需在模式可由学习器几何表达时使用。

Abstract: Per-point data-valuation scores -- Data Shapley, influence, leverage -- are routinely used to select training data: keep the points with the top $k$ scores. We argue that top-$k$ by a fixed, mode-blind per-point score is the wrong selection rule precisely in the regime where data selection matters. The reason is that a point's value is not a fixed number but depends on what else has been selected -- a near-duplicate of an already-chosen point adds almost nothing, however high its score -- and a per-point scalar cannot express this interaction.
  The theory quantifies this failure exactly: in an equal latent-mode model, fixed top-$k$ selection incurs a closed-form excess risk over adaptive coverage because it repeatedly spends budget on already-covered modes. The gap is largest when the budget is tight, the learner representation is redundant, and missing a mode is costly.
  The practical rule is simple: use balance when the labels available at selection identify the learner's modes, and use explicit coverage when they do not. On standard classification tasks with strong pretrained features, class-balancing the same per-point scores recovers the missing coverage and matches or outperforms generic set coverage across our datasets. Without labels, label-free coverage improves accuracy by up to +0.20 while raw top-$k$ can fall below random; when labels are coarser than the modes, coverage within coarse strata performs best. We turn this distinction into a pre-specified diagnostic and validate it across both favorable and stress-test settings.
  The limits are part of the claim: the advantage is representation-relative. Coverage fails when the relevant modes are not expressed in the learner geometry, as with spurious subgroups, or when data quality varies independently of coverage -- exactly as the theory predicts.

</details>


### [140] [Precedence-Aware Resource Allocation: Extending the AUTOC Framework to Multi-Level Treatments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34812)
*Olin E. Gilster,Shengpu Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文将面向二元处理的AUTOC评估框架推广到具有优先约束（precedence constraint）的多级处理场景，提出ML-AUTOC指标与优先感知分配策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实决策中资源分配常涉及强度可变的多级干预，且高级别处理需先施加低级别处理，形成优先约束，使固定预算下的分配更复杂。已有CATE估计研究指出估计精度不等于有效分配效果，因此需要排序式评估；但现有AUTOC仅适用于二元处理。

Method: 提出Multi-Level AUTOC（ML-AUTOC），作为AUTOC在多级处理与优先约束下的推广；并设计优先感知（precedence-aware）策略，将真实的边际处理效应映射为固定预算下可行的处理分配方案。

Result: 在多级处理设定下，最大化已实现的处理效应可能需要在个体之间重新分配处理层级，而非沿用逐级递增的朴素分配。

Conclusion: ML-AUTOC为多级处理下的优先排序规则提供了跨预算水平的评估工具，配合优先感知策略可更准确地反映预算约束下的实际分配收益。

Abstract: Many real-world decision problems require allocating limited resources across individuals to maximize total benefit often through interventions that vary in intensity rather than binary treat-or-not decisions. In these settings, higher levels treatments require first applying lower levels of treatment, inducing a precedence constraint that complicates allocation under a fixed budget. While the causal inference literature has largely emphasized accurate estimation of conditional average treatment effects (CATE), recent work has shown that estimation accuracy alone does not guarantee effective resource allocation, motivating ranking-based evaluation frameworks. The Area Under the Targeting Operating Characteristic (AUTOC) provides an effective way to evaluate prioritization rules across budget levels, but existing formulations are limited to binary treatments. We introduce Multi-Level AUTOC (ML-AUTOC), which is a generalization for AUTOC designed for multi-level treatment settings with precedence. We also propose precedence-aware policies for mapping true marginal treatment effects to feasible treatment assignments under fixed budget constraints. Our results show that in the setting of multi-level treatments, maximizing realized treatment effects may require re-allocation of treatments across individuals.

</details>


### [141] [Can System-1-Style Decision Models Replace Dedicated Rerankers in RAG? A Systems Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34816)
*Qiaomu Li,Ying Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文考察能否用System-1式决策模型替代专用重排器：在截断候选文本长度后，部分决策模型配置能达到有竞争力的重排质量，但仅一种配置满足延迟要求，且调优后的重排器仍更快、显存占用更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统依赖专用重排器提升相关性，但重排带来额外延迟，团队希望寻找更快的替代方案。System-1式决策模型直接输出结构化预测而非生成长文本，理论上可用更低推理开销达到相近重排效果，因此需要实证检验这一假设。

Method: 先在11个基准上筛选21个开放权重模型，再在相同候选列表上对比一个有前景的决策模型与专用重排器；诊断默认配置在长候选集上的失败，并采用限制候选文本长度的策略；最后在新检索数据上进行冻结评估，同时测量延迟与GPU显存。

Result: 决策模型默认设置在许多长候选集上失败，仅对被完成的请求打分会产生有误导性的排名；限制候选文本长度可大幅恢复可靠性，并使部分配置具备竞争力。冻结评估支持可靠性与排名结论，但仅两种入选配置中的一种满足延迟要求。不同工作负载下更优模型会发生变化，调优后的重排器仍更快且显存占用远低。

Conclusion: 决策模型可以完成有竞争力的重排，但替代重排器需综合权衡质量、延迟与显存；实践建议是先调优现有重排器，再考虑替换。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems often use a dedicated reranker to choose which retrieved candidates reach the language model. Reranking improves relevance but adds latency, so teams look for faster alternatives. System-1style decision models make direct, structured predictions instead of generating long responses. Could one provide comparable reranking quality with less inference overhead? We first screen 21 open-weight models on 11 benchmarks, then compare a promising decision model with a dedicated reranker on identical candidate lists. The default decision-model setup fails on many long candidate sets, so scoring only the requests it completes gives a misleading ranking. Limiting candidate text length largely restores reliability and makes some configurations competitive. A frozen evaluation on new retrieval data supports the reliability and ranking results, but confirms the latency requirement for only one of the two selected configurations. Across workloads, which model ranks better changes, and a tuned reranker remains faster and uses far less GPU memory. A decision model can perform competitive reranking, but replacing a reranker requires weighing quality, latency and memory together, and teams should tune the reranker they already run first.

</details>


### [142] [Transporting Quantiles to Codebook: Scalable Vector Quantization without Codebook Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34828)
*Yuwei Zeng,Zekun Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlowVQ：将神经量化重构为“联合建模+聚类”过程，用轻量流匹配模型学习潜空间，由源分布经概率流ODE输运得到结构化码本，缓解码本坍缩并实现码本充分利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VQ-VAE的离散表示学习是现代自回归生成模型的基础组件，但普遍存在码本坍缩（codebook collapse）与码利用率低的问题，需要一种更优的量化方案。

Method: 提出FlowVQ：把神经量化表述为联合建模与聚类。训练时同步学习一个轻量流匹配模型，其对应一条保持概率质量的概率流ODE，从通常取固定良好形态的源分布出发输运；并在源空间按偶分位数划分先验码C_0，经学习到的流映射得到码本C_1，再在诱导分布下取Voronoi质心，形成结构化离散化。

Result: 在图像分词与生成任务，以及两个需要高精度分词的任务上进行评估，结果表明方法具备可扩展性，码本表达能力提升且被充分利用。

Conclusion: FlowVQ作为标准向量量化的简单有效替代，通过流匹配驱动的结构化离散化改善了码本利用率与表达性，并可扩展到多类分词/生成任务。

Abstract: Effective discrete representation learning with VQ-VAE serves as a fundamental component of modern autoregressive generative models. However, it is widely known to suffer from codebook collapse and inefficient code utilization. To address this issue, we propose FlowVQ, a simple yet effective alternative to standard vector quantization by formulating neural quantization as a joint modeling and clustering process. Specifically, we learn the latent space using a lightweight flow matching model alongside training, corresponding to a probability flow ODE that transports an often fixed well-behaved source distribution while preserving probability mass. Based on this, we define the codebook C_1 by transporting even-quantile partitioned prior codes C_0 from the source space through the learned flow, and obtaining the Voronoi centroids under the induced distribution, leading to a structured discretization. We evaluated on image tokenization and generation, as well as on two tasks requiring high-precision tokenization, demonstrating scalability and improved expressivity of the codebook with full utilization.

</details>


### [143] [Streaming Recurrent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34833)
*Mohamed Elsayed,Minh Pham,A. Rupam Mahmood*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出流式循环记忆 SRM，在"不存储原始经验"的流式学习约束下，用单步 BPTT 训练循环状态，实现对延迟反馈的有效预测与控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然智能可从高带宽、连续的感知运动流中即时学习并适应延迟反馈；而主流深度学习方法依赖存储数据集、回放缓冲区与保留上下文来做信用分配，在高数据率与紧内存/算力约束下难以实用。因此需要研究：流式学习器能否维持一个对未来预测和控制（含延迟反馈）仍有用的状态。

Method: 提出 Streaming Recurrent Memory (SRM)：一种为流式场景设计的循环记忆，保留"日后可能有用"的信息，同时可用单步（one-step）BPTT 训练。其递推被设计为支持按内容或时间检索过去信息、跟踪已流逝步数、以及组合结果依赖输入观测顺序的变换；关键设计是固定"输入如何写入循环状态"，只学习"如何从状态读出"。

Result: 在受控记忆任务与流式应用中，SRM 用单步 BPTT 即可有效学习；在联想回忆与在线语言建模上优于循环基线，在带延迟奖励的联想 T-Maze 中达到接近完美的成功率。

Conclusion: 固定循环状态中输入的存储方式、仅学习状态的读出方式，可在不进行长程反向传播、不存储原始经验的条件下实现有效流式学习，说明流式学习器能够维持对延迟反馈有用的记忆状态。

Abstract: Natural intelligence learns from a continuous stream of sensorimotor experience: data can arrive at high bandwidth, and behavior adapts to feedback as soon as it arrives, even when it is delayed. In contrast, most modern deep learning systems rely on stored datasets, replay buffers, and retained contexts through which credit is later assigned. At high data rates, storing and revisiting past experience can be impractical under tight memory and computational constraints. This paper studies recurrent memory under a streaming learning constraint: the learner learns from experience as soon as it arrives without storing raw experience. We ask whether a streaming learner can maintain a state that remains useful for future prediction and control from delayed feedback. We introduce Streaming Recurrent Memory (SRM), a recurrent memory designed for the streaming setting which preserves information that may matter later while remaining trainable with one-step backpropagation through time (BPTT). SRM provides a streaming-compatible recurrence that is designed to recall past information by content or time, track elapsed steps, and compose transformations whose outcome depends on the order in which inputs are observed. Across controlled memory tasks and streaming applications, SRM learns effectively with one-step BPTT, outperforming recurrent baselines in associative recall and online language modeling while achieving near-perfect success in associative T-Maze with delayed rewards. These results suggest that fixing how inputs are stored in the recurrent state, while learning how that state is read out, enables effective streaming learning without long-range backpropagation.

</details>


### [144] [When Respondents Are Scarce: LLM-Derived Semantic Priors for Bayesian Confirmatory Factor Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34843)
*Shaohuai Feng,Mohd Wira Mohd Shafiei,Mohd Sukri Shafie,Siyang Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出语义—经验更新框架（SEUF），将大语言模型对题项的改述与嵌入，结合假设的题项—因子对应关系，转化为贝叶斯验证性因子分析的预设先验，从而在小样本下提升因子结构估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会与行为研究中，目标人群规模小或难以接触时，招募额外被试成本高或不可行，导致可用于估计问卷因子结构的数据有限。而题项措辞本身在数据收集前就蕴含结构信息，能否在不替代经验验证的前提下改善小样本估计尚不明确。

Method: 构建SEUF框架：利用大语言模型的改述与嵌入，与假设的题项—因子归属对齐，生成预设先验并注入贝叶斯验证性因子分析。评估数据包括617名产业园区利益相关者的38题问卷，以及FEVS与RIASEC两项公开调查。

Result: 三种工具中语义结构在控制措辞后依然稳健。仅50名被试时，SEUF的载荷估计精度相当于传统CFA的70–82名被试，即在指定精度基准下减少29–39%的被试量；两项公开调查中载荷误差更低、可信区间覆盖率更优，且数据越有限增益越大。

Conclusion: 利用问卷中已嵌入的结构信息，SEUF提供了一种更节约资源的测量路径，为难以接触人群的研究设计与经验推断开辟新方向。

Abstract: Across social and behavioral research, measuring latent constructs can be challenging when target populations are small or difficult to reach. Recruiting additional respondents may be costly or infeasible, leaving researchers with limited data for estimating questionnaire factor structures. Yet item wording contains structural expectations before data collection, and whether these can improve small-sample estimation without replacing empirical validation remains unclear. We introduce the Semantic-Empirical Updating Framework (SEUF), which converts large language model paraphrases and embeddings, aligned with hypothesized item-factor assignments, into prespecified priors for Bayesian confirmatory factor analysis. We evaluated SEUF using a 38-item survey of 617 industrial-park stakeholders and two public surveys, FEVS and RIASEC. Semantic structure persisted after wording controls across all three instruments. With 50 respondents, SEUF achieved loading precision comparable to conventional CFA with 70-82 respondents, equivalent to 29-39% fewer respondents at the specified precision benchmark. Results showed lower loading errors and improved credible-interval coverage relative to conventional CFA in both public surveys. Gains were greatest when respondent data were limited, highlighting the value of semantic priors under information scarcity. By harnessing structural information already embedded in questionnaires, SEUF advances a more resource-efficient approach to measurement and opens new directions for study design and empirical inference in hard-to-reach populations.

</details>


### [145] [AgriDefend: A $205 Offline Edge Device that Detects Crop Stress Before Visible Symptoms via Multimodal AI Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34861)
*George Xu,Nikhil Vincent,Elizabeth S. Forbes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AgriDefend 是一款自包含的消费级边缘设备，融合冠层图像与土壤探针信号，在树莓派 5 上本地完成五分类作物健康预测，温室干旱试验准确率 87%，土壤信号比可见萎蔫提前约 4 天预警，物料成本仅 205 美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有田间作物监测设备多为单一模态（仅摄像头看冠层或仅探针读土壤），无法在地下部胁迫显现于地表之前进行检测，存在预警滞后与信息不全的问题。

Method: 采用多模态融合架构：MobileNetV2 CNN 分析冠层图像；随机森林分类器整合单一土壤探针的趋势特征（湿度、pH 变化率、N/P/K 变化率、相对于环境的本底 CO2 作为土壤呼吸代理量）；再由逻辑回归元学习器将两路输出融合为五类作物健康预测。全部计算在 Raspberry Pi 5 上以 INT8 TensorFlow Lite 完成，无云端或蜂窝连接。

Result: 在采用野生萝卜的受控温室干旱试验中，融合模型达到 87% 准确率（R²=0.88，MAE=0.05），INT8 TFLite 推理耗时 1.4 s；土壤信号比肉眼可见的萎蔫提前约 4 天出现。完整物料清单（BOM）为 205 美元，约为同类商业节点的五分之一。

Conclusion: 多模态、可持续的作物感知可落地为面向弱连接场景的实用消费电子产品；地下土壤趋势信号具备早期胁迫预警价值，且低成本边缘实现可行。

Abstract: Most in-field crop-monitoring devices sense a single modality -- either a camera looking at the canopy or a probe reading the soil -- and so cannot detect stress that begins below ground before it becomes visible above it. We present AgriDefend, a self-contained consumer-grade edge device that fuses both. A MobileNetV2 convolutional neural network (CNN) analyses canopy imagery, and a Random Forest classifier integrates trend features from a single soil probe: moisture, the rate of change in pH and in N, P, K, and belowground CO2 relative to ambient (a proxy for soil respiration). A logistic-regression meta-learner fuses the two into a five-class crop-health prediction, all on a Raspberry Pi 5 with no cloud or cellular link. On a controlled greenhouse drought trial with wild radish, the fused model reached 87% accuracy (R^2=0.88, MAE=0.05) at 1.4 s INT8 TensorFlow Lite inference, and its soil signals preceded visible wilting by about four days. The complete bill of materials is $205, roughly a fifth of comparable commercial nodes, positioning multimodal, sustainable crop sensing as a practical consumer-electronics product for connectivity-limited settings.

</details>


### [146] [Priority-Preserving Advantage Aggregation for Multi-Reward Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34862)
*Xiangchi Yuan,Xingjian Diao,Zheyuan Liu,Chunhui Zhang,Dachuan Shi,Kejing Xia,Zhenbang Du,Shenglong Yao,Soroush Vosoughi,Wenke Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 PAPO，一种优先级感知的优势聚合方法，通过非对称融合主/辅优势，解决 RL 后训练中"优势优先级反转"问题，在保持主目标最优的同时兼顾辅助目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 后训练常同时优化多个不平等优先级的目标（如正确性与推理效率）。现有 RL 中广泛使用的加性奖励聚合会破坏优先级：当主、辅优势冲突时，足够大的辅助信号可推翻主目标诱导的偏好，作者称之为"优势优先级反转"。而奖励层级门控虽能更直接地保护优先级，却会抹去未通过主条件的 rollout 样本中有用的辅助区分信息。

Method: 提出 Primary Advantage Policy Optimization (PAPO)：一种优先级感知的优势聚合方法，非对称地组合主优势与辅助优势。当两目标一致时保留辅助监督；当冲突时平滑衰减辅助信号，使融合时主符号得以保持，实现"主一致排序"（primary-consistent ordering）。该规则适用于 GRPO 的序列级优势与 actor-critic PPO 的 token 级优势。

Result: 在多个领域与多个模型上的实验表明，PAPO 在 GRPO 与 PPO 两类优化框架下均持续取得最佳的主目标表现，同时辅助目标性能达到最佳或具竞争力。

Conclusion: PAPO 为多目标、优先级不等的 LLM 后训练提供了一种简洁有效的优势聚合方案，兼顾了优先级保护与辅助信号保留之间的权衡。代码已开源。

Abstract: LLM post-training often optimizes multiple objectives with unequal priorities, such as correctness and reasoning efficiency. However, the widely used additive reward aggregation in RL can violate these priorities: when primary and auxiliary advantages conflict, a sufficiently large auxiliary signal can overturn the preference induced by the primary objective. We term this problem \emph{advantage priority inversion}. Although reward-level gating can protect priority more directly, it still removes potentially useful auxiliary distinctions among rollout samples that fail the primary condition. To address this trade-off, we propose Primary Advantage Policy Optimization (\textbf{PAPO}), a priority-aware advantage aggregation method that asymmetrically combines a primary and an auxiliary advantage. PAPO retains auxiliary supervision when the objectives agree and smoothly attenuates conflicting auxiliary signals, preserving the primary sign during fusion for \emph{primary-consistent ordering}. This rule applies to sequence-level advantages in GRPO and token-level advantages in actor--critic PPO. Experiments on multiple domains and models show that PAPO consistently achieves the best primary objective while maintaining best or competitive auxiliary-objective performance across both GRPO- and PPO-based optimization. \href{https://xiangchi-yuan.github.io/papo-project/}{Code is available}.

</details>


### [147] [Checkpoint Reload Explains Most of the Measured State Gap in Two State Discovery Reproductions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34891)
*Weijia Han,Lisha Qu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究World Machine中State Discovery的“训练-推理状态一致性”问题：训练时并行更新存储的每序列潜状态，推理时却从全零开始顺序rollout重建；在Toy1D上两个配对检查点实验的解码状态MSE比已发表值高27%–36%，而用“写入该表的最后epoch权重”做rollout可消除90%–91%的逐维Wasserstein-1距离，说明主要差距来自检查点重载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 维护每序列潜状态的时间模型在推理时若存储表不可用，需重建状态，由此产生基本一致性疑问：重建状态是否与训练中形成的一致？作者针对State Discovery（训练时逐epoch并行更新存储状态、推理时从全零顺序rollout）检验这一差距的来源。

Method: 在Toy1D上选取两个配对检查点运行；比较三类对象的解码状态：(1) 存储的状态表，(2) 训练结束后重载“最佳验证检查点”的rollout，(3) 用实际写入该表的最后epoch权重进行的rollout；以解码状态MSE与逐维Wasserstein-1距离度量差异，并检验因果蒸馏与冻结SD更新两项干预是否满足预设判据。

Result: 两个配对检查点运行的解码状态MSE高出已发表值27%–36%。将rollout改用写表权重后，平均每维Wasserstein-1距离下降90%–91%。因果蒸馏与冻结SD更新在两种检查点下均未满足预设判据。

Conclusion: 在这些运行中，检查点重载（即权重版本与写表权重不匹配）解释了所测差距的大部分，而非rollout机制或状态更新规则本身；作者据此提示复现与报告时应明确rollout所用权重版本，并对因果蒸馏/冻结SD的有效性持保留态度。

Abstract: Temporal models that maintain per-sequence latent states during training must reconstruct those states when the stored table is unavailable at inference. This creates a basic consistency question: do the reconstructed states agree with those formed during training? We study this question for State Discovery in World Machine, which updates stored states in parallel each epoch but performs inference by sequential rollout from an all-zero start. On Toy1D, we analyze two paired-checkpoint runs whose decoded-state MSE is 27 to 36 percent above the published value. We compare the stored table with rollouts under the best-validation checkpoint reloaded after training and the last-epoch weights that wrote the table. Matching the rollout to the table-writing weights removes 90 to 91 percent of the mean per-dimension Wasserstein-1 distance. Causal distillation and frozen SD updates do not satisfy their pre-specified criteria under either checkpoint. Overall, checkpoint reload accounts for most of the measured gap in these runs.

</details>


### [148] [End-to-End MXFP8 Reinforcement Learning on Blackwell: What Matching Buys, When It Pays Off, and How It Fails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34915)
*Wengee Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在 verl 中实现端到端 MXFP8 RL（Megatron/TE 训练 + vLLM/SGLang 推理），在 B200 上以 Qwen3-8B 与 Qwen3-30B-A3B 量化评估：采样器与学习器同量化网格可显著降低 train-rollout KL，MXFP8 的收益随 micro-batch 规模出现拐点（dense 约 4.5k tokens），MoE 端到端提速明显，并给出权重同步必须保持的七条正确性条件及检测手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低精度算术可降低大模型 RL 的成本，但 RL 要求采样器（rollout）与学习器（learner）在同一数值语义下达成一致；已有框架在两侧都跑 FP8，却缺乏对"这种设计究竟带来什么收益、何时更快、如何失败"的系统性实测与正确性刻画。

Method: 在 verl 中实现端到端 MXFP8 RL：训练侧用 Megatron/Transformer Engine，推理侧用 vLLM 与 SGLang，覆盖 dense 与 MoE 模型；在 B200 上以 Qwen3-8B、Qwen3-30B-A3B 测试。实验包括：固定权重下比较 BF16 采样器与不同比例（1/2、全部）线性层置于学习器量化网格的 train-rollout KL；跨网格 FP8 管线的 MoE KL 对比；micro-batch token 数扫描与 host 等待的 trace 分析；以及形式化七条权重同步必须满足的条件并配套在线引擎校验、硬件故障复现与 CPU 故障注入。

Result: (1) 匹配性：单引擎固定权重下，半数线性层同网格使 train-rollout KL 降低 11%，全部同网格降低 16%；同步后的权重与学习器 FP8 前向权重逐字节一致；MoE 模型上使用不同网格的 FP8 管线从首步起 KL 即为同网格管线的两倍；无量化 KV cache 时，端到端 BF16 的一致性仍高 2.5–10 倍。(2) 速度：MXFP8 训练有每 micro-batch 固定开销与每 token 节省，dense 设定下需超过约 4.5k tokens/micro-batch 才划算，trace 显示新增时间主要来自 GPU 等待 host；端到端 MoE 步时降低 10.7%、生成时间降低 24%，短序列 dense 负载反而慢 5%。(3) 正确性：七条条件可被配套检查逐条验证；硬件上遇到的七次失败各违反一条条件，其中五次为静默失败；检查在首次机会即捕获两次、且互为互补，CPU 故障注入复现其中五次。

Conclusion: MXFP8 RL 的收益高度依赖工作负载：采样器与学习器共享量化网格是保证一致性的关键，但与 BF16 端到端相比仍需量化 KV cache 才能缩小一致性差距；速度收益在 MoE 与长序列上显著，短序列 dense 场景反而受损，且瓶颈部分来自 host 侧而非算力。权重同步的正确性可由七条可检验条件覆盖，多数失败是静默的，因此在线检查与故障注入应作为低精度 RL 流水线的必备组件。结果均为单随机种子、最多 60 步，作者不对最终模型质量作结论，需更长训练与多次重复验证。

Abstract: Low-precision arithmetic can make reinforcement learning (RL) for large language models cheaper, but RL requires the sampler and the learner to agree. Several frameworks now run FP8 on both sides. We implement end-to-end MXFP8 RL in verl (Megatron/Transformer Engine training; vLLM and SGLang rollout; dense and MoE models) and measure on B200 GPUs, with Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-30B-A3B, what this design buys, when it is faster, and how it fails. (1) Matching. For an MXFP8 learner, a sampler on the learner's grid is more consistent than a higher-precision BF16 sampler: in one engine at fixed weights, train-rollout KL is 11% lower with half of the sampler's linear layers on the grid and 16% lower with all of them, and the synchronized weights we checked equal the learner's FP8 forward weights byte for byte. On the MoE model, an FP8 pipeline whose sampler uses another grid has twice the shared-grid pipeline's KL from the first step. Without a quantized KV cache, BF16 end to end remains 2.5-10x more consistent. (2) Speed. MXFP8 training adds a fixed cost per micro-batch and saves a per-token cost, so in our dense setting it pays off only above about 4.5k tokens per micro-batch; a micro-batch sweep on identical data confirms the per-micro-batch cost, and a trace shows the GPU waiting for the host for most of the added time. End to end, MXFP8 cuts MoE step time by 10.7% and generation time by 24%, and slows a short-sequence dense workload by 5%. (3) Correctness. We state seven conditions every weight synchronization must preserve and checks that test them against the live engine. Each of the seven failures we met on hardware broke one condition, five silently; the checks caught two at their first opportunity, each missed by the other checks, and CPU fault-injection tests reproduce five. All runs are single-seed and at most 60 steps long; we make no claim about final model quality.

</details>


### [149] [A Certified Robustness Framework for Backdoor Poisoning Attacks using $f$-Divergences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34934)
*Evan Rose,Alina Oprea*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个通用的随机平滑框架，通过对数据子采样与特征、标签、测试样本加噪的组合来随机化输入，并基于 f-散度给出可组合的认证程序，首次实现一般 ℓp 约束投毒威胁下 bagging + 加性噪声的鲁棒性认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对训练时鲁棒性的随机平滑应用局限于特定威胁模型和特定平滑机制，缺乏能统一表达复杂对手约束（被污染训练样本数量、特征与标签扰动幅度、测试时污染）的通用框架。

Method: 框架通过结合数据子采样（bagging）与特征噪声、标签噪声、测试样本噪声实现输入随机化；发展基于 f-散度的可组合认证流程，可处理任意威胁模型与加噪策略的组合，并将多种已有认证机制精确恢复为特例。

Result: 实验上首次在一般 ℓp 约束的投毒对手下，完成 bagging 结合加性 Laplace、Gaussian 与 Uniform 噪声的鲁棒性认证。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了统一、可组合的随机平滑认证框架，可精确涵盖既有认证方案作为特例，并拓展到更一般的投毒威胁模型。

Abstract: Machine learning systems trained on untrusted data are vulnerable to data and backdoor poisoning attacks, in which an adversary corrupts training and test samples to manipulate predictions at inference time. Randomized smoothing yields provable defenses against such attacks, but existing applications to train-time robustness are limited to specific threat models and smoothing mechanisms. We propose a general framework that allows expressing complex adversary constraints on the number of corrupted training examples, feature and label perturbation magnitudes, and test-time corruptions. Our framework randomizes inputs by combining data subsampling with feature, label and test-sample noising. We develop a composable certification procedure based on $f$-divergences that handles arbitrary combinations of threat models and noising strategies, and recovers several prior certification regimes exactly as special cases. We demonstrate our framework experimentally by certifying, for the first time, robustness under general $\ell_p$-constrained poisoning adversaries under bagging with additive Laplace, Gaussian, and Uniform noises.

</details>


### [150] [Relational Composition as Parallel Transport: What Per-Entity Frames Force in Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34945)
*Aakash Sarkar,Ananya Mukherjee,Motoshige Sato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文把"每个实体拥有独立坐标架"视为注意力中的对称性，推导不变分数与等变消息的普适形式，并用表示论给出机制（读出函数）能精确复合群运算所需的最小维度阈值及可预计算精度上界，最后在教学任务与合成/语义任务上验证阈值效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内容词共享坐标架而新实体没有，孤立旋转坐标不改变任何量，因此"逐实体坐标自由"应被视为对称性；作者追问该对称性在注意力机制中强制了什么结构、以及机制能表达哪些关系复合。

Method: 以逐实体正交传输帧为对称群：(1) 推导不变量只依赖范数与传输内积 ⟨x_i, T_ij x_j⟩，等变消息落在 span{x_i, T_ij x_j} 中；(2) 端点通道数约束传输秩，单向量实体必须外部提供关系，标准注意力对应"共享帧的平坦平凡扇区"；(3) 用表示论为每个机制赋整数维阈值，分析凸胞读出、偶读出（可解码覆盖）、仿射/二次读出；(4) 实验：合成群任务扫描宽度与种子、StepGame、解析版 CLUTRR。

Result: 正交传输下最小宽度处 17/17 拟合种子通过长度 1000 测试，两倍宽度仅 7/18；12 个模型从权重本身获得到显式长度的正确性证书。C3×A4（忠实宽度 5）上宽度 4 降维学习 72 状态双覆盖：符号盲注意力 6/48 种子，单调变体 0/48；冻结覆盖上交叉熵最优仿射头为均匀，二次头精确。CLUTRR 上传输复合在 4–10 跳领先匹配 Transformer 41 分，但强制等距损失 65 分；StepGame 交换代数中等距有益。低于阈值时不可见部分给出训练前可算的精度上限。

Conclusion: 逐实体帧使每条关系成为一个算子，即关系复合的天然单位：静态嵌入给每词一向量，上下文模型给每次出现一向量，而帧语义给每个关系一个算子。表示论维度阈值决定了机制的可达精度与是否需要更宽的读出或结构约束。

Abstract: A dot product means something only when both vectors share a coordinate system. Content words have one; a fresh entity does not: rotating its coordinates alone changes nothing. We take this per-entity frame freedom as a symmetry and ask what it forces in attention. For orthogonal transports, invariant scores depend only on norms and the transported inner product $\langle x_i,T_{ij}x_j\rangle$; equivariant messages lie in the span of $x_i$ and $T_{ij}x_j$. Endpoint channel count bounds transport rank, so with one vector per entity relations must be supplied; standard attention is the flat sector of a shared frame. Representation theory attaches an integer to each mechanism; the readout decides which. With orthogonal transitions, a convex-cell readout composes a finite group exactly only at or above its smallest faithful real representation dimension. At this minimum, exactness forces a faithful representation. An even readout can decode a cover at smaller width, and the gap is unbounded. Below its threshold, the part of the group a mechanism cannot see sets an accuracy ceiling computable before training. 17/17 fitting seeds at minimal width passed tests through length 1000, versus 7/18 at twice the width; 12 models carry correctness certificates to explicit lengths, from the weights alone. On $C_3\times A_4$ (faithful width 5), descent at width 4 learned the 72-state double cover under sign-blind attention (6/48 seeds; monotone 0/48); on a frozen cover the cross-entropy-optimal affine head is uniform, a quadratic head exact. On parsed CLUTRR, transported composition beats a matched transformer by 41 points at 4-10 hops, but forcing isometries costs 65 points; on StepGame's commutative algebra, isometries help. Static embeddings gave each word one vector; contextual models gave each occurrence its own; per-entity frames make each relation an operator, the unit in which relations compose.

</details>


### [151] [Correlation-Aware Exploration for Long-Trace Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34949)
*Junxing Hu,Zicheng Zhang,Zhiqian Zhang,Ai Han,Yifeng Zhang,Pengzhang Liu,Ling Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Info-GRPO：以潜变量与轨迹的互信息构建"种子条件熵"引导机制，把多样性放在整条推理轨迹层面而非逐token随机性上，缓解GRPO训练中的策略坍缩与熵正则化冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO训练长链思维（数千token）时易发生策略坍缩；标准补救是熵正则化，但它在每个token上把概率质量从少数高概率token推向极低概率长尾，与收敛所需的熵衰减直接冲突，且系数在长轨迹下难以设定。

Method: Info-GRPO，一个信息论框架：利用潜变量与其诱导轨迹之间的互信息。训练时给prompt附加潜种子（latent seeds），使模型探索与先验相关的多样策略；同时对种子条件熵（seed-conditioned entropy）加以引导使之收敛，从而在整条推理轨迹上携带多样性。

Result: 在多个推理基准上优于vanilla GRPO与熵正则化GRPO。AIME24上Avg@8较GRPO在四个backbone上分别提升3.75%、1.66%、4.16%、4.17%；达到同等多数投票准确率所需样本数减少8倍。

Conclusion: 将探索从逐token扰动转移到轨迹级潜变量条件化，可在不阻碍熵衰减的前提下维持多样性，为长链推理RL训练提供更可控的探索机制。

Abstract: Reasoning models trained with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) now produce chains of thought thousands of tokens long, and recent studies reveal that such training is prone to policy collapse. Entropy regularization is the standard remedy, but it works by pushing probability mass away from the few tokens that carry it and toward the vast low-probability tail of the vocabulary, and it applies this force once for every token of the reasoning trace. This puts the bonus in direct conflict with the entropy decay that convergence requires, and makes its coefficient difficult to set in the long-trace regime. To resolve this tension, we propose Info-GRPO, an information-theoretic framework that cultivates correlation between the policy and a latent prior. Rather than raising entropy everywhere, Info-GRPO builds on the mutual information between a latent variable and the trajectory it induces: augmenting prompts with latent seeds during training lets the model explore diverse policies correlated with the prior, while the seed-conditioned entropy is guided toward convergence. Diversity is therefore carried across whole reasoning traces rather than by per-token randomness, and exploration no longer fights entropy reduction. Experiments show that Info-GRPO outperforms vanilla GRPO and entropy-regularized GRPO across diverse reasoning benchmarks. On AIME24, it improves $Avg@8$ over GRPO by 3.75%, 1.66%, 4.16%, and 4.17% on four backbones, and it reaches the same majority-vote accuracy with $8\times$ fewer samples. Code and models will be publicly released.

</details>


### [152] [MINT: Mitigating Structural Inconsistency via Attentive Message Passing for Financial Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34950)
*Yin-Ju Wu,Bi-Cheng Yan,Berlin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MINT，一个面向交易网络的图神经框架，通过注意力消息传递机制缓解结构不一致，并结合PU学习应对标签稀缺，用于非法账户检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将交易网络建模为有向多重图并用GNN在同质性假设下检测异常，但直接套用GNN面临两个关键挑战：恶意账户伪造连接导致的结构不一致，以及标注数据缺乏导致的标签稀缺。

Method: MINT包含两部分：1）差异性感知的特征传播，通过量化双向交易流中节点间的偏差来追踪恶意账户；2）交易显著性模块，通过自适应正则化节点间交互强度来缓解结构不一致。此外采用正-未标注（PU）学习优化模型，仅利用少量非法节点和大量未标注节点。

Result: 在三个金融交易基准（两个大规模公开数据集和一个内部真实数据集）上的大量实验验证了MINT优于多个先进基线方法。

Conclusion: MINT通过差异性感知传播、交易显著性正则化与PU学习相结合，有效缓解结构不一致与标签稀缺问题，在非法账户检测任务上取得优越性能。

Abstract: Illicit account detection is a critical task for financial risk control systems, aiming to pinpoint malicious actors within transaction networks. Existing efforts typically recast transaction networks into directed multigraphs and leverage graph neural networks (GNNs) to detect anomalies via topological structures under the homophily assumption. However, directly grafting GNNs onto transaction graphs is faced with at least two pivotal challenges, i.e., structural inconsistency and label scarcity. The former arises from deceptive connections forged by malicious actors, while the latter stems from the lack of annotated data. In light of these, we introduce MINT, a novel graph neural framework that mitigates structural inconsistency via an attentive message-passing scheme on transaction graphs. Specifically, MINT introduces a discrepancy-aware feature propagation, which tracks malicious actors by quantifying deviations between nodes across bi-directional transaction flows. A transaction salience module is proposed to mitigate structural inconsistency by adaptively regularizing the interaction strength between nodes. Furthermore, to alleviate label scarcity, MINT is optimized via Positive-Unlabeled (PU) learning, which exploits only a minority of illicit nodes coupled with a majority of unlabeled ones. Extensive experiments conducted on three financial transaction benchmarks, including two large-scale public and one in-house real-world datasets, validate the superior effectiveness of MINT over several cutting-edge baselines.

</details>


### [153] [Improving Reasoning with Rollout Agreement for Group-Based Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34951)
*Sohyun Son,Yerim Hong,Jaeyun Park,Mujeen Sung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过盲配对分析发现，GRPO 中正确答案的 rollout 内部一致度差异反映了推理有效性差异（低一致度轨迹中虚假推理占 35%，高一致度仅 22%），据此提出 RoAR，将 rollout 一致度作为推理级奖励加入 GRPO，在五个数学推理基准上取得 55.5% 平均 pass@1，并显著降低虚假推理率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RLVR（如 GRPO）利用 rollout 多样性提升推理性能与训练效率，但"答案正确"的 rollout 之间的差异究竟代表有效的替代推理路径，还是尚未暴露的错误，缺乏系统认识；若盲目利用这种差异引导学习，可能强化无效推理。

Method: 1) 对 GRPO 训练的模型进行盲配对分析：针对同一题目，比较一致度最高与最低的正确轨迹，共 126 对，人工标注虚假推理（内部不一致、误用题设条件等）；2) 提出 RoAR（Rollout-Agreement Reward），在 GRPO 对已验证正确 rollout 的奖励中注入"推理级一致度"信号；该信号直接由现有 on-policy rollout 组计算，无需人工撰写推理依据、额外采样或单独训练的过程评估器。

Result: 126 对配对中，低一致度轨迹虚假推理率 35%，高一致度 22%；五个月数学推理基准上，1.5B 的 GRPO-RoAR 仅用 7K 训练样本、零样本评测即达 55.5% 平均 pass@1，分别高于 GRPO 与 GRPO-CARE 6.0 与 5.5 个百分点；对四个 1.5B 模型均答对的 100 道题做独立盲审，虚假推理率分别为 GRPO-RoAR 18%、GRPO 32%、GRPO-CARE 27%、DRA-GRPO 37%。

Conclusion: 正确 rollout 之间的差异并非都代表有益多样性，其中相当部分对应未解决的推理错误；因此在使用 rollout 差异引导学习时应将"推理有效性"作为重要考量。RoAR 以零额外标注成本的一致性奖励有效提升正确性与推理质量，但仍残留约 18% 虚假推理，说明该问题未被完全解决。

Abstract: Recent methods for Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) use rollout diversity to improve reasoning performance or training efficiency. Despite these gains, the extent to which differences among correct-answer rollouts reflect useful alternative reasoning or unresolved errors remains poorly understood. We investigate this question through a blind paired analysis of a model trained with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), comparing the highest- and lowest-agreement correct-answer traces for the same problem. Across 126 pairs, low-agreement traces have more spurious reasoning than high-agreement traces (35% vs. 22%), including internal inconsistencies and misuse of problem conditions. Based on these findings, we propose RoAR (Rollout-Agreement Reward), which adds reasoning-level agreement to GRPO's rewards for verified-correct rollouts. RoAR computes this signal from existing on-policy rollout groups, requiring no human-written rationales, additional sampling, or separately trained process evaluator. On five mathematical reasoning benchmarks, the 1.5B GRPO-RoAR model reaches 55.5% mean pass@1 under zero-shot evaluation using 7K training examples, exceeding GRPO and GRPO-CARE by 6.0 and 5.5 percentage points. A separate blind audit of 100 problems answered correctly by all four 1.5B models finds spurious rates of 18% for GRPO-RoAR, 32% for GRPO, 27% for GRPO-CARE, and 37% for DRA-GRPO. Our findings identify reasoning validity as an important consideration when using rollout differences to guide learning. Our code is available at https://github.com/ldi-kyunghee/RoAR.

</details>


### [154] [Beyond feature attribution: Recovering latent interaction structures in machine learning models via localized CBFI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34962)
*Sejong Oh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出局部化的基于案例特征重要性（CBFI）框架，将实例预测分解为结构上区分的成分，把独立主效应（G1）与纯交互作用（G4）分离，从而显式揭示复杂表格模型中被加性解释方法掩盖的特征协同结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SHAP、LIME 等加性框架把非线性交互吸收进单个特征分数，掩盖了模型的结构逻辑；在高风险场景的自动化审计中，理解模型行为逻辑与预测精度同等重要，因此需要显式暴露潜在交互结构。

Method: 提出局部化 CBFI 框架：不采用传统数值归因，而是把实例级预测分解为结构上不同的成分，区分独立主效应（G1）与纯交互项（G4），以捕捉显式特征协同，并为诊断特征组合提供有原则的方法。

Result: 在葡萄酒质量分类与医疗保险回归任务上，局部化 CBFI 成功解耦了被加性模型隐藏的复杂交互；保真度测试与合成真值基准表明，识别出的交互精确对应梯度提升和深度神经网络等复杂架构中嵌入的非线性结构依赖模式。

Conclusion: 该方法将可解释 AI 范式从简单的数值归因推进到交互驱动贡献的还原，为复杂表格模型的自动化审计提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Background: Understanding individual predictions in complex tabular models remains challenging, as existing additive frameworks like SHAP and LIME often obscure structural logic by absorbing nonlinear interactions into individual feature scores. Exposing these latent interaction structures is essential for automated auditing in high-stakes environments where understanding the logic of model behavior is as essential as predictive accuracy. Methods: To address this limitation, we introduce the localized case-based feature importance (CBFI) framework. Unlike traditional numerical attribution, our method decomposes instance-specific predictions into structurally distinct components, effectively separating independent main effects (G1) from pure interactions (G4) to capture explicit feature synergies and provide a principled approach for diagnosing feature combinations. Results: Comprehensive experiments on wine quality classification and medical insurance regression demonstrate that localized CBFI successfully disentangles complex interactions hidden by additive models. Furthermore, fidelity tests and synthetic ground-truth benchmarks confirm that the identified interactions correspond precisely to the nonlinear structural dependency patterns embedded in complex architectures, including gradient boosting and deep neural networks, thereby successfully shifting the explainable AI paradigm from simple numerical attribution to the recovery of interaction-driven contributions.

</details>


### [155] [Adaptive Memory Index Evolution for Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34977)
*Yuntong Hu,Tongli Su,Yuanfu Sun,Shuo Ma,Qiaoyu Tan,Liang Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将"记忆访问状态"从记忆内容中解耦，提出记忆索引演化（Memory Index Evolution）这一独立适配层，并实现轻量插件 AMIE，通过部署期检索流量与蒸馏语义教师的保留/抑制判断来在线更新每个记忆的访问状态，从而在不改内容、不重训宿主的前提下重加权后续检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程智能体虽持续积累经验，但"存有记忆"不等于"需要时能取用"。现有系统把记忆内容适配与访问适配混在一起优化，缺少对"持久化访问更新"这一能力的单独刻画与独立可控层，因而难以定位、复用与迁移该能力。

Method: 将目标形式化为 Memory Index Evolution；提出 AMIE：轻量插件层，从部署期检索反馈中演化每条记忆的访问状态，不改存储内容、不重训宿主记忆系统。用语义教师产生 keep/suppress 判断并蒸馏为"检索条件化"策略，将选择性动作累积为有界状态，用于重加权宿主检索。另设零调用策略（仅累积检索频次）作为对照，并做受控分析（持续抑制竞争项、抑制反复有用记忆）。

Result: 在 5 个检索型宿主与 3 个基准上，训练后的 AMIE 在全部 15 项下游 F1/准确率对比中均有提升（+0.70~3.37 个百分点，相对最高 25%），在全部 10 项检索指标对比中均有改进。意外发现：仅累积检索频次的零调用策略在若干设置下可持平或优于语义策略，说明检索流量本身即为强访问适配信号；学习到的选择性在"高频检索记忆仍有用"时提升鲁棒性。受控分析显示：持久抑制竞争项可暴露此前未被看到的证据，而抑制反复有用的记忆则构成互补的失效模式。

Conclusion: 持久化记忆访问可作为检索型智能体记忆系统中一个实用、可插拔的独立适配层，与它所服务的内容相区分；检索流量与学习式选择性各有价值，二者结合在多种宿主与基准上稳定增益。

Abstract: Long-horizon agents continually accumulate experience, yet retaining a memory does not guarantee access when it is needed. Existing systems can adapt both memory content and access; we isolate persistent access updates as a separately controlled adaptation layer. We formulate this target as Memory Index Evolution and instantiate it with Adaptive Memory Index Evolution (AMIE), a lightweight plug-in layer that evolves per-memory access state from deployment retrievals without modifying stored content or retraining the host memory system. AMIE distils a semantic teacher's keep/suppress judgments into a retrieval-conditioned policy and accumulates its selective actions into bounded state that reweights subsequent host retrievals. Surprisingly, even a zero-call policy that simply accumulates retrieval frequency can match or outperform semantic policies in several settings, revealing retrieval traffic itself as a strong signal for access adaptation, while learned selectivity improves robustness when frequently retrieved memories remain useful. Across five retrieval-based hosts and three benchmarks, trained AMIE improves downstream F1 or accuracy in all 15 comparisons, by 0.70--3.37 percentage points (up to 25% relative), and improves retrieval in all 10 comparisons. Controlled analyses show that persistent competitor suppression can expose previously unseen evidence, while suppressing repeatedly useful memories creates the complementary failure mode. Together, these results establish persistent memory access as a practical adaptation layer, distinct from the content it serves, in retrieval-based agent memory systems.

</details>


### [156] [Attention under Bounded Key-Query Similarity: Space Complexity and Transformer Anisotropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35020)
*Justin Y. Chen,Ying Feng,Piotr Indyk,Michael Kapralov,Ekaterina Kochetkova,Boris Prokhorov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在键/查询余弦相似度被限制在零附近小区间（即无法完美对齐）的各向异性假设下，本文给出了注意力近似的近空间最优算法及其空间下界，并证明简单均匀采样在典型参数范围内近乎最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有KV-cache压缩与注意力最坏情况空间复杂度的紧结果依赖“查询可与键完美对齐”的构造，这与真实Transformer中键/查询几何的各向异性、余弦相似度普遍偏小的经验观察相矛盾，因此需要在对齐受限的更现实假设下重新刻画注意力近似所需的空间。

Method: 假设键/查询余弦相似度落在零附近的小区间内（查询无法与任何键过度对齐），在此模型下研究注意力近似的空间复杂度：构造一个近空间最优的近似算法，推导相应的空间下界，并分析简单均匀采样算法的表现。

Result: 给出了近空间最优的算法与匹配的空间下界；值得注意的是，下界所对应的键/查询余弦相似度分布恰好呈现文献中经验建立的各向异性特征；同时证明简单均匀采样在典型参数范围内近乎最优，从而解释了其在真实数据集上的意外有效性。

Conclusion: 将各向异性（对齐受限）纳入注意力近似分析，可得到更符合实际的空间复杂度刻画；该框架兼顾上下界，并理论性地支持了均匀采样作为实用KV-cache压缩基线的有效性。

Abstract: A growing body of work on KV-cache compression aims to reduce the memory requirements of autoregressive generation. These methods compress the stored context while approximately preserving attention computation, enabling generation with reduced memory consumption. Almost tight theoretical results on the worst case space complexity of attention have recently been obtained, but they are achieved by instances where queries can be perfectly aligned with keys. This directly contradicts the observed anisotropy of the key/query geometry in real transformers. In particular, it has been shown empirically that key/query cosine similarities are small even for most correlated pairs, i.e., near-perfect alignment of queries with keys does not happen in practice. In this paper, we study the space complexity of attention approximation under the assumption that key/query cosine similarities belong to a small interval around zero, i.e., a query cannot align too much with any key. We give a nearly space optimal algorithm and a corresponding space lower bound. Surprisingly, the distribution of key/query cosine similarities in our lower bound exhibits the anisotropy property empirically established and studied in the literature. Additionally, we show that the simple uniform sampling algorithm is nearly optimal for a typical range of parameters, supporting its surprising effectiveness on real datasets.

</details>


### [157] [BellowsKV: One Latent for Many Layers, Expanded on Demand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35023)
*Jiamu Zhang,Hanjie Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BellowsKV 是一种免训练 KV 缓存压缩方法：将 n 个相邻层的隐藏状态映射为一个逐 token 的共享潜变量，各层通过专属投影从中读取自己的 K/V，并在保持潜变量全维度的前提下用低精度量化（BellowsKV-Quant）与块内共享逐出（BellowsKV-Evict）实现压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程多轮推理与工具调用使 KV 缓存成为 GPU 服务的主要内存瓶颈。单层压缩方法（量化、降维、token 逐出）在高压缩比下精度显著下降；跨层共享缓存方法在高压缩比下同样急剧退化。作者发现两点关键现象：相邻层的 KV 表示差异较大，而相邻层输入隐藏状态高度相似；且在同一内存预算下，压缩共享潜变量的维度比量化它造成的精度损失大得多。

Method: 免训练框架：以每 token 的隐藏状态为中介，将 n 个相邻层的 K/V 映射到单个共享潜变量，推理时每层经层特定投影从该潜变量还原自身 K/V；潜变量保持全维度，改用量化与逐出实现压缩——Quant 以低精度存储潜变量（因潜变量无主导离群通道而近似无损），Evict 在每个块内做一次共享逐出决策（因各层对 token 重要性的排序高度一致）。

Result: 在 Llama-3.1-8B、Mistral-7B、Qwen2.5-7B 上，BellowsKV-Quant 在 3.6× 压缩下保留约 99% 的全缓存精度，13–14× 压缩下保留 97–98%；而最强跨层方法在 16× 压缩下至多保留 84%。

Conclusion: 作者认为，跨层 KV 缓存压缩的正确切入点在于共享隐藏状态而非共享 KV 表示，且潜变量应保持全维度、以量化而非降维/逐出为主要压缩手段；该结论由三模型上高压缩比下的精度保持得到支持，并已开源代码。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) now serve as the backbone of agents that reason over many turns, call tools, and pursue long-horizon tasks. To avoid recomputation, a model stores the attention keys and values of all past tokens in a key-value (KV) cache. Each layer maintains its own cache, so KV-cache memory can become the main bottleneck for GPU serving. Most compression methods operate within a single layer, by quantizing the cache entries, reducing the channel dimension, or evicting less important tokens. All three lose substantial accuracy at high compression ratios. Cross-layer methods share one compressed cache across adjacent layers to go beyond this limit. However, these methods also degrade sharply at high compression ratios. We find that the KV-cache representations of adjacent layers are quite different, whereas the input hidden states of adjacent layers are highly similar. We also find that, under the same memory budget, reducing the dimension of this shared latent loses far more accuracy than quantizing it. Based on these findings, we propose BellowsKV, a training-free method that maps the hidden states of n adjacent layers to one shared latent per token. At inference time, each layer reads its own keys and values from this latent through a layer-specific projection. We keep the latent at full dimension and compress it in two other ways: BellowsKV-Quant stores the latent at low precision, which is nearly lossless because the latent has no dominant outlier channels; BellowsKV-Evict makes one shared eviction decision per block, because the layers rank token importance very similarly. Across Llama-3.1-8B, Mistral-7B and Qwen2.5-7B, BellowsKV-Quant retains about 99% of full-cache accuracy at 3.6x compression and 97-98% at 13-14x, where the strongest cross-layer method retains at most 84% at 16x. Code: https://github.com/MorrisZJ/BellowsKV

</details>


### [158] [AutoPRL: Automated Path Representation Learning via a Learnable View Generator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35032)
*Sean Bin Yang,Ying Sun,Yunyao CHeng,Hao Miao,Jilin Hu,Bin Yang,Torben Bach Pedersen,Christian S. Jensen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 AutoPRL，一个高效的自监督路径表示学习框架，通过自动数据增强、跨视图解耦对比学习和自适应协同训练三个模块解决现有方法视图生成无效与训练低效的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 道路网络上的路径表示学习对旅行时间估计、路径排序等下游任务至关重要，但现有自监督方法依赖启发式增强与计算昂贵的对比学习，导致视图生成效果差、训练效率低。

Method: AutoPRL 包含三部分：(1) 自动数据增强模块 AutoDA，用变分自编码器为每个拓扑感知节点嵌入建模高斯隐分布，生成语义有意义且多样的特征级路径视图，减少信息损失；(2) 跨视图解耦对比学习机制 CVDC，在节点级与路径级视图上解耦正负样本对的优化，提升对比学习稳定性与效率；(3) 自适应协同训练模块 ACT，基于不确定性动态调整损失权重以平衡多目标，保证训练高效可扩展。

Result: 在三个真实数据集上的大量实验表明，AutoPRL 在多项下游任务上在有效性与效率两方面均取得稳定且较强的性能。

Conclusion: AutoPRL 通过特征级自动增强、解耦对比与自适应多任务加权，为路径表示学习提供了一种有效且高效的自监督范式。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning has emerged as a powerful paradigm for Path Representation Learning (PRL) in road networks. PRL aims to learn informative representations of paths traversed by moving objects, such as vehicles, where each path consists of a sequence of connected road segments. These representations are essential for various downstream tasks, including travel-time estimation and path ranking. However, existing methods often suffer from ineffective view generation and inefficient training procedures due to their reliance on heuristic augmentations and computationally intensive contrastive learning. To address these issues, we propose AutoPRL, an effective and efficient self-supervised PRL framework that integrates three key components. The Automatic Data Augmentation (AutoDA) module employs variational autoencoders to generate semantically meaningful and diverse feature-level path views by modeling each topology-aware node embedding with a Gaussian latent distribution, thereby mitigating information loss and enhancing representation quality. Second, the Cross-View Decoupled Contrastive (CVDC) learning mechanism improves training efficiency by disentangling the optimization of positive and negative pairs across node- and path-level views, enabling more stable contrastive learning. Third, an Adaptive Co-Training (ACT) module dynamically adjusts uncertainty-based loss weights to balance multiple objectives, ensuring efficient and scalable training. Extensive experiments on three real-world datasets demonstrate that AutoPRL achieves strong and consistent performance in both effectiveness and efficiency across multiple downstream tasks.

</details>


### [159] [Mapping the Solution-Set Structure of Grokking on Latin Squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35036)
*Zihan Gu,Lulu Zhao,Tianyi Ma,Hua Zhang,Wenbo Su,Yanan Cao,Yue Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究 grokking（从记忆到泛化的延迟转变）中"哪些任务能使所有最小范数终点从部分观测中泛化"，将成对输入任务建模为 K_{q,q} 的边着色（以拉丁着色为结构化族），把观测实例按终点集合分为全好、混合、无好三类，并证明有限阿贝尔群族渐近全好、而均匀随机拉丁方下全好任务渐近稀少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往工作主要解释 grokking 的"延迟"机制（拟合后主损失变为权重衰减），却较少回答：在这一优化问题（最小范数终点）下，究竟什么样的任务结构能保证从部分观测泛化。作者希望从任务本身的结构性质出发，刻画泛化能力的有无与边界。

Method: 将成对输入任务形式化为完全二部图 K_{q,q} 的边着色问题，以拉���着色作为结构化任务族；把权重衰减主导阶段的解刻画为"最小范数终点"，并根据观测实例对应的终点集合将其分类为 all-good / mixed / no-good。在此基础上进行渐近分析：分别针对有限阿贝尔群族与均匀随机拉丁方给出定理证明；并辅以实验，考察加法运算的重标记（稠密多项式表示）以及合数阶任务上的训练动态。

Result: 证明了两条形成对照的结论：(1) 有限阿贝尔群任务族在部分观测下渐近为 all-good；(2) 在均匀随机拉丁方下，对基于观测的学习者而言 all-good 任务渐近稀少，主要表现为 mixed 或 no-good。实验显示，加法运算的重标记尽管具有稠密多项式表示仍能泛化；在合数阶任务上，有限训练结果显示预测器先学到输出的粗粒度部分，再学到完整标签。

Conclusion: grokking 中的泛化能力本质上取决于任务结构与观测方式：具有阿贝尔群结构的任务族可被最小范数终点可靠泛化，而一般（随机拉丁方式）结构则难以保证。泛化与表示的稠密程度无必然冲突，且复杂任务呈现"先粗后细"的分阶段学习。该框架为从任务结构角度理解 grokking 提供了分类工具与理论边界。

Abstract: Grokking is a delayed transition from memorisation to generalisation. A network first fits observed examples while remaining near chance on held-out inputs, then generalises abruptly. Prior work has focused mainly on this delay, finding that the network's main loss becomes weight decay after the fitting phase. We name the solution to this optimization problem as the minimum-norm endpoint, and study which tasks make all minimum-norm endpoints generalise from partial observations. Specifically, we model pairwise input tasks as edge colourings of $K_{q,q}$, with Latin colourings as our structured family, and classify each observed instance as all-good, mixed, or no-good by its endpoint set. Furthermore, we prove two contrasting results: (1) Finite-abelian-group families are asymptotically all-good from partial observations; (2) Under a uniform random Latin square, all-good tasks become asymptotically rare for observation-based learners, leaving mixed or no-good behaviour. Moreover, experiments show that relabellings of addition generalise despite their dense polynomial representations. Finally, on tasks of composite order, finite-training results suggest that predictors learn a coarse part of the output before the full label.

</details>


### [160] [Risk-Aware Output Control Should be the Focus of Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35052)
*Yahya Alkhatib,Yuyang Xiao,Wee Peng Tay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，主张机器遗忘（Machine Unlearning, MU）应从追求"参数空间不可区分性"转向"带统计保证的输出空间控制"，认为后者才是具有操作意义、可扩展且可解释的遗忘目标，并用经验证据和已有理论结果加以论证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MU 研究多致力于让遗忘后模型尽可能接近在保留数据上从头重训练得到的模型（参数空间不可区分性）。作者认为这一目标与实际部署需求和用户关切不一致：机器学习系统存在偏见、有害内容、幻觉、敏感信息泄露等失效模式，用户真正在意的是模型输出行为是否被有效控制，而非参数是否与重训练模型相近。

Method: 立场性论证（position paper）方法：先批判参数空间不可区分性目标的对齐性问题，再提出以统计保证的输出空间控制作为更合理的遗忘定义，从"支持严格且鲁棒的目标""可扩展至现代部署场景""可提供可解释保证"三方面论证其操作意义，并结合经验证据与既有理论结果（虽未按此框架提出，但与之相容）进行支撑。

Result: 作者通过经验证据与理论结果的重新解读，说明带统计保证的输出空间控制在实践中可行、在部署规模下可扩展，并能给出可解释的保证；同时表明已有若干理论结果与这一框架一致，可作为该立场成立的支撑。

Conclusion: MU 应以统计保证下的输出空间控制为核心目标，而非参数空间相似性。这一转向使遗忘目标更严格、更鲁棒、更具可扩展性与可解释性，更契合实际部署与用户需求。

Abstract: Well-documented failure modes of machine learning (ML) systems, including bias, toxicity, hallucination, and leakage of sensitive information, has driven the rise of machine unlearning (MU). Most existing work, however, targets parameter-space indistinguishability, seeking unlearned models that are as close as possible to models retrained from scratch on retained data. In this paper, we argue that this objective is misaligned with practical and user-relevant concerns: MU should prioritize statistically guaranteed output-space control rather than parameter-space similarity. We contend that output control with statistical guarantees is the operationally meaningful notion of unlearning, as it supports rigorous and robust objectives, scales to modern deployment settings, and enables interpretable guarantees. We substantiate this position with empirical evidence and with recent theoretical results that, although not originally formulated in this framing, are consistent with it.

</details>


### [161] [Reinforcement Learning Done Right for Explicit Confidence Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35059)
*Zefeng He,Yichen He,Yuan Lin,Yang Yang,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RLEC方法，首次实现单一LLM对显式置信目标（用户指定准确率p）的答/弃权控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在不确定时会给出看似合理但错误的答案，误导用户并损害可靠性。用强化学习训练模型学会弃权是自然思路，但现有方法多采用固定误差惩罚，无法在推理时适配不同置信要求。

Method: 研究显式置信目标设定：用户在提示中指定目标p，模型直接决定作答或弃权，目标是使已作答问题的准确率不低于p。作者指出单一模型难以区分不同p的两个根源：(1)区分不同p的奖励信号在优化过程中常被丢失；(2)原始误差惩罚在p趋近1时无界增长。RLEC通过保留区分置信目标所需的学习信号、引入保持最优作答条件不变的稳定奖励族、并用奖励调度平衡有效学习与训练稳定性来解决。

Result: 在多样基准与多种模型规模上的实验表明，RLEC基本保持通用能力，并取得有竞争力的校准性能；支持细粒度目标控制，且训练后无需外部决策系统或阈值校准。

Conclusion: 将显式置信目标纳入训练策略，使行为校准更直接，为LLM行为校准开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can produce plausible but incorrect answers when they are uncertain, which can mislead users and undermine model reliability. Training models to abstain with reinforcement learning is a natural approach, but most existing methods use a fixed error penalty and therefore cannot adapt their behavior to different confidence requirements at inference time. We study \emph{explicit confidence targets}, where a user specifies a target \(p\) in the prompt and the model directly chooses whether to answer or abstain, with the goal of achieving an accuracy of at least \(p\) among answered questions. However, learning to respond to different confidence targets with a single model has proven difficult, and prior attempts have failed to distinguish different targets. We show that this failure comes from two problems: the reward signal that distinguishes different values of $p$ is often lost during optimization, while the original error penalty grows without bound as $p$ approaches one. We propose RLEC (\textbf{R}einforcement \textbf{L}earning with \textbf{E}xplicit \textbf{C}onfidence Targets), which preserves the learning signal needed to distinguish confidence targets, introduces a stable reward family that keeps the desired answer condition unchanged, and uses reward scheduling to balance effective learning and training stability. Together, these designs enable the first successful training of a single LLM for explicit confidence target control. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks and model scales show that RLEC largely preserves general capabilities and achieves competitive calibration performance. RLEC also supports fine-grained target control without an external decision system or threshold calibration after training. By making explicit confidence targets part of the trained policy, RLEC opens a new and more direct path toward behavioral calibration.

</details>


### [162] [A JiT-Based Denoising Defense Method Against Adversarial Examples for Radio Signal Automatic Modulation Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35084)
*Shiwei Liu,Linyuan Wang,Nuolin Sun,Libin Hou,Bin Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于JiT（直接预测干净图像的扩散生成模型）思想的1D Transformer流匹配去噪网络，用于无线电信号调制识别中对抗样本的防御，通过把任意输入投影回干净信号的低维流形实现无需攻击先验的净化，在RML2018.01A上优于PGD对抗训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动调制分类（AMC）是频谱监测与电磁态势感知的核心技术，DNN虽特征提取能力强但易受对抗攻击；常用防御手段对抗训练存在跨攻击类型泛化差、训练代价高的缺陷，且实际部署中往往无法获知攻击信息。

Method: 受JiT启发，训练一个面向信号的1D Transformer去噪网络，以流匹配（flow matching）目标直接预测干净信号样本，学习将任意样本映射回干净样本低维流形的变换；推理时直接将任意输入送入该网络完成投影式净化，无需先验攻击知识。实验在RML2018.01A上进行，不提供攻击信息，仅使用单一经验选定的固定去噪起始时刻。

Result: 相对PGD对抗训练：PGD攻击下鲁棒准确率相对提升至少20.58%，干净样本分类准确率相对提升10.20%；面对对抗训练未见过的攻击类型，防御更强，在较高攻击强度下鲁棒准确率相对提升至少39.72%。

Conclusion: 基于JiT的流匹配去噪防御可作为一种攻击无关、计算上更轻量的AMC对抗防御方案，在鲁棒性与干净精度上均优于对抗训练，并在未见攻击下表现出更好的泛化性。

Abstract: Automatic modulation classification (AMC) of radio signals is a core technology for spectrum monitoring and electromagnetic situational awareness. Deep neural networks (DNNs) play an important role in this field owing to their powerful feature extraction capabilities and end-to-end learning paradigm. However, their vulnerability to adversarial attacks poses serious security challenges to practical deployment. Adversarial training, a common defense, has limitations including poor generalization across attack types and high training costs. Inspired by Just image Transformers (JiT), a diffusion generative model that directly predicts clean images, this paper proposes a JiT-based denoising defense against adversarial examples for radio signal modulation classification. During training, a signal-specific 1D Transformer denoising network is optimized via flow matching to directly predict clean signal samples. It thereby learns a mapping that projects arbitrary examples back onto the low-dimensional manifold of clean signal examples. During inference, arbitrary examples are fed into the trained network and directly projected onto this manifold. This enables denoising defense without prior knowledge of attacks. Experiments on the RML2018.01A dataset were conducted without attack information, using a single empirically selected, fixed denoising start time. Compared with PGD-based adversarial training, the proposed method achieves a relative improvement of at least 20.58% in robust accuracy under PGD attacks. It also achieves a relative improvement of 10.20% in classification accuracy on clean examples. When evaluated against attack types unseen during adversarial training, the proposed method provides stronger defense than adversarial training. In particular, at higher attack strengths, it achieves a relative improvement of at least 39.72% in robust accuracy.

</details>


### [163] [Spherical Brownian Diffusion for Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35101)
*Andrea Miele,Ilija Bogunovic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出球面布朗扩散（Spherical Brownian Diffusion），以布朗运动作为球面上规范各向同性扩散；通过高维近似速率理论分离"腐蚀过程"与"推理采样器"的贡献，实验表明在Sudoku上布朗与矩匹配vMF几乎无差异，而采样器选择带来25.0个百分点的巨大差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续扩散语言模型存在两个耦合设计：如何腐蚀连续token表示、如何在生成时逆过程恢复。近期工作用测地线与vMF路径在单位球面上生成，但尚不清楚该几何上应选用何种前向过程，以及该选择相对于推理阶段的重要性有多大。

Method: 1）引入Spherical Brownian Diffusion：热半群给出有原则的前向过程、无量纲的加性噪声时钟与精确的祖先桥（ancestral bridge）；2）用交叉熵学习干净token后验，兼容多种采样器；3）推导布朗与矩匹配vMF腐蚀之间的高维近似速率，并据此进行受控替换，以分离腐蚀与推理的影响。

Result: Sudoku上，在四个采样器、五个预算且其他设置完全一致的条件下，布朗与矩匹配vMF结果几乎相同；而固定腐蚀过程后，仅采样器选择在180次调用预算下即产生25.0个百分点的差距。借助祖先桥将该增益拆分为两部分：重加噪状态带来的记忆效应（Sudoku与GSM8K上存在）与token选择效应（GSM8K上存在，小预算时最大）。

Conclusion: 在球面连续扩散语言模型中，前向腐蚀路径的选择影响甚微，推理采样器的选择才是性能主导因素；推理任务上类别式精化更优，而无条件文本生成宜采用保持多样性的随机更新。

Abstract: Continuous diffusion language models involve two coupled design choices: how to corrupt continuous token representations and how to invert that corruption at generation time. Recent work has explored language generation on the unit sphere through geodesic and von Mises--Fisher (vMF) paths, raising the question of which forward process to use on this geometry and how much that choice matters relative to inference. We introduce Spherical Brownian Diffusion, using Brownian motion as the canonical isotropic diffusion on the sphere. Its heat semigroup provides a principled forward process, an additive dimension-free noise clock, and an exact ancestral bridge, while a clean-token posterior learned with cross-entropy supports multiple samplers. To separate corruption from inference, we derive high-dimensional approximation rates between Brownian and moment-matched vMF corruption and use this relationship for controlled substitutions. On Sudoku, Brownian and moment-matched vMF give nearly identical results across four samplers and five budgets under otherwise identical settings. In contrast, with the corruption process fixed, sampler choice produces a 25.0-point spread at a sampler budget of 180 calls. Using the ancestral bridge, we split this gain into a memory effect from re-noising the state, present on Sudoku and GSM8K, and a token-selection effect on GSM8K and largest at small budgets. Across reasoning tasks, categorical refinement improves performance, whereas unconditional text generation benefits from stochastic updates that preserve diversity.

</details>


### [164] [FlowGenesis: Representation-Driven Self-Organization of Neural Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35108)
*Maksim A Kazanskii*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FlowGenesis框架，用"表征通量"（Representation Flux）指导神经元的出生（分裂）与剪枝，使网络结构在训练中自适应演化，无需预设目标架构或额外架构搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络的结构通常在训练前固定，但其内部表征持续演化，二者存在不匹配；现有方法需预设架构或进行独立架构搜索。

Method: 以minibatch平均激活变化量定义Representation Flux：持续高Flux的神经元进行近似保函数的分裂；低Flux且低活跃度的神经元被剪枝。容量随表征学习反馈演化。

Result: 图像与文本分类中，不同初始规模网络收敛到各数据集相近的神经元数量区间且预测性能相当；在任务增量持续学习中，减少后续任务的神经元与参数分配，准确率与固定宽度渐进神经网络（PNN）相当。

Conclusion: 神经架构可通过表征学习与结构变化的相互反馈实现自适应，为"训练前预设网络容量"提供替代范式。

Abstract: Deep neural networks typically rely on architectures specified before training, despite the continuous evolution of their internal representations. We introduce FlowGenesis, a framework in which neural architectures adapt through neuron birth and pruning guided by Representation Flux, a local statistic capturing changes in minibatch mean activations. Neurons exhibiting persistently high Flux undergo splitting designed to approximately preserve the network function, while neurons with low Flux and low activity are pruned. This mechanism allows network capacity to evolve without prescribing a target architecture or performing a separate architecture search. Experiments on image and text classi- fication show that networks initialized with different sizes approach similar final neuron-count ranges within each dataset while maintaining comparable predictive performance. We further extend FlowGenesis to continual learning, where representation-guided structural adaptation reduces the neurons and parameters allocated to successive tasks while maintaining accuracy comparable to fixed-width Progressive Neural Networks in a task-incremental learning setting. These findings demonstrate that neural architectures can adapt through feedback between representation learning and structural change, suggesting an alternative to prescribing network capacity before training.

</details>


### [165] [Subspace Crystallization in Grokking: Rank Compression Tracks the Phase Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35112)
*Shlok Mehendale,Paras Chopra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过平均场MLP与单层Transformer在模运算任务上的实验，论证有效秩下降（压缩）是grokking的伴随特征而非其成因：压缩发生在泛化起点之后，表现为"子空间结晶"，且人为施加低秩约束反而延迟或阻断grokking。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究将grokking（网络在记忆训练数据后很久才泛化）与权重矩阵有效秩下降联系起来，但"秩压缩是驱动grokking还是仅仅跟随其后"这一问题尚未解决，本文旨在判定二者的因果方向。

Method: 在模算术任务上训练平均场MLP与单层Transformer，逐步骤追踪有效秩（effective rank）与测试准确率的时序关系；定义"充分子空间"（保留99%测试准确率所需的最少顶部奇异方向）并引入10%输出容差下的方向计数以衡量间隔增长的影响；最后用因子化全秩模型作对照，对Transformer的MLP输入权重与嵌入层人为施加低秩约束进行干预实验。

Result: (i) 所有Transformer均在250步内记忆训练数据，而grokking起点（测试准确率首次达95%的步数）在6,500至21,000步间变化；有效秩最陡下降总出现在起点后约900步，且仅记忆不泛化的网络压缩程度远小。(ii) 子空间结晶：Transformer过渡期内所需方向数从可用方向的四分之一以上降至约十分之一。(iii) 对MLP输入权重限秩会延迟起点，对嵌入层限秩使20次运行中15次无法grokking。

Conclusion: 压缩是grokking的标志性签名（signature），而非其成因；因果干预实验表明低秩约束不能加速反而阻碍泛化，因此有效秩下降应被视为泛化过程的结果或伴随现象。

Abstract: Grokking describes understanding something deeply and intuitively. In neural networks, it is the phenomenon where a network generalizes long after memorizing its training data. This delay has been linked to a fall in the effective rank of the network's weight matrices, but whether this rank compression drives grokking or follows it is still unresolved. We study a mean-field MLP and a one-layer transformer trained on modular arithmetic and observe that: (i) Compression follows the onset, not memorization. Every transformer memorizes its training data within 250 steps, while the grokking onset (the first step at which test accuracy reaches 95%) varies from 6,500 to 21,000 steps. The steepest fall in effective rank comes about 900 steps after the onset, however late the onset occurs, and networks that memorize without grokking compress far less. (ii) Subspace crystallization. We call the span of the fewest top singular directions of a weight matrix that preserve 99% of test accuracy its sufficient subspace. Its size can shrink as the margin between the correct and the best wrong output grows, so we also count the directions needed to keep the outputs within 10%. In the transformer, this count falls during the transition from a quarter or more of the available directions to about a tenth, which we call subspace crystallization. (iii) Imposing low rank does not speed up grokking. Against a factorized full-rank control, capping the rank of the transformer's MLP input weights delays the onset, and capping the rank of its embedding stops 15 of 20 runs from grokking. Compression is therefore a signature of grokking rather than its cause.

</details>


### [166] [UTA: Universal Training Adaptive Learning Rate Decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35113)
*Anda Tang,Junjie Wang,Xuanlin Yang,Cong Fang,Zhouchen Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出通用训练自适应（UTA）学习率衰减方法，将LRD建模为闭环的形状-尺度问题，通过连续时间形式实现训练状态自适应与分段最优形状，在LLaMA与ResNet50/ViT-B上稳定提升性能且开销可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习率衰减（LRD）对训练高性能模型至关重要，但多数设计依赖经验驱动。UBA（Universal Budget Aware）LRD虽有理论框架，却依赖SGD动力学，难以适用于Adam类优化器，且其关键"优化难度参数"含义不透明。

Method: 提出UTA（Universal Training Adaptive）LRD。区别于UBA固定离线、仅以迭代次数为锚点的轨迹，UTA将LRD视为闭环的形状-尺度问题；借助连续时间形式化，使LRD具备训练状态自适应能力，并获得分段最优的形状，从而将外部迭代与内部训练状态联系起来，在进展形状与学习率尺度两方面逼近最优路径。

Result: 在语言模型（LLaMA 60M–1B）与视觉模型（ResNet50、ViT-B，ImageNet）上取得一致性提升；即插即用、开销可忽略。消融实验验证了其在峰值学习率、最终衰减比例、参数φ0、各组件有效性以及微调场景泛化等方面的稳健性。

Conclusion: UTA弥补了UBA在优化器适用性与参数可解释性上的缺口，兼具理论形式与工程实用性，可作为通用的学习率衰减方案推广至大模型预训练与微调。

Abstract: Learning rate decay (LRD) is critical for training high-performance models, yet most designs remain empirically driven. Although the Universal Budget Aware (UBA) LRD offers a theoretical framework, its reliance on SGD dynamics limits its applicability to modern Adam-type optimizers and leaves its key `optimization difficulty parameter' opaque. To bridge this gap, we propose the Universal Training Adaptive (UTA) LRD. Unlike UBA's fixed offline trajectory anchored solely to iterations, UTA treats LRD as a closed-loop shape-and-scale problem. By continuous-time formulation, UTA is training-state adaptive with segment-wise optimal shape. This adaptivity links external iterations with internal training states, enabling a near-optimal path in both progression shape and learning rate (LR) scale, a property unattainable by other LRDs, including UBA. Empirically, we demonstrate consistent improvements across language models (LLaMA 60M to 1B) and vision models (ResNet50, ViT-B) on ImageNet with plug-and-play ease and negligible overhead. Extensive ablation studies further confirm UTA's robustness across dimensions, including peak LR, final decay ratio, its parameter $\varphi_0$, component efficacy and generalization to fine-tuning scenarios, underscoring its practical implementability.

</details>


### [167] [STeP-TS: Shared Temporal Filters with Pruning for Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35128)
*Hanne Raum,Jasper Hoffmann,Joschka Boedecker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种紧凑的单层卷积框架，学习各变量的共享多尺度时域滤波器组，以轻量变量特定标量权重组合各时域分量，并据此实现结构化剪枝与幅度自适应归一化，在极少参数和FLOPs下取得有竞争力的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量时间序列预测需同时捕捉多尺度时间依赖以及共享与变量特有的动态模式；现有大模型架构虽性能强但计算与内存开销大，紧凑模型在严格预算下难以保持预测性能。

Method: 1) 单层卷积框架，为每个变量学习共享的多尺度时域滤波器库；2) 用轻量变量特定标量权重加权各时域分量，兼顾共享结构与变量特异动态；3) 利用学习到的权重与滤波器输出能量作为结构化剪枝信号，裁剪卷积滤波器以降低复杂度；4) 引入幅度自适应归一化机制，保留变量相关的时间幅度信息。

Result: 在多个数据集上，预测性能与最先进方法相当，同时参数量与推理FLOPs显著更少，并实现较低的单样本延迟。

Conclusion: 紧凑的单层卷积加变量特定标量加权设计可在极低计算与内存预算下维持强预测能力，且模型内在的权重与能量信号为结构化剪枝提供了自然依据，幅度自适应归一化有助于保留变量幅度特征。

Abstract: Multivariate time series forecasting requires capturing temporal dependencies across multiple scales while accounting for both shared and variable-specific dynamics. Compact forecasting models have emerged as an attractive alternative to increasingly large architectures, but preserving strong predictive performance within tight computational and memory budgets remains challenging. We propose a compact one-layer convolutional framework that learns a shared bank of multi-scale temporal filters of each variable. Lightweight variable-specific scalar weights determine the contribution of each temporal component, enabling the model to capture shared temporal structure while adapting to variable-specific dynamics. The learned weights and filter-output energy further provide a natural signal for structured pruning of convolutional filters, reducing computational complexity with minimal degradation in forecasting performance. We additionally introduce an amplitude-adaptive normalization mechanism that preserves variable-dependent temporal amplitudes. Experiments on multiple datasets demonstrate forecasting performance competitive with state-of-the-art methods while requiring substantially fewer parameters and inference FLOPs and achieving low per-sample latency.

</details>


### [168] [TUON: A Tensor-Aware Optimizer Utilizing Tensor-SVD and Polarization for Large Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35130)
*Anda Tang,Xuanlin Yang,Junjie Wang,Zhouchen Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出TUON，一种基于T-SVD张量代数、采用"先混合再分离"策略的张量感知优化器，可捕捉跨层相关性并保留层内多样性，在GPT-124M/350M与Qwen-0.6B/4B上稳定优于Muon和TEON。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型预训练的瓶颈日益集中于优化器效率，优化器设计正从AdamW等向量感知方法转向矩阵感知方法（如Muon）。现有矩阵感知优化器虽利用矩阵结构，却对各层独立处理，忽略跨层相关性；已有的张量感知优化器则依赖矩阵化（matricization）式张量代数，仅适用于浅层堆叠和特定模块，存在结构维度失衡与跨层错位问题。

Method: TUON基于由T-SVD导出的变换式张量代数：先用酉变换Q混合各层，再对每个变换后的切片分别做极性分解（polarization），最后通过逆变换还原更新量。理论上证明TUON是在Q-变换张量谱范数下的最速下降，其更新即T-SVD的张量极性因子；并建立变换非冗余性、与Muon/TEON的范数与光滑性比较，以及收敛性保证。

Result: 在GPT-124M/350M和Qwen-0.6B/4B上TUON一致优于Muon与TEON。消融实验验证了变换机制的有效性、超越两层堆叠的跨层结构作用、超出QKV范围的模块适应性，以及对不同极性分解方法的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: TUON通过变换式张量代数实现可扩展的张量感知优化，兼顾跨层相关性与层内多样性，克服了矩阵化方法的维度失衡与层错位限制，可作为Muon/TEON的通用改进方案推广到更深层与更多样化的模块。

Abstract: Large scale language model~(LLM) pretraining is increasingly bottlenecked by optimizer efficiency, driving optimizer design from vector-aware methods such as AdamW to matrix-aware methods. Matrix-aware optimizers such as Muon exploit matrix structure but treat layers independently, neglecting cross-layer correlations. Tensor-aware optimizers are therefore needed, yet existing ones rely on a matricization-based tensor algebra and remain restricted to shallow stacking and specific modules. In this paper, we propose \textbf{TUON}, a novel optimizer built on a transform-based tensor algebra derived from the Tensor-SVD (T-SVD). Unlike methods that polarize a globally unfolded matrix, TUON mixes layers via a unitary transform $\boldsymbol{Q}$ and then polarizes each transformed slice, and inverts the transform to recover the update. This mix-then-separate strategy captures cross-layer correlations while preserving per-layer diversity, avoiding the structural dimension imbalance and cross-layer misalignment of unfolding. Thus it successfully scales to more layers and diverse modules beyond QKV. Theoretically, we prove that TUON performs steepest descent under a $\boldsymbol{Q}$-transformed tensor spectral norm, and identify its update as the tensor polar factor of the T-SVD. We further establish transform non-redundancy, norm and smoothness comparisons with Muon and TEON, and a convergence guarantee. Empirically, TUON improves consistently over Muon and TEON on GPT-124M/350M and Qwen-0.6B/4B, with ablations confirming the transformation mechanism, cross-layer structure beyond two-layer stacking, adaptability beyond QKV, and robustness to the polarization method.

</details>


### [169] [Locked In: Multi-Objective RL for the AI Data Center Build-Out](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35147)
*Satyapragnya Kar,Rajarshi Das,PR Panigrahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将AI数据中心长周期建设规划形式化为分层多目标马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），用带半马尔可夫管理者-工作者层次结构的偏好条件多目标智能体求解，在真实站点数据上验证了成本、碳、水、可靠性之间的可控权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI数据中心建设是数万亿美元、持续数十年的大型基础设施工程，但选址、供电采购与冷却决策仍依赖静态单点评分工具，且只在"第零天"数值上评估一次。而站点水、电、碳画像在10–20年资产寿命内显著漂移，且关键决策不可逆、长期绑定资本与物理设施，因此静态工具与问题本质不匹配。

Method: 1) 将长期建设形式化为分层多目标MDP：战略承诺被限制在离散窗口内、产生切换成本、并仅在依赖来源的交付周期后才可投入使用；运行调度仅限物理已存在容量。2) 采用偏好条件多目标智能体，内部为半马尔可夫"管理者-工作者"层次结构，可在推理时沿成本、碳、水、可靠性方向引导。3) 用轻量级反事实推演模型验证长期动态。4) 在真实站点数据的三类承诺机制（不可逆、可逆、一次性承诺）下做评估。

Result: 在全部三种机制下，偏好条件化均产生经确认、可控的目标权衡（覆盖两组目标对）；施加锁定（不可逆）带来覆盖更均匀的前沿，且在水压力硬约束生效前进行前瞻性改造的比率远更稳定，优于自由可逆设定。紧凑反事实动态模型对慢变状态跟踪良好，但在水压力维度上被简单持续性基线可测地超越。

Conclusion: 锁定与不可逆性建模会显著改变最优规划行为，偏好条件多目标强化学习可为长期基础设施规划提供可引导的策略族。作者如实报告反事实模型的局限（水压力轴劣于持续性基线），并开源环境、智能体、基线及评估工具，全部基于真实并网队列、水压力与电网排放数据。

Abstract: AI data center build-out is a multi-trillion-dollar, multi-decade infrastructure program. Yet siting, power procurement, and cooling decisions are still made with static, single-point scoring tools. These tools are evaluated once, on day-zero numbers. This is a poor fit. A site's water, power, and carbon profile drifts substantially over the 10 to 20 year asset life. The governing decisions are also irreversible, and they bind capital and physical plant for years. We formalize long-horizon build-out as a tiered multi-objective Markov decision process. Strategic commitments are gated to discrete windows, incur switching costs, and energize only after source-dependent lead times. Operational dispatch is restricted to capacity that physically exists. We solve the problem with a preference-conditioned multi-objective agent that has a semi-Markov manager and worker hierarchy. The agent recovers a family of policies that a planner can steer across cost, carbon, water, and reliability at inference time. We validate long-horizon dynamics with a lightweight counterfactual rollout model. Across three commitment regimes (irreversible, reversible, and commit-once) on real site data, preference conditioning yields a confirmed, steerable trade-off on both objective pairs in every regime.
