Imposing lock-in produces a more evenly covered frontier and a far more consistent rate of anticipatory retrofit ahead of a binding water-stress constraint than a freely reversible formulation. Finally, a compact counterfactual dynamics model tracks slow-moving state well. It is measurably outperformed by simple persistence on the water-stress axis. We report this limitation rather than obscure it. We release the environment, agents, baselines, and evaluation harness. All of them are grounded in real interconnection-queue, water-stress, and grid-emissions data.

</details>


### [170] [Accelerating SO(2)-Equivariant Materials Models through Low-Precision Emulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35164)
*Denghui Lu,Alexander Maeder,Alexandros Nikolaos Ziogas,Mathieu Luisier,Manasa Kaniselvan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlashSO(2) 用整数 Ozaki 方案的定制“仿真”低精度算子加速 GNN 的 SO(2) 等变层，在吞吐、精度和显存上同时超越 TF32。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转等变 GNN 架构提升了 ML 通用材料模型的物理保真度，但训练与推理中的高精度张量运算限制其可扩展性。采用 FP8 等低精度 MMU 格式可将吞吐提升至多 30x(60x)（Hopper/Blackwell），却可能带来严重精度损失，因此需要兼顾速度与精度的方案。

Method: 在整数 Ozaki 方案框架下设计定制的“仿真”低精度算子，用以加速 GNN 的 SO(2) 等变处理层——该层主导多数先进 MLIP 与多数通用 MLH 模型的运行时；由此构建框架 FlashSO(2)。

Result: 在吞吐、精度与显存占用三方面同时优于 TF32。SO(2) 层级别：吞吐达 MALOQ(UMA) 参考实现的 40x(7x)，在 GH200 上实现最高 236(232) 有效 Tflop/s，为 FP32 峰值的 3.5x。模型级别：MALOQ 训练比纯 FP32 快 3.9x、峰值显存降低 58% 且精度无损；UMA 分子动力学模拟加速 3.3x、峰值显存降低 51%。

Conclusion: FlashSO(2) 将等变材料模型的速度-精度帕累托前沿推进到原生低精度格式之外，并显著降低利用其缩放律所需资源。

Abstract: Rotationally equivariant architectures improve the physical fidelity of machine-learned (ML) universal materials models based on graph neural networks (GNNs). However, their scalability is limited by the use of non-trivial, high-precision tensor operations during both training and inference. Adopting the reduced-precision formats of matrix multiplication units (MMUs), e.g., FP8 instead of FP32, provides massive increases in throughput, up to 30x (60x) on NVIDIA Hopper (Blackwell) GPUs, but may lead to drastic loss of accuracy. Here, we introduce tailored 'emulated' low-precision operations under the integer Ozaki scheme to accelerate the 'SO(2)-equivariant' processing layers of GNNs, which dominate the runtime of (1) many state-of-the-art ML interatomic potentials (MLIPs) and (2) most universal ML Hamiltonian (MLH) models. Our framework, FlashSO(2), outperforms TF32, the most accurate native low-precision MMU format, in throughput, accuracy, and memory footprint at once. At the SO(2) layer level, it reaches 40x (7x) the throughput of MALOQ's (UMA's) reference implementation, an MLH (MLIP) model, sustaining up to 236 (232) effective Tflop/s on an NVIDIA GH200, 3.5x the FP32 peak. At the model level, FlashSO(2) helps train MALOQ 3.9x faster than its pure-FP32 version while reducing peak memory by 58% at no loss in accuracy. In molecular dynamics simulations with UMA, the speedup factor is 3.3x, and the peak memory is reduced by 51%. FlashSO(2) thus pushes the speed-accuracy Pareto frontier of equivariant materials models beyond the native low-precision formats and substantially lowers the resources required to exploit their scaling laws.

</details>


### [171] [Biology-Guided Context-Aware Residual Propagation for Spatial Gene Expression Prediction from Histology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35167)
*Vu Van An,Long D. Nguyen,Ho Viet Duc Luong,Binh P. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BCARP将组织学图像嵌入视为形态学视觉先验，在面向转录组的隐空间中逐步精炼spots表征，并融合核引导的局部细胞上下文与原型引导的全局上下文传播，配合模式尺度解码器，在3个ST数据集与10个HEST-1k队列上取得最高平均相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间转录组(ST)成本高、通量有限、实验复杂，难以大规模常规使用；从H&E图像预测空间基因表达是可扩展替代方案，但组织学仅提供分子活动的间接形态学视角。现有方法多为单阶段从视觉特征解码表达（即使加入空间聚合、检索或多分辨率融合），而spot水平表达不仅取决于局部形态，还涉及细胞身份、邻域组成与组织级组织方式，单阶段解码形式受限。

Method: 提出BCARP（Biology-guided Context-Aware Residual Propagation）。不做直接从组织学嵌入预测表达，而是将其作为形态视觉先验，在转录组导向的隐空间中逐步精炼spot表征；每一步精炼整合核(nuclei)引导的局部细胞上下文与原型(prototype)引导的全局上下文传播，实现近邻与空间远距离spot间的信息交换；再由模式尺度解码器将空间表达分解为原始表达上的模式预测并进行尺度校准。

Result: 在3个空间转录组数据集与10个HEST-1k队列上评估，BCARP相对其他基线取得最高的平均相关性类指标表现。

Conclusion: 数值结果支持"生物学引导的局部—全局残差传播"作为组织学→转录组预测中视觉解码的有效替代范式。

Abstract: Spatial transcriptomics provides spatially resolved molecular profiling, but its cost, limited throughput and experimental complexity hinder routine large-scale use. Predicting spatial gene expression from hematoxylin-and-eosin-stained histology images offers a scalable alternative, yet remains challenging because histology provides only an indirect morphological view of molecular activity. Existing methods typically decode expression from visual features in a single stage, even when enhanced with spatial aggregation, retrieval or multi-resolution fusion. This formulation is restrictive because spot-level expression reflects not only local morphology but also cellular identity, neighborhood composition and tissue-level organization. We propose BCARP, a Biology-guided Context-Aware Residual Propagation framework for histology-guided spatial transcriptomics prediction. Instead of directly predicting gene expression from histology embeddings, BCARP treats them as morphology visual priors and progressively refines spot representations in a transcriptomics-oriented latent space. Each refinement step integrates nuclei-guided local cellular context with prototype-guided global context propagation, enabling information exchange across both neighboring and spatially distant spots. A pattern-scale decoder further separates spatial expression into pattern prediction from raw expression with scale calibration. Evaluated on three ST datasets and ten HEST-1k cohorts, BCARP achieves the highest mean correlation-based performance against other baselines. Numerical results support biology-guided local-global residual propagation as an effective alternative to visual decoding for histology-to-transcriptomics prediction.

</details>


### [172] [DAC-Loop: Distillation-Aware Compression for Remote Sensing CNN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35175)
*Rico Staedeli,Lorena Raichle,Konstantinos Tzevelekakis,Aron Asefaw,Damian Borth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出蒸馏感知压缩循环DAC-Loop，将知识蒸馏与结构化剪枝、迭代稀疏化结合，在EuroSAT与NWPU-RESISC45上实现超90%参数削减下仍保持高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型模型在遥感场景分类上性能强，但边缘设备算力、内存与能耗受限，难以部署；紧凑CNN虽实用，仍需进一步提升压缩效率并保持高精度。

Method: 在紧凑CNN骨干上，采用知识蒸馏学生模型，并在结构化通道剪枝前后各施加一次蒸馏；同时设计迭代压缩调度，交替执行结构化剪枝与非结构化稀疏化，形成闭环压缩流程。

Result: 在EuroSAT与NWPU-RESISC45上，DAC-Loop相较一次性剪枝与单阶段蒸馏恢复，持续改善精度—压缩权衡，参数削减超过90%时仍保持较强精度。

Conclusion: 将蒸馏与剪枝统一于迭代闭环，是构建轻量遥感分类模型的有效路径。

Abstract: While large-scale models achieve strong results in remote sensing scene classification, deploying them on edge devices remains difficult due to limited compute, memory, and energy budgets. Compact CNN backbones are therefore a practical choice in many real-world settings, offering efficient inference with well-optimized implementations on embedded hardware. To push this efficiency further, we investigate how far a knowledge-distilled student model can be compressed while preserving high accuracy. We propose a Distillation-Aware Compression Loop (DAC-Loop) that integrates (i) knowledge distillation applied both before and after structured channel pruning, and (ii) an iterative compression schedule that alternates structured pruning with unstructured sparsification. Across EuroSAT and NWPU-RESISC45, the DAC-Loop consistently improves the accuracy-compression trade-off relative to standard one-shot pruning and single-stage KD recovery, maintaining strong accuracy even beyond 90% parameter reduction. These results demonstrate that combining distillation and pruning in a unified iterative loop offers an effective path toward compact remote sensing models.

</details>


### [173] [LEAP: a Latent Embedding Anomaly Patch-Transformer for time-series anomaly detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35190)
*Cristhian Moya-Mota,Ignacio Aguilera-Martos,Diego García-Gil,Julián Luengo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出LEAP（Latent Embedding Anomaly Patch-Transformer），用掩码预测替代重构式检测，并以潜表示新颖性、局部时序对比与通道选择池化构成免标签异常证据，在TSB-AD基准上取得VUS-PR 0.5179（多变量）与0.6194（单变量）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列异常检测以重构式神经网络为主，但由于目标即输入，表达力强的模型连异常也能重构，残差失去信息量；大规模基准显示深度检测器常难超越简单统计基线，当前领先方法依赖表示学习，因此需要一个不依赖重构、也不依赖注意力打分的框架。

Method: LEAP在每个窗口内移除若干patch，由剩余时序与跨通道上下文预测被隐藏的patch，使预测误差衡量学到的动态对隐藏片段的解释能力；采用确定性互补分组掩码，保证每个时间步都在隐藏状态下被打分，且训练与推理的可见比例一致。在此之上引入三路免标签证据：按局部密度校准的潜嵌入新颖性、局部时序对比、以及按六项免标签准则筛选通道后逐通道新颖性池化。单变量变体将多尺度掩码预测打分与潜新颖性结合。

Result: 在TSB-AD协议下，完整模型于180条留出多变量序列上VUS-PR为0.5179±0.0022；单变量变体于TSB-AD-U的350条留出序列上为0.6194±0.0016，均为三次随机种子平均。TSB-AD-M上的五阶段消融配合配对Wilcoxon符号秩检验，表明各组件逐级带来统计显著增益。

Conclusion: 以掩码预测打分加潜表示证据可绕开重构范式与注意力打分的内在缺陷，在统一基准上取得有竞争力且经统计检验支持的性能，并通过消融验证各组件必要性与渐进增益。

Abstract: Time-series anomaly detection is dominated by reconstruction-based neural detectors, whose objective is structurally flawed. Since the target is also the input, an expressive model can learn to reconstruct anomalies too, leaving uninformative residuals. Recent large-scale benchmarks show that, on curated data, elaborate deep detectors rarely beat simple statistical baselines, while current leaders rely on representation learning. We propose LEAP (Latent Embedding Anomaly Patch-Transformer), which combines masked prediction with latent representation-based anomaly evidence, without relying on reconstruction or attention-based scoring. The model removes patches from a window and predicts them from the remaining temporal and cross-channel context, so prediction errors measure how well learned dynamics explain hidden segments. Deterministic complementary group masking ensures every time step is scored while hidden, with identical visible fractions during training and inference. LEAP adds three label-free evidence sources: latent-embedding novelty calibrated by local density, local temporal contrast, and per-channel novelty pooled over channels selected by six label-free criteria. Under the TSB-AD protocol, the full model achieves a VUS-PR of $0.5179 \pm 0.0022$ on $180$ held-out multivariate series. On TSB-AD-U, a univariate variant combining multi-scale masked-prediction scoring with latent novelty, without contrast or channel-pool novelty, achieves $0.6194 \pm 0.0016$ on $350$ held-out series. Both results average three random seeds. A five-stage ablation on TSB-AD-M, supported by paired Wilcoxon signed-rank tests, shows statistically significant gains from each successive component.

</details>


### [174] [Understanding Practical Optimization in Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35209)
*Egor Shulgin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文主张：对实用优化方法应"忠实建模"——直接分析实际程序所定义的目标、假设、更新与计算，而非简化代理算法；并据此对子网络训练、隐私优化与线性最小化预言机型优化器给出了新理论与框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习实践中的优化方法与经典理论分析所研究的算法存在显著差异：实践中会训练子模型、裁剪或归一化梯度、使用逐层的非欧几里得方向、或近似昂贵的内部计算。以往理论往往用简化代理（proxy）来分析，导致结论与实际优化行为脱节。作者希望让理论保留决定实际优化行为的关键特征。

Method: ① 目标层面：用"先sketch再微分"与"把sketch分布纳入目标"两类构造对比，刻画子网络训练的偏差来源；② 隐私优化：分析自适应分位数裁剪在固定分位数下的偏差，设计分位数调度（quantile schedule）以在重尾噪声下恢复近优非凸收敛率；再将平滑归一化与误差补偿结合，在无需有界梯度假设的标准条件下给出分布式隐私收敛保证；③ LMO类优化器：建立并验证逐层广义平滑性（generalized smoothness）模型，据此构建面向Muon/Scion风格方法的Gluon框架，并分析Muon中近似正交化的精度与步长的耦合关系。

Result: (1) 独立子网络训练（IST）会收敛到"偏移不动点"，根源是"先sketch后求导"使更新对原目标有偏；模型无关稀疏训练（MAST）将sketch分布纳入目标后，sketched梯度无偏并具备收敛保证。(2) 自适应分位数裁剪在任意固定分位数下保留偏差；采用合适的分位数调度可在重尾噪声下达到近优非凸收敛率；结合平滑归一化与误差补偿，可在不假设梯度有界的前提下获得分布式隐私收敛保证。(3) 逐层广义平滑模型得到验证，Gluon框架统一刻画Muon/Scion类方法；Muon中近似正交化精度至关重要——正交化越不精确需越小步长，故其精度应与学习率联合调优。

Conclusion: 实用优化方法只有在其理论保留决定优化行为的关键特征时才能被正确理解；否则，若用简化代理替换实际程序，可能得出与实际优化动态不符的结论。具体地，偏差的来源（sketch位置、分位数设定、正交化精度）本身应作为理论对象，并可作为超参数与学习率一同调节。

Abstract: Modern machine learning relies on practical optimization methods that often differ from the algorithms studied in classical analyses: they train submodels, clip or normalize gradients, use layer-wise non-Euclidean directions, or approximate costly inner computations. This thesis analyzes the objective, assumptions, update, and computation defined by these procedures, rather than those of simplified proxies. For submodel training, we show that Independent Subnetwork Training can converge to a shifted fixed point because sketching before differentiation biases the update for the original objective. Model-Agnostic Sparsified Training instead incorporates the sketch distribution into its objective, which makes sketched gradients unbiased and admits convergence guarantees. For private optimization, we show that adaptive quantile clipping retains bias at any fixed quantile, while suitable quantile schedules recover near-optimal nonconvex rates under heavy-tailed noise. We then combine smoothed normalization with error compensation and obtain distributed private convergence guarantees under standard assumptions, without assuming bounded gradients. For optimizers based on linear minimization oracles, we develop and validate a layer-wise generalized smoothness model and build on it the Gluon framework for Muon- and Scion-style methods. We also show that the approximate orthogonalization in Muon matters: less precise orthogonalization calls for smaller stepsizes, so its precision should be tuned together with the learning rate. Overall, practical methods are better understood when theory retains the features that determine their optimization behavior.

</details>


### [175] [Efficient Exact Unlearning for Federated Zeroth-Order Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35211)
*Weijun Yin,Yanqing Yao,Falko Dressler,Di Wang,Youming Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文将总变差稳定性与精确遗忘相结合，提出两种稳定零阶联邦学习算法 FedZOO-S/FedZOO-NS 及其配套精确遗忘流程 FedZOUL-S/FedZOUL-NS，实现高效且可证明的数据删除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦零阶优化（FedZOO）通过避免反向传播、仅传输随机种子与标量系数，降低了边缘智能协同训练的内存与通信开销；但现有方法缺乏高效精确遗忘机制，无法在用户请求删除数据时彻底移除训练数据影响。作者的关键洞察是：学习算法的总变差稳定性使删数据前后模型分布存在重叠，从而可通过耦合复用计算实现精确遗忘。

Method: 联合设计两种稳定零阶学习算法及对应精确遗忘流程：(1) FedZOO-S 通过客户端与 mini-batch 子采样获得稳定性，FedZOUL-S 保留较早更新、从首个受影响步重启实现高效遗忘；(2) FedZOO-NS 在子采样基础上加入高斯噪声以增大更新分布重叠，FedZOUL-NS 通过拒绝—反射耦合（rejection–reflection coupling）在耦合被接受时复用受影响更新。理论层面证明两种算法的精确遗忘性（模型与保存状态同"在保留数据上重训"具有相同联合分布），并给出稳定性与收敛保证及期望遗忘代价上界。

Result: 实验表明可实现有效遗忘且模型效用具竞争力，累积遗忘时间较重新优化最多减少 56.1%；与一阶基线相比，通信开销最多降低约四个数量级。

Conclusion: 通过将总变差稳定性作为设计原则，可把精确遗忘嵌入零阶联邦学习，兼顾隐私删除正确性、训练收敛性与通信/计算效率，为边缘智能场景下的可遗忘协同训练提供可行方案。

Abstract: Federated zeroth-order optimization (FedZOO) reduces the memory and communication costs of collaborative training for edge intelligence by avoiding backpropagation while communicating only random seeds and scalar coefficients. However, existing methods lack efficient mechanisms for exact unlearning to fully remove the influence of training data when users request deletion. A key insight is that total variation stability of the learning algorithm creates overlap between model distributions before and after data deletion, allowing exact unlearning to reuse computation through coupling. Based on this observation, we jointly design two stable zeroth-order learning algorithms and their corresponding exact-unlearning procedures: \textsc{FedZOO-S} obtains stability through client and mini-batch subsampling, enabling \textsc{FedZOUL-S} to perform efficient unlearning by preserving earlier updates and restarting from the first affected step. \textsc{FedZOO-NS} further combines subsampling with Gaussian noise to increase overlap between update distributions; \textsc{FedZOUL-NS} exploits this overlap through rejection--reflection coupling to reuse affected updates when the coupling is accepted. We prove exact unlearning for both algorithms: the model and saved state have the same joint distribution as retraining with the corresponding learner on retained data. We further establish stability and convergence guarantees for the learning algorithms and derive upper bounds on expected unlearning cost. Experiments demonstrate effective unlearning with competitive model utility and up to $56.1\%$ less cumulative unlearning time than re-optimization. Compared with first-order baselines, our methods reduce communication overhead by up to approximately four orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [176] [One-Shot Federated Model Editing for Device Dynamics at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35212)
*Youming Tao,Weijun Yin,Huanyi Xie,Yanqing Yao,Dongxiao Yu,Xiuzhen Cheng,Di Wang,Falko Dressler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出F-SOSA，首个稳定的单轮联邦学习算法，通过局部模型子采样与曲率感知融合实现稳定聚合，并在此基础上构建单轮精确模型编辑算法F-SOSA-U（遗忘）与F-SOSA-I（增量学习），使编辑模型与重训练统计不可区分，仅需约O(ρ)比例开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统FL需多轮持续连接，不适用于车辆网络等设备频繁加入/离开的动态场景；单轮FL虽只需一次通信，但存在模型固化问题，无法适应后续客户端动态（新贡献加入、旧贡献撤回）。

Method: 识别出高效编辑的关键在于学习稳定性，据此提出F-SOSA：以精心调校的速率对局部模型进行子采样以实现稳定聚合，并采用曲率感知模型融合保持效用；证明其训练误差趋于零、泛化误差界合理小；进一步发展两个单轮编辑算法——F-SOSA-U（遗忘撤回贡献）和F-SOSA-I（增量纳入新贡献）。

Result: 理论证明编辑后模型及所有中间算法状态与从头重训练统计不可区分，期望开销仅为重训练成本的O(ρ)比例（ρ量化稳定性）；在城市移动性仿真实验中匹配重训练性能，累计时间与通信开销分别至少降低36%和22%。

Conclusion: 学习稳定性是单轮联邦学习实现高效精确模型编辑的基础；F-SOSA在保持理论保证的同时兼顾效用与效率，为动态客户端环境下的联邦学习提供了可编辑的单轮方案。

Abstract: Standard federated learning (FL) requires persistent device connectivity across multiple communication rounds, making it unsuitable for dynamic environments such as vehicular networks, where devices frequently join and leave. One-shot FL offers a promising alternative by completing training in a single communication round. However, one-shot FL suffers from model solidification and cannot readily adapt to subsequent client dynamics, including new client contributions and the withdrawal of previous contributions. We therefore consider exact model editing, which removes withdrawn contributions through unlearning or incorporates newly contributed ones through incremental learning without retraining from scratch. We identify that efficient editing fundamentally hinges on learning stability. To this end, we propose F-SOSA, the first stable one-shot FL algorithm that enables rapid and exact model editing. F-SOSA leverages sub-sampling of local models with carefully tuned rate for stable aggregation and performs curvature-aware model fusion to preserve utility. We prove that F-SOSA achieves vanishing training error and reasonably small generalization error bounds. Building on this stability, we further develop two single-round editing algorithms: F-SOSA-U for unlearning and F-SOSA-I for incremental learning. They ensure that the edited model, along with all intermediate algorithmic states, is statistically indistinguishable from retraining while incurring only an expected $O(ρ)$ fraction of its cost, where $ρ$ quantifies stability. Experiments demonstrate that our approach matches retraining performance while reducing cumulative time and communication overhead by at least 36% and 22%, respectively, under realistic urban mobility simulations.

</details>


### [177] [SkillLifeBench: Benchmarking Security Across the Lifecycle of Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35215)
*Chenxu Zhang,Zhou Liu,Wu Xin,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 SkillLifeBench，用 388 个配对场景将技能复用中的三种状态（存储代码、选择版本、实际加载实现）与任务结果关联，并在运行时任务与自主生命周期任务两条决策轨道上评测 14 个模型，揭示生命周期任务更能区分模型，且执行溯源证据可显著提升恢复成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web agent 在复用技能时，技能的存储代码、被选中的版本与实际加载的实现可能在不同任务与更新之间发生偏离，这种"状态漂移"会导致难以察觉的行为差异与安全风险。但现有基准难以将这些分离状态与任务实际结果挂钩，也缺乏对 agent 在完整生命周期中检查、选择与重配置技能能力的系统评测。

Method: 构建 SkillLifeBench，包含 388 个配对场景，覆盖实现变更、接口变更、运行时变更、组合变更与版本变更五类变化。评测 14 个模型，设置两条配对决策轨道：89 个运行时任务，以及 60 个自主生命周期任务（由引入案例衍生出 12 个模板），允许 agent 检查、选择并重配置技能以完成可验证的用户请求；联合成功定义为"完成任务且未达成注册的攻击目标"。此外开展匹配的恢复研究，对比日志与元数据、确定性执行一致性规则、完整恢复三种条件下的表现。

Result: 在运行时任务上 GPT-5.6-Sol 与 Sonnet 4.6 均为 82.0%，但在生命周期任务上分别达 96.7% 与 56.7%，差异巨大。生命周期评测中受挑战任务成功率为 77.5%，联合成功率仅 65.2%，并有 103 次运行同时完成了任务与攻击目标。恢复研究中，加入执行溯源后联合成功率从仅用日志与元数据的 49.35% 提升至 65.58%；确定性执行一致性规则达 64.94% 且良性效用更高，完整恢复达 69.48%。

Conclusion: 生命周期任务能够区分运行时得分相近的模型，暴露被单一运行时指标掩盖的能力差距；执行证据（溯源、一致性规则、恢复机制）可有效改善故障恢复并同时提升安全性与良性效用。SkillLifeBench 为技能状态一致性与 agent 生命周期管理提供了可复现的评测框架。

Abstract: Web agents reuse skills whose stored code, selected version, and loaded implementation can diverge across tasks and updates. SkillLifeBench links these states to task outcomes in 388 paired scenarios spanning implementation, interface, runtime, composition, and version changes. We evaluate 14 models on two paired decision tracks: 89 runtime tasks and 60 autonomous lifecycle tasks spanning 12 templates derived from introduced cases. Lifecycle tasks allow agents to inspect, select, and reconfigure skills while completing verifiable user requests. Joint success requires task completion without the registered attack goal. GPT-5.6-Sol and Sonnet 4.6 both achieve 82.0% on runtime tasks, but reach 96.7% and 56.7% on lifecycle tasks. Across lifecycle evaluations, challenged task success is 77.5%, while joint success is 65.2%; 103 runs complete the task and the attack goal. Adding execution provenance in a matched recovery study raises utility joint success from 49.35% with logs and metadata to 65.58%. A deterministic execution-consistency rule reaches 64.94% with higher benign utility, while full restoration reaches 69.48%. Lifecycle tasks distinguish models with similar runtime scores, and execution evidence improves recovery.

</details>


### [178] [NOMAD-EEG: Native Omni-Montage Adaptive Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35246)
*Abdulkader Helwan,Khadidja Henni,Lina Abou-Abbas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NOMAD-EEG 是一个 12.0M 参数、与电极数量/身份/顺序及输入长度无关的生成式脑电基础模型，在多个任务上以小模型超越 7 个更大的脑电基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脑电基础模型普遍绑定于训练时的通道集合与输入长度，而实际脑电记录在导联方式、通道数与时长上差异极大，缺乏通用性。

Method: 构建基于扩散的生成式模型：沿时间方向使用无分块（patch-free）的状态空间层，跨电极使用注意力并以电极的 3D 头皮位置作为唯一身份标识；在单一语料 147 小时无标注脑电上预训练，用冻结线性探针在 10 个数据集（1–64 电极）上与 7 个基础模型统一协议对比，并测试两个未见人群。

Result: 在 EEGMMIDB 运动想象（平衡准确率 0.442）、Siena 癫痫发作检测（macro F1 0.622）、Bonn 发作检测（AUROC 0.999）上超越全部 7 个模型；TUEP 癫痫诊断准确率最高（0.640）；微调后 TUEV 事件分类 kappa 0.635、加权 F1 0.814 最高；在未见 8 电极导联的自闭症 P300 检测中为 5 个模型中最佳（AUROC 0.718）；仅用 19 电极中的 5 个，平均 AUROC 仅从 0.942 降至 0.891。

Conclusion: 以位置标识电极、无分块地建模时间，使一个预训练于单一语料的小模型在癫痫发作检测、癫痫样事件分类、癫痫诊断与运动想象任务上优于更大的脑电基础模型。

Abstract: Purpose. Electroencephalography (EEG) montages, channel counts and recording lengths vary widely, but most EEG foundation models are bound to the channel set and input length they were trained on. We aimed to build a generative EEG foundation model independent of the number, identity and order of electrodes and of the input length. Methods. NOMAD-EEG is a 12.0 M-parameter diffusion model that alternates state-space layers along time, without patches, with attention across electrodes identified only by their 3D scalp position. It was pre-trained on 147 h of unlabelled EEG from a single corpus and evaluated with frozen linear probes on ten datasets with 1 to 64 electrodes against seven foundation models run under one protocol, and on two unseen populations. Results. Despite its small size (12.0 M parameters against 69 M for REVE) and single pre- training corpus, NOMAD-EEG outperformed all seven foundation models on EEGMMIDB mo- tor imagery (balanced accuracy 0.442), Siena seizure detection (macro F1 0.622) and Bonn seizure detection (AUROC 0.999), had the highest accuracy of the seven compared models on TUEP epilepsy diagnosis (0.640) and, after fine-tuning, the highest \k{appa} (0.635) and weighted F1 (0.814) on TUEV event classification. It was the best of five models on P300 detection in autism with an unseen eight-electrode montage (AUROC 0.718). With only 5 of 19 electrodes, its mean AUROC fell only from 0.942 to 0.891. Conclusion. Identifying electrodes by position and modelling time without patches let one small model, pre-trained on one corpus, outperform much larger EEG foundation models on seizure detection, epileptiform event classification, epilepsy diagnosis and motor imagery.

</details>


### [179] [Conversational Generalized Linear Bandits with Hybrid Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35262)
*Yurui Zou,Qirun Zeng,Jinhang Zuo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出混合反馈对话广义线性bandit框架（物品绝对奖励+关键词相对比较），设计HyConUCB算法，统一置信界并自适应选择关键词比较，取得更优后悔界并经实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话bandit方法假设物品与关键词反馈同质（全绝对奖励或全成对比较），而实际中物品反馈常为点击/评分（绝对），关键词反馈更适合比较（相对），二者异质性未被建模。

Method: 将问题形式化为混合反馈的对话广义线性bandit，提出HyConUCB：由异质反馈构建统一置信界，按信息增益与方向覆盖度的平衡自适应选择关键词比较查询。

Result: 给出联合利用两类反馈源的精细化后悔分析，后悔界优于仅奖励与改编的仅比较两类基线；合成与真实数据集实验验证有效性。

Conclusion: 混合反馈建模能有效利用异质信号，统一置信界与自适应比较选择提升了对话推荐的理论与实证表现。

Abstract: Conversational bandits improve recommendations by actively querying users about key-terms to elicit their item preferences. However, existing methods typically assume homogeneous feedback for items and key-terms, using either absolute rewards or pairwise comparisons. In practice, item feedback often takes the form of clicks or ratings, while comparisons are more natural for key-terms. To address this gap, we study conversational generalized linear bandits with hybrid feedback, combining absolute item rewards and relative key-term comparisons. We propose Conversational UCB with Hybrid Feedback (HyConUCB), which constructs unified confidence bounds from heterogeneous feedback and adaptively selects key-term comparisons by balancing information gain and directional coverage. Through a refined regret analysis that jointly exploits both feedback sources, we establish a regret bound that improves upon reward-only and adapted dueling-only benchmarks. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets validate the effectiveness of our algorithm.

</details>


### [180] [InterQ-RL: Interaction-Aware Correction of Execution Mismatch in Quantized Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35303)
*Yingbo Hao,Song Liu,Guanghua Yu,Zhaojing Zhou,Jianchen Zhu,Yi Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 InterQ-RL，用"交互感知"的定界执行前缀校正来消除量化 rollout 在对齐后残留的历史性策略失配，在 W8A8-KV8 下使多轮 agent RL 的量化端点性能逼近 BF16。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮 agent RL 依赖长闭环 rollout，生成与 KV-cache 开销成为主要瓶颈；量化 rollout 可降本，但低精度执行器同时充当行为策略，造成与 learner 的执行失配。现有量化 RL 方法面向单轮生成，只做 learner 前向与量化 rollout 的对齐，或校正当前 token 的 rollout/learner 比率，未建模产生当前状态的历史策略，因此残差失配沿到达状态的策略历史累积。

Method: InterQ-RL（Interaction-aware Quantized RL）：在完成量化对齐之后，对"到达该状态的策略历史"施加定界（bounded）执行前缀校正；在固定位移预算下，按有效支撑度（effective support）在动作片段间分配校正强度；整个过程不需要额外模型前向。

Result: 在 ALFWorld、WebShop、ScienceWorld 上评测 4B–30B 的 dense 与 MoE 骨干，所有量化组共用同一套 8-bit 权重/激活/KV cache（W8A8-KV8）rollout。四个环境-骨干组合中，InterQ-RL 均取得最强量化端点结果，与 BF16 差距在 0.7 分以内，其中两个设置超过 BF16；学习速度不慢于仅对齐方案，且避免了后者的后期性能回退。

Conclusion: 多轮 agent RL 的量化失配关键在于策略历史而非当前动作；对齐后的前缀校正是一种无需额外前向、开销低的补正手段，可在 W8A8-KV8 下稳定缩小量化与 BF16 的差距。

Abstract: Multi-turn agent reinforcement learning (RL) relies on long closed-loop rollouts, making generation and KV-cache costs a major bottleneck. Quantized rollout reduces these costs, but its low-precision executor also serves as the behavior policy and can induce execution mismatch with the learner. Existing quantized-RL methods are designed primarily for single-turn generation. They align the learner forward with quantized rollout or correct the current-token rollout-learner ratio, without modeling the policy history that produces each agent state. Even after quantization alignment, residual rollout-learner mismatch accumulates along the state-reaching policy history, so a current action may look locally aligned despite a mismatched history. We introduce InterQ-RL (Interaction-aware Quantized Reinforcement Learning) to correct this post-alignment mismatch. It applies bounded execution-prefix correction over the state-reaching policy history, allocates correction strength across action segments by effective support under a fixed displacement budget, and requires no additional model forward pass. We evaluate dense and mixture-of-experts (MoE) backbones from 4B to 30B on ALFWorld, WebShop, and ScienceWorld, with all quantized arms sharing the same rollout with 8-bit weights, activations, and KV cache (W8A8-KV8). Across four environment-backbone settings, InterQ-RL achieves the strongest quantized endpoint result, matching BF16 to within 0.7 points and exceeding it in two settings. It matches or exceeds the learning speed of alignment alone while avoiding its late performance regression.

</details>


### [181] [Inference Latency-Aware Tsetlin Machine Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35304)
*Yefan Zeng,Shengyu Duan,Rishad Shafik,Alex Yakovlev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Tsetlin Machine（TM）训练超参数T与s对分类精度和推理延迟的联合影响，提出一种以精度为约束、朝更大s方向推进的有向搜索策略，在gem5+ARM平台上于六个数据集上将推理延迟最多降低16.34%，精度损失最小仅0.06%，且配置评估次数远少于穷举网格搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TM适合资源受限边缘设备的低延迟推理；其子句为布尔字面量的AND，字面量与TA动作均可比特级表示，从而支持按位求值并在任一字面量为0时提前退出。然而训练超参数T、s对精度与推理延迟的共同作用尚不清晰，且穷举(T, s)网格搜索训练成本过高，因此需要一种低成本地将超参数配置导向低延迟的方法。

Method: 基于T与s近似成反比、精度沿该关系近乎恒定的性质，指出推理延迟主要随s增大而降低；据此提出有向搜索策略：将精度设为约束，在固定评估预算下沿T-s反比方向逐步增大s以压缩推理延迟，替代高成本的全网格穷举搜索。实验在gem5模拟器搭配ARM处理器上进行，覆盖六个数据集。

Result: 对部分数据集，搜索可在保持基线精度的前提下增大s，从而以可忽略的精度代价降低推理延迟：六个数据集上推理延迟最多下降16.34%，部分数据集精度仅下降0.06%；同时所需配置评估次数远少于穷举网格搜索。

Conclusion: 推理延迟主要由s而非T控制，沿T-s反比关系的有向搜索可在近似保持精度的条件下有效减少延迟，是一种显著优于穷举网格搜索的低成本超参数调优方案，适用于对延迟敏感的边缘部署场景。

Abstract: The Tsetlin Machine (TM) is a logic-based machine learning model well suited to low-latency inference on resource-constrained edge devices. Because each clause is an AND over Boolean literals, both the literals and their associated Tsetlin Automaton (TA) actions can be represented at the bit level, enabling bitwise evaluation and an early exit that is triggered as soon as any included literal evaluates to zero, forcing the clause output to zero. In this work, we study how the two TM training hyperparameters, T and s, govern both classification accuracy and inference latency. Building on the inversely proportional T-s relationship along which accuracy is nearly constant, we find that inference latency is reduced primarily by increasing s; rather than exhaustively searching the (T, s) grid at high training cost, we propose a directed search strategy that treats accuracy as a constraint and advances toward larger s to reduce inference latency within a fixed evaluation budget. Experimental results on the gem5 simulator with an ARM processor show that, for certain datasets, the search is able to increase s while preserving the baseline accuracy, thereby reducing inference latency at negligible accuracy cost: across six datasets, the method reduces inference latency by up to 16.34%, while some datasets exhibit an accuracy reduction of only 0.06%. The proposed strategy requires far fewer configuration evaluations than an exhaustive grid search.

</details>


### [182] [Shared Context, Separate Engines: Streaming Vision--Language Inference on Edge SoCs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35309)
*Yilong Li,Chengpo Yan,Yijing Zeng,Hanlei Wu,Mingjun Song,Suman Banerjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个异构加速器协同的VLM推理运行时，通过将流式版本化上下文与各引擎本地KV状态解耦，实现跨CPU/GPU/NPU的有状态并发推理，在多种边缘SoC上显著超越单引擎基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘SoC同时集成CPU、GPU和NPU，但现有VLM运行时只把生成放在单一引擎上，其余引擎闲置。流式VLM本身具备并行性（新帧到达与旧查询作答可重叠），但不同引擎算力与访存特性不同、同时运行会相互争抢资源，因此不存在跨SoC与工作负载通用的阶段放置方案；此外各引擎本地KV状态彼此隔离，使其他引擎无法接手后续工作。

Method: 提出\name运行时，协调异构加速器并发执行有状态VLM推理：(1) 将流的版本化上下文与各引擎KV状态分离，任一引擎可通过复用、导入或重建状态来继续该流；(2) Stateful Streaming Generation：一个引擎接收新帧，另一引擎同时作答；(3) Concurrent Sequence Generation：并行服务不同的就绪请求；(4) 状态感知调度：将请求派发至预计最先完成的引擎；(5) 每个答案读取固定的已发布历史版本，从而与串行参考结果保持一致。

Result: 在Apple M4 Max、Rockchip RK3588和Qualcomm QCS6490上均优于最强单引擎基线（含上下文复用情形）。在两条RK3588视频流上，相比带上下文复用的最佳单引擎吞吐高19.6%，相比无共享状态的并行引擎高27.2%。跨引擎KV导入使首token延迟相比前缀重计算降低4.6倍。Stateful Streaming Generation吞吐最高达串行执行的2.48倍，状态感知调度使查询完成时间最多缩短72.7%。

Conclusion: 将上下文版本化与引擎本地KV状态解耦，是释放边缘SoC异构算力、实现高效有状态流式VLM推理的关键，可在保持与串行参考一致性的前提下获得显著的吞吐与延迟收益。

Abstract: Edge SoCs pack CPUs, GPUs, and NPUs, yet vision--language model (VLM) runtimes keep generation on one engine and leave the others idle. Streaming VLMs expose further parallelism: new frames arrive while earlier queries are still being answered. Engines differ in compute and memory access and contend when they co-run, so no single placement of stages wins across SoCs and workloads. Backend-local KV state keeps other engines from continuing the work. \name coordinates heterogeneous accelerators to execute stateful VLM inference concurrently. It separates a stream's versioned context from each engine's KV state, so any engine can continue the stream by reusing, importing, or reconstructing that state. Stateful Streaming Generation ingests new frames on one engine while another answers, and Concurrent Sequence Generation serves different ready requests in parallel. State-aware dispatch sends each request to the engine expected to finish it first. Each answer reads a fixed, published version of its history and matches its serial reference. \name beats the strongest single-engine baselines on Apple M4 Max, Rockchip RK3588, and Qualcomm QCS6490, even when they reuse context. On two RK3588 video streams, it delivers $19.6\%$ more throughput than the best single engine with context reuse and $27.2\%$ more than parallel engines without shared state. Cross-engine KV import cuts first-token latency by a factor of $4.6$ over prefix recomputation. Stateful Streaming Generation delivers up to $2.48\times$ the throughput of serial execution, and state-aware dispatch cuts query completion time by up to $72.7\%$.

</details>


### [183] [CoPE-CGL: Community-aware Prototype Extraction for Replay-Free Continual Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35313)
*Bo-Young Lim,Seokhwan Kim,Kisung Lee,Hyuk-Yoon Kwon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoPE-CGL 是一个无回放的持续图学习框架，用多跳拉普拉斯平滑预计算的无参数结构先验来锚定学习、记忆与推理，在等存储、无任务标识条件下匹配最强回放基线，且速度更快、零遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续图学习方法通常只针对直推或归纳单一设定，且往往需要存储过去的子图或推理时依赖任务标识。作者希望提出一种无回放、不保留历史图数据、推理时无需任务标识，同时能统一处理直推与归纳场景的框架。

Method: 提出 CoPE-CGL：利用多跳拉普拉斯平滑预计算的参数无关结构先验，结合拓扑注入的瓶颈适配器、社区级原型，以及基于节点-原型匹配多数投票的动态路由。默认协议假设每个测试图来自单一已学任务，另设节点级变体处理混合任务图。每任务仅增加常数级存储，不保留历史图数据。

Result: 在等存储、无任务标识下，CoPE-CGL 与获得真实任务标识的最强回放基线相当：Arxiv 与 CoraFull 上 AP 分别为 95.17%/95.72% 对 94.28%/95.82%，运行速度快 5.9–14.0 倍且零遗忘。在四个直推、三个归纳基准及混合流上，相对多达十一个基线中的次优者分别最高超出 1.41、6.32、9.33 个 AP 百分点；端到端速度比各基准最准竞争者快 1.3–7.9 倍，并可扩展到最大规模基准图。混合两任务图上节点级变体取得 CGL 方法中最优 AP。

Conclusion: CoPE-CGL 通过无参数结构先验实现了高效、无回放、无任务标识的持续图学习，统一覆盖直推与归纳设定，但设计面向具有社区结构的同ophilic图。

Abstract: Continual graph learning (CGL) maintains models as graphs evolve without retraining from scratch, while mitigating catastrophic forgetting. Existing methods typically target either transductive or inductive settings and often store past subgraphs or require task identifiers at inference. We propose Community-aware Prototype Extraction for Continual Graph Learning (CoPE-CGL), a replay-free framework that uses a parameter-free structural prior, precomputed by multi-hop Laplacian smoothing, to anchor learning, memory, and inference. CoPE-CGL combines topology-infused bottleneck adaptation, community-level prototypes, and dynamic routing by majority vote over node-to-prototype matches. The default protocol assumes each test graph comes from one learned task; a node-level variant handles mixed-task graphs. Each task adds at most a constant amount of storage, with no past graph data retained. At equal storage and without task identifiers, CoPE-CGL matches the strongest replay baseline even when it receives true task identifiers: average performance (AP) is 95.17% and 95.72% versus 94.28% and 95.82% on Arxiv and CoraFull, respectively, with 5.9-14.0 times faster runtime and zero forgetting. Across four transductive and three inductive benchmarks and mixed streams, CoPE-CGL exceeds the second-best of up to eleven baselines by up to 1.41, 6.32, and 9.33 AP percentage points, respectively. It runs 1.3-7.9 times faster end-to-end than the most accurate competitor on each benchmark and scales to the largest benchmark graph. On graphs mixing two tasks, its node-level variant achieves the best AP among CGL methods. The design targets homophilic graphs with community structure.

</details>


### [184] [Should Pseudo-Labels Share Mini-Batches with Labeled Examples? Semi-Supervised Continual Active Learning with Limited Training Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35345)
*Shiqi Zhang,Tuomas Virtanen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文首次形式化了持续主动学习（CAL）中伪标签样本是否以及如何与有标签样本共享固定数量小批次训练预算的问题，提出SSCAL方法：基于无标签样本到有标签近邻的距离生成伪标签，限制其在每个小批次中的占比，并重加权有标签损失以保持其期望梯度贡献不变。在冻结嵌入上，伪标签器可增量更新且无需额外前向计算。实验在2个音频数据集、2种冻结编码器、6种采集函数、10个随机种子下进行，SSCAL在训练步数相同的四种协议中平均排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动学习通过查询信息量大的样本降低标注成本，持续主动学习（CAL）进一步通过每轮固定小批次步数训练单一模型来降低总梯度步数。伪标签可在无标注情况下提供监督，但会增加训练成本。在CAL中每轮仅训练固定数量小批次，因此伪标签样本是否以及如何与有标签样本共享这些批次仍是一个开放问题。

Method: 将问题形式化为每次查询后的两个决策：(i) 哪些无标签样本应以伪标签进入训练；(ii) 它们应占每个小批次的多大比例。提出SSCAL（半监督持续主动学习），可与任意采集函数配合：基于无标签样本到有标签近邻的距离生成伪标签，对其占比设上限，并重加权有标签样本损失以保持其期望梯度贡献不变；在冻结嵌入上伪标签器增量更新，无需额外模型前向传播。

Result: 在2个音频数据集、2种冻结编码器、6种采集函数和10个随机种子的设置下，在训练步数相等的四种协议中，SSCAL平均排名第一，肯定性地回答了研究问题。

Conclusion: 伪标签样本可以在CAL的固定训练预算内与有标签样本有效共享小批次，且其占比需受控并配合损失重加权以维持有标签监督的梯度贡献；SSCAL作为一种与采集函数无关、计算开销低的方案，在等训练步数条件下优于对比协议。

Abstract: Active learning (AL) reduces annotation by querying informative examples. Continual active learning (CAL) also cuts the training cost, the total number of gradient steps, by training one model across AL rounds with a fixed number of mini-batch steps per round. Pseudo-labels add supervision without annotation, but they increase training cost. In CAL, where every round trains on a fixed number of mini-batches, whether and how pseudo-labeled examples should share these mini-batches with labeled examples is therefore open. We provide the first formalization of this problem as two decisions after each query, namely (i) which unlabeled examples should enter training with a pseudo-label and (ii) what share of each mini-batch they should take. Our method, semi-supervised continual active learning (SSCAL), works with any acquisition function. It admits pseudo-labels based on distances from unlabeled examples to labeled neighbors, caps their share, and reweights the loss on labeled examples to keep their expected gradient contribution unchanged. On frozen embeddings, its pseudo-labeler updates incrementally without extra model forward passes. Across two audio datasets, two frozen encoders, six acquisition functions and ten seeds, SSCAL ranks first on average among four protocols with equal training steps, answering the question affirmatively.

</details>


### [185] [OneEEG: A Unified Signal-Visual Multimodal Language Model with Task-Aware Mixture of Projectors for Multi-Task EEG Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35355)
*Jiacheng Zhang,Xueyi Zhang,Xin Lu,Siqi Cai,Haizhou Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 OneEEG，一个面向多任务脑电理解（EEG understanding）的统一 EEG-MLLM 框架：在自建多任务指令微调数据集 OneEEG-Instruct 上联合建模数值型与视觉型两类 EEG 表征，并用任务感知的混合投影器（MoP）将异构 EEG 特征对齐到 LLM 嵌入空间；同时建立 OneEEG-Bench 评测基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG 在临床评估、脑机接口与认知状态分析中应用广泛，但现有 EEG 建模因任务范式不同而高度碎片化，缺乏统一的跨任务模型；多模态大语言模型（MLLM）虽提供灵活的多模态推理接口，但对 EEG 输入的理解能力仍然有限。

Method: 1) 构建多任务 EEG 指令微调数据集 OneEEG-Instruct；2) 统一框架同时建模数值型 EEG 表征与视觉型 EEG 表征（visual EEG rendering）；3) 提出任务感知的混合投影器 MoP（Mixture of Projectors），按任务族将异构 EEG 特征对齐至 LLM 嵌入空间；4) 构建 OneEEG-Bench，用于评测现有 MLLM 与本方法的多任务 EEG 理解能力。

Result: 实验表明现有 MLLM 解释 EEG 输入的能力有限；OneEEG 通过融合数值与视觉 EEG 表征并采用任务感知的 EEG-to-LLM 投影，取得更强性能。消融与分析进一步揭示了视觉 EEG 渲染方式、MoP 变体、路由器（router）行为以及不同 EEG 任务间特征离散度的影响。

Conclusion: 统一的 EEG-MLLM 框架可有效缓解 EEG 建模的任务碎片化问题，数值与视觉表征互补、任务感知投影是性能提升的关键；OneEEG-Bench 为该方向提供了标准化评测手段。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) is widely used in clinical assessment, brain-computer interfaces, and cognitive-state analysis, yet EEG modeling remains fragmented across task paradigms. Recent multimodal large language models (MLLMs) provide a promising interface for flexible multimodal reasoning, but their ability to understand EEG inputs remains limited. In this work, we propose OneEEG, a unified EEG-MLLM framework for multi-task EEG understanding. By training on our curated OneEEG-Instruct, a multi-task EEG instruction-tuning dataset, OneEEG jointly models numerical EEG representations and visual EEG representations, and introduces a task-aware Mixture of Projectors (MoP) to align heterogeneous EEG features across different task families with the LLM embedding space. We further establish OneEEG-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating current MLLMs and our method on multi-task EEG understanding. Experiments show that existing MLLMs have limited ability to interpret EEG inputs, while OneEEG achieves stronger performance by integrating numerical and visual EEG representations with task-aware EEG-to-LLM projection. Extensive ablations and analyses further reveal the effects of visual EEG renderings, MoP variants, router behavior, and feature dispersion across EEG tasks.

</details>


### [186] [Benchmarking uncertainty quantification for engineering regression under graded distribution shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35356)
*Ahmad Lawal,Rahim Pasbanigoloojeh,Asmau Lawal Bilbis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建沿物理意义偏移变量设计的三级偏移阶梯（同分布、软偏移、硬偏移），在7个公开工程数据集上评测5个族共12种回归不确定性量化方法，发现硬偏移下所有方法覆盖率大幅下降且方法间无系统性优劣。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程中的机器学习模型常在训练条件之外使用，此时可靠的不确定性量化（UQ）最为关键；而现有回归UQ方法比较多依赖随机划分，只能评估同分布下的校准性，无法反映部署时的真实偏移风险。

Method: 提出三级偏移阶梯（同分布、软偏移、硬偏移），沿具有物理意义的偏移变量构造评测场景；在7个公开工程数据集（5个外推设置、1个未见的涡扇故障模式、1个冬季空气质量传感器部署）上评测来自5个方法族的12种UQ方法；所有调参仅使用源域数据。

Result: 同分布下，共形方法在每个数据集上至少覆盖0.946的测试点；硬偏移下，11种始终输出区间的方法平均覆盖率降至0.40–0.75，且方法排序无系统性差异（Friedman p=0.63）；加权共形化分位数回归在其作答点上接近名义覆盖率，但平均对85%的硬偏移点弃权，且在Concrete数据集上仅覆盖其作答的0.67；源域验证上的增益可预测同分布与软偏移下的提升，但不能预测硬偏移下的提升（Spearman rho=0.04）。

Conclusion: 工程场景的UQ评测应按部署单元划分校准数据、报告子组覆盖率与区间锐度，并将弃权显式视为一种运行结果；随机划分得到的校准结论不足以支撑迁移部署。

Abstract: Machine learning models in engineering are often used beyond their training conditions, where reliable uncertainty quantification (UQ) matters most. Existing comparisons of regression UQ methods rely on random splits, which assess calibration only in distribution. We introduce a three-rung shift ladder (in distribution, soft shift and hard shift) built along physically meaningful shift variables, and evaluate 12 methods from five families on seven public engineering datasets: five extrapolation settings, an unseen turbofan fault mode and a winter air-quality sensor deployment. All tuning uses source data only. In distribution, conformal methods cover at least 0.946 of test points on every dataset. Under hard shift, mean coverage falls to 0.40-0.75 across the 11 methods that always return an interval, and no systematic difference in method ranks is detected (Friedman p = 0.63). Weighted conformalised quantile regression stays near nominal on the points it answers, but abstains on 85\% of hard-shift points on average and covers only 0.67 of the few Concrete points it answers. Source-validation gains predict improvement in distribution and under soft shift, but not under hard shift (Spearman's rho = 0.04). Engineering evaluations should split calibration data by deployment unit, report subgroup coverage and sharpness, and treat abstention as an explicit operational outcome.

</details>


### [187] [TransECG-SSL: Hybrid Self-Supervised Representation Learning for ECG Arrhythmia Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35378)
*Ahtisham Ayyub,Fasih Haider,Muhammad Rehan Usman,Saturnino Luz,Christos Politis,Muhammad Arslan Usman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 TransECG-SSL 自监督框架，用掩码重建+对比学习的混合目标在无标注 ECG 上预训练 Transformer 编码器，冻结后线性探测评估跨数据集心跳表征迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨数据集泛化是 ECG 心律失常自动分类的主要难题，源于人群、采集协议、标注习惯与类别分布差异；希望学习跨独立数据集仍具信息量的表征，减少对数据集专属标注数据的依赖。

Method: Transformer 编码器采用混合自监督目标（掩码信号重建 + 对比学习）预训练；随后冻结编码器，在患者不交叉（patient-disjoint）划分下以线性探测评估；覆盖三个公开 ECG 数据库、七种源-目标设定，并与随机初始化、架构匹配的源域有监督预训练、纯重建 SSL、纯对比 SSL 及目标域专属 SSL 对比。

Result: 混合 SSL 在所有源-目标设定中稳定优于随机初始化 Transformer，平衡准确率也高于架构匹配的源域有监督预训练；纯重建 SSL 与混合 SSL 表现接近，纯对比预训练明显更差，说明掩码重建对迁移性至关重要；但跨数据集迁移的平衡准确率仍低于目标域专属自监督预训练，少数类心律失常在严重类别不平衡下依旧困难。

Conclusion: 结果验证了标签高效的自监督学习在跨数据集 ECG 表征迁移上的潜力，同时揭示域偏移与类别不平衡带来的持续局限。

Abstract: Cross-dataset generalisation remains a major challenge for automated electrocardiogram (ECG) arrhythmia classification because of differences in patient populations, acquisition protocols, annotation practices, and class distributions. Learning representations that remain informative across independent datasets could reduce reliance on dataset-specific labelled data. We propose TransECG-SSL, a self-supervised framework for transferable heartbeat representation learning from unlabelled ECG data. A Transformer encoder is pretrained using a hybrid objective combining masked signal reconstruction and contrastive learning, then frozen and evaluated by linear probing under patient-disjoint evaluation. Experiments across three public ECG databases and seven source--target settings show that Hybrid SSL consistently outperforms randomly initialised Transformers and achieves higher balanced accuracy than architecture-matched source-supervised pretraining. Reconstruction-only SSL remains competitive with Hybrid SSL, whereas contrastive-only pretraining performs substantially worse, highlighting the importance of masked reconstruction for transferability. Cross-dataset transfer nevertheless reduces balanced accuracy relative to target-specific self-supervised pretraining, while minority arrhythmia classes remain challenging under severe class imbalance. These findings demonstrate the potential of label-efficient self-supervised learning for cross-dataset ECG representation transfer while highlighting persistent limitations from domain shift and class imbalance.

</details>


### [188] [Intent-Preserving Adversarial User Simulation for Multi-Turn Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35426)
*Atri Vivek Sharma,Brian Formento,Alessio Lomuscio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 StressSim：一种保持用户意图的对抗式用户模拟器框架，通过遗传式提示优化为每个任务实例搜索高层对抗计划，用于对多轮智能体进行压力测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 的用户模拟器过于配合，会高估智能体性能，掩盖真实鲁棒性缺陷。

Method: 在任务实例级别优化模拟器的高层对抗计划；用遗传提示优化循环进化候选规划提示，以 rollout 轨迹、细粒度任务完成信号和有效性反馈为筛选依据，偏好在不改变用户底层目标的前提下降低任务成功率的对话。在 MultiWOZ 以及 tau-bench 的 retail、airline 域上，以三个目标 LLM 智能体为对象，与标准模拟器和手工设计的非协作用户行为对比；每任务优化预算 20 次 rollout，仅做提示优化、不微调模型。

Result: tau-bench 上 pass^4 最高下降 69%（gemini-3.8-flash）与 62%（gpt-5.6-sol）；目标保持率下降最多 10%；敏感性分析显示增大预算可进一步降低任务成功率。

Conclusion: StressSim 在保持对话真实性与用户目标的前提下，揭示了标准及手工设计模拟器遗漏的鲁棒性缺口，且仅靠轻量提示优化即可实现。

Abstract: Multi-turn agents are commonly evaluated with LLM-based simulated users, which can be overly cooperative and can therefore overestimate an agent's performance. We here introduce StressSim, an intent-preserving adversarial user simulator framework for stress-testing agents with challenging yet valid user interactions. StressSim optimises the simulator's high-level adversarial plan for each task instance. Candidate planner prompts are evolved with a genetic prompt-optimisation loop that uses rollout traces, granular task-completion signals, and validity feedback to favour conversations that reduce task success without changing the user's underlying goal. We evaluate the approach on the MultiWOZ dataset and the retail and airline domains from tau-bench with three target LLM agents, comparing against standard simulators and hand-designed non-collaborative user behaviours. We demonstrate the effectiveness of StressSim in exposing robustness gaps across frontier LLMs, with tau-bench pass^4 scores decreasing by up to 69% on gemini-3.8-flash and 62% on gpt-5.6-sol. StressSim maintains goal preservation performance (which decreases by at most 10%), using an optimisation budget of 20 rollouts per task. Sensitivity analysis suggests that increasing the budget further decreases task success. Overall our results demonstrate that StressSim exposes robustness gaps missed by standard and hand-designed user simulators while maintaining conversation realism. This is achieved through lightweight prompt optimisation alone, without model fine-tuning.

</details>


### [189] [Prior Predictive Matching for Bayesian LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35457)
*Tomasz Kuśmierczyk,Eliezer de Souza da Silva,Marcin Sendera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种在微调前即可闭式计算的先验预测估计器，用于为贝叶斯LoRA选择先验尺度：仅依赖秩与先验尺度四次方的乘积，并由残差协方差做矩匹配，理论上最优先验精度随秩按r^{-1/2}缩放，故单次估计可跨秩泛化；在RoBERTa-large上无需验证集即改善NLL与校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯LoRA能提供有原则的不确定性估计，但需要人为指定低秩权重更新上的先验（精度/尺度），该超参数选择缺乏依据且通常依赖验证数据或边际似然。

Method: 对网络做二阶展开，刻画低秩先验如何驱动函数空间相对预训练输出的偏离；证明先验预测量仅通过"秩×先验尺度四次方"的乘积依赖先验；利用同一潜在权重更新抽样所导致的样本残差间协方差，构造矩匹配估计器，在微调前闭式估计先验尺度；并由理论分析给出最优先验精度随秩r按r^{-1/2}缩放。

Result: 回归与分类的受控实验验证了二阶近似的有效性，估计器能恢复真实先验尺度；在RoBERTa-large上，相较MAP选择与边际似然选择，在不使用验证数据的情况下改善预测NLL与校准，同时OOD检测性能具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 先验尺度可由微调前的先验预测矩闭式估计，且因秩-尺度缩放律而具备跨秩可迁移性，为贝叶斯LoRA提供了无需验证数据的实用超参数选择方案。

Abstract: Bayesian variants of Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) provide principled uncertainty estimation when fine-tuning large foundation models, but require specifying the prior over the low-rank weight updates. We address this hyperparameter selection problem by introducing a closed-form prior-predictive estimator that operates before fine-tuning.
  We analyze a second-order expansion of the network and quantify how the low-rank prior drives function-space deviations from the pretrained outputs. We also show that these prior-predictive quantities depend on the prior only through the product of the adaptation rank and the fourth power of the prior scale. Because the training targets share a single draw of the latent weight update, we can leverage the covariance between example residuals to derive a moment-matching estimator. Furthermore, our theoretical analysis establishes that the optimal prior precision scales as $r^{-1/2}$ with the rank $r$, allowing a single estimate to generalize across all ranks.
  Controlled experiments across regression and classification tasks validate our approximation and show that our estimator recovers the true prior scale. On RoBERTa-large, our approach improves predictive negative log-likelihood (NLL) and calibration over both MAP and marginal-likelihood selection without requiring validation data, while achieving competitive out-of-distribution detection.

</details>


### [190] [Multi-Horizon Future Discovery for Physics-Informed Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35468)
*Shuwei Wang,Qiang Guan,Brian Qiu,Haoxiang Huang,Haoxi Wang,Chenyu Li,Bo Xu,Sen Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HTC²框架，将多时间尺度对比表示学习与哈密顿相空间结构结合，用正样本对表示距离作为内在奖励、以有界多步哈密顿变差调制，集成SAC与PPO，在六个DeepMind Control任务上验证探索效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理控制环境中的有效探索需要智能体找到那些后果能揭示动力学未知方面的动作。但现有多数内在动机方法依赖即时预测误差、状态新颖性或局部物理变化，容易忽略在较长时序跨度上才显现的信息性结果。作者因此探究：学习可达未来的结构化表示能否为探索提供更有效的基础。

Method: 提出Hamiltonian Temporal Contrastive Curiosity（HTC²）：(1) 多时间尺度时序表示学习——以时间跨度（horizon）为条件的对比评论家，从同一回合内采样的未来状态对学习时序关系；(2) 以正样本对表示之间的距离作为内在奖励，鼓励探索在学习表示中尚未被充分组织的未来；(3) 引入物理动力学先验——用有界多步哈密顿变差对该奖励进行调制，而非直接奖励哈密顿量本身的变化，以避免退化。方法可与SAC和PPO结合。

Result: 在六个DeepMind Control Suite任务上进行评估，与ICM、RIDE、E3B、Plan2Explore及单步哈密顿好奇心基线对比；并通过状态覆盖率评估、组件消融与相空间诊断，刻画时序学习与物理结构各自对探索行为的贡献。

Conclusion: 结构化建模可达未来（多跨度时序对比表示）结合哈密顿相空间先验，可为物理控制任务提供更有效的内在探索信号；消融与诊断结果用于区分时序学习与物理结构两部分的独立作用。

Abstract: Exploration in physical control environments requires agents to identify actions whose consequences reveal previously unexplored aspects of the underlying dynamics. However, many intrinsic motivation methods rely on immediate prediction errors, state novelty, or local physical changes, which may overlook informative outcomes that emerge across extended temporal horizons. We investigate whether learning structured representations of reachable futures can provide a more effective basis for exploration. To this end, we introduce Hamiltonian Temporal Contrastive Curiosity (HTC$^2$), a framework that combines multi-horizon temporal representation learning with Hamiltonian phase-space structure. A horizon-conditioned contrastive critic learns temporal relationships from future-state pairs sampled within the same episode. The distance between positive-pair representations is then used as an intrinsic reward, encouraging the agent to explore futures that remain insufficiently organized in the learned representation. To incorporate information about physical dynamics, we additionally modulate this reward using bounded multi-step Hamiltonian variation, rather than directly rewarding changes in the Hamiltonian itself. We integrate HTC$^2$ with both Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) and Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), and evaluate it across six DeepMind Control Suite tasks. Comparisons with ICM, RIDE, E3B, Plan2Explore, and a single-step Hamiltonian curiosity baseline are complemented by state-coverage evaluations, component ablations, and phase-space diagnostics to characterize the contributions of temporal learning and physical structure to exploratory behavior.

</details>


### [191] [Amplify, Locate, Estimate (ALE) on Rare-Failure Rates of Fine-Tuned LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35472)
*Patrick Reichherzer,Jonathan Evertz,Thorsten Holz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 ALE 方法，利用安全微调引起的 token 对数概率变化构造放大方向，将稀有失败概率抬高以便低成本测量，再通过得分函数估计与重要性采样外推回真实稀有概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全微调使不良行为足够罕见，直接采样测频的代价随失败概率降低而急剧上升（固定相对精度下采样预算与失败概率成反比），亟需高效的稀有失败概率估计方法。

Method: ALE（Amplify, Locate, Estimate）：(1) 以安全微调前后的 token 对数概率变化定义"放大方向"，沿该方向提高失败率以实现廉价测量；(2) 基于所选放大批次，用失败样本的 token 概率恢复对数失败率的局部斜率，构建得分函数估计量并外推至稀有目标率；(3) 同时以放大批次给出重要性采样估计。

Result: 在 41 个评估目标上，估计值与参考值之比的中位数接近 1，其中 37 个 ALE 估计落在独立直接测量参考值的三倍范围内。消融实验表明，在约 1000 条带标签 rollout 加一次廉价无标签扫描的预算下，ALE 可估计 Qwen3-8B 微调模型的失败概率低至约 10^-5，而直接测量需数十万条 rollout。

Conclusion: 通过放大—定位—外推的框架，ALE 在远低于直接采样的预算下实现了对极稀有安全失败概率的可靠估计，为安全微调的稀有失效评估提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Safety fine-tuning can make undesirable model behaviors sufficiently rare that measuring their frequency by direct sampling becomes prohibitively expensive. We introduce Amplify, Locate, Estimate (ALE), a method for estimating such rare failure probabilities efficiently. ALE uses the change in token log-probabilities induced by the safety fine-tuning to define an amplification direction, along which we increase the failure rate for cheap measurement. We develop a score-function estimator that uses the selected amplified batch to recover the local slope of the log failure rate from the token probabilities of the sampled failures, enabling extrapolation back to rare target rates. We also use the amplified batch to obtain an importance-sampling estimate. Across 41 evaluation targets, ALE's estimate-to-reference ratios have a median close to one, with 37 ALE estimates within a factor of three of reference values obtained from independent direct measurements. Ablation tests show that with a budget of up to $\sim$1000 labeled rollouts, in addition to a cheap unlabeled scan, ALE estimates failure probabilities for Qwen3-8B fine-tunes down to about $10^{-5}$, where direct measurement can require hundreds of thousands of rollouts, as the required sampling budget scales inversely with failure probability at fixed relative precision.

</details>


### [192] [GPDPO: Learning from Reward Conflicts via Group Pareto-Dominance Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35480)
*Zixuan Xu,Haoxuan Chen,Jiangxin Sun,Jian-Fang Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GPDPO从被冲突过滤的rollout中挖掘Pareto支配关系，转化为辅助偏好信号与原始策略目标联合优化，实现多奖励对齐且无需额外采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多奖励RL对齐LLM时，冲突奖励信号导致更新方向模糊、优化不稳定；现有方法（奖励重加权、梯度协调、rollout过滤）仅在单响应或组级信号上操作，未利用采样响应间的关联信息，导致被冲突过滤的rollout中仍有可用监督被丢弃。

Method: 提出Group Pareto-Dominance Policy Optimization (GPDPO)：在组内采样响应中识别Pareto支配关系，将其转化为成对偏好辅助信号，并与原策略目标联合优化，从而复用被过滤的rollout，无需额外采样或奖励评估。

Result: 在API-Bank上跨多种模型规模与奖励配置，GPDPO持续优于竞争性多奖励优化基线；分析显示其同时降低了组相对与成对奖励冲突。

Conclusion: 利用响应间关系型监督可有效提升多奖励对齐的稳定性与效果，代码将开源。

Abstract: Multi-reward reinforcement learning offers a promising framework for aligning large language models (LLMs) with diverse objectives, but conflicting reward signals can induce ambiguous update directions and hinder stable optimization. Existing approaches address such conflicts through reward reweighting, gradient coordination, or rollout filtering, yet they primarily operate on per-response or group-level optimization signals and underexploit relational information among sampled responses. As a result, conflict-filtered rollouts may still contain useful supervision that is discarded during training. We propose Group Pareto-Dominance Policy Optimization (GPDPO), a multi-reward policy optimization method that recovers pairwise supervision from conflict-filtered rollouts. GPDPO identifies Pareto-dominance relations among sampled responses and converts them into auxiliary preference signals, which are jointly optimized with the original policy objective. This enables more effective reuse of existing rollouts without additional sampling or reward evaluation. Experiments on API-Bank across multiple model scales and reward configurations show that GPDPO consistently outperforms competitive multi-reward optimization baselines. Further analysis demonstrates that GPDPO reduces both group-relative and pairwise reward conflicts, supporting the effectiveness of exploiting relational supervision for multi-reward alignment. Code will be released.

</details>


### [193] [Evaluating Bayesian deep learning for robust biomedical time-series classification under distribution shift: A sleep scoring case study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35484)
*Mads Birch Sørensen,Ulrike Richter,Francois Gastambide,Michael Riis Andersen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 以啮齿动物睡眠分期为案例，系统评估9种贝叶斯近似推断方法在两类网络骨干上的分布漂移鲁棒性，发现多盆地（multi-basin）方法最优，BMA相对点估计提升OOD精度与校准，但ID增益有限，且未能改善评分者间分歧检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学时间序列分类中，分布漂移（跨基因型、实验室、物种）常导致模型精度与校准性下降；作者希望量化贝叶斯模型平均（BMA）在多大程度上能提升鲁棒性。

Method: 以啮齿动物睡眠分期为案例研究，在ID数据与5个OOD数据集上，比较9种近似推断方法（涵盖单盆地与多盆地两类方法的代表性近似），并在两种神经网络骨干上系统评估，对照点估计基线。

Result: 多盆地方法表现最佳，优于基线及单盆地方法；BMA相对点估计在两种架构上普遍提升OOD精度与校准，而ID增益较小；BMA常改善选择性预测以及OOD检测与伪迹检测，但未改善评分者间分歧检测。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯启发式深度学习（尤其是多盆地近似推断的BMA）是缓解生物医学时间序列分布漂移的有效手段，但其收益具有任务依赖性——并非所有不确定性相关任务（如评分者间分歧检测）都能从中受益。

Abstract: In biomedical time-series classification, machine learning models often suffer from deteriorating accuracy and calibration due to distribution shift. We investigate to what degree Bayesian-inspired deep learning improves robustness through Bayesian model averaging (BMA). Using rodent sleep scoring as a case study, we evaluate BMA under distribution shifts in genotype, laboratory, and species. We systematically compare nine approximate inference methods spanning single- and multi-basin approaches across two neural network backbones on in-distribution (ID) data and five out-of-distribution (OOD) datasets. In our results, multi-basin methods perform best, outperforming baselines and single-basin methods. Compared with point estimates, BMA generally improves OOD accuracy and calibration across both architectures, while yielding only modest ID gains. It also often improves selective prediction and OOD and artifact detection, but, interestingly, not inter-rater disagreement detection.

</details>


### [194] [When Fine-Grained Credit Assignment Helps and Hurts: Multimodal Agentic Reinforcement Learning through Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35497)
*Lei Zhao,Zongheng Cao,Xinyu Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文以电影预告片剪辑为测试平台，构建四种不同复杂度的多模态工具使用任务，系统考察细粒度过程奖励（过程奖励）在何时有效、何时失效，并提出衰减式过程奖励策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习已被用于训练多模态智能体进行推理与工具调用，但"细粒度信用分配（credit assignment）何时能提升学习、何时无效"这一问题尚不清楚。视频剪辑恰好同时涉及多模态感知、长程规划、工具使用与延迟结果反馈，是研究该问题的理想试验台。

Method: 构造四类电影预告片剪辑任务——Content Selection、Narrative Sequencing、Rough Cut、Fine Cut，对应真实剪辑流程并覆盖不同任务复杂度与交互步数；以 outcome-only GRPO 为基线，对比（1）衰减式过程奖励，（2）由 LLM judge 提供过程奖励的 GRPO+PRM（固定过程奖励权重），并考察 on-policy self-distillation（OPSD）的密集 token 级监督效果。

Result: 衰减式过程奖励在 Fine Cut 上收益最大：该任务在基线下的平均回合数超过 60，相对提升 45.4%。在三个回合数约 30–40 的短程任务上，outcome-only GRPO 仍具竞争力，衰减式过程奖励相对增益最多 10.4%。固定权重的 GRPO+PRM 早期能改善工具使用并鼓励视觉检查，但随训练推进出现过度推理与冗余工具调用，过程奖励持续上升而任务完成率与结果分数下降（奖励黑客）。将过程奖励权重衰减至零可缓解该行为并保留早期收益。OPSD 在密集 token 级监督下仅带来微小提升或显著退化，且对特权信息（privileged information）选择高度敏感。

Conclusion: 细粒度过程奖励的价值取决于任务时程与训练阶段：长程任务获益显著，短程任务边际有限；固定权重的 PRM 会诱发奖励黑客，衰减权重是有效折中方案。分析揭示师生分歧度与梯度量级的差异，并推测长轨迹中信息性信号可能被稀释。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has shown promise for training multimodal agents to reason and use tools. However, it remains unclear when fine-grained credit assignment improves learning and when it fails to improve task performance. Video editing provides a useful testbed for this question, combining multimodal perception, long-horizon planning, and tool use with delayed outcome feedback. We construct four movie-trailer editing task types---Content Selection, Narrative Sequencing, Rough Cut, and Fine Cut---that reflect real-world editing workflows and span different levels of task complexity and interaction horizon. Decaying process rewards provides the largest benefit on Fine Cut, with a 45.4% relative gain over outcome-only GRPO on tasks averaging more than 60 turns under outcome-only GRPO. On the three shorter-horizon task types averaging 30--40 turns under the same baseline, outcome-only GRPO remains competitive, with decaying process rewards yielding at most a 10.4% relative gain. Process rewards from an LLM judge initially improve tool use and encourage visual inspection. As training progresses, however, GRPO+PRM with a fixed process-reward weight increasingly exhibits excessive reasoning and redundant tool calls: process rewards continue to rise while task completion rates and outcome scores decline. Decaying the process-reward weight to zero mitigates this reward-hacking behavior while preserving the early gains. Despite dense token-level supervision, on-policy self-distillation (OPSD) yields little improvement or substantial degradation and is highly sensitive to the choice of privileged information. Our analysis reveals differences in teacher--student disagreement and gradient magnitudes, and suggests possible dilution of informative signals over long trajectories.

</details>


### [195] [Model reduction in time: Learning flow maps for reducing stochastic dynamical systems in time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35504)
*Shreya Jha,Benjamin Peherstorfer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FM-FTM：学习流映射替代多步时间积分的非侵入式降阶方法，加速随机动力系统集合预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力系统预测（即使使用降阶模型）因需要大量顺序时间积分步而计算昂贵；随机系统的集合预测尤为显著。

Method: 先用一阶轨迹匹配（FTM）学习随机概率流动力学的确定性表示，再将其随时间演化压缩为流映射，一次评估即可直接给出长时程预测；整体为非侵入式降阶框架。

Result: 在含随机强迫的演化问题数值实验中，集合预测加速最高达两个数量级，同时准确预测时间边缘分布与集合统计量。

Conclusion: FM-FTM可有效替代多步积分，在大幅降低计算成本的同时保持随机动力学统计精度。

Abstract: Predictions with dynamical systems can be computationally expensive---even with reduced models---because reaching future prediction times can require many sequential time integration steps. We propose Flow-Map First-Order Trajectory Matching (FM-FTM), a nonintrusive model reduction approach that accelerates ensemble predictions of stochastic dynamical systems by learning flow maps that replace multiple time-integration steps. Our method uses first-order trajectory matching (FTM) to learn a deterministic representation of the stochastic probability-current dynamics and then reduces its evolution over time into a flow map whose evaluation directly yields predictions over long time horizons. Numerical experiments on evolution problems with stochastic forcing demonstrate that FM-FTM accelerates ensemble predictions by up to two orders of magnitude while accurately predicting time marginals and ensemble statistics.

</details>


### [196] [S3AC: Stabilized, Selective, and Scalable Actor-Critic for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35518)
*Shuo Shen,Guangran Cheng,Chengqi Lyu,Xiangsen Wang,Yanan Sun,Wenwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 S3AC：一套稳定的长 CoT critic 训练配方，结合 token 级优势与 prompt 级基线，并以 DAPS 难度感知采样提升 RLVR 训练效率，在 1.5B–397B 模型上优于 GRPO-X。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 critic-free 的 RLVR 方法存在两点不足：缺乏 token 级信用分配；依赖成组 rollout 估计 prompt 特定基线。引入 critic 可同时解决这两个问题，但长 CoT 场景下 critic 训练困难——轨迹长度多样性覆盖不足，且新初始化的 value head 导致训练早期梯度被其主导。

Method: 提出 S3AC（Stabilized, Selective, and Scalable Actor-Critic）：以实用的 critic 训练配方缓解上述问题，训练出的 critic 同时提供 token 级优势与 prompt 级基线。基于 prompt 级基线估计难度，提出 Difficulty-Aware Prompt Sampling（DAPS），在生成前结合难度估计与结果历史选择 prompt，减少 rollout 浪费且无需离线难度初始化。

Result: 在 1.5B 至 397B 的 dense 与 MoE 模型上，S3AC 相较 GRPO-X 在分组 rollout 设置下均取得更优表现；在 35B 规模下可维持单条 rollout 训练，而所测 critic-free 基线在初期收益后出现退化。

Conclusion: 长 CoT 场景下的 critic 训练是可实现且有效的：通过稳定化 critic 训练获得 token 级信用分配与 prompt 级基线，并进一步用于难度感知采样，可在不同规模模型上稳定提升 RLVR 表现并支撑单 rollout 训练。

Abstract: Critic-free methods are widely used in reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) for long chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning, avoiding difficult and costly critic training. Common alternatives lack token-level credit assignment and typically rely on grouped rollouts to estimate prompt-specific baselines. A critic can address both limitations, but long-CoT critic training is hindered by insufficient coverage of trajectories with diverse lengths and early gradients dominated by the newly initialized value head. We introduce S3AC (Stabilized, Selective, and Scalable Actor-Critic) for LLM reasoning, built around a practical critic-training recipe. The trained critic provides token-specific advantages and prompt-specific baselines. Using the prompt-level baseline to estimate difficulty, we propose Difficulty-Aware Prompt Sampling (DAPS). DAPS combines this estimate with outcome history to select prompts before generation, reducing rollout waste without offline difficulty initialization. S3AC improves group-rollout performance across dense and mixture-of-experts models from 1.5B to 397B parameters, outperforming a modern GRPO variant (GRPO-X). At 35B, it sustains single-rollout training while tested critic-free baselines deteriorate after initial gains.

</details>


### [197] [Beyond Modeling Order: Dual-Path Spatiotemporal Representation Learning for Cross-Subject EEG Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35540)
*Zehui Xiao,Yi Zheng,Guixing Fu,Wenkai Lu,Lingfei He,Tao Tan,Yue Liu,Silun Wang,Junhai Xu,Mengting Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出双路径时空对比学习框架DP-STCL，通过TS与ST两条不同建模顺序的独立编码器及三重对比关系约束，缓解表征偏置，实现跨被试EEG情绪识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG情绪识别中跨被试泛化困难，源于神经活动与情绪相关时空模式的显著个体差异。现有方法多依赖分布对齐或对比学习，却较少关注固定时空建模顺序所带来的表征偏置。

Method: 核心假设：同一EEG信号的高层情绪表征应在不同时空建模路径下保持一致。据此设计两个参数独立的互补编码器（时间到空间TS路径、空间到时间ST路径），在不同整合顺序下建模时空信息；以相同情绪刺激建立跨被试关联，并用三种互补关系联合约束跨被试、跨建模路径的表征差异；另引入低参数开销的LoRA少样本自适应策略应对目标被试标注稀缺。

Result: 在SEED、SEED-IV、SEED-V与FACED四个数据集上的大量跨被试实验表明，DP-STCL稳定优于对比方法。

Conclusion: 跨路径表征一致性假设有效，可抑制顺序相关偏置并促进稳定、判别性强的刺激相关情绪表征；LoRA少样本适配进一步提升实用性，验证了该框架在跨被试EEG情绪识别中的有效性。

Abstract: Cross-subject generalization remains a major challenge for EEG-based emotion recognition due to pronounced inter-subject variability in neural activity and emotion-related spatiotemporal patterns. Existing methods mainly alleviate inter subject differences through distribution alignment or contrastive learning, while paying limited attention to representation bias associated with fixed spatiotemporal modeling orders. We hypothesize that high-level emotion representations of the same EEG signal should remain consistent across different spatiotemporal modeling paths. Based on this hypothesis, we propose a Dual-Path Spatiotemporal Contrastive Learning (DP-STCL) framework for cross-subject EEG emotion recognition. DP-STCL employs two complementary encoders with independent parameters, namely a Temporal-to-Spatial (TS) path and a Spatial to-Temporal (ST) path, to model EEG spatiotemporal information under different integration orders. During representation learning, the same emotional stimulus is used to establish cross subject associations, while three complementary relations jointly constrain representation variations across subjects and modeling paths. This promotes stable and discriminative stimulus-related emotion representations. To further handle limited labeled target subject data, we introduce a LoRA-based few-shot adaptation strategy with low parameter overhead. Extensive cross-subject experiments on SEED, SEED-IV, SEED-V, and FACED show that DP-STCL consistently outperforms the compared methods, demonstrating its effectiveness in improving cross-subject EEG emotion recognition.

</details>


### [198] [Efficient probabilistic weather forecasting at quarter-degree resolution with two-sided transport attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35545)
*Junu Choi,Youngjoon Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Astra 是一个 0.25° 集合数值天气预报模型，在 8 块 H200 GPU 上训练，通过 Kantorovich Attention 与自特征化 CRPS 损失，以 GenCast 约 9.8% 的硬件归一化训练算力达到其同等 CRPS 水平，并在多数指标上优于 ECMWF 集合预报与 GenCast。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率概率天气预报计算代价高昂，现有集合扩散/概率模型（如 GenCast）训练与推理算力需求大，难以在有限资源下实现高分辨率、高性能的概率预报。

Method: 提出 Astra：0.25° 集合模型，训练仅用 8 块 H200 GPU。核心为两点：(1) Kantorovich Attention——双边传输注意力，每个区域自行选择读取来源与写入目标，使各区域以 token 分辨率收发信息；(2) 自特征化 CRPS 损失，将评分扩展到模型自身的多尺度特征上。

Result: 在 WeatherBench 2 上，Astra 在 5 个高空目标、第 1–10 天的平均 CRPS 与 GenCast 持平，而硬件归一化训练算力仅为后者的 9.8%。以 ERA5 为真值，其在全部 600 个变量×层次×预报时效组合上 CRPS 与集合平均 RMSE 均低于 ECMWF 集合预报，且在 73.5% 的组合上 CRPS 低于 GenCast。单个 15 天成员所需浮点运算量比 GenCast 的 GPU 实现少 39 倍；50 个成员在单张消费级 GPU 上 40 分钟内完成，显存占用低于 21 GB。

Conclusion: Astra 以显著更低的训练与推理算力实现了与 GenCast 相当乃至更优的概率预报精度，并在与业务级 ECMWF 集合预报的全面对比中占优，表明高效注意力设计与特征级概率评分可有效降低高分辨率概率天气预报的计算门槛。

Abstract: High-resolution probabilistic weather forecasting remains computationally demanding. Here we present Astra, a 0.25$^\circ$ ensemble model trained on eight H200 GPUs. It combines Kantorovich Attention, a two-sided transport attention in which each region chooses where it reads from and writes to, so every region sends and receives at token resolution, with a self-featurised continuous ranked probability score (CRPS) that also scores forecasts in the model's multiscale features. In WeatherBench 2, Astra matches GenCast's CRPS averaged over five upper-air targets and days 1-10 with 9.8% of its hardware-normalised training compute. Verified against ERA5, its CRPS and ensemble-mean root-mean-square error are lower than the ECMWF ensemble's on all 600 variable, level and lead-time combinations, and its CRPS is lower than GenCast's on 73.5% of them. A 15-day member needs 39 times fewer floating-point operations than GenCast's GPU implementation, and 50 members take under 40 min on one consumer GPU, within 21 GB.

</details>


### [199] [Balanced Sampling in Stochastic Multi-objective Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35551)
*Mengfan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究随机多目标赌博机中Pareto最优臂之间的公平采样问题，用KL散度度量不公平性，提出自适应top-two采样算法，在Pareto集稳定时可达到O(log T/T)的期望不公平性最优速率及O(T^{-1})方差界，并给出下界证明稳定性是必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线广告等实际场景需要在多个Pareto最优臂所代表的不同权衡间均衡采样，但如何度量公平性缺乏共识；同时Pareto最优集未知，早期采样决策造成的失衡难以消除，导致公平性难以实现。

Method: 以真实Pareto集上均匀分布与采样轨迹经验分布之间的KL散度度量不公平性，该度量可精确分解为次优臂采样代价与Pareto最优臂间失衡两部分；基于不公平性的递推形式提出自适应top-two采样算法，结合采样计数与Pareto成员资格的经验证据为每个臂定义增益以指导采样，无需预先锁定估计的Pareto集；并提出鼓励探索欠采样臂的变体。

Result: 当Pareto集在小扰动下稳定时，期望不公平性与方差分别达到O(log T/T)与O(T^{-1})界；信息论下界表明log T/T速率对稳定实例上的均匀公平策略不可避免，与上界匹配；若无稳定性，期望不公平性可达ω(log T/T)，说明稳定性是关键条件。探索型变体在足够长的时间域上保持期望不公平性速率，同时将方差收紧至O(log² T/T²)。数值实验支持上述结论。

Conclusion: KL散度是度量多目标赌博机公平性的合适指标且具可分解性；自适应top-two采样在稳定Pareto集下达到最优不公平性速率，稳定性是不可回避的必要条件；通过鼓励探索欠采样臂可在长期内进一步降低方差并避免持续性分配不足。

Abstract: In stochastic multi-objective bandits, Pareto optimal arms may represent different trade-offs among objectives. Balanced sampling across them is important in practical settings such as online advertising. Yet, there seems to be little consensus on how to measure fairness, to our best knowledge. Achieving fairness is also tough as the set of Pareto optimal arms is unknown, and early sampling decisions may create imbalances that are difficult to erase afterwards. We measure unfairness by the KL divergence from the uniform distribution over the true Pareto set to the empirical measure induced by the sampling trajectory. Nicely, this measure admits an exact decomposition that separates the cost of sampling suboptimal arms from the imbalance among Pareto optimal arms. With a recursive form for unfairness, we develop \emph{adaptive top-two sampling}, which combines sampling counts and empirical evidence of Pareto membership to define a gain for each arm and guide sampling without committing to an estimated Pareto set. When the Pareto set is stable under small perturbations, we establish an $O(\log T/T)$ and $O(T^{-1})$ bound on expected unfairness and variance, respectively. Information-theoretic lower bounds show that the $\log T/T$ rate can be unavoidable for uniformly fair policies on stable instances, matching our upper bound. More surprisingly, stability is indeed crucial, as the expected unfairness can be $ω(\log T/T)$ without it. Finally, we create a variant that encourages exploration of undersampled arms to reduce the risk of persistent underallocation. At sufficiently large horizons, it preserves the rate on the expected unfairness while admitting a sharper bound of $O(\log^2 T/T^2)$ on variance. Numerical studies support these findings and suggest practical insights.

</details>


### [200] [Multi-Fidelity Training Reshapes Uncertainty Decomposition in Atomistic Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35560)
*Matteo Tolloso,Davide Bacciu,Luigi Bonati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文用MACE等变模型的深度集成，在ANI-1x上比较"从头高保真训练"与"低保真预训练+高保真微调"两种多保真策略，系统考察其预测不确定性的排序能力与校准性。结论是：预训练降低误差、提升排序，但不改善校准，且能量外推下校准恶化，退化主要发生在高保真优化阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多保真训练（大量低保真数据+少量高保真标签）在原子尺度建模中日益普及，但其对预测不确定性（尤其是校准性）的影响尚缺乏系统研究；而实际应用（如主动学习采集）依赖可靠的不确定性。

Method: 采用均值-方差MACE（mean-variance MACE）模型构建深度集成，在ANI-1x上对比两种训练范式：高保真从头训练 vs. 低保真预训练后高保真微调。全程追踪偶然不确定性（aleatoric）、认知不确定性（epistemic）与总不确定性；设置两类分布偏移测试：未见分子体系与更高能量构型。另进行训练动力学分析，定位校准退化来源，并评估基于不确定性的采集函数效果。

Result: 预训练在所有测试集上降低预测误差并改善基于不确定性的排序，但并未带来良好校准的预测方差；在能量外推条件下校准退化更严重。训练动力学表明校准退化主要发生于高保真优化阶段，此阶段学习到的偶然方差在留出数据上变得过度自信。此外，多保真微调得到的基于不确定性的采集函数优于从头高保真训练。

Conclusion: 不确定性排序与校准反映的是不同性质，必须在多保真原子模型中被分开评估，尤其是在外推情形下；多保真预训练有利于精度与主动学习采集，但不能替代针对校准的专门处理。

Abstract: Multi-fidelity training, which combines abundant low-fidelity data with scarce high-fidelity labels, is increasingly common in atomistic modeling, but its effect on predictive uncertainty remains largely unexplored. We investigate this question using deep ensembles of mean-variance MACE models trained on ANI-1x, contrasting high-fidelity training from scratch with low-fidelity pretraining followed by fine-tuning. We characterize aleatoric, epistemic, and total uncertainty throughout training and under two distribution shifts: unseen molecular systems and higher-energy configurations. We find that pretraining reduces prediction error and improves uncertainty-based ranking across all test sets, but does not yield well-calibrated predictive variances; calibration deteriorates more severely under energy extrapolation. Training-dynamics analysis localizes most of this calibration degradation to high-fidelity optimization, during which the learned aleatoric variance becomes overconfident on held-out data. Moreover, multi-fidelity fine-tuning produces a more effective uncertainty-based acquisition function than high-fidelity training from scratch. These findings show that uncertainty ranking and calibration capture distinct properties and must be assessed separately in multi-fidelity atomistic models, especially under extrapolation.

</details>


### [201] [High-resolution tactile observation makes hidden racket-control mechanisms learnable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35563)
*Haoyang Tian,Zhengwei Li,Houcheng Li,Xu Yang,Peigeng Gu,Xinyue Wang,Tairan Zhu,Shuo Jiang,Long Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文用864节点触觉-惯性手套同步100 Hz采集70余名、五个技能等级参与者的逾14000次羽毛球击球数据，提出受感觉运动控制启发的力学双层框架，从触觉历史推断“驱动物体”与“稳定握持”两类控制并解析其时空力实现，发现高技能对应“选择性”而非“广泛”的控制，并将机制转化为个性化指导，随机队列中机制指导组提升0.72个专家评定技能等级（仅等级反馈组0.05）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家虽掌握精细的手-物操作技能，但对快速触觉交互的有意识感知有限，难以言明其控制机制；羽毛球拍控制需要快速精细的手部力协调，可作为揭示此类机制的严苛测试平台。

Method: 构建864节点触觉-惯性手套（同步100 Hz采集），覆盖70余名参与者、五种技能水平的逾14000次击球；提出受感觉运动控制启发的、基于力学的双层框架，从触觉历史中推断“物体驱动控制”与“握持稳定控制”，并解析二者的时空力实现；据此设计针对力控制与力-运动协调的个性化指导，并在随机队列中验证。

Result: 技能水平越高，控制越呈“选择性”而非“广泛性”：技能特异性更强、具备预期性调节、远端局部化调控、几何力矩抵消更低；时空协调呈非单调的“习得—精炼”模式。随机队列中，机制指导学习者提升0.72个专家评定技能等级，仅等级反馈组仅0.05。

Conclusion: 基于触觉的力学双层框架可将难以言说的专家手部力控制机制显式化、可计算化，并能转化为有效的个性化训练反馈，使控制机制真正“可操作”。

Abstract: Experts can master fine hand-object manipulation skills, yet limited conscious perception of rapid tactile interactions hinders their ability to articulate the underlying control mechanisms. Badminton racket control requires rapid, fine hand-force coordination, providing a demanding testbed for uncovering these mechanisms. An 864-node tactile-inertial glove with synchronized 100-Hz acquisition recorded over 14,000 strokes from more than 70 participants across five skills. Inspired by sensorimotor control, our mechanics-grounded two-layer framework inferred object-driving and grip-stabilizing control from tactile history, while resolving their spatiotemporal force realization. We found that higher proficiency was associated with selective rather than extensive control: greater skill specificity, anticipatory regulation, focal distal modulation and lower geometric torque cancellation, while spatiotemporal coordination showed a non-monotonic acquire-refine pattern. We translated these mechanisms into personalized guidance targeting force control and force-motion coordination. In a randomized cohort, mechanism-guided learners gained 0.72 expert-rated skill levels versus 0.05 with level-only feedback, making control mechanisms actionable.

</details>


### [202] [ExCal: Optimization-Free Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection via Excess Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35568)
*Kadir-Kaan Özer,René Ebeling,Markus Enzweiler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ExCal，一种仅用正常样本训练的多变量时间序列异常检测器，融合原始状态检索、信号/导数子空间与跨时间尺度的稳健统计；在 TSB-AD-M 的 180 条序列上 AUC-PR 达 0.421±0.37、VUS-PR 达 0.452±0.37，在 24 种方法中排名第二。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多变量时序异常检测方法多依赖梯度优化或异常标签，且需要合适的在线推断机制。作者希望在无梯度优化、无异常标签、仅用正常数据的前提下，构建一个性能可与非预训练强基线相当的检测器，并借助多视角（原始状态、信号与导数、跨尺度稳健统计）提升检测稳健性。

Method: ExCal 由三类"专家映射"构成：原始状态检索、信号/导数子空间表示、跨时间尺度的稳健统计。每个专家映射从训练前缀中获得较高的"正常性下限（normality floor）"；打分时仅保留正向超出量（positive excess），对广泛触发的映射进行衰减，并加入一个小的秩（rank）项。参考记忆与投影通过非梯度的方式拟合，无需异常标签。由于异常流行度与秩依赖于完整打分序列，推断为批式、直推式（transductive）而非在线。

Result: 在来自 17 个 TSB-AD-M 家族的 180 条序列上，三次随机种子平均得到 AUC-PR 0.421±0.37、VUS-PR 0.452±0.37，在 24 种方法中排名第二。Holm 校正的配对检验显示：在两个主指标上与 AxonAD、StreamVAE（TSB-AD-M 上两个领先的非预训练方法）均无显著差异，而相对其余 21 个基线具有显著正向差异。在三条序列上的单次运行探针测得模型封装（model-wrapper）时间中位数为 0.0662 s。消融实验表明，主指标变化最大的是原始检索与超出量幅度保留两项组件。所得分数可按专家分解，但其诊断价值与在线适用性尚未验证。

Conclusion: ExCal 以无梯度、无标签、仅正常样本的训练方式，在 TSB-AD-M 上达到与领先非预训练方法统计不可区分的检测性能，且推理开销低；但方法当前为批式直推式，在线适用性与分数的诊断解释能力仍待进一步检验。

Abstract: We introduce ExCal, a normal-only multivariate time series anomaly detector that combines raw state retrieval, a signal/derivative subspace, and robust statistics across temporal scales. Each expert map receives a high normality floor from the training prefix. During scoring, positive excess is retained, attenuated for broadly firing maps, and combined with a small rank term. The method fits reference memories and projections without gradient optimization or anomaly labels. Prevalence and ranks depend on the complete scored sequence, making inference batch and transductive rather than online. On 180 series from 17 TSB-AD-M families, ExCal obtains AUC-PR $0.421\pm0.37$ and VUS-PR $0.452\pm0.37$ over three seeds, ranking second among 24 methods. Holm-adjusted paired tests detect no significant difference from AxonAD or StreamVAE, which are the two leading non-pretrained methods on TSB-AD-M, on either primary metric, while finding significant positive differences against the other 21 baselines. A separate single-run probe over three series measures median model-wrapper time of $0.0662$ s. Ablations associate the largest primary-metric changes with raw retrieval and excess-magnitude preservation.
