The evaluated score is decomposable by expert, but its diagnostic utility and online suitability remain untested.

</details>


### [203] [Distilling neural networks into equations to predict planetary instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35617)
*Simon Alford,Edward Gu,Omar Costilla-Reyes,Dan Tamayo,Miles Cranmer,Kevin Ellis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出一种两阶段机器学习流程，从N体模拟数据中提取出可解释的解析方程，用于预测多行星系统何时发生动力学不稳定，其精度优于现有解析估计，并覆盖质量比、间距和偏心率的大范围参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多行星系统动力学不稳定的预测问题三百年来缺乏解析进展；近期机器学习虽预测精度高，但属黑箱模型，无法与既有科学理解相衔接，因此需要可解释的解析结果。

Method: 先在N体模拟数据集上训练神经网络，再将其潜在知识蒸馏为闭式代数表达式：第一阶段学习可解释的变量基，第二阶段用符号回归搜索方程。

Result: 所得方程在多行星系统不稳定性预测上优于现有解析估计，适用于广泛的质量比、轨道间距与偏心率范围；仅用少量变量即可刻画预测景观的结构，且这些变量可进行动力学解释。

Conclusion: 符号学习与神经网络相结合，可从复杂动力学系统中提取科学洞见，为黑箱预测模型与理论理解之间搭建桥梁。

Abstract: The problem of predicting when a multi-planet system will become dynamically unstable has defied analytical progress over the past three hundred years. Recent machine learning advances achieve high predictive performance of instability dynamics, but as black-box systems cannot be interpreted to connect to existing scientific understanding. Here we show analytic equations that predict when a multi-planet system will undergo instability. We discover these equations by first training a neural network on a dataset of N-body simulations, and then distilling its latent knowledge into closed-form algebraic expressions. Our two-stage pipeline first learns an interpretable variable basis, then searches for equations using symbolic regression. The resulting equations outperform existing analytic estimates of instability and apply across wide ranges of mass ratios, spacing, and orbital eccentricities. They capture structure in the prediction landscape with a small number of variables amenable to dynamical interpretation. These results demonstrate how symbolic learning can be combined with neural networks to extract scientific insight from complex dynamical systems.

</details>


### [204] [Adapt for Planning, Not just Prediction: Optimistic Test-Time World Model Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35636)
*Akshay Mete,Dileep Kalathil,P. R. Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出乐观测试时自适应（OpTTA），在预测损失之外加入规划器的目标达成代价项，使世界模型在部署时朝"乐观于到达目标"的方向更新，从而提升规划成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜世界模型通常在离线预训练后冻结，部署时遇分布偏移会退化；而面向规划的TTA是闭环的（规划器用模型rollout选动作，产生的转移又驱动下一次更新）。纯预测目标只拟合自身动作的结果，可能对"能达成目标的动作"仍然判断错误，低预测误差未必带来更好的规划。

Method: 受自适应控制中"奖励偏置最大似然"启发，OpTTA的目标含两项：观测转移上的预测损失，以及规划器在候选rollout上的目标达成代价，二者对模型参数共同最小化；规划项使模型偏向对达成目标乐观，且不需额外环境交互。实验覆盖3个不同编码器与训练目标的JEPA世界模型、操作/到达/导航任务、视觉与环境偏移，规划器包括CEM与基于梯度的规划器，并分别考察仅适应预测器与同时适应编码器。

Result: 仅适应预测器时，OpTTA相对冻结模型将最终规划成功率平均提升13.5个百分点，预测-only目标（AdaJEPA）为6.9个百分点；同时适应编码器时分别为13.5与7.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 面向规划的世界模型测试时自适应，在预测损失之外引入乐观的、任务感知的项能带来实质收益，说明预测精度与规划效用不一致，任务导向的目标更适配闭环TTA。

Abstract: Latent world models are typically pre-trained offline and kept frozen at test time, so planning with them can degrade under distribution shift. Test-time adaptation (TTA) can mitigate this degradation by updating the model during deployment. Unlike pre-training on fixed data, TTA for planning is closed-loop: the planner uses model rollouts to select actions, and the resulting transitions drive the next update. This raises the question: which objective should guide TTA for planning? Under a prediction-only objective, the model is fit only to the outcomes of its own actions, so it can remain wrong about the actions that would reach the goal, and low prediction error need not translate into better planning. Inspired by reward-biased maximum likelihood from adaptive control, we introduce Optimistic Test-Time Adaptation (OpTTA). Its objective has two terms, the prediction loss on observed transitions and the planner's goal-reaching cost on its candidate rollouts, both minimized over the model parameters. The planning term biases adaptation toward models that are optimistic about reaching the goal, without any additional environment interaction. Across visual and environment shifts on manipulation, reaching and navigation tasks, OpTTA raises final planning success over the frozen model by 13.5 percentage points on average, compared with 6.9 for the prediction-only objective (AdaJEPA), when only the predictor is adapted. When the encoder is adapted as well, the gains are 13.5 and 7.5 points. These experiments span three JEPA world models with different encoders and training objectives and, for temporal straightening, both CEM and gradient-based planners. Our results suggest that test-time adaptation of world models for planning benefits from an optimistic, task-aware term in addition to the prediction loss.

</details>


### [205] [Proofs of strong forms of the Bovdi--Geche universality conjecture and a complex analogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35645)
*Victor Bovdi,Ambrus Pal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文证明了两条单神经元网络的通用性定理：一是有限域上激活函数为离散对数变形的Bovdi–Geche通用性猜想（强形式），二是复二元神经元且权重向量不依赖于目标函数的Aizenberg通用性定理的推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答文献中悬而未决的两个问题：Bovdi–Geche关于有限域上单神经元（激活函数为离散对数的某种变形）的通用性猜想，以及Aizenberg定理中权重向量是否可独立于目标函数的推广问题。

Method: 前一定理借助有限域上乘法特征和的Weil界；后一定理借助关于某些映射到环面（tori）的伸缩（dilates）的等分布定理。

Result: 建立了有限域上单神经元通用性的强形式，并给出复二元神经元在权重向量与目标函数无关情形下的Aizenberg型通用性定理，从而肯定地回答了文献中的另一问题。

Conclusion: 单神经元的通用性在有限域与复数两类设定下均可由解析数论工具（Weil界、环面上的等分布）给出严格证明，扩展了Bovdi–Geche与Aizenberg的经典结果。

Abstract: We prove two universality theorems for neural networks consisting of a single neuron. First we prove a strong form of the Bovdi--Geche universality conjecture on neurons over finite fields, where the activation function is a certain modification of the discrete logarithm, using the Weil bound on multiplicative character sums over finite fields. Then we show a generalisation of Aizenberg's universality theorem for complex binary neurons with a weight vector independent of the target function, positively answering another question in the literature. The proof of the latter uses an equidistribution theorem for the dilates of certain maps into tori.

</details>


### [206] [Active Learning of Conditional Generative Models via the Transport Neural Tangent Kernel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35652)
*Jayoung Ryu,Kyunghyun Cho,Romain Lopez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出传输神经正切核（tNTK），将条件生成模型的Wasserstein检验风险分解为均值项与形状项，用tNTK作为高斯过程协方差做分布感知的主动学习，在多种条件生成模型与数据集上优于基于条件均值的NTK基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高通量科学实验产出的是分布型输出，需要条件生成模型来建模并在新输入处预测；而获取新条件（新抗原、新细胞类型）的实验数据代价高昂，因此需要自动化实验设计。现有主动学习方法假设输出为标量或向量，无法处理分布型输出，因而需要新的方法。作者还指出基于均值的主动学习对分布形状“失明”。

Method: 1）理论分析：证明条件生成任务的Wasserstein检验风险可分解为均值项和形状项，说明基于均值的方法无法捕捉形状信息。2）提出tNTK：一个可计算的核，刻画生成模型预测分布对参数的敏感度；在确定性极限下退化为标准NTK，并在小参数扰动下上界预测的后验Fréchet方差；对仿射高斯传输给出闭式表达，其余情形给出可计算的蒙特卡洛估计。3）将tNTK视为高斯过程的协方差函数，贪婪地选择使池中总GP后验方差最小的输入，形成分布感知的主动学习策略。

Result: 在四类条件生成模型（包括条件flow matching与条件Monge gap）上，针对合成分布、意图标注句子和单细胞扰动响应数据进行评估，tNTK均优于由条件均值计算的标准NTK基线，表明分布感知主动学习对生成建模任务具有价值。

Conclusion: tNTK为分布型输出的主动学习提供了有理论依据且可计算的工具，能够同时利用均值与形状信息，优于均值基线的NTK方法，适用于实验设计昂贵的高通量科学场景。

Abstract: Many high-throughput scientific experiments produce distributional outputs, requiring conditional generative models to interpret the data, and to predict outcomes at new inputs. Acquiring experimental data at new conditions (e.g., for a new antigen or a new cell type) is often expensive, motivating the need for automated experimental design methods. However, existing active learning frameworks are limited as they assume scalar or vector-valued outputs. We first show that the Wasserstein test risk of a conditional generative task decomposes into a mean term and a shape term, demonstrating why mean-based active learning is blind to distributional shape. To capture both, we propose the transport Neural Tangent Kernel (tNTK), a tractable kernel that measures how sensitive a generative model's predicted distribution is with respect to its parameters. The tNTK recovers the standard NTK in the deterministic limit and upper-bounds the posterior Fréchet variance of the prediction under small parameter perturbations. It admits a closed-form expression for affine Gaussian transport and tractable Monte Carlo estimators otherwise. Treating the tNTK as the covariance function of a Gaussian process, we greedily select inputs that minimize the total GP posterior variance across the pool, yielding a distribution-aware active learning strategy. We evaluate this strategy across four classes of conditional generative models, including conditional flow matching and conditional Monge gap, on synthetic distributions, intent-labeled sentences, and single-cell perturbation responses. In these experiments, the tNTK outperforms the baseline NTK computed from the conditional mean, demonstrating the value of distribution-aware active learning for generative-modeling tasks.

</details>


### [207] [A Hybrid Framework for Explainable Aviation Risk: Causal Chain Extraction from Safety Reports with Physics-Based Failure Quantification and Interventional Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35658)
*Yutian Pang,Yifang Tian,Jiacheng Xie,Xinyu Zhao,Jueming Hu,John-Paul Clarke,Hans-Arno Jacobsen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"文本+物理"混合框架：用本地大模型从56,202份美国事故报告中抽取有序因果链构建因果知识图，并以物理模型量化工况下的失效概率；发动机失去动力案例中化油器结冰模型仅用温度与露点达AUC 0.61。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空事故报告积累了数十年，但既有计算研究多把报告归入固定分类体系，缺乏对失效成因的因果刻画与定量失效概率评估，难以支持针对性干预。

Method: 混合框架：文本侧用本地大语言模型从56,202份美国事故报告抽取有序因果链，聚合为因果知识图；每个记录携带驱动因素导出的物理因子建立面向事故总体的发生模型；用泄漏噪声或门（leaky noisy-OR）把因子合成为失效模式概率。以化油器结冰为案例，仅用温度与露点区分1,205起结冰事故；并以反事实推断计算必要性概率，用FCI算法检验后门调整的可识别性。

Result: 在2,000份报告评测样本上，因果链回收90.8%的调查员标注原因；结冰模型AUC 0.61，接近密度比上限0.64；常天气时段不构成有效对照（飞行集中于晴好天气）；后几年物理模型使发生AUC提升0.03，对严重度无提升；四起具名事故中热疏忽的必要性概率为0.29–0.50（单调性假设下）；FCI不许可所学网络上最大的后门调整。

Conclusion: 文本因果链与物理模型互补：物理模型的统计增益有限，其价值主要在于可解释性与干预支持；因果发现算法给出的可识别性约束限制了后门调整的合法性。

Abstract: Aviation authorities hold decades of accident reports whose narratives record how investigators reconstructed each accident. Computational work on these archives has mostly sorted reports into fixed taxonomies. In this work, we propose a hybrid framework in which a text-based corpus identifies the leading factors of a failure mode. Physics-based models then quantify how likely that failure is at a stated operating point. Ordered causal chains extracted from 56,202 American accident reports with a local large language model aggregate into a causation knowledge graph. On an evaluation sample of 2,000 reports, the chains recover 90.8% of investigator-flagged causes. Each physical factor whose drivers the record carries receives an occurrence model calibrated to the accident population. A leaky noisy-OR fitted to the corpus combines the factors into a failure mode probability that supports intervention. For engine power loss, the carburetor icing model separates 1,205 icing accidents from other accidents using temperature and dewpoint alone, reaching 0.61 in area under the receiver operating characteristic curve against a density ratio ceiling of 0.64. Routine weather hours give no valid control, because flying concentrates in fair weather. On later years the physics adds 0.03 to that area for occurrence and nothing for severity, so its value lies in interpretability. On four named accidents, abduction gives a probability of necessity of 0.29 to 0.50 for the heat omission, under monotonicity. The Fast Causal Inference algorithm does not license the largest backdoor adjustment on the learned networks.

</details>


### [208] [SCAFFOLD under Hessian Similarity: Sharp Convergence Rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35663)
*Zhirayr Tovmasyan,Slavomír Hanzely,Egor Shulgin,Peter Richtárik,Sebastian U. Stich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析带 minibatch 控制变量的 SCAFFOLD，在凸与非凸目标下都建立了对 Hessian 相似度 δ 的线性依赖，并为所分析方法给出匹配下界；同时指出部分参与时即使客户端完全相同也未必能从更多本地步数中获得完整通信收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SCAFFOLD 用控制变量纠客户端漂移，但其与客户端数据相似性的关系尚未完全清楚：原相似性保证只覆盖二次函数且需更强的二阶相似假设，后续弱凸分析（Luo et al., 2025）对 L-光滑损失仅得到 √(Lδ) 依赖。作者希望厘清并改进相似性依赖。

Method: 分析采用 minibatch 控制变量的 SCAFFOLD：凸设定下用精确梯度证明目标间隙为 (L/K+δ)D_0²/R 量级；非凸设定下对客户端选择变体在一致 Hessian 相似度下证明平稳性保证；两种设定均推广到随机梯度；并对常数本地步长给出匹配下界；另外分析部分参与情形。

Result: 凸精确梯度下，R 轮、每轮 K 步本地更新的目标间隙为 (L/K+δ)D_0²/R，D_0 为到最优解的初始距离；当 K=Θ(L/δ) 时比 minibatch SGD 速率改进 Θ(L/δ) 倍。非凸情形得到类似平稳性保证。随机梯度下保证延续，并给出匹配下界。部分参与下，即使客户端完全相同也不一定能获得额外本地步数带来的全部通信收益。

Conclusion: SCAFFOLD 的收敛对 Hessian 相似度呈线性依赖，优于先前 √(Lδ) 结果，并可由匹配下界说明其紧性；但部分参与限制了对本地步数通信收益的利用。

Abstract: One of the main challenges of federated learning is heterogeneous data split among the clients, leading to biased local updates called client drift. The SCAFFOLD algorithm (Karimireddy et al., 2020) corrects client drift with control variates, but its relation to the similarity of client data is not yet fully understood: the original similarity-based guarantees cover only quadratics with a stronger second-order similarity assumption, and a follow-up weakly convex analysis (Luo et al., 2025) obtains a $\sqrt{Lδ}$ dependence for $L$-smooth losses with averaged Hessian similarity $δ$. We analyze SCAFFOLD with minibatch-based control variates and establish linear dependence on Hessian similarity beyond quadratics, for both convex and nonconvex objectives. In particular, for convex objectives with exact gradients, $R$ SCAFFOLD rounds with $K$ local steps lead to an objective gap of order $(L/K+δ)D_0^2/R$, where $D_0$ is the initial distance to a minimizer. When $K=Θ(L/δ)$, it improves the minibatch SGD rate by a factor $Θ(L/δ)$. For nonconvex objectives, we prove an analogous stationarity guarantee under uniform Hessian similarity for a client-selection variant. In both settings, we extend these guarantees to stochastic gradients and prove matching lower bounds for the analyzed methods with constant local stepsizes. We also show that, under partial participation, even identical clients need not obtain the full communication benefit of additional local steps.

</details>


### [209] [CounterFlow: Target-Conditioned Flow Matching for Visual Counterfactuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35679)
*Paul Sevellec,Elisa Fromont,Romaric Gaudel,Laurence Roze,Paul Viallard,Laetitia Houeix*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CounterFlow 提出一种基于条件流匹配（Conditional Flow Matching）的可解释AI框架，通过在原始像素空间中以目标类别为条件构造向量场，直接将源图像引导至用户指定类别，从而生成逼真且计算高效的视觉反事实解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉反事实解释方法多依赖 GAN 或扩散模型，存在计算开销大、生成流程复杂的问题，且像素空间的可操作性与真实部署适配性不足。作者希望获得既高真实感、又低计算成本、可直接落地部署的反事实生成方案。

Method: 提出 CounterFlow：基于条件流匹配，学习以目标类别为条件的向量场，直接在原始像素空间中对源图像做连续流变换，将其朝用户指定类别方向引导，得到语义层面的高真实度修改；相较 GAN/扩散架构无需多步随机采样或对抗训练。

Result: 在标准基准上，CounterFlow 在视觉真实感（visual realism）与目标类别切换（target class switching）两项指标上均达到 state-of-the-art，且计算开销显著低于传统 GAN/扩散方案。

Conclusion: 条件流匹配可作为视觉反事实解释的高效生成范式；直接在原始像素空间建模有利于实际部署。摘要未给出具体量化数值与用户研究，后续可关注其对模型无关性、对生成反事实的忠实度与稀疏性的评估。

Abstract: In eXplainable AI, counterfactual explanations have gained significant traction by offering users direct, intuitive, and actionable insights into model decisions. We introduce CounterFlow, a novel framework based on Conditional Flow Matching that generates visual counterfactual explanations by directly steering a source image toward a user-specified target class. By leveraging target-conditioned vector fields, CounterFlow identifies highly realistic semantic modifications while requiring substantially lower computational overhead than traditional GAN- or diffusion-based architectures. Empirical evaluations on standard benchmarks demonstrate that CounterFlow achieves state-of-the-art performance in both visual realism and target class switching. By working directly in the raw pixel space, CounterFlow offers a more practical solution for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [210] [GNN-VIEW: Visual Inspection and Explanation of Graph-based Earth-System Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35693)
*Alessandro Lucatelli,Ferdinand Unterhuber,Katharina Krenzer,Mennatallah El-Assady,Benedikt Soja,Christina Humer,Joel Oskarsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GNN-VIEW：一个交互式浏览器工具，通过暴露图的信息通路支持基于图的Earth-system模型开发，包括训练前调试图结构与训练后基于归因的可解释性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络等图模型是Earth-system建模的重要方向，图连通性可在训练前约束学习过程。但图由算法构建，开发者除静态绘图外缺乏对最终结果的直觉，且绘图需额外工作、不够细致；训练成功后模型如何得到预报也不清楚，难以调试与理解失败原因。

Method: 提出基于浏览器的交互式工具GNN-VIEW。训练前暴露图的信息通路，供开发者调试图结构；训练后采用基于归因（attribution-based）的可解释性方法，揭示模型在给定预报中使用了哪些输入特征。

Result: 在两个场景展示工具价值：海洋预报模型图构建阶段；以及解释一个已训练天气预报模型在Laura飓风期间降水预测不佳的原因。

Conclusion: GNN-VIEW通过交互式地呈现图结构与归因解释，帮助开发者训练前发现图结构问题、训练后理解模型失败原因，提升图基Earth-system模型的开发效率与可理解性。

Abstract: Machine learning Earth-system models are becoming increasingly important, and so is their development. Graph-based models, one of the most prominent approaches, allow developers to constrain the learning process before training via graph connectivity. Yet, developing these models remains challenging: the graph is built algorithmically with little intuition about the final result beyond static plotting, which requires extra work and is often not detailed enough. Even after successful training, how the model arrives at forecasts remains obscure. We present GNN-VIEW, an interactive browser-based tool that supports the development of such systems. GNN-VIEW exposes the graph's information pathways, allowing model developers to debug the graph structure before training. After training, GNN-VIEW uses attribution-based explainability to reveal which input features are used by the trained model for a given forecast. This helps developers understand why a model fails. We showcase the usefulness of GNN-VIEW in the construction stage of a graph for an ocean forecasting model, and in explaining poorly predicted precipitation of a trained weather forecasting model during Hurricane Laura.

</details>


### [211] [MiRL: Error-Targeted Reinforcement Learning for Correcting Terminal Agent Misbehaviors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35697)
*Runchu Tian,Zaid Khan,Zun Wang,Justin Chih-Yao Chen,Mohit Bansal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 MiRL（Misbehavior-driven RL），将"错误靶向学习"从单轮任务推广到长时程终端智能体。方法先剖析策略错误，归纳出跨环境复现的"错误行为"（misbehavior），再从这些错误发生处启动 rollout 做强化学习，只有通过测试且不再犯该错误的 rollout 才得奖励。在 Qwen3.5-4B 上，8 类错误行为占错误步骤的 52.1%；训练后在未见前缀上错误重犯率下降 43%–57%，Terminal-Bench Lite 达 51.8%（TMax-4B 为 47.4%），Terminal-Bench 2.1 基本持平（21.3% vs 20.6%），且仅用 35% 的 rollout 量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 错误靶向学习思路清晰（定位弱点→针对性训练→验证是否修复），但现有方法仅面向单轮任务；而现有对智能体失败的分析只用于评估与调试，未用于训练。终端智能体需在真实 shell 环境中完成长时程自然语言任务，错误分散在长轨迹中，直接整体训练效率低，故需一种能从错误出发、可泛化且高效的训练范式。

Method: MiRL 分两步：（1）错误剖析——对策略在大量环境中的错误步骤进行归类，提炼出跨环境反复出现的"错误行为"（如某类错误操作模式），并定位其所在步骤作为训练起点；（2）错误靶向 RL——从这些错误行为出现的步骤处启动 rollout（而非从任务起点），奖励条件为双重：rollout 必须通过任务测试且不重复该错误行为，从而直接针对弱点优化。相比从零起点采样，该方式显著提升训练信号密度。

Result: 在 Qwen3.5-4B 上，8 类错误行为覆盖 52.1% 的错误步骤，说明少数模式主导失败；MiRL 训练后，在未见前缀（unseen prefixes）上错误行为重复率平均下降 43%–57%，表明学到的是可泛化的行为修正而非死记；Terminal-Bench Lite 上 51.8% 优于 TMax-4B 的 47.4%，Terminal-Bench 2.1 上 21.3% 与 20.6% 基本持平，同时 rollout 消耗仅为 TMax-4B 的 35%。

Conclusion: 结果表明错误靶向学习可成功迁移到真实、长时程的终端任务：通过提炼可泛化的错误行为并从错误步骤处做定向 RL，能在更少采样下取得更强或相当的终端基准表现。该工作把"失败分析"从评估工具转为训练信号，为长时程智能体的高效改进提供了可解释、可度量的路径。

Abstract: Error-targeted learning gives a legible way to improve a model: identify specific weaknesses of the model, train the model to fix these weaknesses, and check whether the weaknesses are fixed. We study how to apply error-targeted learning to terminal agents, which solve long-horizon tasks specified in natural language by interacting with a command shell. Existing error-targeted methods are designed for single-turn tasks, and existing analyses of agent failures are used to evaluate and debug agents rather than to train them. We propose Misbehavior-driven RL (MiRL), which first profiles the errors of a policy to find generalizable misbehaviors: kinds of wrong steps that recur across many environments. MiRL then trains the policy with RL, starting rollouts at the steps where these misbehaviors occur and rewarding a rollout only if it passes the tests and avoids the misbehavior. On Qwen3.5-4B, eight misbehaviors account for 52.1% of wrong steps. After training with MiRL, the policy repeats the measured misbehaviors 43% to 57% less often on average on unseen prefixes. MiRL outperforms TMax-4B on Terminal-Bench Lite (51.8% vs. 47.4%) and matches it on Terminal-Bench 2.1 (21.3% vs. 20.6%), while using 35% of its rollouts. These results show that error-targeted learning can be applied to realistic, long-horizon terminal tasks.

</details>


### [212] [Triage: Skip, Skim, or Refine Attention Tiles in Video Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35706)
*Yeonjae Kim,Omin Kwon,Seong Hoon Seo,Jae W. Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Triage：一种免训练的三级分档注意力方法，按块自适应选择"跳过/低精度NVFP4/低精度+残差修正"，在RTX Blackwell上以融合核单遍执行，实现视频DiT端到端最高2.32x加速且PSNR反而更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频DiT的注意力开销由数万视觉token主导。现有稀疏化、低精度或两者结合的方法对所有保留块采用统一精度，忽略了近似误差在不同块间差异极大的事实：许多块可近乎零误差跳过，另一些低精度已足够，少数即便低精度仍误差显著。单一精度因此造成算力浪费或精度损失。

Method: 1) 将每个tile分配到三档近似级别：跳过（用块均值替代）、NVFP4"略读"、NVFP4 + 残差修正的"精算"；2) 轻量路由器基于块统计量预测各档误差，仅当预测误差收益超过额外计算代价时将块升级到更高精度档，由单一"价格"参数控制权衡；3) 融合核在RTX Blackwell的FP4张量核上单遍执行全部三档。全程免训练。

Result: 在Wan2.2、HunyuanVideo、LTX-2.5上，端到端生成相比dense FlashAttention最高加速2.32x；在全部5个设定中均快于所有基线，且PSNR比PSNR最高的基线还高0.3–3.0 dB。

Conclusion: 块间误差异质性使得"分档"优于"统一精度"：细粒度的误差预测路由可在同等或更低算力下获得更高保真度，配合硬件级FP4融合实现速度与质量的双赢，且无需额外训练。

Abstract: Attention over tens of thousands of visual tokens dominates the inference of video diffusion transformers (DiTs). Existing methods reduce this cost through sparsity, low precision, or both, but use a single precision for all kept tiles. We observe that the output error introduced by approximation varies widely across tiles. Many tiles can be skipped with little error, others must be computed but gain little beyond low precision, and a few retain substantial error even at low precision. We present Triage, a training-free attention method that assigns each tile one of three approximation tiers: skipping it by replacing it with its block means, skimming it in NVFP4, or refining it with NVFP4 residual correction. A lightweight router predicts each tile's error under each tier from block statistics and moves a tile to a more accurate tier only when the predicted error reduction justifies the extra compute, with a single price setting this trade-off. A fused kernel executes all three tiers in a single pass on the FP4 tensor cores of RTX Blackwell GPUs. Across Wan2.2, HunyuanVideo, and LTX-2.5, Triage accelerates end-to-end generation by up to 2.32x over dense FlashAttention, and in each of five settings it runs faster than every baseline while reaching 0.3-3.0 dB higher PSNR than the highest-PSNR baseline.

</details>


### [213] [Detecting bus delays and service anomalies in Niterói with autoencoder latent feature representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35710)
*Matheus Germano,Claudio E. C. Campelo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 检验单一无监督GPS表征能否同时用于单程延误检测与城市级异常诊断：结果显示两项用途均未获支持（潜在表征未提升延误检测；多数异常外部验证为循环论证），仅剩日级稳定信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公交乘客对服务的判断基于两类失败：单程晚点与整日系统级失调。作者希望用NetMob 2026尼泰罗伊公交GPS数据验证：一个无监督表征是否可同时支撑这两种诊断，从而避免分别构建模型。

Method: 自编码器将每趟行程压缩为64维隐向量；冻结编码器接入监督式延误分类器，重构误差作为异常分数。对比逐行程汇总特征的梯度提升基线，并做消融式预处理松弛检验；对异常分数进行三项外部校验（含去除降雨因素的重训）；用三个自编码器检验日级信号稳定性，并比较池化分位阈值与分线路重标定阈值。

Result: 隐含表征未改善延误检测：汇总特征GB达0.846±0.013 ROC-AUC，最优潜在配置0.838±0.016，松弛预处理后差距显著。异常分数三项外部校验中两项为循环论证：去除降雨后降雨关联消失（5.4% vs 5.0%被标记，p=0.84），3月23日"全城中断"亦消失。留存的是日级信号，跨三个自编码器稳定（Spearman ρ≥0.96），主要受日类型驱动，退化遥测日在工作日/周末分层内仍可区分。阈值而非分数主导检出：池化分位切点在最差退化日仅标记5.7%行程，线路内重标定则达29.3%。延误模型几乎依赖单一特征，无单个隐维可解释，22条长期问题线路-方向承担半数延误行程。

Conclusion: 单一无监督表征不足以同时胜任行程级延误检测与系统级异常诊断；潜在表征相对手工汇总特征无增益，异常分数的多数外部相关性为循环产物。有效的仅是粗粒度日级可分离性，且检出效果高度依赖阈值标定方式。作者以阴性结果形式给出可复现的评估陷阱提示。

Abstract: Bus riders judge service by two failures: single trips that run late, and days on which the whole system misbehaves. We ask whether one unsupervised representation of raw GPS telemetry can serve both diagnoses on the NetMob 2026 Niterói bus dataset. An autoencoder compresses each trip into a 64-dimensional latent vector; the frozen encoder feeds supervised delay classifiers, and the reconstruction error serves as an anomaly score. Two findings are negative. First, the latent representation does not improve delay detection: gradient boosting on per-trip summary features reaches $0.846 \pm 0.013$ ROC-AUC against $0.838 \pm 0.016$ for the best latent setup, and the gap becomes significant once the preprocessing filters are relaxed. Second, two of the three external checks of the anomaly score were circular. Retraining without rainfall removes the rain association ($5.4\%$ against $5.0\%$ flagged, $p = 0.84$) and an apparent citywide disruption on 23 March. What survives is a day-level signal stable across three autoencoders (Spearman $ρ\geq 0.96$), driven mostly by day type, with degraded-telemetry days still separable within weekday and weekend strata. The alarm threshold limits detection more than the score does: a pooled percentile cut flags $5.7\%$ of trips on the worst degraded day, while re-scaling within each bus line flags $29.3\%$. The delay model rests almost entirely on one feature, no latent dimension is individually interpretable, and 22 chronic line-directions carry half of all delayed trips.

</details>


### [214] [Quantitative Generalization Bounds for Multi-Layer Transformers: A Few Long Strings can Beat Many Short Ones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35715)
*Marius Henry,Michael Rizvi-Martel,Michael Hahn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文对固定精度Transformer同时考虑分布内统计泛化与长度泛化两条轴，给出以参数规模、量化空间和字母表大小表示的泛化保证，并揭示训练长度与样本数之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer能泛化到比训练时更长、更难的问题实例，但理论理解滞后。既有研究多将分布内统计泛化与长度泛化分开讨论，二者如何相互作用尚不清楚，因此需要联合处理。

Method: 针对固定精度（量化）Transformer，建立同时刻画两条泛化轴的统一分析框架，推导泛化界，其依赖量包括模型参数规模、量化空间以及输入序列的字母表大小；并分析样本数与训练长度需求之间的权衡关系。

Result: 得到同时覆盖统计泛化与长度泛化的保证；分析了样本数与训练长度的权衡，发现增加训练长度可比增加样本数在效率上呈指数级更优；实验结果支持理论预测。

Conclusion: 两条泛化轴可被统一处理，参数规模、量化精度与字母表大小共同决定泛化能力；相较单纯扩充数据，延长训练序列长度是更高效的泛化途径。

Abstract: Transformer-based language models exhibit an astonishing capacity to generalize to longer and more difficult problem instances than those seen in training. However, theoretical understanding of this phenomenon still lags behind. Unlike most machine learning models, the generalisation abilities of transformers inherently require considering two axes. On one hand, one must examine in-distribution statistical generalisation, which concerns the ability of a model to broaden a function from training samples to novel samples of the same distribution. On the other hand, given the sequential nature of language data, one must also consider length generalisation, which reflects whether a model trained on inputs of a given length can extrapolate to longer ones.These two notions have mostly been studied separately, leaving open how they interact. In this work, we give a joint treatment of both axes for fixed-precision transformers. We give generalisation guarantees in terms of the parameter sizes of the involved models, as well as the quantization space and the alphabet size that supply the sequences. Finally, we illustrate how the requirements in the sample count and training length trade off. In particular, we find that increasing training length can be exponentially more efficient than increasing the sample size. Experiments support predictions of the theory.

</details>


### [215] [Audit Where Hacks Pay: Budgeted Strong-Verifier Audits Against Reward Hacking in GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35716)
*Shurun Li,Michael Hua Wang,Haibo Zeng,Long Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在GRPO中，可将有限的强验证器预算分配到"审计哪些弱通过轨迹"与"捕获作弊如何给奖励"两个设计维度上；以"低通过数优先整组审计"(LB)能最小化作弊残留的优势质量，在真实LLM训练中把作弊压至近零，且比同预算的无偏校正更省标签、留出准确率更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可验证奖励强化学习依赖单元测试等廉价检查，策略会学会钻空子（hacking）；而更强的验证器太贵，无法在每次rollout上运行。因此需要研究如何在GRPO中高效花掉有限的强验证器预算。

Method: 理论：在固定策略状态、中心化优势下，期望更新仅通过每条轨迹的边际审计概率与校正量依赖于设计；把被抓作弊的奖励对失败奖励做镜像会翻转全作弊组的优势符号；在通过数型设计中，若标签价值随通过数下降，则"低通过数优先的整组审计"(LB)在固定校正下最小化留在作弊上的优势质量。实证：在真实LLM训练中，用冻结面板上该优势质量的符号预测作弊是否被抑制；在LeetCode与Countdown-Code上比较LB、同惩罚同预算的随机审计，以及两种无偏校正（逐项奖励级IPW、组级优势校正）和全审计。

Result: 起始状态下该优势质量的符号在39次运行中的33次（含16次预注册中的13次）正确预测了作弊是否被抑制。LB使作弊接近零，随机审计则不能且消耗更多标签。Countdown-Code中两种无偏校正虽也降低作弊，但留出准确率比LB低6.1–11.8与8.0–11.5个点（同预算）；全审计（组级校正在期望上复现之）在三个种子下与LB相差约两点以内，而LB仅用28%的标签；按通过数分配同一校正可挽回大部分损失（一种子）。

Conclusion: 训练结果不仅取决于期望更新，也取决于其估计的随机性；审计设计应同时考虑期望优势质量与估计方差。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards trains on cheap checks, such as unit tests, that a policy can learn to game, while a stronger check is too expensive to run on every rollout. We study how to spend a limited strong-verifier budget in GRPO, separating which weakly passing rollouts to audit from what reward a caught hack receives. At a fixed policy state with centered advantages, the expected update depends on a design only through each rollout's marginal audit probability and correction; reflecting a caught hack's reward about the failure reward negates the advantages of an all-hack group; and auditing whole groups with the fewest weak passes first (low-pass budgeted audits, LB) minimizes the advantage mass left on hacks among pass-count designs with a fixed correction when a label's value falls with the pass count. We tested this analysis in actual LLM training: from each run's starting state, the sign of this mass on a frozen panel predicted whether hacking would be suppressed in 33 of 39 runs, including 13 of 16 preregistered ones. In LeetCode (Wong et al., 2026) and Countdown-Code (Khalifa et al., 2026), LB drives hacking to near zero, while random audits with the same penalty and budget do not and use more labels. We also find that the randomness of the update affects training: in Countdown-Code, two unbiased corrections, per-item reward-level IPW and a group-level advantage correction, bring hacking down too but end 6.1-11.8 and 8.0-11.5 points lower in held-out accuracy than LB at the same budget. A full audit, which the group-level correction reproduces in expectation, stays within about two points of LB in three seeds, while LB uses 28% of its labels, and allocating the same correction by pass count recovers most of the loss (one seed). Training outcomes thus depend not only on the expected update but also on the randomness of its estimate.

</details>


### [216] [Quantifying the failure of symmetry learning in linear diffusion models due to initialization and finite-sample effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35720)
*John J. Vastola,Kanaka Rajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文在线性扩散模型这一可理论分析的设定下，研究扩散模型为何无法学习数据分布的对称性，并指出三种潜在对称破缺来源中"目标噪声"最为顽固。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据分布普遍存在对称性（如任意朝向的狗都出现在"狗图片"分布中），但现代生成模型往往无法学习或利用这些对称性，导致样本效率低、泛化差。已有研究缺乏对"扩散模型在多大程度上学不到对称性、以及何种对称破缺来源最应负责"的定量刻画。

Method: 将问题置于线性扩散模型这一可解析处理的框架中；考察三类对称破缺来源——初始权重矩阵非对称、带噪学习目标、有限样本噪声；借助随机矩阵理论中的局部定律（local laws）推导一般情形（含子空间内对称）下平均对称性误差的解析公式。

Result: 目标噪声导致的对称破缺最为稳健，即使在样本充足的区域依然存在，而初始权重非对称与有限样本噪声的影响可在样本丰富时被削弱；推导出的平均对称误差公式表明，规模更小的对称性（子空间维度更低）更难被学习。

Conclusion: 扩散模型的对称性学习瓶颈主要来自学习目标本身的噪声，而非初始化或样本量；对称结构越小越难学。这为理解生成模型的样本效率与泛化提供了理论解释，并提示改进方向应聚焦于降低目标噪声或显式注入对称性先验。

Abstract: Data distributions often possess one or more symmetries: for example, all orientations of any specific dog are included in the distribution of all possible dog pictures. Despite the omnipresence of such symmetries, modern generative modeling approaches may fail to incorporate or learn them, which can lead to lower sample efficiency and poorer generalization. To what extent do diffusion models fail to learn data distribution symmetries, and what sources of symmetry breaking are most responsible? Here, we answer this question in the theoretically tractable setting of linear diffusion models. We show that of three potential sources of symmetry breaking---a non-symmetric initial weight matrix, a noisy learning target, and finite-sample noise---target noise produces the most robust symmetry breaking, since it persists even in the sample-rich regime. Using local laws from random matrix theory, we derive formulas for average symmetry error in fairly general cases involving symmetry within a subspace and show that smaller symmetries are harder to learn.

</details>


### [217] [Can JEV Support Clinical Trial Criteria Assessment?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35721)
*Mengxian He,Ziyue Gao,Chun Li,Shunyi Zhang,Hao Wei,Minqing Zhang,Wu Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在2,048个患者-条件对的严格零数据保留（ZDR）评测中，非生成式决策模型JEV与模式约束的Qwen Flash在批量模式下宏观F1相近，但JEV在MIMIC-III上显著占优（0.768 vs 0.534），主要源于对"信息不足"类条件更高的召回（56.3% vs 9.9%）；JEV延迟与成本亦更低，但存在临床证据解读错误，需结合病历核验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床试验预筛需区分"条件未满足"与"证据不足"，而现有基于大语言模型方案多为生成式输出，存在格式约束、成本与延迟问题，且需在保护临床数据隐私（ZDR）前提下评估其可靠性，因此需要与非生成式决策模型进行系统性比较。

Method: 回顾性比较JEV（非生成式决策模型，直接输出原生选项概率）与模式约束的Qwen Flash。数据来自TrialGPT及由MIMIC-III、MIMIC-IV衍生的问答数据集，共2,048个测试患者-条件对，全程遵循严格零数据保留（ZDR）策略。两种模型分别以单条件与共享同一患者上下文的批量模式进行判定，以参考标签衡量性能，并记录作业延迟与估算成本。

Result: 批量模式下，JEV与Qwen在TrialGPT（宏观F1 0.652 vs. 0.660）与MIMIC-IV（0.807 vs. 0.814）表现相近；在MIMIC-III上JEV显著更优（0.768 vs. 0.534，Δ=+0.234，95% CI 0.187–0.281），主要来自"信息不足"标签条件召回率更高（56.3% vs. 9.9%）。JEV在三套数据上的中位作业延迟更低（1.18–1.40 s vs. 2.80–8.90 s），每条件估算成本降低53%–74%。

Conclusion: JEV适合作为初筛组件，提供准则级判断并标注潜在信息缺口以供人工复核；其在高信息缺失场景下的召回优势与效率优势明显。但JEV在临床证据解读上仍存在可观测错误，其输出在影响入组资格决策前必须与病历核验。

Abstract: Clinical trial prescreening requires distinguishing unmet conditions from insufficient evidence in patient records. Given patient records, criterion questions, and predefined answer options, we retrospectively compared JEV, a non-generative decision model returning native option probabilities, with schema-constrained Qwen Flash across 2,048 test patient-condition pairs from TrialGPT and question-answer datasets derived from MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV, all evaluated under strict Zero Data Retention (ZDR) policies to ensure clinical data privacy. Both models assessed conditions individually or in batches sharing the same patient context, with performance measured against reference labels. In batch mode, JEV and Qwen achieved similar macro-F1 scores on TrialGPT (0.652 vs. 0.660) and MIMIC-IV (0.807 vs. 0.814), whereas JEV substantially outperformed Qwen on MIMIC-III (0.768 vs. 0.534; $Δ$ = +0.234, 95% CI: 0.187-0.281). This advantage largely reflected higher recall for conditions labeled as having insufficient information (56.3% vs. 9.9%). Across all three datasets, JEV also had lower observed median job latency (1.18-1.40 vs. 2.80-8.90 s) and 53%-74% lower estimated cost per condition. These findings position JEV as an initial assessment component that provides criterion-level judgments and flags potential information gaps for review. JEV's observed errors in interpreting clinical evidence underscore the need to verify its outputs against patient records before they inform eligibility decisions.

</details>


### [218] [TRACE: Memory-Efficient Time Series Class-Incremental Learning via Temporal-Spectral Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35727)
*Junhui Mao,Junru Zhang,Liming Ye,Cheng Peng,Haoran Shi,Xiaoli Tang,Baokun Wang,Bin Dou,Yabo Dong,Han Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TRACE，一种无回放、内存高效的时间序列类增量学习框架，通过冻结的时序-频谱表示与类特定自编码器生成式建模保留类知识，在五个基准上保持竞争性精度同时将保留内存降低约98.26%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web互联传感应用持续产生行为类别不断演化的多变量时间序列，TSCIL需在不遗忘旧知识的前提下适应新类。但严格隐私与内存约束下保留多样且演化的类分布仍是根本挑战：现有方法难以权衡"保留足够历史知识以保证识别准确"与"最小化持续适应所需内存"。

Method: TRACE为无回放框架，用生成式建模替代样本重放：采用紧凑且冻结的时序-频谱表示，配合轻量级类特定自编码器捕捉类内复杂变化；各类模型独立保存，避免递归生成与灾难性遗忘；通过基于重构的评分直接分类，无需保留历史样本或大型统计矩阵。

Result: 在五个TSCIL基准上取得竞争性分类精度；相比最强分析基线TS-ACL，平均保留内存降低约98.26%；在GrabMyo上精度相对提升约33.43%。

Conclusion: TRACE表明：以类特定生成式模型替代样本回放，可在极低内存占用下有效缓解灾难性遗忘，为隐私受限场景下多变量时间序列类增量学习提供可行方案。

Abstract: Web-connected sensing applications continuously generate multivariate time series with evolving behavioral categories, making Time Series Class-Incremental Learning (TSCIL) essential for adapting to new classes without forgetting previously acquired knowledge. However, preserving diverse and evolving class distributions under strict privacy and memory constraints remains a fundamental challenge. Existing approaches often face a trade-off between retaining sufficient historical knowledge for accurate recognition and minimizing the memory required for continual adaptation. To address these limitations, we propose TRACE, a memory-efficient, replay-free framework that preserves class-specific knowledge through generative modeling rather than sample rehearsal. TRACE employs a compact, frozen temporal-spectral representation and lightweight class-specific autoencoders to capture complex intra-class variations. By independently preserving each class model, TRACE avoids recursive generation and catastrophic forgetting, while enabling direct classification through reconstruction-based scoring without retaining historical samples or large statistical matrices. Extensive experiments across five TSCIL benchmarks demonstrate that TRACE achieves competitive classification accuracy while reducing mean retained memory by approximately 98.26% compared with TS-ACL, the strongest evaluated analytical baseline, and achieving an approximately 33.43% relative improvement in accuracy on GrabMyo.

</details>


### [219] [Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning Using Routed Ensembles and Trajectory Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35733)
*Zhenshuo Zhang,Ruoxuan Xiong,Hongyang R. Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究多目标强化学习中目标冲突对样本效率的影响，提出目标冲突系数并给出近最优遗憾界，进而设计路由集成策略学习算法；同时提出核代理轨迹归因算法用于轨迹选择。实验显示路由集成在Meta-World与MO-Gymnasium上平均性能提升4.3%，核归因与留一分析相关性提升50%，用于轨迹选择时奖励提升27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标RL的核心挑战是学习能平衡相互竞争目标的策略（如安全对齐中的有用性vs诚实性、机器人中的速度vs能耗）。最优策略随目标权重组合急剧变化，变化程度取决于目标冲突程度，导致逐偏好策略学习样本效率低下。因此需要量化冲突对学习效率的影响，并设计更高效的策略学习与数据利用方法。

Method: 1）引入“目标冲突系数”，据此推导在线设定下的近最优遗憾界，量化目标失配对学习效率的影响；2）设计路由集成策略学习（routed ensemble policy learning）：维护一小组基策略，动态将轨迹路由到潜在奖励最高的策略；3）将轨迹选择视为缓解目标冲突的另一途径，设计核代理轨迹归因（kernel surrogate trajectory attribution）算法，估计一组轨迹的反馈信号如何影响其他轨迹上的策略学习，核函数可刻画轨迹间的协同与拮抗等非线性交互。

Result: 在多种环境与基准上评估：路由集成在Meta-World与MO-Gymnasium在线环境中平均性能较基线提升4.3%；核代理归因与留一法分析的相关性较现有基线提升50%；将核归因应用于轨迹选择时，奖励较基线归因方法提升27%。

Conclusion: 目标冲突程度可被形式化度量并直接决定学习效率上限；通过路由集成在策略层面、通过核代理轨迹归因在数据层面缓解冲突，均能显著提升多目标RL的性能与样本效率，且核方法能捕捉轨迹间非线性交互带来的归因优势。

Abstract: Multi-objective reinforcement learning is a fundamental problem for modern AI, with applications spanning post-training, reasoning models, and robotic control. A central challenge is to learn policies that balance competing objectives, such as helpfulness versus honesty in safety and alignment, or speed versus energy in robotics. The optimal policy can vary sharply with objective-weighting combinations, and the extent of this variation depends crucially on the degree of conflict among objectives, making per-preference policy learning sample-inefficient. In this paper, we introduce objective-conflict coefficients and derive near-optimal regret bounds for online settings using this measure, thereby quantifying the impact of misalignment on learning efficiency. Then, we design routed ensemble policy learning, an algorithm that maintains a small set of basis policies and dynamically routes trajectories to the policy with the highest potential reward. Finally, we consider trajectory selection as another approach to mitigate objective conflict, and design a kernel surrogate trajectory attribution algorithm that estimates how feedback signals from a set of trajectories influence policy learning on other trajectories. The kernel can capture nonlinear interactions such as synergy and antagonism between trajectories. We evaluate our approach across diverse environments and benchmarks: routed ensembles improve average performance by 4.3% over baselines for online environments from Meta-World and MO-Gymnasium, and kernel surrogate attribution improves correlation with leave-one-out analysis over existing baselines by 50%. When applied to trajectory selection, kernel-based attribution yields a 27% improvement in reward over baseline attribution methods.

</details>


### [220] [LUCID: Local Understanding of Covariate Importance with Conditional Density Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35740)
*Yixiao Wang,Srikar Katta,Jon Donnelly,Emanuele Borgonovo,Cynthia Rudin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LUCID，一种模型无关的局部变量重要性（VI）度量与估计器，通过在给定其余观测特征的完整条件分布中采样合理值来扰动目标特征，解决了现有扰动策略忽略特征依赖导致反事实不真实的问题，并给出收敛保证与不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解机器学习模型的局部层面变量重要性至关重要。基于局部置换的VI方法简单有效，但现有扰动策略往往忽略特征依赖，或仅通过受限近似处理依赖。若扰动特征时未充分尊重特征依赖，会产生不现实的反事实样本，从而在观测数据分布不支持的区域评估模型。

Method: 提出新的模型无关局部VI度量与估计器LUCID（Local Understanding of Covariate Importance with conditional Density estimation）。其核心是利用条件密度估计，从给定其余观测特征时目标特征的完整条件分布中抽取合理值来扰动特征；同时建立LUCID与现有VI度量之间的理论联系，并给出具有收敛保证和不确定性量化的估计器。

Result: 建立了LUCID与现有VI度量之间的理论联系；证明了所提估计器的收敛性并提供了不确定性量化；在模拟数据和真实数据示例中展示了LUCID稳定的性能和实用价值。

Conclusion: 通过充分尊重特征依赖、基于完整条件分布生成扰动值，LUCID避免了在数据分布不支持区域评估模型的问题，是一种性能稳定、具有理论保障且实用的模型无关局部变量重要性方法。

Abstract: Computing local-level variable importance (VI) is critical for understanding machine learning models. Local permutation-based VI metrics offer a simple yet powerful approach, measuring how much a model's output changes when perturbing its inputs. However, existing perturbation strategies often ignore feature dependence or account for it only through restricted approximations. Perturbing a feature without fully respecting this dependence can generate unrealistic counterfactuals, thereby evaluating the model in regions unsupported by the observed data distribution. We address this gap by introducing a new model-agnostic local VI metric and estimator, Local Understanding of Covariate Importance with conditional Density estimation (LUCID). LUCID perturbs features of interest using plausible values drawn from the full conditional distribution given the remaining observed features. We establish theoretical connections between LUCID and existing VI measures, and provide an estimator with convergence guarantees and uncertainty quantification. Finally, we demonstrate LUCID's stable performance and practical utility across simulated and real-world examples.

</details>


### [221] [Improving Signals, Declining Encoder: A Known-Answer Test of Label-Free Monitoring in Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35766)
*Nilotpal Kakati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，自监督表征学习中常见的无标签监控信号（训练损失、特征统计等）在编码器发生"表征崩塌/遗忘"时几乎全部失效：损失持续下降、多数信号甚至给退化模型更高评分，常规变点检测也难以在崩塌起始处报警。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督训练没有标签，实践者只能依赖损失曲线和特征统计等无标签信号来判断训练质量、选择检查点。但这类信号是否真正能反映"编码器丢失了已学到的东西"——即表征可读性的突然下降——尚未被验证。作者希望在一个生成因子已知的受控设定中，检验这些监控手段对表征退化的敏感性。

Method: 使用生成因子已知的合成图像训练 LeJEPA 编码器，共 10 个随机种子。对每个训练过程施加可识别的表征退化情形（编码器丢失部分已学信息），并以"生成因子可读性"（如线性探针/回归可解码程度）作为退化与否的客观判据。同时追踪 11 种无标签信号（损失、特征统计量等），并应用标准变点检测方法，考察信号能否在退化起始附近发出警报，以及基于这些信号挑选检查点会选到什么时刻。

Result: 10 个种子中有 9 个出现生成因子可读性的骤降并持续走低，且退化方向在因子空间中几乎一致。同期训练损失继续下降；11 种无标签信号中多数持续"改善"，并把发生退化的训练评为优于未退化者。标准变点检测很少能在退化起始附近触发警报；按这些信号选择检查点，通常会落在退化发生之后。

Conclusion: 常用的无标签监控信号不足以反映自监督编码器的表征退化，甚至方向相反，可能导致实践中错误地保留受损模型。需要开发对表征内容变化敏感的新监控与检查点选择准则。

Abstract: Self-supervised training is monitored without labels, through the loss and statistics of the learned features. We report a case where we know the encoders lost part of what they had learned, so we can check whether these signals noticed. We trained LeJEPA encoders on synthetic images with known generating factors. In nine of ten seeds, the readability of those factors fell abruptly and stayed down, nearly always along the same direction in factor space. Meanwhile the loss kept falling, and most of the eleven label-free signals we tracked kept improving and rated the declining runs better than runs that did not decline. Standard change-point detection rarely flagged the decline near its onset, and selecting checkpoints by these signals mostly chose ones after it.

</details>


### [222] [A theory of noisy linear recurrent neural network learning dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35785)
*John J. Vastola,Kanaka Rajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 内在噪声可显著调控循环神经网络（RNN）的表征学习：在白噪声输入、线性激活的可解析模型中，噪声强烈打破解空间简并，并在噪声足够高时使非简并解在参数受限条件下不再最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物神经网络普遍存在难以预测的内在涨落（固有噪声），但其理论后果、尤其在学习中的作用尚不清楚：噪声是塑造表征学习，还是仅作为无关干扰项？本文旨在厘清这一问题，检验噪声是否从根本上决定所学到的表征。

Method: 构建可解析处理的含噪RNN学习模型：线性激活函数＋白噪声输入，并区分"丰富区间"（权重以小值初始化）等情形，分析噪声对读入权重、读出权重与循环权重对齐方向的影响，同时考虑参数尺度受限（如通过正则化）的情形。

Result: 在丰富区间中，噪声强烈调控读入、读出与循环权重所对齐的方向；噪声会显著打破解空间的简并性，在某些情形下甚至决定非简并解是否存在。此外噪声产生类似相变的现象：当噪声足够高时，若参数尺度受限（如经正则化），非简并解已不再最优。

Conclusion: 噪声并非无关的干扰因素，而是表征的基本决定因素；本文结果拓展了近期关于RNN表征学习的解析工作，表明内在噪声应被视为影响学习结果与解结构的核心变量。

Abstract: Biological neural networks are noisy: their dynamics involve difficult-to-predict fluctuations which are likely due not just to chaos, but also to a variety of low-level biophysical processes. Despite the omnipresence of intrinsic noise, its theoretical consequences are incompletely understood, especially in the context of learning. For example, does noise meaningfully shape representation learning, or is it better viewed as a nuisance factor whose presence or absence changes little about what solution is found and when? Here, we argue that intrinsic noise can significantly modulate representation learning in recurrent neural networks (RNNs), since it can strongly break solution-space degeneracies and in some cases control whether a non-degenerate solution exists at all. We show that this is true for an analytically tractable model of noisy RNN learning with a linear activation function and white noise inputs. We find that, in the so-called rich regime (where weights are initialized with small values), noise strongly modulates the directions in which the read-in, readout, and recurrent weights align. Moreover, we find that noise produces a kind of phase transition: for sufficiently high noise, a non-degenerate solution is not even optimal if parameter scales are limited (e.g., via regularization). These results extend recent analytic work on RNN representation learning and show that noise can act as a fundamental determinant of representations.

</details>


### [223] [Selective Tie Learning: Mitigating Spurious Correlations in Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35787)
*Christian Moya,Purav Matlia,Elliott Thornley,Guang Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究偏好优化中的伪相关（虚假特征依赖）问题，提出以平局（tie，即同等偏好回复）增广偏好数据来减少伪依赖，并给出平局选择的理论准则：估计每个平局对伪依赖的影响量，取降幅最大的子集在一阶意义下最优；并探讨用隐式奖励边际幅度作为代理、用反事实代理与广义Gauss–Newton曲率近似在语言模型上落地。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好优化会学到伪相关；用tie数据增广能降低伪依赖，但不同tie的纠正效果不同，因此"选哪些tie用于增广"是关键问题，而直接计算影响量需要知道伪特征与目标函数曲率，通常不可得或代价高昂。

Method: (1) 在对数线性策略下推导每个tie对伪依赖的影响量，证明等规模子集中选取预测降幅最大的tie集合是一阶最优；(2) 分析tie的隐式奖励边际幅度作为影响量代理的有效条件；(3) 在语言模型上用反事实代理估计伪依赖影响、用广义Gauss–Newton近似曲率；(4) 在对数线性策略、神经模型与大型语言模型上验证选择规则。

Result: 所提出的选择规则在对数线性策略、神经模型和大型语言模型上，均能以更少数量的tie达到与均匀选择相当的伪依赖降低幅度。

Conclusion: 按预测影响量筛选tie进行增广是提升数据效率的可行策略；隐式奖励边际在特定条件下可作为影响量的有效代理；相关理论可定性迁移至神经模型与LLM实践。

Abstract: Preference optimization can learn spurious correlations, and augmenting the preference data with ties (equally preferred responses) can reduce spurious reliance. However, ties differ in their corrective effect, making their selection for augmentation important. For log-linear policies, we derive each tie's influence on spurious reliance and show that taking the largest predicted reductions is first-order optimal among subsets of equal size. Computing these influences requires knowledge of the spurious features and the curvature of the training objective, which is often unavailable or expensive to estimate. To this end, we explore using the magnitude of a tie's implicit reward margin and characterize the conditions under which it is a useful proxy for predicted influence. To test the theory and its qualitative transfer to neural models, we estimate influence with a counterfactual proxy for spurious reliance and use a generalized Gauss--Newton approximation to curvature for language models. Across log-linear policies, neural models, and large language models, our selection rules consistently achieve comparable reductions in spurious reliance with fewer ties than uniform selection.

</details>


### [224] [FOCUS: Active Critic Learning for Long-Horizon Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35801)
*Zhengping Jiang,William Jurayj,Liaoyaqi Wang,Chunsheng Zuo,Anqi Liu,Benjamin Van Durme*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FOCUS：一种利用选择性分支（selective branching）主动学习 critic 的强化学习方法，通过从最能降低估计误差的状态采样续写来精化中间价值估计，并用 TD(λ) 优势为片段打分，在长程智能体任务上优于 GRPO 与 VinePPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程语言模型智能体的结果奖励稀疏，难以对早期动作做信用分配；学习型 critic 的中间价值估计不准，而从头/中间状态重复采样评估价值成本高昂。

Method: 提出 FOCUS：rollout 控制器采用选择性分支，从预期最能降低 critic 估计误差的状态采样续写，为 critic 提供主动学习数据；精化后的价值估计以 TD(λ) 优势为相邻状态间的片段打分，采样证据同时作为 critic 训练目标；并通过模块化上下文管理接口将上下文管理与 rollout 分配解耦，以支持长程智能体 RL。

Result: 在相同每步生成预算下，使用压缩触发的片段边界进行价值估计与信用分配，FOCUS 在 legal reasoning、deep search、code localization 基准上超过 GRPO 与 VinePPO；Qwen3.5-9B 规模上 BrowseComp-Plus 提升 7.2 点（55.4 vs. 48.2），DeonticBench 提升 4.1 点（56.5 vs. 52.4）。

Conclusion: 选择性分支的主动 critic 学习结合压缩边界上的 TD(λ) 信用分配，可在匹配生成预算下显著改善长程智能体强化学习的效果。

Abstract: Sparse outcome rewards make it difficult for long-horizon language-model agents to assign credit to earlier actions. Learned critics provide value estimates for intermediate states, but these estimates can be inaccurate, while estimating intermediate values with repetitive continuation samples from points within a trajectory incurs substantial costs. To mitigate these issues, we introduce FOCUS, an RL method that uses selective branching to help the critic network learn its value estimator. The rollout controller facilitates active learning for the critic by sampling continuations from states that are expected to most reduce the critic's estimation error. These refined value estimates then score the segment between consecutive states with a TD($λ$) advantage, while the sampled evidence supplies training targets for the critic. FOCUS supports long-horizon agentic reinforcement learning through a modular context-management interface that decouples context management from rollout allocation. Our experiments show that using compaction-induced segment boundaries for value estimation and credit assignment, FOCUS outperforms GRPO and VinePPO with matched per-step generation budgets on legal reasoning, deep search, and code localization benchmarks, improving held-out accuracy at Qwen3.5-9B scale by 7.2 points over the strongest baseline on BrowseComp-Plus (55.4 vs. 48.2) and by 4.1 points on DeonticBench (56.5 vs. 52.4).

</details>


### [225] [Work Agents: Systems, Capabilities, and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35845)
*Zishan Xu,Enjun Du,Mingqi Gao,Cheng Yang,Junhao Shen,Zirong Chen,Zhiyuan Yao,Yifu Guo,Yuquan Lu,Jinyang Huang,Zhengxi Lu,Siyi Liu,Ziyu Zheng,Yongqi Zhang,Difan Zou,Weiwen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出以"工作"为中心的系统级视角，将工作型智能体视为基础模型与运行时执行框架的结合体，用工作持久性与工作开放性两个维度刻画工作条件，将数字工作分为五类，梳理可靠执行所需的五类核心能力、优化途径与评估方法，并展望未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型正从"信息生成"迈向"数字工作执行"，智能体已能在多工具、多应用、多工作流中完成复杂任务；但现有研究缺乏关于智能体如何可靠完成被委托工作的统一视角，概念、能力划分与评估标准彼此割裂。

Method: 论文为综述/立场型工作：(1) 提出工作型智能体 = 基础模型 + 运行时执行框架（runtime harness），在有状态工作空间中执行任务并交付可验证结果；(2) 引入工作持久性（work persistence）与工作开放性（work openness）两个正交维度刻画工作条件；(3) 按任务范围与组织方式将数字工作划分为五种形态；(4) 基于该框架归纳可靠执行所需的五类核心能力，并分析模型优化、执行框架优化及二者联合优化；(5) 从工作结果视角梳理现有评估方法与指标。

Result: 构建了一个统一的概念框架，实现工作条件、工作形态、核心能力、优化手段与评估方法的系统对齐；指出当前评估偏重过程与信息生成、缺乏对可验证工作结果的衡量，并明确三类优化（模型、执行框架、联合）的互补关系。

Conclusion: 未来研究应聚焦持续性工作委托、开放环境适应、以及工作执行与能力提升之间的相互促进关系；该框架为理解、优化与评估工作型智能体提供统一基础，支撑构建能交付可靠、可验证且具实际价值工作成果的系统。

Abstract: As foundation models evolve from information generation to digital work execution, agents are increasingly capable of completing complex tasks across tools, applications, and workflows. However, existing research lacks a unified perspective on how agents reliably accomplish delegated work. To address this gap, we present a work-centric, system-level perspective that views work agents as systems combining foundation models and runtime harnesses to execute tasks in stateful workspaces and deliver verifiable outcomes. We introduce two complementary dimensions, work persistence and work openness, to characterize working conditions, and classify digital work into five forms according to task scope and organization. Building on this framework, we identify five core capability families required for reliable work execution and examine approaches to model optimization, harness optimization, and their joint optimization. We further analyze existing evaluation methods and metrics from the perspective of work outcomes. Finally, we discuss future research directions, including sustained work delegation, adaptation to open environments, and the interaction between work execution and capability improvement. This paper provides a unified framework for understanding, optimizing, and evaluating work agents, supporting the development of systems capable of delivering reliable, verifiable, and practically valuable work outcomes.

</details>


### [226] [Auto-Research Arena: Benchmarking and Advancing Auto-Research in Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35866)
*Xi Chen,Chongjian Ge,Yiran Xu,Yiqun Mei,Henrik I. Christensen,Hao Tan,Ziwen Chen,Hanwen Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Auto-Research Arena，一个面向自主研究系统的多目标基准，用"准入性-改进度"分离的设计在统一实验环境中评估并发现语言建模研究思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主研究系统能力增强后，基准不仅需评估性能，还应支持有意义的研究进展；现有基准缺乏统一环境下的多目标比较与可复用发现。

Method: 将可采纳性（模型质量与资源约束）与改进度（所选目标的进展）分离；在语言模型预训练中实例化 Arena，设九个目标（覆盖模型、数据、训练、推理效率），用两个基础模型评估七个研究 harness，并与简单顺序搜索基线对比，再将发现复用于质量驱动搜索。

Result: Arena 能清晰区分不同 harness 在各研究目标上的表现；多种复杂方法被简单顺序搜索基线超越，说明自主研究系统存在较大提升空间；发现的模型与系统思路范围更广，复用于新搜索在同等数据与 FLOPs 下训练损失更低，同模型规模下 held-out BPB 更低。

Conclusion: Arena 为自主研究系统的基准评测与扩展语言建模研究范围提供了统一框架。

Abstract: As autonomous research systems become more capable, benchmarks must both evaluate their performance and support meaningful research progress. We introduce Auto-Research Arena, a multi-target benchmark for evaluating autonomous research systems and discovering ideas in language modeling. Arena supports diverse research objectives within a shared experimental environment by separating admissibility from improvement: model quality and resource constraints determine whether a result qualifies, while the selected target measures progress among admissible results. We instantiate Arena in language model pretraining with nine targets spanning model, data, training, and inference efficiency, and evaluate seven research harnesses using two foundation models. Arena clearly differentiates existing harnesses across research objectives, with several sophisticated methods outperformed by our simple sequential search baseline, indicating substantial headroom in current auto-research systems. Beyond evaluation, Arena reveals a substantially broader frontier of model and system ideas aligned with active research directions. Reusing these discoveries in a new quality-driven search yields lower training loss under matched data and FLOPs and lower held-out BPB at the same model size. Together, Arena provides a common framework for benchmarking autonomous research and expanding its scope in language modeling. The code is available at https://github.com/Auto-Research-Arena/auto-research-arena

</details>


<div id='gr-qc'></div>

# gr-qc [[Back]](#toc)

### [227] [Observational constraints on a non-minimally coupled scalar field in non-metricity gravity with an extra degree of freedom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33146)
*Saddam Hussain,Ghulam Murtaza,Avik De*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在非度规引力中考察非最小耦合标量场，采用空间平坦FLRW下非平凡联络类，触发额外动力学自由度；用DESI BAO、两套超新星与宇宙钟数据检验幂律与指数耦合两模型，并结合动力系统分析论证其宇宙学可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重合规范（coincident gauge）会抑制联络带来的额外自由度，本文希望利用非平凡联络类激活该自由度，考察非最小耦合标量场在非度规引力中能否给出可行的背景演化与热历史。

Method: 在空间平坦FLRW度规下选取非平凡联络类，构建含额外动力学自由度的非最小耦合标量场模型；具体取幂律与指数两类耦合函数，标量场势取指数形式。将两模型与DESI BAO、两套独立超新星汇编及宇宙钟数据联合拟合，并辅以自治动力系统（临界点）分析。

Result: 两模型均能再现延长的物质主导期，随后进入稳定的晚期de Sitter吸引子，拟合参数与标准ΛCDM一致；动力系统分析给出刚性流体、暂态物质主导与稳定de Sitter临界点序列，观测约束与动力学分析相互一致。

Conclusion: 非平凡联络类引入的额外自由度可支撑一类与观测相符、热历史可行的非最小耦合非度规引力模型，其宇宙学可行性得到观测与动力学两方面支持。

Abstract: We investigate a non-minimally coupled scalar field in non-metricity gravity, adopting a non-trivial connection class in spatially flat FLRW spacetimes. In contrast to the coincident-gauge choice, this connection class activates an additional dynamical degree of freedom (DoF) that modifies the background evolution. We study two representative models, defined by power-law and exponential coupling functions with the exponential potential for scalar field. Confronting both models with recent combined datasets - DESI BAO, two independent supernova compilations, and cosmic chronometers - we find that each reproduces an extended matter-dominated era followed by a stable late-time de-Sitter attractor, with inferred parameters consistent with the standard $Λ$CDM model. A complementary autonomous dynamical-system analysis identifies a sequence of critical points describing successive stiff-fluid, transient matter-dominated, and stable de-Sitter phases, ensuring a viable thermal history of the universe. The observational and dynamical results are mutually consistent and support the cosmological viability of the proposed models.

</details>


### [228] [A unified dark sector from a single canonical scalar field in Bohmian Wheeler-DeWitt cosmology with a cuscuton-inspired potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33187)
*Anant Badal,Rikpratik Sengupta,Kaushik Bhattacharya*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 作者在de Broglie-Bohm诠释下从Wheeler-DeWitt波函数中提取含cuscuton型超势生成的双曲势标量场的FLRW迷你超空间宇宙学历史，发现κ≫1时可得到尘埃+真空的单场描述，且经典历史以1.3/κ的D_V偏差逼近ΛCDM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验cuscuton型超势能否在量子宇宙学框架下产生大层次κ（标量质量与de Sitter哈勃率之比），并考察其波函数能否自然给出暗能量+尘埃的等效宇宙学行为。

Method: 对空间平坦FLRW迷你超空间（单个正则标量场+由cuscuton型超势生成的双曲势）做正则量子化，在de Broglie-Bohm诠释下由Wheeler-DeWitt方程导出约化波方程，求绝热高斯解，并做完整数值求解与Bohmian轨迹比较。

Result: 势在临界值以下有唯一非简并正极小，κ²≃3b/δ可发散（原双曲势仅κ≤3/√2）；κ≫1时得到描述de Sitter真空的绝热高斯解、平均激发密度按a^{-3}红移的经典振荡凝聚，以及等于有效齐次振子零点能除以物理元胞体积的领先量子密度（亦为尘埃型）；数值解在10^{-3}水平支持零点能律、复现其对Bohmian粒子在波包内位置的依赖、显示哈勃率量子修正随元胞作用量倒数收敛并支持单场尘埃+真空描述；经典历史以D_V中1.3/κ的偏差逼近ΛCDM距离关系。

Conclusion: cuscuton型超势可容许标量振荡与膨胀率之间的大层次，量子宇宙学波函数在κ≫1下自然给出尘埃+宇宙学常数（w=-1）的单场等效描述；该模型未拟合数据，仅作理论预测。

Abstract: We quantize a spatially flat FLRW minisuperspace containing one canonical scalar field with the hyperbolic potential generated by a cuscuton-type superpotential, and extract cosmological histories from the Wheeler-DeWitt wave function in the de Broglie-Bohm interpretation. For the canonical potential parameter, related to the original cuscuton couplings, below an explicit critical value the potential has a unique nondegenerate minimum with positive height, and the ratio $κ$ of the scalar mass to the de Sitter Hubble rate at the minimum diverges as $κ^2\simeq 3b/δ$, where $δ$ is the relative distance to criticality. For the original hyperbolic potential, $κ\leq 3/\sqrt{2}$, whereas its cuscuton-inspired generalization allows a large hierarchy between scalar oscillation and expansion rates. For $κ\gg1$, small excitation and sufficiently large cell action, the reduced wave equation has an adiabatic Gaussian solution describing a de Sitter vacuum, a classical oscillating condensate whose averaged excitation density redshifts as $a^{-3}$, and, at the packet centre, a leading quantum density equal to the zero-point energy of the effective homogeneous oscillator divided by the physical cell volume, which is also dust-like. Full numerical solutions of the Wheeler-DeWitt equation support the averaged zero-point law at the reported $10^{-3}$ level, reproduce its dependence on the position of the Bohmian particle inside the packet, show that quantum corrections to the present Hubble rate converge as the inverse of the cell action, and support a single-field dust-plus-vacuum description at the homogeneous background level when $κ\gg1$. The classical histories approach the $Λ$CDM distance relations with a deviation of $1.3/κ$ in $D_V$. The dark energy component has $w=-1$ by construction, and the model is not fitted to data.

</details>


### [229] [Black holes from a nonlocal equation of state: horizon geometry, singularity structure and thermodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33222)
*Hishan Farfán-Bachiloglu,C. R. Muniz,Francisco Tello-Ortiz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究静态球对称Kerr–Schild时空中各向异性流体的非局域状态方程，证明其可化为封闭物质量的线性方程并唯一给出Yukawa型密度分布，从而构造出具有有限程"毛发"、大尺度指数屏蔽的中性解，并完成严格视界分类、奇点判据与热力学分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非局域引力与"毛球"（hairy）解缺乏可解析处理、且往往需借助非线性电动力学实现正则核心；本文旨在以非局域状态方程作为闭包，构造既具有解析可控性、又能统一描述无毛洞、Schwarzschild型黑洞、双视界与极端构型的解族。

Method: 在静态球对称Kerr–Schild度规中引入各向异性流体的非局域状态方程，将其约化为关于封闭质量（enclosed matter）的线性方程，唯一确定Yukawa型密度剖面；对得到的解进行视界分类与表面引力比较，用Królak/Tipler判据分析奇点强度，并研究Ricci标量与Kretschmann标量平方根的可积性；进一步将闭包推广为幂律形式，借助不完全伽马函数给出插值解，最后用Lambert W函数定位热容发散并用热力学拓扑（缺陷荷）分类。

Result: （1）解具有有限程毛发、大尺度指数衰减，渐近质量是唯一守恒荷；（2）精确视界分类为：无视界构型、Schwarzschild型黑洞、含Cauchy视界的双视界几何、以及质量与半径可闭式给出的极端构型；双视界区内表面引力的绝对值始终大于外视界的；（3）中心质量为零时，静态分支趋于整体单极子、非静态分支趋于Kantowski–Sachs型区域；（4）奇点满足Królak强曲率判据，并在类空分支的非径向测地线上为Tipler强奇点；零中心质量时Ricci标量及Kretschmann标量平方根在不变体积测度下局部可积；（5）幂律推广给出不完全伽马函数解，在奇异核心与具de Sitter核的正则黑洞间插值，且无需非线性电动力学；（6）得到闭式表面引力、扩展第一定律恒等式与Smarr关系，并定位热容发散；（7）热力学拓扑区分出单一缺陷的Schwarzschild型区与双反向绕数缺陷的极端区（于最大Hawking温度处合并）。

Conclusion: 非局域状态方程可作为自洽闭包，生成解析可控、有限程毛发的黑洞解族，并统一覆盖无视界、双视界、极端与正则核心等情形；该框架系统给出视界结构、奇点强度、曲率可积性与热力学（含拓扑）性质的完整刻画。

Abstract: We show that a nonlocal equation of state for an anisotropic fluid reduces to a linear equation for the enclosed matter and uniquely selects a Yukawa-type density profile in a static, spherically symmetric Kerr--Schild geometry. The resulting neutral solution has finite-range hair, exponentially screened at large distances, with the asymptotic mass as its only conserved charge, and admits an exact horizon classification: horizonless configurations, Schwarzschild-like black holes, two-horizon geometries with a Cauchy horizon, and an extremal configuration whose mass and radius are obtained in closed form. We prove that the magnitude of the inner surface gravity exceeds that of the outer horizon throughout the two-horizon regime. When the central mass vanishes, the geometry approaches a global monopole in the static branch and a Kantowski--Sachs-type region in the nonstatic branch. The singularity satisfies Królak's strong-curvature criterion and is Tipler-strong along non-radial geodesics in the spacelike branch. For vanishing central mass, the Ricci scalar and the square root of the Kretschmann scalar are locally integrable with respect to the invariant spacetime volume, despite diverging at the origin. A power-law generalization of the nonlocal closure yields solutions expressed through incomplete gamma functions, interpolating between the singular core and a regular black hole with a de~Sitter core, without invoking nonlinear electrodynamics. We obtain closed-form surface gravities, an extended first-law identity and a Smarr relation, and locate the heat-capacity divergence using the lower branch of the Lambert function. Finally, thermodynamic topology distinguishes a Schwarzschild-like regime with one defect from an extremal regime with two opposite-winding defects that merge at the maximum Hawking temperature.

</details>


### [230] [Hyperboloidal Initial Data with Prescribed Asymptotic Charges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33266)
*Károly Csukás,János Östör,István Rácz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 提出一种在求解爱因斯坦约束方程之前即可直接解析控制渐近荷的双曲型初值构造新方法，并用Fuchsian-inspired谱求解器以转动物理黑洞为例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上渐近荷（如Bondi能量、角动量）只能在约束方程求解完成后由数值解反推，缺乏先验控制，难以针对目标渐近时空性质构造初值。

Method: 采用约束方程的抛物-双曲形式（parabolic-hyperbolic formulation），配合受Fuchsian分析启发的谱求解器；将决定无限塔渐近荷的渐近自由度作为输入，用以规定解的渐近行为，再求解约束方程。

Result: 作为概念验证，以M=1非转动Schwarzschild时空双曲面诱导的种子数据为起点，显式构造出Bondi能量E_B=2、角动量J_B=1/2的转动黑洞双曲型初值。

Conclusion: 渐近荷可在约束求解前被直接解析设定，验证了该方法的可行性，为按目标渐近性质定制初值提供了新途径。

Abstract: A novel method is introduced for constructing hyperboloidal initial data with direct analytic control over asymptotic charges prior to solving the Einstein constraint equations. Using the parabolic-hyperbolic formulation of the constraints together with a Fuchsian-analysis-inspired spectral solver, the asymptotic degrees of freedom, which determine infinite towers of asymptotic charges, including the Bondi energy and angular momentum, are used to prescribe the asymptotic behavior of solutions. As a proof of concept, we explicitly construct hyperboloidal initial data for a spinning black hole with Bondi energy $E_B = 2$ and angular momentum $J_B = 1/2$, starting from seed data induced on a hyperboloidal slice of the non-rotating Schwarzschild spacetime with mass $M=1$.

</details>


### [231] [Impact of nonlinear electrodynamics on quasinormal modes of rotating black strings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33309)
*F. S. Mirekhtiary,I. Sakalli*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究了由负指数幂-Maxwell场源生的旋转反de Sitter黑洞弦的无质量标量扰动。由于旋转相当于静态弦的局域boost，旋转谱可由静态谱在频率依赖动量下精确得到；用Horowitz-Hubeny级数、Chebyshev配点法和时域演化（各两套独立代码）计算，并检验了平面Schwarzschild-AdS极限。电荷使最阻尼模式对在约化电荷 q̂_c=0.72626 处于虚轴上合并，形成例外点；大横动量下模式距光锥的距离按 k^{1/(1-4s)} 增长，取代中性膜的 k^{-1/5} 律；旋转把例外点延拓为电荷-旋转平面中的一条线，并产生纯阻尼轴对称模序列，且不出现超辐射增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前用于该几何的近视界估计遗漏了非中性电荷带来的模式合并（例外点）、大动量标度律改变以及旋转引入的额外谱结构，因此需要一套完整、可靠且经多重数值交叉验证的准正规模谱计算来厘清幂-Maxwell电荷与旋转对标量扰动谱的影响。

Method: 采用三种数值方法：Horowitz-Hubeny级数展开、Chebyshev配点法以及时域演化，每种方法均由两套独立代码实现，并以平面Schwarzschild-AdS极限作为校验。利用旋转为静态弦局域boost的性质，将静态谱在频率依赖的有效动量下解析延拓得到旋转谱；度规函数中电荷项为 -Qr^p，p=2/(1-2s)，s=-1/2 时退化为线性项并给出代数视界。

Result: 在约化电荷 q̂_c=0.72626 处最轻阻尼模式对实部归零、在虚轴上合并（例外点），超过该电荷后弦以无振荡方式衰减；在分支端点谱为精确超几何形式，可解析表达。大横动量下模式距光锥距离按 k^{1/(1-4s)} 增长，取代中性膜的 k^{-1/5} 律。旋转将例外点延拓为电荷-旋转参数平面中的一条线，并额外产生一族纯阻尼轴对称模式，且始终未观察到超辐射增长。

Conclusion: 幂-Maxwell电荷不仅改变阻尼率，还能定性改变谱结构（模式合并、标度律改变、解析可解极限），旋转则把这些结构推广为参数空间中的一维曲线而不引发不稳定。三种独立数值方法的一致结果确认了这些结论，并表明早先的近视界估计完全遗漏了上述效应。

Abstract: We study massless scalar perturbations of rotating anti-de Sitter black strings sourced by a power-Maxwell field with negative exponent $s$. The charge enters the metric function as $-Qr^{p}$ with $p=2/(1-2s)$, and the case $s=-1/2$ gives a linear term with an algebraic horizon. Since rotation is a local boost of the static string, the rotating spectrum follows exactly from the static one evaluated at a frequency-dependent momentum. We compute the spectrum with the Horowitz-Hubeny series, Chebyshev collocation and time-domain evolution, each implemented in two independent codes and checked against the planar Schwarzschild-AdS limit. The charge acts in two ways. At the reduced charge $\hat q_c=0.72626$ the least damped pair of modes meets on the imaginary axis, so more strongly charged strings ring down without oscillation, and at the endpoint of the branch the spectrum is exactly hypergeometric. At large transverse momentum the distance of the modes from the light cone grows as $k^{1/(1-4s)}$, replacing the $k^{-1/5}$ law of the neutral brane. Rotation continues the exceptional point into a line in the plane of charge and rotation, adds a purely damped axisymmetric tower, and never produces superradiant growth. A near-horizon estimate used earlier for this geometry misses all of these effects.

</details>


### [232] [Neutron Star--Primordial Black Hole Binaries: Dynamical Tides, Resonance Locking, and Gravitational Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33314)
*Isaac Legred,Nicolás Yunes*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 中子星与低质量原初黑洞双星的轨道演化可因动力学潮汐而显著偏离常规致密双星：当星体振荡模式与轨道保持共振锁定时，引力波辐射会中介加速旋近或反向旋出，并可能在脉冲星计时或引力波中留下可观测特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 典型致密双星（中子星—原初黑洞）的质量比极端，其轨道演化是否仍遵循经典引力波衰减图像尚不清楚；太阳系巨行星—卫星系统中已知的动力学潮汐效应提示此类体系中可能存在未被考虑的共振耦合，值得系统研究。

Method: 以解析/理论建模为主：考虑中子星的g模（或f模）振荡与轨道运动之间的共振锁定条件，在准静态共振演化近似下，结合引力波辐射反作用计算轨道半长轴与偏心率的演化方程，并区分共振锁定成立与否两种情形，给出加速旋近与反向旋出的判别条件。摘要未给出具体数值模拟细节。

Result: 表明在共振锁定条件下，动力学潮汐可显著改变轨道演化速率，甚至使轨道膨胀（outspiral）取代旋近；引力波辐射作为能量与角动量耗散机制主导共振锁定期间的演化路径。摘要层面未报告定量数值结果。

Conclusion: 动力学潮汐共振锁定是极端质量比致密双星轨道演化中不可忽略的物理通道，其效应有望通过脉冲星计时残差或引力波波形（相位漂移、轨道频率演化异常）加以检验，为搜寻低质量原初黑洞提供新的观测窗口。

Abstract: The orbital evolution of a neutron star and a much lower-mass primordial black hole can differ strikingly from that of typical compact binaries. Dynamical tides, analogous to those in the Solar System's giant planet-moon systems, can accelerate inspiral or induce outspiral, depending on the orbital configuration. We show how gravitational-wave emission mediates this evolution when neutron star modes remain locked in resonance with the orbit. These effects may leave observable signatures in pulsar timing or gravitational waves.

</details>


### [233] [Constraining Ultralight Dark Matter in Dynamical Dark Energy Cosmologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33332)
*Muhammad Awais,Hafiz Inam Ullah,Rafael C. Nunes,Paula S. Ferreira,Tonatiuh Matos*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在动力学暗能量框架下，一致处理标量场暗物质（SFDM）的背景与线性扰动演化，结合Planck、DESI DR2 BAO与Pantheon+联合约束，得到SFDM质量单侧95%下限 log10(mφ/eV) > -24.04，且SFDM不改变动力学暗能量的约束及其低红移phantom穿越证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有SFDM分析多局限于标准ΛCDM框架，作者希望检验在动力学暗能量背景下，用SFDM替代冷暗物质是否仍与观测兼容，以及是否会影响对暗能量演化的推断。

Method: 构建标量场暗物质加动力学暗能量的模型，在背景和线性扰动层面自洽实现；用贝叶斯参数推断，联合Planck宇宙微波背景、DESI DR2重子声学振荡和Pantheon+ Ia型超新星数据约束参数空间。

Result: 理论预言显示，质量越轻，物质涨落的尺度依赖抑制越强，对CMB温度功率谱的修改越显著；质量越重则趋近冷暗物质极限。主联合分析给出SFDM质量的单侧95%下限 log10(mφ/eV) > -24.04。用SFDM替代标准冷暗物质流体，未定性改变对动力学暗能量的约束，也未改变其演化证据（包括低红移phantom穿越）。

Conclusion: SFDM与动力学暗能量可在观测数据下良好共存，且不削弱暗能量动力学演化的观测偏好；因此暗能量演化的证据对暗物质微观性质（在此质量范围内）不敏感。

Abstract: We investigate the cosmological implications of scalar-field dark matter (SFDM) in a dynamical dark energy framework, extending previous analyses that considered SFDM within the standard $Λ$CDM cosmological model. We consistently implement the scenario at both the background and linear perturbation levels. We perform Bayesian parameter inference using Planck CMB measurements, DESI DR2 baryon acoustic oscillation (BAO) data, and Pantheon+ Type Ia supernovae to constrain the model parameter space. Our theoretical predictions show that lighter scalar-field masses produce increasingly strong scale-dependent suppression of matter fluctuations and more pronounced modifications to the CMB temperature power spectrum, whereas heavier masses progressively approach the cold dark matter limit. For the SFDM mass, we obtain a one-sided 95\% lower limit of $\log_{10}(m_φ/{\rm eV})>-24.04$ from our main joint analysis. We find that replacing the standard cold dark matter fluid with SFDM does not qualitatively alter the constraints on dynamical dark energy or the evidence for its evolution, including a phantom crossing at low redshifts.

</details>


### [234] [Accurate pulsar timing array likelihoods: better low-rank covariance for gravitational wave background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33444)
*Heling Deng,Ken D. Olum,Rutger van Haasteren*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 针对脉冲星计时阵中随机引力波背景的秩减Fourier对角协方差模型存在频谱分辨率不足导致参数推断偏置的问题，作者提出通过最大化对真实模型似然实现系综平均的对数似然来构造低秩协方差，使基域协方差对完整模型谱敏感且一般为非对角，在保持相当计算成本的同时提高了似然精度与谱特征恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PTA分析在间隔约0.1 yr^-1的离散频率上以Fourier基建模随机过程，采用由对应频率处功率谱密度决定的对角Fourier域协方差。该秩减建模虽显著加速似然评估并在多数情形下近似良好，但对小于约0.1 yr^-1尺度的谱特征不敏感，可能引入参数推断偏置。

Method: 提出一种低秩协方差的新构造：对从真实模型似然抽取的实现系综，最大化其平均对数似然以优化低频过程的低秩协方差。所得基域协方差矩阵对整体模型谱敏感，且一般为非对角矩阵。

Result: 在两个模拟数据集上，相比传统Fourier对角模型，该方法的似然精度更高，对谱特征的恢复更忠实；基准测试表明其计算成本与传统方法相当。

Conclusion: 通过系综平均对数似然最大化构造的低秩非对角协方差，可克服Fourier对角模型在亚频段尺度谱特征上的盲区，在几乎不增加计算开销的前提下改善PTA似然评估与参数推断的可靠性。

Abstract: In pulsar timing array analyses, the stochastic process describing the gravitational wave background is typically modeled using a Fourier basis at discrete frequencies with an interval $\sim 0.1~\mathrm{yr}^{-1}$, with a diagonal Fourier-domain covariance determined by the power spectral density at the corresponding frequencies. Although this rank-reduced modeling significantly accelerates likelihood evaluation and provides a good approximation in many scenarios, it can lead to biased parameter inference because it is insensitive to spectral features on scales smaller than $\sim 0.1~\mathrm{yr}^{-1}$. We introduce a better construction of the low-rank covariance for low-frequency processes by maximizing the log-likelihood averaged over an ensemble of realizations drawn from the true model likelihood. In this approach, the basis-domain covariance matrix is sensitive to the entire model spectrum and is generally non-diagonal. Using two mock datasets, we demonstrate improved likelihood accuracy and more faithful recovery of spectral features compared to the conventional Fourier-diagonal model. Benchmark tests show that this new model requires a computational cost comparable to that of the conventional approach.

</details>


### [235] [Acoustic Schwarzschild-like black hole and analogous Hawking radiation in Einstein-bumblebee gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33481)
*Chikun Ding,Yuebing Zhou,Yu Shi,Yuehua Xiao,Jiliang Jing*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在Einstein-bumblebee理论中用Gross-Pitaevskii理论导出类Schwarzschild声学黑洞，得到其引力视界与内外声学视界，计算霍金温度与哑洞（dumb hole）温度上限T_h/8，并研究标量粒子辐射的灰体因子与能量发射率对调节参数ξ和洛伦兹破坏耦合常数ℓ的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将声学黑洞（模拟引力）与洛伦兹破坏的bumblebee引力理论结合，考察洛伦兹破坏与调节参数如何影响声学视界结构、霍金辐射及相关辐射观测量，为与真实天体物理黑洞观测对比提供可能特征。

Method: 在类Schwarzschild时空背景下，基于Einstein-bumblebee理论结合Gross-Pitaevskii理论构造声学类Schwarzschild黑洞，求解视界位置；计算霍金温度T_h与哑洞温度T_ac；通过标量粒子在哑洞外的辐射计算灰体因子与能量发射率随频率的分布。

Result: 该黑洞具有两类视界：引力视界r_h=2M，以及依赖ξ与ℓ的内、外声学视界（2M<r_ac-≤4M，r_ac≥4M）。T_h仅依赖ℓ，而T_ac同时依赖ℓ与ξ，且存在上限T_acm=T_h/8。低频谱段灰体因子随ξ增大而增大、随ℓ增大而减小，高频时趋于1；能量发射率呈类Planck谱，在峰值频率ω_m处取极大，并同样随ξ增大而增大、随ℓ增大而减小。

Conclusion: 声学视界与温度、灰体因子及能量发射率对ξ与ℓ呈现相反的依赖关系，该特征是本文最具实验价值之处，可直接用于与真实天体物理黑洞的观测结果进行基准检验。

Abstract: We derive an acoustic Schwarzschild-like black hole and study its analogous Hawking radiation in Einstein-bumblebee theory by using Gross-Pitaevskii theory in the background of Schwarzschild-like spacetime. This black hole have two kinds of event horizons: one is the gravitational horizon $r_h=2M$(black hole horizon), the others are the internal and external acoustic horizons $2M<r_{ac-}\leq4M,\;r_{ac}\geq4M$(dumb hole horizon) which depend on the tuning parameter $ξ$ and Lorentz violation coupling constant $\ell$. The corresponding Hawking temperature $T_h$ depends only on $\ell$, however $T_{ac}$ depends on $\ell$ and $ξ$. The dumb hole temperature $T_{ac}$ has a maximum $T_{acm}=T_h/8$. When considering the scalar particles radiating from the dumb hole, we find that in low frequency, the grey-body factor increases with the tuning parameter $ξ$, but decreases with $\ell$. In the high frequency, it approaches to $1$. Its energy emission rate is similar to the Planck's black spectrum where there has a maximum at a given peak frequency $ω_m$. The energy emission rate also increases with the tuning parameter $ξ$, but decreases with $\ell$. This opposite dependence relationship is the most experimentally valuable feature and can directly benchmark against observations of real astrophysical black holes.

</details>


### [236] [Gravitational Waves from Ringing Dyonic Black Holes and Estimation of Parameters in Einstein-Bumblebee-Maxwell Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33569)
*Sikha Gogoi,Umananda Dev Goswami*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Einstein-bumblebee-Maxwell引力中dyonic RN型黑洞在标量与引力扰动下的准正规模频率，用6阶Padé平均WKB法计算，并用时间演化验证，还借助Fisher分析评估LISA对黑洞参数的约束能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lorentz对称性破缺（bumblebee场）框架下的带电黑洞其准正规模谱尚未充分研究；同时需评估未来空间引力波探测器LISA对此类改进引力理论黑洞参数（质量与Lorentz破缺参数）的测量能力。

Method: 在Einstein-bumblebee-Maxwell理论下构建dyonic RN型黑洞背景；对标量和引力扰动导出有效势，采用6阶Padé平均WKB近似计算QNM频率；通过数值时间演化提取ringdown主导模式进行交叉验证；再用Fisher信息矩阵方法针对LISA估计黑洞质量与Lorentz破缺参数的测量精度。

Result: 给出了振荡频率与阻尼率随自由模型参数的变化规律，时间演化结果与WKB谱一致；对质量约1e6–1e8太阳质量、低红移的超大质量黑洞，LISA探测QNM的信噪比较高；对光度距离3 Gpc的代表性源，质量≥1e6太阳质量时探测器参考系中的黑洞质量与Lorentz破缺参数均得到良好约束。

Conclusion: bumblebee Lorentz破缺参数显著影响QNM谱；WKB近似与时间演化互相印证方法的可靠性；LISA有潜力对超大质量dyonic黑洞的质量及Lorentz破缺参数给出有效约束，为检验Lorentz不变性破缺提供引力波观测途径。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the quasinormal mode (QNM) frequencies of dyonic Reissner-Nordström (RN)-like black holes associated with scalar and gravitational perturbations in the framework of Einstein-bumblebee-Maxwell gravity theory. The QNMs are calculated using the 6th-order Padé averaged WKB approximation method. We analyse the dependence of oscillation frequencies and damping rate of the BHs for the free model parameters. To further verify the QNM spectrum, we study the time evolution of both the scalar and gravitational perturbations around the BHs and extract the dominant QNM from the resulting ringdown signal. Moreover, we try to estimate the BH parameters using Fisher analysis method for the future gravitational wave detector named Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA). We found that for supermassive BHs with mass around $10^6$ -- $10^8 M_\odot$ and at lower redshifts, the signal-to-noise ratio for the detection of a QNM signal by the LISA is high. Further, for a representative source at a luminosity distance of $3$ Gpc, the BH mass at the detector reference frame and the Lorentz violating parameter are well constrained for supermassive BHs with mass $\ge 10^6.$

</details>


### [237] [A no-go theorem for closed timelike curves in globally null-foliated spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33706)
*Hongsheng Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出基于单一标量方程（零梯度/零程函方程）的第四类闭合类时曲线判据：若时空存在梯度处处为零的全局函数，则不含闭合类时曲线，且该假设与稳定因果性逻辑独立；据此首次证明Kundt时空与Robinson–Trautman时空在零坐标全局单值区域不含CTC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有CTC判据分三族（时间函数判据、能量条件判据、拓扑判据），且研究方式多为逐个时空分析，缺乏形式初等、不依赖能量条件的统一判据；同时陀螺波等情形下CTC是否存在长期悬而未决。

Method: 引入第四族判据：要求时空存在全局定义的、梯度处处为零（null）的标量函数，即满足零程函方程。证明核心为一个代数事实——零余向量永不与类时向量正交，故该函数沿任意类时曲线严格单调增长，曲线无法闭合。进一步论证该假设与稳定因果性逻辑独立（互不蕴含），并将其应用于Kundt与Robinson–Trautman时空（任意宇宙学常数与旋转一形式），以及分析Misner、Ori、Ahmed构造。

Result: 证明满足全局零梯度的时空不含CTC；该判据可处理经典时间函数定理失效的情形；首次确立：在零坐标全局单值的任意区域，Kundt时空与Robinson–Trautman时空不含CTC，与宇宙学常数、旋转一形式无关，解决了陀螺波遗留问题；并表明Misner、Ori、Ahmed构造实质是紧致零方向转为类时，其中宇宙学常数、膨胀与剪切均为旁观者。

Conclusion: 零程函方程单独即可充当CTC判据，构成独立于能量条件与稳定因果性的新判据族；它给出Kundt与Robinson–Trautman时空的无CTC性（首次），并统一解释了经典构造中各类参数的非实质性作用。

Abstract: The study of closed timelike curves has proceeded one spacetime at a time, and the criteria available form three families, the time function criteria, the energy condition criteria, and the topological criteria. We initiate a fourth family of criteria, elementary in form and resting on a single scalar equation rather than any energy condition. We prove that a spacetime admitting a globally defined function whose gradient is everywhere null cannot contain a closed timelike curve, and that the hypothesis is logically independent of stable causality rather than weaker than it. The proof turns on one algebraic fact, that a null covector is never orthogonal to a timelike vector, so the function grows strictly along every timelike curve and cannot close. Because the null eikonal equation alone decides the criterion, we can use it where the classical time function theorem is silent. We establish for the first time that no Kundt spacetime and no Robinson--Trautman spacetime can contain a closed timelike curve on any region where the null coordinate is globally single valued, whatever the cosmological constant and the rotation one form, which settles a question left open for gyratonic waves. We further show that the Misner, Ori and Ahmed constructions reduce to the statement that a compact null direction has turned timelike, and that the cosmological constant, the expansion and the shear are spectators in all of them.

</details>


### [238] [Forecasting Strongly Lensed Binary Black Hole Rates from the Stochastic Gravitational-Wave Background with TianQin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33751)
*Zheng-Cheng Liang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一个仅用天琴（TianQin）的框架，将毫赫兹随机引力波背景（SGWB）约束的双黑洞（BBH）族群信息传播到地面探测器可探测的强透镜BBH并合事件的预测中。


<details>
