  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫赫兹SGWB可对恒星级双黑洞及其宇宙并合历史提供族群层面的探测；但如何把SGWB的族群约束定量转化为地面探测器强透镜BBH并合事件的预测，尚缺明确联系。

Method: 以天琴一年模拟观测推断BBH族群，将后验传播通过含像级选择的星系尺度透镜模型；区分"至少探测到一个像"的透镜事件与"两像均被探测并成功关联"的透镜系统。

Result: 在系统放大率阈值 μ_sys≥2 下，预测中位事件率 R_event^det=5.50×10⁻⁴ yr⁻¹，关联双像系统率 R_sys^det=2.54×10⁻⁴ yr⁻¹，可探测系统比例中位数 F_sys^det=3.35×10⁻⁴；相较仅用先验的传播，合并的16–84百分位宽度分别收窄6.5%、6.4%和4.0%。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了毫赫兹SGWB推断与地面引力波观测强透镜BBH并合预测之间的定量联系。

Abstract: The millihertz stochastic gravitational-wave background (SGWB) provides a population-level probe of stellar-mass binary black holes (BBHs) and their cosmic merger history. We develop a framework using TianQin alone to propagate SGWB-informed BBH population constraints into forecasts of strongly lensed BBH mergers detectable by ground-based gravitational-wave detectors. The framework distinguishes between lensed events with at least one detected image and lens systems in which both images are detected and successfully associated. Using a one-year mock observation, we infer the BBH population and propagate the resulting posterior through a galaxy-scale lensing model with image-level selection. For the adopted system-magnification threshold, \(μ_{\rm sys}\ge 2\), the median predicted rate of lensed events with at least one detected image is \(R_{\rm event}^{\rm det}=5.50\times10^{-4}\,\mathrm{yr}^{-1}\), while the median rate of associated two-image lens systems is \(R_{\rm sys}^{\rm det}=2.54\times10^{-4}\,\mathrm{yr}^{-1}\); the median detectable system fraction is \(\mathcal{F}_{\rm sys}^{\rm det}=3.35\times10^{-4}\). Compared with the prior-only propagation, the pooled 16th--84th percentile widths decrease by 6.5\% for the event rate, 6.4\% for the system rate, and 4.0\% for the system fraction. Our work establishes a quantitative link between millihertz SGWB inference and forecasts of strongly lensed BBH mergers for ground-based gravitational-wave observations.

</details>


### [239] [Massive Scalar Quasinormal Modes and Quasibound States of Charged Black Holes with Logarithmic corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33883)
*Prosenjit Paul,Rickmoy Samanta*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究对数形变Reissner-Nordström黑洞时空中无质量/有质量中性标量场的准正模与准束缚态，比较多种半解析与数值方法并给出正交性验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程（红外）度规形变如何影响黑洞周围标量场的势垒、振荡、俘获与衰减，尚缺乏系统刻画；需要一个可精确求解、且具物理来源（非线性电动力学、暗物质完美流体）的形变模型来定量研究。

Method: 选取非线性电动力学源出的对数形变RN黑洞精确解（亦见于唯象暗物质完美流体模型）。分析QNM（弛豫）与QBS（小视界吸收的俘获模）；采用一阶WKB、六阶WKB、全局匹配法、推广的Leaver法（适配对数几何）与时域演化进行交叉验证；并用局部Padé近似与Gamow隧穿作解析估计；最后在视界正则化与共同复径向围道下，用相对论双线性框架检验独立计算的QNM-QBS对的正交性。

Result: 小标量质量使势垒外移、升高并降低其曲率；一阶WKB给出在固定多极数与泛音下阻尼减弱。各方法在其有效范围内QNM估计一致，全局匹配与Leaver对QBS也高度吻合。以参考俘获态为基准，局部Padé的束缚能误差约0.04%，而领头Gamow隧穿低估阻尼约25%。增大标量质量或非线性耦合会增强束缚，但因视界吸收增加而缩短寿命。视界正则化加共同复围道可证明独立计算的QNM-QBS对正交。

Conclusion: 对数形变在红外层面显著改变标量场谱结构：势垒与阻尼、俘获与吸收之间存在可控的竞争关系；多方法一致性保证了数值可靠性，正交性结果为该形变时空中QNM/QBS的谱分解与微扰展开提供了自洽基础。

Abstract: We study how long-range deformations in a black-hole metric affect the oscillation, trapping, and decay of a neutral, minimally coupled massive scalar field. We choose a logarithmic deformation of Reissner-Nordström (RN) sourced by nonlinear electrodynamics. It provides an exact solution of Einstein gravity and admits a finite completion. The same geometry also arises in a phenomenological perfect-fluid dark-matter model. In this setup, we analyze both quasinormal modes (QNMs), which describe relaxation, and quasibound states (QBSs), which describe trapped modes with small horizon absorption. We first show that a small scalar mass moves the potential barrier outward, raises it, and reduces its curvature. First-order WKB then predicts weaker damping at every fixed finite multipole and overtone. We then compare several approaches, namely sixth-order WKB, global matching, a generalized Leaver method (adapted to the logarithmic geometry) and time-domain evolution. All methods yield consistent QNM estimates within their ranges of validity. Global matching and Leaver also agree closely for QBSs. Further analytic estimates with a reference trapped state show that a local Padé approximation gives a binding-energy error of about $0.04\%$, while leading Gamow tunnelling underestimates damping by about $25\%$. Across the sampled QBSs, increasing the scalar mass or nonlinear coupling strengthens binding but shortens the lifetime through greater horizon absorption. Finally, using an appropriate horizon regularization and a common complex radial contour, we demonstrate orthogonality of an independently computed QNM-QBS pair using a suitable relativistic bilinear framework.

</details>


### [240] [Gravitational Radiation Coupled to a real massive scalar Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33928)
*Yue Ma,Xianghao Wu,Weidong Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究波规范下含正质量实标量场的 Einstein–Klein–Gordon 系统的小数据整体解渐近行为，证明度规扰动线性化迹分量的 $\hat t^{-1}\simeq t^{-1}$ 最优衰减率，并建立了定量单点可观测性估计：初始 Klein–Gordon 能量由该首阶系数控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画非线性波动系统的晚期渐近行为与最优衰减率，并把"无穷远/单点处的辐射信息"与初始能量定量联系起来，从而为可观测性与逆问题提供依据。

Method: 在波规范（wave gauge）下处理含正质量实标量场的 Einstein–Klein–Gordon 系统；对小数据整体解分析度规扰动的线性化部分，提取其迹分量的一阶渐近展开，并结合加权/精确衰减估计与能量方法导出首阶系数与初始 Klein–Gordon 能量之间的定量不等式。

Result: 度规扰动线性化迹分量满足 $\hat t^{-1}\simeq t^{-1}$ 的最优衰减率；同时得到一个定量单点可观测性估计，即初始 Klein–Gordon 能量可由该首阶（时间反演型）系数加以控制。

Conclusion: 正质量项与波规范下的非线性耦合仍使解具有与线性理论一致的最优衰减结构；首阶辐射系数携带足以控制初始能量的信息，可用于稳定性与可观测性/逆问题分析。

Abstract: We study the asymptotic behavior of small-data global solutions to the Einstein--Klein--Gordon system with a positive-mass real scalar field in wave gauge. We show that the linearized trace component of the metric perturbation admits a $\hat t^{-1}\simeq t^{-1}$ optimal decreasing rate. Moreover, a quantitative one-point observability estimate is established: the initial Klein-Gordon energy is controlled by this leading order coefficient.

</details>


### [241] [Comment on "Effect of perfect fluid dark matter..." [Phys. Dark Univ. 34 (2021) 100891]](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34019)
*Ming-Hsun Li,Kwei-Chou Yang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该Comment澄清Shaymatov等人所用黑洞度规的物理内涵：该度规源自Li与Yang(2012)，实为冷暗物质与phantom场双组分体系，不应被当作单一"完美流体暗物质"(PFDM)处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shaymatov等人在Phys. Dark Univ. 34 (2021) 100891中把Li-Yang度规误读为单组分PFDM（总状态方程w_total=-1），且未区分原模型参数区间，可能导致极端天体物理应用中的误用，故需澄清。

Method: 通过理论辨析：追溯度规出处，比较其原始双组分（冷暗物质+phantom场）构造与"单一PFDM且w_total=-1"解读的物理一致性，并梳理原模型的参数分区。

Result: 指出若按单组分PFDM、总状态方程w_total=-1处理，该几何与暗物质晕所需的引力吸引性质不相容；同时明确原模型的参数适用范围。

Conclusion: 应将该度规理解为冷暗物质与phantom场共存的双组分系统，并在正确参数区间内使用，以避免在极端天体物理场景中的误用。

Abstract: This Comment clarifies the physical interpretation of the black hole spacetime metric employed by Shaymatov \textit{et al.} [Phys. Dark Univ. 34 (2021) 100891]. We point out that the metric, originally derived in Li and Yang (2012), describes a specific dual-component system consisting of cold dark matter and a phantom field background. Treating this geometry as a single-component ``Perfect Fluid Dark Matter'' (PFDM) with a total equation of state $w_{\mathrm{total}} = -1$ is physically incompatible with the gravitational attraction required for dark matter halos. Furthermore, we clarify the distinct parameter regimes of the original model to prevent potential misapplications in extreme astrophysical scenarios.

</details>


### [242] [Repetitive Magnetic Penrose Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34164)
*Ke Wang,Xin-Yun Hu,Xiao-Xiong Zeng*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文将Ruffini等人提出的非线性迭代重复Penrose过程推广到磁Penrose过程（Kerr度规＋Wald磁场、电磁场与电荷作试验场处理），指出最优能量提取条件下的核心机制为"充电反馈"：黑洞电荷单调累积至约半个Wald电荷，单极电压随Q线性下降并过零变号，磁增益被逐渐抵消，重复磁Penrose过程被迫终止并退化为力学Penrose过程，从而排除了单次磁Penrose过程所预言的>100%能量提取效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单次磁Penrose过程在理论上可给出超过100%的能量提取效率，但缺乏对多次/迭代过程自洽性的考察；同时在非线性重复框架下，外场与黑洞电荷的反馈效应此前未被纳入，需检验高效率结论是否在实际迭代中得以维持。

Method: 以Kerr时空为背景，采用Wald解描述外部磁场，将电磁场视为试验场、电荷视为试验电荷；在赤道面上求解最大能量提取的最优条件；构建迭代重复的磁Penrose过程，追踪带电碎片落入导致的黑洞电荷演化、Wald解单极电压随Q的变化，以及母粒子衰变中的电荷守恒约束。

Result: 最优条件下迭代过程的核心机制为充电反馈；充电后黑洞电荷约为Wald电荷的一半；中性母粒子衰变时电荷守恒迫使落入碎片带电、另一碎片带相反电荷，使黑洞电荷单调累积；Wald解单极电压随Q线性减小并在某点过零反号，磁增益被电荷逐渐中和。

Conclusion: 重复磁Penrose过程因充电反馈导致的磁增益衰减而被迫终止，最终回落为纯力学Penrose过程，因此单次磁Penrose过程所预言的>100%能量提取效率在迭代框架下不会实现。

Abstract: The nonlinear iterative repetitive Penrose process established recently by Ruffini et al.\ is generalized to the magnetic Penrose process. The spacetime background is the Kerr metric, the external magnetic field is described by the Wald solution, the electromagnetic field is treated as a test field, and the charge is treated as a test charge. We analyze the optimal conditions for maximum energy extraction on the equatorial plane and find that under these conditions the core mechanism of the iterative process is charging feedback. After charging, the black hole charge is approximately half of the Wald charge. When the neutral parent particle decays, charge conservation forces the infalling fragment to carry charge while the other fragment carries the opposite charge, so the black hole charge accumulates monotonically. The monopole voltage of the Wald solution decreases linearly with the charge $Q$ and crosses zero to reverse sign at a certain point, so the magnetic gain is gradually neutralized by the charge. The repetitive magnetic Penrose process is therefore forced to terminate and eventually falls back to the mechanical Penrose process. The energy extraction efficiency $>100\%$ predicted by the single magnetic Penrose process will therefore be prevented.

</details>


### [243] [Finite-Distance Imaging of Relativistic Equilibrium Tori around Genuine Kerr--Bertotti--Robinson Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34331)
*Hao-Peng Yan,Xiang-Qian Li,Xiao-Jun Yue*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 结合灰体发射-吸收广义相对论光线追踪，计算了中性Kerr–Bertotti–Robinson黑洞周围静止、压强支撑环面（torus）在有限距离（50M）处的图像，并系统考察外部场、填充度、倾角、自吸收与自旋对亮度、视尺寸及不对称性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往环面成像研究多基于Kerr黑洞与无穷远观测者，缺少对带有外部电磁场（KBR解）的真实黑洞、有限距离成像、以及灰体自吸收与平衡自由面共同作用的定量刻画，需要厘清外部场与源结构演化如何改变可观测图像。

Method: 采用广义相对论光线追踪（含灰体发射与吸收），源区由常比角动量平衡自由面界定；观测者置于r=50M。设置外部场序列、环面填充度序列、观测倾角序列与无量纲自旋0.5→0.95序列进行对比计算。

Result: 沿外部场序列，积分亮度与视尺寸均显著增大；提高填充度时（代表性序列）亮度增至5.9倍而强度加权像尺寸仅增大10%；增大观测倾角增强横向亮度对比；自吸收增强可与更高绝对亮度共存（灰体近似下发射与吸收同尺度）；共同压强中心与填充度下，自旋由0.5增至0.95使逆行序列因平衡收缩而暗化约7倍，顺行亮度仅变化2%。

Conclusion: 平衡边界、流体运动与光学深度共同决定有限距离环面图像的亮度、尺度与不对称性；外部场、填充度、倾角与自旋对亮度与尺寸的作用机制不同且可相互解耦。

Abstract: We calculate finite-distance images of stationary, pressure-supported tori around genuine, electrically neutral Kerr--Bertotti--Robinson black holes using general relativistic ray tracing with gray emission and absorption. The emitting volume is bounded by a constant-specific-angular-momentum equilibrium free surface, and the observer is placed at a radius of $50M$. Along the investigated external-field sequence, integrated brightness and apparent size both increase strongly as the field modifies the equilibrium source and photon propagation. Higher torus filling has a different effect: in a representative sequence, brightness grows by a factor of $5.9$ while the intensity-weighted image size increases by only $10\%$. Increasing viewing inclination enhances lateral brightness contrast. Stronger self-absorption can coexist with higher absolute brightness because emission and absorption scale together in the adopted gray prescription. At common pressure center and filling, increasing the dimensionless spin from $0.5$ to $0.95$ dims the retrograde sequence by a factor of about $7$ as its equilibrium contracts, whereas prograde brightness changes by only $2\%$. These contrasting responses show how the equilibrium boundary, fluid motion, and optical depth jointly shape the brightness, scale, and asymmetry of finite-distance torus images.

</details>


### [244] [Topological analysis of novel charged-AdS black hole solutions embedded in dark matter halos with quintessence field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34334)
*Muhammad Yasir,Hafiza Bushra Mujahid,Faisal Javed,Shahid Qaisar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 构造了同时含NGC 4649型暗物质晕与动态精质场的带电AdS黑洞解，并用Duan-φ-映射拓扑理论与广义自由能方法系统分析其热力学拓扑结构、临界行为与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物质晕与精质场在宇宙学中普遍存在，但其对带电AdS黑洞时空结构与热力学相结构的联合影响尚缺乏统一分析框架。

Method: 从修正爱因斯坦场方程出发，引入电荷、宇宙学常数、NGC 4649型暗物质分布及变状态方程精质场，解析构造黑洞解；通过度规函数与视界结构分析时空几何；采用Duan-φ-映射拓扑理论结合广义自由能方法研究热力学拓扑、临界性与稳定性；并考察ω_q相对参考值的分数偏移效应。

Result: 暗物质晕增强引力吸引；精质场提供排斥贡献并显著改变时空渐近行为；ω_q的变化系统性地改变相结构与临界行为，体现精质场的关键作用。

Conclusion: 暗物质晕与精质场对带电AdS黑洞的几何与热力学性质具有竞争性影响，拓扑方法与广义自由能框架可一致刻画其临界性与稳定性，ω_q是调控相结构的重要参数。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the properties of a charged anti-de Sitter (AdS) black hole in the presence of a NGC 4649-type dark matter halo and a surrounding quintessence field. Starting from the modified Einstein field equations (EFEs), we construct the corresponding black hole solutions by incorporating the effects of electric charge, cosmological constant, dark matter distribution, and quintessence characterized by a variable equation of state. We analyze the resulting spacetime geometry through the metric function and horizon structure, showing that the dark matter halo strengthens the gravitational attraction, whereas the quintessence field provides a repulsive contribution that substantially alters the asymptotic behavior of the spacetime. Furthermore, the thermodynamic properties are investigated by applying the Duan-\(φ\)-mapping topological theory together with the generalized free energy method. This framework allows us to examine the topological structure, critical behavior, and stability conditions of the black hole system in a consistent manner. We further study the fractional shifts relative to the reference value of \(ω_q\) and observe that variations in \(ω_q\) produce systematic changes which highlight the important role of the quintessence field in determining the phase structure and critical behavior of the black hole system.

</details>


### [245] [Relativistic diffusing particles, entropy and varying cosmological constants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34341)
*Z. Haba*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 论文讨论具有非守恒能动张量的Einstein方程，将方程右端记为 $8\pi G T^{\mu\nu}-g^{\mu\nu}\Lambda+\Sigma^{\mu\nu}$，其中 $\Sigma^{\mu\nu}$ 描述粘性与热流，并引入随时空变化的牛顿"常数" $G$ 与宇宙学项 $\Lambda$。协变守恒律把能动张量的非守恒与 $G$、$\Lambda$ 的变化联系起来，且 $\nabla_\mu T^{\mu\nu}$ 可由温度 $T$ 与熵 $S$ 表出（联系热力学第一定律）。非守恒源于构成粒子的扩散；文中研究均匀各向同性时空中扩散大质量粒子气体，其线性重子型状态方程参数 $0<w_t\le 1/3$，长时间极限下趋于无质量扩散粒子气体，并给出空间平坦膨胀宇宙中无质量相对论粒子可解模型的能量密度、熵与相空间分布结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是超越标准广义相对论中能动张量严格守恒的假设，考察能动张量非守恒（由粒子扩散引起）与基本"常数" $G$、$\Lambda$ 随时间空变化之间的内在一致性，并借助热力学第一定律为这种非守恒给出物理解释，进而构造可解模型以定量描述扩散粒子气体在膨胀宇宙中的演化。

Method: 方法上，把Einstein方程右端分解为理想流体项 $8\pi G T^{\mu\nu}$、宇宙学项 $-g^{\mu\nu}\Lambda$ 与粘性/热流项 $\Sigma^{\mu\nu}$；要求总能动张量 $\tilde T^{\mu\nu}$ 协变守恒，由此导出 $T^{\mu\nu}$ 的非守恒与 $G$、$\Lambda$ 变化之间的关系；将 $\nabla_\mu T^{\mu\nu}$ 与热力学第一定律联系，用温度 $T$ 和熵 $S$ 表达；在均匀各向同性（FRW）时空中建立扩散大质量粒子气体模型，采用随时间变化的线性重子型状态方程 $P_t=w_t\rho_t$（$0<w_t\le1/3$），并对无质量相对论粒子构造可解模型求解。

Result: 主要结果包括：给出协变守恒约束下能动张量非守恒与 $G$、$\Lambda$ 变化的定量关系，并将 $\nabla_\mu T^{\mu\nu}$ 用温度与熵表述；证明膨胀宇宙中扩散大质量粒子气体在无穷长时间极限下演化为扩散无质量粒子气体；在空间平坦膨胀宇宙的无质量相对论粒子可解模型中，得到能量密度、熵与相空间分布的具体结果。

Conclusion: 结论是：能动张量的非守恒可与时变的 $G$、$\Lambda$ 相容，且可由粒子扩散及热力学第一定律自然解释；扩散大质量粒子气体随宇宙膨胀渐近趋于无质量极限，该框架为描述含粘性、热流与扩散的非守恒宇宙流体提供了自洽可解的理论模型。

Abstract: We discuss Einstein equations with a non-conserved energy-momentum $T^{μν}$. The energy-momentum tensor $\tilde{T}^{μν}$ on the right hand side of Einstein equations is expressed in the form $8πGT^{μν}-g^{μν}Λ+Σ^{μν}$ where $G$ is the Newton constant, $Λ$ is the cosmological term and $T^{μν}$ is the energy-momentum of an ideal fluid.
  The remaining part $Σ^{μν}$ describes the viscosity and a heat flow.
  We discuss space-time-dependent Newton ``constant" $G$ and the cosmological term $Λ$. The covariant conservation law for $\tilde{T}^{μν}$ imposes a relation between the energy-momentum (non)conservation and the variation of $G$ and $Λ$. The covariant derivative $\nabla_μT^{μν}$ can be related to the first law of thermodynamics and expressed by the temperature $T$ and the entropy $S$. The energy-momentum non-conservation in our models is a consequence of a diffusion of its constituting particles.
  We describe a gas of diffusing massive particles in a homogeneous isotropic space-time as a system with a time-dependent linear barotropic equation of state
  $P_{t}=w_{t}ρ_{t}$ with $0<w_{t}\leq \frac{1}{3}$.
  The gas of massive diffusing particles in an expanding universe tends in a limit of infinite time to the gas of diffusing massless particles.
  We obtain results on the energy density, entropy and phase space dstribution
  in a soluble model of massless relativistic particles in a spatially flat expanding universe.

</details>


### [246] [Geometric criticality and regular quasibound spectra of axial Proca fields in Schwarzschild spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34528)
*Antonio Sousa Ribeiro,Francisco Artur Pinheiro Alves Junior*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文研究Schwarzschild时空中奇宇称Proca势的局部几何临界点与整体准束缚谱的关系：证明了该势的临界点恰与ISCO（r=6M）重合，并导出精确的折点标度律，但连续分数数值计算表明谱并未在该处发生简并，从而明确区分了"势的几何临界性"与"谱简并"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作常将有效势的局部极值合并（势临界性）直接等同于准正规模/准束缚态谱的定性转变或简并。作者希望检验这一直觉在奇宇称Proca场（有质量矢量场）中是否成立，厘清"几何势临界"与"谱退化"是否为同一现象。

Method: 将轴向势 V_ax=(1-2M/r)[ℓ(ℓ+1)/r²+μ²] 按 μ² 归一化，证明其径向形式与类时测地线有效势一致，其外极小与内极大在 r=6M（ISCO）处合并，临界条件为 (Mμ_c)²=ℓ(ℓ+1)/12；引入 δ=1-(Mμ)²/(Mμ_c)²，解析推导折点标度：驻点间距∝δ^{1/2}、势垒—势阱深度∝δ^{3/2}、曲率幅度∝δ^{1/2}。对 ℓ=1,2,3 的前三个轴向准束缚支用连分式法求频率，并用Riccati打靶法交叉校验；指出固定半径下径向算符对 δ 呈仿射依赖，从而给出条件性的单根解析解释。

Result: 精确折点标度律成立；连分式与打靶法结果一致，频率在阈值两侧平滑连续，近临界行为近似线性；谱在几何临界点处不出现简并或突变。

Conclusion: 主要结论是：对考察的各支，几何势的临界性与谱简并是两回事——局部势的拓扑变化（驻点合并）不足以决定整体准束缚谱的退化行为；势临界性只提供条件性的解析框架而非谱判据。

Abstract: We investigate how a local geometric critical point of the odd-parity Proca potential relates to the global quasibound spectrum in Schwarzschild spacetime. After normalization by $μ^2$, the axial potential $V_{\rm ax}=(1-2M/r)[\ell(\ell+1)/r^2+μ^2]$ has the same radial form as the timelike-geodesic effective potential. Its outer minimum and inner maximum merge at $r=6M$, the Schwarzschild innermost stable circular orbit, when $(Mμ_c)^2=\ell(\ell+1)/12$. With $δ=1-(Mμ)^2/(Mμ_c)^2$, we derive exact fold scalings: stationary-point separation $\proptoδ^{1/2}$, barrier--well depth $\proptoδ^{3/2}$, and curvature magnitudes $\proptoδ^{1/2}$. Continued-fraction calculations for the first three axial quasibound branches with $\ell=1,2,3$, cross-checked for selected modes by independent Riccati shooting, show regular frequency continuation across the threshold and approximately linear near-critical behavior. At fixed radius the radial operator is affine in $δ$, supporting a conditional simple-root analytic interpretation. The main result is the explicit distinction between geometric potential criticality and spectral degeneracy for the branches examined.

</details>


### [247] [Existence of static axisymmetric solutions to the Einstein equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34555)
*Philippe G. LeFloch,Luc Rozoy*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构造了在圆作用下不变、满足正宇宙常数静态爱因斯坦方程的平滑黎曼度规与标量势，底流形为挖去两个不相交开球的三维球体；势在内部为正、在三个非退化视界处为零，两个内视界面面积由两个小且可比的黑洞质量参数控制，视线半径线性随质量增长、参考中心间距按总质量的立方根缩放，而宇宙学尺度保持不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究正宇宙常数下多黑洞静态解的存在性与刚性：希望在宇宙学视界包围的紧致区域中构造双黑洞（三个视界）的静态解，并刻画其几何尺度（视界半径与间距）如何依赖于黑洞质量，同时建立由单个边界分量的内禀度规决定整体解的唯一性。

Method: 在加权Hölder空间中同时控制线性化方程与非线性余项：将Kottler视界环与de Sitter空间拼接构造近似解并估计其误差，使压缩映射论证闭合；求解带两个障碍张量的约化椭圆爱因斯坦方程，通过调整两个轴向位置消去障碍张量的系数，并用收缩的Bianchi恒等式与辅助Killing向量场的能量估计使投影为零。

Result: 得到一族正宇宙常数下的静态解，其势在内部为正、在由三个非退化视界组成的边界上为零；两个内视界面面积由两个充分小且可比的质量参数任意控制，内视界半径与各自质量线性相关，参考中心间距按总质量的三次方根缩放，两种尺度以不同速率趋于零而宇宙学长度固定。

Conclusion: 对固定宇宙常数，两个边界分量之一的内禀度规即足以确定内部单连通、边界梯度非零的静态解，且该解在等距意义下、并允许势乘以正常数的情况下唯一。

Abstract: We construct smooth Riemannian metrics and scalar potentials that are invariant under a circle action and satisfy the static Einstein equations with positive cosmological constant. The underlying compact three-manifold is a ball with two disjoint open balls removed. The potential (or lapse function) is positive in the interior and vanishes at the boundary, consisting of three non-degenerate horizons. The areas of the two inner boundary components are controlled by two arbitrary parameters, namely the masses of the black holes, which are taken to be sufficiently small and comparable. Each inner horizon radius scales linearly with the individual mass parameters, while the separation of their reference centers scales as the cube root of the total mass. These two scales are assumed to be sufficiently small at different rates, whereas the cosmological length remains fixed. The main analytical challenge is to control the linearized equation and the nonlinear remainder simultaneously near the shrinking horizons, between the centers and in the outer region. Weighted Hölder estimates quantify the growth of the inverse and show that the error of an approximation joining Kottler collars to de Sitter space is small enough to close a contraction argument. Namely, we solve a reduced elliptic Einstein equation that is endowed with two obstruction tensors; varying the two axial positions removes their coefficients. The contracted Bianchi identity and an energy estimate for an auxiliary vector field (Killing field) introduced by the projection to vanish. Finally, for a fixed cosmological constant, the intrinsic metric of one of the two boundary components is sufficient to determine a static solution with simply connected interior and non-vanishing boundary gradient, up to isometry and multiplication of the potential by a positive constant.

</details>


### [248] [Exact spinning wormholes in five dimensions from Toda integrability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34672)
*Masato Nozawa*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构造了五维Einstein-phantom-scalar系统的精确解，描述连接两个渐近平直区域的等角动量旋转虫洞，并利用可积Toda结构证明其正则性、无奇点性、喉的唯一性及角动量上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ellis-Bronnikov虫洞是非旋转的经典精确解，而旋转虫洞的精确构造及其性质（正则性、喉的唯一性、角动量限制）在解析层面尚不清楚，因此需要构造一个可解析处理的旋转推广。

Method: 在cohomogeneity-one拟设下，将径向场方程约化为可积的 \(A_1\oplus A_2\) Toda系统，由此得到闭式的一般局部解；再利用Toda结构解析地施加正则性与渐近平直条件，筛出由质量、等角动量和标量荷组成的三参数解族。

Result: 所得时空测地完备、无视界、稳定因果且无曲率奇点；Toda结构进一步用于证明虫洞喉的唯一性，并在固定喉面积下给出角动量的尖锐上界；同时证明在某一渐近端静止、另一端相对旋转的参考系中存在唯一ergosurface；在适当标度极限下标量场消失，解趋于等角动量极端Myers-Perry黑洞的外部区域。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了旋转Ellis-Bronnikov虫洞的可积精确构造，并借助Toda系统揭示了其全局性质与参数约束，将旋转虫洞与极端Myers-Perry黑洞的外部区域联系起来。

Abstract: We construct a new exact solution to the five-dimensional Einstein-phantom-scalar system that describes a rotating wormhole connecting two asymptotically flat regions. The solution represents a rotating generalization of the Ellis-Bronnikov wormhole with equal angular momenta. Under the cohomogeneity-one ansatz, we demonstrate that the radial equations reduce to an integrable $A_1\oplus A_2$ Toda system, from which the general local solution can be obtained in closed form. This integrable structure enables us to implement the regularity and asymptotic-flatness conditions analytically, thereby singling out a three-parameter family of solutions characterized by the mass, the equal angular momenta, and the scalar charge. The resulting spacetimes are geodesically complete, horizonless and stably causal, and contain no curvature singularities. The Toda structure further allows us to prove the uniqueness of the wormhole throat and to derive a sharp upper bound on the angular momentum for a fixed throat area. We also establish the existence and uniqueness of an ergosurface with respect to the stationary frame that is nonrotating at one asymptotic end, while the opposite end rotates relative to this frame. In a suitable scaling limit, the scalar field vanishes and the solution approaches the exterior region of the extremal Myers-Perry black hole with equal angular momenta.

</details>


### [249] [The $C^0$-inextendibility of Kerr spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35012)
*Bobby Eka Gunara*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文证明了本文所考察的全 Kerr 实现及其所有连通覆盖时空的 $C^0$-不可延拓性，覆盖正质量、非零自转的次极端、极端与超极端情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kerr 时空在奇异环处的可延拓性问题长期缺乏统一处理：既有结果多依赖特定因果结构或全局假设，且对极端/超极端情形及覆盖时空适用性不明。作者希望给出不依赖全局因果假设、在共形与因果锥放宽下仍稳定的 $C^0$-不可延拓性判据。

Method: 以固定端点因果同伦类中固有时间无界作为奇异环处的障碍；证明该障碍在任意正连续共形重标度下以及 Kerr 因果锥连续放宽下保持不变，无需全局因果性假设；并在固定坐标范数下对稳态与轴对称扰动给出尖锐估计，同时构造在环附近遭遇任意大曲率的完备类时测地线。

Result: 得到正质量、非零自转下 subextremal/extremal/overextremal 三区间统一的 $C^0$-不可延拓性结论，适用于全 Kerr 实现及所有连通覆盖时空；给出稳态与轴对称扰动的坐标范数尖锐界，并给出环附近曲率无界的完备类时测地线。

Conclusion: 奇异环处"固定端点因果同伦类中固有时间无界"是不依赖全局因果假设的稳健 $C^0$ 障碍，对共形重标度与因果锥放宽均具稳定性，从而为 Kerr 族（含极端与超极端）的不可延拓性提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We prove the $C^0$-inextendibility of the full Kerr realizations considered in this paper and all their connected covering spacetimes. The result holds for positive mass and nonzero rotation in the subextremal, extremal, and overextremal regimes. The obstruction at the singular ring is expressed by unbounded proper time in a fixed-endpoint causal homotopy class. It persists under every positive continuous conformal rescaling and under continuous widening of the Kerr causal cones, without any global causality assumption. We give sharp bounds in a fixed coordinate norm for stationary and axisymmetric perturbations and describe complete timelike geodesics that encounter arbitrarily large curvature near the ring.

</details>


### [250] [Quasinormal modes of thick branes in $f(R,T)$ gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35096)
*Yu-Peng E,Hai-Long Jia,Chun-Chun Zhu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究了 $f(R,T)=R+\alpha T$ 引力中厚膜的张量扰动与准正规模，导出薛定谔型方程与双势垒有效势，共振峰与准正规模实部吻合，$b$ 与 $\alpha$ 分别控制势垒宽度与高度，$f(R,T)$ 修正显著改变势与谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究修改引力（$f(R,T)$ 修正）在膜世界扰动中的可观测印记，检验厚膜背景下的张量扰动与准正规模是否携带修改引力的特征。

Method: 从厚膜背景解出发，扰动度规导出张量扰动的薛定谔型方程并构造有效势；分析透射谱并与共振峰对照；采用时域演化与直接积分两种方法提取准正规模。

Result: 有效势呈双势垒结构，其性质依赖模型参数；$b$ 主要控制势垒宽度、$\alpha$ 主要控制势垒高度；透射谱共振峰与准正规模实部良好吻合；两种方法对低阶模式结果一致，高阶模式因阻尼增强与数值污染难以提取。

Conclusion: $f(R,T)$ 修正显著影响有效势与准正规模谱，可视为修改引力在膜世界扰动中的潜在印记。

Abstract: We study tensor perturbations and quasinormal modes (QNMs) of thick branes in $f(R,T)=R+αT$ gravity. Starting from the background thick brane solutions, we derive the Schrödinger-like equation for gravitational perturbations and construct the corresponding effective potential. The effective potential exhibits a double-barrier structure whose properties depend on the model parameters. We analyze transmission spectra and find good agreement between resonance peaks and the real parts of the quasinormal frequencies. The parameters $b$ and $α$ mainly control the width and height of the potential barriers, respectively. QNMs are extracted using both time-domain evolution and direct integration methods, showing consistent results for low-lying modes. Higher overtones become increasingly difficult to extract due to stronger damping and numerical contamination. Our results indicate that $f(R,T)$ corrections significantly affect both the effective potential and the quasinormal spectrum, providing a possible imprint of modified gravity in braneworld perturbations.

</details>


### [251] [An asymptotically conical regular black hole in four-dimensional nonpolynomial quasitopological gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35248)
*Jia-Zhou Liu,Shao-Wen Wei,Yu-Xiao Liu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在四维非多项式拟拓扑引力中构造了一个渐近锥形正则黑洞：从线性约化函数出发得到渐近锥形Schwarzschild型解，再引入保持渐近锥的非线性形变，获得具有de Sitter核心与有限曲率不变量的精确解，并在Hamiltonian与Wald框架下建立两解的熵及第一定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 渐近锥形黑洞与正则（无奇点）黑洞各有研究价值，但将二者结合、且在四维非多项式拟拓扑引力框架下给出精确解并系统讨论其热力学的工作尚缺乏，本文即针对这一空白。

Method: （1）在四维非多项式拟拓扑引力中取线性约化函数，先得到渐近锥形Schwarzschild型解；（2）引入非线性形变，在保持领头阶渐近锥的同时给出具有de Sitter核心、曲率不变量有限的精确黑洞解；（3）分别在Hamiltonian框架和Wald框架下推导两个解的黑洞热力学，计算熵并检验第一定律。

Result: 正则模型的熵中除常规项外还含有有理项与对数项；两个解均满足黑洞热力学第一定律。

Conclusion: 非线性形变可在保留渐近锥结构的前提下实现带de Sitter核心的正则黑洞，且该类解在Hamiltonian与Wald两种框架下热力学自洽。

Abstract: In this work, we construct an asymptotically conical regular black hole in four-dimensional nonpolynomial quasitopological gravity. Starting from a linear choice of the reduced function, which yields an asymptotically conical Schwarzschild-like solution, we introduce a nonlinear deformation that preserves the leading asymptotic cone while producing an exact black hole solution with a de Sitter core and finite curvature invariants. We further establish the corresponding black hole thermodynamics for both solutions within the Hamiltonian and Wald frameworks. The entropy contains rational and logarithmic terms in the regular model, and both solutions satisfy the first law of black hole thermodynamics.

</details>


### [252] [Bargmann-invariant witness of the graviton commutator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35253)
*Hiroki Matsui*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 论文提出，双物质波干涉仪的Bargmann不变量相位可作为量子引力特征信号；该相位来源于引力子的对易子（辐射反作用），无法被任何“逐支确定”的经典引力场重现。真实引力下该效应比实验能力低约60个数量级，但囚禁离子类比体系可在量级为1处检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有退相干/相干性判据不足以证明引力是量子的：对Wigner正定的引力子态，牛顿相位与引力子态特征泛函均可由经典逐支确定场重现，因此仅靠退相干不能作为量子引力的证据。需要寻找一个与经典引力场本质不相容的可观测量。

Method: 考虑物质与线性化引力的线性耦合，计算物质波干涉的干涉相干因子并将其精确分解为三部分：保守的牛顿相位、引力子态的特征泛函、以及对易子相位。随后分析Wigner正定引力子态下前两项的经典可重现性，并论证Bargmann相位仅隔离出对易子项；给出对易子相位的性质（与引力子态无关、与牛顿相互作用无关、受which-path退相干限制）及其在引力与囚禁离子类比体系中的量级估计。

Result: 干涉相干因子精确因子化为牛顿相位×引力子态特征泛函×对易子相位；Wigner正定态下前两项可经典重现，故退相干本身不能证明引力量子化。Bargmann相位仅提取对易子项，该项与引力子态及牛顿相互作用无关，并受which-path退相干上界约束。数值上，引力的辐射反作用比实验可达低约60个数量级，而囚禁离子类比可在量级为1处检验。

Conclusion: Bargmann不变量相位提供了判别“量子引力 vs. 经典逐支确定引力场”的干净判据，弥补了退相干判据的不足。真实引力效应当前不可观测，但囚禁离子量子模拟可作为原理性验证平台。

Abstract: We identify the phase of a Bargmann invariant of two matter-wave interferometers as a quantum gravity signature that cannot be reproduced by any branchwise-definite classical gravitational field. For matter coupled linearly to linearized gravity, the interferometric coherence factorizes exactly into a conservative Newtonian phase, the characteristic functional of the graviton state, and a commutator phase. For Wigner-positive graviton states, the first two are classically reproducible; therefore, decoherence alone cannot certify that gravity is quantized. The Bargmann phase isolates the commutator, which is independent of the graviton state and of all Newtonian interactions and is bounded by which-path decoherence. For gravity, this radiation-reaction effect is sixty orders of magnitude below experimental reach, but trapped-ion analogs can test it at order unity.

</details>


### [253] [From Specific Heat Bounds to Penrose-like Inequalities: A Unified Thermodynamic Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35264)
*Kai-Peng Lu,H. Lu,Run-Qiu Yang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在明确且具物理动机的假设下，论文提出一个"局部到全局"的热力学机制，统一组织了此前互不关联的若干黑洞不等式（Penrose型不等式），并推广至渐近Schwarzschild-AdS黑洞/膜与全息对偶中的单调性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于黑洞的局部热力学约束与全局几何不等式（如Penrose型不等式）各自独立发展，缺乏统一的结构性联系，作者希望提供一个把二者串联起来的统一视角。

Method: 选取渐近平坦、静态、球对称黑洞为起点，在显式物理假设下构造局部热力学量之间的关系，并整合为全局几何不等式；再推广到渐近Schwarzschild-AdS黑洞与膜；在零能条件（NEC）下直接证明一个核心积分不等式（针对一般静态平面对称渐近Schwarzschild-AdS黑膜）。

Result: 给出多种Penrose型不等式的统一热力学表述；在渐近Schwarzschild-AdS情形下得到相应推广；全息框架下这些界对应对偶场论中归一化热力学量的单调行为；核心积分不等式可在NEC下对一般平面对称黑膜直接建立。

Conclusion: 本文目标并非给出局部界的完全一般性证明，而是揭示连接局部热力学约束与全局几何不等式的统一结构视角；把局部界推广到所考虑类别之外仍是开放问题。

Abstract: Under explicit and physically motivated assumptions, we identify a local-to-global thermodynamic mechanism that organizes several previously disconnected inequalities on black holes. We first focus on asymptotically flat, static, and spherically-symmetric black holes, and provide a unified thermodynamic perspective on various Penrose-like inequalities. We also discuss the generalizations in asymptotically Schwarzschild anti-de Sitter black holes/branes. In holographic settings, these bounds imply monotonic behavior of normalized thermodynamic quantities in the dual field theory. Notably, one central integrated inequality can be established directly under the NEC in general static planar symmetric asymptotically Schwarzschild anti-de Sitter black branes. Our purpose is not to claim a fully general proof of the proposed local bounds, but to expose a unified structural perspective connecting local thermodynamic constraints to global geometric inequalities. Establishing the local bounds beyond the classes considered here remains an open problem.

</details>


### [254] [Gravitational-Wave Signatures and Periodic Orbits of Charged Particles Around a Five-Dimensional Rotating Black Hole: Comparison with the Kerr Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35386)
*Bahromjon Shokirov,Tursunali Xamidov,Chen Zhou,Sanjar Shaymatov,Chengxun Yuan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究5维旋转Myers–Perry黑洞在均匀磁场中赤道面带电粒子的周期轨道及其EMRI引力波，并与带磁场Kerr黑洞对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验额外维度（D=5）与磁场联合效应对极端质量比旋近中周期轨道与引力波可探测性的影响，明确D=5与Kerr黑洞的观测差异。

Method: 在D=5旋转Myers–Perry黑洞均匀磁场时空中求解带电粒子赤道面测地/洛伦兹方程，分析周期轨道、势阱与频率；采用数值kludge方法计算10M☉致密天体绕10^6M☉黑洞的引力波波形、频谱与辐射反作用时标，并估算LISA信噪比。

Result: D=5黑洞束缚更弱：ISCO能量为0.95–0.99（Kerr为0.78–0.94），势阱深度小数倍，周期轨道更靠近分界线；磁场可使方位频率低于径向频率并在远场产生环状轨道。D=5信号周期更长、频率更低、振幅小约18%；βM=0.02时最强谱线为7.9 mHz（Kerr 9.9 mHz）。周期轨道寿命约数小时至数天，LISA信噪比7–31（Kerr 15–54）。

Conclusion: 额外维与磁场显著改变周期轨道结构与引力波特征：D=5情形束缚更弱、频率更低、信噪比更低，但LISA在有限寿命内仍具备可探测性，可作为区分D=5与Kerr黑洞的潜在观测特征。

Abstract: We study periodic orbits of charged particles in the equatorial plane of a five-dimensional ($D=5$) rotating Myers--Perry black hole (BH) immersed in a uniform magnetic field, and the gravitational waves emitted by these orbits in an extreme mass-ratio inspiral. All results are compared with the Kerr BH in the uniform magnetic field. We find that a charged particle is much more weakly bound to the $D=5$ dimensional BH, i.e. its energy at the innermost stable circular orbit is close to unity, $0.95$--$0.99$, compared with $0.78$--$0.94$ for the Kerr BH, and the depth of the potential well is several times smaller. As a result, the periodic orbits of the $D=5$ dimensional BH lie much closer to the separatrix. The magnetic field can make the azimuthal frequency of the orbit smaller than the radial one and produces loops in the orbits far from the BH. Using the numerical kludge method, we compute the gravitational waveforms for a compact object of $10M_\odot$ orbiting a BH of $10^6M_\odot$. At the same magnetic field, the $D=5$ dimensional signal has a longer period, lower frequencies, and an amplitude smaller by about $18\%$. We show that for the magnetic parameter $βM=0.02$ the strongest spectral line is at $7.9$\,mHz, compared with $9.9$\,mHz for the Kerr BH. An estimate of the radiation reaction shows that the periodic orbits survive from less than an hour to a few days in the presence of an external magnetic field. Over these lifetimes, the signal-to-noise ratio for LISA is $7$--$31$ for the rotating BH in $D=5$ dimensions, compared to the Kerr BH where it is about $15$--$54$.

</details>


### [255] [Phase-Space Constraints on a General Class of Eckart-like Viscous Cosmologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35405)
*David R. Moreno,J. Bayron Orjuela-Quintana,Yeinzon Rodriguez*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 利用动力系统分析研究一类由无量纲体粘滞压强 Ω_Π(Ω_m) 刻画的粘滞暗物质宇宙学，证明可行的早期辐射期要求无粘滞、物质主导期要求小粘滞压强、晚期唯一允许的吸引子是 Λ 主导不动点，故背景演化必须紧贴 ΛCDM；但线性扰动层面体粘滞产生尺度依赖的耗散拖曳，增大哈勃摩擦从而抑制结构增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对 H_0 张力与结构增长（S_8/σ_8）张力等宇宙学疑难，考察体粘滞暗物质能否提供偏离 ΛCDM 的可行物理通道，并系统厘清该类模型在背景与扰动两个层面的容许参数空间。

Method: 在 Eckart 形式体系中把体粘滞效应参数化为无量纲压强 Ω_Π ≡ Ω_Π(Ω_m)；采用动力系统方法确定各宇宙演化阶段（辐射期、物质主导期、晚期加速期）的可行性条件与不动点稳定性；并以幂律参数化 Ω_Π = -ξ̂_0 Ω_m^s（ξ̂_0、s 为实参数）作具体检验，同时在线性扰动层面分析亚视界尺度上粘滞导致的耗散效应。

Result: 可行的早期辐射期要求粘滞完全消失；物质主导期要求粘滞压强很小；晚期唯一物理上可接受的吸引子为宇宙学常数主导不动点，因此背景膨胀必然近似重复 ΛCDM。对幂律模型：s ≥ 1 且 ξ̂_0 ≪ 1 时可复现 ΛCDM 背景；s < 1 时出现向过去积分的边界，暗物质密度转为负值、粘滞压强变为非物理复数。由于背景与 ΛCDM 几乎一致，该模型难以缓解 H_0 张力；但在亚视界尺度上体粘滞对物质密度涨落产生尺度依赖的耗散拖曳，等效增大哈勃摩擦，从而提供抑制结构增长的途径。

Conclusion: 体粘滞暗物质模型在背景层面被动力系统约束得几乎等同于 ΛCDM，故不足以解决 H_0 张力；其可观测区分度主要来自线性扰动阶段——尺度依赖的粘滞耗散可压低物质聚集，为缓解结构增长张力提供可行通道，后续应重点以扰动层面观测（如 fσ_8、弱透镜）检验该类模型。

Abstract: We study the cosmological dynamics of a general class of viscous dark matter models using a dynamical system analysis. In the Eckart formalism, these viscous cosmologies are characterized by a dimensionless bulk viscous pressure $Ω_Π\equiv Ω_Π(Ω_m)$, where $Ω_m$ is the matter density parameter. Our analysis demonstrates that a viable early radiation era requires the complete absence of viscosity, a proper matter-dominated epoch demands a small viscous pressure, and the only physically admissible late-time attractor is the cosmological constant-dominated fixed point; consequently, the background expansion must closely track standard $Λ$CDM evolution. We validate these general results using the power-law parameterization $Ω_Π= -\hatξ_0 Ω_m^s$, where $\hatξ_0$ and $s$ are real parameters; for $s \geq 1$ and $\hatξ_0 \ll 1$, the model reproduces the $Λ$CDM background, while for $s < 1$ the system encounters a past-time integration boundary where the dark matter density transitions into negative values and its viscous pressure evaluates to nonphysical complex numbers. Since the background expansion essentially duplicates $Λ$CDM, the model is unlikely to resolve the $H_0$ tension. However, large differences arise at the linear perturbative level; indeed, on sub-horizon scales, bulk viscosity causes a scale-dependent dissipative drag on the matter density fluctuations; this increases the Hubble friction, offering a channel to suppress structure growth.

</details>


### [256] [Decoupled asymmetric thin-shell wormholes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35407)
*A. López-Hormazábal,Hishan Farfán-Bachiloglu,Kazuharu Bamba,Francisco Tello-Ortiz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过将 Schwarzschild 外部时空与其最小几何变形（MGD）拼接，构造了反射不对称薄壳虫洞；不对称性完全由引力解耦扇区承载，并研究了能量条件、线性稳定性及旋转推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 薄壳虫洞通常人为拼接两个时空，缺乏物理动机；希望借助最小几何变形这一有物理来源的解耦机制，构造反射不对称（两侧不等价）的薄壳虫洞，并系统考察其能量条件与稳定性。

Method: 以 Schwarzschild 外部与其 MGD 变形（同质量、同红移函数，变形侧由标量场耦合非线性电动力学源的黑洞、取实标量分支）通过薄壳拼接；推导壳与体（bulk）总径向零能量的精确恒等式；用跨壳动量流（等于体径向零能量密度）导出闭式线性稳定性条件；再将拼接推广到 Kerr 时空并按自旋精确求解。

Result: 壳与体的总径向零能量满足精确恒等式，在变形足够强时为正；保留动量流后得到闭式线性稳定条件。证明：若壳响应为常规型，稳定性要求体在喉部违反径向零能量条件；而变形可扩大稳定域，并把转变半径移入光子球内部。Kerr 情形下拼接按自旋精确可解，确定壳与体间角动量的分配及壳表现为旋转流体的区域。自由进动模型给出喉部引力波信号的估计。

Conclusion: MGD 解耦扇区可作为产生反射不对称薄壳虫洞的物理机制；强变形可使总径向零能量转正并扩大稳定域，但常规壳响应下仍须体在喉部违反径向 NEC。旋转推广给出角动量分配规律，并提供喉部引力波信号的进动估计。

Abstract: We construct reflection-asymmetric thin-shell wormholes by joining a Schwarzschild exterior to its minimal geometric deformation, which shares the same mass and redshift function, so that the asymmetry is carried entirely by the gravitational-decoupling sector. The deformed side is a black hole sourced by a scalar field coupled to nonlinear electrodynamics, in its branch with a real scalar. We show that the total radial null energy of shell and bulk obeys an exact identity and becomes positive for a sufficiently strong deformation. The momentum flux across the shell equals the radial null-energy density of the bulk, and retaining it yields the linear stability condition in closed form. We prove that stability with a conventional shell response requires the bulk to violate the radial null energy condition at the throat; the deformation instead enlarges the stable domain and moves the transition radius inside the photon sphere. For a stationary extension built on Kerr, the junction is solved exactly in the spin, which fixes how the angular momentum is shared between shell and bulk and where the shell behaves as a rotating fluid. A free-precession model provides an estimate of the gravitational-wave signal of the throat.

</details>


### [257] [Quasibound modes of rotating acoustic black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35493)
*H. S. Vieira,V. B. Bezerra*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个球形坐标下旋转声学黑洞的度规，求得了无质量Klein–Gordon方程的精确解析解，并由此得到准束缚态频率的解析表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准束缚态刻画被困于有效视界附近的长寿命共振模式，对理解波的传播、模式稳定性与能量约束十分关键；声学黑洞是实验可及的视界物理实现，可把理论预言与实验观测联系起来。

Method: 引入描述旋转声学黑洞的球形坐标度规，求解该背景中无质量Klein–Gordon标量扰动方程，通过施加适当边界条件获得精确解析解。

Result: 给出了系统准束缚态频率的精确解析表达式。

Conclusion: 该解析结果有助于研究类比时空中的波传播、模式稳定性与能量约束，并可能建立引力黑洞相关现象的理论预测与实验室观测之间的直接联系。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce a metric describing rotating acoustic black holes in spherical coordinates. This formulation allows us to obtain exact analytical solutions of the massless Klein--Gordon equation governing scalar perturbations in the corresponding background spacetime. By imposing the appropriate boundary conditions, we derive an exact analytical expression for the quasibound state frequencies of the system. Quasibound states play a central role in the dynamics of black hole analog systems, as they characterize long-lived resonant modes trapped in the vicinity of the effective horizon. Their investigation provides valuable insights into wave propagation, mode stability, and energy confinement in analog spacetimes. Moreover, because acoustic black holes offer experimentally accessible realizations of horizon physics, the study of quasibound states may establish a direct connection between theoretical predictions and laboratory observations, thereby contributing to a deeper understanding of phenomena that also arise in gravitational black hole environments.

</details>


### [258] [Gravitationally Induced Polarization-Angle Rotation in Pulsar-Black Hole Binaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35589)
*Jyotijwal Debnath,Amodio Carleo,Delphine Perrodin,Pratik Tarafdar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文将旋转矢量模型（RVM）与零测地线数值积分相结合，研究了脉冲星—黑洞双星中引力光线偏折、平行移动、参考系拖曳及相对论自旋—轨道耦合对观测偏振位置角（PPA）轮廓的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲星辐射高度偏振，其偏振特征通常用RVM描述；但若脉冲星处于含黑洞伴星的双星系统中，相对论光子传播效应可能显著改变偏振轮廓，而现有RVM未包含这些效应，需要定量评估其影响并探索其作为几何探针的潜力。

Method: 将RVM与零测地线数值积分结合，沿光子轨迹计算偏振方向；分别考虑不旋转与旋转黑洞伴星，并纳入相对论自旋—轨道耦合导致的轨道几何演化；以选定参考构造为基准量化平行移动引起的附加偏振旋转，并系统扫描双星参数与脉冲星自旋取向的依赖关系。

Result: 不旋转黑洞情况下，观测偏振方向的变化源于引力光线偏折引起的几何位移；旋转黑洞下，参考系拖曳引入额外几何位移，平行移动产生相对参考构造的附加偏振旋转。引力光线偏折可显著扭曲特征S形PPA轮廓，尤其在上合附近，并可在所考虑的超大质量黑洞系统中反转其整体变化方向；相对论自旋—轨道演化亦影响偏振轮廓的轨道相位依赖，在超大质量黑洞系统中尤为明显。

Conclusion: 相对论光子传播可同时改变脉冲星偏振轮廓的形状与相位依赖，当此类脉冲星—黑洞双星被发现时，偏振观测有望成为探测其几何结构的有用探针。

Abstract: Radio pulsars emit highly polarized radiation whose polarization properties are commonly described by the Rotating Vector Model (RVM). In this work, we investigate the effects of gravitational light bending and parallel transport on the polarization profile of a pulsar in a hypothetical binary system with a black hole (BH) companion. We combine the RVM with numerical integration of null geodesics to calculate the polarization direction along the photon trajectory and model the orbital-phase-dependent variation of the observed polarization position angle (PPA). We consider both non-spinning and spinning black hole companions and include the evolution of the orbital geometry due to relativistic spin-orbit coupling. For a non-spinning black hole, the change in the observed polarization direction arises from the geometric shift associated with gravitational light bending. For a spinning black hole, frame dragging introduces an additional geometric shift, while parallel transport produces an additional polarization rotation measured relative to our chosen reference construction. We investigate the dependence of these polarization changes on the binary parameters and the orientation of the pulsar spin. We find that gravitational light bending can significantly distort the characteristic S-shaped PPA profile, particularly near superior conjunction, and can reverse its overall sense of variation for the supermassive black hole system considered here. Relativistic spin-orbit evolution also affects the orbital-phase dependence of the polarization profile, particularly in the supermassive black hole system. These results show that relativistic photon propagation can modify both the shape and the phase dependence of pulsar polarization profiles and may provide a useful probe of the geometry of pulsar-BH binaries when such systems are discovered.

</details>


### [259] [Nonlinear Tides of Binary Neutron Stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35602)
*Guangzhou Guo,Zhen Pan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 非线性潮汐耦合恒星振荡并改变双星旋近的引力波信号；本文用流体力学模拟与模耦合模型研究中子星双星中的非线性潮汐，给出基模频移，并指出后旋近阶段可产生约1弧度的额外相位偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性潮汐响应不足以描述强潮汐场下的中子星振荡，需明确非线性潮汐对基模频率与引力波相位的影响量级，以评估其对下一代引力波探测的观测意义。

Method: 对旋近中子星双星开展非线性潮汐的流体力学模拟；同时在模耦合模型框架下，将四极基模频移按潮汐强度展开至二阶，得到半解析结果，并与模拟对比。

Result: 两种方法在微扰区一致，在强潮汐场下因微扰论失效而出现分歧；旋近后期，等质量双星基模频率降低约10%，并在线性潮汐响应之外引入量级为O(1)弧度的引力波相位偏移。

Conclusion: 非线性潮汐在中子星双星后旋近阶段产生可观的频率与相位修正，是下一代引力波探测器的有前景的观测目标。

Abstract: Nonlinear tides couple stellar oscillations and reshape the gravitational wave (GW) signal from inspiralling binaries. We present hydrodynamic simulations of nonlinear tides in inspiralling neutron-star binaries and, within a mode-coupling model, derive a semi-analytic quadrupolar fundamental mode-frequency shift through second order in the tidal strength. The two approaches agree in the perturbative regime, but diverge at stronger tidal fields, where perturbation theory breaks down. In the late inspiral, nonlinear tides reduce the fundamental-mode frequency by ${\sim}10\%$ for equal-mass binaries and add a GW phase shift of $\mathcal{O}(1)$ radian beyond the linear tidal response. These effects make nonlinear tides a promising target for next-generation GW detectors.

</details>


<div id='quant-ph'></div>

# quant-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [260] [The Cost of a Real Grover Oracle: Minecraft Seed Search from Software to Quantum Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33139)
*Evan M. Reeves,Elias Sopkin,Thomas A. Searles*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对Minecraft 1.13.1种子恢复的Grover oracle进行资源估计，证明将已知常数算术直接在Fourier基中实现可将放置谓词宽度由6n+3降至4n+3量子比特，双量子比特门数标度由Θ(n³)降至O(n²)，代表性构造需275量子比特、48,896个CX门、深度4,482，远优于基线的467量子比特、2,038,804个CX门、深度186,328。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序化内容生成（PCG）可作为量子搜索的天然应用场景，但已有Grover oracle的构造通常沿用通用寄存器间算术，导致量子比特宽度与双量子比特门数开销过大。作者希望量化"已知常数算术特化"（known-constant arithmetic specialization）对oracle资源的具体影响，并考察其在真实硬件上的可执行性与编译后行为。

Method: 构造面向Minecraft 1.13.1种子恢复的Grover oracle：将结构放置谓词（structure-placement predicate）与针对32位残差的线性同余生成器（LCG）可逆逆变换相结合。采用Fourier基下的直接常数算术、约化宽度算术、相位标记（phase marking）与uncomputation。对约化宽度算术、相位标记、反计算及完整Grover循环使用独立Java参考实现进行验证，生产宽度电路则做结构化审计。资源核算采用与后端无关的Qiskit level-0 {u, CX}公共基计数。随后在IBM处理器与Quantinuum H2-1上执行完整Grover循环中的约化宽度放置电路。

Result: 放置谓词宽度从6n+3降至4n+3量子比特；双量子比特门数结构标度从Θ(n³)降至O(n²)。代表性非零r折叠残差搜索构造：275量子比特、48,896个CX门、深度4,482；原寄存器到寄存器基线为467量子比特、2,038,804个CX门、深度186,328。多放置合取揭示了"宽度—重计算"权衡，精确枚举发现基准下候选约简存在平台。硬件实验中，特化构造在单次迭代后标记态概率提升幅度大于基线；IBM约化宽度实验表现出显著的routing敏感性。

Conclusion: 结果量化了在PCG衍生oracle中特化已知常数算术的资源收益，并表明其编译后的硬件行为强烈依赖于routing与执行上下文。

Abstract: We present the resource estimation of how known-constant arithmetic changes the cost of a Grover oracle for Minecraft 1.13.1 seed recovery, an application of quantum search to procedural content generation. The oracle developed here combines a structure-placement predicate with a reversible inverse transform of the linear congruential generator for the 32-bit residual search. Reduced-width arithmetic, phase marking, uncomputation, and complete Grover loops are verified against an independent Java reference, while production-width circuits are structurally audited. Direct Fourier-basis constant arithmetic reduces the placement-predicate width from 6n+3 to 4n+3 qubits and the structural scaling of the two-qubit gate counts from Theta(n^3) to O(n^2). Under backend-agnostic common-basis Qiskit level-0 {u, CX} accounting, the representative nonzero-r folded residual-search construction uses 275 circuit qubits, 48,896 CX gates, and depth 4,482, versus 467 qubits, 2,038,804 CX gates, and depth 186,328 for the original register-to-register baseline. Multi-placement conjunctions expose a width-recomputation tradeoff, and exact enumeration identifies a plateau in candidate reduction for the benchmark. We executed reduced-width placement circuits within complete Grover loops on IBM processors and Quantinuum H2-1 and show larger one-iteration increases in marked-state probability for the specialized construction than for the baseline. These results quantify the resource benefits of arithmetic specialization and show substantial routing sensitivity in the reduced-width IBM hardware experiments. Together, the results quantify the effect of specializing known-constant arithmetic within a PCG-derived oracle and show that its compiled hardware behavior strongly depends on routing and execution context.

</details>


### [261] [Fully quantum training of parametric circuits with Quantum Hamiltonian Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33144)
*Marco Burato,Leonardo Banchi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种完全在量子计算机上实现的量子哈密顿下降（QHD）训练方法，用量子比特编码参数并在第二寄存器上相干控制参数化电路；证明对具光滑周期延拓的代价函数（含参数化电路）仿真误差与网格分辨率无关、线路情形下随参数数最多线性增长；并揭示理想动力学仅依赖一条控制绝热性的曲线，其余自由度可用于最小化门数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数化量子电路的训练多依赖经典局部优化器，每次只用一个点评估代价或梯度；QHD 虽提供全局搜索与隧穿机制，但其假定代价函数可由经典计算，且数字实现误差随每参数量子比特数指数增长、时间调度直接沿用经典优化，实现性差。作者希望把 QHD 全部放到量子计算机上，用于训练参数化量子电路。

Method: 提出在线（online）步骤：将参数编码为量子比特（第一寄存器）的量子粒子位置，代价函数作为势能，两个时间调度分别控制动能与势能，使波函数全局探索并向全局最小隧穿；同时这些参数比特相干地控制第二寄存器上的参数化电路。对具有光滑周期延拓的代价函数（包含参数化电路的代价）分析数字模拟误差；分析理想动力学对两条时间调度的依赖结构，并据此在剩余自由度上最小化门数来设计调度。

Result: 证明仿真误差界与网格分辨率无关，且对线路情形误差随参数数目至多线性增长（克服了此前按每参数量子比特数指数增长的界）；证明理想动力学仅通过一条控制搜索绝热性的曲线依赖两条调度，其余自由度可用于降低门数；在基准函数与参数化电路上的模拟显示，在同等代价下，按此方式设计的调度显著提高了找到全局最小值的概率。

Conclusion: QHD 可在量子计算机上端到端地训练参数化量子电路，其数字实现具有与网格无关、随参数数线性增长的误差保证；时间调度可归约为单一绝热性曲线，从而在固定代价下优化调度、提升全局最优搜索成功率。

Abstract: Parametric quantum circuits are usually trained by classical, local optimizers, which use the quantum computer only to evaluate the cost function, or its gradient, one point at a time. Quantum Hamiltonian Descent (QHD) takes a different route: in this quantum algorithm for continuous optimization, the parameters become the position of a quantum particle, with the cost function as its potential, and two time schedules control kinetic and potential energy so that the wavefunction explores the whole landscape and tunnels towards the global minimum. QHD, however, assumes a classically computable cost function, and its digital implementation is poorly understood: standard error bounds grow exponentially with the number of qubits per parameter, and the schedules are borrowed from classical optimization. Here we propose to train parametric circuits with QHD entirely on the quantum computer, through an online step in which the qubits encoding the parameters coherently control the circuit on a second register. We prove that, for cost functions with a smooth periodic extension, including those of parametric circuits, the simulation error admits a bound independent of the grid resolution, and that for circuits the bound grows at most linearly with the number of parameters. We also show that the ideal dynamics depends on the two schedules only through a single curve, which controls the adiabaticity of the search, and that the remaining freedom can be used to minimize the gate count. Simulations on benchmark functions and parametric circuits show that, at equal cost, schedules designed in this way markedly increase the probability of finding the global minimum.

</details>


### [262] [Direct tests of conformal symmetry with Rydberg atoms: assessment and proposals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33156)
*Junchen Rong,Slava Rychkov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出利用里德堡原子阵列直接检验共形对称性的方案：将原子排列成环、测量等时两点关联函数以直接验证CFT预言的特征函数形式，估计所需实验快照数，并优化拉比频率Ω与失谐Δ的绝热制备曲线，在更短时间内获得更高保真度，表明现有技术即可实现直接实验检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形场论（CFT）预言的临界态对称性此前缺乏直接的实验验证手段；里德堡原子阵列提供了可调控、可局域测量的量子模拟平台，因而有望用于对共形对称性进行直接的实验检验。

Method: 将原子排布在圆周几何上，测量等时两点关联函数，与CFT预言的特征函数形式直接比对；统计上估算分辨该行为所需的实验快照数量；并对制备目标临界态的含时脉冲曲线（拉比频率Ω与失谐Δ）进行优化，以实现更高效的绝热制备。

Result: 优化后的脉冲曲线在远短于以往实验的演化时间内达到更高保真度；并给出了以足够统计精度分辨CFT关联行为的快照数估计。

Conclusion: 结合关联函数测量与优化绝热制备，利用当前里德堡原子技术开展共形对称性的直接实验检验是可行的。

Abstract: We propose a direct test of conformal symmetry using Rydberg-atom arrays. By arranging the atoms on a circle and measuring equal-time two-point correlation functions, the characteristic functional form predicted by conformal field theory can be tested directly. We estimate the number of experimental snapshots required to resolve this behavior with sufficient statistical precision. We further optimize the time-dependent ramp profile of the Rabi frequency~$Ω$ and detuning~$Δ$ used to prepare the target critical state adiabatically. The optimized profile achieves higher fidelity over much shorter evolution times than those employed in previous experiments. Taken together, these results suggest that a direct experimental test of conformal symmetry is feasible with current Rydberg-atom technology.

</details>


### [263] [Genuinely multipartite entangled qudits: one rule to link them all](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33160)
*Gonçalo M. Quinta,Rui André*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文把链路（link）多项式重铸为超图，给出构造纯qudit态的显式公式，证明对任意link类、任意粒子丢失模式，态的多体纠缠结构都与对应链路完全一致，并据此证明了m-resistant纯qudit态的存在性猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 链路图提供多体纠缠的直观图像：切掉一条环可能释放其它环，正如追踪掉一个粒子（trace out）可解除其余粒子间的纠缠。这种类比可按"丢失部分粒子后哪些粒子组仍保持纠缠"对多体纠缠进行分类，而这正是鲁棒量子网络的核心问题。然而，实现给定link类的纯态此前只在特殊情形下已知。

Method: 将link多项式重新表述为超图（hypergraph），并基于此构造纯qudit态的显式解析公式；证明该构造在任意粒子丢失模式下，其纠缠结构与对应链路精确匹配。

Result: 对每个link类都构造出匹配的纯qudit态；同一构造还能控制真多体纠缠（genuine multipartite entanglement），精确判定哪些保持纠缠的粒子组是真正的多体纠缠，并可使全部组均为真多体纠缠。由此证明了猜想：对任意粒子数与任意m，均存在m-resistant纯qudit态——丢失任意m个粒子后仍纠缠，丢失m+1个后完全可分。

Conclusion: link类（含GME结构）在纯qudit态层面可被完全实现，并证明m-resistant纯qudit态对任意n与m普遍存在，填补了此前仅特殊情形可实现的空白。

Abstract: Links of rings offer an intuitive picture of multipartite entanglement. Cutting one ring can free some of the others, just as losing a particle, i.e. tracing it out, can disentangle some of the remaining ones. This analogy classifies multipartite entanglement according to which groups of particles stay entangled when others are lost, a question at the heart of robust quantum networks. However, pure states realizing a given link class were known only in special cases. Here, we close this gap. Recasting link polynomials as hypergraphs, we give an explicit formula for pure qudit states and prove that, for every link class, their entanglement under any pattern of particle loss matches the link exactly. The same construction also controls genuine multipartite entanglement, determining exactly which of the groups that stay entangled are genuinely multipartite entangled, and all of them can be made so. As a consequence, we prove the conjecture that $m$-resistant pure qudit states, which stay entangled after the loss of any $m$ particles but become fully separable after the loss of $m+1$, exist for any number of particles and any $m$.

</details>


### [264] [Post-Quantum Perfect Zero-Knowledge Arguments for NP From Any One-Way Permutation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33185)
*Frédéric Dupuis,Rémi Ligez,Khaireddine Medhioub,Louis Salvail,Louis-Charles Thibodeau*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 证明后量子单向置换足以构造对所有NP语言的、可抵抗量子敌手的完美零知识经典论证，具体通过证明NOVY承诺方案的后量子塌缩绑定性并直接沿用Blum哈密顿回路协议实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完美零知识论证的经典构造通常依赖更强的假设（如单向函数之外的承诺原语或特殊结构），而能否在仅假设后量子单向置换、且安全性针对量子敌手的前提下实现NP的完美零知识论证尚不清楚，本文填补这一空白。

Method: 第一步，基于任意置换利用交互哈希构造的NOVY比特承诺方案，证明在后量子单向置换假设下对量子多项式时间承诺方具有sum-binding与collapse-binding性质；第二步，利用NOVY方案的塌缩绑定性，证明Blum针对HAMCYCLE的完美零知识论证协议原样即可在量子敌手面前保持完美零知识与可靠性论证性质。

Result: NOVY方案在后量子单向置换下对量子承诺方是sum-binding与collapse-binding的；由此Blum的HAMCYCLE完美零知识论证无需修改即对量子敌手成立，从而得到后量子单向置换蕴含NP的完美零知识经典论证。

Conclusion: 首次确立后量子单向置换足以构造针对量子敌手的NP完美零知识经典论证，说明塌缩绑定性是连接经典承诺与后量子零知识协议的关键桥梁。

Abstract: In this paper, we show for the first time that post-quantum one-way permutations are sufficient for perfect zero-knowledge classical arguments for all of NP with security guaranteed against quantum adversaries. We first establish that Naor, Ostrovsky,Venkatesen, and Yung's bit commitment scheme built from any permutation using interactive hashing, called the NOVY scheme, is sum-binding and collapse-binding against quantum polynomial-time committers provided the permutation is post-quantum one-way. We then show that the collapse-binding property of the NOVY scheme allows for Blum's perfect zero-knowledge argument for HAMCYCLE to remain so exactly as it stands against quantum adversaries.

</details>


### [265] [Quantum Heuristic Separation of Comb Inequalities for the Travelling Salesman Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33188)
*Yohan Finet,Victor Drouin-Touchette*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出一种针对旅行商问题中梳（comb）不等式的量子启发式分离算法：先用多项式规模的量子线路制备"固定齿数梳"的均匀叠加态（其中包含将数据寄存器汉明重量以one-hot形式相干编码到辅助寄存器的子线路），再用Grover-Mixer QAOA搜索被违反的不等式，并给出mixer酉算子、完整问题哈密顿量与资源分析的显式线路构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梳不等式是TSP整数规划中不可或缺的割平面，但其分离（判定是否存在被当前LP解违反的梳）在经典计算上非常困难，缺乏高效精确算法；作者希望借助量子搜索的潜在加速，为这一组合优化中的关键分离子问题提供新的求解思路。

Method: 1) 设计多项式门数与量子比特数的态制备流程，生成所有"齿数固定"的梳的均匀叠加；2) 提出将数据寄存器汉明重量相干编码为辅助寄存器one-hot形式（unary）的量子线路，作为受控约束的构造模块；3) 以该叠加为搜索空间，采用Grover-Mixer Quantum Alternating Operator Ansatz（QAOA的算子交替变体）进行被违反梳的搜索；4) 显式构造mixer酉算子与完整问题哈密顿量，并做资源（门数、比特数）分析。

Result: 在给定多项式预算（QAOA轮数、参数优化步数、采样次数）下算法运行时间为多项式；但作者明确指出，未提供任何证据表明该预算足以在存在被违反梳时以常数概率找到它，即算法的完备性/成功概率保证缺失。文中给出了mixer、Hamiltonian的显式线路与资源估计。

Conclusion: 该工作将梳不等式分离表述为受约束的量子搜索问题，给出可实现的线路模块（均匀叠加制备、one-hot汉明重量编码、mixer与Hamiltonian构造），在形式上获得多项式运行时间，但缺少成功概率的严格论证，属于启发式方案，其实际有效性仍有待验证。

Abstract: Comb inequalities are essential cutting planes for the travelling salesman problem, yet their separation remains computationally challenging. We propose a quantum heuristic separation algorithm for this family of inequalities. The algorithm uses a procedure that prepares a uniform superposition of all combs with a fixed number of teeth using polynomially many gates and qubits. As part of this procedure, we present a circuit that coherently encodes the Hamming weight of a data register in one-hot form in an auxiliary register. The separation algorithm then searches for violated inequalities using the Grover-Mixer Quantum Alternating Operator Ansatz. Its running time is polynomial under prescribed polynomial budgets for the number of QAOA rounds, parameter optimization steps and samples gathered, although we provide no evidence that such budgets suffice to find a violated comb with constant probability when one exists. We provide an explicit circuit construction for the mixer unitary, the full problem Hamiltonian and a resource analysis.

</details>


### [266] [Study of localization properties under subsystem periodic driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33189)
*Arpita Goswami,Ranjan Modak,Shaon Sahoo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文研究仅驱动子系统（周期性驱动的干净链耦合到静态Aubry-André链构成的双腿梯形）能否实现完全去局域化，发现不能，而是出现局域态与去局域态共存的混合相，并用局域有效哈密顿量与Floquet哈密顿量的非局域性交叉频率解释其适用范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期驱动可调控量子局域化，已知整体驱动一维Aubry-André链在低于临界频率时可进入完全去局域相。作者追问：若只驱动系统的一部分，是否同样能实现去局域化，从而发展"子系统驱动"这一替代性的Floquet工程途径。

Method: 构建双腿梯形模型：一条静态Aubry-André链耦合一条周期性驱动的干净紧束缚链；进行精确数值计算（Floquet分析）；解析推导高频区的局域有效哈密顿量；并通过量化Floquet哈密顿量的涌现非局域性确定有效描述的适用上限。

Result: 从局域静态梯形出发，子系统驱动在低于临界频率时无法实现完全去局域化；取而代之的是局域态与去局域态共存的混合相，去局域态占比随驱动频率非单调变化。局域有效哈密顿量虽在高频区解析导出，仍能预测中频区的局域化转变；其有效性与非局域性指标给出的交叉频率低于局域化转变的临界频率。

Conclusion: 仅驱动子系统即可改变量子格点体系的输运性质，为Floquet工程提供了一条新途径；同时表明局域有效哈密顿量的预言能力可延伸至局域化转变，但其严格适用性受Floquet哈密顿量非局域性所限。

Abstract: Periodic driving provides a powerful route for controlling localization and engineering effective Hamiltonians in quantum systems. A localized one-dimensional Aubry-André chain can be driven into a fully delocalized phase when the entire system is driven below a critical frequency. Here, we ask whether such delocalization can be achieved by driving only a part of the system. We address this question using a two-leg ladder comprising an Aubry-André chain coupled to a periodically driven clean tight-binding chain. Exact numerical calculations show that, starting from a localized static ladder, subsystem driving below a critical frequency cannot achieve complete delocalization. Instead, a mixed phase emerges, with localized and delocalized states coexisting and the fraction of delocalized states varying nonmonotonically with the driving frequency. This behavior can be understood using local effective Hamiltonians. Although these effective Hamiltonians are derived analytically in the high-frequency regime, they can nevertheless predict the localization transition occurring in the moderate-frequency regime. In fact, by quantifying the emergent nonlocality of the Floquet Hamiltonian, we show that these effective Hamiltonians provide a valid description up to a crossover frequency that is lower than the critical frequency associated with the localization transition. Our results demonstrate an alternative route to Floquet engineering, in which the transport properties of a quantum lattice system can be modified by driving only a subsystem.

</details>


### [267] [A New Non-Affine Family in the Karlsson-Szollosi Intersection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33207)
*André Ranchin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明六阶复Hadamard矩阵的两个二参数族（Karlsson族与Szöllősi族）的交集在标准Hadamard等价下等于仿射Diţă曲线 D_6^(1) 与新发现的非仿射单参数族 C_6^(1) 之并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前已知两族的交集包含仿射Diţă曲线 D_6^(1)，但交集是否严格更大尚不清楚；澄清六维复Hadamard矩阵族的相交结构对相关分类问题及六维互无偏基（MUB）的搜寻有直接意义。

Method: 在Karlsson坐标下给出Szöllősi族 X_6^(2) 的新参数化，并对Karlsson–Szöllősi交集进行构造性分类，分析块可约形式与内摆线（hypocycloid）约束的相互作用。

Result: 得到 K_6^(2) ∩ X_6^(2) = D_6^(1) ∪ C_6^(1)（在标准Hadamard等价意义下），其中 C_6^(1) 是新的非仿射单参数复Hadamard矩阵族；由此说明该交集严格大于已知的仿射Diţă曲线。

Conclusion: 两参数Hadamard族的交集具有比原认知更丰富的一维结构；新的参数化与分类方法揭示块可约形式与内摆线约束的关系，可为六维互无偏基的搜索提供支持。

Abstract: We show that $K_6^{(2)} \cap X_6^{(2)} = D_6^{(1)} \cup C_6^{(1)}$ up to standard Hadamard equivalence, where $C_6^{(1)}$ is a new non-affine one-parameter family of complex Hadamard matrices. This demonstrates that the intersection of the two-parameter Hadamard families of Karlsson and Szöllősi is strictly larger than the known affine Diţă curve $D_6^{(1)}$. Our new parametrisation of the Szöllősi family $X_6^{(2)}$ in Karlsson coordinates and our constructive classification of the Karlsson-Szöllősi intersection shed light on the intersection of block-reducible forms and hypocycloid constraints. This may support the search for mutually unbiased bases in six dimensions.

</details>


### [268] [Quantum-Relativistic Anomalous Proper Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33220)
*Pablo L. Saldanha,Nelson Yokomizo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在引力和固有时服从量子叠加原理的假设下，大质量物体处于两位置叠加态并后选择时，另一区域的固有时可呈经典广义相对论不允许的反常值，甚至为负值；并提出可在现有技术下实现的干涉实验方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子力学与广义相对论的交叉效应长期缺乏直接实验证据，而仅靠平直时空中的牛顿势效应无法检验二者的深层结合。作者希望探讨引力和固有时满足量子叠加原理这一假设的可观测后果，从而寻找可实验检验的新预言。

Method: 理论分析：设引力与固有时满足量子叠加原理，构造大质量物体在两个空间位置的量子叠加态，并进行合适的后选择，计算空间中另一区域的有效固有时；同时设计利用地球引力场中干涉仪内量子粒子内部自由度的实验方案，以测量反常的有效固有时。

Result: 发现后选择后的有效固有时可超出经典广义相对论所允许的取值范围，甚至变为负值，对应有效的时间倒流；并论证探测内部自由度反常有效固有时的实验在现有技术条件下原则上可行。

Conclusion: 引力的量子叠加性质能导致经典广义相对论中不存在的反常固有时效应，该效应原则上可由可实现的干涉实验检验，若实现将首次提供量子力学与广义相对论相互作用超越平直时空牛顿势效应的实验证据。

Abstract: We investigate surprising consequences of the hypothesis that gravity and proper time obey the quantum superposition principle. We show that, for a massive body prepared in a superposition of two positions and post-selected in a suitable final state, the elapsed proper time in another region of space can assume an anomalous effective value lying outside the range allowed by classical general relativity. Remarkably, the effective elapsed proper time can even become negative, corresponding to an effective evolution backward in time. We also propose an experiment to observe an anomalous effective proper time associated with the internal degrees of freedom of a quantum particle in an interferometer under Earth's gravitational field. The proposed experiment is, in principle, feasible with current technology, and its realization could provide the first experimental evidence of an interplay between quantum mechanics and general relativity beyond the effects of the Newtonian potential in flat spacetime.

</details>


### [269] [Trial-space co-design for quantum simulation on noisy hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33246)
*Bo peng,Vibin Abraham,Himadri Pathak*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出"问题—硬件联合设计仿真基"的思路：在选定测量基与测量方案之前，先利用物理问题信息与器件特性来确定紧凑的态与演化时间基，从而提升噪声硬件上量子模拟的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有噪声硬件上的量子模拟通常按理想机器设计，器件局限只在基组与测量方案确定后才被考虑，导致资源与精度利用不佳。

Method: 将物理问题信息与器件响应提前纳入决策，选择构成紧凑仿真基的量子态与演化时间；由于目标问题本身不变，方法只改变求解所用的表示。

Result: 在多体与分子模型上，该方法能揭示常规时间演化遗漏的有用方向，但也暴露出部分方向过于相似而难以可靠估计；真实器件数据显示硬件响应会改变优选态与演化时间。七天采集的四比特实验表明，在同等硬件条件下，相较均匀采样，问题引导的时间选择使投影能量误差中位数降低54%。

Conclusion: 结果构成仿真基设计作为量子算法与执行硬件之间实用接口的原理性验证。

Abstract: Quantum simulations on noisy hardware are often designed as if the machine were ideal, with device limitations considered only after the basis and measurement plan have been chosen. We instead use information about the physical problem and the device at an earlier stage, to decide which states and evolution times should form a compact simulation basis. Because the target problem remains unchanged, the method adapts only the representation used to solve it. Tests on many-body and molecular models show that this approach can reveal useful directions that ordinary time evolution misses, while also exposing an important limitation: some of these directions can become too similar to estimate reliably. Realistic device data further show that hardware response can change the preferred states and evolution times before execution. Across seven acquisition days, a four-qubit experiment shows a median 54\% reduction in projected-energy error from problem-informed time choices relative to uniform sampling under matched hardware conditions. These results provide a proof of principle for simulation-basis design as a practical interface between quantum algorithms and the hardware that executes them.

</details>


### [270] [Variational Quantum Optimization of Noisy Polarization Qubit Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33290)
*Timur Javid,Brian Doolittle,Colin P. Lualdi,Brittany Conner,Michael Vayninger,Eric Chitambar,Paul G. Kwiat*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将变分优化引入量子网络协议，用以抵消硬件不完美的影响，并在光学平台上以简单prepare-and-measure协议完成原理验证，实现可与传统方法匹敌的自动化偏振补偿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子网络可支撑高级通信协议、分布式量子计算与增强传感，但当前量子网络节点的硬件噪声与不完美严重制约其应用，需要一种能自适应补偿硬件缺陷的手段。

Method: 将网络协议参数化以适配变分方法，在光学测试平台上对简单prepare-and-measure协议做变分优化；比较多种梯度估计方法，并分析硬件不完美与暗计数对优化性能的影响。

Result: 实现自动化偏振补偿，效果与传统方法相当；给出不同梯度估计方法的性能对比，并量化硬件不完美和暗计数对优化效果的影响。

Conclusion: 变分优化可有效补偿量子网络节点硬件缺陷；作者提出自然扩展方向：引入全局优化、混合梯度估计方法，以及将变分优化应用于光纤网络中的部署节点。

Abstract: Quantum networking enables advanced communication protocols, distributed quantum computing, and enhanced sensing. These applications are often hindered by current realizations of quantum network nodes, which utilize noisy and imperfect hardware. Here, we apply variational optimization to counteract the effects of such imperfections. We parameterize network protocols for use with variational methods and then use these methods to optimize simple prepare-and-measure protocols on an optical testbed as a proof-of-principle demonstration. We demonstrate automated polarization compensation, achieving results comparable to conventional methods. We also analyze the effects of hardware imperfections and dark counts on the performance of our optimizations. We compare the performance of various gradient estimation methods and propose natural extensions, including further improvements through the use of global optimization and hybrid gradient estimation methods, as well as the application of variational optimization to nodes deployed on fiber networks.

</details>


### [271] [Twirled and swirled worlds: a compositional approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33294)
*Sina Soltani,Thomas D. Galley,Marco Erba,John H. Selby*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将 twirled/swirled 世界推广到有限维 GPT 的完全范畴化（compositional）框架，将态、效应与多体变换统一视为过程，区分系统作用与过程空间作用，并给出不变子理论满足过程层析的条件与判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPT 中的 twirling/swirling 以往多在不完全范畴化、单系统或特定语境下讨论；缺少对态、效应、多体变换统一处理的理论，也缺少对“不变子理论是否满足过程层析”这一操作性判据的系统刻画。

Method: 在有限维 GPT 与紧群的框架下进行完全范畴化处理：把态、效应和多体变换统一为过程；区分系统作用与过程空间作用，并以“过程空间作用须尊重顺序与并行复合”来定义理论层面的作用；对物理理论作用取平均得 twirling，对非物理理论作用取平均得 swirling；借助表示论分析不变子理论的过程层析性。

Result: 证明不变子理论一般未必满足过程层析，并给出其满足的充分条件（存在不变的动力忠实态或内部经典参考系）；对局部层析的母理论，证明每个理论对称性都可表为线性的系统作用，且不变子理论满足过程层析；证明不变多体过程空间非局部层析当且仅当某二分的一侧共享非平凡不可约表示；刻画了辅助系统的必要性、所需携带的表示，以及无辅助过程层析时纠缠的必要性。

Conclusion: 建立统一 twirled 子理论与把非相干模拟推广到紧群的框架；并将双局域经典理论恢复为经典理论的 twirled 子理论、将实量子理论恢复为复量子理论的 swirled 子理论，由其表示结构导出各自层析性质。

Abstract: Twirled and swirled worlds describe generalised probabilistic theories (GPTs) restricted by symmetry-imposed superselection rules. We extend these constructions to a fully compositional setting for finite-dimensional GPTs and compact groups, treating states, effects, and multipartite transformations uniformly as processes. We distinguish system actions from process-space actions. We define actions on a theory as a whole by requiring the corresponding process-space actions to respect sequential and parallel composition. Averaging physical theory actions defines twirling, while averaging nonphysical theory actions defines swirling. We show that invariant subtheories might not satisfy process tomography, and establish conditions under which they do, involving invariant dynamically faithful states or internal classical reference frames. For locally tomographic parent theories, we prove that every theory symmetry admits a representation by linear system actions and that the invariant subtheory satisfies process tomography. An invariant multipartite process space fails local tomography if and only if some bipartition has sides sharing a non-trivial irreducible representation. We characterise when ancillary systems are necessary, which representations they must carry, and when entanglement is required for ancilla-free process tomography. We develop a framework for unifying twirled subtheories and extend incoherent simulations to compact groups. Finally, we recover bilocal classical theory as a twirled subtheory of classical theory and real quantum theory as a swirled subtheory of complex quantum theory, deriving their tomographic properties from their representation structure.

</details>


### [272] [A new analytical finding for the Born-Infeld integral potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33326)
*Leonardo Paulo Guimarães De Assis,Priscila Valdênia dos Santos,Sergio de Oliveira Vellozo,José Abdala Helayël Neto*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文指出Born-Infeld短程势的积分形式可近似精确地表示为库仑势与汤川势之差，据此给出经典真空极化与有效质量的物理解释，提出覆盖全空间的U(r)，并闭式计算出类氢原子的能级一阶移动，与高精度光谱数据比较从而得到最大场强参数b的模型依赖下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Born-Infeld非线性电动力学在短距离（接近点电荷）处的势函数缺乏直观物理解释，且此前尚缺乏可直接与高精度光谱实验对照的解析能移结果，因此需要给出可解释的势形式并从中导出可检验的b约束。

Method: 利用Born-Infeld势在短程的积分形式近似为库仑势减汤川势的差，用经典真空极化和有效质量作解释；据此构造覆盖全空间的新U(r)；在该势下解析（闭式）计算类氢原子能级的一阶微扰能移；并与高精度光谱数据对比。

Result: 得到类氢原子能移的闭式解析表达式；与高精度光谱数据比较后，给出最大场强参数b的模型依赖下界（受所用势模型假设影响）。

Conclusion: Born-Infeld短程势可被库仑—汤川分解有效描述并具清晰物理图像，新U(r)可推广至全空间并支持闭式能移计算，从而由光谱数据给出b的下界；该界限依赖模型，需进一步检验其普适性。

Abstract: A new finding regarding the Born-Infeld potential for short range distances from the charge revealed that its integral form can be almost exact written as the difference between a Coulomb-type potential and a Yukawa-type potential. This allowed for an immediate physical interpretation in terms of classical vacuum polarization and an effective mass. Subsequently, a new U(r) is proposed to cover the entire space. This formulation is then applied to calculate, in a closed manner, the first-order energy shifts in hydrogen-like atoms; a comparison of these shifts with high-precision spectroscopic data provides a model-dependent lower bound for the maximum field intensity parameter b.

</details>


### [273] [Junction Hook Errors in the Color Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33347)
*Salahedeen Issa,Margaret Martonosi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文指出Kishony-Fowler基于"任意子颜色"分类的hook error判定不完整，提出一类新的"junction hook error"，它在任意距离下都可与d-2个单量子比特故障组合成不可检测的逻辑错误，因此该schedule下所有稳定子（含bulk）仍需flag qubit等额外保护；但最小权重逻辑算符至多含一个junction，故junction hook error无法累积，schedule仍能把电路级距离提升至d-⌊(d+3)/6⌋。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三角色码中hook error会降低有效距离。近期Kishony与Fowler(arXiv:2603.28852)提出按"hook error为哪种任意子提供捷径"来分类可降距的hook error，并据此为各颜色的稳定子选择CNOT顺序（color-dependent syndrome extraction schedule）以规避这些错误。作者认为该分类不完备，可能遗漏了某些仍能降低距离的hook error，从而高估了该schedule的安全性。

Method: 从编码几何结构出发分析hook error的端点：逻辑算符的三条彩色任意子弦相交处定义为junction；指出当hook error的两个数据量子比特在junction处终止两条不同颜色的任意子弦时，该错误不对应单一任意子类型。对d=7,9,11，逐一枚举非角点plaquette，构造由该hook error加d-2个单数据量子比特故障组成的d-1故障集合，并验证其可形成不可检测的逻辑错误。进一步结合最小权重逻辑算符中junction数量的上界（至多一个）做累积性分析。

Result: 对d=7,9,11，每个非角点plaquette都存在一个被调度的hook error，与d-2个单数据量子比特故障共同构成不可检测的逻辑错误，总故障数仅d-1，且这些hook error全部属于junction hook error，未被Kishony-Fowler的分类覆盖。由此说明在该schedule下bulk稳定子同样需要flag qubit等额外保护才能恢复完整的电路级距离。同时，最小权重逻辑算符至多含一个junction，故junction hook error不能像单色malign hook error那样在同一最小权重逻辑算符上累积。

Conclusion: Kishony-Fowler的任意子类型分类不完备，junction hook error是独立一类可降距hook error，使得color-dependent schedule下所有稳定子（含bulk）仍需hook error防护措施才能达到完整电路级距离；但由于junction数量受限、这类错误不能累积，该schedule仍能将电路级距离提升至d-⌊(d+3)/6⌋，优于空间均匀调度的d/2。

Abstract: Color-dependent syndrome extraction schedules have been proposed to mitigate hook errors in the triangular color code. Recent work by Kishony and Fowler (arXiv:2603.28852) identifies distance-reducing hook errors by the type of anyon for which they provide a shortcut and chooses the CNOT ordering for stabilizers of each color to avoid these errors. We identify an additional class of distance-reducing hooks, which we call junction hook errors, that is not captured by this classification. The two data qubits of a junction hook error terminate anyon strings of two different colors at the point where the three colored strings of a logical operator meet, which we call the junction. As such, the hook error does not correspond to a single type of anyon, yet it still reduces the distance. For distances $d=7,9,11$, we verify that every non-corner plaquette admits a scheduled hook error that, together with $d-2$ single data qubit faults, produces an undetectable logical error using $d-1$ faults. Each of these is a junction hook error. Because of this, every stabilizer, including those in the bulk, still requires additional protection from hook errors (such as flag qubits) to restore the full circuit-level distance with this schedule. A minimum weight logical operator has at most one junction, however, so junction hook errors cannot accumulate on one minimum weight logical operator in the way that single-color malign hook errors can. This helps explain why the schedule still improves the circuit-level distance to $d-\lfloor (d+3)/6\rfloor$, rather than the $d/2$ distance associated with spatially uniform color code scheduling.

</details>


### [274] [Semiclassical dynamics of higher-order squeezing: self-adjoint extensions and coherent returns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33378)
*A. E. Teretenkov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文通过边界振幅与相干态的半经典传播，把高阶压缩哈密顿量的自伴扩张问题与其经典轨迹在有限时间内到达无穷的奇异性联系起来；用量子实现的幺正射线矩阵刻画经过无穷后的Husimi测度极限（点质量及权重），并证明任意固定次数穿越后的极限由射线矩阵的幂决定，而非其模方矩阵的迭代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶压缩哈密顿量存在多个自伴实现（扩张），其经典轨迹在有限时间内发散到无穷，量子与经典两侧的“边界行为”此前缺乏统一描述；作者希望用半经典相干态传播与边界振幅把这两个看似独立的问题联系起来，并澄清无穷穿越后概率测度的极限结构。

Method: 以边界振幅联系自伴扩张问题；用离散傅里叶变换给出每个量子实现的“汇到源”射线矩阵；证明结合满足精确扩张边界条件的有限阶近似尾解、全范数高斯匹配与平方可和残差估计；对正能量情形推导任意固定穿越次数后的极限；并用保持能量的Liouville扩张的集中复振幅实现相应极限。

Result: 首次穿越无穷后，完整Husimi概率测度收敛为出射经典分支上的点质量，权重等于射线矩阵元素的模方；对正能量，任意固定穿越次数后的振幅按幺正射线矩阵的幂演化，而不是其模方矩阵的迭代；这些极限可由Liouville扩张下的集中复振幅实现，从而区分确定性经典粘合与相干边界混合。

Conclusion: 量子自伴扩张的选择，通过离散傅里叶型射线矩阵，精确编码了经典轨迹穿越无穷后的分支权重与多次穿越的相干混合规律；模方矩阵只适用于单次穿越的权重，多次穿越需要保留相位（幺正矩阵幂），这为区分确定性经典粘合与相干边界混合提供了判据。

Abstract: Higher-order squeezing Hamiltonians have several self-adjoint realizations, while their classical trajectories reach infinity in finite time. We relate these extension problems through boundary amplitudes and the semiclassical propagation of coherent states. A discrete Fourier transformation gives the sink-to-source ray matrix of each quantum realization. After the first passage through infinity, the full Husimi probability measure converges to point masses on outgoing classical branches, with weights equal to the squared moduli of the matrix entries. The proof combines finite-order approximate tail solutions satisfying the exact extension boundary condition with full-norm Gaussian matching and a square-summable residual estimate. For positive energy, we determine the limit after any fixed number of passages: amplitudes evolve by powers of the unitary ray matrix, not by iterations of its squared-modulus matrix. We also realize these limits by concentrated complex amplitudes for an energy-preserving Liouville extension, distinguishing deterministic classical gluings from coherent boundary mixing.

</details>


### [275] [Decision-aware validation of zero-noise extrapolation in QAOA optimization landscapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33386)
*Vicenzo Scavino Alfaro*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在QAOA第一层MaxCut（4–6量子比特）的模拟中，零噪声外推（ZNE）虽能降低观测量的均方误差（MSE），却可能在有限采样下导致参数选择的决策遗憾（regret）略微上升；这种效应经过严格多重检验仅少数条件成立，但可复现，并且无法从聚合MSE中看出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ZNE通常以外推后观测量的精度（如MSE）作为有效性判据，但变分量子优化是用这些观测量来选择参数。因此需要回答：ZNE带来的精度增益是否、以及在多大程度上会与更差的决策同时出现。

Method: 对4–6量子比特MaxCut的QAOA深度一进行模拟，每个采样景观为120×120网格，在相同每格总采样预算下比较Richardson一至三阶与未缓解估计。记录MSE与所选网格最小值的理想遗憾。先做675条件的探索性筛选（逐点随机种子存在碰撞），用Holm族错误率校正；再以每条件100次新鲜配对重复进行冻结确认；并在有界控制网格上计算精确一阶与二阶矩，区分偏差路径（残余偏差重排均值景观）与方差路径（Richardson权重放大有限采样噪声）。

Result: 540个重复条件中仅有4个经Holm校正后同时表现为MSE方向性改善与遗憾上升，冻结确认全部复现：MSE中位改善7.65–73.09%，对应归一化遗憾中位上升仅0.0031–0.0073。精确矩分析未发现偏差路径导致的逆转：缓解后的均值景观保留理想极小值，ZNE可使期望MSE降低一半以上，但有限采样选择仍带来更高平均遗憾。决策损失主要来自方差路径。

Conclusion: 在该基准下，通过严格检验仍存活的决策损失数量少且幅度小，但可复现，且不能由聚合MSE揭示。作者给出配对基准，并建议在面向优化的误差缓解评估中，除估计误差外同时报告决策遗憾。结论限于模拟的穷举网格，不涉及真实硬件或优化器循环。

Abstract: Zero-noise extrapolation (ZNE) is usually validated by the accuracy of mitigated observables, yet variational quantum optimization uses those observables to select parameters. We ask whether, and by how much, an accuracy gain from ZNE coincides with a worse decision. In depth-one simulations of the quantum approximate optimization algorithm (QAOA) for MaxCut on four to six qubits, each sampled $120\times120$ landscape yields both its mean-squared error (MSE) and the ideal regret of its selected grid minimum, comparing Richardson orders one to three with unmitigated estimation at the same total shot budget per grid point. In an exploratory screen of 675 conditions, whose per-point random seeds collide, a familywise (Holm) analysis retains only four of 540 replication conditions as showing both directional MSE improvement and regret increase. A frozen confirmation with 100 fresh paired repetitions per condition reproduces all four: median MSE improvements of 7.65-73.09% accompany median normalized regret increases of only 0.0031-0.0073. We distinguish a bias route, in which residual bias reorders the mean landscape, from a variance route, in which Richardson weights amplify finite-shot noise. Exact first and second moments on a bounded control grid find no bias-route reversal: mitigated mean landscapes preserve the ideal minima, and ZNE can more than halve expected MSE, yet finite-shot selections incur higher mean regret. Decision losses that survive strict testing are few and small in this benchmark, but reproducible and not revealed by aggregate MSE. We present a paired benchmark and recommend reporting decision regret alongside estimation error when mitigation is assessed for optimization. Results concern simulated exhaustive grids, not hardware or optimizer loops.

</details>


### [276] [Exact operator bounds on Leggett-Garg violations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33429)
*Rathindra Nath Das,Arnab Kundu,Nemai Chandra Sarkar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立 LG 不等式违背与时空密度核负本征值权重（负性）之间的普适精确关系：对任意维度、初态、动力学与测量规则，给出满足关联的核负性的最小下限，且该下限非零当且仅当至少一个 LG 不等式被违背。


<details>
