  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LG 不等式检验跨时刻关联能否由历史的联合正概率分布刻画，违背要求任何复现该关联的准概率分布含负值；但反之负值存在并不必然意味着违背，二者定量联系此前不清楚。作者希望给出 LG 违背与核负性之间的普适定量界，并刻画不同测量方案（Lüders 投影 vs. 分辨自旋能级测量）以及加入额外不相容可观测量时可达的最小负性。

Method: 将密度矩阵描述推广到多时刻的时空密度核，以其负本征值权重作为负性量度；对任意测量次数的 LG 检验，通过精确关系求解"最兼容"（即负性最小）的核，导出与系统维度、初态、动力学、测量规则无关的普遍下界，并分别分析三时刻 Lüders 型检验与可分辨单个自旋能级的测量，以及在模型中引入额外不相容量子比特可观测量后的进一步精确最小值。

Result: 得到 LG 违背与负性权重的普适界：最小核负性非零当且仅当至少存在一个 LG 不等式违背。三时刻 Lüders LG 检验最多只能证明（certify）1/8 的负性，而分辨单个自旋能级的测量可逼近 1/2。进一步加入不相容量子比特可观测量可给出新的精确最小值，该值可超过仅 LG 的上限，而不增大 LG 违背量。

Conclusion: LG 违背与时空密度核负性之间存在精确的定量对应：负性最小值是 LG 违背存在的充要标志；测量方案的选择显著影响可认证的负性比例（Lüders 仅 1/8，自旋能级分辨可近 1/2）；额外不相容可观测量能突破 LG 单独给出的负性上限，说明仅用 LG 违背度量非经典性是不充分的，需结合测量分辨率与可观测量集合来评估。

Abstract: Leggett-Garg (LG) inequalities test whether correlations measured at different times admit a positive joint probability distribution over histories. A violation requires negative values in any joint quasiprobability distribution reproducing those correlations, but such distributions can have negative values even without a violation. Spacetime density kernels extend the density-matrix description to multiple times and are used to study timelike entanglement. We establish a universal bound that relates LG violations to their negative eigenvalue weight. For LG tests involving any number of measurements, we determine the least compatible kernel negativity through an exact relation valid for any system dimension, initial state, dynamics, or measurement rule. This minimum is nonzero if and only if at least one LG inequality is violated. Three-time Lüders LG tests certify at most one eighth of negativity, whereas measurements resolving individual spin levels can approach one half. Including additional incompatible qubit observables gives a further exact minimum that can exceed the LG-only ceiling without increasing the LG violation.

</details>


### [277] [Designing Quantum Solutions to the Closest String Problem with the Non-Variational QWOA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33532)
*Benjamin Rossdeutscher,Jingbo Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出 Closest String 问题的端到端量子实现（基于 NV-QWOA），自动将任意实例编译为门级量子线路，给出资源估计并提出不依赖实例调优的相位分离参数 γ 方案，使最优与近优解被稳定放大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Closest String 属 NP 难组合优化问题，搜索空间随规模指数增长，需要借助量子算法探索加速可能；同时已有量子方法多为变分框架，缺乏显式门级实现与可推广的参数设定。

Method: 采用非变分量子行走优化算法（NV-QWOA）：自动化流程将任意实例转为由一/二比特门构成的线路，依次完成候选解编码、目标函数求值，并用 NV-QWOA 对高质量解做幅度放大；同时提出依赖问题规模、无需实例特定优化的相位分离参数 γ 设定，以抑制模 2π 的过度相位缠绕。

Result: 解析资源估计给出量子比特复杂度 O(nm log k)、线路深度 O(pm log² n + pn log n)（n 为串长、m 为参考串数、k 为字母表规模、p 为迭代次数）；在所模拟的各问题规模下算法均稳定放大最优与近优候选解。

Conclusion: 工作提供了显式门级实现、改进的相位缩放方法，并为硬件实验及与经典算法的基准对比奠定基础。

Abstract: Quantum computing offers promising approaches to combinatorial optimization problems whose search spaces grow exponentially with problem size. The Closest String problem belongs to this category, which seeks a string that minimizes the maximum Hamming distance to any string in a given collection of reference strings. In this work, we present an end-to-end quantum implementation of the Closest String problem using the Non-Variational Quantum Walk Optimization Algorithm (NV-QWOA), evaluating its resource requirements and solution amplification capabilities. Our automated workflow converts arbitrary problem instances into quantum circuits expressed in one- or two-qubit gates that encode candidate solutions, evaluate the objective function, and apply NV-QWOA optimisation to increase the measurement probabilities of high-quality solutions. Analytical resource estimates yield a qubit complexity of $\mathcal{O}(nm\log k)$ and a circuit depth of $\mathcal{O}(pm\log^2 n + pn\log n)$, where $n$ is the string length, $m$ is the number of reference strings, $k$ is the alphabet size, and $p$ is the number of NV-QWOA iterations. We introduce a revised prescription for the phase-separation parameter, $γ$, that depends on problem size but requires no instance-specific optimization. This prescription mitigates excessive phase wrapping modulo $2π$, which can weaken the relationship between objective values and the phases guiding the optimization dynamics. Across all simulated problem sizes, the algorithm consistently amplifies optimal and near-optimal candidates. This work provides an explicit gate-level implementation, an improved phase-scaling method, and a foundation for hardware experiments and benchmarking against classical algorithms.

</details>


### [278] [When is a bosonic operator a Hamiltonian?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33545)
*Felix Fischer,Hermann Schulz-Baldes,Daniel Burgarth,Davide Lonigro*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究由x、p的多项式（厄米多项式型）算子所定义量子动力学的唯一性问题，提出一种仅依赖系数即可判定唯一解存在性及解族大小的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续变量量子信息中越来越多使用奇异的非高斯多项式算子，但同一多项式可能定义零个、一个或无穷多个物理上不同的量子动力学；这类障碍在代数层面不可见，且会破坏有限维截断的收敛性（不收敛或悄然收敛到错误动力学）。

Method: 开发一种可实现为算法的判定方法，输入仅为算子多项式的系数，无需用户提供泛函分析层面的信息，即可判定三种情形（唯一、无解、无穷族）中哪一种发生，并给出解族规模。

Result: 对高斯算子，答案恒为唯一；对非高斯算子——连续变量量子计算不可或缺的范畴——即使在简单且物理自然的哈密顿量上，其他两种情形（无解、无穷多解）也会出现。作者将算法应用于近期文献中的算子。

Conclusion: 非高斯多项式算子定义动力学的唯一性不能想当然，必须加以判定；所提算法提供了纯系数层面的可判定判据，对有限维截断的可靠性与连续变量量子计算的正确建模具有直接意义。

Abstract: Hermitian polynomials in the quadrature operators $x$ and $p$ are the basic ingredients of continuous-variable quantum information, and increasingly exotic ones are now routinely engineered in superconducting circuits and trapped ions. However, such a polynomial need not define unique quantum dynamics: it may define exactly one, none, or an infinite family of physically distinct ones. Such obstructions are invisible to algebraic arguments and can compromise finite-dimensional truncations, which may fail to converge or silently converge to the wrong dynamics. We develop a method, implemented as an algorithm, which decides which case occurs, and how large the family is, from the coefficients alone, with no functional-analytic input from the user. For Gaussian operators the answer is always one; for non-Gaussian ones, a regime continuous-variable quantum computing cannot do without, the other outcomes occur already for simple and physically natural Hamiltonians. We apply the algorithm to operators drawn from the recent literature.

</details>


### [279] [Competing Fault Histories in Lattice-Surgery Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33591)
*Niharika Verma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究格点手术宇称测量中"不同物理故障产生相同探测记录却携带相反逻辑标签"的歧义问题，在距离3合并-分裂线路中量化保留特定单故障解码决策的代价，并通过通道衰减干预与预注册似然预测检验该机制的物理归因与可推广性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测器记录对物理故障存在简并：单门故障与复位/读出对在完整综合测量上可能互相竞争，导致解码器难以区分逻辑上相反的故障。作者希望弄清这种歧义对格点手术宇称测量逻辑性能的实际代价，并验证"特定物理通道造成该代价"这一解释是否可作为预测依据。

Method: 在距离3的merge–split线路中比较单门故障与reset/readout对在三个完整综合测量上的竞争；用全综合测量概率给出同时95%置信下界；通过衰减8个相关通道并与匹配的非目标抑制作对照，比较override效应的变化；并以采样前固定的似然预测来选取决策，在混合Pauli噪声及扩展制备与恢复后进行测试。

Result: 在某一噪声点，保留所选单故障决策的代价获得16.64 ppm的同时95%下置信界；改变这些决策在低噪声下有害、较高噪声下有利；两种干预对解码效应的影响不同，从而区分出与这些位置相关的贡献；扩展制备与恢复后测试得到13.20 ppm的降低，同时95%区间为[5.55, 20.84] ppm。

Conclusion: 修正后的决策需放弃部分单故障纠正，机制适用范围有限：在相同见证选择规则下，完全展开的距离5线路未识别出候选，说明该有限噪声解码权衡可与具体物理通道联系，但其预测逻辑宇称性能的能力受距离与线路规模限制。

Abstract: Different physical faults can produce the same detector record while carrying opposite logical labels. We investigate the consequence of this ambiguity for a lattice surgery parity measurement. In a distance-three merge--split circuit, single-gate faults compete with reset/readout pairs on three complete syndromes. Full-syndrome probabilities give a simultaneous 95\% lower confidence bound of $16.64$ parts per million on the cost of retaining the selected single-fault decisions at one noise point. Changing those decisions is harmful at low noise and beneficial at higher noise. To test the physical explanation, we attenuate eight implicated channels and compare the change in override effect with a matched non-target suppression. The two interventions change the decoder effect differently, resolving a contribution associated with those locations. Likelihood predictions fixed before sampling then select useful decisions under mixed Pauli noise and after extending preparation and recovery. The latter test gives a reduction of $13.20$ parts per million, with simultaneous 95\% bounds $[5.55,20.84]$. The mechanism is limited in scope: the revised decisions give up certain single-fault corrections, and under the same witness-selection rule no candidate is identified in the fully expanded distance-five circuit. These results connect a finite-noise decoding trade-off to specific physical channels and test how far that explanation can be used to predict logical-parity performance.

</details>


### [280] [Krylov geometry and optimal recovery under uncertain coherent noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33607)
*Abhishek Chowdhury*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文研究带未观测角度的相干旋转误差在给定校准信息下的恢复极限，借助两个多项式投影（物理Krylov链投影与低角度幂投影）给出弱噪声下最优纠缠失真（entanglement infidelity）的精确领头项，并刻画未知角度分布下最优最坏先验失真随校准量β_r的标度规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从含未观测角度的相干旋转中恢复时，可恢复性同时取决于编码和校准所揭示的噪声信息，但二者对恢复极限的联合作用缺乏定量刻画；尤其在角度分布未知、仅有少量校准数据（如单一矩量β_r）时，最优解码器能达到的失真量级尚不清楚。

Method: 针对已知有限维生成元与一次最终恢复，构造两个多项式投影：物理Krylov链投影将可纠正误差幅度与首次态依赖失真分离，投影到低角度幂的子空间识别相干解码器可抵消的部分。在显式秩条件与矩条件下推导领头最优纠缠失真的精确值并构造达优恢复。对未知角度分布，针对所有与校准相容的分布优化单一解码器；当谱频率与校准频率为公共频率的整数倍时，利用矩的正性将其化为精确有限维优化，并分析β_r单一校准数据下基于生成元幂次可纠正性（最高到r-1次）的最坏先验失真标度。

Result: 在秩与矩条件下得到弱噪声领头最优纠缠失真的精确表达式及对任意有限逻辑维数d可达的恢复；最优最坏输入误差在领头阶介于该失真值的1至d倍之间。未知角度分布下：若唯一校准数据为β_r=E[sin^{2r}(θ/2)]，最优最坏先验失真为O(β_r)当且仅当生成元幂次至r-1次均可纠正；若首个不可纠正幂次为m<r，最优损失按β_r^{m/r}标度，其尖锐系数由一普适矩问题确定。无损耗四光子存储中，两个校准时长下的粒子数测量可保留完全信息下的四阶误差系数（固定对称窄噪声族、正方差且四阶矩有限），而单一时长仅允许噪声宽度二次的损失。

Conclusion: 编码结构与校准信息共同决定相干旋转的可恢复极限，可由Krylov链与低角度幂两个投影统一刻画；校准仅提供β_r时，最优失真的标度由生成元的最小不可纠正幂次m与矩阶r之比m/r决定，且在四光子存储中通过双时长粒子数测量可达到完全信息下的四阶精度。

Abstract: Recovery from a coherent rotation with an unobserved angle depends on both the encoding and what calibration reveals about the noise. We determine their combined limit for a known finite-dimensional generator and one final recovery. Two polynomial projections organize the answer. A physical Krylov chain separates correctable error amplitudes from their first state-dependent distortion; projection onto lower-angle powers identifies the part that a coherent decoder can cancel. Under explicit rank and moment conditions, their combination gives the exact leading optimal entanglement infidelity at weak noise and a recovery attaining it for any finite logical dimension. The optimal worst-input error lies between one and $d$ times that infidelity at leading order, for logical dimension $d$. When the angle distribution is unknown, we optimize one decoder for every distribution compatible with the calibration. When spectral and calibration frequencies are integer multiples of a common frequency, moment positivity turns this into an exact finite optimization. Small measured means can still allow rare finite rotations. If the sole calibration datum is $β_r=\mathbb E[\sin^{2r}(θ/2)]$, with $θ$ the wrapped rotation phase at that common frequency, the optimal worst-prior infidelity is $O(β_r)$ exactly when generator powers through degree $r-1$ are correctable. When the first uncorrectable power has degree $m<r$, the optimal loss scales as $β_r^{m/r}$, with a sharp coefficient fixed by a universal moment problem. In a loss-free four-photon memory, population measurements at two calibration durations retain the fully informed quartic error coefficient for a fixed symmetric narrow-noise family with positive variance and finite fourth moment; a single duration permits loss quadratic in the noise width.

</details>


### [281] [The Coherent State Projection Eigenvalue Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33613)
*Peter D. Drummond,Margaret D. Reid*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了投影相干态在连续极限下是称生成算符的本征态，并给出包含守恒本征值的相空间微分恒等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作提出玻色Hilbert空间的循环称投影扩展相干态表示，可大幅降低具称玻色量子系统相空间模拟中的采样误差，但缺乏对该投影相干态连续极限性质的严格理论刻画。

Method: 对投影相干态的连续极限建立本征值定理，从理论上证明该极限下投影相干态为称生成算符的本征态，并据此推导相空间中的微分恒等式。

Result: 证明投影相干态在连续极限下确实是称生成算符的本征态，所得微分恒等式显式包含守恒的本征值。

Conclusion: 该本征值定理为投影相干态表示提供了严格的数学基础；基于这些微分恒等式改进多体物理中Fokker-Planck方程与随机方程的应用将在别处给出。

Abstract: Recent papers have developed a cyclic symmetry projected extension for coherent state representations of bosonic Hilbert space. This extension can greatly reduce sampling errors in phase-space simulations of bosonic quantum systems that have symmetries. Here we prove an eigenvalue theorem for the continuum limit of the projected coherent states. Our intuitively appealing result is that in the continuum limit the projected coherent states are eigenstates of the symmetry generating operator. This result gives a differential identity in phase-space that includes the conserved eigenvalues. Detailed applications of these differential identities to obtaining improved Fokker-Planck and stochastic equations in many-body physics will be given elsewhere.

</details>


### [282] [Path-Distinguishability Gaps at Fixed Visibility in Noisy Qubit Interferometers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33632)
*Mohd Asad Siddiqui*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 固定含噪双路径干涉仪下，刻画"可达区域"：对每个可行条纹可见度，Helstrom最优路径可区分度构成至多两个闭区间，且可能被间隙分隔；间隙起源与轴向标记极化、非幺正制备等因素相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 波粒二象性关系只给出可见度与路径可区分度的上界，却不能确定在给定（含噪）干涉仪中二者实际可达的取值集合。本文意在刻画固定源、制备通道、路径相关相互作用与后续标记通道条件下的完整可达区域。

Method: 以带量子比特标记（marker）的双路径干涉仪为模型，固定源、制备通道、路径相关相互作用与后续标记通道，仅改变标记输入态；用Helstrom最优判据计算路径可区分度，分析固定条纹可见度下可区分度的区间结构（数值/解析几何分析），并给出显式信道与定态Lindblad演化实现。

Result: 每一可行条纹可见度下，Helstrom最优可区分度构成至多两个闭区间且可有间隙；两区间来自具有相同条纹幅度模的相反轴向标记极化。固定复条纹幅度则得连通区间；平衡原始路径区分下，出现间隙需非幺正（nonunital）标记制备。对平衡纯源、相对π旋转且无后续标记噪声，给出某一垂直于制备位移的旋转轴下间隙存在的充要几何判据；并给出间隙随制备几何与演化变化的显式信道及定态Lindblad实现，含固定分数可见度下间隙起始与优化的精确公式。

Conclusion: 可见度—可区分度可达集在固定含噪干涉仪中一般非凸、甚至非连通，二象性不等式不足以描述其结构；在有限分辨率、校准误差条件下给出排除某些可区分度区间的充分界。

Abstract: The wave--particle duality relation bounds visibility and path distinguishability but does not determine their attainable values in a fixed noisy interferometer. We characterize the complete attainable region for a two-path interferometer with a qubit marker, varying the marker input while fixing the source, preparation channel, path-dependent interaction, and subsequent marker channel. At each feasible fringe visibility, the Helstrom-optimal distinguishabilities form at most two closed intervals, which can be separated by a gap. The intervals arise from opposite axial marker polarizations with the same fringe-amplitude modulus. Fixing the complex fringe amplitude instead gives a connected interval; for balanced original-path discrimination, a gap requires nonunital marker preparation. For a balanced pure source, relative $π$ rotations, and no subsequent marker noise, we obtain a necessary and sufficient geometric criterion for a gap under some rotation axis perpendicular to the preparation displacement. Explicit channels and a time-independent Lindblad realization show how the gap depends on preparation geometry and evolution, with exact onset and optimization formulas at fixed fractional visibility. We also give sufficient bounds for excluding distinguishability intervals under calibrated finite-resolution errors.

</details>


### [283] [Task-Directed Spectral Design for Local Quantum Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33723)
*Guy Hay,Nir Sharon*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究在投影保真度访问下、已知参数模型/可信中心/可微下游任务时的局部纯态层析；利用拉回Fubini–Study度量与任务度量定义坐标不变的广义本征方向，构建固定的测地保真度探针库，并给出任务方差最优性与贪心预算最优性证明，数值上以极少设定数取得优于自适应方法的任务误差与采集成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多层析任务只需状态的低维函数（任务相关量），而非完整状态；自适应/反馈式测量虽灵活但设定多、延迟高、采集成本大，因此希望在实际采集前就设计一个无需反馈、设定数少的固定测量方案，并保证其局部任务方差最优性。

Method: 1) 以拉回Fubini–Study度量和下游任务度量构造坐标不变的广义本征方向；2) 将其实现为固定的测地保真度探针库；3) 证明两项最优性：与任务坐标对齐的探针在方向上唯一地最小化方差；当先验与测量信息在该基下对角时，递减回报贪心规则在谱库上精确最小化整数预算的局部任务方差代理；4) 在共同单发信息与大预算条件下给出与任意探针方向的绝对/相对差距阶O(B^-3)与O(B^-2)；5) 给出闭式局部估计量及任务度量定位投影（定位成立时不会增大二次任务损失）；6) 六比特数值实验（Schmidt谱设计）与自适应方法对比。

Result: Schmidt谱设计仅激活一至两个测量设定，在所有测试维度与副本预算下平均任务误差低于事后最佳自适应结果；全态层析中在测试的局部区间内平均不保真度同样更低；固定批式方案无反馈回路、设定数远少，在所给延迟模型下采集成本约为自适应方法的10^3–10^4分之一。

Conclusion: 在已知参数模型与可微下游任务的局部纯态层析中，基于任务度量与Fubini–Study度量几何构建的固定测地探针库可达到局部任务方差意义下的最优（含贪心整数预算最优及大预算下的收敛阶保证），并以极少的测量设定、无反馈、低采集成本在实践中优于自适应方案。

Abstract: Many tomography tasks require only a low-dimensional function of the state. We study local pure-state tomography under projective-fidelity access when a parametric model, a credible center, and a differentiable downstream task are known before acquisition. The pullback Fubini--Study metric and the task metric define coordinate-invariant generalized eigendirections, which we realize as a fixed library of geodesic fidelity probes. We prove two optimality results: the probe aligned with a task coordinate is its unique variance-minimizing location up to orientation, and, when prior and measurement information are diagonal in this basis, a diminishing-return greedy rule exactly minimizes the integer-budget local task-variance surrogate over the spectral library. Under common per-shot information and the stated large-budget condition, the absolute and relative gaps to arbitrary probe directions are respectively \(O(B^{-3})\) and \(O(B^{-2})\). A closed-form local estimator is followed by a task-metric localization projection that cannot increase quadratic task loss when localization holds. In six-qubit numerical experiments, the Schmidt-spectrum design activates only one or two settings and has lower mean task error than the post hoc best adaptive results across all tested dimensions and copy budgets. For full-state tomography, it likewise has lower mean infidelity throughout the tested local regime. The fixed batch uses no feedback loops and far fewer settings; under the illustrative latency model, its acquisition cost is roughly three to four orders of magnitude lower than that of the adaptive methods.

</details>


### [284] [Induced Bell-CHSH observables in the Fewster-Verch measurement scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33773)
*M. S. Guimaraes,I. Roditi,S. P. Sorella*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在Fewster–Verch测量框架下，用1+1维自由Majorana场与被局域耦合的Majorana探针，显式构造出诱导的二值Bell可观测量；在两套显式可及性假设下，其在由真空制备的态上达到Tsirelson界，并可推广到耦合标量场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Summers–Werner定理只表明互补楔形区上真空极大违反Bell–CHSH不等式，属于存在性陈述：既不给出可观测量，也不给出测量装置。本文旨在把该存在性结果落实为算子层面的显式实现。

Method: 在Fewster–Verch框架中，让自由Majorana场与Majorana探针局域耦合，取探针可观测量诱导出的系统可观测量作为Bell可观测量。关键技术点：(1) 诱导的系统涂抹因子精确分解为 f_h = k·b_{λ,h}，支撑由紧支撑耦合剖面k控制；(2) 二值性通过标定并归一化诱导涂抹因子来实现，而非要求探针可观测量本身二值；(3) 仅对探针做正规序，得到Alice与Bob诱导双线性之积，这是非乘性诱导映射本身无法提供的。

Result: 构造出1+1维自由Majorana场的二值Bell可观测量，其支撑受耦合剖面k控制、二值性由归一化保证，并借探针侧正规序获得A、B诱导双线性之积；在显式可及性假设下，所制备真空态中的关联饱和Tsirelson界（极大违反CHSH）。构造可推广至耦合标量场。

Conclusion: Summers–Werner的极大真空纠缠可由显式、局域且物理上可实现（探针诱导）的二值可观测量饱和Tsirelson界来体现，从而把纯存在性陈述提升为具体测量方案；同构思路可移植到耦合标量场。

Abstract: The Summers-Werner theorem shows that the vacuum of a relativistic quantum field theory violates the Bell-CHSH inequality maximally on complementary wedges, but it is an existence statement: it exhibits neither the observables nor an apparatus that measures them. We give an explicit operator-level realization within the Fewster-Verch measurement framework, obtaining dichotomic Bell observables of a free Majorana field in $1+1$ dimensions as observables induced by local couplings to Majorana probes. The induced system smearing factorizes exactly as $f_h=k\,b_{λ,h}$, so its support is controlled by the compactly supported coupling profile $k$; dichotomy is imposed by calibrating and normalizing the induced smearings rather than required of the probe observable; and a probe-only normal ordering yields the product of Alice's and Bob's induced bilinears, which the non-multiplicative induced map alone does not provide. Under explicit attainability hypotheses the induced observables saturate the Tsirelson bound in a state prepared from the vacuum. The construction extends to coupled scalar fields.

</details>


### [285] [Efficient Block Encodings of Vibrational Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33800)
*Shashank G. Mehendale,Ignacio Loaiza,Shreyas Malpathak,Stepan Fomichev,Danial Motlagh,Artur F. Izmaylov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为分子振动哈密顿量的二次量子化 n-模表示构造了基于 Pauli 分解与张量超压缩（THC）的 LCU 表示及高效 block-encoding 电路，并将 BLISS 技术推广到振动哈密顿量以降低 LCU 1-范数，从而显著降低量子相位估计的 Toffoli 与 T 门成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子振动模拟是量子计算在量子化学中有前景的应用，但现有 block-encoding 方法电路成本高；需借助哈密顿量分解与 Toffoli-量子比特权衡来降低成本。

Method: 针对与二次量子化电子哈密顿量代数结构平行的振动哈密顿量，基于 Pauli 分解与 THC 推导 LCU 表示，构造对应的 block-encoding 电路；并将 Block-Invariant Symmetry Shift（BLISS）推广至振动哈密顿量以减小 LCU 1-范数。

Result: BLISS-THC 相较现有 block-encoding 方法使量子相位估计的 Toffoli 成本降低两个数量级以上；相较近期 Trotter 方法 T 门数最多降低九倍，且节省随体系规模增大而增加。渐近上（忽略多对数因子），Pauli 与 THC LCU 以额外辅助比特换取模式数标度的二次改进，THC 还在模态数标度上再获二次改进。

Conclusion: 哈密顿量因式分解与 Toffoli-量子比特权衡对降低分子振动量子模拟的计算成本至关重要。

Abstract: Simulation of molecular vibrations is emerging as a promising application of quantum computing in quantum chemistry. In this work, we develop new strategies for block-encoding the second-quantized $n$-mode representation of the vibrational Hamiltonian, whose algebraic structure closely parallels that of the second-quantized electronic Hamiltonian. We derive linear-combination-of-unitaries (LCU) representations of this Hamiltonian based on Pauli decomposition and tensor hypercontraction (THC) and construct efficient circuits for the corresponding block encodings. We also extend the Block-Invariant Symmetry Shift (BLISS) technique to vibrational Hamiltonians, yielding a lower LCU 1-norm and, consequently, a lower overall circuit cost. For the molecules considered here, BLISS-THC reduces the Toffoli cost of quantum phase estimation by more than two orders of magnitude relative to existing block-encoding approaches. In comparison with recent Trotter-based methods, it shows up to a ninefold reduction in the T-gate count, with the relative savings tending to increase with system size. Asymptotically, and up to polylogarithmic factors, both the Pauli and THC LCUs trade additional ancillas for a quadratic improvement in scaling with the number of modes, while THC provides an additional quadratic improvement in scaling with the number of modals. These results highlight the importance of Hamiltonian factorizations and Toffoli-qubit tradeoffs for reducing the computational cost of quantum simulations of molecular vibrations.

</details>


### [286] [Initial-State Preparation for Strongly Correlated Molecules from Matrix Product States: Benchmarking and Environment-Aware Divide-and-Conquer Compilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33834)
*Haiqi Liu,Longfei Chang,Zhendong Li,Wei-Hai Fang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 系统基准评测了分析型、变分型和混合型MPS编译方法（含作者提出的分治环境感知算法DCE），给出不同体系的实用选型指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典优化的矩阵乘积态（MPS）编译为量子线路是强关联分子初态制备的可行路径，但现有方法的精度、门开销与经典可扩展性缺乏系统评估。

Method: 在氢链与铁硫簇等强关联体系上，比较代表性解析法、变分法和混合法MPS编译；以态不保真度、CNOT门数、经典运行时间与并行可扩展性为指标；并提出分治环境感知编译算法（DCE），按各局部等距近似对制备态保真度的贡献进行优化。

Result: 直接变分编译在约10个量子比特、约10量级键维或中等精度（不保真度约10^-1）的小体系上门数更省；分治方法在更大体系、键维约10–10^2且目标不保真度低于10^-1时扩展性更好；在同一类方法中，环境感知优化比基于酉距离的编译精度更高，并保持任务级并行性；对FeMoco，DCE配合纠缠最小化轨道（EMO）得到的MPS是资源高效的初态制备路径。

Conclusion: 两类方法存在互补的适用区间，为强关联分子量子模拟中MPS编译方法的选择与后续开发提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Compiling classically optimized matrix product states (MPSs) into quantum circuits is a promising route to initial-state preparation for strongly correlated molecules, but the accuracy, gate cost, and classical scalability of existing MPS compilation methods have not been systematically assessed. Here, we benchmark representative analytical, variational, and hybrid MPS compilation methods for strongly correlated systems including hydrogen chains and iron-sulfur clusters. We compare state infidelity, CNOT gate count, classical runtime, and parallel scalability across different system sizes and bond dimensions. We also introduce a divide-and-conquer environment-aware compilation algorithm (DCE), which optimizes each local isometry approximation according to its contribution to the prepared-state fidelity. The results reveal complementary operating regimes: direct variational compilation methods are gate-efficient for some smaller molecular systems with $O(10)$ qubits and $O(10)$ bond dimensions or moderate accuracy targets with infidelity of order $10^{-1}$, whereas divide-and-conquer methods scale more favorably for larger systems and relatively higher bond dimensions of $O(10\text{-}10^2)$, particularly when target infidelities below $10^{-1}$ are required. Within the latter family, environment-aware optimization improves accuracy over unitary-distance-based compilation while retaining task-level parallelism. For the FeMo-cofactor (FeMoco), we find the DCE algorithm together with the MPS obtained in the entanglement-minimized orbitals (EMOs) provides a resource-efficient route to initial-state preparation. Our results provide practical guidance for selecting and developing MPS compilation methods for strongly correlated molecular simulations.

</details>


### [287] [Helstrom geometry with time-dependent spectral calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33848)
*Kaushlendra Kumar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在有限标量-量子比特-标量谱三元组框架下研究链接强度的含时变化，将谱速率分解为可区分性与标定两部分，并刻画与给定正谱记录相容的迹距离历史。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作表明该谱三元组的Connes距离在因子2/Λ下重现Bloch球迹度量；本文旨在厘清链接强度含时变化（独立于量子比特动力学）对谱距离速率的影响。

Method: 对Dirac算符中各向同性链接强度μ_L、μ_R的含时变化进行分析，将谱速率拆分、分离为可区分性贡献与标定贡献；给出退极化算例，并构造固定Λ下的正弦链接失衡。

Result: 退极化算例显示迹距离单调收缩时谱距离仍可增长；一般而言，给定正谱记录可对应多种相容的迹距离历史，包括收缩型与复苏型实现；固定Λ下保持Dirac谱与量子比特度量的正弦失衡使标量-标量距离以失衡周期的一半振荡。

Conclusion: 谱速率中的标定贡献可与可区分性变化解耦，谱信息不足以唯一确定迹距离历史；链接失衡可仅影响标量-标量距离而不改变Dirac谱与量子比特度量。

Abstract: In a recent work we introduced a finite scalar-qubit-scalar spectral triple whose Connes distance reproduces the Bloch-ball trace metric up to a factor $2/Λ$, where $Λ^2=μ_L^2+μ_R^2$ for isotropic link strengths $μ_L,μ_R$ appearing in the proposed Dirac operator. Here we analyse time-dependent variation of these links, independent of the qubit dynamics. In doing so, we separate the distinguishability and calibration contributions to the spectral rate. A depolarizing example demonstrates spectral growth despite a monotonic contraction of the trace-distance. More generally, we characterize the admissible trace-distance histories compatible with a given positive spectral record and exhibit contracting and reviving realizations. At fixed $Λ$, we also study a sinusoidal link imbalance that preserves the Dirac spectrum and qubit metric, while the scalar-scalar distance oscillates with half the period of the imbalance.

</details>


### [288] [Hidden Fisher Information in Many-Body Quantum Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33893)
*Atta ur Rahman,Muhammad Noman,Samira Elghaayda,Saeed Haddadi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出矩Fisher层级 $F_M^{(k)}$ 与读出复杂度尺度 $k_{90}$，量化量子Fisher信息（QFI）中最优读出实现的难度；在非线性集体自旋传感中揭示多体动力学将QFI转移至高阶 $SU(2)$ 张量扇区，$N=100$ 时一阶矩仅获取约 $10^{-2}$ 的QFI、$k_{90}\simeq 28$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QFI 虽给出经全测量优化后的终极精度极限，却无法刻画最优读出实验实现的难易程度，缺乏衡量"测量复杂度"的定量指标。

Method: 引入矩Fisher层级 $F_M^{(k)}$（度 ≤ $k$ 的集体自旋矩所能获取的QFI占比）及读出复杂度尺度 $k_{90}$（恢复90% QFI所需的最低度数）；将其应用于非线性集体自旋传感器，并在弱阻尼下对对称对数导数（SLD）做按张量秩分解的分析。

Result: 多体动力学可保持较大QFI，却把最优读出转移到低阶集体可观测量无法触及的高秩 $SU(2)$ 张量扇区；$N\le 100$ 时一阶矩仅获取约 $10^{-2}$ 的QFI，$k_{90}$ 增至约28；弱阻尼下SLD分析显示非线性协议把最优读出推向最高可用秩并强烈削弱其低秩权重。

Conclusion: 非线性切空间剪切是多体量子计量中有限尺寸读出复杂度瓶颈的物理机制。

Abstract: Quantum Fisher information (QFI) determines the ultimate precision of a quantum sensor after optimization over all allowed measurements, but it does not quantify how difficult the optimal readout is to implement. We introduce a moment Fisher hierarchy $F_M^{(k)}$, which measures the fraction of QFI accessible to collective spin moments up to degree $k$, together with a readout-complexity scale $k_{90}$ defined by the minimum degree required to recover $90\%$ of the QFI. Applying this framework to a nonlinear collective-spin sensor, we show that many-body dynamics can preserve large QFI while transferring the optimal readout into high-rank $SU(2)$ tensor sectors inaccessible to low-order collective observables. For system sizes up to $N=100$, first moments access only $\sim 10^{-2}$ of the available QFI, while the degree required to recover $90\%$ of it grows to $k_{90}\simeq 28$. A rank-resolved analysis of the symmetric logarithmic derivative (SLD) under weak damping shows that the nonlinear protocol pushes the optimal readout toward the highest available tensor ranks while strongly depleting its low-rank weight. These results identify nonlinear tangent-space shear as the mechanism behind a finite-size readout-complexity bottleneck in many-body quantum metrology.

</details>


### [289] [Resource-Optimal Screening for Two-Tier Quantum System Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34003)
*Yi-Ting Chen,Aniket S. Dalvi,Nils Quetschlich*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种资源最优的量子处理器筛查策略：采用两层风险检测架构与最优实验设计，轻量筛查探针定期运行、仅在检测到显著变化时触发完整表征，并推导出闭式最优电路规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QPU需频繁基准测试以追踪硬件漂移，但随机基准测试等标准协议资源消耗大，会与应用负载争夺稀缺的QPU时间。

Method: 将两层风险式测试架构与最优实验设计结合：轻量筛查探针常态化运行，检测到统计显著变化才触发昂贵全表征；在显式资源约束下最大化探针检测灵敏度。针对指数保真度模型推导闭式最优电路规模，并引入"有效门计数"使分析硬件无关。分别实例化于门质量验证（与OED随机基准测试对比）与编译器基准测试（形式化为配对假设检验、推导区分编译策略的最优电路规模）。

Result: 门质量验证中，最优设计筛查探针在等资源成本下检测率超过带OED的随机基准测试4倍以上；两类应用虽观测量与检验统计量不同，却得到结构相同的闭式最优探针电路规模表达式（保真度的函数）。在Rigetti Ankaa-3、IQM Garnet、IQM Emerald上的数值与硬件实验均验证理论预测，观测最优电路规模与推导值一致。

Conclusion: 该框架以低资源开销实现高灵敏硬件漂移筛查，闭式结果具普适性，并经多台真实QPU实验验证。

Abstract: Quantum processing units (QPUs) require frequent benchmarking to track hardware drift, yet standard protocols such as randomized benchmarking are resource-intensive and compete with application workloads for scarce QPU time. We present a resource-optimal screening strategy that combines a two-tier, risk-based testing architecture with optimal experiment design: a lightweight screening probe runs regularly and triggers expensive full characterization only when statistically significant change is detected, while the probe itself is designed to maximize detection sensitivity under explicit resource constraints. For an exponential fidelity model, we derive closed-form optimal circuit sizes and introduce an effective gate count that renders the analysis hardware-independent. We instantiate the strategy for two applications. For gate-quality verification, we show that optimally designed screening probes outperform randomized benchmarking with optimal experimental design (OED) by more than 4$\times$ in detection rate at equal resource cost. For compiler benchmarking, we formalize compiler comparison as a paired hypothesis test and derive the optimal circuit size for distinguishing compilation strategies. Despite their different observables and test statistics, both applications yield structurally the same closed-form expression for the optimal probe circuit size as a function of gate fidelity, illustrating the generality of the framework. Numerical and hardware experiments on QPUs (Rigetti Ankaa-3, IQM Garnet, IQM Emerald) confirm the theoretical predictions, with observed optimal circuit sizes matching the derived values across all devices.

</details>


### [290] [Quantum neuroplastic learning for time-series processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34055)
*Mostafizur Rahaman Laskar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出量子神经可塑学习（QNL），借鉴突触形成、强化与修剪，通过重新组织量子储层中比特间的耦合来训练，而非仅训练经典读出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子储层计算中物理系统通常固定，仅训练经典读出层，限制了系统随任务自适应与长期记忆的能力；生物学习中的突触可塑性提示可动态重构量子比特间的连接以提升性能。

Method: 用资格迹（eligibility traces）引导对耦合、场、门角度和演化时间的局部编辑；当某次编辑的统计改进上界超过复杂度代价时保留该编辑。在重启与在线监控两种协议下，评估短/长期记忆、非线性自回归处理与预测、信道均衡等任务。

Result: 仿真中QNL提升了记忆容量、形成长期回忆并跨多任务持续适应，对需要长时记忆的任务收益最大；在IBM超导处理器ibm_fez上，42比特并行运行6个七比特储层（共128万次采样），仿真中学得并在执行前冻结的配置将信道均衡符号错误率从9.7%降至2.2%，优于同等预算调优的基线。

Conclusion: 可塑的量子储层（训练耦合等结构参数）相比固定系统+经典读出更具优势，且学得配置可迁移至真实量子硬件并取得更低错误率。

Abstract: Quantum machine learning uses quantum states and dynamics to represent and process data. In quantum reservoir computing, the physical system is typically fixed and a classical readout is trained. Inspired by the formation, strengthening and pruning of synapses during biological learning, we propose quantum neuroplastic learning (QNL), which reorganizes the couplings among the qubits of a quantum reservoir, much as experience rewires connections among neurons. Eligibility traces guide local edits to couplings, fields, gate angles and evolution time, and an edit is retained when a statistical bound on its improvement exceeds a complexity cost. We evaluate QNL under restarting and online monitored protocols on short- and long-term memory, nonlinear autoregressive processing and forecasting, and channel equalization tasks. In simulation, QNL enhances memory capacity, develops long-term recall and sustains adaptation across multiple tasks, with the largest benefit for tasks that require extended memory. On the IBM superconducting processor \texttt{ibm\_fez}, with six seven-qubit reservoirs running in parallel on 42 qubits (1.28 million shots in total), configurations learned in simulation and frozen before execution reduced the channel-equalization symbol error rate from 9.7\% to 2.2\% and achieved a lower error rate than a matched-budget tuned baseline.

</details>


### [291] [Two exact quantum queries use at most six variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34079)
*Yaqiao Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 证明了恰好用至多两次量子查询可计算的全布尔函数至多依赖6个输入变量，且该界是紧的，给出首个无条件证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前该结论依赖于对至多4变量布尔函数的数值分类，缺乏无条件的解析证明；同时两查询情形下查询路径向量不必相互正交，干扰使标量权重计数论证失效，构成主要障碍。

Method: 给出一直接的解析证明；建立刚性的支撑-分支分解：固定第一查询支撑中的坐标后，第二查询诱导出一族精确的单查询分支。

Result: 得到全布尔函数在恰好两次量子查询下至多依赖6个输入变量的界，并证明该界是紧的。

Conclusion: 首次以无条件解析方式确立了该6变量上界，且其结构描述本身可能具有独立价值。

Abstract: We give a direct analytic proof that every total Boolean function computable exactly with at most two quantum queries depends on at most six input variables. This bound is sharp. A previous derivation relied on a numerical classification of Boolean functions on at most four variables; to the best of our knowledge, ours is the first unconditional proof. The main difficulty beyond the one-query case is that query-path vectors need not be mutually orthogonal, so interference prevents the scalar weight-counting argument from applying. We overcome this difficulty by establishing a rigid support-and-branch decomposition: after the coordinates in the first-query support are fixed, the second query induces a family of exact one-query branches. This structural description may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [292] [Feasibility of Distributed Fault-Tolerant Quantum Computing: From Quantum Links to Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34143)
*Takumi Kobayashi,Hodaka Kurokawa,Yasunari Suzuki,Hideo Kosaka*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对由微波或光链路互联的超导处理器构成的分布式容错量子计算进行了全面的资源分析，表明在有限通信资源（每节点单条通信信道、1 MHz 贝尔对产生率、90% 保真度）下即可显著降低每节点所需物理量子比特数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实用化容错量子计算需要大量物理量子比特，单处理器扩展困难；分布式架构通过量子网络互联多处理器是可行途径，但远程贝尔对生成技术难度高，因此需在有限通信资源下评估计算性能。

Method: 针对微波链路与光链路互联的超导处理器，开展分布式容错量子计算的综合资源分析，考察通信信道数量、贝尔对产生率与保真度、复用程度等参数对每节点物理量子比特需求和执行时间的影响。

Result: 在实验上可期的前瞻性微波链路假设（1 MHz 物理贝尔对产生率、90% 保真度）下，每节点仅需一条物理通信信道即可大幅降低每节点物理量子比特需求，代价是执行时间约增加十倍；反之，若要降低执行时间开销，则需显著更多的通信资源，包括更高贝尔对保真度和大规模复用。

Conclusion: 结果证明了在有限通信资源下实现分布式容错量子计算的潜在可行性，并为权衡量子链路性能、处理器规模与执行时间提供了定量依据。

Abstract: Practical fault-tolerant quantum computation requires a large number of physical qubits, posing a major challenge for scaling up individual quantum processors. Distributed architectures offer a promising solution by interconnecting multiple processors through quantum networks. However, remote Bell-pair generation remains technically demanding, making it essential to assess computational performance under limited communication resources. In this work, we present a comprehensive resource analysis of distributed fault-tolerant quantum computation using superconducting processors connected by microwave or optical links. We show that even a single physical communication channel per node can substantially reduce the physical-qubit requirements per node under experimentally motivated, forward-looking microwave-link assumptions of a 1 MHz physical Bell-pair generation rate and 90% fidelity, although at the cost of a roughly tenfold increase in execution time. Conversely, reducing the execution-time overhead requires substantially greater communication resources, including higher Bell-pair fidelities and extensive multiplexing. These results demonstrate the potential feasibility of distributed fault-tolerant quantum computation with limited communication resources and provide a quantitative basis for balancing quantum-link performance, processor size, and execution time.

</details>


### [293] [Estimation of covariance matrix for Stokes-like measurement under phase-space deformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34176)
*Pinaki Patra*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究各向异性振子中相空间非对易性的可观测特征，提出通过光学Stokes类测量刻画非对易参数θ和η的光力协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相空间非对易性（位置-位置参数θ、动量-动量参数η）在高能与时空中被广泛讨论，但缺乏低能可及的实验检验途径，因此需要建立与光学可测量量的联系。

Method: 从含θ、η的形变相空间代数出发，用Darboux变换构造正则变量下的等价哈密顿量；导出协方差矩阵，分析非对易参数对机械动力学与光学响应的影响；将振子与相干光学探针耦合，解析给出时变光学相干衰减与非线性Kerr相位；设计干涉检测方案：出射光场与相位可控参考光在平衡分束器合束，用光电探测器测量光电流（Stokes类测量）。

Result: 有效动力学显示振子自由度之间存在关联；光学响应给出了修正的简正模频率，其变化量可用于估计θ与η的实验值。

Conclusion: 论文建立了非对易空间动力学、高斯态关联与实验可及光学可观测量之间的理论联系，提示了一条检验相空间非对易性特征的光力途径。

Abstract: We investigate the signatures of phase-space noncommutativity in an anisotropic oscillator and propose an optomechanical protocol to experimentally characterize it through Stokes-like measurements of an optical field. Starting from a deformed phase-space algebra characterized by the position-position and momentum-momentum noncommutativity parameters $θ$ and $η$, we employ the Darboux transformation to construct an equivalent Hamiltonian in canonical variables. The resulting effective dynamics exhibits correlations between the oscillator degrees of freedom. We derive the corresponding covariance matrix and examine how the noncommutative parameters enter the mechanical dynamics and the optical response.
  By coupling the oscillator to a coherent optical probe, we obtain analytical expressions for the time-dependent optical coherence attenuation and the nonlinear Kerr phase. We then formulate an interferometric detection scheme through Stokes-like measurements, in which the outgoing optical field is combined with a phase-controlled reference beam at a balanced beam splitter, and the resulting photocurrents are measured using photodetectors. The optical response provides access to the modified normal-mode frequencies, which enables us to estimate experimental values of $θ$ and $η$. Our results establish a theoretical connection between NC-space dynamics, Gaussian-state correlations, and experimentally accessible optical observables, suggesting an optomechanical route toward testing signatures of phase-space noncommutativity.

</details>


### [294] [Resonant Multiphoton Conversion Mediated by a Shared Vibration in Cavity QED](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34249)
*Yu-Mu Liu,Qiu-Yun Yang,Na Wang,Ming-Lei Guo,Hong-Fu Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 两个发射体子系综共享的振动可同时建立多光子共振与虚交换路径的相长干涉；相反位移（整数比 r≥2）导致"位移中性"，复合态与 r+1 个腔光子共振且转换路径相长叠加，建立位移比与转换阶数的直接对应；而独立振动虽可调至同一共振，其路径在领头阶相消，说明单纯能量共振不足以保证相干转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多光子相干转换通常依赖能量共振匹配，但共振条件本身是否足以保证转换路径的相干叠加尚不清楚。作者希望探究发射体集体振动自由度（相对位移）能否作为额外调控手段，同时决定多光子共振条件与量子干涉性质。

Method: 理论分析两个发射体子系综共享同一振动模式的情形，考虑相反位移且幅度比为整数 r≥2。通过追踪振动位移在虚光子交换过程中的累积，分析位移中性条件（r 次激发在位移较小子系综中与另一次激发产生的位移精确抵消），并比较共享振动与独立振动两种情形下的转换路径干涉（领头阶求和）。进一步在弱驱动极限下计算光子统计（二阶与三阶关联）以及退相干通道。

Result: 当发射体与腔频率匹配时，复合体系与 r+1 个腔光子共振；振动介导的交叉相互作用使转换路径相长叠加，位移比直接决定转换阶数。独立振动可调至同一共振，但转换路径在领头阶相消。r=2 时该转换抑制三光子符合，而二光子统计接近泊松。弱驱动下，干涉可依据位移相对方向屏蔽所有延迟的二光子关联，三光子统计仍对其敏感。两种共振态均使振动处于未位移状态，故相干性不受共享振动的直接退相位影响，发射体退相位成为更紧的限制。

Conclusion: 相对振动位移可作为控制相干多光子转换及其噪声响应的有效手段；位移中性将位移比与转换阶数联系起来，并提供对振动的退相干保护。

Abstract: We show that a vibration shared by two emitter subensembles can jointly establish multiphoton resonance and constructive interference among the virtual exchange pathways responsible for coherent conversion. For opposite displacements with an integer magnitude ratio $r\geq2$, the displacements induced by $r$ excitations in the less displaced subensemble and one in the other cancel exactly, a condition we call displacement neutrality. When the emitter and cavity frequencies match, the resulting composite is resonant with $r+1$ cavity photons, while the vibration-mediated cross interaction makes the conversion pathways add constructively, establishing a direct relation between the displacement ratio and the conversion order. In contrast, independent vibrations can be tuned to the same resonance, but their conversion pathways cancel at leading order, demonstrating that energy resonance alone does not guarantee coherent conversion. For $r=2$, this conversion suppresses three-photon coincidences while leaving the two-photon statistics nearly Poissonian. In the weak-driving limit, interference can also screen the two-photon correlations at all delays from the relative direction of the displacements, whereas the three-photon statistics remain sensitive to it. Because both resonant states leave the vibration undisplaced, their coherence is protected from direct dephasing by the shared vibration, leaving emitter dephasing as the tighter constraint. These results identify relative vibrational displacements as a means of controlling coherent multiphoton conversion and its noise response.

</details>


### [295] [Minimal ancilla for boosting type-I photonic fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34257)
*A. A. Melkozerov,S. S. Straupe,M. Yu. Saygin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出仅用两个独立辅助单光子的增强型I型聚变门，直接成功概率达5/8，且输出模式固定、鲁棒性更好、资源更省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚变门是线性光学量子计算与通信中制备大规模纠缠光子态的关键，但无额外光子资源时成功率仅1/2；已有增强方案需四个辅助单光子或一个两比特Bell态，制备概率性且资源开销大。

Method: 设计仅含两个独立辅助单光子（无需纠缠辅助输入）的增强型I型聚变门线路；线路以被动元件为主，仅含一个主动元件，依据中间光电探测结果施加相移或交换两个模式。

Result: 直接成功概率为5/8，与四单光子方案相当而辅助光子数减半；成功输出占据固定模式，无需额外条件路由；对有宣布的辅助光子损耗更鲁棒；生成六比特环形图态所需平均单光子数较其他I型方案减少超过4.6%。

Conclusion: 该方案为可扩展的基于聚变的线性光学量子信息处理提供了一种资源高效的basic原语。

Abstract: Fusion gates enable the creation of large entangled photonic states for linear-optical quantum computing and communication protocols. However, their success probability is limited to $1/2$ without additional photonic resources. Previous schemes for boosting type-I fusion have used four ancillary single photons or a two-qubit Bell state, whose preparation is probabilistic and resource intensive. Here we propose a boosted type-I fusion gate using only two independent ancillary single photons and no entangled ancillary input. It achieves a direct success probability of $5/8$, matching that of the previous four-single-photon proposal while using half as many ancillary photons. The circuit is predominantly passive, with a single active element that applies a phase shift or swaps two modes according to intermediate photodetection outcomes. Unlike the previous schemes, successful outputs occupy fixed modes and require no further conditional output routing. In particular, we show that the gate is more robust to heralded ancillary-photon loss than previously proposed boosted gates and reduces the average number of single photons required to generate a six-qubit ring graph state by more than $4.6\%$ compared with other type-I schemes. It therefore offers a resource-efficient primitive for scalable fusion-based photonic quantum information processing.

</details>


### [296] [Observation of measurement-induced localization phenomena in a solid-state spin system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34262)
*Haolin Zhang,Shaoyi Xu,Zihang Zheng,Mengqi Wang,Mingzhe Liu,Zhiyuan Zhao,Ya Wang,Tianyu Xie,Fazhan Shi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作在金刚石中相互作用的 ¹³C 核自旋体系上实验观测到测量诱导局域化现象：单自旋投影测量或全局弱测量均可随测量频率增加显著延长自旋自相关时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子测量可深刻改变多体动力学，例如驱动从体积律混沌相到面积律局域相的相变。混沌相在一般多体系统中自然出现，但实验实现完全由测量驱动的局域相十分困难，主要瓶颈在于需要近乎完美地把系统与环境耗散隔离。已有实验多基于线路模型，缺少在真实多体哈密顿量演化下的验证。

Method: 利用金刚石中相互作用的 ¹³C 核自旋体系作为高度隔离的实验平台；与以往基于量子线路的实验不同，系统在物理多体哈密顿量下连续演化，测量以一定时间间隔介入。实验实施两类测量方案：对单个自旋的投影测量，以及对整个系统的全局弱测量；测量自旋自相关时间随测量频率的变化，并结合数值模拟进行对照分析。

Result: 随着测量频率提高，自旋自相关时间被显著延长；单自旋投影测量与全局弱测量两种方式均呈现该趋势。实验结果与数值模拟共同表明，量子测量可将多体系统驱入局域化区域。

Conclusion: 实验证明在真实多体哈密顿量演化、高隔离的核自旋平台上可由测量诱导局域化，为后续实验研究测量诱导多体动力学及相关纠缠现象奠定基础。

Abstract: Quantum measurement can profoundly impact the dynamics of many-body systems, for instance, inducing phase transitions from volume-law chaotic phases to area-law localized phases. While chaotic phases emerge naturally in generic many-body systems, the experimental realization of a fully localized phase driven by measurement remains challenging, primarily owing to the stringent requirement of near-perfect isolation of the system from environmental dissipation. Here we experimentally observe measurement-induced localization phenomena in an interacting $^{13}\mathrm{C}$ nuclear spin system in diamond. In contrast to previous circuit-model-based experiments, our well-isolated system evolves under a physical many-body Hamiltonian with measurements intervening at intervals. By implementing either projective measurements on a single spin or global weak measurements on the entire system, we observe substantially prolonged spin autocorrelation times as the measurement frequency increases. Our results, combined with numerical simulations, provide evidence that quantum measurement can drive a many-body system into a localized regime, and lay the groundwork for future experimental investigations into measurement-induced many-body dynamics and related entanglement phenomena.

</details>


### [297] [Two-qubit binegativity can increase under local amplitude damping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34322)
*Xiao-Lan Zong,Wei Song,Ming Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出反例，否证了“两比特态的双负性(binegativity)是纠缠单调量”这一猜想：对满秩态的单个量子比特施加振幅阻尼可使其双负性增大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双负性此前被普遍猜想为两比特态的纠缠单调量，但缺乏严格检验，作者试图通过构造反例来检验该猜想的普适性。

Method: 构造一个满秩两比特态，仅对其中一个量子比特施加振幅阻尼信道，并精确计算输出态的双负性变化；进一步构造随单一参数连续变化的一族反例，并在单向LOCC协议下考察测量后各分支双负性的概率加权平均。

Result: 存在满秩态，经单侧振幅阻尼后双负性严格增大，且输出态仍为满秩，增量可精确解析表达；得到一族连续参数化的反例。在相应单向LOCC协议中，局部测量后各分支双负性的概率加权平均值也高于初始态。

Conclusion: 双负性不是两比特态的纠缠单调量，原猜想不成立；即使引入LOCC框架下按概率加权的平均形式，该量仍不具备纠缠单调性。

Abstract: It has been conjectured that the binegativity of two-qubit states is an entanglement monotone. We present a full-rank state as a counterexample: applying amplitude damping to only one of the qubits can increase the binegativity of the state. The output state remains full rank, and the amount of increase can be calculated exactly. In addition, we construct a family of counterexamples that vary continuously with a parameter. In the corresponding one-way local operations and classical communication protocol, the probability-weighted average of the binegativity over the branches after the local measurement is also higher than that of the initial state.

</details>


### [298] [Complete characterization of internal-external entanglement trade-offs in two-mode Gaussian states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35183)
*Yasusada Nambu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在固定局域辛特征值下，精确刻画非对称双模高斯态中内部纠缠（模间负度$\mathcal{N}_{A:B}$）与外部纠缠（高斯纯化下的负度$\mathcal{N}_{AB:CD}$）之间的权衡关系，解析给出$(\mathcal{N}_{AB:CD},\mathcal{N}_{A:B})$平面可达区域的边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内部纠缠与外部纠缠之间的权衡关系此前缺乏对非对称双模高斯态的精确刻画，仅依赖数值采样难以确定可达区域的边界结构，需要解析结果与严格的可行性证明。

Method: 以负度分别量化内部与外部纠缠；通过数值采样推测$(\mathcal{N}_{AB:CD},\mathcal{N}_{A:B})$平面的可达区域边界结构，再对全局辛谱进行参数化，在固定$\mathcal{N}_{AB:CD}$时导出剩余谱参数的闭合可行区间；构造性地证明区间内每一点都对应物理协方差矩阵，从而证明上下界之间所有中间$\mathcal{N}_{A:B}$值均可实现；并分析上下界取等条件（上界由等幅反号关联达到，下界正分支对应某一全局辛特征值等于1）。

Result: 得到可达区域的解析边界与$\mathcal{N}_{A:B}$的锐利上下界，证明其间所有中间值均可实现；识别出两个外部纠缠阈值；导出饱和的单配性型不等式；并回收对称态极限作为特例。

Conclusion: 非对称双模高斯态的内部—外部纠缠权衡可由闭合解析区间完全刻画，边界锐利且内部连续可达，为高斯体系中纠缠分配与单配性关系提供了精确的定量框架。

Abstract: We characterize the exact trade-off between internal and external entanglement in asymmetric two-mode Gaussian states at fixed local symplectic eigenvalues. Internal entanglement is quantified by the negativity $\mathcal{N}_{A:B}$ between the two modes, while external entanglement is quantified by the negativity $\mathcal{N}_{AB:CD}$ across a Gaussian purification. Numerical sampling suggests the boundary structure of the attainable region in the $(\mathcal{N}_{AB:CD},\mathcal{N}_{A:B})$ plane, which we then determine analytically. Parametrizing the global symplectic spectrum yields a closed admissible interval for the remaining spectral parameter at fixed $\mathcal{N}_{AB:CD}$. We prove constructively that every point in this interval corresponds to a physical covariance matrix, implying that all intermediate values of $\mathcal{N}_{A:B}$ between the resulting sharp bounds are achievable. The upper bound is attained for opposite-sign correlations of equal magnitude, while the positive branch of the lower bound occurs when one global symplectic eigenvalue equals unity. We also identify two external-entanglement thresholds, derive a saturated monogamy-type inequality, and recover the symmetric-state limit.

</details>


### [299] [A Reduction Bound for Entanglement Detection by Positive Free Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34338)
*Chin-Yen Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明：与紧支撑自由无穷可分分布相对应的正自由映射，在任何辅助维度下都不强于约化判据，因此无法检测PPT纠缠；证明手段是联合Fock实现与由自由Lévy数据导出的算子正性证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Collins与Hayden、Nechita曾用自由概率构造出k-正但非(k+1)-正的映射族并用于纠缠检测。本文关注其中"正自由映射"这一类的检测能力边界，即它们在纠缠判别中能否超越约化判据。

Method: 针对与紧支撑自由无穷可分分布相联系的自由映射，结合联合Fock表示与从自由Lévy数据得到的算子正性证书，对所有辅助维度给出一致性的上界比较。

Result: 所得自由映射在任意辅助维度下均不强于约化判据；特别地，它们不能检测任何PPT纠缠态。

Conclusion: 这类基于自由无穷可分分布的正自由映射在纠缠检测中受约化判据限制，属于"弱判据"，其判别力不超出PPT（Peres–Horodecki）判据所能覆盖的范围。

Abstract: Collins, Hayden, and Nechita used free probability to construct families of $k$-positive but not $(k+1)$-positive maps and applied them to entanglement detection. We prove that positive free maps associated with compactly supported freely infinitely divisible distributions are no stronger than the reduction criterion in every ancillary dimension. In particular, these maps cannot detect positive partial transpose entanglement. The proof combines a joint Fock realization with an operator positivity certificate derived from free Lévy data.

</details>


### [300] [Exact Identification of Quantum Correlations in the Minimal Bell Scenario](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34364)
*Liang-Liang Sun,Jie Wang,Sixia Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出了最小Bell场景（二元输入、二元输出）中量子关联集的充要判据：一个关联是量子的当且仅当可分解为一个极端量子关联与一个经典关联之和；该判据源自"每个具有量子优势的Bell泛函都有唯一量子最大化点"的证明，并据此对非局域暴露点所在的极大面结构进行分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bell非局域性把关于量子理论本质的哲学争论转化为可实验检验的数学问题，量子力学位于局域实在论与最一般无信号理论之间；但即便在最小的Bell场景中，刻画量子集精确边界这一长期开放问题仍未解决——该场景生成集的极端点已知，边界本身却难以刻画。

Method: 提出可计算的充要判据：关联为量子当且仅当其可分解为极端量子关联与经典关联（即位于某极端量子点与局域多面体的Minkowski和中）。判据的数学基础是证明每个呈现量子优势的Bell泛函存在唯一量子最大化点；进一步按局域确定性顶点，对包含每个非局域暴露点的有限种极大面结构进行分类。

Result: 得到最小Bell场景中量子关联集的精确、可判定刻画；证明量子优势泛函的量子最大化点唯一性；给出含非局域暴露点的极大面结构的有限分类，从而严格区分物理可实现关联与非物理（无信号超量子）关联。

Conclusion: 解决了最小Bell场景中刻画量子边界的长期开放问题；为探寻量子理论背后的底层原理（如信息因果性等）以及确定量子关联的终极信息论极限提供了新的基础与工具。

Abstract: Bell nonlocality places the long-standing philosophical disputes over the nature of quantum theory on a mathematically rigorous, experimentally testable footing, positioning quantum mechanics between local realism and the most general no-signaling theories. Yet the precise quantum boundary has remained elusive even in the minimal Bell scenario, where the extreme points generating the set are known. Here we characterize this boundary through a computable necessary and sufficient criterion: a correlation is quantum if and only if it can be decomposed into an extreme quantum correlation and a classical correlation. The criterion follows from our proof that every Bell functional exhibiting quantum advantage admits a unique quantum maximizer. We further classify the finitely many maximal-face structures containing each nonlocal exposed point according to their local deterministic vertices. By sharply distinguishing physically realizable correlations from nonphysical ones, these results resolve a long-standing open problem and provide a new foundation for identifying the principles underlying quantum theory and determining the ultimate information-theoretic limits of quantum correlations.

</details>


### [301] [Pauli-ball discrepancy and stability of quantum codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34399)
*Aryeh Lev Zabokritskiy "Yohananov"*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出Pauli-ball discrepancy这一二次型差异度量，给出量子Singleton界与Hamming界等号参数下的精确分解（基准值+非负余项），并证明该余项可线性控制Gram矩阵距离、diamond误差与纠缠不保真度，在五量子比特情形给出尖锐的秩二Gram常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子码的Pauli误差像在Hilbert空间中的覆盖均匀性缺乏统一、可量化的度量与误差界工具，尤其缺少能将码的代数界（Singleton/Hamming）与恢复信道性能（diamond误差）直接联系起来的框架。

Method: 定义对所有误差半径求和的二次型Pauli-ball discrepancy；在量子Singleton界取等（最小距离≥3）与素幂局部维数下one-error Hamming界取等时，推导discrepancy的"显式基准+精确非负余项"分解；利用半径为e的Pauli球的尺寸V_e建立余项与误差像Gram矩阵偏离单位阵的Hilbert–Schmidt距离平方的线性界；在KV_e≤d条件下进一步线性约束与噪声无关的polar恢复的diamond误差和纠缠不保真度；并对五量子比特计算尖锐的秩二Gram常数与旋转码的精确逻辑信道；纯态情形给出基准加边际纯度缺陷加权和的形式。

Result: 余项恰在纯量子MDS码与非退化完美码处为零；余项线性上界Gram矩阵与单位阵的Hilbert–Schmidt距离平方；当KV_e≤d时线性上界polar恢复的diamond误差与纠缠不保真度；五量子比特得到尖锐秩二Gram常数，并构造达到该线性阶的旋转码及其精确逻辑信道；纯态下discrepancy等于纯态基准加边际纯度缺陷的正加权和，故绝对最大纠缠态（存在时）恰为最小化者。

Conclusion: Pauli-ball discrepancy为量子码提供统一的覆盖均匀性度量，其精确余项结构将码的最优性（MDS、完美码、AME态）与可操作的信道性能指标定量联系，并给出尖锐常数与显式构造。

Abstract: The Pauli-ball discrepancy, a quadratic discrepancy summed over all error radii, measures how uniformly the Pauli error images of a $K$-dimensional quantum code cover the $d$-dimensional Hilbert space. At parameters attaining equality in the quantum Singleton bound with minimum code distance $\geq3$, or in the one-error Hamming bound with prime-power local dimension, the discrepancy of every projector of the prescribed rank equals an explicit benchmark plus an exact nonnegative remainder, which vanishes precisely for pure quantum maximum-distance-separable codes and nondegenerate perfect codes, respectively. For a Pauli ball of radius $e$ below half the target distance and size $V_e$, this remainder linearly bounds the squared Hilbert--Schmidt distance of the error-image Gram matrix from the identity. When $KV_e\leq d$, it linearly bounds the diamond error and entanglement infidelity of a noise-independent polar recovery under any noise in that ball's span. For five qubits, we obtain the sharp rank-two Gram constant and exact logical channels of rotated codes attaining this linear order in diamond error. For pure states, the discrepancy equals a pure-state benchmark plus a positive weighted sum of marginal-purity defects, so absolutely maximally entangled states, when they exist, are exactly the minimizers.

</details>


### [302] [Bosonic Loss Codes with Bounded Occupation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34411)
*Avraham Kreindel,Aryeh Lev Zabokritskiy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究在q个玻色模式、总激发数N、每模式至多B个激发的Fock态编码，证明了对任意固定B与q/N>1/B存在显式可达速率（每激发逻辑量子比特数），并给出精确码维数的显式上界（含简并码）；对截断并重归一化的纯损耗信道给出近似码，具指数小最坏情形误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在光子损耗信道下，基于Fock态的玻色纠错编码是量子信息存储与通信的关键方案。已有工作（如qutrit精确码速率约0.275）未给出对一般q、N、B的显式可达速率与普适的维数上界，作者希望填补这一空白并量化近似码相对精确码的增益。

Method: 将编码限制在总激发N、每模式激发数不超过B的Fock态子空间，针对至多δN个光子损耗的所有错误图样构造精确纠错码（容忍简并）；通过组合/计数方法证明可达速率与维数上界的显式表达式；再对截断至至多δN次损耗并重归一化的纯损耗信道构造近似码，分析其最坏情形误差。

Result: 对任意固定B与q/N>1/B，证明存在对所有足够小δ>0为正的显式可达速率；B=2、q=N时，δ<0.1355速率均为正，δ=0.1时约0.281，优于此前qutrit精确码约0.275。给出所有此类精确码（含简并码）维数的显式上界。近似码具指数小最坏情形误差；当损耗概率p∈(0,1/(B+1)]时改进精确码速率下界，B=2、q=N、p=0.1时速率在δ≈0.158以内保持为正。

Conclusion: Fock态编码在光子损耗下可实现正速率精确纠错，且每模式激发上限B与比值q/N决定速率；近似码在有限损耗概率下显著扩展可纠错范围并超越精确码速率界，同时所给维数上界为编码性能设定了理论极限。

Abstract: We study Fock-state codes on $q$ bosonic modes, with total excitation $N$ and at most $B$ excitations per mode, that exactly correct every pattern of at most $δN$ photon losses, for $q$ proportional to $N$. For every fixed cap $B$ and every ratio $q/N>1/B$, we prove explicit achievable rates, positive for all sufficiently small $δ>0$, in logical qubits per excitation. At $B=2$ and $q=N$, the rate is positive for every $δ<0.1355$ and is about $0.281$ at $δ=0.1$, compared with the previously established exact-code qutrit rate of about $0.275$. We also give an explicit upper bound on the dimension of every such exact code, including degenerate codes. For the pure-loss channel truncated to at most $δN$ losses and renormalized, we obtain approximate codes with exponentially small worst-case error. When the loss probability is positive and at most $1/(B+1)$, they improve our positive exact-code rate bounds; at $B=2$, $q=N$, and loss probability $0.1$, they remain positive up to $δ\approx0.158$.

</details>


### [303] [Emergent Recursive Kernels in Quantum Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34430)
*Ce Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明由输入耦合加独立采样Haar幺正构成的固定演化量子储备池，在大维度、固定历史长度极限下存在精确的递归核；该核可将每次核评估化为仅与历史长度线性相关的标量递推，与储备池规模无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用量子储备池做学习比模拟其动力学简单得多，但缺乏对储备池核性质的精确解析刻画，需要说明其计算简洁性是否牺牲表达能力。

Method: 解析推导大维度极限下的Haar储备池递归核：利用记忆正交性与传播重叠的因子化，将核评估约简为线性于历史长度的标量递推；证明自平均性，并把核系数与乱序时序关联函数（OTOC）联系起来；分析有限维效应及可调性质（记忆保持、时间交互阶数）。

Result: 得到精确、规模无关的递归核；证实自平均；核系数对应OTOC；有限维分析显示计算简洁性未损失表达力；在NARMA10基准上，调优核的预测误差低于所测Ising与Sachdev–Ye–Kitaev储备池。

Conclusion: Haar量子储备池核具有解析简洁性与可调性，可通过调节记忆保持和时间交互阶数适配不同学习任务，并在基准任务上优于物理储备池。

Abstract: Learning with a quantum reservoir can be far simpler than simulating its dynamics. We show that a quantum reservoir governed by a fixed evolution, consisting of an input coupling followed by an independently sampled Haar unitary, admits an exact recursive kernel in the large-dimension limit at fixed history length. Memory orthogonality and factorization of propagated overlaps reduce each kernel evaluation to a scalar recurrence, linear in history length and independent of reservoir size. We establish self-averaging around this limit and connect the kernel coefficients to out-of-time-ordered correlators. Our analysis of finite-dimensional effects shows that the computational simplicity of the Haar kernel does not come at the expense of expressive power. Moreover, we demonstrate how the kernel can be tuned for different learning tasks by adjusting properties such as memory retention and temporal interaction order. On the NARMA10 benchmark, the tuned kernel achieves lower prediction error than the tested Ising and Sachdev--Ye--Kitaev reservoirs.

</details>


### [304] [Ancilla-free optimal quantum adder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34446)
*Junhong Nie,Jialiang Tang,Xiaoming Sun,Wei Zi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 构造了无辅助量子比特、仅用X、CNOT、Toffoli门的精确n位量子加法器，门数O(n)、深度O(log n)，两者均渐近最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有精确量子加法器要么需要额外辅助量子比特（ancilla），要么门数或并行深度不能同时达到渐近最优，缺乏同时满足无ancilla、O(n)门、O(log n)深度的构造。

Method: 基于全连接拓扑、仅使用X、CNOT、Toffoli门且经典可逆的电路；核心是加速加法中的"受控阶梯"(controlled ladder)。先将数据分块，复用已有控制比特作为条件工作空间，得到O(n log log n)门、O(log n)深度的递归构造；再通过两次短路径森林操作协调块间传播，降低每块工作空间需求，最终得到O(n)门的非递归实现。

Result: 得到无辅助比特的精确n位加法器：门数O(n)，深度O(log n)，且门数与深度均达到渐近下界。

Conclusion: 该工作表明无ancilla的精确量子加法可同时实现最优门数与最优深度的并行化，为量子算术电路的低开销高并行设计提供新思路。

Abstract: We construct an exact $n$-bit quantum adder with $O(n)$ gates and $O(\log n)$ depth that requires no ancillary qubits. The circuit uses only $X$, CNOT, and Toffoli gates under all-to-all connectivity and is classically reversible. Our construction accelerates the controlled ladder underlying addition. Grouping the data into blocks and use existing control qubits as conditional workspace gives a recursive construction with $O(n\log\log n)$ gates and $O(\log n)$ depth. We then coordinate inter-block propagation via two short-path forest operations to cut the per-block workspace requirement, yielding a nonrecursive implementation with $O(n)$ gates. Both the gate count and circuit depth are asymptotically optimal.

</details>


### [305] [Path integral control in the complex plane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34483)
*Aarón Villanueva*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将路径积分控制推广到复平面，采用循环复维纳过程，并在精确的控制-噪声匹配条件下于驱动噪声空间定义测度变换，从而为正、耗散率不等的成对非厄米跳跃通道给出紧凑处理，并导出交叉熵最优的反馈与开环控制公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路径积分控制结合随机量子轨迹已成为量子最优控制的有前景替代方案，但既有框架主要限于实噪声空间，难以紧凑处理复值噪声与不等耗散率的非厄米跳跃通道，因此需要向复平面推广。

Method: 提出基于循环复维纳过程的复平面路径积分控制扩展；在驱动噪声空间定义测度变换，采用精确的控制-噪声匹配条件，并允许秩亏状态扩散；将该形式主义应用于随机薛定谔方程。

Result: 给出对成对非厄米跳跃通道（耗散率不等且为正）的紧凑统一处理；推导出交叉熵最优的反馈控制与开环控制公式。

Conclusion: 复平面路径积分控制是量子最优控制的可行推广，其控制-噪声匹配条件与秩亏扩散相容，可为随机薛定谔方程提供统一的最优反馈/开环控制方案。

Abstract: Path integral control is a stochastic optimal control framework that computes optimal policies as weighted averages over state trajectories. It was recently shown that, combined with stochastic quantum trajectories, the path integral control approach constitutes a promising alternative for quantum optimal control. In this work, we propose an extension of path integral control to the complex plane using circular complex Wiener processes. The change of measure is defined on the driving noise space under an exact control-noise matching condition, which also accommodates rank-deficient state diffusions. Applied to stochastic Schrödinger equations, the resulting formalism gives a compact treatment of paired non-Hermitian jump channels with unequal positive dissipation rates and yields cross-entropy-optimal feedback and open-loop control formulas.

</details>


### [306] [Real-Time Correlation of Relaxation, Dephasing, and Two-level System Dynamics in Transmon Qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34485)
*Ambrose Yang,Alexander M. Young,Paal H. Prestegaard,Atharv Joshi,Matthew S. Ai,Q. Rumman Rahman,Faranak Bahrami,Matthew P. Bland,Jui-Lin Niu,Ourania-Maria Glezakou-Elbert,Ray D. Chang,Jocelyn Liu,Zhengyi Lu,Robert J. Cava,Nathalie P. de Leon,Andrew A. Houck*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 超导transmon量子比特的性能会因少数强耦合双能级系统缺陷谱扩散进入共振而出现持续数分钟至数小时的弛豫率与退相干率相关性升高（T1/T2 excursions），作者通过交错测量、噪声谱学与TLS谱学确认了其物理起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料改进显著提升了transmon的T1与T2，但最先进器件仍存在性能随时间波动的现象，这种波动限制门保真度与处理器整体性能，其微观噪声来源尚不明确。

Method: 采用交错的寿命与相干时间测量、噪声谱学（noise spectroscopy）以及双能级系统谱学（TLS spectroscopy），对弛豫率与退相干率随时间变化的相关性进行联合表征。

Result: 观测到T1与T2的"偏移"（excursions）：弛豫率和退相率在数分钟至数小时尺度上相关升高；证据表明这些偏移由单个或少数强耦合TLS缺陷的谱扩散（spectral diffusion）进入与量子比特频率共振所致，而非分布式弱噪声背景。

Conclusion: 所提出的测量协议能有效识别导致超导量子比特性能时间波动的噪声源，为抑制TLS诱导的涨落、提升门保真度提供了诊断工具。

Abstract: Materials improvements in transmon qubits have yielded remarkable advances in qubit lifetimes (T1)and coherence times (T2). However, state-of-the-art superconducting qubits experience temporal fluctuations in their performance, which limit gate fidelities and quantum processor performance. We observe correlated increases in qubit relaxation and dephasing rates that last for minutes to hours, which we call T1 and T2 excursions. Using interleaved lifetime and coherence time measurements, noise spectroscopy, and two-level-system (TLS) spectroscopy, we find that these excursions are caused by single or few strongly-coupled TLS defects that spectrally diffuse into resonance with the qubit frequency. Our measurement protocol provides a powerful tool for understanding the noise sources that cause temporal fluctuations in superconducting qubit performance.

</details>


### [307] [QQLink: A Declarative Framework for Multi-QPU Quantum Workflow Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34486)
*Vicente Leyton Ortega,Narasinga Rao Miniskar,Thomas Naughton,Travis S. Humble*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出 QQLink——一个用于分布式量子协议的编程与编排层，用工作流节点、延迟测量引用和声明的纠缠在单一程序中表达跨节点依赖，并自动推导跨节点协调。规划器以与量子比特足迹总规模线性的时间构造可证明无环的依赖图，程序可经模拟器执行或下译为分区硬件上的动态电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式量子协议需要"一个节点产生的测量结果控制另一节点的操作"这类跨节点依赖，现有编程模型难以在单一程序中表达并自动处理这种协调，也缺少从程序到模拟器与分区硬件的统一执行路径。

Method: 1) 语言/编排层：以工作流节点、延迟测量引用（deferred measurement references）、声明的纠缠为原语，统一表达跨节点依赖并自动推导协调；2) 规划器：基于指令量子比特足迹构造依赖图，线性时间完成并保证无环；3) 执行路径一：模拟器后端，含穷举前馈分支测试（层析完备输入集）与随机拓扑序计划重放；4) 执行路径二：多个执行器进程按计划运行、消息延迟，联合量子态由集中式模拟服务持有，并做去除必需跨节点依赖的消融；5) 下译为动态电路，与手写同构造电路比较两比特门与条件块计数；6) 硬件：两块 IBM Heron 处理器各划分为虚拟 QPU，间隔一周的两组会话中执行。

Result: 模拟器路径上所有前馈分支测试的输出态失真度不超过 5×10^-12，无错误经典输出；任意随机拓扑序重放终态不变。多进程执行器在完整计划下每次运行均正确，移除必需跨节点依赖则导致协议失败。下译电路在两比特门与条件块计数上与手写实现一致。两个 IBM Heron 芯片的虚拟 QPU 上实现同芯片执行，但会话间差异很大，损耗超出基于校准的噪声模型预测；相干性敏感检验未能确立相干 Toffoli 操作与目标重复码叠加态的保持。

Conclusion: 结果支持 QQLink 的工作流语义在所测执行路径上的正确性（模拟器、多进程编排、下译等价性），同时揭示了当前分区硬件的实现局限（会话间波动大、超模型损耗、相干性检验不通过）；跨物理联网 QPU 的执行属未来工作。

Abstract: Distributed quantum protocols require measurement outcomes produced at one node to control operations at another. We present QQLink, a programming and orchestration layer that expresses these dependencies in a single program, using workflow nodes, deferred measurement references, and declared entanglement, and derives the cross-node coordination automatically. Its planner constructs a provably acyclic dependency graph in time linear in the total size of the instructions' qubit footprints. The same programs execute through simulator backends or are lowered to dynamic circuits on partitioned quantum hardware. We evaluate seven protocol workflows. Exhaustive feedforward-branch tests on the simulator runtime path, over tomographically complete input sets, yield output-state infidelities of at most $5\times10^{-12}$ and no incorrect classical outputs, and replaying every plan in random topological orders leaves each final state unchanged. Separate executor processes run the plans with delayed messages, with the joint quantum state held by a centralized simulation service. Every run with the full plan is correct, and removing required cross-node dependencies causes protocol failures. The lowered circuits match hand-written implementations of the same construction in two-qubit gate and conditional-block counts. Runs on two IBM Heron processors, each partitioned into virtual QPUs, demonstrate same-chip execution in two sessions a week apart. The runs also show large variation between sessions and losses beyond what calibration-derived noise models predict. Coherence-sensitive checks do not establish coherent Toffoli operation or preservation of the intended repetition-code superposition. These results support QQLink's workflow semantics on the tested execution paths and identify limits of the current hardware realization; execution across physically networked QPUs remains future work.

</details>


### [308] [Wave Is All You Need: A Quantum Language Model Based on Fully Connected Quantum Boltzmann Machine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34492)
*Jun Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出基于全连接量子玻尔兹曼机（FC-QBM）的自注意力机制，并以其为核心构建量子语言模型 FC-QBM-LM；通过语义量子态的幺正演化隐式实现位置编码与自注意力，8量子比特模型仅需44个参数，在唐诗语料上收敛良好且对噪声鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典自注意力需将连续语义流离散为矩阵并显式做矩阵乘法，计算与参数开销大；作者希望借助量子态演化直接完成位置编码与注意力计算，并保证测量结果天然满足归一化条件，从而探索量子语言模型的可行路径。

Method: 以 FC-QBM 为基础构造自注意力机制：对语义量子态做幺正演化，隐式实现位置编码与自注意力，避免离散矩阵分解与显式矩阵乘法；严格推导该机制的计算原理并证明其与经典自注意力数学等价；推导 FC-QBM-LM 的训练与生成数学原理并给出两个对应算法；构建8量子比特模型在唐诗语料上训练，并比较以Y轴/ X轴旋转门结尾的量子线路，测试小样本与8种噪声强度下的表现，最后做五言绝句生成实验。

Result: 8量子比特设置下量子层仅用44个参数实现完整的位置编码与自注意力流程；无噪声时收敛良好；以Y轴旋转门结尾的线路收敛优于X轴旋转门版本；小样本条件下、8种噪声强度下仍保留超过99%的无噪声收敛性能；五言绝句生成初步展现联想发散、意象内容生成与人类一致性词序三项能力。

Conclusion: 该工作从理论与实验上表明 FC-QBM 自注意力可实现与经典自注意力等价的隐式注意力计算，且参数极简、噪声鲁棒，为量子语言模型提供了可行原型；但实验规模限于8量子比特、单类语料，能力仅为"初步"展现。

Abstract: This work proposes a self-attention mechanism based on the fully connected quantum Boltzmann machine (FC-QBM), and further constructs a fully connected quantum Boltzmann machine language model (FC-QBM-LM) using the mechanism as the core engine. By performing unitary evolution on semantic quantum states, the mechanism implicitly implements positional encoding and self-attention computation, eliminating the need to decompose continuous semantic streams into discrete matrices for explicit matrix multiplication, with the measurement results naturally satisfying the normalization condition. Specifically, this work rigorously derives the computational principle of the self-attention mechanism based on FC-QBM, and demonstrates that this mechanism is mathematically equivalent to the classical self-attention mechanism. The mathematical principles governing the training and generation of FC-QBM-LM are thoroughly derived, and the two corresponding algorithms are subsequently developed. An 8-qubit FC-QBM-LM is finally constructed and trained on the corpus of Tang poetry. Under the 8-qubit setting, the quantum layer implements the entire positional encoding and self-attention computation pipeline with only 44 parameters. In the noiseless scenario, the model exhibits favorable convergence. Furthermore, the quantum circuit proposed in this work, which terminates with Y-axis rotation gates, achieves better convergence than the counterpart terminating with X-axis rotation gates. Under the small-sample regime and across eight distinct noise intensity levels, the model demonstrates strong noise robustness, retaining over 99% of its noiseless convergence performance. Experiments on five-character quatrain generation reveal that the model preliminarily exhibits three core capabilities: associative and divergent thinking, content generation across imagery and human-aligned word ordering.

</details>


### [309] [Model-Uncertainty-Aware Digital Twins for Non-Markovian Quantum Repeater Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34506)
*Muhammad Waqas Haseeb*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: QMemTwin: 一种物理约束的数字孪生，用可收缩无源状态空间模型库+有限样本校正Akaike权重处理存储纠缠寿命的模型不确定性，并在数据不支持时弃答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子中继控制器需在相邻链路生成时可靠估计已存储纠缠。固定指数寿命无法刻画非马尔可夫复活（revival），而模型族已知时复杂的替代模型又属多余；核心问题是如何在微观模型族不确定时仍给出可靠、物理合法的估计。

Method: 构建物理约束数字孪生：标定一族"可收缩无源（contractive passive）状态空间模型"，以有限样本校正的Akaike权重组合各候选幅度；用时间留出（temporal holdout）检验判定动态是否被支持，不支持则弃答。无源性保证每个候选及其凸幅度平均对应完全正、保迹的幅度阻尼信道。评估含支持的被动时不变案例与非平稳结构漂移案例，并在2/4/8链路中继链上测goodput。

Result: 匹配单赝模（one-pseudomode）情形下，正确设定的单赝模拟合最优。未知族情形下，相对双赝模模型，QMemTwin降低population RMSE与Bell-fidelity RMSE（Holm校正后配对效应仍显著）。灵活无源模型在未知族上均值误差略低，但与QMemTwin统计上不可区分，且在匹配情形下精度下降。时间留出门控对结构漂移检出率高、误报率低、AUROC良好。混合队列在8链路无显著goodput增益；预声明高回流压力队列中8链路goodput相对双赝模模型有显著提升。

Conclusion: 结果支持三条原则：模型族已知时直接用已知族；族不确定时显式计入模型不确定性；动态不受支持时弃答而非外推。无源约束为组合估计提供了信道层面的物理合法性保证。

Abstract: Quantum-repeater controllers need reliable estimates of stored entanglement as adjacent links are generated. Fixed exponential lifetimes miss non-Markovian revivals, while complex surrogates are unnecessary when the microscopic family is known. We present QMemTwin, a physics-constrained digital twin that accounts for model uncertainty. It calibrates a bank of contractive passive state-space models, combines their amplitudes with finite-sample-corrected Akaike weights, and abstains when temporal-holdout data indicate unsupported dynamics. Passivity guarantees a completely positive, trace-preserving amplitude-damping channel for each candidate and their convex amplitude average. We evaluate [N cases] synthetic memories: [N supported] supported passive time-invariant cases and [N structural] nonstationary structural-shift cases. In the matched one-pseudomode regime, the correctly specified one-pseudomode fit performs best. Across [N unknown] supported cases with uncertain families, QMemTwin reduces population RMSE by [population reduction]% and Bell-fidelity RMSE by [Bell reduction]% relative to the two-pseudomode model; both paired effects remain significant after Holm correction. The flexible passive model has a slightly lower mean error for unknown families but is statistically indistinguishable from QMemTwin and loses accuracy in the matched regime. The temporal-holdout gate detects [detection]% of structural shifts, with [false alarm]% false alarms on supported families and AUROC [AUC]. In 2-, 4-, and 8-link repeater chains, the mixed cohort shows no significant 8-link goodput gain. A predeclared high-backflow stress cohort yields an 8-link goodput gain of [goodput gain]% over the two-pseudomode model, with paired p = [p value]. These results favor using a known family when available, accounting for model uncertainty otherwise and abstaining on unsupported dynamics.

</details>


### [310] [Quantum Anomaly Detection in network traffic using Variational Quantum Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34635)
*Mir H M Ilham,Hardique Dasore,Abdulrahman Ababneh,Hafidz A Kabira,Samyam Shrestha,Jeyasri Sundar,Shah Haque,Eric Howard*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究评估了四种变分量子分类器（VQC）架构在网络流量异常检测中的表现，发现电路表达能力与优化稳定性之间存在权衡：深层电路（VQC-4）准确率最高达89.0%但对训练条件敏感，最优配置（VQC-3）为ZFeatureMap+RealAmplitudes+ADAM；传统SVM与随机森林仍可在合成数据上实现完美分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络安全态势日益复杂，需要更先进的网络异常检测系统。经典机器学习模型虽有效，但在处理高维数据时会出现计算瓶颈，因此作者希望探索量子机器学习（QML）在该任务上的潜力。

Method: 生成合成网络流量数据集进行评估；构建四种不同的VQC架构（VQC-1至VQC-4），系统性地改变特征编码方式、参数化ansatz电路、量子神经网络类型与经典优化器；并与SVM、随机森林等经典模型进行对比分析。

Result: 深层电路VQC-4准确率最高可达89.0%，但对训练条件敏感；较浅的VQC-1表现欠佳，但优化曲线一致且稳定；VQC-2存在类别相关偏差，难以找到有效决策边界并产生大量假阴性；VQC-3（ZFeatureMap + RealAmplitudes + ADAM）综合表现最佳。经典模型（SVM、随机森林）在合成数据上达到完美分类，存在明显性能差距。

Conclusion: 经典算法在类别分离相对简单的结构化数据分类上仍占主导地位；但部分VQC配置已展现出学习决策边界的能力，提示其在未来面向更高噪声、更高维问题中的应用价值。

Abstract: The increasingly complex cybersecurity landscape requires more advanced network anomaly detection systems. Although, classical machine learning models are known to be effective, they reach computational bottlenecks when processing high-dimensional data. This study investigates the potential of Quantum Machine Learning (QML) for network traffic anomaly detection by evaluating four distinct Variational Quantum Classifier (VQC) architectures. A synthetic network traffic dataset is generated for the evaluations. The VQC models systematically varied feature encoding methods, parameterized ansatz circuits, quantum neural network types, and classical optimizers. Experimental results show that a trade-off exists between circuit expressivity and optimization stability. Deeper circuits (VQC-4) achieved higher accuracy of upto 89.0\%, while simultaneously being sensitive to training conditions. A shallower variant (VQC-1) under performed, although it had consistent and stable optimization curves. Class-dependent biases were also observed such as in VQC-2, where the model struggled to find an effective decision boundary, and produced a large number of false negatives. The best performing model in this study (VQC-3) combined ZFeatureMap with RealAmplitudes ansatz, optimized using ADAM. Comparative analysis against classical machine learning models such as Support Vector Machine and Random Forest reveal a performance gap, where classical models are able to achieve perfect classification performance on the synthetic data. It is concluded that classical algorithms remain dominant in their ability to classify structured data with relatively simple class separations. However, due to the observed potential of certain VQC configurations to learn decision boundaries to classify data, their utility for future applications in noisier, higher-dimensional problems is highlighted.

</details>


### [311] [Neutrinos as Quantum Information Carriers? A Gap Analysis Against the DiVincenzo Criteria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34666)
*Ramanuj Awasthi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文是一篇"差距分析"短文，利用现有文献将中微子作为量子信息平台与DiVincenzo七项判据逐条对比，结论是中微子更适合被视为优秀的"飞行"信息载体，而非完整量子计算平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子、囚禁离子、中性原子、超导电路已是成熟量子信息平台，而中微子具有独特优势：可在数千公里尺度上展现量子干涉（味振荡），且相互作用极弱、能近乎无损地穿透地球。作者希望系统评估中微子作为新型量子信息平台的可行性与短板，为后续研究指明方向。

Method: 采用差距分析（gap analysis）方法：将DiVincenzo七项判据分为两类——五项与计算相关、两项与通信相关，依据现有文献逐一比较中微子满足各项判据的程度，并识别每项判据上的差距。

Result: 中微子具有高保真度和良好相干性；但缺乏清晰的量子比特定义与初态制备，不具备通用量子门、单量子比特读出以及静止—飞行量子比特转换能力，这些方面表现为失败或极差。

Conclusion: 中微子最佳定位是卓越的飞行信息载体，但极难操控；作者系统列出了全部差距，可用于指导未来工作。

Abstract: Photons, trapped ions, neutral atoms, and superconducting circuits are all well-known quantum information platforms. Neutrinos offer a unique possibility, displaying quantum interference (flavour oscillation) over thousands of kilometres, yet interacting so weakly that they can travel through the Earth with minimal interaction. This short paper is a gap analysis. We use information from the existing literature to compare neutrinos to the seven DiVincenzo requirements. Firstly, there are five requirements to do with computation and secondly two requirements to do with communication. We analyse where the gaps are for each requirement. Overall, we find that neutrinos have high fidelity and good coherence; however, they do not have a clear qubit definition and initialisation. They do not have universal gates, individual qubit readout, or stationary-to-flying conversion, meaning they either fail or are very poor. Therefore, we think they are best considered as an exceptional flying information carrier; however, they are very difficult to control. We have outlined all of the gaps which can be used to inform future work.

</details>


### [312] [Rank-one Factored Gradient Descent without Restricted Isometry: Application to Quantum Pure State Tomography on Multi-Qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34696)
*Takahiro Kajisa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 针对量子态层析中低权重Pauli测量导致RIP失效的问题，提出将交换可观测量按setting分组的max-sliced全变差损失，并证明其景观无虚假局部极小、全局极小即为真态（相差相位），在四量子比特上数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FGD是低秩半正定矩阵恢复的可扩展非凸方法，因子分解自动满足秩与正定约束，但其良性损失景观理论大多依赖测量映射的RIP。在量子态层析应用中，受探测效率与漂移限制，可稳定统计的测量仅限于低权重Pauli可观测量，此时RIP以最坏方式失效，现有理论无法保证。

Method: 首先证明部分权重Pauli测量下RIP的失效性；随后按setting捆绑相互交换的可观测量，将状态前推为各setting的概率分布，构造max-sliced全变差（TV）损失；在取到最大值的setting唯一的层（stratum）上分析损失景观，并以测量映射在一般纯态上的单射性为唯一条件。

Result: 在最大值setting唯一的层上不存在虚假局部极小；只要测量映射在一般纯态上单射，全局极小即等于真态（相差一个相位）。在四量子比特上数值验证了上述结论，并与使用普通损失的FGD进行对比。

Conclusion: 新损失避免了RIP依赖，为低权重Pauli测量下的量子态层析提供了更强的景观保证，数值结果支持理论，且优于普通损失下的FGD。

Abstract: Factored gradient descent (FGD) is a scalable nonconvex method for low-rank positive semidefinite matrix recovery, in which the rank and positivity constraints are satisfied automatically by the factorization. Most of the existing theory that certifies benignity of its loss landscape, however, relies on a restricted isometry property (RIP) of the measurement map. In quantum state tomography, one of its applications, the measurements that can be acquired with stable statistics are limited by detection efficiency and drift to Pauli observables of low weight; we first show that under such partial-weight Pauli measurements RIP fails in the worst possible way. We then bundle the mutually commuting observables setting by setting, pushing the state forward to the probability distribution of each setting, and introduce a max-sliced total-variation loss. On the stratum where the setting attaining the maximum is unique there is no spurious local minimum, and provided only that the measurement map is injective on generic pure states, the global minimum is the truth up to a phase. Finally we verify these claims numerically on four qubits and compare against FGD with the ordinary loss.

</details>


### [313] [Target Detection with Asymmetric Gaussian Classical Correlation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34698)
*Su-Yong Lee,Yonggi Jo,Jihwan Kim,Zaeill Kim,Yong Sup Ihn,Duk Y. Kim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究热态经分束器产生的双模高斯经典关联特性，通过分析单模测量对另一模的影响区分经典与量子关联，并证明经典关联态（尤其非对称关联）在目标检测中随环境热噪声增强显著优于量子关联态，同时展示局域高斯操作的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典关联与量子关联的区分及其在实际量子任务中的价值尚不清晰，特别是在高斯体系中，经典关联是否能在噪声环境下带来实际优势需要系统研究。

Method: 将热态输入分束器产生双模高斯态，显式分析对一个模进行测量如何影响另一个模，据此区分经典关联与量子关联；随后在开-关（on-off）探测下，考察信号模与本振模具有不同平均光子数的非对称经典关联态在目标检测中的表现，并引入局域高斯操作。

Result: 随着环境热平均光子数增加，经典关联态在开-关探测下的目标检测性能显著超过量子关联态；非对称经典关联（信号与本振光子数不同）尤为有利；局域高斯操作进一步带来检测优势。

Conclusion: 高斯经典关联态在高噪声目标检测任务中具有实际优势，通过非对称关联设计与局域高斯操作可有效提升检测性能，为量子照明类协议中经典资源的利用提供了新思路。

Abstract: We study the characteristics of two-mode classical correlation in Gaussian regime, which is produced by impinging a thermal state into a beam splitter.
