It is explicitly investigated how a measurement on one mode affects the other. The result enables the distinction between classical and quantum correlations. Then, we show how classically correlated states are advantageous in target detection, specifically for an asymmetric classical correlation with different mean photon numbers in the signal and idler modes. Under on-off detection, the classically correlated states significantly outperform quantum correlated states as the environmental thermal mean photon number increases. Furthermore, we demonstrate the advantage of local Gaussian operations in the target detection.

</details>


### [314] [Quantum ordinal selection with two comparisons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34762)
*Hongyu Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在至多两次无噪声相干成对比较下、于指定名次阈值内选中目标的最优成功概率，用比较特化的半定模型刻画可达成功率，并给出小规模精确最优与最小选择问题的近优算法族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅用多项式次数上界会高估两次比较所能达到的成功率，需要更紧的、与具体比较结构相关的刻画来得到真实最优值。

Method: 建立比较特化的半定规划模型，完整刻画可达到的成功概率；借助计算机辅助证明求精确最优；针对最小选择构造显式算法族并解析确定其精确最优；对所有可行两次比较算法给出界。

Result: 给出七个对象下每个名次阈值的精确最优，以及八个对象时最小选择问题的精确最优；所构造算法族的渐近成功率与无限制最优相差不超过0.016%，且该相对差距的极限严格为正；两次比较预算下最小选择的最优量子—经典成功比收敛于约2.48。

Conclusion: 半定模型为两次相干比较的成功率提供精确刻画并纠正次数上界的高估；显式算法族在最小选择上渐近接近无限制最优，量子相对经典的增益存在且有限。

Abstract: We study the optimal probability of selecting an object within a prescribed rank threshold using at most two noiseless coherent pairwise comparisons of an unknown strict order. Polynomial degree bounds alone can overestimate the success attainable with two comparisons. We develop a comparison-specific semidefinite model that completely characterizes attainable success. Computer-assisted proofs establish the exact optimum for every rank threshold at seven objects and for minimum selection at eight. For minimum selection, we construct an explicit algorithm family and determine its exact optimum. Bounds for all admissible two-comparison algorithms show that the family's asymptotic success is within 0.016% of the unrestricted optimum. We also prove that this relative gap has a strictly positive limit. The optimal quantum-to-classical success ratio for minimum selection converges to approximately 2.48 under the same two-comparison budget.

</details>


### [315] [Measure of set coherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34784)
*Jiabo Panel,Yu Guo,Yuqin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文建立了集合相干性（set coherence）的公理化度量框架：证明自由操作为共同保交换信道，检验平均/最大/求和三类鲁棒性度量的公理满足性，并提出基于成对对易子的度量族，阐明其在近似量子广播任务中的操作意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集合相干性刻画一组量子态能否在某一公共基下同时非相干，是单态相干性的推广。但此前缺乏系统的资源理论框架：自由操作未明确、度量缺乏公理化检验（如平均鲁棒性与最大鲁棒性仅被零散引入，未验证是否满足度量公理，也未讨论完备性、强超可加性等性质）。因此需要建立统一框架并区分不同度量的能力层级。

Method: 1) 确定集合相干性的自由操作为共同保交换（common commutativity-preserving）量子信道，由此建立SCM（集合相干性度量）公理体系；2) 以公共参考基下单态鲁棒性的平均、最大、求和三种聚合方式构造鲁棒性度量，逐一检验公理；3) 提出基于两两对易子（pairwise commutators）的多类SCM，包括Schatten p-范数型度量；4) 分析文献中的通用四阶Bargmann间隙度量的性质；5) 在最优近似量子广播情境下讨论SCM的操作意义。

Result: 平均鲁棒性是SCM，但既非unified也非complete；最大鲁棒性是unified SCM，但既不complete也不强超可加；求和鲁棒性是complete SCM。基于Schatten p-范数的SCM是complete且强超可加的；文献中的通用四阶Bargmann间隙度量同样是complete且强超可加的集合相干性量化子。此外，集合相干性的量值能紧密反映最优近似量子广播任务的性能。

Conclusion: 在共同保交换信道作为自由操作的公理框架下，不同鲁棒性聚合方式给出的度量具有严格分层的性质（complete / unified / 强超可加各不相同），成对对易子型与Bargmann间隙型度量可提供完备且强超可加的量化；SCM在近似量子广播中具有明确操作解释，可作为该任务性能的有效刻画指标。

Abstract: Set coherence is a quantum resource for a set of states that characterizes whether a collection of quantum states contains coherence, namely whether these states fail to be simultaneously incoherent in a common basis. In this work, we show that the free operations of set coherence are common commutativity-preserving quantum channels, from which we then establish an axiomatic framework for set-coherence measure (SCM). Within this framework, we investigate the robustness based measures deduced by taking, respectively, the average, maximum, and sum of the single-state robustnesses over a common reference basis, where the first two were previously introduced in the literature and we check here whether they satisfy our SCM axioms. Consequently, we show that the averaged robustness is an SCM but it is neither unified nor complete, the maximal robustness is a unified SCM but is neither complete nor strongly superadditive, whereas the sum robustness is a complete SCM. Furthermore, we propose several classes of SCMs based on pairwise commutators. We show that the Schatten $p$-norm based SCMs are complete and strongly superadditive. We also find that the universal fourth-order Bargmann gap measure proposed in the literature is a complete and strongly superadditive quantifier of set coherence. Finally, we discuss the operational meaning of the SCM in the context of optimal approximate quantum broadcasting, and show that the amount of set coherence can closely reflect the performance of this task.

</details>


### [316] [A General Framework for Leggett--Garg Violation in Neutrino Oscillations and Matter Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34832)
*M. M. Ettefaghi,H. Hajipour*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 利用赝密度矩阵（PDM）在静态条件下计算两味中微子振荡的四时刻量 K4，检验类 Horodecki 判据并显式评估物质效应（特别是 MSW 共振附近）对时间关联的修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Leggett–Garg 不等式依赖宏观实在论与无干扰测量假设，而中微子实验中无干扰测量难以实现；因此需要一种替代 PDM 的方案来刻画时间关联，并厘清 Horodecki 型判据与具体味观测量在 LGI 违反上的关系。

Method: 以赝密度矩阵替代无干扰测量，考察时间泡利关联张量的 Horodecki 型判据：对任意幺正量子比特演化，相应标量 M_R = 2；随后在恒密度物质中对味测量显式数值计算 K4。

Result: M_R = 2 刻画了关联张量与优化后的时间构造，但并不意味着某个固定味观测量在任意中微子能量或基线处都违反 LGI；数值算例显示物质效应改变时间关联，在所选参数下 MSW 共振附近出现显著的物质–真空差异，而所选非共振情形差异普遍较小。

Conclusion: 物质诱导的时间关联值得进一步研究，尤其在大气中微子中，并对 DUNE、Hyper-Kamiokande 等长基线与大气中微子实验的后续研究具有潜在意义。

Abstract: The Leggett--Garg inequality (LGI) constrains temporal correlations under the assumptions of macrorealism and noninvasive measurability. We study the four-time quantity \(K_4\) for two-flavor neutrino oscillations using a pseudo-density matrix (PDM) under static conditions, instead of non-invasive measurement. With this approach, it becomes possible to examine a Horodecki-type criterion for the temporal Pauli-correlation tensor. The Horodecki criterion had been proven for CHSH inequality (R. Horodecki et al. Phys. Lett. \textbf{A200}, 340), For any unitary qubit evolution, the corresponding scalar is \(M_R=2\). Although, this result characterizes the correlation tensor and the optimized temporal construction, it does not imply that a fixed flavor observable violates the LGI at every neutrino energy or baseline. We therefore evaluate \(K_4\) explicitly for flavor measurements in matter of constant density. The numerical examples illustrate how matter effects modify the temporal correlations, with a pronounced matter--vacuum separation near the MSW resonance for the chosen parameters and generally smaller differences in the selected off-resonance cases. This finding motivates further investigation of matter-induced temporal correlations in atmospheric neutrinos and their potential relevance to future studies with long-baseline and atmospheric-neutrino experiments, including DUNE and Hyper-Kamiokande.

</details>


### [317] [Modulation and protection of entanglement in a two-qubit system via two interacting oscillator control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34899)
*Pablo Carlos López Vázquez,Andrés García,Josué Romero*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过辅助谐振子间的可调耦合（位置-位置与动量-动量）和局域退相位相互作用，实现两比特纠缠的控制与保护，并给出解析解与耗散条件下的分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在开放量子系统中，两比特纠缠会因退相位和热耗散而退化；本文探索利用辅助玻色模之间的工程化耦合作为调控手段，来控制和保护纠缠。

Method: 每个比特通过退相位相互作用耦合到各自的局域谐振子，两个谐振子之间具有独立可调的位置-位置与动量-动量耦合。采用特征函数表示求得复合系统动力学的解析解，并将纠缠动力学与耦合谐振子的稳定性性质相联系；进而用局域与全局主方程描述热耗散的影响。

Result: 识别出纠缠复兴、抑制与衰减三类不同动力学区间；给出使谐振子耦合有效减缓纠缠退化的构型；在考虑的耗散条件下，合适的谐振子耦合能够保持相当可观的纠缠量。

Conclusion: 建立了辅助玻色模的动力学稳定性与离散变量量子系统中纠缠可控性之间的联系，为通过工程化谐振子耦合保护纠缠提供了潜在策略。

Abstract: We investigate the control and protection of two-qubit entanglement through engineered interactions between two auxiliary harmonic oscillators. Each qubit is coupled to a local oscillator via a dephasing interaction, while the oscillators interact through independently tunable position-position and momentum-momentum couplings. Employing the characteristic-function representation, we obtain analytical solutions for the composite-system dynamics and examine how the stability properties of the coupled oscillators determine the evolution of two-qubit concurrence. Our analysis reveals distinct dynamical regimes exhibiting entanglement revivals, suppression, or decay, and identifies configurations in which oscillator interactions effectively reduce entanglement degradation. We further investigate the influence of thermal dissipation using local and global master-equation descriptions, demonstrating that suitable oscillator couplings can preserve substantial two-qubit entanglement under the dissipative conditions considered. These results establish a connection between the dynamical stability of auxiliary bosonic modes and the controllability of entanglement in discrete-variable quantum systems, providing a potential strategy for entanglement protection through engineered oscillator interactions.

</details>


### [318] [Heisenberg-Scaling Quantum Metrology of Electron-Photon Coupling via Multi-Electron PINEM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34919)
*Zihang Zou,Feng-Xiao Sun,Yunquan Liu,Qiongyi He*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究多电子PINEM中耦合强度估计的精度极限，证明量子Fisher信息随电子数平方标度；提出压缩光探测+零差探测方案，在电子具非零电子间相干时达到海森堡标度，并用辅助光模条件抑制相位噪声恢复该标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由电子量子光学已成为相干电子-光相互作用的平台，但其在量子增强计量学中的潜力尚未被充分探索；本文旨在确立多电子PINEM的精密测量基本极限并寻找可达海森堡标度的实用方案。

Method: 以量子Fisher信息分析多电子光子诱导近场电子显微镜（PINEM）中耦合强度估计的精度极限；设计结合压缩光探针与光学零差探测的可实验方案；分析不完美电子制备导致的电子相位噪声如何转化为波动的光学位移；引入与同一电子系综耦合的辅助光模测量信号模的位移噪声，实现条件抑制。

Result: 量子Fisher信息随电子数呈二次标度；所提方案在电子间相干非零时相对电子数达到海森堡标度，并在光子数增强方面优于传统PINEM传感；电子相位噪声经转化为光学位移涨落后限制精度，而辅助光模的条件测量可抑制该噪声并恢复海森堡标度。

Conclusion: 多电子相干性是自由电子量子计量学的可用资源，该工作为纳米尺度电子-光相互作用的精密测量开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Free-electron quantum optics has recently emerged as a promising platform for coherent electron-light interactions, yet its potential for quantum-enhanced metrology remains largely unexplored. Here, we investigate the fundamental precision limit of coupling-strength estimation in multi-electron photon-induced near-field electron microscopy (PINEM), demonstrating that the quantum Fisher information scales quadratically with electron number. We then propose an experimentally accessible scheme combining squeezed-light probing and optical homodyne detection that attains Heisenberg scaling with respect to electron number, provided the electrons exhibit nonzero inter-electron coherence, while surpassing conventional PINEM sensing in photon-number enhancement as well. We further analyze the effect of imperfect electron preparation, showing that electron phase noise is converted into a fluctuating optical displacement and eventually limits the precision. To overcome this limitation, an auxiliary optical mode coupled to the same electron ensemble measures the displacement noise in the signal mode, allowing its conditional suppression and recovery of the Heisenberg scaling. Our work establishes multi-electron coherence as a resource for free-electron quantum metrology and opens the route to precision measurements of nanoscale electron-light interactions.

</details>


### [319] [Membership in the cone of quantum states over time is NP-hard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34972)
*Matt Hoogsteder-Riera*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究"态随时间"(state over time, stote)算子所生成的凸锥 $\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}$，证明其包含一个与双分体可分锥线性同构的暴露面，由此推出 $\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}$ 及其对偶的精确/弱成员判定与弱优化均 NP-难，且无有限半定表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献 Hoogsteder–Calsamiglia–Winter 2025 将刻画 stote 算子 (ρ⊗1)⋆J_E 所生成凸锥 $\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}$ 的问题列为公开问题；理解该锥的几何与计算复杂度对量子信息中过程/通道的可分离性判定有直接意义。

Method: 在输出系统取 $B=A'R$（$A'$ 为输入 $A$ 的副本）时，构造显式见证 $W_0\in\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}^*$ 与显式单射线性映射 $L$，使得 $\sigma\in\mathrm{SEP}_+(A{:}R)\iff L(\sigma)\in\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}(A{:}A'R)$，即把可分锥嵌入为 $\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}$ 的暴露面；并给出定量稳定性界以保证逆多项式精度下的弱成员归约有效；最后借助 Fawzi 定理排除有限半定表示。

Result: $\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}$ 含与 $\mathrm{SEP}_+(A{:}R)$ 线性同构的暴露面；由可分性判定的 NP-难性推出 $\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}$ 及其对偶的精确成员、弱成员与弱优化问题均为 NP-难（弱成员在逆多项式精度下成立），故除非 P=NP 否则不存在多项式时间成员测试；当 $d=\dim A\ge2$、$r=\dim R\ge2$ 且 $d+r>5$ 时，$\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}(A{:}A'R)$ 无有限半定表示。

Conclusion: stote 锥的刻画问题在计算上本质困难：它把量子可分性问题硬编码为一个暴露面，因而继承其 NP-难度；同时其半定可表示性在一般维数下不成立。这为 stote 锥的算法研究设定了不可逾越的下界，也说明需转向松弛、近似或受限维数的途径。

Abstract: A state over time (stote) pairs a state $ρ$ with a channel $\mathcal{E}$ as the Hermitian, generally non-positive operator $(ρ\otimes\mathbf{1})\star J_\mathcal{E}$. Characterizing the convex cone $\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}$ these operators generate was posed as an open problem in~\cite{HoogstederCalsamigliaWinter2025}. We exhibit a linear copy of the bipartite separable cone as an exposed face of $\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}$: for output systems of the form $B = A'R$ with $A'$ a copy of the input, there is an explicit witness $W_0\in\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}^*$ and an explicit injective linear map $L$ with $σ\in \mathrm{SEP}_{+}(A{:}R)\iff L(σ)\in\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}(A{:}A'R)$. Composing with the NP-hardness of separability shows that exact membership, weak membership, and weak optimization are NP-hard for $\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}$ and its dual. A quantitative stability bound makes the weak-membership reduction valid at inverse-polynomial accuracy. Thus polynomial-time membership tests do not exist unless $\mathrm{P}=\mathrm{NP}$. Independently of this complexity assumption, Fawzi's theorem implies that $\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}(A{:}A'R)$ admits no finite semidefinite representation when $d=\dim A\ge2$, $r=\dim R\ge2$, and $d+r>5$.

</details>


### [320] [Charge-Coupled Device-Based Arbitrary Waveform Generation for Qubit Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34988)
*Shinichi Morisaka,Ryutaro Ohira*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于电荷耦合器件（CCD）的任意波形发生架构，将波形采样以电荷包形式存储并按时钟顺序读出为电压样本，多通道并行时间交织实现高有效采样率，再上变频至微波驱动量子比特。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可扩展量子控制系统中，微波信号的持续生成与传输导致低温互连密度高、热负载大，需要一种能降低低温互连与热负荷的波形生成途径。

Method: 采用CCD沟道以电荷包形式存储波形采样点，按顺序时钟读出转换为电压样本；多通道并行运行并时间交织以提高有效采样率；生成的基带波形经上变频至微波频率用于量子比特操控。

Result: 摘要未给出定量实验结果，仅说明该架构通过"以存储电荷替代持续生成/传输的微波信号"这一方式，为降低低温互连密度和热负载提供了可行路径。

Conclusion: 以电荷形式存储波形信息可减少低温环境下微波信号的实时生成与传输需求，有望降低互连密度与热负载，为可扩展量子控制系统提供新的控制信号生成方案。

Abstract: We propose an arbitrary waveform generation architecture based on charge-coupled devices (CCDs) for qubit control. Waveform samples are stored as charge packets in CCD channels and sequentially clocked out as voltage samples. Multiple channels are operated in parallel and time-interleaved to achieve high effective sampling rates. The generated baseband waveforms are then upconverted to microwave frequencies to drive qubit operations. By representing waveform information as stored charge rather than as continuously generated or transmitted microwave signals, the proposed architecture provides a path to reducing cryogenic interconnect density and lowering heat load in scalable quantum control systems.

</details>


### [321] [From Single Spins to Ensembles: A Calibrated Symbolic Regression Pipeline for NV Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35016)
*Evan Zhang,Zhenghang Zhi,Zewei Wang,Zeheng Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出符号回归流程，将单中心NV自旋结构迁移为可解释的集合模型，零场集合RMSE降低56.2%，并能外推预测未见磁场下的集合谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NV集合谱是众多个体中心光谱的叠加，各中心处于不同局域环境，需要将集合谱与单中心联系起来；现有模型缺乏既有物理可解释性又能校准样品差异的手段。

Method: 符号回归流程：在约束搜索下从单中心ODMR谱中选取厄米算符项并拟合系数，保留表达式作为集合的源模型；再进行集合校准，显式权重组合单中心谱响应，并计入共振位移、局域无序、取向与光学响应，同时保持单中心贡献可访问。

Result: 公开数据上零场集合保持频率RMSE由0.008835降至0.003872（归一化PL），较未校正组合改善56.2%；QuTiP模拟128个位点、四种取向的集合中，用0与3 mT校准的模型对独立留出的5.17 mT谱预测误差为0.000207–0.000228（三个随机种子），与0.000200的噪声标准差相当。

Conclusion: 保留源哈密顿量并校准样品相关谱差异，该流程架起了局域自旋物理与集体光学读出之间的桥梁，使集合线型的各贡献可分解并用于物理解释。

Abstract: Characterizing nitrogen-vacancy (NV) ensembles requires connecting their collective spectra to individual centers that experience different local environments. We develop a symbolic-regression pipeline that carries single-center spin structure into a calibrated ensemble model by generating individual spectral responses and combining them with explicit weights. A constrained search selects Hermitian operator terms from single-center optically detected magnetic resonance spectra, fits their coefficients, and retains the selected expressions as source models for the ensemble. Ensemble calibration then accounts for resonance shifts, local disorder, orientation, and optical response while keeping the individual spectral contributions accessible. On public single-center and ensemble data, the zero-field ensemble held-frequency root-mean-square error decreases from 0.008835 to 0.003872 in normalized photoluminescence, a 56.2% improvement over matched uncorrected composition. In controlled QuTiP ensembles of 128 sites spanning four NV orientations, models calibrated at 0 and 3 mT predict an independently held-out 5.17 mT spectrum with errors of 0.000207-0.000228 across three random seeds, comparable to the 0.000200 noise standard deviation. Resolving the simulated response into its individual-center contributions shows how local spectral diversity combines into the ensemble line shape. By retaining the source Hamiltonians while calibrating sample-dependent spectral differences, the pipeline connects local spin physics to collective optical readout and makes the contributions to an ensemble line shape available for physical interpretation.

</details>


### [322] [Self-Correcting 3D Surface Code with Growing Local Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35099)
*Jinkang Guo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在 Z_q 上构造了随系统尺寸增长而增大局域维度的自纠错三维表面码，证明其在临界温度以下、q=Θ(N) 时两错误扇区的存储时间至少按 exp[Ω(N^{1/3})] 增长，数值结果与解析结论一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统三维表面码等自纠错量子存储受限于固定局域维度，难以在保持简单局域几何与局域热动力学的前提下获得长存储时间；作者希望探索"增大局域希尔伯特空间维度"作为设计自纠错量子存储的新资源。

Method: 在 Z_q 上构造自纠错三维表面码：保留普通三维表面码的局域几何、稳定子支撑结构及体内平移不变性，仅修改稳定子的泡利幂次；采用 Lee 度量刻画错误权重；考虑仅含单量子位 X^{±1}、Z^{±1} 更新的局域 Metropolis 动力学，每次更新能量变化至多为与 q、系统尺寸 N 无关的常数。

Result: 证明在临界温度以下、局域维度取 q=Θ(N) 时，X 与 Z 两个错误扇区的存储时间均至少按 exp[Ω(N^{1/3})] 增长；数值模拟结果进一步支持上述解析结论。

Conclusion: 增大局域希尔伯特空间维度可作为设计自纠错量子存储器的新资源，且无需牺牲简单的局域几何与局域热动力学。

Abstract: We construct self-correcting 3D surface codes over $\mathbb Z_q$ with local dimension $q$ growing with system size. The model preserves the local geometry and stabilizer support of the ordinary 3D surface code and remains translation invariant in the bulk, while modifying only the stabilizer Pauli powers. We use the Lee metric to characterize error weight and consider local Metropolis dynamics with single-qudit $X^{\pm1}$ and $Z^{\pm1}$ updates, each changing the energy by at most a constant independent of $q$ and system size $N$. We prove that, below the critical temperature, when the local dimension scales as $q=Θ(N)$, the memory times in both error sectors grow at least as $\exp\left[Ω(N^{1/3})\right]$. Our numerical results further support these analytical results. Together, these results establish growing local Hilbert-space dimension as a new resource for designing self-correcting quantum memories while retaining simple local geometry and local thermal dynamics.

</details>


### [323] [Multinomial shot sampling for quantum circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35119)
*Ijaz Ahamed Mohammad,Daniel Nagaj,Martin Plesch*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出用多项分布（分解为条件二项抽取、以BTPE在期望常数时间内实现）直接从末态概率分布生成计数，使采样代价与shots数S无关，从而大幅加速量子线路经典模拟中的采样环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Qiskit、PennyLane等主流框架在已获得完整状态向量的情况下，仍逐条生成并处理每个shot，采样开销随shots数S线性增长；而若只需测量计数，这种逐shot处理是多余的。

Method: 将计数采样视为从多项分布抽样，分解为一系列条件二项分布抽样，每个二项抽样由BTPE算法在期望常数时间内完成；据此实现采样器，并针对概率集中于少数结果的状态进一步缩短计算。

Result: 在最多20量子比特、10^6 shots的范围内与Qiskit采样原语对比，所有测试配置下均更快，加速比随shots数增长，S=10^6时多数配置超过10^3倍；采样结果与参考实现在统计上不可区分。

Conclusion: 基于多项分布的计数直接采样可将采样代价与shots数解耦，兼顾正确性与显著效率提升，适合替代逐shot采样流程。

Abstract: In classical simulations of quantum circuits, sampling shots (measurement outcomes) from the final state is a routine but costly step. In widely used software, including Qiskit and PennyLane, its cost grows linearly with the number of shots $S$, even when the simulation has already produced the full state vector, because every shot is generated and processed individually. If only the measurement counts are needed, this is unnecessary. The counts can be drawn directly from a multinomial distribution, decomposed into a sequence of conditional binomial draws, each performed in expected constant time by the BTPE algorithm. The sampling cost is then independent of $S$. We describe an implementation of this approach, which also shortens the computation for states whose probability is concentrated on a few outcomes, and compare it with Qiskit's sampling primitives for up to 20 qubits and $10^6$ shots. Our implementation is faster in every configuration tested, by a factor that grows with the number of shots and exceeds $10^3$ in most configurations at $S = 10^6$, and its samples are statistically indistinguishable from those of the reference implementations.

</details>


### [324] [Comparing classical and quantum QUBO solvers: optimality, trajectory diagnostics, and limits of solver selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35126)
*Gerhard Hellstern,Cedric Schacht,Martin Zaefferer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在10类共180个(14–48变量)QUBO基准及扩展至128变量的合成实例上，比较了分支割平面(classical)、张量网络(DMRG)与固定调度LR-QAOA三类求解器，强调"解质量"与"最优性证书"必须分开评估；优化解随机移位后搜索难度结构变化，平行回火在60 s内全数命中而分支割平面在n=48时几乎失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QUBO求解器比较常混淆“找到好解”与“证明最优”；同时缺乏对张量网络/变分方法与经典求解器在受控条件下的公平对比，以及解释其性能差异的诊断工具。作者希望厘清认证能力、搜索能力与结构特征（如树宽、纠缠）之间的关系，并检验LR-QAOA是否存在解质量优势。

Method: 1) 主基准：10族180个实例(14–48变量)，另加已知最优的更难合成实例(至128变量)，并构造“最优解随机重定位”变体以剥离结构偏置；2) 比较分支割平面、平行回火、张量网络DMRG(热启动)与固定线性斜坡LR-QAOA，区分解质量与最优性认证；3) 结构诊断：用树宽界预测轨迹键维需求(Spearman相关，样本内/外)；4) 轨迹诊断与受控消融：识别DMRG扫描中的激发态俘获并测试重启缓解；5) 独立的Wishart随机矩阵缩放研究。

Result: 主基准上分支割平面匹配全部参考能量并在600 s内认证176/180实例；最优解随机移位后n=48时仅命中10个中的4个最优且零认证，而平行回火每次运行均达全部最优(60 s预算)。树宽界与键维需求相关ρ=0.78(样本内)、0.73(样本外)，但对运行时间仅给出粗略估计。DMRG热启动扫描存在激发态俘获，重启变分搜索可缓解。LR-QAOA相对经典对比方法无解质量提升。Wishart更难设定下，n≥72时所有被测经典方法在给定预算内均未达到最优。

Conclusion: 求解器比较须将解质量与最优性认证分开报告，并明确实例结构偏置；树宽与轨迹诊断可解释张量网络行为但不构成可验证的求解器选择规则；LR-QAOA未显示解质量优势；Wishart结果不构成量子优势证据，但界定了值得后续研究的困难区域。

Abstract: Comparing solvers for quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) requires distinguishing solution quality from optimality certification. We compare classical, tensor-network, and fixed-schedule quantum approaches on 180 benchmark instances from ten families with 14-48 variables, supplemented by harder synthetic instances with known optimal solutions, extending to 128 variables. On the main benchmark, a general-purpose branch-and-cut solver matches all reference energies and certifies 176 instances within a 600,s cap. When the known optima are moved to randomly chosen bitstrings, the search comparison changes: at n = 48 the branch-and-cut solver finds only four of ten optima and certifies none, whereas parallel tempering reaches every optimum in every tested run at a 60,s budget. Structural and trajectory diagnostics explain aspects of tensor-network performance. A treewidth bound ranks trajectory bond-dimension demand at Spearman $ρ= 0.78$ in sample and $0.73$ out of sample, but provides only a coarse runtime estimate. Controlled experiments identify excited-state trapping during warm-started density-matrix renormalization group (DMRG) sweeps and demonstrate mitigation by restarting the variational search. These diagnostics do not yield a validated solver-selection rule. The tested linear-ramp quantum approximate optimization algorithm (LR-QAOA) runs offer no solution-quality improvement over the classical comparators. In the separate Wishart scaling study, on the harder setting, none of the classical methods tested reaches the optimum at the sampled sizes from n = 72 onward within the tested budgets. While this does not provide evidence for quantum advantage, it identifies a domain for further investigation.

</details>


### [325] [External-field-modified entropic exchange in confined quantum systems: static versus time-dependent electric fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35178)
*Vinod Prasad*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述外电场如何改变空间受限原子的信息含量，对比均匀静电场与单色时变激光场的响应，以香农熵（位置熵Sr、动量熵Sp及总和ST，满足BBM界）为核心量，以受限氢原子(CHA)和其紧致限粒子在球盒中(PISB)为参考体系进行分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外部场（静电场与激光场）会改变空间受限原子的电子结构信息含量，而信息论量（如香农熵）能提供比能量、极化率更普适的描述。需要系统厘清受限体系在均匀静态场与单色时变场下熵的响应规律，并与自由原子和紧致极限作对比。

Method: 采用信息论熵框架，计算位置空间香农熵Sr、动量空间香农熵Sp及其和ST=Sr+Sp（检验BBM界）。以受限氢原子(CHA)及作为其紧致极限的球盒粒子(PISB)为参考体系：先分析无场情形下总熵随限制半径的变化；再在限制半径6 a.u.下，对最低十个m=0能级计算熵的Stark图，并比较基态极化率。

Result: 无场时CHA的总熵随限制半径呈非单调变化，而PISB的总熵严格标度不变。基态极化率：自由原子为4.5 a.u.，CHA中降至4.06 a.u.，而PISB中高达46.8 a.u.；相应地，两体系在Stark场下的熵响应也呈现显著差异。

Conclusion: 空间受限显著调制原子的熵信息含量：CHA与PISB在无场熵的半径依赖、极化率及Stark熵响应上均表现出本质差异，说明限制效应对电场下原子信息结构起关键作用，BBM界可作为统一的约束判据。

Abstract: We review how external electric fields reshape the information content of spatially confined atoms, and we contrast the response to a uniform static field with that to a monochromatic, time-dependent laser field. The central quantities are the Shannon entropies in position and momentum space, Sr and Sp, and their sum ST = Sr +Sp, which obeys the BBM bound. Using the confined hydrogen atom (CHA) and the particle in a spherical box (PISB), its tight confinement limit, as reference systems, we first show that the field-free total entropy of the CHA depends non-monotonically on the confining radius whereas that of the PISB is strictly scale invariant. We then present Stark maps of the entropies for the ten lowest m = 0 levels at the confining radius of 6 a.u. The ground-state polarizability is reduced from the free-atom value 4.5 a.u. to 4.06 a.u. in the CHA but is 46.8 a.u. in the PISB, and the entropic response of the two systems differs correspondingly.

</details>


### [326] [Quantized-resonance description of a quantum emitter dynamics in a lossy multimode cavity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35184)
*Lucas Weitzel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于共振量子化方案模拟一维有损腔中原子动力学，论证多模效应的描述必须采用多个离散模式，并揭示所需最小模式数随光-物质耦合强度增加而增大、且动力学呈现强烈的位置依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在cQED多模区域，量子发射体可与多个共振场模耦合，产生实验可及的特征；但有损腔中常用的有效离散模式方法在超过两个模式时的精度鲜有基准检验，因此需评估多模描述的必要性与适用范围。

Method: 采用Weitzel等（2026，arXiv:2512.18478）提出的共振量子化处方模拟一维有损谐振腔中单原子的动力学，分别以光-物质耦合强度和原子位置为参数，考察截断模式数对结果收敛性的影响。

Result: 所需最小模式数随耦合强度增大而增加；多模区域下原子动力学对位置呈强依赖；并讨论了各结果的物理解释及所用形式化方法还原物理相关量的途径。

Conclusion: 两模近似不足以刻画多模区动力学，需采用足够多的离散模式；共振量子化框架可自洽地给出有损腔多模效应的可靠描述并提取物理量。

Abstract: In the multimode regime of cavity quantum electrodynamics (cQED), light-matter coupling can be strong enough such as to allows a quantum emitter to couple with multiple resonant field modes. This regime leaves distinctive signatures in the behavior of both the field and the emitter, which are already accessible by modern experimental techniques. To model such effects in lossy cavities, effective discrete-mode approaches have been widely employed, despite their accuracy beyond two modes remaining rarely benchmarked. In this work, we demonstrate the necessity of employing multiple modes to describe the multimode signatures by analyzing the dynamics of an atom in a one-dimensional lossy resonator. The atom's dynamics is simulated using the formalism developed in Weitzel et al. (2026) (arXiv:2512.18478), where a resonance quantization prescription is provided. We investigate its dependency with respect to two different parameters: the light-matter coupling strength and the atom's position. In the former case, we find that the minimal number of modes necessary to capture the essential features of the atom's dynamics increases with the coupling strength. In the latter, we further observe that the multimode regime leads to a strong position-dependency of the atom's dynamics. In all cases, we discuss the physical interpretation of our results and further discuss about how the employed formalism can be used to recover physically relevant quantities.

</details>


### [327] [Basis-set error bounds for first-quantised quantum simulation of chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35205)
*Dominic W. Berry,Arkin Tikku,Sophia Simon,Ryan Babbush*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文严格证明了量子化学量子模拟中，基组误差（相对连续极限）在基组规模N下以O(N^{-1})收敛，涵盖平面波与空间网格两类基组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前关于基组估计误差以O(N^{-1})收敛的结论仅来自解析与数值研究，量子算法所关心的量（基态能量、时间演化观测量、动能）的误差缺乏严格分析。

Method: 对平面波基和空间网格基（周期与开放边界）建立严格误差界：分析基态能量误差、时间演化态误差及观测量误差，并引入边界无显著泄漏条件以分离边界基组误差与离散分辨率误差。

Result: 基态能量误差为O(N^{-1})；态误差弱于O(N^{-1})，但观测量误差为Õ(N^{-1})；平滑位置观测量误差为O(N^{-5/3}+tN^{-1})；动能误差为O((t+log N)/N)。

Conclusion: O(N^{-1})的误差标度在量子算法相关的众多情形下严格成立（动能仅多一个对数因子），为基组规模选择提供理论依据。

Abstract: In quantum simulation of electronic structure it is often expected that the estimate of the ground energy converges to the continuum basis limit at a rate of $\mathcal O(N^{-1})$ for a basis of size $N$. This has been found in analytical and numerical studies, but without a rigorous treatment of the error in the quantities considered in quantum algorithms. Here we show the $\mathcal O(N^{-1})$ scaling of the error for a wide range of cases needed for quantum simulation of chemistry. We show the error in the ground energy scales as $\mathcal O(N^{-1})$ for plane-wave as well as spatial grid bases. For the spatial grid we consider both a periodic system and a non-periodic system with open boundaries. For time evolution, our bound on the error in the state itself is weaker than $\mathcal O(N^{-1})$, but we show that the error in observables scales as $\widetilde{\mathcal O}(N^{-1})$ in important cases. In particular, we may consider a smoothed observable that describes the position of particles. We show that this error scales as $\mathcal O(N^{-5/3}+tN^{-1})$ for time $t$, again both for plane-wave and spatial grid bases. For the non-periodic grid case we use a condition that the true evolution would not result in significant leakage past the boundaries, which introduces basis error unrelated to the resolution of the discretisation. We also show the error in the kinetic energy of a particle scales as $\mathcal O((t+\log N)/N)$ for the plane-wave and grid bases. Together, these results show that $\mathcal O(N^{-1})$ scaling of the error holds for many of the relevant cases for quantum algorithms (with a trivial log factor for kinetic energy).

</details>


### [328] [A Control Line Multiplexing Framework for Scalable Quantum Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35227)
*Dayu Li,Naibin Zhou,Zhiyuan Chen,Zihua Chen,Yu Xu,Yuan Li,Daojin Fan,Wenhao Chu,Xiawei Chen,Hui Deng,Jiacheng Gui,Linyin Hong,Bo Fan,Weiping Lin,Haonan Zou,Haoxin Lou,Xintao Zhao,Sirui Cao,Ying Chong,Qingling Zhu,Chen Wang,Yisen Hu,Kaili Zhang,Feifan Su,Haibin Zhang,Jianghan Yin,Yajie Du,Yinan Jiang,Haoping Luo,Weifeng Yang,Jiafei Wang,Jin Lin,Futian Liang,Hao Rong,Kai Yan,Ming Gong,Jinjin Li,Maliang Liu,Cheng-Zhi Peng,Shaowei Li,Xiaobo Zhu,Jian-Wei Pan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出周期性可扩展的混合复用框架，将全部门操作（含双比特门）以多色微波脉冲与带通滤波器实现频分复用控制，1:4演示中并行FDM CZ门平均误差(6.4±0.2)×10⁻³、并行FDM XY(π/2)门(10.6±0.2)×10⁻⁴，无显著复用退化，可把控制线数量降低1–2个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错超导量子计算机预计需数百万物理比特，而现有"每比特独立控制线"范式在布线与稀释制冷机冷量预算上无法扩展到该规模；经典通信中的频分复用（FDM）思想有潜力缓解该瓶颈，但迁移到超导量子控制需全微波架构，且双比特门仍多为单极性脉冲驱动，是缺失环节。

Method: 构建全微波、周期性可扩展的混合复用框架：门操作均通过多色（polychromatic）微波脉冲加带通滤波器进行频分复用，配合片上直流源实现单/双比特门与读出的统一控制；在1:4复用配置下对并行FDM CZ门与并行FDM XY(π/2)门进行实验标定，并与非复用情形对比误差，统计检验复用引起的性能退化。

Result: 1:4演示中并行FDM CZ门平均误差(6.4±0.2)×10⁻³，并行FDM XY(π/2)门平均误差(10.6±0.2)×10⁻⁴；复用未带来统计上显著的性能退化；控制线数量可降低1–2个数量级。

Conclusion: FDM门控制方案补齐了可扩展复用控制架构中最后的缺失模块（微波驱动的双比特门），结合片上直流源，构成迈向通用容错量子计算的完整复用控制路径。

Abstract: Universal fault-tolerant superconducting quantum computers are expected to require millions of physical qubits; yet extending the prevailing dedicated-control-line paradigm to this scale would exceed the wiring density and cooling-power budget of dilution refrigerators. Microwave frequency-division multiplexing (FDM) revolutionized classical communications by enabling many independent channels to share common transmission infrastructure. Translating this principle to superconducting quantum control, however, requires an all-microwave architecture and experimental validation of frequency-multiplexed gate control. Recent work in quantum error correction has demonstrated microwave-based readout, single-qubit gates and leakage suppression, whereas two-qubit gates remain predominantly driven by unipolar pulses and thus constitute the missing element of an all-microwave architecture. Here, we propose a periodically extensible hybrid multiplexing framework in which all gate operations are frequency-division multiplexed using polychromatic microwave pulses and band-pass filters, thereby reducing the required control-line count by one to two orders of magnitude. In a 1:4 demonstration, we achieve average errors of $(6.4\pm0.2)\times10^{-3}$ for parallel FDM CZ gates and $(10.6\pm0.2)\times10^{-4}$ for parallel FDM $XY(π/2)$ gates, without statistically significant multiplexing-induced degradation. Together with on-chip DC sources, this FDM-based gate-control scheme completes the last missing module in a scalable multiplexed-control architecture toward universal fault-tolerant quantum computing.

</details>


### [329] [Quantum-Classical Framework for the Joint Optimization of Bidirectional EV Charging and Trip Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35300)
*Mustafa Musab,Sebastian Schmitt,Markus Wappler,Jörg Lässig,Daniel Müssig*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出混合量子-经典Benders分解框架，用逻辑冲突割替代需大量辅助量子比特的可行性割，并设计稀疏递归SoC公式降低约束矩阵密度，在基准算例上恢复MILP最优解并在IBM量子硬件上端到端验证小规模实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: V2G双向充电使电动车队可作为分布式能源资产，但充电调度与行程分配的联合优化是NP-hard问题；且双向功率流造成SoC动态非单调，削弱了标准基于对偶的Benders割的有效性，同时编码可行性割的量子比特开销过大。

Method: 构建混合量子-经典Benders分解框架：引入基于逻辑的冲突割（logic-based conflict cuts），几乎不需要辅助量子比特；提出稀疏递归SoC建模，使约束矩阵密度在时域长度T下降低T/3倍。

Result: 在基准算例上以较低的辅助量子比特预算恢复MILP最优解；作为最小可行性验证，在IBM量子硬件上端到端执行了一个小规模实例。

Conclusion: 逻辑冲突割与稀疏递归SoC公式能显著降低量子资源开销并保持最优性，初步证明混合量子-经典Benders方法求解V2G车队联合优化问题的可行性。

Abstract: The integration of bidirectional (V2G) charging allows electric vehicle (EV) fleets to operate as distributed energy assets. However, the joint optimization of charging schedules and trip assignments is NP-hard. Moreover, bidirectional power flows create non-monotonic state-of-charge (SoC) dynamics that weaken standard dual-based Benders cuts. We propose a hybrid quantum-classical Benders decomposition framework. To overcome the qubit overhead associated with encoding feasibility cuts, we introduce logic-based conflict cuts, which require near-zero auxiliary qubits. Furthermore, we develop a sparse recursive SoC formulation that reduces the constraint-matrix density by a factor of T/3 in the horizon length T. We demonstrate that our framework recovers MILP-optimal solutions on benchmark instances with a low auxiliary-qubit budget, and, as a minimal proof of concept, we execute a small instance end-to-end on IBM quantum hardware.

</details>


### [330] [Spectral fingerprint recognition of mobile signals via Rydberg atomic sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35301)
*Yi-Ming Yin,Ming-Yao Cui,Ling-Yu Du,Jin-Hang Fang,Luan-Yi Wei,Bang Liu,Li-Hua Zhang,Kai Wang,Kai-Bin Huang,Dong-Sheng Ding*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出基于里德堡原子电磁诱导透明（EIT）光谱与深度学习的移动信号分类框架：构建手机语音通话、Wi-Fi、5G的谱指纹数据集，CNN基线分类准确率93.44%，并用渐进式微调应对光谱漂移，60秒连续监测帧级识别准确率达87.67%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 里德堡原子传感器具备自校准、宽带覆盖和高灵敏度的优势，但面向真实无线信号的智能识别潜力尚未被充分探索，因此作者希望将量子射频传感与深度学习结合，实现对商用移动信号的智能分类与实时监测。

Method: 以里德堡原子EIT光谱为探测手段，采集三种商用手机品牌的蜂窝语音通话、Wi-Fi与5G信号的谱指纹数据，构建数据集；训练卷积神经网络进行分类；针对时间和环境变化引起的光谱漂移，采用渐进式微调策略持续自适应预训练模型；并在动态变化场景（语音、Wi-Fi及二者共存）下开展60秒连续实时监测。

Result: CNN基线分类准确率为93.44%；经渐进式微调后可在多种部署场景下保持稳健分类；60秒连续测量中整体帧级识别准确率达87.67%。

Conclusion: 结果表明里德堡原子平台结合深度学习可实现智能量子射频感知，具备实时无线信号感知与频谱监测的应用潜力，为智能化量子射频探测提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Rydberg-atom sensors have emerged as a promising quantum technology for broadband radio-frequency detection owing to their intrinsic calibration, wide frequency coverage, and high sensitivity. However, their potential for intelligent identification of real-world wireless signals remains largely unexplored. Here, we demonstrate a quantum-enabled framework for mobile signal classification based on Rydberg electromagnetically induced transparency spectroscopy and deep learning. Three types of wireless emissions, including cellular voice call, Wi-Fi, and 5G signals from three commercial smartphone brands, are measured to construct a spectral-fingerprint dataset. A convolutional neural network trained on this dataset achieves an initial baseline classification accuracy of 93.44\%. To compensate for spectral drifts induced by temporal and environmental variations, a progressive fine-tuning strategy is employed to continuously adapt the pretrained model, enabling robust classification across diverse deployment scenarios. Furthermore, continuous real-time monitoring is demonstrated under dynamically varying wireless conditions, including cellular voice call, Wi-Fi, and their coexistence. The proposed system achieves an overall frame-level recognition accuracy of 87.67\% over a 60-s measurement. These results establish a pathway toward intelligent quantum radio-frequency sensing and highlight the potential of Rydberg-atom platforms for real-time wireless signal awareness and spectrum monitoring.

</details>


### [331] [Sublinear thermal state and ground state preparation for free fermions via system-bath interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35370)
*Zhiyan Ding,Lin Lin,Ke Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过在自由费米子体系中引入与系统规模成正比的局域浴，构造耗散算法，将热态制备的总模拟时间降到亚线性 $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt N/\varepsilon)$，并证明有能隙基态制备只需 $\mathrm{polylog}(N/\varepsilon)$ 时间；同时给出该类浴协议下的下界，指出限制进一步加速的两类障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有耗散式热态与基态制备算法即使混合时间仅为多对数级，总哈密顿量模拟时间仍需至少随系统规模 $N$ 线性增长。作者希望突破这一线性时间壁垒，并厘清决定进一步复杂度改进的浴与滤波器设计约束。

Method: 以自由费米子为平台，设计规模与系统规模成正比的浴及局域相互作用（浴协议），从任意初态出发实现耗散演化；在固定逆温度、有界单粒子带宽条件下分析热态制备的迹范数误差，并对有能隙系统的基态制备给出多对数时间算法；进一步在该浴协议框架内建立热态制备的下界。

Result: 热态制备达到迹范数误差 $\varepsilon$ 的总模拟时间为 $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{N}/\varepsilon)$（固定逆温度、有界单粒子带宽），有能隙基态制备仅需 $\mathrm{polylog}(N/\varepsilon)$ 时间，均从任意初态出发。下界分析识别出两种阻碍更快速制备的因素：偏离目标热态的四阶偏差，以及所有浴量子比特共享的随机频率所引发的涨落。

Conclusion: 结果表明耗散式态制备中的线性模拟时间壁垒可以被克服，并明确了浴与滤波器设计对进一步复杂度提升的制约，为后续复杂度改进指明了方向。

Abstract: Dissipative algorithms for preparing thermal and ground states typically require a total Hamiltonian simulation time that grows at least linearly with system size $N$, even when their mixing time grows only polylogarithmically. We achieve sublinear simulation time for free fermions by engineering local interactions with a bath whose size grows in proportion to the system size. Starting from arbitrary initial states, our thermal state preparation algorithm achieves trace-norm error $ε$ in total simulation time $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{N}/ε)$ at fixed inverse temperature and bounded single-particle bandwidth, while ground state preparation of gapped systems requires only $\mathrm{polylog}(N/ε)$ time. We further establish lower bounds for thermal state preparation within the bath protocols considered, identifying two obstacles to faster preparation, namely fourth-order deviations from the target thermal state and fluctuations induced by a random frequency shared by all bath qubits. Our results demonstrate that the linear simulation time barrier in dissipative state preparation can be overcome and clarify the bath and filter-design constraints that govern further complexity improvements.

</details>


### [332] [Multipartite dynamics beyond bipartite entanglement equilibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35377)
*Yan Liu,Ya-Wen Sun,Ren-Jie Ying,Jun-Yuan Zeng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 纠缠平衡并不代表量子信息动力学的终结：固定三划分的完整Schmidt谱可先趋于平稳，而其局部幺正不变量刻画的多体关联结构仍在演化；二者在自对偶kicked-Ising模型中于有限时间窗内精确分离，在Haar随机线路中渐近分离，并可通过量子纠错码独立调控多体动力学时标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子热化研究以纠缠熵（或Schmidt谱）达到平衡作为信息动力学结束的标志，隐含"平衡时间先后"式的单一弛豫图像。作者质疑这一假设，试图厘清二分纠缠与多体关联结构是否只是同一弛豫过程的不同阶段，还是对应不同的物理通道。

Method: 以固定三划分下的完整Schmidt谱与局部幺正不变量（多体关联结构的判据）作为两类观测量，在三种平台上比较其平衡行为：(1) 自对偶kicked-Ising动力学；(2) Haar随机线路；(3) 量子纠错码构造，将逻辑自由度耦合到局部scrambling热浴，通过弱逻辑—热浴耦合分离时标。

Result: 自对偶kicked-Ising模型中，Schmidt谱平稳而多体不变量继续演化，该分离出现在有限时间窗内；Haar随机线路中呈渐近分离。在纠错码加局部scrambling热浴的设定下，普通纠缠经快速局部scrambling迅速平衡，而弱逻辑—热浴耦合驱动多体关联以更慢时标重组，从而实现独立可调的时间尺度。

Conclusion: 二分纠缠与多体关联结构探测的是量子信息动力学的不同物理通道，而非同一弛豫过程的不同阶段；这比单纯比较平衡时间先后更为深刻，并提示热化研究需区分谱平衡与关联结构平衡。

Abstract: Entanglement equilibration need not mark the end of quantum-information dynamics in quantum thermalization. We show that the complete Schmidt spectra of a fixed tripartition can become stationary while its multipartite correlation structure, distinguished by local-unitary invariants, continues to evolve. This separation occurs exactly within a finite window in self-dual kicked-Ising dynamics and asymptotically in Haar-random circuits. We further demonstrate an independently tunable timescale for multipartite dynamics using quantum-error-correcting codes. When coupled to a locally scrambling bath, ordinary entanglement approaches equilibrium through rapid local scrambling, while weak logical--bath coupling drives a slower reorganization of multipartite correlations. These results reveal a deeper distinction than the ordering of equilibration times: bipartite entanglement and multipartite correlation structure can probe distinct physical channels of quantum-information dynamics, rather than merely different stages of the same relaxation process.

</details>


### [333] [Multimode continuous-variable quantum key distribution with CMOS-compatible photonic-electronic integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35410)
*Yiming Bian,Sarah Bastiaens,Saeed Hakimi,Huy Quang Nguyen,Aboobackkar Sidhique,Cédric Bruynsteen,Govind J. Wanjalkar,Ali Al Hadi Amhaz,Jasper Jans,Axl Bomhals,Ulrik L. Andersen,Tobias Gehring,Xin Yin,Adnan A. E. Hajomer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出CMOS兼容的光电集成多模连续变量QKD架构，以偏振复用作为双模示例，实现硅光收发芯片与28 nm CMOS跨阻放大器共集成，并通过多模安全性分析计入模式串扰，在10 km现网光纤上获得6.69 Mbit/s的平均有限码长安全密钥率估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复用与光电集成是通向可扩展、高吞吐QKD网络的路径，但需要多通道收发机，以及能够计入模式耦合的安全性分析。

Method: 基于硅光子的发射与接收芯片并行制备与探测两个偏振模式；接收端与双通道28 nm CMOS跨阻放大器共集成，两通道散粒噪声裕度均超过20 dB；采用导频辅助的酉变换数字恢复，免除主动光学偏振控制；建立计入模式串扰的多模安全性分析。

Result: 在2%残余串扰下，多模分析将仿真安全距离由39 km延长至84 km，接近无串扰极限89 km；在10 km现网光纤上，平均总有限码长安全密钥率估计为6.69 Mbit/s，比同一数字恢复数据的单模分析高12%。

Conclusion: 该工作为基于适于批量生产技术的可扩展多模量子安全网络提供了一条可行路线。

Abstract: Multiplexing and photonic-electronic integration offer a route to scalable and high-throughput quantum key distribution (QKD) networks through parallel transmission and compact hardware suited to volume manufacturing. Realizing this route requires multichannel transmitters and receivers, and a security analysis accounting for mode coupling. Here we present a CMOS-compatible photonic-electronic multimode continuous-variable QKD architecture, using polarization multiplexing as a two-mode example. Silicon-photonic transmitter and receiver chips prepare and detect two polarization modes in parallel. The receiver is co-integrated with a dual-channel 28-nm CMOS transimpedance amplifier, providing shot-noise clearances exceeding 20 dB in both channels. Pilot-assisted unitary digital recovery removes the need for active optical polarization control, while a multimode security analysis accounts for the mode crosstalk. At 2 percent residual crosstalk, the multimode analysis extends the simulated secure distance from 39 to 84 km, near the crosstalk-free limit of 89 km. Over 10 km of deployed fibre, the mean total finite-size secret-key rate estimate is 6.69 Mbit/s, 12 percent above the single-mode analysis from the same digitally recovered data. These results establish a route to scalable multimode quantum-secured networks built on technology suited to mass production.

</details>


### [334] [Population Capacity and the Geometry of Thermomajorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35433)
*F. Meroi,C. Massri,M. Losada,G. Bellomo,G. M. Bosyk*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文以"布居容量"(population capacity)——热操作可将布居浓缩到任一能级集合上的最大值——为统一工具，刻画热操作下的准经典态转换：容量构成规范化、单调、次模的秩函数，其基多面体恰为热可达布居区域，因而给出完备的可达性约束体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热操作下的态转换与热主序(thermomajorization)判据已知，但缺乏一个能统一刻画可达性、可观测量优化、转换证书与近似转换的几何/结构框架。作者希望找到一种"公共的布居容量几何"，使这些不同问题由同一套约束函数刻画。

Method: 引入每个能级集合上的容量函数，证明其为规范化、非减的次模秩函数，并以基多面体精确表述热可达布居区域；利用嵌套容量链可被某可达态同时饱和的性质处理可观测量极大化；以热主序前缀作为精确转换证书，用最多 n−1 次二元取功探针验证；近似情形下由活跃容量约束构造加权主序列得到的特定可达态（O(n² log n)）；最后将迹距离误差与容量优势联系起来。

Result: (1) 容量界构成完备的可达性约束系统；(2) 任一嵌套容量链可被同时饱和，从而得到任意给定可观测量的最大期望，含系统哈密顿量；(3) 目标的热主序前缀足以通过至多 n−1 次二元取功探针认证精确转换；(4) 固定比较权重时，所构造状态最小化一切加权凸可分残差代价，并与目标中心平滑区域实现共同首次接触；(5) 最优迹距离误差等于目标相对初态的最大容量优势，该差距在某个目标前缀处达到，且等于同一组二元探针中最大正优化储能亏空除以能隙。

Conclusion: 统一的布居容量几何同时决定可达性、可观测量优化、转换证书与普适近似，为准经典热态转换提供了完整而可计算的约束框架。

Abstract: We characterize quasiclassical state conversion under thermal operations through population capacities: the maximum population that thermal operations can concentrate on each set of energy levels. These capacities form a normalized, nondecreasing submodular rank function whose base polytope is exactly the thermally reachable population region. Their bounds therefore provide a complete system of accessibility constraints. Every nested chain of capacities can be simultaneously saturated by a reachable state, yielding the maximum expectation value of any prescribed observable, including the system Hamiltonian. For a prescribed target, its thermomajorization prefixes suffice to certify exact conversion through at most $n-1$ binary work-extraction probes. For approximate conversion, we construct a distinguished reachable state from active capacity constraints using a weighted principal sequence in $O(n^2 \log n)$ operations. At fixed comparison weights, this state minimizes every weighted convex separable residual cost and realizes a common first contact with the corresponding target-centered smoothing regions. Finally, the optimal trace-distance error equals the largest capacity advantage of the target over the initial state. This gap is attained at a target prefix and equals the largest positive optimized energy-storage deficit among the same binary probes, divided by their energy gap. Thus a common population capacity geometry determines accessibility, observable optimization, conversion certificates, and universal approximation.

</details>


### [335] [Multistability and Critical Frustrated Superradiance in Open Anisotropic Quantum Rabi Trimer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35445)
*Ya-Ping Ni,Yi-Ge Wu,Jie-Qiao Liao,Jin-Feng Huang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究开放各向异性量子Rabi三聚体的非平衡稳态，结合半经典与量子方法绘制相图，揭示各向异性光-物质耦合、腔耗散与复光子跳跃诱发的临界现象及多稳相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放Rabi/Dicke三聚体中的非平衡量子相变尚不清楚，尤其各向异性耦合、耗散与复光子跳跃如何共同影响多稳性与阻挫超辐射相（FSP）缺乏系统刻画。

Method: 以开放各向异性量子Rabi三聚体为模型，结合半经典平均场与量子力学处理（含量子涨落）计算非平衡稳态，绘制相图，并分析普适标度行为与临界指数。

Result: 发现丰富多稳相；FSP可被正、负光子跳跃共同稳定（闭系Rabi/Dicke三聚体中不存在）；正常超辐射相与FSP各具独立耗散阈值，超阈值后被抑制；旋转与反旋转相互作用项对量子相变呈互为倒易效应；FSP出现有限涨落临界指数，且各相临界指数依赖耦合强度。

Conclusion: 各向异性与耗散可调控多稳性和FSP，拓展了少体系统中非平衡量子相物理的研究范围，并为量子态工程及腔/电路量子电动力学提供应用前景。

Abstract: We study the nonequilibrium steady states of an open anisotropic quantum Rabi trimer. Combining semiclassical and quantum mechanical treatments, we map out the system's phase diagram and elucidate the critical phenomena induced by anisotropic light-matter coupling, cavity dissipation, and complex photon hopping, which give rise to abundant multistable phases. Counterintuitively, the frustrated superradiant phase (FSP) can be stabilized by both positive and negative photon hopping, a feature absent in closed Rabi and Dicke trimers. We further uncover two independent dissipation thresholds for the normal superradiant phase and the FSP. Unlike isotropic open quantum Rabi trimers, both phases are suppressed once the dissipation exceeds their respective critical values. The rotating and counter-rotating interaction terms exhibit mutually reciprocal effects on the quantum phase transitions. By analyzing universal scaling behaviors with quantum fluctuations incorporated, we reveal an unprecedented finite-fluctuation critical exponent for the FSP. Moreover, the critical exponents of individual phases are coupling-strength dependent. The multistability and FSP can be tuned by anisotropy and dissipation. Our results broaden the scope of nonequilibrium quantum phase physics in few-body systems and hold promising applications for quantum state engineering and cavity/circuit quantum electrodynamics.

</details>


### [336] [Electron-photon entanglement in real space from quantum electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35467)
*Ivan P. Christov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在真实空间与时间上解析研究电子与其散射光子之间的纠缠：基于费米振子表象的非相对论QED，在长度规范下写出光子系统构型空间中固定光子数分量的Fock耦合方程，截断至单光子并在一维波导模型原子上数值求解（不采用旋转波近似），发现全局电子-光子纠缠主要是瞬态的，而局部熵呈现空间相反分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子与它散射出的光子之间的纠缠通常只在模式空间或渐近态中讨论，缺乏在真实空间与真实时间上的分辨图像；作者希望揭示纠缠在散射过程中的时空生成与演化机制，特别是全局纠缠与局部（空间分辨）纠缠的差异。

Method: 从非相对论QED的费米振子表象出发，在长度规范下于光子构型空间中写出固定光子数分量的Fock耦合方程；光子经由非局域核在原子上产生。方程截断至单光子，在具有单一横模、耦合增强的一维波导模型原子上，用真实空间网格表示电子与光子并数值求解，全程不使用旋转波近似。原子被短离共振激光脉冲驱动；全局纠缠通过电子-光子态的整体性质刻画，局部纠缠则由在两个光子数分量上采样的"walker"获得。

Result: 全局电子-光子纠缠基本是瞬态的：在脉冲期间达到峰值，随着极化电子云返回原子实而下降，最终留下接近直积态的态——电子态与（场真空叠加单个散射光子的态）之积。局部纠缠对两粒子呈相反的空间图样：电子熵在原子核处最小，并映射偶极耦合的局部强度；光子熵在原子附近最大（光子产生与吸收之处），而在出射波包中较小。

Conclusion: 纠缠的时空分辨分析表明，散射后残余纠缠很小，纠缠主要短暂地产生于脉冲驱动期间；局部纠缠熵的空间分布反映耦合结构与光子产生/吸收位置，可作为诊断光-物质关联在空间上如何形成与释放的工具。

Abstract: We study the entanglement between an electron and the photon it scatters, resolved in real space and time. Starting from nonrelativistic QED in Fermi's oscillator representation, we write Fock's coupled equations for components with fixed photon number in the photon configuration space, in the length gauge, where the photon is created around the atom through a nonlocal kernel. Truncated at one photon, the equations are solved numerically without the rotating-wave approximation for a one-dimensional model atom in a waveguide with a single transverse mode and enhanced coupling. The atom is driven by a short, off-resonant laser pulse, with the electron and photon represented on real-space grids. The global electron-photon entanglement is largely transient. It peaks during the pulse and decreases as the polarized electron cloud returns to the core, leaving a state close to a product of the electron state and a superposition of the field vacuum with a single scattered photon. Local entanglement, obtained from walkers sampled across both photon-number components, exhibits opposite spatial patterns for the two particles. The electron entropy is smallest at the nucleus and maps the local strength of the dipole coupling, whereas the photon entropy is largest near the atom, where photons are created and absorbed, and smaller in the outgoing wave packets.

</details>


### [337] [Benchmarking quantum-annealing-based approaches for factory layout planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35478)
*Xiangqian Wu,Philipp Schworm,Marcel Wagner,Jan C. Aurich*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文系统基准测试了四种基于量子退火（QA）的工厂布局规划方法（IBQM、HDQM、HCQM、HNLM），并与遗传算法、粒子群优化及禁忌搜索-模拟退火混合方法在14个布局实例上对比解质量、计算时间与可扩展性；结果显示QA方法计算时间最多降低94.51%，其中IBQM在78.6%实例中解质量最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工厂布局规划早期阶段直接影响制造成本、效率与生产率，而传统自动规划方法在解质量与计算量之间存在权衡。已有QA研究多局限于单一QA建模形式，未充分利用支持QA与经典方法组合的多种模型类型的混合QA求解器，因此需要系统比较不同QA建模途径的潜力。

Method: 实现并基准测试四种基于QA的方法及对应求解器：迭代二次无约束二值模型（IBQM）、混合离散二次模型（HDQM）、混合约束二次模型（HCQM）、混合非线性模型（HNLM）。为提升可扩展性，针对IBQM提出迭代分解策略，针对HDQM引入候选选择流程。在14个具有不同布局规模、几何复杂度、空间约束与运输路径配置的实例上，与遗传算法、粒子群优化、禁忌搜索-模拟退火混合方法对比解质量、计算时间与可扩展性。

Result: QA类方法解质量具竞争力且计算优势显著，相对经典参考方法计算时间最多减少94.51%。IBQM在78.6%的实例中取得最佳解质量，最大改进达17.41%，同时计算时间减少81.34%。

Conclusion: 混合及迭代式QA建模在工厂布局规划中可实现解质量与计算效率的良好平衡，为QA在工业布局规划中的进一步应用提供了依据。

Abstract: The earlystage of factory layout planning has a direct effect on the operating costs, efficiency, and productivity of manufacturing systems. Conventional automated planning approaches often face a trade-off between solution quality and computational effort. Recent studies have investigated the potential of quantum annealing (QA) for solving factory layout planning problems. However, existing studies have mainly focused on individual QA-based formulations, whereas current hybrid QA solvers support multiple model types that combine QA and classical methods. To explore the potential of QA for factory layout planning, this paper benchmarks four QA based approaches implemented with the corresponding solvers, including an iterative binary quadratic model (IBQM), a hybrid discrete quadratic model (HDQM), a hybrid constrained quadratic model (HCQM), and a hybrid nonlinear model (HNLM). To improve scalability, an iterative decomposition strategy is developed for IBQM, while a candidate selection procedure is introduced for HDQM. The approaches are benchmarked against genetic algorithm, particle swarm optimization, and a hybrid tabu search and simulated annealing approach on 14 layout instances with different layout sizes, geometric complexity, spatial restrictions, and transport route configurations. The comparative analysis considers solution quality, computation time, and scalability. The results show that QA-based approaches achieve competitive solution quality and substantial computational advantages, reducing computation time by up to 94.51% relative to the classical reference methods. Among them, IBQM obtains the best solution quality in 78.6% of the instances, with a maximum improvement of 17.41% associated with an 81.34% reduction in computation time. These findings provide a basis for their further application in industrial layout planning.

</details>


### [338] [Fast and accurate rare-event simulation by Markov chain Monte Carlo for general quantum codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35498)
*Junichi Haruna,Kotaro Murakami*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 针对 splitting 方法中单比特翻转 MCMC 采样器在部分码上被"困住"导致估计偏差的问题，提出将局部平衡单比特翻转提议与簇交换（cluster-exchange）移动相结合的混合核，并在十二个码上验证其正确性与效率优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Splitting 方法用于估计量子纠错码在稀有事件区域（直接蒙特卡罗采样代价过高）的逻辑错误率，其依赖于对故障构型的 MCMC 采样，通常采用单比特翻转提议。但已有报告指出单比特翻转在部分码上会被困住，从而使估计产生偏差，需要新的采样核来修正。

Method: 设计新核：将局部平衡的单比特翻转提议与一种簇交换移动相结合。在码容量比特翻转噪声下，对十二个码以两种方式基准测试新核、单比特翻转核与直接蒙特卡罗：(1) 将平均故障权重与一个独立于 MCMC 的参考值比较；(2) 测量达到固定精度所需的计算时间。

Result: 第一种测试中，新核在所有码上均与参考值一致，包括单比特翻转被困住的码；第二种测试中，在单比特翻转被困之处新核更快，部分码上快数个数量级；在最低错误率下，新核在所有码上都快于直接蒙特卡罗，部分码上快四个数量级以上。

Conclusion: 簇交换与局部平衡单比特翻转相结合的混合核消除了单比特翻转的陷落偏差，兼具正确性与显著的计算效率提升，是 splitting 方法在稀有事件区域估计逻辑错误率的更优采样核。

Abstract: The splitting method estimates logical error rates of quantum error-correcting codes deep in the rare-event regime, where direct Monte Carlo sampling is prohibitively expensive. It relies on a Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) sampler of faulty configurations, usually driven by one-bit-flip proposals. However, one-bit flips have been reported to be trapped on some codes, which biases the estimate. To address this issue, we propose a new kernel that combines locally balanced one-bit-flip proposals with a cluster-exchange move. We benchmark both kernels and direct Monte Carlo on twelve codes under code-capacity bit-flip noise in two ways, by comparing the mean fault weight with an MCMC-independent reference and by measuring the computation time to reach a fixed accuracy. In the first, our kernel agrees with the reference on every code, including those where one-bit flips are trapped. In the second, it is faster than one-bit flips wherever they are trapped, by orders of magnitude on some codes. Furthermore, at the lowest error rate, it is faster than direct Monte Carlo on every code, by more than four orders of magnitude on some.

</details>


### [339] [Geometric aspects of fault tolerance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35513)
*Jonathan Conrad*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从几何角度研究容错：给出容错纤维丛框架的算子代数表述，证明Knill-Laflamme条件的推广蕴含平坦联络；为GKP码建立该框架，证明各极化类型的模空间可光滑嵌入正则化分布空间中有限维子空间的Grassmannian；并说明与Jacobian相伴的GKP码可产生由高斯态码空间投影得到的魔术态，识别出一类CCZ型魔术态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错码通常以代数条件（如Knill-Laflamme条件）刻画，缺乏统一的几何语言；同时GKP连续变量码的模空间结构与魔术态资源之间的几何联系尚不清晰。作者希望用纤维丛与联络的几何视角统一理解离散与连续变量容错码，并借此构造新的魔术态。

Method: 1) 构造容错纤维丛框架的算子代数形式化表述，并证明推广的Knill-Laflamme条件推出该框架上的平坦联络；2) 将该框架应用于GKP码，对每种极化类型考察其模空间，证明其光滑嵌入到正则化分布空间中有限维子空间构成的Grassmannian；3) 对与Jacobian相伴的GKP码，通过高斯态在码空间上的投影构造魔术态，并识别出CCZ型魔术态。

Result: 得到推广Knill-Laflamme条件与平坦联络之间的等价/蕴含关系；证明GKP码各极化类型模空间到Grassmannian的光滑嵌入；构造出由高斯态码空间投影产生的魔术态，并明确给出一类CCZ型魔术态。

Conclusion: 容错码可用纤维丛加平坦联络的几何语言统一描述，该表述自然涵盖GKP等连续变量码并揭示其模空间的Grassmannian嵌入结构；Jacobian型GKP码可作为魔术态（含CCZ型）的几何来源，为魔术态资源的构造提供新途径。

Abstract: We highlight three geometric aspects of fault tolerance. First, we construct an operator-algebraic formulation of the fiber bundle framework for fault tolerance, and show that a generalization of the Knill-Laflamme conditions implies a flat connection for this framework. Next, we develop this framework for Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill (GKP) codes. We show that, for each polarization type, their moduli space embeds smoothly into a Grassmannian of finite-dimensional subspaces of a regularized distribution space. Finally, we explain how GKP codes associated with Jacobians give rise to magic states that can be obtained by code space projections of Gaussian states and identify a $\rm CCZ$-type magic state arising this way.

</details>


### [340] [Quantum state revivals, time quasicrystals, and entanglement steering in a Fibonacci-driven central-spin model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35555)
*Anushka Agrawal,Adithya A. Vasista,Tanay Nag,Sayan Choudhury*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文在无无序的Fibonacci驱动中心-卫星自旋模型（中心与卫星自旋间Ising相互作用）中，提出无需Floquet框架即可实现完美复现与时间准晶（TQC）的路径：在λ=2mπ或θ=π等精细调控点可完全抑制纠缠产生，从而获得长寿命准周期振荡，并在Clifford点得到宇称依赖的完美振荡与Bell-cat/自旋猫态，可用于Heisenberg极限度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是寻找超越Floquet范式（周期性驱动）的非平衡物相。传统离散时间晶体依赖Floquet驱动，而准周期（Fibonacci）驱动下的时间准晶及完美复现机制尚缺乏清晰、可解析的判据，尤其在不依赖无序的情况下如何稳定长寿命准周期响应仍需阐明。

Method: 构建中心自旋与N个卫星自旋以Ising相互作用耦合的中心-卫星模型，施加Fibonacci（准周期）横向驱动序列；通过解析推导考察相互作用强度λ与横向驱动场θ的精细调控点（λ=2mπ、θ=π）下中心-卫星纠缠的生成条件，分析卫星自旋磁化的准周期振荡与周期-3复现；进而数值考察偏离精细点时的鲁棒性，并在特殊Clifford点分析宇称依赖的Fourier响应及轨迹的纠缠结构。

Result: (1) λ=2mπ时，任意横向驱动强度与驱动序列下，频闪时刻中心-卫星间无纠缠产生，卫星自旋磁化呈无限长寿命准周期振荡；(2) 对具有确定浴磁化的初态，θ=π时对任意λ均无纠缠产生，卫星磁化在Fibonacci时刻呈现周期-3复现；(3) 偏离精细极限时存在一段参数区，准周期响应在所探测时间尺度内不衰减，形成长寿命的π-脉冲邻近TQC；(4) 在特殊Clifford点，卫星自旋磁化呈完美准周期振荡且Fourier响应依赖宇称，该响应在偶数卫星自旋下即使偏离Clifford点仍可持续，形成Clifford邻近TQC；(5) Clifford点处系统沿纠缠轨迹演化，动力学生成Bell-cat与自旋猫态，其集体自旋磁化涨落随卫星自旋数平方标度，支持Heisenberg极限度量。

Conclusion: 该工作表明准周期Fibonacci驱动可在无无序条件下，通过Ising耦合中心的纠缠抑制机制，实现完美复现、周期-3复现及稳健的长寿命时间准晶，并揭示Clifford点兼具完美准周期响应与猫态资源生成，为超越Floquet范式的非平衡物相与量子度量学提供新途径。

Abstract: Motivated by the search for non-equilibrium phases of matter beyond the Floquet paradigm, we propose a route to realize perfect revivals and time quasicrystals (TQCs) in a Fibonacci-driven central-spin model with Ising interactions between the central and satellite spins in the absence of disorder. We analytically demonstrate that when the interaction strength, $λ$ is tuned to $2 m π$, no entanglement is generated between the central and satellite spins at stroboscopic times, independent of the strength of the transverse driving field and the drive sequence. This leads to indefinitely long-lived quasiperiodic oscillations in satellite spin magnetization. Furthermore, for initial states with a definite bath magnetization, no entanglement is generated when the transverse driving field, $θ$ is tuned to $π$ for any value of $ λ$, and the satellite spin magnetization oscillations exhibit period-3 revivals at Fibonacci times. Examining the robustness of these oscillations away from this fine-tuned limit, we identify a parameter regime where the quasiperiodic response does not decay over the timescales probed, giving rise to a long-lived $π$-pulse-proximate TQC regime. We further show that at special Clifford points, the system exhibits perfect quasiperiodic oscillations of the satellite spin magnetization, with a parity-dependent Fourier response. This response persists over the timescales probed, even away from the Clifford points for an even number of satellite spins, leading to the formation of a Clifford-proximate TQC. Finally, we demonstrate that at this Clifford points, the system is steered along an entangled trajectory, leading to the dynamical generation of Bell-cat and spin-cat states. These states exhibit magnetization of collective spin fluctuates quadratically with the number of satellite spins, thereby enabling Heisenberg-limited metrology.

</details>


### [341] [Equilibrium propagation in non-equilibrium quantum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35585)
*Aditya Gandotra,David D. Noachtar,Aashish A. Clerk*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作将平衡态中的平衡传播算法推广到驱动-耗散多体量子系统，利用开放系统的量子细致平衡对称性与新推导的非平衡静态磁化率关系，在无需自由能的情形下实现高效训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平衡传播（EP）依赖可调参数的能量函数，适合自学习物理系统，但其核心瓶颈在于每次参数更新后都需反复弛豫到系统基态，代价高昂。作者希望在无需基态弛豫的前提下保留EP的高效性。

Method: 将EP扩展到多体驱动-耗散量子系统，使动力学自然弛豫到依赖参数的非平衡稳态；针对一类开放系统，证明量子细致平衡对称性的存在仍可支撑高效EP策略，并系统推导一类新的非平衡静态磁化率关系作为算法基础；在驱动-耗散横场Ising模型与参量驱动玻色子晶格两个实验相关模型上进行验证分析。

Result: 尽管非平衡稳态缺乏自由能，对于所研究的一类开放系统，量子细致平衡对称性仍足以构造高效的EP训练方案；作者推导出的非平衡静态磁化率关系是该方案成立的关键，并在两个代表性模型上给出训练范式的具体分析。

Conclusion: 通过把EP移植到驱动-耗散量子体系并借助量子细致平衡与新型磁化率关系，可绕过平衡态EP中反复基态弛豫的难题，为自学习量子物理系统提供了一种非平衡训练范式，并具备在实验相关模型上落地的潜力。

Abstract: Equilibrium propagation (EP) is a powerful training algorithm for self-learning physical systems that is widely used for a variety of machine learning inspired tasks. It relies on the existence of an energy function that depends on tunable training parameters, and can be extended to apply to many-body quantum systems in equilibrium. A key challenge however is the need to repeatedly relax to the system ground state after each parameter update. We show here a route for circumventing this problem by extending EP to many-body driven-dissipative quantum systems, where the dynamics naturally relaxes to a parameter-dependent non-equilibrium steady state. Despite the lack of a free energy, we find that for a class of open systems, a quantum detailed balance symmetry still allows for an efficient EP strategy. This relies crucially on a new class of non-equilibrium static susceptibility relations that we derive in detail. We analyze examples of our new non-equilibrium training paradigm for two experimentally-relevant models: a driven-dissipative transverse field Ising model, and a parametrically-driven bosonic lattice.

</details>


### [342] [Did the Gran Sasso Experiment Rule Out the Diósi-Penrose Theory? Musing on the 2021 Result and the Present Experimental Status](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35608)
*Catalina Curceanu,Nicola Bortolotti,Lajos Diósi,Simone Manti,Kristian Piscicchia*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文澄清Gran Sasso 2021年低本底实验对Diósi-Penrose (DP) 模型的排除逻辑，指出XENONnT等后续实验（R0 > 4.9×10⁻¹⁰ m @90% CL）仅参数化约束了马尔可夫白噪声DP动力学，并未排除Penrose寿命判据本身或所有色噪声、耗散型等修正的引力相关自发坍缩理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2021年Gran Sasso实验排除了马尔可夫DP动力学与Penrose质量密度分辨率结合的特定组合，但后续文献（Majorana Demonstrator、XENONnT）不断收紧R0边界，易被误读为对Penrose引力坍缩思想的整体否证。作者旨在明确界定该实验结论的逻辑范围，纠正常见的概念混淆。

Method: 以概念分析与文献重构为主：梳理Gran Sasso实验所检验的具体模型假设（马尔可夫、有效白噪声的DP动力学 + 以核空间量子弥散标定R0的无参数处方），对比Diósi随机动力学与Penrose仅作为寿命判据的角色，并将结论置于Majorana Demonstrator与XENONnT最新限制的实验语境中。

Result: Gran Sasso 2021给出R0 > 5.4×10⁻¹¹ m (95%)，超过锗中R0 ≈ 5×10⁻¹² m的无参数预言，故该特定组合被排除；XENONnT进一步提升至R0 > 4.9×10⁻¹⁰ m (90% CL) 与 R0 > 4.5×10⁻¹⁰ m (95% CL)，为当前世界领先限制。但这些结果仅参数化约束马尔可夫白噪声DP动力学。

Conclusion: 实验排除了"D iósi随机动力学 + Penrose质量密度分辨率"这一特定组合，而非Penrose（及Diósi）独立于动力学的寿命判据，也不涉及色噪声、耗散或其他修正的引力相关自发坍缩理论；后续更强边界只是强化而非扩大这一排除的逻辑范围。

Abstract: In 2021, a low-background experiment at the Gran Sasso National Laboratory searched for spontaneous radiation predicted by the standard Markovian Diósi-Penrose (DP) dynamics and obtained the new bound $R_0 > 5.4 \times 10^{-11}\,\mathrm{m}$ at $95\%$ probability for the short-length cutoff parameter. For germanium at the experimental temperature, the parameter-free prescription identifying $R_0$ with the spatial quantum spread of a nucleus gives $R_0 \simeq 5 \times 10^{-12}\,\mathrm{m}$. That particular combination, Diósi's stochastic dynamics and the Penrose-motivated mass-density resolution, was therefore excluded. Subsequent searches strengthened the cutoff bound. The Majorana Demonstrator improved it in 2022, and XENONnT has now obtained the world-leading limits $R_0 > 4.9 \times 10^{-10}\,\mathrm{m}$ at $90\%$ confidence level and $R_0 > 4.5 \times 10^{-10}\,\mathrm{m}$ at $95\%$ confidence level. These advances reinforce the 2021 exclusion but do not alter its logical scope: they \emph{parametrically constrain} the Markovian, effectively white-noise DP dynamics, not Penrose's (and Diósi's) lifetime criterion independently of a dynamics, and not all colored-noise, dissipative, or otherwise modified gravity-related spontaneous collapse theories. We reconstruct what Gran Sasso did and did not rule out and place that conclusion in the present experimental context.

</details>


### [343] [Quantum-enhanced metrology with classical shadows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35632)
*Luca Ion,Manuel Gessner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种无需选定特定可观测量的量子计量协议：从固定的随机局域测量集合中估计Pauli矩，并组合成优化的矩估计量，配合Pauli经典阴影的"阴影信息"给出方差与样本复杂度保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计量依赖对参数信息最大化的可观测量的测量，但此类最优观测量往往难以实验实现（需复杂电路或多体测量）。因此需要一种不预先指定可观测量的通用、高效计量方案。

Method: 采用固定的随机局域测量（Pauli）集合估计各Pauli矩，将其线性组合为method-of-moments估计量并做优化；针对局域Pauli经典阴影推导"阴影信息"（shadow information）以刻画估计量方差与样本复杂度；并分析可分离态的灵敏度极限作为基准。

Result: 给出基于阴影信息的方差与样本复杂度保证；确定可分离态灵敏度极限，并证明纠缠可带来常数因子的灵敏度提升，以one-axis-twisted（单轴扭转）探针为例进行说明。

Conclusion: 该协议实现了基于电路的、无需人为选择观测量的高效量子计量；在给定经典阴影框架下，纠缠的增益表现为常数因子提升，而非Heisenberg型标度改进。

Abstract: Quantum metrology relies on observables that maximize information about the parameter of interest, but are often hard to access. We develop a protocol that combines Pauli moments estimated from a fixed ensemble of randomized local measurements into an optimized method-of-moments estimator. For local Pauli classical shadows, we derive the shadow information which determines estimator variance and sample-complexity guarantees. Identifying the separable-state sensitivity limit, we show that entanglement can lead to a constant-factor sensitivity enhancement, as illustrated with one-axis-twisted probes. This protocol allows for efficient circuit-based metrology without choosing an observable.

</details>


### [344] [Quantum-Inspired and Optimization-Based CT Reconstruction Through Spin-Error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35657)
*Hyunju Lee,Jeonghwa Lee,Yushuang Sam Yang,Clement Chu,Anthony B. Murphy,Kyungtaek Jun*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在稀疏视图与含噪正弦图条件下，量子优化的CT重建精度已超越经典方法，但QUBO规模随图像增大而爆炸。本文不改动CT-QUBO目标函数，采用模拟退火（SA）加自旋误差缓解（SEMO）作为迈向更大规模量子CT的初步步骤，在54视图ROI与9视图探测器行损坏实例上取得接近或优于未缓解混合求解器的重建精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子优化CT重建在精度上展现优势，但更大图像需要更多的二值变量与相互作用：即使采用迭代ROI细化，60×60的ROI在三种X射线质量衰减系数编码下仍需10,800个二值变量，规模扩展成为主要瓶颈。

Method: 保持CT-QUBO目标函数不变，先运行模拟退火（SA），再施加面向优化的自旋误差缓解（SEMO）。评估对象为三个54视图ROI重建实例及一个含20%探测器行损坏的9视图重建，并与选定的混合量子-经典求解器结果对比，以错误像素数与QUBO能量作为指标。

Result: ROI实例中，SEMO将低预算SA输出的错误像素数由13降至1、38降至0、54降至15；20%探测器行损坏下四对SA/SEMO中有三对实现精确重建；对一个混合求解器输出应用SEMO后，ROI错误由78像素降至1像素。改进具实例依赖性：某含噪正弦图案例中QUBO能量下降但图像域误差略增；某ROI案例中细化图像即使QUBO能量低于真值仍保留15个错误像素。

Conclusion: 在所评估的问题规模上，SA与SEMO的重建精度与选定未缓解混合求解器相当或更优，为未来更大规模、并行化的量子CT图像重建提供了初步的解质量基准。

Abstract: Quantum-optimization-based computed tomography (CT) reconstruction has demonstrated quantum supremacy in reconstruction accuracy over conventional classical methods under sparse-view and corrupted-sinogram conditions. However, scaling this approach remains challenging because larger images require increasingly many binary variables and interactions in the underlying quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) model. Even with iterative region-of-interest (ROI) refinement, a 60 by 60 ROI with three X-ray mass attenuation coefficients requires 10,800 binary variables under our encoding. As an initial step toward larger quantum CT images, we investigate simulated annealing (SA) followed by spin-error mitigation for optimisation (SEMO), without altering the CT-QUBO objective. We evaluate three 54-view ROI reconstruction instances and nine-view reconstruction with detector-row corruption, comparing SA and SEMO with selected hybrid quantum-classical solver results. For the ROI cases, SEMO reduced the numbers of incorrectly reconstructed pixels in low-budget SA outputs from 13 to 1, 38 to 0, and 54 to 15. With 20 percent detector-row corruption, three of four selected SA and SEMO pairs achieved exact reconstruction. Applying SEMO to one hybrid-solver output reduced its ROI error from 78 to 1 pixel. Improvements were instance-dependent: in one corrupted-sinogram case, QUBO energy decreased while image-domain error increased slightly; in one ROI case, the refined image retained 15 incorrect pixels despite having lower QUBO energy than ground truth. At the evaluated problem sizes, SA and SEMO attained reconstruction accuracy comparable to or better than selected unmitigated hybrid-solver results, providing an initial solution-quality benchmark for future larger-scale, parallelized quantum CT image reconstruction.

</details>


### [345] [Quantum computation of energy level degeneracy and thermodynamical properties of spin systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35660)
*V. M. Tkachuk,M. I. Samar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种混合量子-经典方法，利用单个辅助量子比特在虚逆温下测量配分函数，再通过逆离散傅里叶变换精确重建已知等间距能谱的自旋体系能级简并度与热力学性质；在36自旋Kagome阻挫Ising反铁磁体上于ibm_kingston处理器验证，噪声下仍保留主要谱特征并再现热容的主要温度依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接测量自旋体系能级简并度与热力学量在经典上随体系规模迅速变得困难；量子硬件上的谱重构又受电路深度、噪声与谱泄漏影响。作者希望以尽量少的量子资源（单个辅助比特）获得对能谱简并度的稳健重构，并在真实超导处理器上检验其可行性。

Method: 1）引入单个辅助量子比特，在虚逆温度β下通过辅助比特的测量估计配分函数Z(β)；2）对位于已知等间距能量网格上的谱，采用逆离散傅里叶变换由Z(β)样本精确反演简并度，无噪声时无谱泄漏；3）量子线路通过可对易相互作用与欧拉序（Eulerian ordering）进行优化以压缩深度；4）在周期Kagome图上构造最大36自旋的阻挫Ising反铁磁体，并在ibm_kingston量子处理器上执行。

Result: 尽管电路深度较大且存在硬件噪声，重构得到的谱仍保留了主要（主导）特征；由重构谱计算的热容能够复现精确结果的主要温度依赖趋势。

Conclusion: 单辅助比特+逆离散傅里叶变换的混合量子-经典框架可在等间距能谱假设下实现无谱泄漏的简并度重构，并在当前含噪超导硬件上对中等规模阻挫自旋体系给出定性可靠的谱与热力学结果，展示了近期量子设备用于统计物理量计算的可行性。

Abstract: We propose a hybrid quantum-classical method for reconstructing energy-level degeneracies and thermodynamic properties of spin systems. A single ancilla qubit is used to measure the partition function at imaginary inverse temperatures. For spectra lying on a known equally spaced energy grid, the inverse discrete Fourier transform allows exact reconstruction of degeneracies in the absence of noise, without spectral leakage. The quantum circuits are optimized using commuting interactions and Eulerian ordering. We demonstrate the method for frustrated Ising antiferromagnets on periodic Kagome graphs with up to 36 spins, using the ibm_kingston quantum processor. Despite substantial circuit depth and hardware noise, the reconstructed spectra retain their dominant features, and the calculated heat capacities reproduce the main temperature dependence of the exact results.
  Keywords: partition function, imaginary temperature, Ising model, heat capacity, ancilla qubit, quantum computing.

</details>


### [346] [$κ$-Parametrized Boltzmann Distribution Sampling on Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35665)
*João Marcos Cavalcanti de Albuquerque Neto,Gustavo Castro do Amaral,Guilherme Penello Temporão*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文在Qiskit Aer模拟器上评估参数化退火调度QAOA（PAQ-QAOA）辅助受限玻尔兹曼机（RBM）的玻尔兹曼分布采样，通过分析可解规模下的逆有效温度κ，给出PAQ-QAOA可近似RBM采样分布的数值证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RBM是生成模型的核心组件，但其训练依赖对未知玻尔兹曼分布的采样，存在计算不可解性；QAOA有望辅助RBM训练并优于纯经典策略，但关键难题是如何针对任意问题规模确定合适的量子线路深度以获得期望的采样结果。

Method: 在Qiskit Aer模拟器上，针对可解问题规模，评估QAOA辅助的玻尔兹曼分布采样；以分布的逆有效温度κ为分析指标，考察参数化退火调度QAOA（PAQ-QAOA）对RBM玻尔兹曼采样的逼近能力。

Result: 基于Qiskit Aer的数值实验表明，在所考虑的可解实例上，PAQ-QAOA能够近似RBM的玻尔兹曼采样；同时指出真实硬件性能与规模扩展性仍是未决问题。

Conclusion: PAQ-QAOA为RBM玻尔兹曼采样提供了一种有前景的量子辅助途径，但当前结论仅限于模拟器与可解规模，尚需在硬件实现与扩展性方面进一步验证。

Abstract: Generative models use training data to produce new samples that resemble the original, with applications in classification and recommendation systems. Restricted Boltzmann Machines (RBMs) are at the core of generative models but suffer from the computational intractability of training, which relies on sampling an unknown Boltzmann Distribution (BD). The Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA) can assist RBM training, potentially providing benefits over strictly classical strategies. One particular problem is determining the quantum circuit depth for an arbitrary problem size that yields the expected sampling results. In this study, we evaluate QAOA-assisted sampling of BDs using Qiskit's Aer Simulator by analysing the inverse effective temperature of the distribution, $κ$, for tractable problem sizes. The results provide numerical evidence, based on Qiskit Aer simulations, that parameterized annealing-scheduled QAOA (PAQ-QAOA) can approximate RBM Boltzmann sampling for the tractable instances considered, while hardware performance and scaling remain open questions.

</details>


### [347] [Bell-pair distillation under imperfect operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35699)
*András Kinyó,Tamás Kiss,Orsolya Kálmán*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了此前提出的"基于局域操作与经典通信(LOCC)、可概率性且普适地从未知两量子比特态的多份拷贝中蒸馏出最大纠缠Bell对"的方案在实际实现中的鲁棒性：考察多种非相干/相干门误差模型及测量读出误差，确定蒸馏能提升目标Bell态保真度的参数区间，并分析双方不对称误差及互补后选择结果产生相同蒸馏动力学的条件以提升成功概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作[PRL 135, 260202 (2025)]提出的普适纠缠蒸馏协议在理想条件下成立，但任何实际量子实现都不可避免地存在门操作误差与测量读出误差。因此需要评估该协议在非理想实现下的鲁棒性，判断其是否以及在何种误差范围内仍具有实用价值。

Method: 构建若干非相干与相干门误差模型以及测量读出误差模型，并引入双方（两参与方）之间的不对称误差；在这些误差模型下量化蒸馏后态相对目标Bell态的保真度，与未蒸馏情形对比，以刻画蒸馏带来保真度增益的误差区间；同时分析互补后选择(postselection)结果对应的蒸馏动力学，找出两者等价的条件。

Result: 该协议在相当宽的误差范围内仍能有效工作，但鲁棒性强依赖于误差类型（相干与非相干、门误差与读出误差之间差异明显）。此外，找到了使互补后选择结果产生完全相同的蒸馏动力学的条件，在此条件下可合并两种结果以提高成功概率。

Conclusion: 该普适纠缠蒸馏协议对实际实现中的不完美具有一定的实用鲁棒性，但其有效性随误差类型显著变化；通过对不对称误差与后选择结果的分析，可在特定条件下通过利用互补结果提升成功概率，为实验实现提供了指导。

Abstract: We recently proposed an entanglement distillation protocol based on local operations and classical communication that probabilistically and universally distills a maximally entangled Bell pair from multiple copies of an unknown two-qubit state [Phys. Rev. Lett. 135, 260202 (2025)]. Here, we investigate the robustness of this protocol against imperfections in its implementation. We consider several incoherent and coherent gate-error models, as well as measurement readout errors, and determine the regimes in which distillation enhances the fidelity with the target Bell state. We find that the protocol remains effective over substantial error ranges, although its robustness depends strongly on the type of error. We further analyze asymmetric errors between the two parties and identify conditions under which complementary postselection outcomes yield identical distillation dynamics, allowing the success probability to be enhanced.

</details>


### [348] [Distillable entanglement is continuous](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35722)
*Lukas Schmitt,David Sutter*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明了二分态可蒸馏纠缠（distillable entanglement）的连续性，给出了对任意二分态成立、依赖系统维度对数尺度的显式渐近连续性界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可蒸馏纠缠是LOCC下渐近最优的纠缠转化速率，但其连续性此前未被确立；而已知条件熵、纠缠成本、相对熵纠缠等量均已具备结构类似的连续性界，因此需要补齐这一缺口。

Method: 构造显式的LOCC协议，使两个任意二分态可相互转化，其ebit代价随两态迹距离趋于零而消失，由此导出连续性界。

Result: 得到对任意二分态成立的显式渐近连续性界，对系统维度呈对数依赖，且结构与条件熵、纠缠成本、相对熵纠缠的已知连续性界相同。

Conclusion: 可蒸馏纠缠是连续的，其连续性界与相关纠缠度量的连续性界在结构上一致，为纠缠资源的渐近理论提供了定量稳定性保证。

Abstract: We prove continuity of distillable entanglement, the optimal asymptotic rate at which copies of a bipartite state can be converted into maximally entangled pairs by local operations and classical communication (LOCC) with vanishing error. We establish an explicit asymptotic continuity bound that holds for arbitrary bipartite states and depends logarithmically on the system dimension. This bound has the same structure as known continuity bounds for conditional entropy, entanglement cost, and relative entropy of entanglement. Our proof constructs an explicit LOCC protocol that converts either of two arbitrary bipartite states into the other at an ebit cost that vanishes as their trace distance tends to zero.

</details>


### [349] [Parametrically Enhanced Ergotropy and Charging Power in a Three-Mode Bosonic Quantum Battery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35734)
*Mustapha Tarboun,Mohamed Amazioug*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出在含相干驱动充电腔、强耗散辅助腔与储能电池腔的开放量子系统中，借助压缩光（腔内简并光学参量放大器）实现量子非互易与定向能量流，从而大幅提升量子电池的储能、充电功率与可提取功（ergotropy）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电池需同时提升储能、充电功率与可提取功；现有方案多依赖被动驱动，能量回漏与光子数涨落限制了性能。作者希望利用压缩光作为相位敏感的主动能量源，并结合损耗工程实现非互易能量俘获。

Method: 系统由相干驱动的充电腔、强耗散辅助腔（介体）与储能电池腔构成；经直接耦合路径与损耗工程间接路径的破坏性量子干涉产生非互易性与定向能量流。充电腔内集成简并光学参量放大器，注入双光子激发与反常关联。基于Lindblad主方程建立严格开放量子系统框架，求解全部期望值动力学与稳态。

Result: 主动参量驱动显著提高储能与峰值充电功率，同时维持电池中的非互易能量俘获；储能、充电功率与ergotropy获得数量级提升。两光子泵浦引入的光子数涨落使分数ergotropy比 W_B/E_B 略有下降，但绝对可提取功仍远高于无助配置。另分析了纯退相位对充电功率、ergotropy及可提取功品质的影响。

Conclusion: 压缩光驱动的主动方案可在保持非互易能量俘获的前提下实现量子电池性能的数量级增强；分数ergotropy的小幅下降是绝对可提取功提升的可接受代价，纯退相位是需关注的主要退相干因素。

Abstract: Quantum batteries are quantum systems that potentially serve as fuel for other quantum devices by receiving and supplying energy. In this work, we propose a theoretical scheme to enhance ergotropy and charging power of quantum Battery in the system under consideration through squeezed light. Moreover, The system comprises a coherently driven charger cavity, a strongly dissipative auxiliary cavity acting as a mediator, and an energy--storage battery cavity. Quantum nonreciprocity and directional energy flow are established via destructive quantum interference between the direct coupling path and the loss--engineered indirect path. A degenerate optical parametric amplifier integrated into the charger cavity acts as an active, phase--sensitive energy source that injects two--photon quantum excitations and anomalous correlations. Using a strictly open--quantum--system framework derived directly from the Lindblad master equation, we obtain the full expectation--value dynamics and stationary states. We show that active parametric driving drastically increases the stored energy and peak charging power while maintaining nonreciprocal energy trapping in the battery. We achieve a multi-order-of-magnitude boost in the battery's stored energy, charging power, and ergotropy. We also analyze the effects of pure dephasing on the charging power, ergotropy, and extractable work quality.. Although two--photon pumping introduces photon--number fluctuations that slightly lower the fractional ergotropy ratio ${\cal W}_{\rm B}/{\cal E}_{\rm B}$, the absolute extractable work remains substantially elevated compared to unassisted configurations.

</details>


### [350] [Decoding with alternating weighted graph sparsification for quantum low-density parity-check codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35735)
*Josu Etxezarreta Martinez,Francisco Garcia-Herrero,Ruben M. Otxoa,Reza Dastbasteh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出 DAWG 解码器：先用 BP 在完整 Tanner 图上迭代若干次，再把后验转移到稀疏化图上继续 BP；集成多个不同转移矩阵与随机化调度的 min-sum BP。与 Relay-BP/Vibe 性能相近，且 LSD-0 后处理调用次数少一个数量级，满足实时解码需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算需要实时解码；完整探测器错误模型 Tanner 图上的 BP 解码器计算量大、延迟高，而 LSD 类后处理器调用频繁会造成长延迟尾部与高昂硬件资源开销，因此需要低成本的 BP 解码方案。

Method: 1) 集成式解码框架：多个 BP 解码器先在完整 detector error model Tanner 图上运行固定轮数；2) 将后验信息通过不同转移矩阵映射到稀疏化图，在稀疏图上追加若干轮 BP；3) 采用串行调度的 min-sum BP，并借鉴 Vibe 思想对每个解码器的调度进行随机化；4) 稀疏化后图规模远小于完整图，用于 LSD-0 后处理；5) 在 bivariate bicycle 码与超密色码电路上进行数值评测。

Result: 与 Relay-BP、Vibe 在搭配 order-0 LSD 后处理时性能相近；相同集成规模下，后处理器调用次数比 Vibe 少一个数量级，延迟尾部极小；LSD-0 仅作用于规模远小于完整模型的稀疏解码图，降低专用硬件资源需求。
