Conclusion: DAWG+LSD-0 是一种可行、低成本的基于 BP 的量子纠错解码算法，兼顾实时性与硬件可实现性。

Abstract: Real-time decoding is a fundamental necessity for building fault-tolerant quantum computers. In this work, we introduce decoding with alternating weighted graph sparsification (DAWG), an ensemble based approach that combines Tanner graph sparsification with ensembling belief-propagation (BP) decoders. The DAWG decoder runs BP decoders over the full detector error model Tanner graph for certain iterations to then transfer the posteriors to a sparsified graph in which additional rounds of BP are run. Each of the decoders in the ensemble uses a different transfer matrix to map the posterior information to the sparsified graph. We use the serial schedule for the minimum-sum BP decoders and, thus, employ Vibe decoding ideas by randomizing the schedules of each of them. The performance of the DAWG decoder is then numerically studied for bivariate bicycle codes and superdense color code circuits to be compared with other state-of-the-art decoding approaches. Importantly, the DAWG decoder shows a similar performance to Relay belief-propagation and Vibe decoding when combined with an order-$0$ Localized Statistics Decoding (LSD-$0$) post-processor. Crucially, the post-processor is called an order of magnitude less times than for Vibe with the same ensemble size, indicating that the introduced latency tail will be minimal. Furthermore, LSD-$0$ operates on a sparsified decoding graph of a fraction of the size of the full detector error model, which simplifies the resources required by specialized hardware to support it. Overall, the DAWG+LSD-$0$ decoder represents a viable low-cost BP-based decoding algorithm for quantum error correction codes.

</details>


### [351] [Competing coherence geometries in positive maps: positivity hierarchies, atomicity, and optimal entanglement witnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35758)
*Massimiliano F. Sacchi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入一类定义在qudit上的正映射族，其Choi算子由两种相互竞争的相干模式构成：独立的交换耦合与一组集体对角耦合。作者精确给出该族每个混合成员的 positivity、完全正性及所有中间 k-正性条件，并完整刻画可分解性与原子性的相结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要一个可精确求解的框架，在任意局域维度下统一比较 k-正性、可分解性、原子性、PPT 边态几何与纠缠见证最优性这几类概念，而现有情形往往只能分别讨论。

Method: 构造由"独立交换耦合"与"集体对角耦合"两类相干模式线性混合的正映射族；对Choi算子逐项推导正性、完全正性及 k-正性阈值，利用"正+余正"显式分解结合对应的PPT态族确定可分解边界，并以嵌入qutrit证书判定原子性；在最大相干端点显式构造正减法得到原子最优纠缠见证。

Result: 高正性阈值依赖于信道重数，而精确可分解边界在无标度坐标下是普适的；对应PPT态构成边态，其探测区域为椭圆（集体重标度后为圆盘）。嵌入qutrit证书表明在整个混合族中不可分解性与原子性重合；纯集体端点在其正区间上全程可分解；最大相干处存在具张成性质的原子最优见证，其PPT探测集严格更大。

Conclusion: 该族构成一个在任意局域维度下同时精确刻画 k-正性、可分解性、原子性、PPT边态几何与见证最优性的可解模型，揭示了阈值普适性、不可分解性与原子性的等价性以及端点行为的根本差异。

Abstract: We introduce a family of positive maps on qudits whose Choi operators combine two competing coherence patterns: independent exchange couplings and a collective set of diagonal couplings. For every mixed member of the family, we derive exact conditions for positivity, complete positivity, and all intermediate levels of k-positivity, and we determine the complete phase structure of decomposability and atomicity. The higher-positivity thresholds depend on the channel multiplicities, whereas the exact decomposability boundary is universal in scale-free coordinates. Its proof combines an explicit positive-plus-copositive decomposition with a matching family of positive-partial-transpose (PPT) states. The latter form PPT edge states whose detection region is an ellipse, or a disk after collective rescaling. An embedded-qutrit certificate shows that indecomposability and atomicity coincide throughout the mixed family. In contrast, the purely collective endpoint is decomposable over its entire positive interval. Finally, at maximal coherence, an explicit positive subtraction produces an atomic optimal entanglement witness with the spanning property and a strictly larger PPT- detection set. The study thus provides an exactly solvable setting in which k-positivity, decomposability, atomicity, PPT-edge-state geometry, and witness optimality can be compared in arbitrary local dimension.

</details>


### [352] [Twist-controlled imaginary-time Mpemba effect in quantum spin systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35784)
*Zhi-Yao Ning,Zi-Xiang Li,Naoki Kawashima*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文在量子自旋系统中通过空间调制局域旋转构造了可控的虚时间Mpemba效应（ITME），在不改变初始双分纠缠谱与关联幅度的前提下，将谱权重再分布隔离为反常弛豫的唯一驱动因素，并给出精确解析交叉时间，在线性XXZ链/梯子及12比特变分量子虚时间演化中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚时间演化是基态制备的核心工具，而Mpemba效应（初始态"越远反而收敛越快"）为加速弛豫提供了可能，但既有研究难以区分弛豫加速究竟源于关联/纠缠结构的改变还是谱权重的再分布，缺乏可精确控制、机制可证的实验/理论平台。

Method: 采用不动点（compact-boson）描述，在量子自旋系统上施加空间调制的局域旋转，同时保持初始双分纠缠谱与关联幅度不变；分析扭转对模式的选择性布居，推导精确解析交叉时间；通过对相互作用的XXZ链与梯子数值计算检验超出不动点理论的有效性；并用12比特变分量子虚时间演化（VQITE）模拟复现反转与受控延迟。

Result: 高阶谐波扭转仅布居高能驻波，结构性绕过慢衰减红外模式从而加速收敛；人为重新注入慢模式布居可系统性地延迟ITME；解析交叉时间与数值（XXZ链、梯子）一致，12比特VQITE模拟成功重现Mpemba反转及其可控延迟。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了严格可控且精确的ITME起点，把谱权重再分布确立为反常弛豫的唯一机制，为在量子基态制备中利用非平衡反常弛豫建立了范式，并给出在现实量子线路中实现的可行路径。

Abstract: We construct a controllable imaginary-time Mpemba effect in quantum spin systems using spatially modulated local rotations without changing initial bipartite entanglement spectra and correlation magnitudes. This feature unambiguously isolates the redistribution of spectral weights as the sole driver of the anomalous relaxation. Using the compact-boson description, we reveal that these twists act as precise mode selectors: higher-harmonic twists exclusively populate high-energy standing waves, structurally bypassing slow-decaying infrared modes to accelerate convergence. We derive exact analytical crossing times for these reversals and demonstrate that intentionally reintroducing the slow-mode population systematically delays the ITME. This exact mechanism is validated beyond the fixed-point theory via calculations on interacting XXZ chains and ladders. Furthermore, a twelve-qubit simulation of variational quantum imaginary-time evolution reproduces the reversals and their controlled delay, providing a concrete route to realize this protocol in realistic quantum circuits. Ultimately, these results provide a strictly controllable and exact starting point for realizing and investigating the imaginary-time Mpemba effect, establishing a foundational paradigm to harness anomalous non-equilibrium relaxation in quantum ground-state preparations.

</details>


### [353] [The $\varepsilon$-smoothed Entanglement of Formation and its relation to the Moreau-Yosida approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35797)
*M. E. Shirokov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文通过在迹范数距离下对形成纠缠（EoF）应用 Mora-Piani-Briegel 的 ε-测度构造，定义了一族凸连续函数 E_{F,ε}，证明其随 ε↓0 点态收敛于 EoF，并系统研究其变分表示、弱次可加性、连续性及一致收敛速率上界，同时讨论向相对熵纠缠等度量的推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EoF 在一般（尤其无限维）双分体量子系统上缺乏凸性、连续性与可计算性等良好性质，直接优化困难；因此需要构造一族正则化（近似）泛函，在保持凸性与连续性的同时逼近 EoF，以便获得可计算的界与收敛速率。

Method: 以迹范数距离为基本度量，将 Mora-Piani-Briegel 的 ε-测度构造应用于 EoF，得到族 {E_{F,ε}}_{ε∈(0,1]}；借助与 EoF 的 Moreau-Yosida 近似族 {E^λ_F}_{λ>0} 的关系进行性质分析；利用已有 EoF 半连续性界导出在单侧边际态秩/能量有界集合上的一致收敛速率上界；并证明无限维系统中最近可分态的存在性（并给出非唯一性）。

Result: E_{F,ε} 为凸连续函数，关于 ε 非增且当 ε→0⁺ 时点态收敛于 EoF；给出其变分表示（ε=0 时退化为 EoF 的标准变分式）；刻划了弱次可加性并分析了连续性；得到在秩/能量有界态集上一致收敛速率的具体可计算上界；并证明无限维双分体系统中对任意态均存在迹范数意义下的最近可分态，且该最近态可不唯一。

Conclusion: ε-测度正则化给出了 EoF 的一族性质良好的凸近似，兼具变分刻画、弱次可加性与可控收敛速率，并揭示了最近可分态的存在性与非唯一性；相应方法可推广至相对熵纠缠等其他纠缠度量。

Abstract: We describe a family of convex continuous functions $E_{F,\varepsilon}$, $\varepsilon\in(0,1]$, on the set of states of a bipartite quantum system (consisting of finite-dimensional or infinite-dimensional subsystems) obtained by applying the Mora-Piani-Briegel construction of $\varepsilon$-measure (proposed in [Phys. Rev. A 109, 052413]) to the Entanglement of Formation (EoF) with the use of the trace norm distance in the role of basic metric. These functions are non-increasing in $\varepsilon$ and converge pointwise to the EoF as $\varepsilon\to0^+$. We conducted a detailed analysis of properties of the functions $E_{F,\varepsilon}$, using (among others) the relationship between the family $\{E_{F,\varepsilon}\}_{\varepsilon\in[0,1]}$ and the family $\{E^λ_F(ρ)\}_{λ>0}$ of Moreau-Yosida approximations of the EoF explored in [arXiv:2609.30246].
  We obtain a variational expression for the functions $E_{F,\varepsilon}$ (which for $\varepsilon=0$ turns into the standard variational expression for the EoF). We also describe the weak subadditivity property of the functions $E_{F,\varepsilon}$ and analyse their continuity properties.
  The semicontinuity bounds for the EoF (presented in [Lob.J.Math., 46, 2632-2658],[arXiv:2404.07978]) allow us to obtain easily computable upper bounds on the rate of uniform convergence of the function $E_{F,\varepsilon}$ to the EoF as $\varepsilon\to0^+$ on the sets of states with bounded rank/energy of one of the marginal states.
  Applicability of the obtained results to other entanglement measures, in particular, to the relative entropy of entanglement, is discussed.
  As an auxiliary result, it is shown that for any state of an infinite-dimensional bipartite quantum system, there exists a nearest separable state w.r.t. the trace norm distance. The non-uniqueness of such a state is also shown.

</details>


### [354] [Sparse Carleman Embeddings for Nonlinear Discrete Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35824)
*Hsuan-Cheng Wu,Xiantao Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究强制二次离散系统的Carleman嵌入，证明截断误差随嵌入阶数几何收敛，并构造保持稀疏性的辅助线性系统表示，进而给出线性于迭代次数、多对数于精度的量子态制备算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散非线性动力系统广泛出现在种群模型、迭代优化与数值模拟中，高维情形下需大量迭代、计算代价高；作者试图借助Carleman嵌入把非线性迭代线性化，并利用量子算法加速归一化n步迭代态的制备。离散时间的核心障碍是：即使原始系数映射稀疏，提升后的更新也可能变稠密，导致高阶嵌入难以高效实现。

Method: 针对强制二次离散系统做Carleman嵌入：在显式稳定性与初值假设下分析截断误差；为克服提升更新稠密化问题，构造精确的辅助线性系统表示，逐个张量因子地施加系数映射，使行、列稀疏度与截断阶数无关；再经重标定后分析历史系统的条件数，并将整体流程转化为量子算法以制备归一化第n次迭代的近似态。

Result: 理论上证明截断误差随嵌入阶数几何下降且对所有迭代一致成立；辅助表示的稀疏度不随截断阶数增长；重标定后的历史系统条件数随迭代次数线性增长、且关于截断阶数一致。据此得到常数成功概率制备归一化n步迭代近似态的量子算法：在相干输入访问假设下，固定稀疏度、稳定性参数及终态范数已知正下界时，查询复杂度关于n线性、关于逆精度多对数；一维与三维数值实验验证了截断嵌入的收敛性。

Conclusion: Carleman嵌入可在保持稀疏可实现性的前提下几何地控制离散二次系统的截断误差，并支撑高效的量子态制备；但其复杂度结论依赖相干输入访问、固定稀疏度与稳定性参数、终态范数正下界等较强假设，实践中这些条件是否满足、以及条件数随n线性增长的影响仍需进一步考察。

Abstract: Discrete nonlinear dynamical systems arise in population models, iterative optimization, and numerical simulations, where computing many iterations can be costly in high dimensions. We study Carleman embedding for forced quadratic discrete systems and its application to quantum state preparation. Under explicit stability and initial-state assumptions, we prove that the truncation error decreases geometrically with the embedding degree, uniformly over all iterations. A central obstacle in discrete time is that the lifted update can become dense even when the original coefficient maps are sparse. We overcome this obstacle by constructing an exact auxiliary linear-system representation that applies the coefficient maps one tensor factor at a time, with row and column sparsities bounded independently of the truncation degree. After rescaling, the associated history system has a condition number that grows linearly with the iteration count, uniformly in the truncation degree. These results yield a quantum algorithm for preparing an approximation to the normalized $n$th iterate with constant success probability. Under coherent input-access assumptions, and for fixed sparsity, stability parameters, and a known positive lower bound on the final-state norm, the oracle query complexity is linear in $n$ and polylogarithmic in the inverse accuracy. Numerical experiments in one and three dimensions illustrate the convergence of the truncated embedding.

</details>


### [355] [Interference-induced dynamical crossovers in quantum walks on disordered square lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35828)
*Ramnarayan Bera,Arijit Roy,Soumyadip Ghosh,Soumen Roy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究无序方格子上Fourier、Grover与Hadamard三种硬币的离散时间量子行走（并与经典随机行走对比），将"缺失边"推广为以系数f部分透射的边，系统映射了(f,p)平面上的输运相图并识别β=1的边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作多聚焦于整条边被随机移除（键稀释）的格子上量子与经典输运的对比，而"部分透射边"这一更一般化情形下量子行走协议（硬币选择）如何影响输运标度尚不清楚，因此需要系统地刻画部分透射无序对量子扩散行为的影响。

Method: 在无序二维方格子上实现含Fourier、Grover、Hadamard硬币的离散时间量子行走，并以经典随机行走作对照。模型分两步：先令边以概率p完全缺失；再推广为缺失边仍保留但具有透射系数f（部分透射）。通过拟合反常扩散指数β(t)、有效扩散系数、逆参与比（IPR）以及边透射的前两阶矩，在(f,p)平面上绘制输运相图，并仅考察有限尺寸边界反射之前的体输运阶段。

Result: 当边完全缺失时，经典与量子行走表现出几乎相同的输运退化。在部分透射推广下，输运对行走协议与硬币对称性高度敏感：经典行走的反常扩散指数β(t)呈渐近稳定；量子行走在初始瞬态之后β(t)单调衰减，导致二维中量子扩散消失。在(f,p)平面上识别出β=1的等值线，用以区分亚扩散与超扩散区域，并给出了有效扩散系数、IPR及边透射矩的行为。

Conclusion: 量子输运强烈依赖于与硬币相关的干涉效应，无法仅由透射统计量（如f及边透射矩）完全描述；经典与量子行走在部分透射无序下的标度行为存在本质差异。

Abstract: We study discrete-time quantum walks with Fourier, Grover, and Hadamard coin operators on disordered square lattices, alongside classical random walks. Edges in the lattice are absent with a probability, $p$. In this case, both classical and quantum walks exhibit an essentially identical degradation of transport. We then study a general version of this model, in which the existing edges are retained. However, the absent edges are no longer absent, but now become partially transmitting, with a transmission coefficient $f$. We find that the response is now quite sensitive to the walk protocol and coin symmetry. The classical walk exhibits asymptotic stability of the anomalous diffusion exponent, $β(t)$. In comparison, quantum walks exhibit a monotonically decaying $β(t)$ beyond the initial transient regime, leading to the absence of quantum diffusion in two dimensions. We map the transport regimes in the $(f,p)$ plane and identify the contour for $β=1$, which separates the subdiffusive and superdiffusive regimes. We also examine the effective diffusion coefficient, the inverse participation ratio, and the first two moments of edge transmission. We restrict ourselves to analyzing bulk propagation prior to finite-size boundary reflections. The results indicate that quantum transport depends significantly on coin-specific interference and cannot be fully described by transmission statistics alone.

</details>


### [356] [Network nonlocality with tripartite entangled sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35838)
*Sudip Chakrabarty,Amit Kundu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子网络中三比特纠缠源的非局域性与网络非局域性的关系，发现二者的关联取决于源的"接线"方式：共享全部源时Svetlichny违背可转化为网络违背，共享单个中心节点时反之需Mermin违背。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往量子网络的非局域性研究多基于双体纠缠源，而三比特纠缠源的非局域性可由Mermin或更严格的Svetlichny不等式刻画，但其网络非局域性如何与这两种单源非局域性概念相关联尚不明确。

Method: 构造基于三比特纠缠源的量子网络，分析不同接线方式（两个无输入中心节点共享全部源、单个中心节点被共享、以及多源情形），结合Bell态测量推导网络不等式与Mermin/Svetlichny不等式之间的转化关系，并对多源情形给出紧界。

Result: 两个无输入中心节点共享全部源时，Bell态测量可将任一Svetlichny违背转化为网络违背，甚至所有纠缠的广义GHZ态都违反网络不等式；仅共享单个中心节点时关系反转，网络违背需要源的Mermin违背；源数量更多时最大违背取决于数量奇偶性，并给出两种情形的紧界。

Conclusion: 多体纠缠源的接线方式决定了网络继承何种类型的非局域性。

Abstract: Quantum networks are built from independent sources, and their nonlocality has mostly been studied with sources that emit bipartite entangled states. A source of three entangled qubits is richer, since its nonlocality can be tested by the Mermin or the more demanding Svetlichny inequality. We ask how network nonlocality relates to these two single-source notions and find that the answer depends on the wiring. When two central nodes without inputs share every source, Bell-state measurements turn every Svetlichny violation into a network violation, and even all entangled generalised Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger states violate the network inequality. When a single central node is shared, the relation reverses, and a network violation requires a Mermin violation of the source. With more sources, the maximal violation depends on whether their number is even or odd, and we give tight bounds for both cases. These results show that the way multipartite sources are wired decides which kind of nonlocality a network inherits.

</details>


### [357] [Complete entropy contraction from conditional hyperboundedness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35863)
*Li Gao,Lijun Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 对与忠实条件期望相容的有限维量子信道，证明条件超有界性与严格GNS收缩共同推出相对熵收缩；对KMS对称量子马尔可夫半群，由时刻t0的条件2→q超有界性与GNS间隙λ>0给出修正对数Sobolev常数α=λ(1-2/q)/[12(λt0+log M+1-2/q)]，并借助完全有界超有界性得到具相同速率的完全熵收缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典与量子情形中超有界性常被用来从较弱的泛函不等式推出强收缩（熵收缩、对数Sobolev不等式），但量子信道在条件期望情形下缺乏相应定量刻画；本文旨在建立条件超有界性、GNS间隙与相对熵收缩之间的定量联系，并给出可迁移到辅助系统的完全版本。

Method: 以与忠实条件期望相容的有限维量子信道为对象，结合条件超有界性与严格GNS收缩推导相对熵收缩；对KMS对称量子马尔可夫半群，将时刻t0的2→q条件超有界性（常数M）与GNS间隙λ组合，通过插值与半群演化估计给出修正对数Sobolev常数α的显式表达式；进一步对辅助系统取完全有界超有界性，将标量结论张量化得到完全熵收缩。

Result: 证得条件超有界性加严格GNS收缩蕴含相对熵收缩；KMS对称半群在2→q条件超有界性与GNS间隙下满足修正对数Sobolev不等式，常数α=λ(1-2/q)/[12(λt0+log M+1-2/q)]；完全有界超有界性给出完全熵收缩，且速率与标量情形一致。

Conclusion: 超有界性可作为从GNS间隙导出熵收缩的桥梁，所得常数显式依赖于超有界常数M、时刻t0与参数q；结果在完全有界框架下保持速率不变，适用于量子马尔可夫半群的完全熵收缩分析。

Abstract: For finite-dimensional quantum channels compatible with a faithful conditional expectation, we show that conditional hyperboundedness and strict GNS contraction together yield a relative entropy contraction. For KMS-symmetric quantum Markov semigroups, a conditional $2\to q$ hyperboundedness at time $t_0>0$ combined with a GNS gap $λ>0$ implies a modified logarithmic Sobolev constant \[ α=\frac{λ(1-2/q)}
  {12(λt_0+\log M+1-2/q)},\] where $M$ is the hyperboundedness constant. As a consequence, completely bounded hyperboundedness over auxiliary systems gives complete versions of entropy contraction with the same rates.

</details>


<div id='physics.comp-ph'></div>

# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [358] [An Efficient Quantum Spectral-Galerkin Method for Linear Elliptic Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33095)
*Dongjie Wang,Sheng Chen*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种高效量子谱-Galerkin方法求解常系数二阶线性椭圆方程，采用边界适配的Jacobi张量积基，使Galerkin系统具有多项式有界的条件数与稀疏性，在解析解满足一致正则性时可在poly(d, log(1/ε))复杂度下制备归一化Galerkin系数态，并在量子态制备层面改进精度依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子数值方法（如固定阶有限差分、有限元离散）精度依赖较差，需要发展能利用谱收敛、在态制备层面以对数精度依赖求解椭圆方程的方法。

Method: 构造边界适配的Jacobi张量积基（将边界条件嵌入逼近空间），得到条件数多项式有界且稀疏的Galerkin系统；对满足一致正则性的解析解利用谱收敛性制备归一化Galerkin系数态；在标准稀疏访问与态制备假设下分析复杂度；并进一步给出线性泛函估计方案。

Result: 对解析解，归一化Galerkin系数态的制备复杂度为poly(d, log(1/ε))（d为空间维数，ε为误差容限），优于固定阶有限差分/有限元离散的精度依赖；但估计指定线性泛函时需额外增加O(1/ε)因子（源自范数与内积估计）。

Conclusion: 边界适配谱-Galerkin框架结合了良态、稀疏的离散系统与谱收敛，可在态制备层面实现对ε的多对数依赖；但泛函输出的读取仍带来1/ε因子，构成端到端复杂度的瓶颈，是后续需改进的方向。

Abstract: This paper presents an efficient quantum spectral-Galerkin method for constant-coefficient second-order linear elliptic equations. The method employs a boundary-adapted Jacobi tensor-product basis that incorporates the boundary condition into the approximation space and produces Galerkin systems with polynomially bounded condition numbers and sparsity. For analytic solutions satisfying a suitable uniform regularity condition, spectral convergence allows the normalized Galerkin coefficient state to be prepared with complexity $\mathrm{poly}(d,\log(1/ε))$, where $d$ is the spatial dimension and $ε$ is the error tolerance, under standard sparse-access and state-preparation assumptions. At the state-preparation level, this improves the precision dependence over fixed-order finite-difference and finite-element discretizations. We further show how to estimate prescribed linear functionals of the solution, with an additional $O(1/ε)$ factor arising from norm and inner-product estimation.

</details>


### [359] [Autonomous atomistic-continuum coupling through uncertainty-aware active learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33272)
*Daniel Abdulah,Hariswaran Sitaraman,Ross E. Larsen,Kevin Patrick Griffin,Grace Wei,Janelle Domantay,Ethan Young,Marc T. Henry de Frahan*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种不确定性感知的代理模型，用于将原子尺度KMC表面化学求解器与连续介质多组分流体求解器跨尺度耦合，并自动触发按需KMC采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子尺度KMC与连续介质流体求解器直接耦合计算成本极高；需在保持用户指定精度的前提下大幅减少昂贵的原子模拟调用次数，并解决多物种反应体系跨尺度数据交换的表达问题。

Method: 构建预测六种物种边界质量通量及其不确定性的代理模型；连续介质求解器监测代理不确定性，超过阈值时自动触发新的KMC模拟进行在线学习。KMC采用本工作构建的DFT标定能量学模型，输出六物种净沉积/解吸速率。以反应性卤化物钙钛矿气相沉积（多组分蒸气射流撞击反应衬底）为演示案例。

Result: 在保守不确定性阈值下，代理耦合方法复现直接耦合的累积质量通量，误差多在10–20%以内，并能捕捉净沉积对物种分压的非单调依赖；该精度目标下在线学习将所需原子模拟次数减少2–3个数量级。

Conclusion: 预测原子–连续介质求解器间交换量时无需显式表征详细晶体结构，物种分辨的质量通量即为足够低维的原子响应表示；不确定性驱动的自主在线学习可实现高效、可控精度的多尺度耦合。

Abstract: We develop an uncertainty-aware surrogate modeling approach for coupling an atomistic kinetic Monte Carlo (KMC) surface-chemistry solver with a continuum multi-species fluid dynamics solver across disparate physical length and time scales. The surrogate model predicts boundary mass fluxes for six species and their uncertainties, which the continuum solver monitors to automatically trigger additional KMC simulations when new training data are needed. This enables efficient multiscale coupling and maintains a user-specified level of accuracy. We demonstrate the approach for reactive halide-perovskite vapor deposition, which involves a multi-species vapor jet impinging on a reactive substrate. The KMC solver uses a DFT-calibrated energetics model that we construct as part of this work and provides net deposition and desorption rates for six species. With a conservative uncertainty threshold, the surrogate-coupled approach reproduces cumulative mass fluxes of the directly coupled approach with errors typically within 10-20%, while capturing the non-monotonic dependence of net deposition on species partial pressure. Importantly, the results show that detailed crystal structure need not be explicitly represented to predict quantities exchanged between atomistic and continuum solvers; species-resolved mass fluxes provide a sufficiently low-dimensional representation of the atomistic response. For this accuracy target, the autonomous online-learning approach reduces the number of required atomistic simulations by two to three orders of magnitude relative to direct multiscale coupling.

</details>


### [360] [A neural accelerated roughness-resolved Discrete Element Method (Rough-DEM) for modeling granular systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33363)
*Xiwei Pan,Liuchi Li*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出"粗糙度解析离散元法"(Rough-DEM)：不再使用唯象摩擦定律，而是显式解析颗粒表面形貌，让摩擦从表面微凸体的无摩擦集体相互作用中涌现；为实现加速引入FE²式算法与FiLM神经网络代理并GPU并行，在二维中既复现了双体Amontons-Coulomb型摩擦行为，又捕捉到现有DEM在同等复杂度下无法捕捉的多体摩擦行为（休止角）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DEM框架的核心均为唯象摩擦定律（通常为Amontons-Coulomb型）来刻画颗粒间摩擦，缺乏物理基础。作者受摩擦学研究的启发，希望以更基于物理的方式处理接触摩擦，用"物理"替代"唯象"。

Method: 构建Rough-DEM框架：显式解析每个颗粒的表面形貌，不采用摩擦定律，而令摩擦由表面微凸体之间的无摩擦集体相互作用涌现。为缓解计算开销，引入类FE²（FE²-like）算法与基于特征级线性调制（FiLM）的神经网络代理模型，二者均在GPU上并行化以加速接触计算，并在二维中实施验证。

Result: (1) 使用简单的表面形貌表示即可复现基于双体（two-body）的Amontons-Coulomb型摩擦行为；(2) 识别出基于多体（many-body）的摩擦行为，特别是休止角，其数值在同等模型复杂度下并非总能被现有DEM框架捕捉。

Conclusion: Rough-DEM以增加计算需求为代价换取物理严谨性，且通过FE²式算法与神经网络代理+GPU并行可有效缓解成本。作者最后讨论了Rough-DEM的未来发展方向，以及其可为悬浮液流变学（设计）和超材料力学等广泛新兴应用带来的新机遇。

Abstract: The Discrete Element Method (DEM) is an indispensable tool for modeling and understanding the basic mechanical behaviors of (dry) granular media, a class of disordered material systems of significant interest to multiple scientific domains from (geo-)physics to (chemical) engineering. At the core of all present DEM frameworks is a phenomenological friction law--commonly chosen to be of the Amontons-Coulomb type--that models the frictional interaction among constituent grains. Here, inspired by research in the tribology community, we present an alternative, fundamentally different framework, termed roughness-resolved DEM (Rough-DEM), in which contact friction is handled in a more physics-based manner. In this framework, instead of using a friction law, we explicitly resolve the surface topography of each grain and let friction emerge from collective frictionless interactions of surface asperities. In essence, we trade phenomenology for physics at the cost of an increased computational demand. To mitigate the cost, we introduce an FE$^2$-like algorithm and a feature-wise linear modulation (FiLM)-based neural network surrogate, both of which are parallelized on GPU to accelerate contact computation. Crucially, using this framework in 2D, we (1) demonstrate the possibility of recovering a two-body-based Amontons-Coulomb-type friction behavior using a \textit{simple} representation of surface topography, and (2) identify many-body-based frictional behaviors--more specifically, the angle of repose values--that \textit{cannot always} be captured by current DEM frameworks under the same level of model complexity. We conclude by discussing future directions for Rough-DEM, as well as exciting new opportunities it could unlock for a wide range of emerging applications, including (designing) suspension rheology and metamaterial mechanics.

</details>


### [361] [A Differentiable Framework for Inverse Identification of Heterogeneous Cohesive Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33711)
*Sida Hao,Jinkyo Han,Bahador Bahmani*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于内蕴粘聚区模型的可微有限元框架，利用物理约束神经网络与离散伴随灵敏度从全局力-位移曲线及稀疏应变测量中识别牵引-分离本构律，并将学习到的本构先验推广至空间非均匀、不可逆界面损伤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从宏观力学响应反演界面牵引-分离律是高度不适定的逆问题：全局力-位移响应对本构细节不敏感，且空间非均匀的粘聚参数带来高维未知量。

Method: 构建基于内蕴粘聚区公式的可微有限元框架；用物理约束神经网络表示满足热力学结构的柔性粘聚律；通过自动微分与离散伴随计算灵敏度，并计入界面损伤的历史依赖性演化；将本构先验扩展到不可逆损伤，由代表性牵引-分离历史构造共享的低维本构族，借助参数共享与潜坐标实现空间变异并正则化反问题。

Result: 双悬臂梁与纤维-基体复合材料数值算例表明：精确复现全局力-位移响应并不必然意味着准确恢复底层粘聚律；引入空间分辨应变测量即使在学习先验约束下也能显著提升本构识别精度；与常规双线性粘聚模型及贝叶斯优化的对比显示，所学本构表示与伴随法识别对高维逆问题更具优势。

Conclusion: 全局响应拟合不足以验证界面本构的反演正确性，需结合局部应变数据；可微有限元＋伴随灵敏度＋低维本构先验是识别空间非均匀粘聚律的有效途径。

Abstract: This work develops a differentiable finite element framework based on an intrinsic cohesive-zone formulation for identifying traction-separation laws from global force-displacement histories and spatially sparse strain measurements. The framework employs physics-constrained neural networks to represent flexible, thermodynamically structured cohesive laws, with sensitivities computed through automatic differentiation and a discrete adjoint that accounts for the history-dependent evolution of interfacial damage. To enable identification of spatially heterogeneous cohesive behavior, we extend learned constitutive priors to irreversible interfacial damage by constructing a shared, low-dimensional family of cohesive laws from representative traction-separation histories. This representation exploits common constitutive structure and parameter sharing to reduce the number of unknowns and regularize the inverse problem, while allowing local cohesive behavior to vary spatially through latent coordinates. Numerical investigations involving double-cantilever beams and fiber-matrix composites demonstrate that accurate reproduction of global force-displacement responses does not necessarily imply accurate recovery of the underlying cohesive laws. Incorporating spatially resolved strain measurements substantially improves constitutive identification, even when the admissible responses are constrained by a learned prior. Comparisons with conventional bilinear cohesive models and Bayesian optimization further illustrate the benefits of the learned constitutive representation and adjoint-based identification for high-dimensional inverse problems.

</details>


### [362] [Two-dimensional framework for modeling load transfer in nanocomposites with thin, stiff, and prestressed reinforcements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34014)
*Rohit Satish Patil,Zhilin Han,Sofia G. Mogilevskaya*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出一个基于Gurtin–Murdoch表面理论与等几何边界元法（IGABEM/NURBS）的框架，用于模拟薄、刚性、预拉伸增强体（纳米片/纳米板）在纳米复合材料中的载荷传递机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米复合材料中纳米片状增强体的载荷传递机制对力学性能至关重要，但缺乏能同时刻画增强体自身弹性、残余表面张力及任意曲率形状的高精度模拟方法。

Method: 将增强体建模为具有自身弹性性质和残余表面张力的开放材料表面（膜），基体为各向同性弹性体；平面应变下每张材料表面表示为任意形状的光滑曲线。理论基于Gurtin–Murdoch理论，以跨表面牵引力跃变表示表面效应；基体弹性场由以未知牵引跃变为核的积分表示精确给出。计算采用NURBS的等几何边界元法，通过求解耦合奇异边界积分方程组获得牵引跃变，进而计算基体场，并给出表面端点应力强度因子（SIF）的提取方法。

Result: 算法在沿直线段或圆弧布置的多个表面的基准解上得到验证；参数研究表明表面曲率、相对间距、取向及相互作用的影响显著；并给出石墨烯纳米片增强环氧树脂的算例。

Conclusion: 该框架为含表面效应增强体的纳米复合材料提供了一个精确、几何自适应的载荷传递分析工具，可有效预测端点应力集中与多增强体相互作用。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel framework for simulating load-transfer mechanisms in nanocomposites reinforced by thin, stiff, and prestressed reinforcements, such as, e.g., nanosheets or nanoplatelets. The reinforcements are modeled as open material surfaces treated as membranes endowed with their own elastic properties and residual surface tension; the matrix is assumed to be isotropic elastic. In the two-dimensional plane-strain setting considered here, each material surface is represented by a sufficiently smooth curve of otherwise arbitrary shape. The theoretical part of the framework is based on the Gurtin--Murdoch theory in which the surfaces are represented by traction jumps across them. The elastic fields in the matrix are exactly expressed by the integral representations written in terms of the unknown traction jumps that are functions of the second-order derivatives of the displacements. The computational part is based on the isogeometric boundary element method (IGABEM) that utilizes non-uniform rational B-splines (NURBS). The unknown traction jumps are found from the numerical solutions of the coupled system of singular boundary integral equations, after which the elastic fields in the matrix are evaluated. The procedure of extracting the values of the stress intensity factors (SIFs) at the tips of the surfaces is described. The proposed algorithm is validated against available benchmark solutions for multiple surfaces located along either straight segments or circular arcs. Selected parametric studies are performed to examine the influence of surface curvatures, relative spacing, orientation, and interactions. An example involving a graphene nanoplatelet-reinforced epoxy is presented.

</details>


### [363] [kstress: a LAMMPS package for calculating the 2D wavevector-dependent stress tensor and its anisotropy in amorphous solids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34206)
*Olivier Benzerara,Jörg Baschnagel,Alexander N. Semenov,Liudmyla Klochko*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 对LAMMPS中计算波矢—时间分辨应力张量σ_{αβ}(q,t)的模块进行优化实现，并以二维多分散Lennard-Jones玻璃形成液体为例给出验证流程，进而阐明仿射模量随波矢模量q的依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: q与t分辨的应力张量可用于获取非晶固体的弹性模量随探测尺度的变化，以及区分非晶固体与液体的长程应力关联；但其计算需遍历所有相互作用对，因而需要与分子动力学代码的力循环接口，计算代价高，且存在冗余计算与存储问题。

Method: i) 优化LAMMPS中计算σ_{αβ}(q,t)的模块：三角函数仅对基本波矢计算，其余通过递推得到；相关波矢以显式列表形式输入而非生成完整网格，从而只计算与存储感兴趣的波矢。ii) 提出代码验证流程，并在二维多分散Lennard-Jones(2DpLJ)玻璃形成液体上进行验证。基于上述实现，系统分析仿射模量对波矢模量q的依赖。

Result: 纵向仿射模量在对应结构因子主峰位置的q_max附近出现极小值；横模与纵模的振动频率在q依赖性上均表现出远低于q_max的峰值；并讨论了该特征与“玻色峰”的可能关联。

Conclusion: 优化后的实现显著提升了q分辨应力张量的计算效率与可控性，验证流程保证了结果可靠性；2DpLJ体系结果显示仿射模量与振动频率对q具有非单调特征，其中频率峰位于q_max以下的特征或与玻色峰相关。

Abstract: The stress tensor resolved in wavevector $\qvec$ and time $t$, $σ_{αβ}(\qvec,t)$, gives access to the elastic moduli of amorphous solids as a function of the probed length scale, and to the long-range stress correlations that distinguish an amorphous solid from a liquid. Its evaluation requires the contribution of every interacting pair and therefore an interface to the force loop of the molecular dynamics code. In the current work, we present: i) an optimized implementation of the LAMMPS~\cite{lammps} module for calculating $σ_{αβ}(\qvec,t)$, in which trigonometric functions are calculated only for the basic wavevectors and, in addition, all the relevant wavevectors are supplied as an explicit list rather than generated as a full grid, so that only the wavevectors of interest are computed and stored, and ii) a validation protocol for the code illustrated for a two-dimensional polydisperse Lennard-Jones (2DpLJ) glass-forming liquid. Based on these technical achievements we elucidated how the affine moduli for the 2DpLJ model depend on the norm $q$ of the wavevector. In particular, we demonstrate that the longitudinal affine modulus shows a minimum near $q_\text{max}$ corresponding to the main peak of the structure factor. Our data also point to the peaks in the $q$-dependencies of vibrational frequencies well below $q_\text{max}$ for both transverse and longitudinal modes. The potential relevance of this feature to the ``boson peak'' is discussed as well.

</details>


### [364] [A GPU-efficient local ADER finite-difference method for compressible-flow DNS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34252)
*Hong Zhang,Xian Wang,Xinliang Li,Yibing Chen,Xing Ji*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 作者提出一种面向GPU的局部ADER有限差分方法，用紧凑十点二次预测量与守恒中心校正量的算子匹配替代多级Runge–Kutta，在保持精度的同时显著降低可压缩流DNS的时间推进开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU上的CFD加速研究多集中于空间离散，而时间推进仍普遍依赖多级Runge–Kutta方法。RK需要重复的残差评估与全域操作，对可压缩流直接数值模拟（DNS）开销较大，成为GPU效率瓶颈。

Method: 构建局部ADER有限差分格式：将紧凑十点二次预测器与守恒中心校正器通过算子匹配耦合；配置两个求积节点、一次Picard迭代修正与选择性耗散。时间状态保持核内局部（kernel-local），halo通信与内部计算重叠，从而避免RK式的全域同步与重复残差评估。

Result: 在固定CFL加密下，光滑对流测试恢复三阶收敛；当背景空间误差占主导时呈近七阶空间精度。在无热传导的302³节点粘性Taylor–Green基准上，单块V100双精度吞吐达2.3087亿节点更新/秒，为空间匹配的三级RK的1.944倍；4至32块V100、18.12亿节点下弱扩展效率达99.44%。

Conclusion: 局部ADER格式可在GPU上以单次局部时间更新获得与传统高阶RK相当甚至更优的精度，并带来约1.9倍吞吐提升与近乎线性的弱扩展性，是GPU可压缩流DNS时间推进的可行替代方案。

Abstract: Algorithm design tailored to graphics processing units (GPUs) can substantially improve the efficiency of computational fluid dynamics (CFD) simulations. Efforts to adapt CFD solvers to GPUs often focus on spatial discretization, while time advancement commonly relies on multistage Runge--Kutta (RK) methods. However, these RK methods require repeated residual evaluations and full-domain operations, increasing the cost of compressible-flow direct numerical simulation (DNS). To reduce this overhead, we develop a local arbitrary high-order derivatives (ADER) finite-difference method that couples a compact ten-point quadratic predictor to a conservative central corrector through operator matching. With two quadrature nodes, one Picard correction, and selective dissipation, smooth convective tests recover third-order convergence under fixed-CFL refinement and near-seventh-order spatial behavior when background spatial error dominates. Temporal states remain kernel-local, and halo communication overlaps interior computation. Tests of smooth advection, periodic turbulence, and wall-bounded flows assess accuracy and flow statistics. On a $302^3$-node viscous Taylor--Green benchmark without heat conduction, throughput reaches 230.87 million node updates per second on one V100 GPU in double precision, 1.944 times that of spatially matched three-stage Runge--Kutta integration. Weak-scaling efficiency reaches 99.44\% from 4 to 32 V100 GPUs at 1.812 billion nodes.

</details>


### [365] [Towards Exact Multidimensional Fluxes: A Structure-Preserving Data-Informed Framework for Riemann Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35265)
*Yucheng Zhang,Clint Dawson*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种"结构保持、数据驱动"的黎曼求解器框架：利用时空通量积分关于时间步长与面尺寸的精确多项式结构，用神经网络仅学习从初始状态到多项式系数的映射，并通过构造保证一致性与对称性，从而获得近精确的多维黎曼通量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限体积与间断伽辽金方法依赖面元上的时空通量积分，而该积分通常由局部黎曼问题解决定。多维黎曼问题因波系相互作用复杂，难以精确求积，因此需要一种既精确又高效的替代方案。

Method: 先证明精确时空通量积分是时间步长与面尺寸的多项式；据此只学习"初始状态→多项式系数"的映射。用高分辨率模拟数据训练神经网络，并在网络结构上硬性嵌入一致性与对称性等结构性质，构建结构保持的多维黎曼求解器。

Result: 在二维欧拉方程的经典内爆/爆炸算例中，将学习型黎曼求解器嵌入非分裂MUSCL-Hancock格式：CFL=0.9 时仍稳定，而使用精确一维黎曼求解器的同一格式失效；与后者在 CFL=0.45 下相比，学习型格式精度略高且计算时间约减少30%。

Conclusion: 若高分辨率模拟收敛到精确通量积分且网络系数预测准确，该框架可提供近精确的通量积分用于数值模拟，兼顾稳定性、精度与效率。

Abstract: Finite-volume and discontinuous Galerkin methods update cells using the space-time flux integral over their faces, which typically depends on the solutions to local Riemann problems. In the multidimensional Riemann problem setting, this integral is difficult to evaluate exactly because of complex wave interactions. In this work, we show that the exact space-time flux integral is a polynomial in the time step and face dimensions. This motivates a learning-based approach, as only the mapping from the initial states to the polynomial coefficients needs to be learned. We propose a structure-preserving, data-informed Riemann solver framework that uses a neural network trained on high-resolution simulation data to learn this mapping. Key structural properties, including consistency and symmetry, are enforced by construction. Provided that the high-resolution simulations converge to the exact flux integrals and the network accurately predicts the coefficients, this framework yields near-exact flux integrals for use in numerical simulations.
  We demonstrate the framework using classical implosion and explosion problems for the two-dimensional Euler equations. In an unsplit MUSCL-Hancock scheme, the learned Riemann solver remains stable at a CFL number of 0.9, whereas the same scheme using the exact one-dimensional Riemann solver fails. Compared with the latter scheme at CFL=0.45, the learned scheme is slightly more accurate and requires approximately 30% less computational time.

</details>
